#CH 201
[ZH] 人越多，白风越兴奋，他不想王爷帮忙：“公子，不要跟我抢。”
[ZH] 申简辰用沉默回答，与陈浩宇在一旁观战。
[ZH] 白风与蔣爷的手下打起来，开始时还可应对自如。
[ZH] 对方毕竟人多，打着打着白风对付起来越来越困难。好在这家伙根骨好，动作灵活多变，又懂得躲闪，搞得对方想要拿住他并不容易。
[ZH] 陈浩宇十分不解，仪王的名头已经很厉害，用名头吓唬吓唬蔣爷多好？偏偏装作申公子，让侍卫跟这些人打来打去，实在麻烦。
[ZH] 正打得激烈，蔣爷带人前来。仔细观察一会儿，对方的手下以一人之力对付他的人，想赢并不容易。可已经打过几十回合，分不出胜负。他猜想那位身材高大，五官俊朗，一身贵气的公子定然武功不低。
[ZH] 蔣爷又见陈浩宇站在一旁，先怀疑是陈浩宇花钱请的打手。又一想，不对！陈浩宇躲在对方身后，没有往常趾高气扬的样子，看来此人身份不一般。
[ZH] “够了！不要再打！”
[ZH] 蔣爷发话，他的手下收起手中的剑，乖乖退到一边。
[ZH] 白风用轻功落下，装作轻松的样子：“小爷正打得爽，你们就退下，太不给小爷面子！”
[ZH] 蔣爷懒得理会白风，瞧向对面气质高贵的公子：“这位公子，你我素不相识，为何来钱塘帮寻事？”
[ZH] 没等申简辰说话，陈浩宇大声说：“蔣爷！申公子是我请来的，你要是识相赶快把字据给我！”
[ZH] 蔣爷当然不信陈浩宇的话，还是回道：“爷爷我在江湖混，说一不二，答应的事绝不反悔，既然你跟朱家姑娘已成亲，必然给你字据，何必找人打进来？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇说得果然没错，对方竟然敢用千里寻的婚姻作为条件，无疑踩到申简辰的老虎尾巴。众人不经意间，他已用轻功移步到蒋爷身边，抓住他的衣领：“把字据拿出来！”
[ZH] 蒋爷身边的人想要靠拢过来，白风用轻功迅速来到王爷身边负责保护，那些人都不敢随意靠近。
[ZH] “都别伤到英雄，退到一边。”蒋爷倒不生气，笑嘻嘻的样子，“英雄，你的功夫厉害，比陈浩宇找的那个叫陆远峰的人还厉害，佩服佩服，字据很快给你。”
[ZH] 蒋爷立刻吩咐手下将字据拿来，他的衣领被抓着，手倒自由，接过手下递来的字据：“英雄，请过目。”
[ZH] 申简辰一手接过字据，一手松开蒋爷，认真将字据看了个遍，没发现以朱家姑娘为赌博条件的任何字样，气消了一半。将字据递给白风：“拿着。”
[ZH] 白风还没接到手中，陈浩宇已迅速拿走：“既是我的，还是让我拿走。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇仔细看一遍，确认无误后，谢道：“感谢申公子，我还有事，改日再登门拜谢。”说完一溜烟跑掉。
[ZH] 当他跑到门外，见疤子等人才带着三位高手前来。
[ZH] “怎么才来？”陈浩宇质问后，没好气地举起字据，“瞧瞧！本公子已拿到，要等你们来黄花菜都凉了！”
[ZH] 疤子嘻嘻笑道：“是晚了些，可把他们请来没有功劳也有苦劳，陈公子总不能让我们白跑。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇瞧三人一眼，这三人他认识，出去浪荡时倒也带过，武功尚可，跟陆远峰比差得太多：“陆远峰呢？”
[ZH] 疤子说：“陈公子有所不知，哥几个最先找的就是陆远峰，结果他人不在，门上还上把大锁，估计是拿着您给的银子逃走了。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇向来大手大脚，根本不在乎陆远峰拿走的银子：“你们三个以后天天跟着我，不管本公子去哪里都跟着，银子少不了你们的。”
[ZH] 三位高手说：“任凭陈公子差遣。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇很满意，转头瞧向疤子等人：“你们几个以后不要再跟着我，关键时候屁事不顶，就知道花本公子的银子。”
[ZH] 疤子与几个小弟围过来央求道：“陈公子，我们错了，不该在赌坊时贪生怕死不帮您，以后您有什么事，哥几个一定鞍前马后，冲锋陷阵，绝不退缩。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇也只是吓唬吓唬他们：“既然是你们死皮赖脸想跟着我，本公子心善，不与你们计较，但需给你们些惩罚，今儿的跑路费就免了，再说本公子出来也没带银子。”
[ZH] 疤子等人虽可惜跑路费，想到只要跟着陈公子，挣钱的机会多得是，便咬咬牙：“跑路费给不给不重要，重要的是陈公子还愿意带哥几个玩儿。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇不再计较：“身上有没有带火折子？”
[ZH] “有！有！”疤子掏出火折子，打开吹出火苗，递到陈公子面前。
[ZH] 陈浩宇并未接，拿出字据，用火点着，扔在地上，目睹它化为灰烬，心中的一块石头终于落地，不用再担心自己会成为陈家的千古罪人。
[EN] Bigger crowds only fired Bai Feng up. "Do not steal my fun, Young Master!"
[ZH] “走！跟本公子去春香楼！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen folded his arms in silence alongside Chen Haoyu to watch. Early exchanges went smooth for Bai Feng, yet sheer numbers slowly pressed him tighter; nimble footwork kept him dodging blades cleanly without letting them pin him down. Watching nearby, Chen Haoyu wondered why on earth Prince Yi did not just drop his royal title to flatten Master Jiang instead of playing "Young Master Shen."
[ZH] 陈浩宇不敢动朱淑真，昨晚的洞房花烛白白给浪费。毕竟是新婚之夜，他不由得情 欲躁动。大事处理完，一阵轻松，决定去春香楼找春金沫。有仪王在钱塘帮，蒋爷一时半会儿去不了春香楼，他正好趁机玩乐一番。
[EN] Mid-brawl, Master Jiang strode into the yard. Sizing up one guard holding off dozens to a standstill—and spotting the tall, aristocratic stranger watching calmly while normally cocky Chen Haoyu stood tucked behind him—Master Jiang smelled real trouble at once.
[ZH] 对于陈浩宇的离开，申简辰懒得理会，想到此事没有那么简单，还需向蒋爷了解清楚。
[EN] "Enough! Hold back!"
[ZH] “我今日来，一是帮陈公子讨要字据；二是问问蒋爷为何设套让陈公子钻，还用朱家姑娘的婚姻为条件？”
[EN] At his bark, gangsters sheathed steel and parted. Floating lightly back to ground, Bai Feng scoffed, "Just warming up—talk about spoilsports!"
[ZH] 蒋爷自知打不过对方，可要坏了他在江湖上立的规矩，也是万万不可：“英雄，你武功高强，蒋爷我佩服，今儿不打不相识，不如做个朋友？我这个人呢，没什么优点，但有一点江湖人还是认可的，就是爱才。不是钱财的财，是人才，尤其是像英雄这号武艺超群之人……”
[EN] Ignoring the guard, Master Jiang addressed the poised nobleman. "Never met before, Young Master—why bring trouble to Qiantang Gang?"
[ZH] 申简辰懒得再听下去：“少废话，赶紧回答问题。”
[EN] Before Shen Jianchen opened his mouth, Chen Haoyu crowed, "Young Master Shen is my backup, Master Jiang! Be smart and cough up my contract!"
[ZH] 蒋爷拱手道：“英雄一看就是说一不二之人，应该能体会到我的难处，我收人钱财，帮人办事，您就别为难我，不如留下来与我帮中弟兄痛饮几杯？”
[EN] Hardly buying that Chen Haoyu commanded a man like this, Master Jiang still kept his swagger. "In the underworld, my word is iron—since you wed Miss Zhu, your contract is yours anyway. Why fight through my gate?"
[ZH] 申简辰根本不予理会：“让你的人都退下，滚得越远越好！”
[EN] Hearing it confirmed straight from Master Jiang's lips—using Qianli Xun's marriage as a pawn!—lit the fuse under Shen Jianchen's temper. In one blur of light-foot speed, he crossed the yard and seized Master Jiang by the throat of his robe. "Hand over the contract!"
[ZH] 蒋爷看出对方今儿不揍他一顿誓不罢休，他也不愿轻易被人揍，向帮中的兄弟大声命令：“都给我上！”
[EN] Gangsters surged forward, only for Bai Feng to flash right beside his prince, freezing them dead.
[ZH] 只见众人手拿刀剑，围将过来。蒋爷则退出圈外，以求自保。
[EN] "Hold off—do not scratch the hero!" Master Jiang grinned easily despite the fist at his collar. "Fierce skill, Hero—beats even Chen Haoyu's Lu Yuanfeng! Contract coming right up!" Signaling a lieutenant to fetch it, he held the paper up. "Inspect away, Hero."
[ZH] 钱塘帮的人看着吓人，不过都是些纸老虎，有王爷加入，应对起来并不难。没多时，大部分人被二人打倒在地，剩下的因为害怕不敢再靠近，手握刀剑，能躲多远就躲多远。
[EN] Taking it with one hand and releasing Master Jiang with the other, Shen Jianchen scanned every line—no mention of Miss Zhu anywhere on the page, cooling half his wrath. Handing it toward Bai Feng—"Take it"—he watched Chen Haoyu snatch it midair: "Mine anyway—I will hold it!"
[ZH] 蒋爷见状，扭头准备跑开，还是被白风抓住：“想跑没那么容易！”
[EN] Checking every word clean, Chen Haoyu bowed hastily. "Much obliged, Young Master Shen—got errands, will repay you another day!" And bolted straight out the gate—running smack into Scarface arriving late with three middling fighters.
[ZH] 申简辰命令道：“给我打！”
[EN] "Late as usual!" Chen Haoyu scoffed, waving the slip. "Already got it! Waiting on you lot, dinner would freeze over!"
[ZH] 只听一阵噼里啪啦的拳脚声……
[EN] "Late, sure, still earned sweat bringing them!" Scarface whined.
[ZH] 钱塘帮的人看到帮主被打，一个个束手无策。令他们惊叹的是，白风的拳头又快又准，专挑不要命处打，帮主是无性命之忧，可伤痛要好起来也得些时日。
[EN] Eyeing the three passable hires—nowhere near Lu Yuanfeng's league—Chen Haoyu frowned. "Where is Lu Yuanfeng?"
[ZH] 白风终于打累：“公子，把他打成这样应该可以了吧？”
[EN] "Hit his house first—big padlock on his gate! Probably took your silver and skipped town!"
[ZH] 申简辰向蒋爷瞧去，满脸满身都是伤，比上次打陈浩宇时还要严重：“可以了。”
[EN] Careless with silver as ever, Chen Haoyu shrugged. "You three shadow me everywhere from now on—good pay!" Accepting at once, the three fell in behind as Chen Haoyu glared at Scarface's crew. "Quit leeching off me—useless when blades flash!" After frantic begging and oaths of loyalty, Chen Haoyu let them off with zero errand pay—"Pass me a fire-starter!"
[ZH] 申简辰靠近蒋爷，蹲下小声说：“现在可不可以告诉我为何要把朱家姑娘牵扯到你们的赌博中？”
[EN] Touching flame to the contract, Chen Haoyu watched it burn to ash on the dirt—a mountain lifted off his chest! "To Chunxiang Brothel!" Starved from a wasted wedding night and safe while Prince Yi tied Master Jiang up, he strutted off toward Chun Jinmo.
[ZH] 蒋爷算是听明白，对方是为朱家姑娘出面，他忍着满身的伤痛说：“蒋爷……我行走……江湖，将……信誉看得……很……很重，为什么……我不能……不能说，不过英雄放心，此事……只针对……陈浩宇，与朱家姑娘无关。”
[EN] Back inside the yard, paying zero mind to Chen Haoyu's exit, Shen Jianchen turned icy eyes back onto Master Jiang. "Two errands brought me here: first, that contract; second, tell me WHY you rigged a trap for Chen Haoyu using Miss Zhu's wedding as the condition!"
[ZH] 申简辰不由佩服起蒋爷，被打成这样，依然不松口：“你不说，我迟早会知道，虽然不是针对朱家姑娘，但你利用她，搞得她嫁给陈浩宇那种货色，今儿打你都是轻的，下次再敢利用朱家姑娘，小心我要了你的狗命！”
[EN] Outmatched physically yet bound by underworld code, Master Jiang tried talking his way clear. "Admire top fighters like you, Hero—why not trade blows for drinks and become friends? If folks say one good thing about me, I treasure true talent..."
[ZH] 申简辰说完，带着白风穿越钱塘帮的大院。蒋爷的手下一个个都不敢靠近，眼睁睁看着二人离开。
[EN] "Cut the crap and answer my question."
[ZH] 钱塘帮的人这才围到蒋爷跟前：“帮主，您没事吧？”
[EN] "A man of iron like you understands my spot," Master Jiang cupped his hands. "Took pay to do a job—cannot sell out a client. Stay and drink with my brothers instead?"
[ZH] 蒋爷很生气，忍着痛骂道：“少……少废话！把我抬……抬进去！”
[EN] "Tell your boys to clear out—far as they can!" Shen Jianchen snapped.
[ZH] 手下安顿好帮主，给他全身上下涂好药。蒋爷一边呻 吟，一边说：“赶紧……去查查，此人……是什么来头。”
[EN] Seeing no talking his way past a beating, Master Jiang barked, "Jump them!" and backed clear as armed gangsters swarmed in.
[ZH] 一位手下说：“此人我见过，昨日陈公子成亲，他还抢亲来着。”
[EN] Paper tigers all—with Prince Yi stepping in beside Bai Feng, the yard emptied in minutes, groaning thugs littering the dirt while survivors scrambled back in terror. Spinning to bolt, Master Jiang felt Bai Feng's iron grip lock onto his collar. "Not going anywhere!"
[ZH] “抢亲？”蒋爷终于明白为何此人为了朱家姑娘前来揍他，“他是谁？你……可知？”
[EN] "Beat him!" Shen Jianchen ordered.
[ZH] 手下说：“这个我倒不知。”
[EN] A crisp storm of punches and kicks echoed across the yard—Bai Feng striking lightning-fast only on flesh that hurt like hell without killing, leaving Master Jiang howling until Bai Feng paused panting. "Enough, Young Master?"
[ZH] “快去！快去……查！”
[EN] Eyeing Master Jiang swollen purple head to toe—worse than Chen Haoyu had looked—Shen Jianchen nodded, crouched down beside him, and murmured, "Ready to tell me why you dragged Miss Zhu into your rigged bet?"
[ZH] 临安的黑道蒋爷再熟悉不过，从未见过此人；白道蒋爷多少也有接触，也从未见过这号人。到底从哪里冒出来？凭借第六感，蒋爷判断此人肯定不一般，恐怕自己一不小心得罪重要人物。
[EN] Grasping at last who this terror fought for, Master Jiang wheezed through split lips, "Walk... the underworld... word is everything... cannot tell you... who... Yet rest easy, Hero... aimed only at... Chen Haoyu... nothing against Miss Zhu..."
[ZH] 泄愤后，申简辰并未回幽林别居，命令白风将马车赶往迷 魂巷，查探金人下落。
[EN] Admiring the boss's stubborn grit despite himself, Shen Jianchen stood up coldly. "Keep quiet—I will dig it out anyway. Aimed at Chen Haoyu or not, using Miss Zhu as bait to trap her with trash like him earned every bruise you got. Pull her into another stunt and I take your life!"
[ZH] 白风边赶马车边朝里问：“王爷为何不将此任务交给林统制，他手底下人多，只要带人把巷子围住，保准金人插翅难飞。”
[EN] Striding out with Bai Feng through a silent lane of terrified thugs, they left Master Jiang groaning as his men rushed to carry him inside and smear salve across his welts.
[ZH] “现今官家正在与金人商谈失地收复之事，宋金表面还是一派祥和，若为一个金人动用官兵，恐怕会破坏表面的祥和，还是你我前去探探，省得惹下大麻烦。”申简辰可不想给金人攻打大宋制造借口，又一想自己也是多虑，他一个穿越而来的未来人哪有能力对历史产生影响。
[EN] "Go... dig up... who that man is!" Master Jiang hissed.
[ZH] 白风夸道：“还是王爷想得周到。”
[EN] "Recognized him, Boss!" a lieutenant spoke up. "That is the exact guy who tried snatching Young Master Chen's bride yesterday!"
[ZH] 二人来到迷 魂巷外，白风找地方将马车拴好，跟随王爷从巷口进入。巷子里偶尔会有人从身旁经过，他们猜想应该是住在巷子里的人。
[EN] "Bride-snatcher?!" Everything clicked—no wonder he came tearing in for Miss Zhu! "Who IS he?!"
[ZH] 两人有意识地记住进来的路，刚开始尚可，走着走着，越来越迷糊，渐渐分不清楚方向，他们就像进入迷宫，在巷子里穿梭来穿梭去。
[EN] "No clue, Boss!"
[ZH] 这么穿梭下去，也不知什么时候能找那个金人，申简辰决定找人问问。正好迎面走来一男子，申简辰示意白风上前打听。
[EN] "Find out—NOW!" Familiar with every underworld face and half the yamen too, Master Jiang sensed cold sweat—he had stepped squarely on a hidden dragon's tail!
[ZH] 白风上前拱手道：“这位大哥，我们第一次来迷 魂巷，找不到出去的路，可否帮我们引路？”
[EN] Venting his fury done, Shen Jianchen skipped Youlin Retreat and ordered Bai Feng straight to Mihun Alley to track the Jurchen.
[ZH] 男子倒爽快：“没问题，二位这边走。”
[EN] Driving along, Bai Feng called back, "Why not hand this to Division Commander Lin? One ring of troops around the alley and that Jurchen has zero escape!"
[ZH] 申简辰趁机问：“你住在迷 魂巷？”
[EN] "Court talks with the Jurchens over returning the lost territories are delicate—keeping surface peace," Shen Jianchen explained. "Mobilize troops over one Jurchen and give them a pretext to tear up talks? Better just you and me scout quietly." *Not that one transmigrator could shift history's avalanche anyway.*
[ZH] 男子说：“当然，只有常年住在这巷子里的人才熟悉弯弯绕绕的路。”
[EN] "Sharp thinking as always, Your Highness!"
[ZH] 申简辰继续问：“不知近期有没有在迷 魂巷见过长相跟临安人不大一样的人？”
[EN] Hitching the carriage outside Mihun Alley, they stepped inside trying to memorize turns—and within ten minutes wound thoroughly turned around inside a living labyrinth. Spotting a passerby, Shen Jianchen nodded to Bai Feng, who stepped forward politely asking directions out.
[ZH] 男子站住想了想：“你这么一问，我倒想起一人来……”
[EN] "Easy—follow me!" the local agreed cheerfully.
[ZH] 申简辰急切地问：“谁？”
[EN] Walking alongside, Shen Jianchen dropped a casual probe. "Live in Mihun Alley long?"
[ZH] 男子说：“昨日，我去一朋友家，他家来了位亲戚，那亲戚的五官长得不像汉人。”
[EN] "Born and bred—only locals survive these twists."
[ZH] 真是踏破铁鞋无觅处，得来全不费功夫，随便找个带路的，竟问出重要信息。申简辰向白风示意，只见白风掏出一两银子，递给那人：“这是带路钱，再麻烦带我们去见见你的朋友。”
[EN] "Seen anyone around lately who looks different from ordinary Lin'an locals?"
[ZH] 那人不敢接银子，谨慎道：“你们是何人？为何要找我朋友的亲戚？”
[EN] Pausing mid-stride, the man snapped his fingers. "Now that you mention it—one guy! Dropped by a friend's house yesterday and saw a relative staying there whose features did NOT look Han at all!"
[ZH] 申简辰解释道：“不必担心，那位长相不像汉人的人有可能是我们的朋友，听说他受了伤，进入这巷子，我们才赶来寻找。”
[EN] Talk about striking gold! At Shen Jianchen's nod, Bai Feng pressed a tael of silver into the man's hand. "Guide fee—take us to your friend's door."
[ZH] 男人半信半疑，可银子是真的，再说眼前的二人看着面善，应该不是坏人。他大胆接过银子：“我那朋友的亲戚确实受了伤，既如此，我带你们前去，你们若是骗人，我朋友会拳法，对付你们不在话下。”
[EN] Eyeing the silver warily, the local hesitated. "Who are you two, chasing my friend's kin?"
[ZH] 男子带着申简辰和白风绕过几条巷子，来到陆远峰家门外，门上有一把大大的锁。
[EN] "Relax—that non-Han man may be an old friend of ours who took an injury and ducked in here, so we came looking," Shen Jianchen reassured him smoothly.
[ZH] 男子说：“你们来得不巧，我的朋友出远门了。”
[EN] Weighing their honest faces against real silver, the local pocketed it. "He really WAS hurt! Come on—and try any tricks, my friend packs mean fists that will flatten you both!"
[ZH] 申简辰问：“何以见得？”
[EN] Winding through four more tight twists, they stopped before Lu Yuanfeng's gate—sealed shut with a hefty iron padlock.
[ZH] 男子说：“他有个习惯，人在临安，出去从来不锁门，只要离开临安必然上大锁。”
[EN] "Bad luck—my friend left town," the local said.
[ZH] 申简辰问：“难道家里没有其他人？”
[EN] "How do you know?"
[ZH] 男子说：“他一人吃饱全家不饿。”
[EN] "Habit of his: stays in Lin'an, never locks up; slaps the big padlock on only when traveling far."
[ZH] 本想找到那金人，仔细打探他的拳法到底从何习得，再问问他来临安的目的，说不定此人还是一条大鱼。就这么失之交臂，申简辰自然面露失望之色：“你这位朋友叫什么名字？以后若有幸遇见好向他打听我那朋友之事。”
[EN] "Nobody else home?"
[ZH] 男子说：“他叫陆远峰，拳头甚是厉害，不过也输过一回，听说是输给他的师傅。”
[EN] "Lives solo—one mouth to feed."
[ZH] 名字已足够，申简辰不想再了解更多。
[EN] Disappointment clouded Shen Jianchen's face—missed the Jurchen AND the mystery behind his Shen Fist! "What is your friend's name, in case we cross paths someday?"
[ZH] 男子把他与白风带出巷子后，申简辰不想回幽林别居，过不了几日要回汴梁成亲，临行前他想见见千里寻。
[EN] "Lu Yuanfeng! Killer fists—only ever heard of him losing once, to his own master."
[ZH] 总不能冒冒失失跑到陈家去找她，该怎么办？申简辰想到一个人，吩咐白风将马车赶往东方府。
[EN] Name noted, Shen Jianchen asked no more. Guided back out to the avenue, he had zero appetite to return to Youlin Retreat—with his wedding trip to Bianliang looming in days, he needed one last sight of Qianli Xun! Impossible to barge into Chen Manor—wait: one man could pull it off! "Bai Feng—to the Dongfang Manor!"

#CH 202
[ZH] 千里寻为长辈敬完茶离开后，几位长辈又聊一回，各自散去。
[ZH] 没过一会儿，王管家前来，向陈江林报告：“老爷，有一事我甚是为难，前来向您与夫人请示。”
[ZH] 陈江林刚取出棋子，还未与姚轻琰开始对战：“什么事？”
[EN] After Qianli Xun finished serving tea to the elders and withdrew, the older generation chatted a while longer before going their separate ways.
[ZH] 王管家照实说：“少夫人要求把二公子院中的丫鬟全部打发走，都用自己的人，昨日我不好当面驳少夫人的面子，她毕竟刚刚嫁过来。回去后仔细思量，此事还需请示老爷和夫人。”
[EN] Before long, Butler Wang came to report to Chen Jianglin. “Master, a tricky matter has come up, and I need instructions from you and Madam.”
[ZH] 姚轻琰拿出一颗棋子，在棋盘上随意挑位置放好：“浩宇院中那些个丫鬟我看着着实不顺眼，也不知他是怎么从外面捣鼓来的？早都想劝他打发走，不如趁此机会给打发了。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin had just set out the Go stones, not yet starting his match against Yao Qingyan. “What is it?”
[ZH] 陈江林也落一子：“夫人说得对，淑真既然有此想法，就按她的意思办。”
[EN] Butler Wang gave it to him straight. “Young Madam wants to dismiss every single maid in Second Young Master’s courtyard and replace them with her own people. Yesterday I couldn’t well turn her down to her face—she only just married into the family. Thinking it over afterward, I realized I had to ask Master and Madam first.”
[ZH] 姚轻琰点点头，向管家问道：“淑真这么做，浩宇是何意思？”
[EN] Yao Qingyan plucked a stone and set it at random on the board. “Those maids in Haoyu’s courtyard have been an eyesore to me for ages—who even knows where he dug them up? I’ve wanted him to send them packing for a long time. Better to take this chance and clear them all out.”
[ZH] 王管家回道：“二公子自然不乐意，不过看上去奈何不了少夫人，最后也就同意了。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin placed a stone of his own. “My wife is right. Since Shuzhen wants it done, do as she says.”
[ZH] 陈江林笑道：“如此看来这儿媳妇还真是娶对了，王管家只管按照淑真说的办。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan nodded and asked the butler, “How did Haoyu take it?”
[ZH] 王管家答应着，又感到为难：“老爷、夫人，还有一难事需禀报。”
[EN] “Second Young Master was none too pleased, of course,” Butler Wang replied, “yet he didn’t seem able to do a thing about Young Madam, so he agreed in the end.”
[ZH] 陈江林边下棋边说：“说吧。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin laughed. “Looks like we picked the right daughter-in-law after all. Just follow Shuzhen’s orders, Butler Wang.”
[ZH] 王管家说：“二公子房中的丫鬟一半不愿离开陈府，需要把她们分到其他院子，该分到哪些院子我拿捏不定，刚才来的路上碰见二爷和三爷，顺口提了一嘴，他们很乐意要。后又碰见二夫人和三夫人，她们坚决不要，说二公子院子里的丫鬟长得太过狐媚，要不得。”
[EN] Butler Wang assented, then hesitated. “Master, Madam, there is one more awkward problem to report.”
[ZH] 姚轻琰手中捏着棋子，正犹豫要不要落下，听到此话，果断落下：“要我说都遣散走，省得惹得各院不得安宁。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin spoke without lifting his eyes from the board. “Go on.”
[ZH] 陈江林十分赞同：“都遣散走。”
[EN] “Half the maids from Second Young Master’s quarters refuse to leave Chen Manor and will have to be reassigned to other courtyards. I wasn’t sure where to put them. On my way over I ran into Second Master and Third Master and mentioned it in passing, and both were eager to take them. Then I ran into Second Madam and Third Madam, who flat-out refused, saying the maids from Second Young Master’s courtyard look far too seductive and can’t be kept.”
[ZH] 王管家领命：“是。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan paused with a stone between her fingers, then snapped it onto the board. “If you ask me, dismiss the whole lot of them before they stir up trouble in the other courtyards.”
[ZH] 管家走后，二人继续下棋，输赢不重要，重要的是互相陪伴。
[EN] Chen Jianglin agreed without hesitation. “Dismiss them all.”
[ZH] 姚轻琰见陈江林老让着她，装作不满：“每次下棋你都让着我，真无趣。”
[EN] “Yes, Master.” Butler Wang bowed and withdrew.
[ZH] “不尽然，我看夫人下棋甚是有趣，尤其是赢的时候，满脸都是笑容。”
[EN] Once the butler left, the couple went back to their game. Winning or losing mattered little; what mattered was each other’s company.
[ZH] 姚轻琰想到儿子之事：“之前你我也想过遣散浩宇院中的丫鬟，后来因担心老太太疼孙子前来阻拦，也就不了了之。你说老太太要是知道淑贞将浩宇院中的丫鬟全部遣散走，会不会闹出些事情来？”
[EN] Noticing Chen Jianglin letting her have the upper hand again, Yao Qingyan put on a pout. “You let me win every time we play. How boring.”
[ZH] “你我静观其变，走一步看一步。”
[EN] “Not at all,” he said. “I find watching my wife play delightful—most of all when you win and your whole face lights up.”
[ZH] 两人又下会儿棋，直到姚轻琰取胜，他们才去花园中散步。
[EN] Yao Qingyan’s thoughts turned back to their son. “Earlier, we thought about dismissing Haoyu’s maids ourselves, only to drop the idea for fear the Old Madam would step in to spoil her grandson. If the Old Madam finds out Shuzhen cleared out every last maid in his courtyard, do you think she’ll make a fuss?”
[ZH] 陈府的花园栽种着许多名贵的花草，春意盎然之际，花草都焕发出生气。二人走累时，到凉亭下休息。
[EN] “Wait and see,” Chen Jianglin said. “We’ll take it one step at a time.”
[ZH] 坐下没多久，陈家生意上管账的方管事匆匆前来，向陈江林报告：“老爷，一大早少夫人身边的韦嬷嬷前来，要求将二公子名下的财产全部划归到少夫人名下，此事重大，我不敢私自做主，只能告诉韦嬷嬷，需向老爷请示后再做定夺。”
[EN] They played on until Yao Qingyan took the match, then strolled out into the garden.
[ZH] 转让陈家的财产不比遣散个把丫鬟，陈江林与姚轻琰一时拿不定主意，他们需了解完情况仔细思量后再作决定。只告诉方管事，先将此事搁下。
[EN] Chen Manor’s garden boasted rows of prized flowers and shrubs, bursting with fresh spring vigor. Once their feet grew tired, the two rested inside a pavilion.
[ZH] 二人已无心在院子里闲逛，姚轻琰吩咐贴身丫鬟映雪将韦嬷嬷唤到她的屋中问话。
[EN] They had barely sat down when Steward Fang, who kept the accounts for the Chen businesses, hurried up to report. “Master, first thing this morning, Young Madam’s Matron Wei came over demanding that every property under Second Young Master’s name be transferred into Young Madam’s name. This is too big for me to decide on my own, so I told Matron Wei I had to ask Master before making any move.”
[ZH] 夫妻二人回到屋中，刚坐下，映雪进来回道：“老爷、夫人，韦嬷嬷已到，正在外面候着。”
[EN] Transferring Chen family assets was a far cry from dismissing a handful of maids. Unable to decide on the spot, Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan knew they needed the full story before weighing their answer. They told Steward Fang to hold off for now.
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“快请韦嬷嬷进来。”
[EN] Their mood for strolling gone, Yao Qingyan sent her personal maid Yingxue to summon Matron Wei to her quarters for a talk.
[ZH] 映雪又出去叫人。
[EN] The couple had no sooner returned to their room and taken their seats than Yingxue stepped in to report. “Master, Madam, Matron Wei has arrived and is waiting outside.”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷走进后，姚轻琰站起来迎上去，拉住韦嬷嬷的手：“嬷嬷既已住到陈家，以后可得常常来我屋里坐坐。”
[EN] “Invite Matron Wei in at once,” Yao Qingyan said.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷被拉住手，依然不忘行礼：“给老爷和夫人行礼。”
[EN] Yingxue stepped back out to bring her in. The moment Matron Wei crossed the threshold, Yao Qingyan rose to greet her and took her by the hand. “Now that you’re living in Chen Manor, Matron, you must drop by my quarters often.”
[ZH] 陈江林说：“韦嬷嬷不必多礼，快上坐。”
[EN] Even with her hand clasped, Matron Wei did not skip proper etiquette. “My greetings to Master and Madam.”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷客气道：“我是下人，哪有上坐的道理？”
[EN] “No need for ceremony, Matron Wei,” Chen Jianglin said. “Please take the upper seat.”
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“快别这么说，嬷嬷在朱家的地位我们也有所耳闻，临安城的贵女哪个不羡慕朱家姑娘身边有嬷嬷这号能人？”
[EN] Matron Wei declined with polite poise. “I am a servant—how could I ever take a seat of honor?”
[ZH] 姚轻琰牵着韦嬷嬷让她上坐，韦嬷嬷执意不肯，挑下位坐了，姚轻琰便不再强求。
[EN] “Don’t talk like that,” Yao Qingyan insisted. “We’ve heard all about your standing in the Zhu household. What noble young lady in Lin’an doesn’t envy Miss Zhu for having someone as capable as you by her side?”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷坐下后谦虚道：“承蒙大家瞧得起，才给我这虚名罢了。我只求待在姑娘身边帮她打理琐事，好让她腾出更多时间写诗作词，不至于辜负老天赏给的才华。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan tried to draw her toward the upper seat, yet Matron Wei stood firm and chose a lower chair instead. Yao Qingyan stopped pressing.
[ZH] 陈江林说：“江南人只知幽栖居士是女词人，却不知女词人的名头得益于儿媳的才华外，还有韦嬷嬷一半功劳。”
[EN] Once seated, Matron Wei spoke with modesty. “Everyone flatters me with an empty reputation. All I ask is to stay by my young lady’s side and handle the mundane chores so she has time to compose poems and ci lyrics without wasting the talent Heaven gave her.”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷“呵呵”笑道：“老爷说这话我爱听，我是个俗人，不会写诗作词，可却喜欢姑娘写，每次大家称赞姑娘诗词写得好，我都开心，就像是我自己写的一般。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin smiled. “All of Jiangnan knows Youqi Jushi as a renowned poetess, yet few realize half that fame belongs to our daughter-in-law’s gift and the other half to Matron Wei.”
[ZH] 三人笑了一会儿，气氛相当和谐。
[EN] Matron Wei chuckled. “Now that is something I love to hear, Master. I’m a plain woman who can’t write a line of verse, yet I love watching my miss write. Every time people praise her poetry, I feel as proud as if I had written it myself.”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷猜到主人家请她来是为姑爷名下财产之事，主人家不便先开口，还是由她主动切入正题。
[EN] The three shared a warm laugh, the air in the room light and cordial.
[ZH] “老爷和夫人请我来应该是有重要的事与我商议，如果没猜错是为姑爷名下的财产转给我家姑娘之事？”
[EN] Matron Wei guessed her hosts had called her in over Young Master’s properties. Since they felt awkward broaching it first, she cut straight to the point. “Master and Madam must have something important to discuss with me. If I’m not mistaken, it concerns transferring Young Master’s assets to my young lady?”
[ZH] 陈江林夸道：“韦嬷嬷果真是明白人，是为此事，本也不是什么大事。事出突然，我与轻琰不了解具体情况，想从嬷嬷这里了解前因后果。”
[EN] “A sharp woman indeed,” Chen Jianglin praised. “Yes, that’s it—not that it’s any great crisis. It just came out of the blue, and Qingyan and I don’t know the full background, so we hoped to hear the ins and outs from you.”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷从袖中取出一张叠好的纸，打开后，丫鬟映雪接过，送至夫人手中。
[EN] Matron Wei pulled a folded sheet of paper from her sleeve and opened it. Yingxue took it and passed it to Madam.
[ZH] 姚轻琰接过先看一遍，叹口气，递给陈江林。
[EN] Yao Qingyan read it through, let out a sigh, and handed it to Chen Jianglin.
[ZH] 陈江林接过，也看一遍，双眉紧锁，叹口气：“唉，原来朱家姑娘是因为纸上写的这些才乐意嫁到陈家。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin read every word, his brows knitting tight, and sighed too. “So Miss Zhu only agreed to marry into the Chen family because of what’s written on this sheet.”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“老爷和夫人不必伤感，婚姻自有上天安排，不管什么原因，姑娘已嫁到陈家，就是陈家人。”
[EN] “Don’t feel disheartened, Master and Madam,” Matron Wei said. “Marriages are arranged by Heaven. Whatever the reason, my miss has married into the Chen household, and that makes her one of the Chens.”
[ZH] 姚轻琰点头问道：“昨晚二人可入洞房？”
[EN] Yao Qingyan nodded and asked, “Did the two of them consummate the marriage last night?”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“姑娘与姑爷本不同意这门亲事，可老天照样把他们安排在一起，这不，连婚都结了。老爷和夫人不必心急，要多给二人些时日，以后会发生什么事我们不得而知，但肯定都是好事。”
[EN] “Both my miss and Young Master opposed this match at first,” Matron Wei answered, “yet Heaven brought them together anyway, and here they are, wed. Don’t rush them, Master and Madam. Give the two of them time. We can’t foresee everything that lies ahead, yet I’m certain it will all turn out for the good.”
[ZH] 听这话，显然二人还未入洞房。
[EN] Her answer made it clear they had not consummated the marriage.
[ZH] 不过韦嬷嬷的话倒令陈江林眉头舒展许多：“淑真既嫁过来，入洞房是迟早的事，也急不得。现下这契约，既是浩宇与儿媳婚前立的，作为长辈不好帮他违约，再说浩宇名下的财产不管归谁都是我陈家的财产，肥水不流外人田。”
[EN] Even so, Matron Wei’s words smoothed the crease between Chen Jianglin’s brows. “Now that Shuzhen has married in, consummation is only a matter of time—no sense rushing it. As for this contract, Haoyu drew it up with our daughter-in-law before the wedding; as elders, we can’t help him break his own word. Besides, whether Haoyu’s assets sit under his name or hers, they remain Chen family property. The fertile water isn’t flowing into an outsider’s field.”
[ZH] 陈江林又看一眼契约，脸上露出笑容：“契约上有一条我甚是喜欢，儿媳只答应每个月给浩宇二十两零花钱。只有二十两浩宇都愿意签下自己的名字，按下手印，真是难得。”
[EN] Glancing down at the contract once more, Chen Jianglin broke into a grin. “There’s one clause here I like very much: our daughter-in-law only agreed to give Haoyu twenty taels of silver a month for spending money. That Haoyu was willing to sign his name and press his thumbprint for a mere twenty taels—now that is rare.”
[ZH] 陈江林把纸张递给映雪，映雪又还到韦嬷嬷手中，韦嬷嬷接过继续收好。
[EN] He handed the paper back to Yingxue, who returned it to Matron Wei to tuck away.
[ZH] “老爷有句话说得好，肥水不流外人田，姑娘嫁过来就是陈家人，姑爷的财产划到姑娘名下也是陈家的产业。再说姑娘不是贪财之人，她之所以嫁到陈家不过是不想再与命运抗争，借着财产的名头让自己嫁得心安理得些。老爷刚才慧眼如炬，一下子看到重点，我家姑娘就是姑爷的克星，不管因为什么原因嫁到陈家只会对陈家有利。”
[EN] “Well said, Master—no fertile water flows to outsiders,” Matron Wei replied. “Once my miss married in, she became a Chen, so transferring Young Master’s assets to her keeps them right inside the family. Besides, my miss isn’t greedy for wealth. She only used those properties as a pretext to stop fighting fate and step into this marriage with an easier mind. Master hit the bull’s-eye just now: my miss is Young Master’s nemesis in the best way. Whatever brought her into this house, she will only bring the Chens good fortune.”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷又一番话说到二位主人的心里。
[EN] Her words struck straight to the couple’s hearts.
[ZH] “嬷嬷真是厉害，看出我与江林同意这门亲事的原因。”姚轻琰也终于露出笑容，向陈江林说，“既如此没什么好犹豫的。”
[EN] “How sharp you are, Matron—you saw right through the reason Jianglin and I agreed to this match,” Yao Qingyan said, smiling at last. She turned to her husband. “In that case, there’s nothing left to hesitate over.”
[ZH] 陈江林点点头：“嬷嬷尽管去兴隆阁找方管事办理此事，我也会派人给他打声招呼。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin nodded. “Go straight to Xinglong Pavilion and have Steward Fang handle the transfer, Matron. I’ll send someone over to pass the word along.”
[ZH] 兴隆阁正是举行婚礼之处，婚礼举行完后一切恢复正常。
[EN] Xinglong Pavilion was the very hall where the wedding had taken place, now back to business as usual.
[ZH] 兴隆阁之所以建在陈府内，不过是为陈家人管理生意，商议生意中的大事方便而设，负责的管事和忙业务的伙计加起来二百多人。二百多人的吃穿住行都设在陈府西侧，陈家人则大多住在北边和东边。
[EN] Built right inside Chen Manor to let the family manage its ventures and confer over major deals, the pavilion housed a combined staff of more than two hundred stewards and clerks. All two hundred lived, ate, and worked along the west wing of the manor, while the Chen family mostly lived on the north and east sides.
[ZH] 陈家的正门开在南边，主要供陈家人进出；西面也开有一道门，供管事与伙计们进出。因此虽都在陈府，往日进进出出基本不照面。
[EN] The manor’s south-facing main gate served the Chen family, whereas a separate west gate served the stewards and clerks. Even living inside the same compound, the two groups crossed paths very seldom.
[ZH] 兴隆阁的一层很大，昨日的婚礼就在一层举行。尽管一大早韦嬷嬷来过，再次进入，还是不由自主四处张望。
[EN] The ground floor of Xinglong Pavilion was cavernous—the wedding banquet had filled it the day before. Though Matron Wei had already visited that morning, stepping back inside still made her look all around.
[ZH] 里面与昨日婚宴的场面完全不同，昨日进来眼前红彤彤一片，今日只觉出忙碌和庄严。一层屋顶的灯笼依旧，白天也点着，因兴隆阁太大，不是所有地方光线都能照到。
[EN] Gone was the sea of festive red from the wedding; in its place hung an atmosphere of brisk, solemn industry. The overhead lanterns stayed lit even in broad daylight because the hall was too vast for sunlight to reach every corner.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷穿过供人行走之处，两边已被忙碌的桌椅占据，而且划归成不同的区域。有的区域伙计伏案在纸上写着什么，有的区域伙计则进进出出穿梭着，有的区域伙计则在整理装订成册的纸张……
[EN] Matron Wei walked down the central aisle flanked by busy desks divided into neat sections. In one area clerks bent over sheets of paper writing; in another clerks hurried back and forth delivering ledgers; in a third they sorted and bound stacks of documents into volumes.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷径直朝最里面的区域而去，越走近，清脆的算盘声越清晰。原来这个区域几个伙计面前都摆着算盘，一边瞧账本，一边熟练地用手指拨动珠子。方管事坐在主桌位，虽未拨弄珠算，却手拿账本，认真翻阅。
[EN] She headed straight for the innermost section, where the crisp clack of abacuses grew louder with every step. Several clerks sat behind abacuses there, checking account books while nimble fingers flicked the wooden beads. Steward Fang sat at the main desk—no abacus in front of him, just a ledger he was studying with focus.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷上前问候：“方管事，我又来了。”
[EN] Matron Wei stepped up with a greeting. “Steward Fang, I’m back again.”
[ZH] 方管事放下账本，笑呵呵地站起来：“刚才老爷已传来话，我这就给您办理。”
[EN] Steward Fang set down the ledger and rose with a jovial smile. “Master just sent word over. I’ll take care of it for you right away.”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷故作惊讶：“我从老爷和夫人的院子出来直奔兴隆阁，一步都没停，并未看到传话的下人，难道方管事与老爷能隔空传话不成？”
[EN] Matron Wei put on a look of mock astonishment. “I came straight here from Master and Madam’s courtyard without stopping once and never saw a messenger. Don’t tell me you and Master can send messages through thin air?”
[ZH] “哪有您说得那么神奇？肯定是您没在意身边经过的人，老爷身边的下人腿脚都快，您前脚进来，他刚走。”方管事亲自端把椅子，“您坐下说。”
[EN] “Nothing magical about it,” he laughed. “You must have missed him as he passed. Master’s runners are fleet-footed—he walked out the moment before you stepped in.” Steward Fang pulled up a chair himself. “Please, sit.”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷也不客气，在椅子上坐下：“既然此事老爷和夫人已同意，方管事赶紧给办了，我还要赶着去忙其他事。”
[EN] Matron Wei took the seat without standing on ceremony. “Now that Master and Madam have approved it, get it done quick, Steward Fang. I’ve got plenty of other things waiting on me.”
[ZH] 方管事坐下，拿出一个册子，上写“财产簿”，并未着急打开，先唠起嗑来：“少夫人能嫁给二公子真是福气，您有所不知，我朝规定每家多处财产需割入一户，所以陈家的财产都在大老爷名下。因老太太疼爱二公子，硬生生通过知府大人的关系，才将几处财产划到二公子名下，没想到二公子疼爱少夫人，竟然愿意全部转让，真是难得。”
[EN] Steward Fang sat down and brought out a volume labeled “Property Register,” though instead of opening it right away, he struck up a chat. “Young Madam is truly blessed to marry Second Young Master. You may not know this, Matron, but Song law requires a family’s scattered properties to be registered under a single household head, so all Chen assets stand under Eldest Master’s name. Only because the Old Madam dotes on Second Young Master did she pull strings with the Prefect to split several properties off into his name. Who would’ve thought Second Young Master dotes on Young Madam so much he’d hand every last one over? Remarkable.”
[ZH] “割入一户”的说法韦嬷嬷倒是知道，只是没想那么多，被方管事这么一说，她更觉出陈家人真是不错，难怪生意能做得这么大：“请问方管事，姑爷名下的财产转到我家姑娘名下可麻烦？”
[EN] Matron Wei knew about the single-household registration rule, though she had not thought that far into it. Hearing Steward Fang explain it made her think even higher of the Chens—no wonder their business had grown so massive. “Tell me, Steward Fang, is it much trouble to transfer Young Master’s properties into my miss’s name?”
[ZH] 方管事打开财产簿：“倒不麻烦，我先需在这簿子上登记好，盖上陈家商行的大印与大老爷、二公子的私印，再拿到官府登记在册即可。”
[EN] Steward Fang opened the register. “No trouble at all. First I log it here, stamp it with the main seal of the Chen firm plus the personal seals of Eldest Master and Second Young Master, and then take it to the local yamen to register.”
[ZH] “有劳方管事。”
[EN] “Much obliged, Steward Fang.”
[ZH] 方管事提笔在簿子上写了一会儿，将笔搁下：“我已记录完毕，等会儿我去二楼找管印章的人将三枚印章盖了，第一步就算完成。”
[EN] He dipped his brush, wrote across the ledger for a while, and set the brush down. “All logged. In a moment I’ll head up to the second floor to the seal-keeper and get the three seals stamped—that finishes the first step.”
[ZH] 方管事这么一说，韦嬷嬷才知道兴隆阁不止一层，她不由向别处瞧瞧，的确看到一处木质台阶，到底有多少级，韦嬷嬷一时数不过来。
[EN] Only then did Matron Wei realize Xinglong Pavilion had more than one story. Glancing around, she spotted a wooden staircase whose steps were too numerous to count at a glance.
[ZH] “还有一事需向嬷嬷问清楚。”
[EN] “One more thing I need to clarify with you, Matron.”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷回过头：“方管事尽管问。”
[EN] She turned back. “Ask away, Steward Fang.”
[ZH] “二公子名下的财产以前都是老爷派人打理，现在归少夫人名下，不知是换人管理？还是用以前的人？您别介意，需问清楚，好回禀老爷。”
[EN] “All the properties under Second Young Master’s name have been run by people Eldest Master appointed. Now that they belong to Young Madam, will you put your own people in charge, or keep the current managers? Don’t take offense—I have to ask so I can report back to Master.”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷心想，能把陈家的财产划归到姑娘名下已是幸运，又何必在乎谁来打理？继续让老爷的人管着，他才会认为财产依然是陈家的，以后也少了对姑娘的芥蒂。再说韦嬷嬷自己照看姑娘原有的财产已够辛苦，再多些她的确忙不过来。
[EN] Getting Chen family assets signed over to her miss was already a windfall, Matron Wei reflected; why quibble over who managed them? Leaving Master’s people in place would reassure him the wealth stayed inside the family and spare her miss any future friction. Besides, Matron Wei already had her hands full managing her miss’s own dowry and estates.
[ZH] “还是由以前的人管理，我有时间替姑娘过去看看，没时间就不过去。”
[EN] “Keep the same managers,” she said. “I’ll drop by on my miss’s behalf when I have time, and skip it when I don’t.”
[ZH] 方管事笑道：“如此甚好。”
[EN] Steward Fang beamed. “That works out best of all.”

#CH 203
[ZH] 财产过继之事已办完，韦嬷嬷离开兴隆阁，回到新婚的院子。
[ZH] 姑娘正站在院中，看上去不大高兴。
[ZH] “姑娘，财产之事已办理完，过程还算顺利，只等方管事去府衙办理后续事宜。”
[ZH] 刚到陈家，千里寻很不适应，加之夏老太太给她下马威，再想到昨日之事，心情自然沉重。嬷嬷说起财产之事办理得顺利，她的心情稍微好些，说明陈浩宇的爹娘对她还不错。
[ZH] “辛苦嬷嬷。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷关切地问：“姑娘今日给长辈敬茶可否顺利？”
[ZH] 苦荞帮着回道：“嬷嬷不知，小姐的公爹和婆婆人倒不错，可陈家的老太太不仅让小姐跪下，还把茶盏扔到地上，实在过分！”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷听后，并不生气，反而笑道：“我当什么事？原来姑娘是在夏老太太处遇到阻碍，无妨，都是小事，对姑娘来讲，哄个老太太不在话下。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷轻松的话语点醒千里寻，她面上的愁容立刻散去：“还是嬷嬷厉害，想到一个‘哄’字，一下子点醒我。老人跟小孩子一样，需要哄，谁会哄她便跟着谁跑，我终于明白，老太太为何喜欢陈浩宇，不过是因为他善于用肉麻的话哄老太太。”
[ZH] 见姑娘释然许多，韦嬷嬷自然开心：“姑娘虽不是真心嫁给姑爷，可却愿意在陈家长辈身上花心思，说明姑娘已接受现实，打算在陈家好好过日子。人无论在哪里，只要心在，没有过不去的坎儿。”
[ZH] 千里寻感激韦嬷嬷说的这番话，又不愿说些见外的感激之语，正好想起件事：“嬷嬷，这院子虽不如望月轩大，可比我在朱府住的园子大多了，我刚转了一圈儿，发现花花草草不少，可没有我喜欢的。我想除屋子要重新布置外，院子里也需栽种些我喜欢的花草。另外我给院子和屋子都取好名字，已写在纸上，一会儿让苦荞拿给您。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷听后并不觉麻烦，也不觉姑娘多事：“我正要带人把望月轩的家具搬来，帮姑娘重新布置屋子，等屋子布置完，再找陈府的花匠，按照姑娘的喜好把院子重新修整一番。”
[ZH] 千里寻搀住韦嬷嬷：“嬷嬷真好，从不拒绝我的任何要求。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷轻轻拍着姑娘的手：“姑娘的要求很合理，我没有拒绝的理由，再说姑娘已打算在陈家过日子，居住的地儿不合心意，住着也不踏实。”
[ZH] 苦荞从屋内拿出一沓纸，递给韦嬷嬷：“小姐给每个屋子都取了名字，连下人住的地儿都不放过。”
[ZH] 千里寻不得不放开韦嬷嬷。
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷伸手接过，大概浏览一遍：“我去望月轩的路上找家做匾额的铺子，不出一日必然做好。”
[ZH] 姑娘布置的任务多，不好再耽搁下去，韦嬷嬷叫上红豆等十人，一起去望月轩搬用得上的家具。
[ZH] 话说乔文仙离开幽林别居，并未回乔府，他只有一个心思，见淑真一面，要当面问个清楚。
[ZH] 南双追上公子后，与他乘坐一辆马车，来到陈府外。公子并未下马车，在外逗留了一会儿，才吩咐去东方家。
[ZH] 东方尘泽正在房中坐于琴旁，欣赏幽栖居士的一首词，研究如何谱曲，准备下次诗会时让想绒姑娘唱出来。
[ZH] 他边抚琴边唱，十分投入：“斜风细雨作春寒，对尊前，忆前欢。曾把梨花，寂寞泪阑干。……”
[ZH] 乔文仙走进来，听出正是淑真那首《江城子·赏春》。在汴梁看到这首词时，他对与淑真的未来充满希望，现在已完全没有希望可言。
[ZH] 东方尘泽正唱得入迷，化雨提醒道：“公子，乔公子来了。”
[ZH] 东方尘泽才停下，抬头见乔兄双眼微红像是刚刚哭过。
[ZH] “乔兄，快请坐，你很少登门拜访，今日我亲自做茶款待你。”
[ZH] 东方尘泽起身准备到茶桌前做茶，被乔文仙一把拦住：“我来不是为品茶，也没心情品，你不必忙活，坐下来听我说正事。”
[ZH] 二人坐下后，东方尘泽说：“乔兄请说。”
[ZH] “明日你举办一场诗会。”
[ZH] 举办诗会是东方尘泽所爱，可明日举行，也忒急了些：“我近期是打算举办诗会，明日太匆忙，能不能推后几日？”
[ZH] “不行，就明日，我等不了那么久。”
[ZH] 从乔兄的表情判断，应该有隐情，东方尘泽小心问：“乔兄能否告诉我为何要这么着急举办诗会？”
[ZH] “我想见淑真。”
[ZH] 乔文仙毫不隐晦的表达倒将东方尘泽给难住，劝道：“乔兄与幽栖居士都已有各自的生活，还是不要互相打扰为好。”
[ZH] “你别误会，我想最后一次单独见她，问她些事情，问完我也就死心。”
[ZH] 东方尘泽继续劝道：“只怕乔兄问来问去，这心是越问越活。”
[EN] Property transfer wrapped up, Matron Wei returned to the newlyweds' courtyard to find her mistress standing outside looking glum.
[ZH] “你放心，绝对是最后一次，从此后我不再去打扰她。”
[EN] "Transfer is done, Miss—smooth sailing, just waiting on Steward Fang to register it at the yamen."
[ZH] 东方尘泽从乔文仙眼中看出些坚定来。心想，不如让他们见一面，也好彻底放下，才能各自过好日子：“我是第一次帮你，也是最后一次，下不为例。”
[EN] Weighed down by unfamiliar Chen walls, Old Madam Xia's morning ambush, and yesterday's heartbreak, Qianli Xun brightened a shade at the smooth news—at least Chen Haoyu's parents treated her well. "Thank you, Matron."
[ZH] 乔文仙终于露出笑容：“多谢东方兄。”
[EN] "Did morning tea go smoothly?" Matron Wei asked gently.
[ZH] 乔文仙心情不好，两人没聊多久，他便离去。东方尘泽已无心再谱曲，为这种事举办诗会，需费一番心思，他一个人拿不定主意，打算找安明轩和曹班商议。
[EN] Kuqiao blurted straight out, "Father-in-Law and Mother-in-Law were lovely, Matron, yet Chen Old Madam ordered Miss to kneel and smashed her tea bowl on the floor! Outrageous!"
[ZH] 刚带化雨走出东方府的大门，一辆马车停在面前，赶马车的人正是白风，不用问便知里面坐着仪王。
[EN] Far from bristling, Matron Wei let out a warm chuckle. "Thought it was real trouble! Just Old Madam Xia? Trivial—coaxing an elderly lady is child's play for Miss!"
[ZH] 查不到金人，暂时将此事搁置，申简辰想起感情之事，他要在回汴梁前见见千里寻。以什么为借口合情合理与她见上一面？他想到举办一场诗会。
[EN] That single word—"coaxing"—clicked a light straight on in Qianli Xun's head, chasing the clouds off her brow. "Trust Matron to nail it in one word! Elders are just big kids craving sweet talk—whoever coaxes them wins them over! Now I get why Old Madam Xia adores Chen Haoyu: the guy pours out syrupy butter nonstop!"
[ZH] 显然由他出面举办已不合适，还容易引起梦容怀疑，便想到东方尘泽。于是离开迷魂巷，直奔东方府而来。
[EN] Pleased to see her cheer return, Matron Wei nodded warmly. "Even if Miss did not marry for love, putting thought into winning over Chen elders shows you accept your new home and mean to live well here. Wherever your heart settles, no wall holds."
[ZH] 东方尘泽上前拜见：“仪王殿下可是为公事而来？我爹正在府中。”
[EN] Touched yet skipping sappy thanks, Qianli Xun pivoted to decorating. "This courtyard is smaller than Wangyue Pavilion yet bigger than my old Zhu Manor garden. Walking around, I saw plenty of flowers yet none I like! Besides redoing the hall, I want my favorite blooms planted—and I named the whole courtyard and every single room! Written on paper for Kuqiao to hand you."
[ZH] 车内传出仪王的声音：“我不找东方大人，找东方公子，请上马车一叙。”
[EN] "Just heading to Wangyue Pavilion to haul your furniture over," Matron Wei agreed effortlessly. "Once the hall is set, I will call Chen Manor's gardeners to redo the yard just how you like."
[ZH] 东方尘泽也不诧异，仪王与他相熟，又不大理会临安政务，找他再正常不过。
[EN] Hugging her arm, Qianli Xun smiled. "Matron never turns down a single wish of mine!"
[ZH] 他走上马车，在仪王对面安心坐下：“仪王找我应该有要事，请说，我能做的，必然尽心竭力。”
[EN] "Reasonable wishes—and living in a house that feels wrong never brings peace." Accepting a thick stack of room names from Kuqiao ("Even the servants' quarters got fancy names!" Kuqiao grinned), Matron Wei skimmed them. "I will swing by a plaque carver on the way to Wangyue Pavilion—hung by tomorrow!" Rounding up Hongdou and the nine trained hands, she set off to haul furniture.
[ZH] 申简辰露出满意的笑容：“也不是什么难事，我需要东方公子明日举办一场诗会，不需要人多，但必须邀请幽栖居士参加。”
[EN] Meanwhile, bolting from Youlin Retreat earlier that morning, Qiao Wenxian had refused to go back to Qiao Manor—driven by one burning need: see Shuzhen face-to-face and ask straight out! Once Nan Shuang caught up to his carriage, they stopped briefly outside Chen Manor's closed gate before turning straight for Dongfang Manor.
[ZH] 仪王抢亲之事，东方尘泽已听说，震惊的同时倒也能理解，毕竟幽栖居士品貌一绝，连他东方尘泽曾经也动过心思，只因没有任何希望，才果断放弃。可仪王与乔兄有同样的要求，倒是把东方尘泽难住。
[EN] Inside his study, Dongfang Chenze sat at his zither setting one of Youqi Jushi's ci lyrics to music for Xiangrong to sing at his next gathering, strumming and singing aloud: *"Slanting winds and fine rain bring a spring chill. / Before the wine cup, / I recall old joys. / Once beside the pear blossoms, / Lonely tears streamed down..."*
[ZH] 申简辰见东方尘泽为难，取出一袋银子：“举办诗会需花费银两，都由我承担。我听说东方公子想让自己的诗会在临安有些名头，明日若东方公子帮我，待我从汴梁回来，定然会利用仪王的身份帮助东方公子，让你的诗会成为临安第一。”
[EN] Stepping inside, Qiao Wenxian recognized Shuzhen's *"Jiangchengzi: Admiring the Spring"*—the exact lyric that had filled him with bright dreams back in Bianliang, now ashes in his mouth.
[ZH] 又是银子又是满足愿望的承诺，对方还是王爷，东方尘泽必须答应。
[EN] Alerted by Huayu, Dongfang Chenze lifted his hands from the strings and saw his friend's red-rimmed eyes. "Brother Qiao! Rare honor—sit while I whisk tea myself!"
[ZH] 他接过银子：“让仪王破费，举办诗会还真少不了银子。我爹对我办诗会一事不甚支持，故而我常常囊中羞涩，您给的银子我就不客气，大胆拿了。只是有一事，需向仪王说明……”
[EN] Catching his arm, Qiao Wenxian shook his head. "No mood for tea. Sit and listen to business."
[ZH] “请讲。”对方顺利答应，申简辰更加满意。
[EN] Seated together, Dongfang Chenze braced himself. "Speak."
[ZH] 东方尘泽揣好银子，犹豫片刻后说道：“仪王来之前，乔兄也找过我，跟您提出同样的要求，不过他说是最后一次单独约见幽栖居士，我才答应帮他举办一场诗会。要不仪王的诗会再推后一日？”
[EN] "Host a poetry gathering tomorrow."
[ZH] 申简辰能猜到文渊的心思，他定是想当面问问幽栖居士是否爱上别人，然后彻底在心里与对方做个了断，只有这样他才能安心过自己的日子。
[EN] Much as Dongfang Chenze loved salons, tomorrow?! "Planned one soon, yet tomorrow is wildly rushed—push it back a few days?"
[ZH] “没想到我与文渊想到一起，给东方公子添麻烦。不必推后，相信东方公子能处理好。安排好地方后，天黑前让人告诉白风即可。”
[EN] "No. Tomorrow. I cannot wait another day."
[ZH] 东方尘泽只好答应，下了马车，目送仪王离开后，匆匆去找两位公子商议。他先来到曹府，下人说曹公子跟安公子去了桥头瓦。
[EN] Reading the raw storm on his face, Dongfang Chenze asked softly, "Why the urgent rush?"
[ZH] 东方尘泽又乘坐马车赶到桥头瓦，以他对二位公子的了解，肯定是去听千里寻才子的话本。于是一进桥头瓦，直奔说书的勾栏。
[EN] "I need to see Shuzhen."
[ZH] 真是凑巧，刚到门口，瞧见两位公子走出来。
[EN] Blunt honesty put Dongfang Chenze in a tight bind. "You and Youqi Jushi both have new lives now, Brother Qiao—better not stir old embers."
[ZH] 东方尘泽迎上去：“你们两个出来玩儿也不叫上我，真不够朋友。”
[EN] "Do not misunderstand—I only want ONE last private talk to ask a few questions, then I let go for good."
[ZH] 曹班说：“本来想去找你，想着你也快成亲，事情比较多，就没敢去打扰。”
[EN] "Ask questions, and I fear your heart only burns hotter."
[ZH] 安明轩问：“东方兄是凑巧与我们碰上，还是专程来找我们？”
[EN] "My word on it—the very last time. After tomorrow, I never disturb her again."
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“当然是专程而来，有件事需与二位商议，让你们给我出出主意。我们去船上，边喝茶边聊。”
[EN] Seeing genuine finality beneath the ache—maybe closure really would set both free—Dongfang Chenze relented. "First time helping you like this, and the LAST—never again!"
[ZH] 三人来到水边，租了一艘喝茶的小船，边欣赏江水，边品茶。
[EN] A tired smile touched Qiao Wenxian's face. "Thank you, Brother Dongfang." Taking his leave shortly after, he left Dongfang Chenze dropping his zither to seek counsel from An Mingxuan and Cao Ban on pulling off a discreet salon overnight.
[ZH] 曹班叹道：“要是把想绒姑娘请来，弹奏一曲，再辗转歌喉，岂不美哉？”
[EN] Stepping out his front gate with Huayu, he froze at a carriage pulling up driven by Bai Feng—Prince Yi! Having struck out in Mihun Alley, Shen Jianchen had come straight here seeking the exact same pretext to see Qianli Xun one last time before leaving for Bianliang!
[ZH] 东方尘泽哪有兴致欣赏美景？难题还未解决。
[EN] Bowing outside the cab, Dongfang Chenze asked, "Calling on my father for official business, Your Highness?"
[ZH] “今日乔兄和仪王先后来找我，请我明日务必举办一场诗会……”
[EN] "Not Master Dongfang—YOU, Young Master Dongfang. Step inside for a word."
[ZH] 安明轩为三人斟茶：“是好事，有什么好为难的？还要与我们两个商议？再说你也好久没举办诗会，再不举办恐怕连我们两个都忘了你的爱好，东方兄还怎么实现举办一场大诗会的理想？”
[EN] Climbing in without surprise, Dongfang Chenze sat opposite the prince. "At Your Highness's service."
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“举办诗会我当然乐意，只是乔兄和仪王如此要求，不过是想借助诗会之名与幽栖居士会面而已……”
[EN] "Simple task," Shen Jianchen smiled smoothly. "Host a poetry gathering tomorrow—keep the guest list small, yet make certain Youqi Jushi attends."
[ZH] 曹班惊讶地问：“是你猜测的？还是他们告诉你的？”
[EN] Having heard all about yesterday's raid—and secretly understanding, having briefly admired Youqi Jushi himself before backing off—Dongfang Chenze blinked at getting the EXACT SAME demand twice in one hour!
[ZH] “他们毫不隐晦，直接告诉我。你们说说这诗会我如何举办？”东方尘泽虽拿了银子也同意了，还需在朋友面前装作为难的样子，不过是想让他们帮忙出出主意。
[EN] Reading his hesitation, Shen Jianchen tossed a heavy silver pouch onto his lap. "Expenses are on me. Word is you want your poetry salon famous across Lin'an—help me tomorrow, and once I return from Bianliang, I will put Prince Yi's name behind you and make your gathering number one in the city!"
[ZH] 安明轩说：“你敢拒绝乔兄也不能拒绝仪王，如此看来，明日诗会必然要举办。不过，如何举办学问可就大了。首先是选地方，要选偏僻一些，大家不常去的地儿；其次是诗会中邀请哪些人，苗姑娘和郡主是万万不能邀请，不仅不能邀请还不能让她们知道你要举办诗会；这其三，还得找到合适的理由把幽栖居士请出来，她刚嫁到陈家，陈家会不会同意她随便出来参加诗会也很难说。”
[EN] Silver, dream fulfilled, AND a royal patron—impossible to refuse! Pocketing the pouch gratefully, Dongfang Chenze hesitated before confessing honestly: "One tiny snag, Your Highness... right before you arrived, Brother Qiao came asking the exact same thing—swearing it is his final private meeting with Youqi Jushi. Should we push Your Highness's gathering back a day?"
[ZH] 东方尘泽叹道：“可不是！你们得给我出出主意。”
[EN] Guessing Wenyuan wanted final closure straight from her lips so he could move on, Shen Jianchen stayed cool. "Funny how Wenyuan and I think alike. No need to delay—I trust Young Master Dongfang to choreograph it smoothly. Send location details to Bai Feng before dusk."
[ZH] 曹班说：“昨日我听说仪王抢亲之事，甚是惊讶，真没想到仪王也对幽栖居士动了心思，乔兄和仪王都要参加诗会，若知道对方想见幽栖居士，会不会打起来？”
[EN] Left with zero escape, Dongfang Chenze bowed out, checked Cao Manor, tracked Cao Ban and An Mingxuan down outside a storytelling hall at Qiaotou Wa, and dragged both aboard a rented tea boat on the river to brainstorm.
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“所以我没敢隐瞒仪王，他倒好，并不介意，让我看着安排。”
[EN] "Both Brother Qiao AND Prince Yi hit me up today demanding a poetry salon tomorrow—just as cover to see Youqi Jushi!" Dongfang Chenze spilled while An Mingxuan poured tea.
[ZH] 曹班说：“既如此，那就少了会打起来的顾虑，我建议先安排幽栖居士与乔兄见面，再安排仪王。”
[EN] "Wild!" Cao Ban gasped. "You guessed it, or they admitted it?"
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“我也是这么想的，顺序定了，说说该在哪里举办诗会？”
[EN] "Admitted it straight to my face! Tell me how the hell I pull this off!"
[ZH] 安明轩说：“永泰茶楼显然不行，苗姑娘经常去，勾栏瓦肆内也不行，人太多。总之不能在城里举办，不如选陈浩宇的醉云楼，基本没人去，安静异常。”
[EN] An Mingxuan ticked off three hurdles cleanly: "Cannot turn down Prince Yi, so it happens tomorrow. One: pick a secluded spot nobody frequents. Two: bar Lady Miao and Princess Jiahe—and keep them clueless it exists. Three: craft a slick excuse to lure Youqi Jushi out on day two of her marriage without tipping off the Chens."
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“好是好，只怕醉云楼有陈家的下人，发现了什么再告诉陈浩宇，对幽栖居士不利。”
[EN] "Wait—after yesterday's raid, if Brother Qiao and Prince Yi both show up chasing Youqi Jushi, won't they brawl?!" Cao Ban worried.
[ZH] 曹班说：“醉云楼只有两个下人，都各司其职，估计没时间关注太多，只要我们安排得隐秘些，应该不会被轻易发现。”
[EN] "Already warned Prince Yi," Dongfang Chenze said. "He didn't blink—told me to stagger it."
[ZH] 曹班这么一说，另二人也都认可，地点算是定下。三人又商议一会儿，谋划好应对各种难题的策略，一起拍板定下。
[EN] "Then schedule Brother Qiao FIRST, Prince Yi SECOND!" Cao Ban proposed. "As for location—Yongtai Teahouse is out (Lady Miao haunts it), Goulan Wasi is too crowded. Must be outside city walls—what about Chen Haoyu's Zuiyun Tower?! Deserted and dead quiet!"
[ZH] 安明轩慨叹一句：“这哪是诗会！”
[EN] "What if Chen servants there rat out Youqi Jushi to Chen Haoyu?" Dongfang Chenze worried.
[ZH] 曹班来一句总结性发言：“假借诗会之名。”
[EN] "Only two staffers there, busy with their own jobs—keep it discreet and nobody notices!"
[ZH] 三人分工完毕，各自去忙。
[EN] Location locked, roles divided: "Some poetry gathering!" An Mingxuan sighed; "A poetry gathering in name only!" Cao Ban summed up as the three split off to put every cog in place.

#CH 204
[ZH] 东方尘泽负责向仪王和乔文仙通知地点，唯一需要注意的是不能让他们身边的女人知道。
[ZH] 三人认为很好解决，只需说邀请仪王和乔兄参加即可，不允许携带女眷。而且不能说是诗会，要说宴会。若女眷问起因由，与他们三人无关，交由仪王与乔文仙自己解决。
[ZH] 东方尘泽还需请一人，那便是花云楼的想绒姑娘。将她请来，好处多多，既有人唱幽栖居士的诗词，解决诗会而无心品诗的尴尬，亦可为幽栖居士找一位说话之人。
[ZH] 曹班与安明轩一个负责请幽栖居士，一个负责找陈浩宇借用醉云楼。二人一致认为找这两人均需去陈府，他们便往陈府而来。
[ZH] 二位公子来到陈府南门外，大门紧闭，半晌见不着一个人影儿。
[ZH] 曹班说：“陈府还有个西门，听说常年开着，不妨去那边瞧瞧。”
[ZH] 安明轩阻止道：“陈家的西门是给忙生意的掌柜和伙计用的，陈家的主人都走南门，你等着，我上前亲自敲门问问。”
[ZH] 安明轩走下马车，亲自去敲陈家的大门：“咚咚咚！”
[ZH] 门被敲开，一个下人问：“公子找哪位？”
[ZH] 安明轩回道：“陈家二公子。”
[ZH] 下人说：“我家公子不在，早早出去了。”
[ZH] 安明轩问：“他何时回来？”
[ZH] 下人回：“说不准，少则四五天，多则半月。”
[ZH] 安明轩又问：“他去了哪里？”
[ZH] 下人为难道：“我家公子去哪里从不与我们说。”
[ZH] 下人正要关门，被曹班拦住：“幽栖居士可在？我们二人也找她。”
[ZH] 下人停下关门的动作：“陈府没有幽栖居士。”
[ZH] 曹班才意识到幽栖居士的称谓陈府的下人根本不知，赶紧解释：“幽栖居士就是朱家姑娘。”
[ZH] 下人明白过来：“哦，您说的是少夫人，没见她走出大门，人应该在。”
[ZH] 终于逮住一个，曹班说：“麻烦通报一声，就说我二人找她。”
[ZH] 陈家下人答应后，关上门，赶去禀报。
[ZH] 说来也巧，韦嬷嬷正安排下人重新布置屋子，千里寻无处可去，又不想在陈府瞎转悠，怕遇见陈家人，她决定到街上逛逛。
[ZH] 快到大门口时，正好被下人碰到。上次她来闹事，这位下人被红豆打过，自然对少夫人印象深刻，一眼认出，上前拜见：“可巧，碰见少夫人，省得我多跑路。”
[ZH] 千里寻问道：“有事？”
[ZH] 下人回道：“外面有两位公子找您。”
[ZH] 千里寻寻思：“两位公子？会是谁？”
[ZH] 边寻思边走出大门，曹班与安明轩出现在眼前。
[ZH] “原来是二位公子，不知找我何事？”
[ZH] 两位公子因幽栖居士亲自出来相见十分开心，上前行礼：“幽栖居士。”
[ZH] 曹班说：“东方兄打算明日举办诗会，想邀请幽栖居士参加。”
[ZH] 既是诗会，千里寻当然愿意替朱淑真前去，又担心在诗会中见到想见又怕见之人：“不知东方公子邀请哪些人物？”
[ZH] 二人猜出幽栖居士的心思，既然是将她骗去，不该说的一定不能说。
[ZH] 曹班继续说：“东方兄一直想让他的诗会在临安有些名头，故而此次只邀请在临安诗词界有名头之人。乔兄自然会去，苗姑娘虽有些才华，名头差些故而没邀请，像仪王和郡主，身份尊贵，可在诗词界排不上名，也不在邀请之列。”
[ZH] 仪王和郡主没有被邀请，千里寻松口气，至于文渊，她已能轻松面对，倒也无妨。只是此次诗会举办怎么与以往不同？连个请帖都没有：“为何不直接送请帖？却是二位公子亲自前来相邀？”
[ZH] 曹班继续编道：“请帖是有的，东方兄说了，对于像幽栖居士这样在诗词界有名头之人需我二人亲自来请，才显得诚意满满。”
[ZH] “原来如此。”千里寻继续问，“还请告知地点和时辰。”
[ZH] 曹班说：“明日巳时在城外钱塘江边的醉云楼，幽栖居士乘坐马车出了城，一直往西南走，大约半个时辰自然看到。要说这醉云楼也是陈家的产业。”
[ZH] 千里寻并不关心是否是陈家的产业，令她疑惑的是东方尘泽举办诗会为何要选这么远的地方？又一想，也许东方尘泽有他自己的道理。便不再多问，只说：“明日我必然按时到达。”
[ZH] 顺利邀请到幽栖居士，两位公子更觉他们的计划实在完美。
[ZH] 还需寻找陈浩宇，安明轩问道：“幽栖居士可否告知陈公子去了哪里？”
[ZH] 千里寻疑惑道：“难道诗会也需请陈浩宇？”
[ZH] 安明轩说：“非也，只因醉云楼是陈公子名下的产业，需向他借用。”
[ZH] 千里寻感到疑惑，举办这么大的诗会没把地方搞定，就敢先邀请人，还好有她在：“不必找陈浩宇，他名下的财产尽数归我，我应该已是醉云楼的主人，明日大家尽管去。”
[ZH] 省去寻找陈浩宇的麻烦，两位公子更是高兴：“如此甚好！”
[ZH] 一切已搞定，两位公子赶着向东方尘泽交差，拜别幽栖居士后匆忙离开。
[EN] Dongfang Chenze handled notifying Prince Yi and Qiao Wenxian—carefully framing it as a men-only banquet rather than a poetry salon so wives and fiancées stayed home—plus inviting Xiangrong from Flower Cloud Pavilion, both to sing Youqi Jushi's lyrics so the salon looked authentic and to keep Youqi Jushi company between meetings.
[ZH] 千里寻不想乘坐马车，步行前往闹市。她边走边想梦容，不知她现在可好？
[EN] Cao Ban and An Mingxuan took on inviting Youqi Jushi and borrowing Zuiyun Tower from Chen Haoyu, heading together to Chen Manor's closed south gate (skipping the busy west business gate). Knocking, An Mingxuan learned Young Master Chen had stepped out for anywhere from four days to half a month. Before the gatekeeper shut the door, Cao Ban jumped in: "Is Youqi Jushi home? We came for her too!"
[ZH] 想完梦容，又想申简辰，回想穿越前发生地震时他向她奔跑而来；回想他们互不相识时在竹林在神柳下相遇；回想她不知仪王就是申简辰时，仪王时不时向她流露出的情感；回想知道仪王是申简辰后他们一起捶丸的情景；回想申简辰去朱府闹，以及抢亲的过程……
[EN] "No Youqi Jushi here."
[ZH] 一幕一幕，在千里寻的脑海中过了一遍又一遍，她时而脸上露出微笑，时而又变得悲戚。
[EN] "Miss Zhu!" Cao Ban clarified.
[ZH] 她在心中提醒自己：“你不是说过以后不再涉足男女之情吗？为何还要想他？”
[EN] "Oh, Young Madam! Still inside—let me announce you."
[ZH] 是千里寻时，说不涉足感情，还真能做到。穿越成朱淑真，鬼使神差地难以做到，还是忍不住不去想他。
[EN] By sheer coincidence, with Matron Wei's crew turning her hall upside down with moving furniture, Qianli Xun had stepped out for a stroll through town and walked straight up to the gatekeeper—one of the bruised guards from her raid, who bowed eagerly to report two young gentlemen waiting outside.
[ZH] 又想到白风活活将一头小猪累死的场景，千里寻又在心中埋怨：“你怎么那么傻？想到这种招数，你我之间的关系根本无法改变，小猪的死就是很好的证明。”
[EN] Stepping out to greet Cao Ban and An Mingxuan, she heard Cao Ban's smooth pitch inviting her to Dongfang Chenze's poetry gathering tomorrow—and asked cautiously who else was invited.
[ZH] 千里寻觉得她之所以会一直想起申简辰，是因为不久前她对申简辰的感情再次萌生。她相信随着时间推移，会慢慢放下，能顺利放下对文渊的感情便是一个很好的证明。
[EN] "Brother Dongfang aims to make his name in Lin'an's literary world, so he invited ONLY top local poets," Cao Ban fibbed smoothly. "Brother Qiao is coming; Lady Miao falls short on renown so she was skipped; Prince Yi and Her Highness outrank us yet hold no literary fame, so neither was invited."
[ZH] 她不禁自问，为何每次都是她必须放下？为何不能是苗知瑜和梦容？她找不到合适的答案，只觉得命运不公。
[EN] Hearing Prince Yi and Mengrong weren't coming eased Qianli Xun's nerves—she could face Wenyuan calmly now. "Why send you two in person instead of a formal invitation card?"
[ZH] 一抬头看到一家酒肆，千里寻脑中闪过“借酒消愁”四个字，不如进去喝上几杯。
[EN] "We have cards—Brother Dongfang insisted luminaries like Youqi Jushi deserve a personal call!"
[ZH] 她走进酒肆，挑一个靠窗的位置，要壶皇都春，自斟一杯，缓缓喝下。
[EN] "Fair enough. Time and place?"
[ZH] 千里寻很少喝酒，尤其是烈酒，更是点滴不沾，一杯皇都春下肚，只觉从喉咙烧到肚内，脸跟着红起来。
[EN] "Hour of the Snake tomorrow at Zuiyun Tower by the Qiantang River—southwest out the city gate, half an hour by carriage. Happens to be a Chen property too."
[ZH] 苦荞建议：“小姐，要不换壶桂花酿？”
[EN] Wondering briefly why hold a big salon so far out yet shrugging it off, Qianli Xun promised to attend.
[ZH] 千里寻并不同意，继续斟上一杯：“桂花酿不是酒，今日我只想喝酒。”
[EN] "Could Youqi Jushi tell us where Young Master Chen went?" An Mingxuan asked next. "Since Zuiyun Tower sits under his name, we need his nod to borrow it."
[ZH] 苦荞反问道：“谁说桂花酿不是酒？去年夏天小姐还不是因喝桂花酿才醉得不省人事。”
[EN] Amused they invited guests before locking down the venue, Qianli Xun waved it off. "Skip hunting Chen Haoyu—all his assets just transferred to me, so Zuiyun Tower is mine now. Everyone come freely tomorrow."
[ZH] 千里寻想起去年之事，那晚申简辰便知朱淑真是千里寻，不知他那晚看到自己坐在文渊怀里是何感受？
[EN] Thrilled to cross both errands off in one shot, the two scholars hurried off to report to Dongfang Chenze.
[ZH] 为何总是错过？都怪她喜欢对申简辰说：“除非猪能上树，老娘才会嫁给你。”
[EN] Skipping a carriage to walk into the bustling market, Qianli Xun let her thoughts drift first to Mengrong—hoping her wound healed clean—and straight from there to Shen Jianchen: running through the earthquake, sparring in the bamboo grove, hinting under the willow, playing chuiwan side by side, storming Zhu Manor, blocking her bridal sedan... Frame after frame played behind her eyes, pulling smiles and sharp stabs of grief by turns.
[ZH] 千里寻对着酒杯自语：“不知道我收回那些话是否还来得及？”
[EN] *Thought you swore off romance?* she scolded herself. Back as Qianli Xun she had kept her heart locked tight; inside Zhu Shuzhen's skin, her walls melted every time! Remembering Bai Feng hounding that poor piglet to death brought a bitter pang: *Why be that stubborn when fate wrote us off?* Surely time would dull Shen Jianchen just as it had dulled Wenyuan—yet why was SHE always the one forced to step back, never Miao Zhiyu or Mengrong?
[ZH] 又一想，怎么可能来得及？她已嫁人，申简辰又有官家赐婚，一切已不可能。
[EN] Spotting a wine shop ahead, four words flashed in her mind—*drown your sorrows*—and she ducked straight inside to a window table, ordering a pot of Capital Spring. Normally untouched by strong liquor, one cup burned fiery trails down her throat and flushed her cheeks hot.
[ZH] 她难过得举起酒杯又要饮下，却被一个声音打断：“一个人喝闷酒多没意思，不如我陪你。”
[EN] "Switch to sweet osmanthus wine, Miss?" Kuqiao suggested.
[ZH] 千里寻抬眼见林觉出现在面前：“你来干什么？”
[EN] "Osmanthus wine is juice—today I want real liquor!" pouring a second cup.
[ZH] 林觉解释道：“别误会，我可不是专程为你而来，刚从勾栏瓦肆听完千里寻才子的话本，正好路过这家酒肆，从窗外看到你一个人独自饮酒，过来打声招呼。”
[EN] "Who says osmanthus wine isn't liquor? Knocked you out cold last summer!"
[ZH] 千里寻没有理会，自顾饮下。
[EN] *Last summer...* The exact night Shen Jianchen realized Zhu Shuzhen was Qianli Xun—watching her sit drunk in Wenyuan's lap! Why did they keep missing by inches?! All because of her loud mouth taunting *"When pigs climb trees!"* Swirling her cup, she muttered, "Wonder if taking those words back is too late..." Of course it was—she was wed, he held an imperial marriage decree.
[ZH] 林觉不请自坐，让酒肆的伙计再拿一只酒杯，自己满上，毫不客气地饮下。
[EN] Lifting the cup in gloom, a familiar voice broke in: "Drinking solo is dull—let me join you."
[ZH] 自从和离后，千里寻也没多讨厌林觉，便没想着把他赶走，随口问道：“怎么一个人出来？没带你家娘子？”
[EN] Looking up, she saw Lin Jue standing beside her table. "What do you want?"
[ZH] 林觉回道：“男人出来有男人的事，老带个女人算什么事儿？”
[EN] "Relax—not stalking you! Just stepped out of Goulan Wasi listening to Master Qianli Xun's huaben, passed the window, spotted you drinking alone, and dropped in to say hi." Taking a seat uninvited, he waved for an extra cup, poured himself full, and knocked it straight down.
[ZH] 千里寻打了一个酒嗝“呃”：“大男子主义！你该不会还把春金巧往家里带？”
[EN] Holding zero grudge since their divorce, Qianli Xun let him sit. "Out solo without your bride?"
[ZH] “那倒不会，主要是现在的娘子听话又温柔，不过春香楼我倒是常去。”林觉毫不隐瞒，现在面对朱淑真他什么话都敢说。
[EN] "Men have men's errands—who drags a wife everywhere?"
[ZH] 千里寻给自己倒上一杯：“你这种人狗改不了吃屎，现在的娘子既然不错，要懂得珍惜，不要再往春香楼跑，好好跟人家过日子。实在喜欢春金巧，可以与你的娘子商量，看能不能给她赎身，放在林府给你当妾室。再这么下去，恐怕你还得和离一次。”
[EN] Letting out a tiny hiccup, Qianli Xun scoffed, "Pure chauvinism! Still sneaking Chun Jinqiao into Lin Manor?"
[ZH] 林觉笑道：“你以为所有人都像你一样敢于走上和离这一步？我现在的娘子可没你那个胆儿，别瞎操心我的事。”
[EN] "Nope—my new wife is gentle and obedient, so no need. Still hit Chunxiang Brothel plenty, though," Lin Jue admitted freely.
[ZH] 千里寻端起酒杯，抿了一口：“我才懒得操心你的破事，只是话赶话说说而已。”
[EN] Pouring herself another cup, Qianli Xun shook her head. "Leopard never changes his spots! Got a decent wife now—cherish her instead of living at the brothel! If you truly care for Chun Jinqiao, talk to your wife about buying her freedom as a concubine—keep this up and you are heading for divorce number two!"
[ZH] 林觉想起一事：“喂，写话本的千里寻才子会不会就是你？”
[EN] Lin Jue laughed aloud. "Think every woman has your nerve to demand mutual divorce? My new wife lacks that kind of spine—save your worry."
[ZH] 千里寻刚才还有些迷糊，林觉的问题让她清醒许多：“你都说是才子，怎么可能是我？再说我哪有写话本的才能？”
[EN] "Like I care about your mess—just chatting."
[ZH] “真的不是你？”林觉眼神中充满怀疑，“你与我在林家和离时，曾当着长辈的面说你是千里寻，难道忘了？当时我不明白，后来听到千里寻才子的美名，猜想可能是你给自己取的假名，不想让他人知道你写话本，才假装成才子。”
[EN] A sudden glint crossed Lin Jue's eyes. "Hey—level with me: is that huaben writer Qianli Xun actually YOU?"
[ZH] 千里寻想起当时为和离不得已说出实情，赶紧掩饰：“当时为跟你和离，瞎编的，随口说出一个名字，没想到竟然跟千里寻才子同名，纯属巧合。”
[EN] Sobering a quick notch, Qianli Xun blinked. "They call him a male master—how could it be me? Since when do I write huaben?"
[ZH] “我真心希望千里寻才子就是你，他写的话本比你写的诗词强过不知多少倍。你写的那些附庸风雅的玩意儿我一点儿都不喜欢，相反很喜欢听千里寻才子写的故事。”林觉失落地说完，靠近朱淑真，压低声音，“可你跟以前的朱淑真一点儿都不像。”
[EN] "Really not?" Lin Jue squinted shrewdly. "Back in Lin Manor's hall during our divorce, you swore in front of my elders your name was Qianli Xun—forgot?! Back then I thought it was gibberish; once Master Qianli Xun blew up across Lin'an, I figured you picked a male pen name to hide writing fiction!"
[ZH] 千里寻掩饰道：“没办法，都是拜你所赐。”
[EN] Scrambling to cover that old slip, she waved it off. "Made a random name up on the spot to shake loose from you—pure fluke it matched the famous writer!"
[ZH] 林觉想解释，感觉有个人影晃在眼前，一抬头，发现白梅若带丫鬟站在面前。
[EN] "Shame—I really hoped it was you," Lin Jue sighed with genuine regret. "His huaben stories wipe the floor with your poetry! Never could stomach those fancy literary verses, yet I am hooked on Master Qianli Xun's tales!" Leaning closer, he dropped his voice. "You really are nothing like the old Zhu Shuzhen..."
[ZH] 原来白梅若想吃糕点，亲自出来购买。买完糕点，见集市热闹，决定逛逛再回去。逛着逛着，丫鬟瞧见主君在酒肆，对面坐着以前的夫人，提醒道：“夫人，您瞧，主君与谁坐在里面饮酒？”
[EN] "All thanks to you," Qianli Xun parried dryly.
[ZH] 白梅若向内看去，不满与嫉妒之情涌上心头，她努力保持镇定，走进酒肆，站在二人面前。竟然发现她的夫君一边与朱淑真说话，还一边露出笑脸，那个享受劲儿与跟她在一起时完全不一样。
[EN] Before Lin Jue could reply, a shadow fell across the table—looking up, both froze as Bai Meiruo stood right beside them with her maid! Out shopping for pastries and strolling the lively market, her maid had spotted Master drinking and laughing warmly across from his ex-wife—a bright, relaxed grin Bai Meiruo never saw at home—lighting instant jealousy inside her chest.

#CH 205
[ZH] 林觉没有了笑容，一脸平静地问：“你怎么跑来？”
[ZH] 在夫君面前白梅若需保持形象，她努力控制好情绪。
[ZH] “奴家出来买些糕点，经过此处，见夫君在此饮酒，进来打声招呼。没想到幽栖居士也在。哦，现在应该称呼您陈家少夫人。”
[ZH] 千里寻听出些不满来，站起来说：“我还有事，失陪，酒钱已付过，算我请客，你们夫妻好好对饮。”
[ZH] 白梅若哪能放过奚落朱淑真的机会，拦住她：“别一见我来，就要走，好像怕我一般，难道你正在与我夫君说些不愿我听到的话？”
[ZH] 林觉批评道：“别瞎胡说，要是没事，赶紧回去。”
[ZH] 白梅若担心夫君生气，立刻改变态度，装作热情的样子，上前拉住朱淑真的手：“幽栖居士，我刚才跟你说笑，你我好不容易碰上，别着急走，坐下来说说话。”
[ZH] 对方态度好转，千里寻不好驳她的面子，关键是白梅若刚才的话显然是怀疑她，就这么走了，岂不显得做贼心虚？
[ZH] “怎么？不称呼我陈家少夫人？”
[ZH] “称呼幽栖居士更亲切些。”白梅若继续装道，“来，我们坐下聊。”
[ZH] 千里寻坐回原位，白梅若则坐在她身旁。
[ZH] 林觉甚觉无趣：“女人家聊的话题我不感兴趣，府衙还有事，失陪。”
[ZH] 白梅若见她来，夫君却要走，倍感失落，对着林觉的背影说：“夫君，早些从府衙回家，厨房里炖了鸡汤，你最近辛苦，需要补补。”
[ZH] 林觉没有回头，简单“嗯”了一声，迅速走出酒肆。
[ZH] 千里寻看到这一幕，觉出白梅若的不易，刚才的不满烟消云散，不由生出怜悯之情：“林觉能娶你，是他上辈子修来的福气。”
[ZH] 白梅若叹口气：“唉，我嫁给他也不知是不是我的福气？”
[ZH] 千里寻不知该如何安慰，只能以沉默回应。
[ZH] 白梅若继续说：“幽栖居士在林家时可听说一位叫春金巧的商妓？”
[ZH] 千里寻怎能不知？因不清楚白梅若的想法，不敢轻易谈论，怕因哪句话说的不对影响林觉与白梅若的夫妻关系。故而没有言语，仅露出微笑，端起酒杯装作品酒的样子。
[ZH] 白梅若见幽栖居士不言语，猜出她的心思，直白地说：“你不必担心与我谈论此人，我早听林府的丫鬟说过，你还未与我夫君和离时，他时常将春金巧带回林府恶心你。”
[ZH] 千里寻松口气，放下酒杯：“你既已知，还问我做什么？”
[ZH] 白梅若不由露出稍显得意的神情：“不过，自从我嫁给夫君，他还从来没把春金巧带回过林府。”
[ZH] 千里寻忽觉自己怜悯白梅若甚是可笑：“林觉肯定没少往春香楼跑。”
[ZH] 白梅若不再得意，暂时将奚落朱淑真的想法搁在一旁，毕竟家中之事不好对他人提起，而眼前这位对自家夫君知根知底，说出来也不怕她笑话。
[ZH] “好姐姐，你帮我出出主意，往后我该怎么办？”
[ZH] 突然被白梅若称呼“好姐姐”，倒令千里寻一时无法适应：“你们的事我不好掺和，再说跟我也没关系。”
[ZH] 白梅若拿过林觉用过的酒杯，给自己满上，端起来：“我敬姐姐，我干了，您随意。”
[EN] The smile vanished off Lin Jue's face at once. "What are you doing here?"
[ZH] 接着连喝两杯，喝得猛些，给呛住，白梅若用丝帕捂住口咳起来，咳得脸色通红。
[EN] Clamping tight control over her jealousy in front of her husband, Bai Meiruo dipped a smooth curtsy. "I came out for pastries, spotted Husband taking wine inside, and stepped in to greet you—never expected Youqi Jushi here too. Oh, excuse me—I should say Young Madam Chen now."
[ZH] 一旁的丫鬟上前帮忙轻拍背部：“夫人，您没事吧？”
[EN] Catching the frosty prickle, Qianli Xun stood up. "Excuse me, I have errands. The wine is already paid—my treat, enjoy your drink together."
[ZH] 对方如此状况，逼得千里寻不得不多说两句：“一切取决于你的态度。”
[EN] Unwilling to let her slip away unneedled, Bai Meiruo stepped in front of her. "Bolting the second I show up, like you are scared of me? Saying things to my husband you do not want my ears catching?"
[ZH] 白梅若的咳嗽缓解许多，坐正后问：“姐姐是何意？”
[EN] "Quit talking nonsense!" Lin Jue snapped. "Got nothing to do, head home!"
[ZH] 千里寻解释道：“我呢，眼里揉不得沙子，才选择和离。当然，在这个时代我的态度不一定是正确的，却符合我的心意。”
[EN] Spooked by his sharp bark, Bai Meiruo flipped instantly into sugary warmth, catching Shuzhen's hand. "Only teasing, Youqi Jushi! Rare running into each other—do not dash off, sit and chat!"
[ZH] 白梅若已领悟，思索一会儿，说道：“姐姐家世好，长得貌美又有才华，自然天不怕地不怕；而我不一样，嫁给夫君算是高攀。家里人认为我嫁了好人家，很多姊妹羡慕不已。我若走姐姐的路，恐被家人所不容。”
[EN] Refusing to look guilty by fleeing, Qianli Xun raised an eyebrow. "Not calling me Young Madam Chen anymore?"
[ZH] 话既已说开，千里寻那股子乐于助人的精神被激发，帮对方出起主意：“林觉这人唯一的优点是找商妓十分专一，从我知道他找商妓到现在还没换过人，说明他对春金巧有感情。你呢，不如主动给春金巧赎身，放在林觉身边当个妾室。既会让林觉感恩于你，还可将春金巧放在身边加以约束，也免了林觉整日往春香楼跑被他人在背后非议。”
[EN] "Youqi Jushi sounds warmer! Sit, sit!"
[ZH] 白梅若脸上露出笑容：“还是姐姐聪明，给我出这么好的主意，我怎么就没想到？今儿我回去定然好好筹谋筹谋。”
[EN] Once both sat down, Lin Jue pushed back bored. "No taste for women's chatter—yamen business calls. Excuse me."
[ZH] 千里寻本以为自己帮了白梅若，不会与对方成闺蜜，怎么说也是朋友，可接下来白梅若的话让她大跌眼镜。
[EN] Crushed watching him bolt the instant she arrived, Bai Meiruo called softly at his back, "Come home early from the yamen, Husband—chicken soup is simmering in the kitchen to build up your strength."
[ZH] 白梅若面色忽然变得平静：“幽栖居士帮我出这么好的主意，我很感激，但你我不可能成为朋友……”
[EN] Giving a clipped grunt without glancing back, Lin Jue strode clean out of the tavern.
[ZH] 刚才还亲热地称呼她“好姐姐”，这会儿称呼又变。千里寻适应片刻后，总结出经验教训，以后帮人前还是先将人看清楚再说。
[EN] Watching her swallow the cold shoulder melted Qianli Xun's irritation into quiet pity. "Lin Jue earned three lifetimes of luck marrying you."
[ZH] 面对白梅若这类人，千里寻是不愿轻易吃亏：“你想多了，我今日无聊，看在你陪我解闷儿的份上，随便帮你出出主意，我可从来没想过要跟你交朋友。”
[EN] Bai Meiruo let out a bitter sigh. "Hard to tell if marrying him was my luck or not..."
[ZH] “你也别生气，你是我夫君的前妻，我若与你成为朋友，任谁见了都会觉得奇怪。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stayed tactfully quiet.
[ZH] 千里寻实在不想待下去，跟这种人多说无益：“我还有事，先行一步。”
[EN] "When Youqi Jushi lived at Lin Manor, did you know a courtesan named Chun Jinqiao?"
[ZH] 白梅若并不愿朱淑真就此离去，重要的话她还未说。
[EN] Unsure where she was steering—afraid of sparking household strife—Qianli Xun stayed mute behind a polite smile, lifting her cup.
[ZH] “幽栖居士可真有本事，搞得郡主因你而受伤，明日我打算去幽林别居探望郡主，不知幽栖居士可否愿意与我同往？”
[EN] Reading her caution, Bai Meiruo laid it bare. "No need to tiptoe around her name—Lin Manor's maids already told me how Husband used to drag Chun Jinqiao home to spite you before your divorce."
[ZH] 千里寻很想探望梦容，可也明白现在最正确的做法便是远离梦容。白梅若如此说，无疑在她伤口上撒把盐。她努力忍住，往酒肆外走。
[EN] Relaxing, Qianli Xun set her cup down. "If you already know, why ask?"
[ZH] 身后传来白梅若的声音：“幽栖居士该不会是做了亏心事，怕见到郡主？”
[EN] A tiny gleam of smugness flickered across Bai Meiruo's face. "Since I married Husband, though, he has never once brought Chun Jinqiao through Lin Manor's door."
[ZH] 千里寻已忍不住，攥紧拳头，忽地回来，伸到白梅若面前：“白梅若，老娘的拳头从不打女人，你若再瞎掰掰，只怕我的拳头也开始打起女人来。”
[EN] Her sudden pity feeling downright silly now, Qianli Xun observed dryly, "Sure he still visits Chunxiang Brothel plenty, though."
[ZH] 白梅若被吓住，不敢再言语。
[EN] The smugness collapsed at once. Dropping her barb—unable to air dirty laundry to strangers, whereas this woman knew her husband inside out—Bai Meiruo leaned forward pleadingly. "Good Sister, give me advice—what do I do?"
[ZH] 千里寻这才收起拳头，“哼”了一声，走出酒肆。
[EN] Threw off stride by the sudden "Good Sister," Qianli Xun shook her head. "None of my business—better I stay out of your marriage."
[ZH] 白梅若缓了一会儿，骂道：“原来是只母老虎！难怪我家官人不要你！”
[EN] Grabbing Lin Jue's cup, Bai Meiruo poured herself full—"To you, Sister!"—and knocked two strong cups straight down, choking into a violent red-faced coughing fit while her maid pounded her back.
[ZH] 既然是步行出来，必然也步行回去。遇到白梅若这等人，窝一肚子火，也得疏散疏散。千里寻希望街市的繁华能让她很快忘记刚才遇到的人和事。
[EN] Softened by the sight, Qianli Xun spoke plainly. "Everything comes down to what you want."
[ZH] 苦荞替小姐鸣不平：“林统制的夫人脑子指定有问题，要不怎么翻脸比翻书还快！”
[EN] Catching her breath, Bai Meiruo asked, "What do you mean, Sister?"
[ZH] 苦荞主动搭话，千里寻也算找到一处发泄途径：“你说得对，她的脑子肯定有问题。那日在捶丸场第一次遇见，说话又懂事又知礼，一点儿都看不出是这等变化无常之人。”
[EN] "I could not stand a single speck of grit in my eye, so I chose mutual divorce. Maybe not this era's textbook choice, yet true to my own heart."
[ZH] 苦荞安慰道：“小姐不必跟她置气。”
[EN] Understanding flickered in Bai Meiruo's eyes. "Sister has powerful family, beauty, and genius—fearing neither Heaven nor earth. I married UP into Lin Manor; my relatives envy my good match. Walk your road and my own family would disown me."
[ZH] 千里寻也安慰自己：“跟她置气只会拉低我的档次。吃一堑长一智，你家小姐我以后帮人时，需把眼睛睁大些，看清楚再帮，否则后悔都来不及。”
[EN] Her helpful streak kicking in once the wall dropped, Qianli Xun offered practical counsel. "Lin Jue's sole virtue is sticking to one courtesan—never switched off Chun Jinqiao the whole time I knew him, proving he genuinely cares for her. Buy her freedom yourself and bring her into Lin Manor as a concubine! One move earns Lin Jue's gratitude, puts Chun Jinqiao right under your thumb where you can keep her in check, AND stops him haunting brothels and drawing street gossip!"
[ZH] 苦荞疑惑地问道：“小姐，您说她为何要那样对待您？”
[EN] Bai Meiruo's face lit right up. "Leave it to Sister's sharp mind! Why did I never think of that?! Going home tonight to put it in motion!"
[ZH] 千里寻想想说：“仔细想想，白梅若刚看到我时，说话的表情和语气才是她真实的情绪流露。林觉走后对我姐姐长姐姐短地称呼，无非是想让我帮她出主意。主意刚出完，她就是忍不住恢复原样。她对我的态度前后变化明显，也许是因为我与林觉和离过。”
[EN] Qianli Xun figured they would part on friendly terms—only for Bai Meiruo's face to snap ice-flat the very next breath!
[ZH] 苦荞不解：“我实在理解不了，您与林统制已和离，她有什么不高兴的？”
[EN] "Thank you for the brilliant advice, Youqi Jushi—yet you and I can never be friends."
[ZH] 千里寻也想不通，她跟白梅若仅仅第二回见面，她也没必要因为朱淑真是林觉的前妻如此待她。
[EN] Whiplash! "Good Sister" one second, frosty wall the next! Making a mental note to vet people before offering free advice, Qianli Xun refused to eat the snub. "Flatter yourself—I was bored, figured you kept me company, and tossed out free tips. Never once planned on befriending you."
[ZH] “人心难测，谁知道她抽哪门子风？以后见她还是躲远些，尽量不跟这种人打交道。”
[EN] "Do not take offense—you are my husband's ex-wife. Becoming chummy would look bizarre to everyone."
[ZH] 二人边说边穿越闹市，渐渐来到人烟稀少之地，陈府的大门出现在眼前。此时天已蒙蒙黑。
[EN] Done wasting air, Qianli Xun stood up. "Business awaits—goodbye."
[ZH] 千里寻瞧着陈府的大门，倍感惆怅。
[EN] Unwilling to let her leave without landing her real sting, Bai Meiruo added sweetly: "Youqi Jushi certainly packs a punch—getting Princess Jiahe stabbed on your account! Tomorrow I am visiting Youlin Retreat to check on Her Highness—care to tag along?"
[ZH] 从昨日起，她住进陈府，以后会从这道门走出，无论走到哪里，都会回到这里。陈府成为她新的人生驿站，也许会永远停留在这里。
[EN] Longing to visit Mengrong yet knowing staying far away was the only kindness left, Qianli Xun felt salt grind straight into the raw wound and turned toward the door without replying.
[ZH] 千里寻累了，想把陈府看作她人生最后一道驿站，希望与陈府里每一个人和谐相处。
[EN] "Afraid to face Her Highness with a guilty conscience?" Bai Meiruo purred behind her.
[ZH] 想到和谐相处，自然想起夏老太太，一位毫无底线宠爱孙子，第一日没给她好脸色的人。千里寻决定，为了在陈府活得自在顺利，要想办法把老太太搞定。
[EN] Snapping straight back with a tight fist thrust an inch from Bai Meiruo's nose, Qianli Xun hissed, "My fist normally skips women—keep flapping that venomous jaw and I make YOU my first exception!"
[ZH] 她跨进陈府的大门，来到居住的院子外，借着月光，“墨香苑”三个字赫然入目。
[EN] Frozen white in terror, Bai Meiruo shut her trap on the spot. Dropping her fist with a cold snort, Qianli Xun marched out—only after she cleared the door did Bai Meiruo dare mutter, "Total tigress! No wonder my husband dumped you!"
[ZH] 千里寻十分满意，赞叹韦嬷嬷办事麻利，一日之内，匾额已挂好，就凭这名字，她以后踏进院子会觉得心情舒畅，不由对里面充满期待。
[EN] Walking home through the bustling streets to cool off, Kuqiao fumed beside her: "Division Commander Lin's wife is touched in the head—flips faces faster than turning a page!"
[ZH] 走进院子，穿过院中的花坛池水，来到正屋跟前，“素心草堂”也已挂上。苦荞与红豆住的侧屋则挂上“悦宁屋”的匾额。
[EN] "Dead right!" Qianli Xun vented. "Seemed sweet and polished at the chuiwan grounds—never guessed she was two-faced! Waste of breath getting mad at someone that petty. Live and learn—next time I check folks close before lending a hand."
[ZH] 苦荞高兴道：“我住的屋子从此后也有名字！”
[EN] "Why treat you so nasty, Miss?"
[ZH] “走，看看别处。”
[EN] "Looking back, her frosty dig at the start was her real face; calling me Sister once Lin Jue left was purely fishing for advice—the second she got it, the mask dropped! Probably bitter just because I was Lin Jue's ex."
[ZH] 她们来到后院，韦嬷嬷的屋子挂着“静宜堂”，陈浩宇的屋子挂着“博雅斋”。
[EN] "You already divorced him—why hold a grudge?!"
[ZH] 苦荞议论道：“博雅斋三个字倒好，可姑爷跟这三个字一点儿不匹配。”
[EN] "People's hearts are murky—steer clear of her from now on."
[ZH] 千里寻批评道：“在我面前议论议论也就罢了，可不能形成习惯，一不小心在陈家人面前也如此议论。”
[EN] As noisy markets gave way to quiet lanes under gathering dusk, Chen Manor's towering gate loomed ahead, bringing a quiet ache to Qianli Xun's chest. Since yesterday, this threshold marked her new waystation—maybe her final stop. Tired of fighting every wind, she hoped to live at ease with everyone inside—including figuring out how to charm Old Madam Xia.
[ZH] 苦荞意识到失言：“小姐批评的是，以后我会管住这张嘴，不乱说话。”
[EN] Stepping inside and walking to her courtyard, moonlight illuminated fresh carved wood above the archway: **Moxiang Courtyard** (*Ink-Fragrance Courtyard*). Thrilled at Matron Wei's lightning efficiency—plaques hung in a single day!—she stepped past flowerbeds and pond to find **Suxin Thatched Hall** (*Pure-Heart Thatched Hall*) above the main door and **Yuening Room** above the side quarters ("My room has a real name now!" Kuqiao cheered).
[ZH] 苦荞如此懂事，千里寻不好再批评下去：“虽与人不匹配，可与这院子匹配，再说陈浩宇都不怎么回来，不用理会他。”
[EN] Rounding into the back yard, **Jingyi Hall** graced Matron Wei's door while **Boya Study**—meaning "Erudite and Refined"—hung over Chen Haoyu's quarters.
[ZH] 千里寻忽觉自己有些过分，霸占了陈浩宇的院子不说，还把他赶到后院。
[EN] "Fine name, Boya Study," Kuqiao giggled, "only it fits Young Master like silk on a pig!"
[ZH] “苦荞，你有没有觉得你家小姐我做得很过分？”
[EN] "Save cracks like that for my ears alone—slip up in front of Chen elders and we catch grief," Qianli Xun cautioned gently.
[ZH] 苦荞小心回道：“在他人看来，应该会认为小姐做得过分，可我了解其中的弯弯绕绕，并不会这么认为。”
[EN] "Got it—zipping my lip from now on!"
[ZH] 千里寻没有说什么，走出后院，往西边拐，经过一道圆形石门，是下人居住的屋子，男仆住的房子挂着“忠勤舍”，丫鬟的房子挂着“勤安居”
[EN] "Does not match him, yet matches the courtyard—and he barely sleeps home anyway." Pausing, a brief twinge pricked Qianli Xun. "Honestly, Kuqiao—seizing his whole yard and shoving him out back—did I go too far?"
[ZH] 到处视察一遍，千里寻很满意。她出陈府时，韦嬷嬷正吩咐人布置屋子，匾额既然都挂上，屋子也肯定布置好，千里寻充满期待：“跟我回素心草堂。”
[EN] "Outsiders might say yes," Kuqiao answered carefully, "yet knowing the whole mess between you two, I say not one bit."
[ZH] 来到素心草堂，里面已褪去奢华之风，不仅素雅清淡，还充满笔墨书香之气。千里寻看着书架上一排一排码得整齐的书卷，赞道：“现在走进来才觉既熟悉又舒心。”
[EN] Rounding through the west moon-gate to admire **Zhongqin Quarters** for male servants and **Qin'an Lodge** for maids, Qianli Xun hurried back eagerly to **Suxin Thatched Hall**. Stepping inside took her breath away: gaudy bridal gold gone, replaced by serene simplicity, clean ink-scented stillness, and shelves stacked neat with her beloved scrolls. "Feels like home at last!"
[ZH] 苦荞径直走到茶桌前：“小姐，我给您做盏茶。”
[EN] "Making Miss a bowl of tea right now!" Kuqiao headed straight to the tea stand.
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“我怎么有种回到成亲前的感觉。”
[EN] "Funny—feels like I never got married at all!"
[ZH] 苦荞已在茶桌前坐好，开始做茶：“那可不是？小姐都把洞房改成了草堂。”
[EN] "Of course it does—Miss turned a bridal suite into a scholar's thatched hall!"
[ZH] 也许是因环境影响，千里寻有了创作的冲动，她走到书桌旁坐下，开始提笔写话本。
[EN] Inspired by the calm ink fragrance, Qianli Xun sat straight down at her desk and picked up her brush to write her huaben.

#CH 206
[ZH] 千里寻正写得专注，韦嬷嬷走进来，本不想打扰，可今日之事需向姑娘汇报，好征求她的意见。
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷先走到苦荞身边，看她做茶。苦荞本想帮着唤小姐一声，被韦嬷嬷阻止，让她不要出声。
[ZH] 两人相视一笑，说起悄悄话。
[ZH] “你做茶的手艺越来越醇熟，以后要是嫁给白风，姑娘都没个做茶的。”
[ZH] “嬷嬷放心，我都想好了，哪怕我嫁给白风，也会每日来伺候小姐。”
[ZH] “哪有这种事？白风是仪王身边的侍卫，迟早要回汴梁。”
[ZH] 苦荞给茶盏中再添些热水，击拂一会儿：“嬷嬷这么一说，倒搞得我犹豫不决。”
[ZH] “没什么可犹豫的。红豆这丫头不错，整日光训练那几个下人也不是个事儿，有时间教教她做茶，你嫁人后，姑娘也好有个做茶的。”
[ZH] “嬷嬷考虑的是。”
[ZH] 茶做好后，苦荞端起来送至小姐的桌前。
[ZH] 千里寻闻到一股茶香，才放下笔，正觉口渴，端起茶盏慢慢喝下。
[ZH] “小姐，韦嬷嬷来了。”
[ZH] 千里寻放下茶盏，瞧见韦嬷嬷向她走来，示意苦荞收了手稿。
[ZH] 苦荞在心里偷乐，韦嬷嬷早都猜到千里寻才子是小姐，只是还没来得及告知小姐，既如此，还是配合她将手稿收起来。
[ZH] 千里寻起身迎上去：“今日辛苦嬷嬷，屋里的布置甚合我心意。”
[ZH] “姑娘满意就好，我来是想告诉姑娘，屋子里收拾起来倒容易，院子里还需些时日，姑娘得耐心等待。”
[ZH] “我有的是耐心。”千里寻将韦嬷嬷拉到椅子跟前坐下。
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“我今日跟陈府的花匠沟通过，他们说明日会来墨香苑重新栽种花草，需要三日才能完成。大致情况我先给姑娘说说，按照姑娘的喜好，会在墨香苑门外栽种些翠竹，院子里原有的花卉能留就留着，再种些姑娘喜欢的菊花，什么小雏菊、百日菊、夏菊、秋菊、墨菊、杭菊等统统种些。再给花坛里种些荷花，姑娘曾说过荷花出什么濯什么的，反正就是夸它好……”
[ZH] 苦荞插嘴道：“是濂溪先生文章里写到的句子：出淤泥而不染，濯清涟而不妖。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷笑道：“对！对！就这两句，还是苦荞记忆力好。”
[ZH] 苦荞谦虚道：“小姐以前经常说，我自然记住。”
[ZH] 千里寻无不满意：“嬷嬷说的我都同意。我看院中没有一棵树，一到夏季也没个乘凉的地儿，不拘什么树栽上一棵，只要好生长，又大又茂盛即可。”
[ZH] “姑娘与我想到一处，我找花匠时，发现他们为陈府培养了好几棵合欢树，长得枝叶繁茂，就想着给姑娘移到院子里来。跟花匠一说，他满口答应，明日就给移进来。”
[ZH] 合欢树的名字让千里寻品出韦嬷嬷的心意，不过是希望她嫁到陈家有一日能与陈浩宇将日子过得和谐美满。可千里寻从未如此想过，只因她心中的人从来不是陈浩宇。
[ZH] “嬷嬷会不会觉得我很过分？”同样的问题千里寻还想听听韦嬷嬷看法。
[ZH] 姑娘突如其来的问题，倒将韦嬷嬷给问住：“姑娘是何意？”
[ZH] “我一嫁到陈家，把陈浩宇赶到后院，又谋了他的财产，还霸占他的院子，一切都按照我的喜好安排，会不会很过分？”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷这才明白，“呵呵”笑道：“见识浅薄之人才会认为姑娘过分，我反而觉得姑娘这么做对姑爷还有陈家只有好处。”
[ZH] 千里寻觉得韦嬷嬷如此说，不过是向着她：“嬷嬷偏袒我，自然认为好，说不定陈家有人此时正在对我的做法说三道四。我才管不了那么多，就是要让陈家人明白，陈浩宇娶我是大错特错！”
[ZH] “既不在乎又何必问我你是否做得过分？”
[ZH] “是啊，我又何必在乎那么多？”千里寻想起明日的诗会，“嬷嬷，东方尘泽邀请我明日参加诗会，我已答应，要不要给陈浩宇的爹娘说一声？”
[ZH] “当然需征求长辈意见。”
[ZH] “如果他们不让我参加该怎么办？”
[EN] Absorbed in writing, Qianli Xun never heard Matron Wei step inside; unwilling to break her flow yet needing to report today's plans, Matron Wei signaled Kuqiao to keep quiet and watched her whisk tea.
[ZH] “老爷和夫人甚是通情达理，应该不会阻拦。”
[EN] "Your tea hand grows silkier by the day," Matron Wei whispered with a smile. "When you marry Bai Feng, who will make Miss's tea?"
[ZH] “那就好，我这就去给他们说一声。”
[EN] "Already planned it, Matron! Even after marrying Bai Feng, I will come wait on Miss every day!"
[ZH] 苦荞挑起灯笼，负责在一旁照明。千里寻借着灯笼中散发的光芒，向公婆的院子方向走去。今日一早去过一次，陈府的路又好记，再次前去，已觉轻车熟路。
[EN] "Silly girl—Bai Feng is Prince Yi's guard; sooner or later he heads back to Bianliang."
[ZH] 走着走着，身后传来一个声音：“天已黑，弟妹这是去哪里？”
[EN] Adding hot water and whisking briskly, Kuqiao paused in sudden dismay. "Put like that, you make me hesitate..."
[ZH] 原来是陈浩东，他正打算找陈江树，看到前面的身影，跟着盯了一会儿，才瞧明白，便上前搭话。
[EN] "Nothing to hesitate over. Hongdou is sharp—drilling servants all day is not enough; teach her tea-whisking in spare moments so Miss stays cared for once you wed."
[ZH] 听到有人唤她弟妹，猜想应该是白天见过的大嫂的夫君，千里寻转过身：“大哥好，我找公爹和婆婆说些事。”
[EN] "Smart thinking, Matron."
[ZH] 陈浩东走近，将弟妹上下打量一番，真是个美人儿，难怪颜兄倾心。他随口问道：“浩宇可在？”
[EN] Setting the finished bowl gently beside her mistress's elbow, Kuqiao's fragrant brew drew Qianli Xun's brush to a halt. Sipping gladly, she spotted Matron Wei approaching and signaled Kuqiao to stash the huaben—Kuqiao hiding a grin since Matron Wei already knew Miss was Master Qianli Xun!
[ZH] 千里寻照实说：“他不在。”
[EN] Rising warmly to seat Matron Wei, Qianli Xun praised the study's fresh layout.
[ZH] 陈浩东装作不满的样子：“弟妹既嫁给浩宇，应当把他看紧些，别再让他出去浪荡，传出去让人笑话。”
[EN] "The rooms were easy; the yard takes three days," Matron Wei reported. "Spoke with Chen Manor's gardeners today—they start tomorrow: emerald bamboo outside Moxiang Courtyard's gate, keeping existing blooms inside while adding every chrysanthemum you love—daisies, zinnias, summer, autumn, ink, and Hangzhou mums—plus lotus roots in the pond, just like that line Miss always quotes about lotus rising clean out of mud..."
[ZH] 千里寻认为这位堂兄实在可笑，陈浩宇那个熊样，陈家人不管，他这当堂兄的也不管，偏偏让她一个刚过门的人来管，好像她嫁过来是专门帮陈家人教训纨绔子弟！
[EN] "Master Lianxi's line!" Kuqiao piped up. "'Rising from the mud yet remaining unstained, washed by clear ripples yet never coquettish!'" [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] “大哥也太瞧得起我，我一个妇道人家怎么管得了他？大哥若无事，我先去忙。”
[EN] "Yes, yes—sharp memory on Kuqiao!" Matron Wei laughed.
[ZH] 千里寻说完，带苦荞继续向前走。
[EN] "Sounds perfect," Qianli Xun beamed. "Only the yard lacks a shade tree for summer—plant anything big, lush, and fast-growing."
[ZH] 陈浩东瞧着朱家姑娘在黑夜中的背影，冷笑道：“你能这么快嫁到陈家也有我一半功劳，可不能辜负我的期望。”
[EN] "Beat you to it! Found several lush hehuan silk trees—whose name literally means 'harmonious union'—growing in the Chen nursery, and the gardener agreed to transplant one right in tomorrow!"
[ZH] 千里寻来到公婆居住的院子，丫鬟不在，无人向内禀告，她大胆走进去。
[EN] Catching the quiet hope woven into the tree's auspicious name—wishing harmony someday with Chen Haoyu—Qianli Xun felt a small tug: her heart would never bend that way. Curious for an elder's honest take, she asked Matron Wei the same question she had asked Kuqiao: "Taking over his yard, moving him out back, taking all his assets—did I go too far?"
[ZH] 公爹与婆婆正在聊天儿，公爹满脸堆笑面向婆婆；婆婆被逗乐，也是面带笑容，轻声笑着。
[EN] Matron Wei chuckled warmly. "Only narrow minds would say so. To my eyes, every move you made benefits both Young Master and the Chen clan."
[ZH] 听娘和嫂嫂说过他们二人感情甚好，亲眼见到，更令千里寻欣羡不已。
[EN] "You always take my side, Matron! Half the house is probably whispering against me right now—not that I care! Let the Chens see marrying me was a massive blunder!"
[ZH] 穿越前的她小时候从未见父母这样过。后来母亲离世，父亲出家，像这种温馨的场面更与她无缘。穿越而来在朱府，也没见朱晞颜如此对待过朱淑真的母亲。
[EN] "If you truly did not care, why ask me?"
[ZH] 千里寻竟然从心底羡慕陈浩宇有如此恩爱的爹娘，自己若能嫁给一人，与他如此恩爱，也算不枉此生。
[EN] "True enough!" Shrugging it off, Qianli Xun remembered tomorrow's outing. "Dongfang Chenze invited me to a poetry gathering tomorrow and I accepted—should I notify Chen Haoyu's parents?"
[ZH] 可她已嫁给陈浩宇，像举案齐眉、比翼双飞、夫唱妇随、情投意合、与子偕老、相濡以沫、白头偕老之类的词语算是与她没有任何缘分。
[EN] "Definitely check with your elders."
[ZH] 千里寻不由叹口气：“唉！”
[EN] "What if they say no?"
[ZH] 听到叹息声，姚轻琰才转过头：“哦，淑真来了，不知你这么晚来有何事？”
[EN] "Master and Madam are broad-minded; they will not block you."
[ZH] 千里寻上前行礼：“东方尘泽邀请我明日参加诗会，儿媳已答应，想着既已嫁到陈家，便过来问问公爹和婆婆的意思？”
[EN] "Great—I will go tell them right now."
[ZH] 朱家姑娘参加诗会是常有之事，总不能因为嫁到陈家就不让她去，再说陈家也不想在临安的文坛落下不好的名声。
[EN] With Kuqiao lighting the way with a lantern down Chen Manor's straight paths, a voice floated from behind in the dark: "Out after nightfall, Sister-in-Law—heading where?"
[ZH] 陈江林说：“往后只要是参加诗会，尽管自己做主，用不着征求我与你婆婆的意见。”
[EN] Turning, Qianli Xun recognized Chen Haodong, on his way to see his father. "Good evening, Eldest Brother. Going to tell Father-in-Law and Mother-in-Law something."
[ZH] 公爹如此开明，千里寻自然欢喜：“儿媳记下。”同时在心中慨叹，有这么好的公爹和婆婆，若嫁的是自己喜欢之人，哪该多好！她不由想到申简辰……
[EN] Stepping closer, Chen Haodong took in her striking poise—*no wonder Brother Yan fell hard!*—and asked smoothly, "Is Haoyu home?"
[ZH] 正想得入迷，听到婆婆的声音：“淑真，浩宇可在？”
[EN] "No."
[ZH] 千里寻回过神来：“一早给奶奶献过茶后，他就不知所踪。”
[EN] Putting on mild reproach, Chen Haodong advised, "Now that you are married to Haoyu, keep a tighter leash on him—letting him run wild outside invites mockery."
[ZH] 姚轻琰继续问：“他去了哪里？”
[EN] Annoyed—the whole Chen clan watched him run wild for twenty years without lifting a finger, and day two they expect the new bride to play nanny?!—Qianli Xun answered coolly, "Eldest Brother flatters me. How could one woman rein him in? If nothing else, excuse me."
[ZH] “儿媳不知。”
[EN] Watching her lantern recede into the night, Chen Haodong sneered softly: "Half the credit for getting you into Chen Manor fast belongs to me—do not let me down!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰起身走上前，拉住儿媳的手：“淑真，我与你公爹已知你跟浩宇签下婚前契约，互不干涉对方的生活，可在我们心里，你就是陈家的儿媳妇。我与你公爹碍于老太太，对浩宇管教不严，把他养成顽劣的习性。你现在嫁给他，定要替我和你公爹好好管教，你放心，无论你如何收拾他，我与你公爹绝不阻拦。”
[EN] Reaching her in-laws' open courtyard with no maid at the door, Qianli Xun stepped inside—and stopped a beat at the doorway: Chen Jianglin sat beaming warmly at Yao Qingyan, making her laugh softly in easy devotion. Never in her modern childhood—losing her mother early, watching her father become a monk—nor in Zhu Manor watching Zhu Xiyan bark at Madam Xiang had she witnessed marital tenderness like this. A genuine pang of envy hit her for Chen Haoyu growing up under love so warm; wedded to him in name only, phrases like growing old together in harmony belonged to other worlds. She let out an involuntary sigh.
[ZH] 千里寻心想，陈家到底是娶儿媳妇，还是娶个管儿子的人？半路上碰到陈浩宇的堂哥劝他管教，现在连公婆也如此。他们自己管不好儿子，却把希望寄托在她身上，实在可笑！
[EN] Hearing it, Yao Qingyan looked up warmly. "Oh, Shuzhen! What brings you over so late?"
[ZH] 一来她没闲工夫，二来她没这义务，三来她也没兴趣，只盼着陈浩宇永远浪荡在外面别回来，又岂会帮陈家管教儿子？
[EN] Stepping in with a curtsy, Qianli Xun asked politely about attending Dongfang Chenze's poetry gathering tomorrow.
[ZH] 婆婆话已说开，不好驳她的面子，千里寻笑道：“谢谢公爹和婆婆信任，陈……，嗯……浩宇要是干了什么坏事，我定然会好好教训。”
[EN] Unwilling to cage a famous poetess—and proud to keep Chen honor shining in Lin'an's literary circles—Chen Jianglin waved breezily. "Go freely to any poetry gathering from now on—no need to ask Mother-in-Law or me!"
[ZH] 面对长辈，不得已去掉“陈”姓，只称呼名字，千里寻还真不习惯，也将自己恶心到。
[EN] "Thank you, Father-in-Law!" How sweet having in-laws this open—if only the husband had been Shen Jianchen...
[ZH] 姚轻琰点点头：“如此甚好，我听说你把原来的屋子重新布置一番，估计也累了，我和你公爹就不多留你，赶快回去休息。”
[EN] "Shuzhen," Yao Qingyan's voice pulled her back, "is Haoyu back?"
[ZH] 千里寻再次行礼：“儿媳谢公爹和婆婆体谅。”
[EN] "Vanished right after serving tea to Grandmother this morning."
[ZH] 离开公婆居住的院子，千里寻一路上依然沉浸在他们恩爱的场景中。
[EN] "Know where he went?"
[ZH] ……
[EN] "No idea."
