[ZH] 青瓜刚体验两天睡在主人房中的感觉，甚是舒适，根本不想回去跟下人挤在一起：“好吧，公子既发话，我……好好学！”
[EN] Qinggua pulled a sour face. "Young Master, you're asking the impossible."
[ZH] 进入陈府，来到自己的院子外，陈浩宇以为走错方向，回头瞧瞧，没错啊。天虽黑，可这方向他走过十几年，怎么可能错？
[EN] "If you don't train hard, I'll boot you out of the back courtyard and back into the West Courtyard. Forget about sleeping in the same room as me."
[ZH] 可他记得自己住的院子外没有栽种竹子，怎么院门两边多了茂密的翠竹？
[EN] Qinggua had tasted two nights of sleeping in the master's quarters and found it far too comfortable to give up for a crowded servants' dorm. "Fine! Since you say so, Young Master, I'll... I'll train hard!"
[ZH] 正疑惑间，青瓜解释道：“公子，就是您的院子，竹子是少夫人让栽种的，今日花匠才种好。您再抬头瞧，您的院子连名字都有了。”
[EN] Inside Chen Manor, Chen Haoyu reached the entrance of his own courtyard and stopped short, convinced he had taken a wrong turn. He glanced back. No, the path was right. Dark as it was, he had walked this route for over a decade; how could he miss it?
[ZH] 陈浩宇抬头看到“墨香苑”三个字，怒火中烧：“俗！俗不可耐！朱淑真！你太过分！”
[EN] Yet he remembered no bamboo outside his gate. Where had these thick clumps of green stalks flanking both sides come from?
[ZH] 陈浩宇冲进院内，向正屋走去。借着月色，瞅到东边种着一棵茂盛的树木，更是来气，无处发泄，回身一脚踢在青瓜的大腿上：“本公子不喜欢在院子里栽树，难道你不知？”
[EN] Before he could ask, Qinggua spoke up. "This is your courtyard, Young Master. Young Madam had the gardeners plant the bamboo today. Look up, too—your courtyard even has a name now."
[ZH] 青瓜一时疏忽，被踢到，捂住大腿：“公子怎能怪我？少夫人让移过来的，我一个下人哪能管得了主人？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu tilted his head up at the plaque reading Moxiang Courtyard and boiled over. "Vulgar! Beyond vulgar! Zhu Shuzhen, you've gone too far!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇又抬头看到“素心草堂”四个字：“起这么个穷酸名，还号称女词人，哼！”
[EN] He stormed through the gate toward the main hall. By the moonlight, he spotted a lush tree planted on the eastern side. With nowhere else to vent his rage, he spun around and kicked Qinggua square in the thigh. "Didn't you know I hate trees in my courtyard?"
[ZH] 千里寻正在看书，一抬头，见陈浩宇站在面前，一脸怒气。
[EN] Caught off guard this time, Qinggua clutched his thigh. "How is that my fault, Young Master? Young Madam ordered it transplanted here. What can a servant do to stop his mistress?"
[ZH] 她将书放下：“你回来的速度比我想象得要快，表现不错，以后跟我好好配合，咱两个都会相安无事。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu looked up again and read the plaque over the main room: Suxin Thatched Hall. "Calling it something that shabby, and she calls herself a poetess. Hmph!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇训斥道：“朱淑真，你太过分！不仅给院门口种竹子，给院子里栽树，还给每个屋子取名字，更过分的是你把我的屋子变成这么个穷酸样，还是人住的地儿吗？”
[EN] Inside, Qianli Xun was reading a book. She glanced up to find Chen Haoyu standing before her, fuming.
[ZH] 千里寻并未将陈浩宇的怒气当回事，站起来，走到他身边：“睁开你的眼睛好好瞧瞧，哪里穷酸了？我的家具全用香樟木打造而成，选用的颜色素淡些，不像你以前的家具尽显奢华，俗不可耐。”
[EN] She set the book down. "You got back faster than I expected. Not bad. Cooperate with me from here on out, and we'll stay out of each other's hair."
[ZH] 陈浩宇再生气，也拿朱淑真没办法，他尽量控制住怒气：“你我不是说好互不干涉吗？为何我在外面玩得好好的，你偏偏为回门这种小事差人把我叫回来？”
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen, you've gone too far!" Chen Haoyu snapped. "Planting bamboo by my gate, sticking a tree in my courtyard, renaming every single room—and worst of all, turning my hall into this bare, penniless dump! Is this even fit for a human being to live in?"
[ZH] 千里寻解释道：“不怪我，老太太前来寻事，给布置的任务，我要不把你找回来，只怕会更麻烦，所以派红豆他们把你叫回来。放心，我还是有契约精神的，绝不限制你的自由。”
[EN] Unbothered by his tantrum, Qianli Xun rose and walked over. "Open your eyes and take a proper look. What's shabby about this? Every piece of my furniture is carved from fragrant camphorwood in clean, understated tones—nothing like that gaudy, tacky heap you used to keep in here."
[ZH] 听到奶奶前来寻事，朱淑真也怕惹麻烦事，陈浩宇转怒为喜：“呵呵，你不是天不怕地不怕吗？怎么害怕起我奶奶来？”
[EN] Furious as he was, Chen Haoyu had no leverage against her. He swallowed his temper. "Didn't we agree not to interfere with each other? I was having a fine time out there. Why did you send people to drag me home over something trivial like a return visit?"
[ZH] “你哪只耳朵听到我怕老太太？我只是怕麻烦，多一事不如少一事，把你找回来可以省去很多麻烦。你呢，明日好好配合我回个门，你好我好大家好，你我也就相安无事。”
[EN] "Don't blame me," Qianli Xun explained. "Old Madam Xia came looking for trouble and handed me that task. If I hadn't fetched you back, it would have meant bigger headaches later, so I sent Hongdou and the men. Relax—I honor my contracts. I won't restrict your freedom."
[ZH] 陈浩宇总算抓住朱淑真的一根软肋，她怕麻烦，以后不如多给她制造些麻烦，让她不舒服，也好报了霸占他的院子和屋子以及遣散丫鬟的仇恨。
[EN] Hearing that Zhu Shuzhen had bent because Old Madam Xia came making trouble turned Chen Haoyu's anger into glee. "Heh! Didn't you act fearless of heaven and earth? Why are you scared of my grandmother?"
[ZH] “本公子不跟你计较，回去睡觉，明儿还要跟你回朱府。”
[EN] "Which ear heard me say I'm scared of Old Madam Xia? I just hate hassle. Less drama beats more drama, and dragging you home saves me a mountain of trouble. As for you, behave tomorrow for the return visit to Zhu Manor. You stay happy, I stay happy, everyone stays happy, and we leave each other in peace."
[ZH] 陈浩宇出了素心草堂，来到后院，看到“博雅堂”三个字，没有刚才那么生气。他已找到对付朱淑真的方法，他决定等回完门，去见奶奶，奶奶这柄利刃他得好好用用。
[EN] At last Chen Haoyu had spotted a chink in her armor: she hated trouble. From now on, he would stir up plenty of it to make her miserable and pay her back for stealing his courtyard, gutting his rooms, and dismissing his maids.
[ZH] 又想到昨日敬茶时，奶奶把朱淑真气走后，还说她明事理，甚是喜欢她，看来奶奶是表面上装作讨厌朱淑真的样子。
[EN] "I won't stoop to arguing with you. I'm going to bed so I can make it to Zhu Manor tomorrow."
[ZH] 陈浩宇管不了那么多，不管是表面讨厌还是实际讨厌，只要讨厌朱淑真，他陈浩宇就得利用。
[EN] Stepping out of Suxin Thatched Hall into the back courtyard, Chen Haoyu glanced at the plaque reading Boya Study without flaring up as before. He had a weapon against Zhu Shuzhen now. After the return visit tomorrow, he would pay his grandmother a call and put that sharp blade to good use.
[ZH] 白天申简辰离开醉云楼回到幽林别居时，正好碰见苗知瑜和白梅若来访。
[EN] Then a snag occurred to him. Yesterday, after Old Madam Xia drove Zhu Shuzhen off during the tea ceremony, she had praised the girl as sensible and admitted she liked her. Maybe Grandmother was only pretending to dislike her.
[ZH] 二人在门口遇到仪王，上前行礼。
[EN] No matter, Chen Haoyu decided. Pretend or real, so long as she gave Zhu Shuzhen grief, he would make use of it.
[ZH] 申简辰吩咐下人将二人带去见梦容，他不愿与二人一同进入别居，大家无话可说，一路走着怪别扭的，趁此机会，不如去神柳下坐坐，便带着白风转身向密林深处走去。
[EN] Earlier that afternoon, when Shen Jianchen returned to Youlin Retreat from Zuiyun Tower, he ran right into Miao Zhiyu and Bai Meiruo arriving at the gate.
[ZH] 白梅若瞅着仪王的背影，悄悄问：“仪王该不会不喜欢我们前来探望郡主？”
[EN] Both women bowed to Prince Yi at once.
[ZH] 苗知瑜否认道：“不会，仪王向来生性冷淡，对郡主以外的女子都比较冷漠。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen instructed a servant to escort them to Qiao Mengrong. Walking in with them meant painful small talk with nothing to say, so he seized the excuse to visit the Divine Willow instead, turning back into the deep woods with Bai Feng in tow.
[ZH] 白梅若小声提醒：“我看未必，难道你忘了？”
[EN] Watching Prince Yi's retreating figure, Bai Meiruo murmured, "You don't think Prince Yi dislikes us coming to visit the princess?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜装作醒悟的样子：“哦，她是个例外。”
[EN] "Not at all," Miao Zhiyu replied. "Prince Yi has always been aloof, distant toward every woman except the princess."
[ZH] 二人在别居下人的带领下来到郡主的闺房小院，再被下人带进院中。
[EN] Bai Meiruo dropped her voice. "I wouldn't be so sure. Have you forgotten?"
[ZH] 下人向里禀报：“郡主，乔家和林家的少夫人前来拜见。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu put on a show of sudden realization. "Ah, right. She is the exception."
[ZH] 失去与淑真的友情，乔梦容更觉孤单，正想结交新朋友。仔细想来，门外的二位都与朱淑真存在产生芥蒂的可能，不如请进来聊聊，探探虚实。
[EN] A retreat servant guided them to the princess's private courtyard and announced at the door, "Your Highness, the young madams of the Qiao and Lin households request an audience."
[ZH] 喜兰明白郡主的心意，走到门口：“郡主有请二位夫人。”
[EN] Having lost Shuzhen's friendship, Qiao Mengrong felt hollow and eager for new companions. Both women outside had every reason to harbor grudges against Zhu Shuzhen; inviting them in for a chat would let her test the waters.
[ZH] 二人才缓缓进入，见郡主面色发白，坐在书桌前，身上披着一件外衣，显然身上的伤还未大好。她们上前拜见：“佳和郡主。”
[EN] Reading her mistress's mind, Xilan stepped to the doorway. "Her Highness invites both madams inside."
[ZH] 乔梦容放下手中的书卷：“喜兰，看坐。”
[EN] They entered at an unhurried pace. Qiao Mengrong sat at her desk, pale and draped in an outer cloak, her wound far from healed. Both guests curtsied. "Greetings, Princess Jiahe."
[ZH] 喜兰上前说：“二位夫人请坐下与郡主说话。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong laid down her scroll. "Xilan, bring seats."
[ZH] 两人挑位置坐下，外面有丫鬟进来为二人上好茶。
[EN] Xilan arranged the chairs, and another maid brought in fine tea.
[ZH] 苗知瑜先开口说话：“许久未见郡主，我二人正好今日无事，便约着前来拜见，还望郡主不要嫌弃。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu spoke first. "It has been ages since we last saw Your Highness. With nothing pressing today, we agreed to pay our respects. We hope we are not intruding."
[ZH] “说的哪里话？你们能来探望，陪我聊天儿，帮我解闷儿，我感谢还来不及，怎能嫌弃？”
[EN] "Nonsense," Qiao Mengrong said. "Coming to visit, keep me company, and lift my spirits—I can't thank you enough. How could it be an intrusion?"
[ZH] 郡主一席话令二人轻松许多。
[EN] Her warmth put both visitors at ease.
[ZH] 白梅若问：“郡主身上可大好？”
[EN] "Has Your Highness recovered from your injury?" Bai Meiruo asked.
[ZH] 乔梦容意识到二人已知哥哥抢亲之事，闹得那么大，又岂能不知？说不定整个临安城的人都在议论。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong realized they knew all about her brother raiding the wedding procession. A scandal that loud had no doubt traveled across all of Lin'an.
[ZH] “看来你们已经知道，我长这么大，还是头一次成为大家议论之人，都是拜朱淑真所赐。”
[EN] "So you already heard. In all my years, this is the first time I've become the talk of the town—all thanks to Zhu Shuzhen."
[ZH] 二人从郡主的话语中听出明显的不满来，她们相视一笑。
[EN] Hearing the raw bitterness in her voice, the two guests traded a subtle smile.
[ZH] 苗知瑜说：“郡主不必忧虑，大家怎敢随便议论仪王和郡主，只是为郡主鸣不平而已。”
[EN] "Your Highness need not fret," Miao Zhiyu said. "Who would dare speak out of turn about Prince Yi or Your Highness? Folks are only indignant on your behalf."
[ZH] “你们不必哄我，议论指定少不了，要是把我当朋友，实话实说，告诉我大家如何议论？”
[EN] "Don't try to flatter me. There's bound to be gossip. If you count me as a friend, give me the truth. What are people saying?"
[ZH] 郡主此话显然愿意与二人做朋友，看来不枉此行。
[EN] Those words signaled that Princess Jiahe wanted them in her circle; their trip had already paid off.
[ZH] 白梅若说：“郡主既愿意跟我二人做朋友，我说了，郡主可别治我的罪。”
[EN] "Since Your Highness is willing to take us as friends," Bai Meiruo said, "promise you won't hold my bluntness against me."
[ZH] “既是朋友，应当无话不谈，何来治罪一说？”
[EN] "Friends speak without holding back. Why would I hold it against you?"
[ZH] 白梅若并非无脑之人，仔细挑些郡主爱听的：“大家都议论幽栖居士有才无德，自己遇不到好姻缘就去勾引仪王，想要攀高枝。还说她最会勾男子的魂儿，不经意间会让男子为她神魂颠倒，做下傻事。还有人说去年全城人议论她喜欢红杏出墙、暗送秋波实在不假。”
[EN] No fool, Bai Meiruo picked the exact tidbits the princess craved. "Everyone says Youqi Jushi has talent without virtue—that failing to land a good marriage herself, she set out to seduce Prince Yi and climb above her station. They say she has a knack for bewitching men, making them lose their heads and do foolish things without even realizing how she hooked them. People are also saying last year's rumors about her wandering eyes and flirting behind closed doors were true all along."
[ZH] 乔梦容发现人的变化实在快，去年大家议论时，她还为淑真鸣不平，今日听到这些话，却认为骂得甚是解气，还认为朱淑真就是这样的人。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong marveled at how fast feelings could turn. Last year, she had bristled in defense of Shuzhen; today, those same jabs felt vindicating, even accurate.
[ZH] “不经意间”和“暗送秋波”的说法，她甚是认同，在她看来朱淑真就是借着陪她，找她聊天儿的空当偷偷勾引哥哥，才使得哥哥心智被迷惑，做下令所有人难以理解的抢亲之事。
[EN] "Without realizing" and "flirting behind closed doors" hit home hardest. In her eyes, Zhu Shuzhen had used every visit and chat at Youlin Retreat as cover to seduce her brother, clouding his mind until he committed a wedding raid nobody could fathom.
[ZH] “没想到大家如此了解她，我真后悔以前没看清她的真面目，捧出一颗真心将她视作闺中密友，结果因她身心俱伤。”
[EN] "I didn't know people saw through her so clear. How I regret failing to recognize her true face sooner. I handed her my whole heart as a confidante, and she left me wounded body and soul."
[ZH] 苗知瑜说：“郡主与她接触的时日并不长，看不出她的真面目实属正常。我可是从小认识她，真正看清她的真面目也是与文渊成亲后，只能说明幽栖居士是个很会掩饰的人。”
[EN] "You only knew her a short while, Your Highness," Miao Zhiyu said. "It's natural you missed her true nature. I grew up knowing her, and even I didn't see through her until after marrying Wenyuan. It only proves how skilled Youqi Jushi is at wearing a mask."
[ZH] 乔梦容冷笑道：“她掩饰得实在太好。不过苗夫人还是比我强，你与她从小相识，都爱好诗词，经常一起参加诗会，也没见你视她为闺中密友，足以看出你与她不是一类人。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong gave a cold laugh. "She wears it well indeed. Still, Madam Miao, you did better than me. You knew her from childhood, shared a love of poetry, and attended salons together, yet you never made her your close friend. That shows you and she were never cut from the same cloth."
[ZH] 苗知瑜解释道：“知瑜曾经也想过这个问题，开始怎么都想不明白，后来还是我的一位闺中密友一语点醒梦中人。她说我虽也参加诗会，却从未被他人非议，说明我时刻谨守闺中女子的操守。而幽栖居士乃性情中人，常常忘记女子的操守，才惹得他人非议。我们二人骨子里甚不相同，故而很难成为真正的朋友。”
[EN] "I used to wonder about that myself," Miao Zhiyu explained. "At first I couldn't figure out why, until a close friend opened my eyes with a single remark. She pointed out that though I attend poetry banquets too, no scandal has ever touched me because I always guard a gentlewoman's propriety. Youqi Jushi follows her impulses and forgets feminine decorum, which invites gossip. At our core we are opposites, so we could never be true friends."
[ZH] 乔梦容叹道：“是啊，她哪有女子的操守？无论品德还是行为都不符合大家闺秀的风范。亏我去年还为她写的那句‘和衣睡倒人怀’正名，如今想来也只有她才能写出厚颜无耻的句子。枉我这么多年默默喜欢她的诗词，从今往后，我再也不会这么傻。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong sighed. "Indeed. What propriety does she have? Neither her morals nor her conduct fit a well-bred lady. And to think last year I defended that line she wrote, 'falling asleep in his arms, fully dressed'! Looking back, only someone as shameless as her could pen such words. All those years I admired her poetry in secret—never again will I be such a fool."
[ZH] 白梅若趁机说：“要是大家知道她品行不端，估计江南女词人的美称很快就没了。”
[EN] Bai Meiruo seized the opening. "Once word gets out about her lack of virtue, her title as the Poetess of Jiangnan won't last long."
[ZH] 这话算是提醒了乔梦容：“她既不仁，也别怪我不义，等我从汴梁回来，需做些事情，让她丢了江南女词人的美称。”
[EN] That sparked an idea in Qiao Mengrong. "Since she showed no kindness, she can't blame me for showing no mercy. Once I return from Bianliang, I'll take steps to strip her of that title."
[ZH] 二人听了更加开心。
[EN] Both visitors brightened at the news.
[ZH] 苗知瑜问：“郡主为何要回汴梁？”
[EN] "Why is Your Highness returning to Bianliang?" Miao Zhiyu asked.
[ZH] 乔梦容脸上才露出笑容：“官家下旨，让我跟哥哥回汴梁成亲。”
[EN] Only then did a genuine smile touch Qiao Mengrong's lips. "His Majesty issued an edict summoning Brother and me back to Bianliang to wed."
[ZH] 二人赶紧恭贺：“恭喜郡主如愿以偿！”
[EN] Both women offered their congratulations at once. "Congratulations on seeing your wish fulfilled, Your Highness!"
[ZH] 乔梦容满意地点点头。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong nodded in satisfaction.
[ZH] 白梅若说：“可惜郡主大婚我二人无法前去，等郡主回到临安，我们再前来贺喜。”
[EN] "What a pity we cannot attend your grand wedding," Bai Meiruo said. "Once you return to Lin'an, we will come offer our congratulations in person."
[ZH] 苗知瑜也说：“恐怕到时不光是你我二人，乔家、林家还有临安所有权贵都会前来贺喜。”
[EN] "When that day comes, it won't just be the two of us," Miao Zhiyu added. "The Qiaos, the Lins, and every noble household in Lin'an will come to celebrate."
[ZH] 乔梦容更开心：“以后你们二人要多与我走动，苗夫人也要将你的闺中密友多给我引荐引荐，希望我再次回到临安时，可以和临安城所有的大家闺秀成为朋友。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong's spirits lifted further. "Come visit me often from now on, both of you. Madam Miao, introduce your close friends to me as well. By the time I return to Lin'an, I hope to count every well-bred lady in the city among my friends."
[ZH] 二人听得出，佳和郡主是想将临安所有大家闺秀联合起来孤立幽栖居士，二人甚是欣慰，更慨叹不枉此行。
[EN] Both guests caught the subtext: Princess Jiahe meant to unite Lin'an's entire gentlewoman circle to freeze Youqi Jushi out. Delighted, they felt their visit could not have gone better.
[ZH] 说完重要的话后，又陪佳和郡主闲聊一会儿，太阳快落山时两人才离开。
[EN] With the real business settled, they chatted a while longer and took their leave as the sun dipped toward the horizon.

#CH 213
[ZH] 神柳下，申简辰一直坐着，想着在醉云楼与千里寻会面的情景，算是向她表露心迹，即使与梦容成亲，他也会为了所爱“守身如玉”。
[ZH] 不管这么做在他人看来是对还是错，关键他自己这么想。他一直将梦容视作妹妹，作为哥哥怎么可能对妹妹下得了手？
[ZH] 天色渐渐暗下，月亮缓缓升起。申简辰坐在石头上，靠着神柳的树干，体会着自己内心最真实的声音，那声音告诉他，根本无法放下千里寻。
[ZH] 他在心中默默向神柳询问：“神柳，尼姑庵的法师告诉我随缘而不攀缘，我打算随了这缘分，明日踏上回汴梁之路，与梦容完婚，可我还是放不下千里寻。哪怕我与她都已成亲，我还是想得到她。你告诉我，我该怎么做？”
[ZH] 一阵风吹来，将树梢上的一条红绸吹落，从空中划过，落下时，轻轻擦过申简辰的面颊。他睁开眼，将那红绸捡起，在眼前展开，上面写着两个名字：楚临风，云清婉。一看名字便知是一对有才情的青年男女。
[ZH] 往下看，有两句诗：曾经沧海难为水，除却巫山不是云。
[ZH] 这两句诗申简辰再熟悉不过，出自唐代元稹的一首《离思》。诗句正好说到他的心里，难道神柳想用这两句诗告诉他必须坚持下去？
[ZH] 再往下看，还有一首绝句：
[ZH] 鸥鹭鸳鸯作一池，须知羽翼不相宜。东君不与花为主，何似休生连理枝？《愁怀》
[EN] Beneath the Divine Willow, Shen Jianchen sat still, replaying his meeting with Qianli Xun at Zuiyun Tower. He had laid his heart bare to her: even married to Mengrong, he would stay chaste for the woman he loved.
[ZH] 此诗令申简辰想起穿越前的一段经历。
[EN] Right or wrong in other people's eyes did not matter; what mattered was his own conscience. He had always seen Mengrong as a sister, and what kind of older brother could bring himself to lay hands on his own sister?
[ZH] 有一次按照约定，他带弟子去千里拳庄比试，结果发现孙恒向千里寻求婚。那人是个妥妥的富二代，比申简辰更有钱，还年轻帅气，交往过的女明星都有好几个。
[EN] Dusk settled and the moon climbed into the sky. Seated on the boulder with his back against the willow's trunk, Shen Jianchen listened to the truest voice inside him, and it said the same thing: he could not let Qianli Xun go.
[ZH] 孙恒让人在千里拳庄外用玫瑰花摆一个大大的红心，红心中间的红色盒子里放着一枚钻戒，看那大小，应该价值不菲。
[EN] In silence, he spoke to the tree. "Divine Willow, the nun up at the nunnery told me to follow fate instead of clinging to it. I'll follow this fate and set off for Bianliang tomorrow to marry Mengrong, yet I still can't let Qianli Xun go. Even with both of us married, I still want her. Tell me what I'm supposed to do."
[ZH] 孙恒对着千里拳庄内大喊：“千里寻！你今日不答应嫁给我！我就赖着不走！”
[EN] A gust of wind shook loose a red silk ribbon from the upper branches. It drifted through the air and brushed his cheek before landing at his feet. Opening his eyes, he picked it up and smoothed it out. Two names stood inked across the top: Chu Linfeng and Yun Qingwan—refined names that spoke of a cultured young couple.
[ZH] 孙恒带来的人跟着大喊：“嫁给他！嫁给他！……”
[EN] Below the names ran a couplet: "No water's wide enough when you have crossed the sea; / No cloud is beautiful but that which crowns the peak." [TN: Translated by Xu Yuanchong]
[ZH] 围观的人越来越多，大家都等着看热闹。
[EN] Shen Jianchen knew those famous lines by Tang poet Yuan Zhen from "Thinking of My Dear Departed." They cut straight to his own heart. Was the Divine Willow urging him not to give up?
[ZH] 申简辰想不通，什么时候千里寻招惹到这等有钱之人，反正他看到眼前的场面，是一点儿自信都没有。
[EN] Further down the ribbon sat a quatrain titled "Longings in Sorrow":
[ZH] 徒弟担心道：“师傅，有这么个人跟你竞争，恐怕你一点儿机会都没有。”
[EN] "Gulls and mandarin ducks swim in the same pond, / Everyone knows they don't belong together. / The Lord of the East has failed as master of flowers, / Why must we be intertwined branches all our lives?" [TN: Translated by Ronald Egan]
[ZH] 申简辰心里虽发怵，嘴上的功夫还是不能少：“孙恒除了有钱，哪一点能比得上我？千里寻连我都看不上，怎么可能看得上他？”
[EN] Those four lines carried Shen Jianchen straight back to a memory before his transmigration.
[ZH] 千里寻带着弟子们走出千里拳庄的大门，面对眼前的阵仗没有丝毫感动，露出不屑的眼神。
[EN] One day, he had brought his disciples to Qianli Fist Manor for a scheduled spar, only to walk in on Sun Heng proposing to Qianli Xun. Sun Heng was a textbook rich second-generation heir—wealthier than Shen Jianchen, young, handsome, and rumored to have dated several actresses.
[ZH] “孙恒，你是不是吃错药？老娘才见过你两面，就跑来求婚，你以为老娘是那种随随便便的人？被你用这场面一哄就答应嫁给你？”
[EN] Outside the manor gates, Sun Heng had arranged roses into a giant red heart with a crimson velvet box at the center holding a diamond ring whose size screamed astronomical price.
[ZH] 孙恒说：“正因为你不是随便的女孩子，我才会向你求婚。”
[EN] Sun Heng bellowed toward the doors, "Qianli Xun! If you don't agree to marry me today, I'm not budging!"
[ZH] 千里寻根本不会相信，为了让孙恒尽快离开，提出要求：“想让老娘答应你，要么跟老娘打一架，要么欣赏一首诗，你选哪一个？”
[EN] His entourage took up the chant: "Marry him! Marry him!"
[ZH] 孙恒说：“你那么厉害，我肯定打不过你，还是欣赏诗吧。”
[EN] Passersby crowded in to gawk. Shen Jianchen had no idea how Qianli Xun had crossed paths with a tycoon like this, and watching the spectacle drained every ounce of confidence from his chest.
[ZH] 千里寻的徒弟二杠拿出一张纸，念出朱淑真的《愁怀》：“鸥鹭鸳鸯作一池，须知羽翼不相宜。东君不与花为主，何似休生连理枝？”
[EN] A disciple fretted beside him. "Master, with competition like that, you don't stand a chance."
[ZH] 二杠一边念，孙恒一边用手机查询。
[EN] Nervous inside, Shen Jianchen kept his chin up. "Aside from money, how does Sun Heng beat me at anything? Qianli Xun doesn't even look twice at me. Why would she want him?"
[ZH] 当二杠念完时，孙恒也查到，大声说：“这首诗是宋代女词人朱淑真写的，不过是慨叹自己嫁错了人，我说得对不对？”
[EN] Qianli Xun strode out of Qianli Fist Manor with her students at her heels, unmoved by the roses and wearing a look of pure disdain. "Sun Heng, did you forget your meds? I've met you twice in my whole life and you show up proposing? You think I'm some cheap pushover who'll say yes just because you threw down a bunch of flowers?"
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“你说得没错，既然知道是什么意思，还不快把你摆的玫瑰花收了，再把钻戒拿走，还千里拳庄一个清静。”
[EN] "It's precisely because you aren't a pushover that I'm proposing," Sun Heng declared.
[ZH] 孙恒十分不解：“你刚不是说过，我选一个条件，你就答应我吗？”
[EN] Qianli Xun did not buy a word of it. Wanting him gone fast, she set her terms. "You want my yes? Either fight me a round, or appreciate a poem. Pick one."
[ZH] 申简辰已忍不住，从人群中走出，大声对孙恒说：“你怎么就不明白？千里寻的意思是你是鸥鹭，她是鸳鸯，你们在一个池子里待着，实在不相配。连管理春天的神仙都不想理会你摆放的这些玫瑰花，又怎么可能为你和千里寻牵线搭桥？人家用一首诗拒绝你，再明显不过，还是赶紧收拾收拾走人吧！”
[EN] "With your skills, I'd never beat you in a fight," Sun Heng admitted. "I'll take the poem."
[ZH] 孙恒被气坏，又不敢动手，这是千里拳庄，眼前的小子又是申拳的后代，他也打不过，只能命人拿了钻戒，再吩咐人上去将玫瑰花踩个稀巴烂，然后扬长而去。
[EN] Her disciple Er'gang pulled out a sheet of paper and recited Zhu Shuzhen's "Longings in Sorrow": "Gulls and mandarin ducks swim in the same pond, / Everyone knows they don't belong together. / The Lord of the East has failed as master of flowers, / Why must we be intertwined branches all our lives?"
[ZH] 孙恒走后，千里寻赞赏道：“你刚才的解释虽有些牵强，大体意思倒不错，谢谢你帮我把他赶走。”
[EN] While Er'gang read, Sun Heng looked it up on his phone. The moment Er'gang finished, Sun Heng announced, "That's by the Song poetess Zhu Shuzhen—a lament about marrying the wrong man. Am I right?"
[ZH] 千里寻第一次感谢他，申简辰终生难忘。也因这次发生的事，申简辰更觉出千里寻不一般，试想天底下有多少女孩子能抵挡住像孙恒那样身家过亿的富二代的诱惑。
[EN] Qianli Xun grinned. "Spot on. Now that you know what it means, sweep up your roses, pack up your ring, and give my dojo back some peace and quiet."
[ZH] 神柳下，申简辰手握红绸，自语道：“看来这对情侣与你我同病相怜，明明心有所属，偏偏不能与所爱之人在一起，只能寄希望于红绸，我还是帮他们将红绸绑好，希望神柳能帮帮他们。”
[EN] Sun Heng blinked in confusion. "Wait—didn't you say if I picked one condition, you'd say yes?"
[ZH] 申简辰站起来，挑一根柳枝将红绸绑好。估摸着苗知瑜和白梅若已离开，便沿着池塘岸边朝幽林别居的方向走去。
[EN] Unable to hold back a second longer, Shen Jianchen stepped out of the crowd and addressed him loud and clear. "Still don't get it? Qianli Xun is saying you're the gull and she's the mandarin duck—stuck in the same pond, completely mismatched. Even the Lord of the East, the god of spring, wants nothing to do with your roses, so why would he play matchmaker for you two? She just turned you down with a poem. Couldn't be clearer. Pack it up and leave."
[ZH] 白风跟上王爷，心里继续琢磨从汴梁回来时，给苦荞买些什么好玩的玩意儿。
[EN] Sun Heng fumed, yet dared not throw a punch. This was Qianli Fist Manor, and the man lecturing him was the heir to Shen Fist; he stood no chance in a brawl. He ordered his men to grab the ring, stomp the roses to pulp, and stormed off.
[ZH] ……
[EN] After Sun Heng left, Qianli Xun gave Shen Jianchen an approving nod. "Your explanation was a bit stretched, yet you got the gist right. Thanks for chasing him off."
[ZH] 天亮后，幽林别居赶出两辆马车离开临安，向汴梁方向行驶。
[EN] It was the first time Qianli Xun had ever thanked him, and he had never forgotten it. That day had also sealed his respect for her; how many women in the world could shrug off a billionaire heir like Sun Heng without blinking?
[ZH] 申简辰没有向临安官员透露出发的时间，他想默默回汴梁接受命运安排。心情本来就不好，又何必让那么多人见证？还是悄悄回去，自己舒坦也不给他人添麻烦。
[EN] Back beneath the Divine Willow, Shen Jianchen held the ribbon and murmured, "Looks like this couple shares our fate—hearts set on someone they can't be with, pinning their last hope on a strip of silk. Let me tie it back up for them. Maybe the Divine Willow will lend them a hand."
[ZH] 乔梦容的伤还未完痊愈，因心情好，容光焕发，看上去比昨日气色好许多。
[EN] He stood, knotted the ribbon onto a low willow branch, and, judging that Miao Zhiyu and Bai Meiruo must have left by now, walked back along the pond toward Youlin Retreat.
[ZH] 一大早，一辆马车也从陈府出发，青瓜与苦荞跟在马车外。
[EN] Bai Feng trailed behind, debating what fun trinkets to buy for Kuqiao in Bianliang.
[ZH] 千里寻第一次与陈浩宇同坐一辆马车，因不习惯，搞得浑身不自在。
[EN] At daybreak, two carriages rolled out of Youlin Retreat and left Lin'an on the road to Bianliang.
[ZH] 陈浩宇倒无所谓，一上马车便呼呼大睡。
[EN] Shen Jianchen had told none of the Lin'an officials his departure hour. He wanted to ride back to the capital without fanfare and meet his fate in peace. His mood was bleak enough without an audience; slipping out spared everyone the bother.
[ZH] 听着他的鼾声，千里寻更是自怜，也更能理解朱淑真，脑中蹦出四句诗来，随口念出：“鸥鹭鸳鸯作一池，须知羽翼不相宜。东君不与花为主，何似休生连理枝。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong's wound had not healed all the way, yet joy put fresh color in her cheeks, leaving her radiant compared with the day before.
[ZH] 这一念倒把陈浩宇吵醒，其实他是懒得跟朱淑真说话，装睡而已。
[EN] That same morning, a carriage departed Chen Manor with Qinggua and Kuqiao walking alongside.
[ZH] 陈浩宇睁开眼不满道：“能不能正常点儿？回个门，还要作诗？搅本公子的好梦！”
[EN] Riding in the same carriage with Chen Haoyu for the first time left Qianli Xun stiff and out of sorts. Chen Haoyu, by contrast, slumped back and started snoring the instant he climbed aboard.
[ZH] 千里寻不再继续自怜，产生了聊天儿的兴致：“喂，陈浩宇，你到底喜欢什么样的女子？”
[EN] Listening to his snores filled Qianli Xun with self-pity and sharper sympathy for Zhu Shuzhen. Four lines popped into her head and slipped off her tongue: "Gulls and mandarin ducks swim in the same pond, / Everyone knows they don't belong together. / The Lord of the East has failed as master of flowers, / Why must we be intertwined branches all our lives?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇一脸调侃：“怎么？想打听本公子的喜好往这方面培养？让本公子好喜欢上你？”
[EN] That roused Chen Haoyu, who had only been faking sleep to avoid talking to her. He cracked his eyes open with a scowl. "Can't you act normal? We're just making a return visit and you're reciting poetry? You ruined my nap!"
[ZH] 千里寻怼道：“培养你个头！能不能好好说话？”
[EN] Shaking off her gloom, Qianli Xun decided to chat instead. "Hey, Chen Haoyu, what sort of woman do you actually like?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇坐端，笑着说：“告诉你也无妨，本公子喜欢黄花大姑娘，像你这种和离过的得靠边站；还得长得漂亮，这一点你倒符合；更重要的是说话需娇滴滴的，听着才舒畅，还需温柔，像你这种说话又粗鲁，动不动使拳头的人，本公子根本瞧不上。”
[EN] He shot her a mocking grin. "What, fishing for my tastes so you can mold yourself to fit and make me fall for you?"
[ZH] 千里寻并不生气，谁让她也不喜欢眼前之人。
[EN] "Mold my foot! Can't you talk like a normal person?"
[ZH] “我作为正室允许你纳一两房妾室，那我就照着你的喜好帮你寻摸寻摸？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu sat up straight and grinned. "No harm telling you. I like untouched maidens, so divorced women like you can step to the back of the line. She has to be pretty—you pass on that count. Most important, she needs a sweet, melting voice and a gentle temper. Someone coarse like you who starts swinging fists at the drop of a hat doesn't even make my list."
[ZH] 千里寻之所以如此想，不过是想利用其他女子拴住陈浩宇，他不至于经常往外跑。陈家长辈见他经常待在家里，说不定不会再要求她帮着管教不成器的后辈。
[EN] Qianli Xun did not take offense; she liked him no better. "As your primary wife, I'm willing to let you take a concubine or two. Want me to scout around for someone matching your tastes?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇一脸狐疑：“你能有这么好心？该不会有什么阴谋？”
[EN] Her real aim was practical: tie Chen Haoyu down at home with women he liked so he would quit gallivanting outside, and maybe the Chen elders would stop nagging her to whip their wayward boy into shape.
[ZH] 千里寻一本正经：“能有什么阴谋？不过是为你好我好大家好而已。再说，我是你的正室，有义务和责任为你考虑，不如你给春金沫赎身，把她放在身边，省得总往春香楼跑，惹得他人非议。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu squinted at her. "You're being this generous? What's the catch?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇再次调侃道：“你跟别的女子真不一样，该不会是以前见惯林觉把商妓往家里带，再见我这样儿的，一对比，觉出我的好来，想跟我好好过日子？看来我得找机会好好感谢感谢林觉。”
[EN] "No catch," Qianli Xun said with a straight face. "Just looking out for all of us. As your primary wife, it's my duty to think of your comfort. Why not buy Chun Jinmo's freedom and bring her home? Beats running to Chunxiang Brothel and giving folks something to gossip about."
[ZH] 千里寻气道：“再胡说！小心我揍你！”
[EN] He smirked again. "You really aren't like other women. Did watching Lin Jue haul courtesans home lower your standards so much that I look like a prize by comparison, making you want a real marriage with me? Guess I owe Lin Jue a big thank-you."
[ZH] 陈浩宇得意道：“才说几句，就原形毕露。告诉你，本公子还没玩够，等我玩够了再考虑纳妾之事。况且我还没有喜欢到要纳春金沫为妾的地步，只不过无聊到春香楼找她玩玩儿而已。你还是管好你自己，少为我的事操心。”
[EN] Qianli Xun flared up. "Talk more trash and see if I don't deck you!"
[ZH] 听到这些话，千里寻很想将陈浩宇暴揍一顿，眼看快到朱府，还需忍着：“你就是个人渣！”
[EN] "A couple of sentences and the mask slips right off," Chen Haoyu gloated. "Let me tell you, I'm not done playing around. I'll think about concubines once I've had my fun. Besides, I don't like Chun Jinmo enough to take her as a concubine—she's just someone to pass time with when I'm bored. Mind your own business and quit fretting over mine."
[ZH] “人渣怎么了？你好，你是江南有名的女词人，还不是嫁给我这个人渣？哼！依我看你这辈子注定会嫁给爱逛青楼之人，认命吧，朱淑真！”陈浩宇斜靠在马车上，一副洋洋得意的样子。
[EN] Qianli Xun itched to thrash him on the spot, yet Zhu Manor was almost in sight, so she held back. "You're pure scum!"
[ZH] 千里寻成功被气道，因犹豫了一会儿，出拳慢了些，陈浩宇成功躲开。
[EN] "So what if I am? Look at you, the celebrated Poetess of Jiangnan, and you still ended up married to scum like me! Hmph. Seems to me you're fated to marry brothel-hoppers for life. Accept your fate, Zhu Shuzhen!" Chen Haoyu leaned back against the carriage wall, grinning ear to ear.
[ZH] “你要是敢打我，一会儿见到岳父大人，在他面前参你一本，让他用朱家的家法治你。”
[EN] That push did it. Qianli Xun threw a punch, yet a split second of hesitation slowed her arm, letting him duck clear.
[ZH] 千里寻收起拳头：“别以为老娘拿你没办法，今日回门，不想惹我娘难过，暂且饶你，等回到陈府，老娘有的是办法对付你。”
[EN] "Hit me and I'll tattle to my father-in-law the second we arrive," he crowed. "Let's see him bring out the Zhu family rules on you."
[ZH] 陈浩宇“哼”了一声，不敢再言语，再说已到陈府侧门外。
[EN] Qianli Xun pulled back her fist. "Don't think I have no way to handle you. Today's the return visit, and I won't upset my mother. You get a pass for now. Once we're back at Chen Manor, I've got plenty of ways to deal with you."
[ZH] 苦荞在外面说：“小姐，姑爷，到了，快下车吧。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu let out a huff and held his tongue as the carriage pulled up outside the side gate of Zhu Manor.
[ZH] 千里寻第一个走下马车，嫂嫂和哥哥前来迎接，千里寻迎上去：“哥哥，嫂嫂。”
[EN] Kuqiao called from outside, "Miss, Young Master, we're here. Please step down."
[ZH] 陈文心夸道：“淑真嫁到陈家就是跟以前不一样，气色看上去更好。”
[EN] Qianli Xun hopped out first, meeting her brother and sister-in-law waiting at the gate. "Brother, Sister-in-law."
[ZH] 陈浩宇也走下马车，听到姐姐的夸奖，故意说：“能不好吗？一路上净给我气受。”
[EN] "Married life in the Chen household agrees with you, Shuzhen," Chen Wenxin praised. "Your complexion looks even brighter."
[ZH] 陈文心上前拧住弟弟的耳朵：“再胡说，小心我把你的耳朵给拧掉，淑真知书达理，怎么可能给你气受？肯定是你老惹她生气，是也不是？”
[EN] Climbing down behind her, Chen Haoyu overheard his sister and chimed in, "Of course she looks great—she spent the whole ride driving me up the wall."
[ZH] 陈浩宇“哎吆”地叫着：“我真是命苦啊，娶个母老虎不说，还有个厉害的姐姐，姐夫，你也不帮帮我？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin marched right up and pinched her brother's ear. "Spout more nonsense and I'll twist this ear right off! Shuzhen is educated and refined; why would she give you grief? You must have been provoking her nonstop, am I right?"
[ZH] 嫂嫂帮忙收拾陈浩宇，千里寻甚是开心，站在一旁看热闹。
[EN] "Ow, ow, ow!" Chen Haoyu yelped. "What a miserable life—married to a tigress and stuck with a fierce older sister! Brother-in-law, aren't you going to save me?"
[ZH] 朱景元劝道：“好了文心，今日浩宇陪淑真回门，就别再拧他耳朵。”
[EN] Watching her sister-in-law handle Chen Haoyu gave Qianli Xun pure delight.
[ZH] 陈文心这才放下：“看在你姐夫的面子上，暂且饶你，今日你需好好表现，听到没？”
[EN] "All right, Wenxin," Zhu Jingyuan interceded. "Haoyu came along for Shuzhen's return visit today. Let go of his ear."
[ZH] “听到了。”陈浩宇乖乖答应。
[EN] Chen Wenxin released him. "For your brother-in-law's sake, I'll let you off this once. Behave yourself today, understand?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇听话的样子，令千里寻纳闷，嫂嫂拧耳朵的招数竟然比她的拳头还管用。
[EN] "Understood," Chen Haoyu answered like a scolded puppy.
[ZH] 新娘子回门也没多大意思，不过是拜见长辈，然后聊聊天儿，摆上宴席，让陈浩宇将朱家家族中所有长辈认认。
[EN] Seeing him turn meek amazed Qianli Xun. Her sister-in-law's ear-pinch worked better than her own fists.
[ZH] 陈浩宇倒没给陈家丢脸，一一行礼。
[EN] The return visit ran its usual dull course: paying respects to the elders, trading small talk, and sitting through a banquet where Chen Haoyu met every senior member of the Zhu clan. To his credit, he did not embarrass the Chen family, bowing properly to every last one.
[ZH] 宴席结束后，家族中的长辈散去，陈浩宇陪姐夫和岳父说话；千里寻则陪陈文心和向夫人拉家常。
[EN] After the feast dispersed, Chen Haoyu stayed in the front hall chatting with his father-in-law and brother-in-law, while Qianli Xun talked domestic matters with Chen Wenxin and Madam Xiang.
[ZH] 正聊着，府衙有官员前来拜见知府大人，两人在外厅说了几句，那位官员不便多打扰，很快离开。
[EN] Midway through, a prefectural official called on Prefect Zhu. They spoke in the outer hall for a few moments before the visitor excused himself.
[ZH] 朱晞颜走到里屋后，说道：“仪王今日一早回汴梁去了，给任何人都没打招呼，我本想着携众位官员相送，没承想连个机会都没有。”
[EN] Returning to the inner room, Zhu Xiyan remarked, "Prince Yi set off for Bianliang first thing this morning without telling a soul. I had planned to lead the city officials to see him off, yet we never got the chance."
[ZH] 陈浩宇想到自己答应把朱淑真还给仪王，担心他不会回来：“岳父大人，仪王以后还会不会再回临安？”
[EN] Remembering his promise to hand Zhu Shuzhen over to Prince Yi, Chen Haoyu worried the prince might not come back. "Father-in-law, will Prince Yi return to Lin'an?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜回道：“当然回，他只是回汴梁完婚而已。”
[EN] "Of course," Zhu Xiyan said. "He only went back to Bianliang to hold his wedding."
[ZH] 朱景元帮着解释：“你成亲那日，官家的圣旨正好到达，将仪王与佳和郡主的婚期提前，要求他们尽快回汴梁完婚。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan filled in the rest. "His Majesty's decree arrived on your wedding day, moving up the marriage between Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe and ordering them back to Bianliang at once."
[ZH] 陈浩宇慨叹，即使是王爷，婚姻也不能自主，谁让他有个皇帝老子。看来自己这样儿已经很不错。还好他有先见之明，与母老虎签下契约。
[EN] Chen Haoyu sighed to himself. Even an imperial prince could not choose his own bride with an emperor for a father. Compared with that, his own lot was not bad—especially since he had the foresight to sign a contract with his tigress.
[ZH] 又瞧瞧朱晞颜，一脸喜色。陈浩宇心想：“你瞎点鸳鸯谱，既害本公子，还害自己的女儿，有什么好高兴的？”
[EN] Glancing at Zhu Xiyan's beaming face, Chen Haoyu grumbled inside: You played matchmaker blind, ruining my life and your own daughter's—what's there to grin about?
[ZH] 只听朱晞颜说：“浩宇啊，回去告诉你父亲，等仪王从汴梁回来，我会携带临安官员为仪王举办一次新婚宴会，让你爹也准备一份厚礼前来参加。”
[EN] "Haoyu," Zhu Xiyan said, "tell your father when you get home that once Prince Yi returns from Bianliang, I will host a wedding banquet with all the officials of Lin'an. Have him prepare a generous gift and attend."
[ZH] 陈浩宇不能在心里继续埋怨，乖乖应声：“是。”
[EN] Choking back his private complaints, Chen Haoyu answered dutifully, "Yes, sir."

#CH 214
[ZH] 儿子携儿媳回门，陈江林则乘坐马车来到钱塘帮。
[ZH] 陈江林来访，面子还是要给的，毕竟他是陈家的老大，掌管陈家家业，能亲自登门拜访，定是有要事。
[ZH] 才两三天功夫，蔣爷身上的伤自然未痊愈，硬撑着坐到椅子上接见陈江林。
[ZH] 陈江林进入后，瞧见蔣爷身上伤势严重，心中窃喜，不知谁给打的，也算是帮陈家出口恶气。
[ZH] 心里如此想，嘴上却不能这么说，他不请自坐。
[EN] While his son and daughter-in-law paid their visit to Zhu Manor, Chen Jianglin rode by carriage to the Qiantang Gang.
[ZH] “蔣爷这是怎么了？谁吃了熊心豹子胆，把您打成这样？”
[EN] As the eldest Chen brother and head of the family fortune, Chen Jianglin warranted respect. For him to call in person meant urgent business.
[ZH] 蔣爷哪敢说是仪王：“不提也罢，人在河边走，哪有不湿鞋？我不能总得势，也得让别人发泄发泄。陈老爷可是稀客，平日都派你家大侄子跟我往来，今日怎么会亲自登门？”
[EN] Only two or three days had passed, so Master Jiang's wounds were nowhere near healed. Still, he propped himself up in a chair to receive his guest.
[ZH] “蔣爷做下什么事，难道不知？为何还问起我来？”
[EN] Stepping inside and seeing Master Jiang battered and bruised, Chen Jianglin felt a surge of quiet glee. Whoever had worked him over had done the Chen family a favor. Keeping his thoughts to himself, he took a seat without waiting to be asked.
[ZH] 蔣爷立刻明白是为赌博之事，笑道：“你可得感谢我，要不是我，陈浩宇能愿意娶个母老虎回去？”
[EN] "What happened to you, Master Jiang? Who had the gall to beat you into this state?"
[ZH] “浩宇再不愿意亲事，迟早得娶。我不明白蒋爷为何突然关心起陈家之事？还要用陈家的产业作为赌注？赢了又不要，让人匪夷所思。”
[EN] Master Jiang dared not breathe a word about Prince Yi. "Not worth mentioning. Walk by the river long enough and your shoes get wet. I can't hold the upper hand every day; got to let folks blow off steam now and then. You're a rare visitor, Master Chen. Usually your eldest nephew handles dealings with me. What brings you here in person today?"
[ZH] 蒋爷谨守江湖规矩，不会随意将真实原因告知陈江林。
[EN] "Do you need me to remind you what you pulled?"
[ZH] “不过是跟陈浩宇开个玩笑，谁让他在春香楼霸占我喜欢的人。我混江湖也明白一个道理，不是自己的不能随便沾手。你们陈家的产业我当然眼红，可我消受不起。只不过采取迂回手段，让您儿子赶紧成亲，好被母老虎管着，春金沫就是我的人。”
[EN] Catching on at once that this was about the wager, Master Jiang chuckled. "You ought to thank me. Without my nudge, would Chen Haoyu ever have agreed to marry that tigress?"
[ZH] 陈江林还是不信，总觉得另有隐情：“真没想到蒋爷还是一个情种，在您眼中，一个商妓竟然比陈家的财产还重要？”
[EN] "Reluctant or not, Haoyu was going to marry sooner or later. What puzzles me is why Master Jiang took such a sudden interest in my family's affairs—wagering the entire Chen fortune, winning, and then walking away without touching a coin. Makes no sense."
[ZH] 蒋爷不想再继续聊下去，担心说多，被陈江林看出什么来：“陈老爷与其前来质问我，不如好好管管你的儿子，都成亲的人，别整日往春香楼跑，省得被知府大人知道挨鞭子。”
[EN] True to underworld code, Master Jiang refused to spill the real story. "Just playing a joke on your boy for hogging the woman I wanted at Chunxiang Brothel. In my line of work, you learn not to reach for what isn't yours. Sure, the Chen fortune makes my mouth water, yet I'd choke trying to swallow it. I just took the roundabout path to push your son into marriage so his tigress could rein him in and leave Chun Jinmo to me."
[ZH] 陈江林听出对方有赶客的意思，他也不想跟蒋爷这种人多说。就目前情形来看，问不出什么，最后叮嘱道：“陈家能发展到今日，首富的地位屹立不倒，也不是谁想拿捏就能拿捏得了，奉劝蒋爷，往后在外面跟浩宇玩时，把握好尺度，否则我陈江林动用临安兵力灭了钱塘帮也不在话下。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin did not buy a word of it. "Who knew Master Jiang was such a romantic—valuing one courtesan above the Chen family's entire estate?"
[ZH] 陈江林说完，起身离去。
[EN] Wary of letting anything slip if the talk dragged on, Master Jiang shifted to the offensive. "Instead of interrogating me, Master Chen, you'd better rein in your son. He's a married man now. Tell him to stay out of Chunxiang Brothel before Prefect Zhu finds out and takes a whip to his hide."
[ZH] 蒋爷被陈江林最后说的话给气到，怎奈身上有伤，不方便随意发作，只吐出两个充满情绪的字：“不送！”
[EN] Hearing the dismissal in his tone, Chen Jianglin saw no point pushing further; he would pry nothing out of the man today. Before rising, he delivered a parting warning. "The Chen family did not reach the top of Lin'an's wealth by letting anyone push us around. Take a word of advice, Master Jiang: when you play around with Haoyu outside, know your limits. Push too far, and calling in the Lin'an garrison to wipe out the Qiantang Gang wouldn't cost me a sweat."
[ZH] 等陈江林走后，蒋爷奚落道：“横什么横！你们陈家表面看着光鲜艳丽，其实里面也不怎么好！”
[EN] With that, Chen Jianglin stood and walked out.
[ZH] 陈府内，陈江树的屋中，包氏与儿子陈浩东也在，三人将下人屏退，关上门窗，坐在桌旁悄悄密谈。
[EN] That parting threat stung Master Jiang's pride, yet his battered ribs kept him from jumping up to roar back. All he managed was a tight, venomous "Don't let the door hit you!"
[ZH] “我看浩宇娶的娘子不好惹，都敢跟老太太顶嘴，我和弟妹到老太太跟前一阵蛊惑，老太太终于发怒，前去闹事，找出她三个错处，结果被她说得一点儿错处都没有，白白闹一回。”
[EN] Once Chen Jianglin was gone, Master Jiang spat in disgust. "Act all high and mighty! Your Chen household looks shiny on the outside, yet it's rotten enough on the inside!"
[ZH] “厉害就好，儿子盼着浩宇赶紧吃厉害娘子的亏，我也好从梦中笑醒。”
[EN] Back at Chen Manor, inside Chen Jiangshu's quarters, Madam Bao and Chen Haodong had dismissed every servant and shut the doors and windows for a private huddle around the table.
[ZH] “老太太倒是让她负责管教浩宇，娘担心，有这么个厉害的媳妇管教，恐怕浩宇会好起来，影响到我儿的前程。”
[EN] "That bride Haoyu married is no pushover," Madam Bao said. "She even talks back to Old Madam Xia! Third Sister-in-law and I stirred the old madam up until she marched over to make trouble, armed with three solid grievances—and that girl talked her way out of every single one. Wasted the whole trip."
[ZH] “娘多虑，浩宇那样儿真的变好，也比不上儿子，我可是从十岁起就学着做生意，他十岁时不过是上树掏鸟窝，下河捉螃蟹，没事欺负欺负姑娘家，哪能跟我比？”
[EN] "The tougher she is, the better," Chen Haodong said. "I can't wait to see Haoyu suffer under a fierce wife. I'll wake up laughing from my dreams."
[ZH] 陈江树终于忍不住：“他自然比不上浩东，做生意除了积累人脉，就是积累经验，浩宇即使变好，什么都没积累，又怎能与浩东比？把心放在肚子里。”
[EN] "Old Madam Xia put her in charge of disciplining Haoyu, though," Madam Bao worried. "What if a wife that tough actually whips him into shape and threatens your future?"
[ZH] “说得有道理。”父子两个都如此说，包氏自然欢喜。转念一想，又觉不对，“既然我儿如此优秀，又何必担心浩宇继承家业？还费尽心思利用蒋爷唱那出戏？”
[EN] "You're worrying over nothing, Mother. Even if Haoyu turns into a saint, he can't hold a candle to me. I started learning business at ten. At ten, he was climbing trees for bird nests, catching crabs in streams, and teasing neighborhood girls. How could he compare?"
[ZH] 陈江树帮儿子解释：“妇道人家就是想得不够长远不够全面，听我好好给你解释。浩东的能力和经验自然比浩宇强，可要继承家业，老太太那一票至关重要。她眼中只有浩宇，浩东什么时候入过她的法眼？为保险一些，才想办法让浩宇快些娶朱家名声不好的姑娘……”
[EN] Chen Jiangshu chimed in. "Of course he can't match Haodong. Business takes years of building contacts and judgment. Even if Haoyu reforms, he starts from zero. Put your mind at ease."
[ZH] 陈浩东忍不住打断父亲：“娘，您不知道，陈家选择继承人时，其中有一条规定娶的正室必须家世清白，贤良淑德，品行端正。这一条朱家姑娘只符合家世清白，而贤良淑德，品行端正实在与她不匹配。族中除奶奶以外其他长辈对此十分看重，所以儿子才费尽心思促成浩宇的婚事。”
[EN] "True enough." Reassured by father and son, Madam Bao brightened—then paused. "Wait. If my son is that far ahead, why worry about Haoyu inheriting the estate at all? Why go through all that trouble setting up that play with Master Jiang?"
[ZH] “原来如此！甚好！甚好！你们父子在外筹谋，我在这后宅见机行事，一切都是为我儿子。”包氏这才明白，接着又叮嘱道，“这些事可要瞒着安雅，她平常跟大嫂走得近，我看她也挺喜欢朱家姑娘，敬茶那日，你没见她对待朱家姑娘那个亲热劲儿。”
[EN] Chen Jiangshu answered with mild condescension. "Women never think far enough ahead. Listen: Haodong beats Haoyu in skill and experience, yet when it comes time to pick an heir, Old Madam Xia's vote carries huge weight. She only has eyes for Haoyu; when has she ever looked twice at Haodong? To be safe, we had to get Haoyu tied down fast to a Zhu daughter with a ruined name..."
[ZH] “娘放心，我有分寸。”陈浩东又担忧道，“听说浩宇跟朱家姑娘签下婚前契约，其中有一条：婚后互不干涉，儿子担心朱家姑娘婚后不管浩宇，任由他四处浪荡，儿子精心设计，一半儿功夫会前功尽弃。”
[EN] Chen Haodong cut in. "Mother, you don't know—one of the Chen clan's rules for choosing the family heir is that his primary wife must come from an upright family, possess virtuous character, and hold an untarnished name. The Zhu girl only passes the first test. Virtuous and untarnished? Not a chance. Every clan elder aside from Grandmother takes that rule to heart. That's why I went to such lengths to push Haoyu's wedding through."
[ZH] 陈江树说：“不会，我们有杀手锏，实在不行拿出来，到时不怕浩宇不吃亏。”
[EN] "So that's why! Brilliant, brilliant! You two scheme outside, and I'll watch for openings inside the house—all for my son." Madam Bao nodded, then added a caution. "Keep every word of this from Anya. She's close with First Sister-in-law, and she seems quite fond of the Zhu girl too. Did you see how warm she was toward her at the tea ceremony?"
[ZH] 陈浩东反应了一会儿，立刻醒悟：“父亲说得对，儿子竟然把这么重要的事情忘记，实在不行时，抖搂出来，估计朱家姑娘气得杀了浩宇的心思都有。”
[EN] "Don't worry, Mother, I know my limits," Chen Haodong said, before a frown returned. "Word has it Haoyu signed a prenuptial contract with the Zhu girl saying they won't meddle in each other's lives. I'm worried she'll leave him alone to keep playing around, wasting half my hard work."
[ZH] 包氏不明所以：“你们父子在跟我打哑谜吗？到底是什么？”
[EN] "Not a chance," Chen Jiangshu said. "We still have a trump card. If push comes to shove, we play it, and Haoyu won't escape getting burned."
[ZH] 陈浩东在包氏耳边悄悄说了一回，包氏也笑道：“原来是浩宇干的呀！他可真行，不仅给自己招个名声不好的媳妇回来，到头来还要遭罪，真是满盘皆输。”
[EN] Chen Haodong paused a beat before lighting up. "You're right, Father! How did I forget something that huge? When the time comes, we spill it, and the Zhu girl will be mad enough to murder Haoyu on the spot."
[ZH] 陈浩东想起财产一事：“想到浩宇名下的财产被朱家姑娘霸占，我着实不爽，等我当家那天，定要把她名下的财产夺回来！”
[EN] Madam Bao blinked. "Talking in riddles right in front of me? What is it?"
[ZH] 陈江树并不在乎：“那些财产跟整个陈家的家业比起来不过是九牛一毛，不必当回事。”
[EN] Chen Haodong leaned over and whispered in her ear. Madam Bao burst into laughter. "So Haoyu was behind that! What a piece of work—not only did he saddle himself with a disgraced bride, he's going to pay for it too. Total disaster for him."
[ZH] 陈浩东回道：“父亲说得在理。”
[EN] A sour thought crossed Chen Haodong's mind. "Thinking of Haoyu's personal properties sitting in the Zhu girl's hands still irks me. The day I take over the family, I'm taking every last one back."
[ZH] 陈江林从钱塘帮回到陈府，将情况向姚轻琰说了一遍。
[EN] Chen Jiangshu waved it off. "Compared with the entire Chen fortune, those holdings are a drop in the bucket. Not worth sweating over."
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“如此看来，字据应该已给浩宇，陈家的产业倒没事，可我难以相信蒋爷会为一个商妓不要陈家的产业？”
[EN] "You're right, Father."
[ZH] 陈江林说：“我也不信，其中定有隐情。蒋爷不愿说，也不能逼迫，只要不会威胁到浩宇和陈家，此事也就作罢。”
[EN] Returning from the Qiantang Gang, Chen Jianglin reported everything to Yao Qingyan.
[ZH] 姚轻琰点点头：“也对，陈家虽然不能得罪黑帮，但也没必要跟这帮人多往来，就此罢手最好不过。你赶紧去给老太太回一声。”
[EN] "So Haoyu got the promissory note back, and the Chen estates are safe," Yao Qingyan mused. "Still, I find it hard to believe Master Jiang gave up the Chen fortune over a courtesan."
[ZH] “你不与我一同去？”
[EN] "I don't buy it either," Chen Jianglin agreed. "There's more to it. Since he won't talk and we can't force him, as long as Haoyu and the family aren't in danger, let's drop it."
[ZH] “只怕老太太见我跟你在一起会生气，我还是不去了。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan nodded. "Fair enough. We shouldn't make enemies of underworld gangs, nor should we get tangled up with them. Better to leave it there. Go report to Old Madam Xia now."
[ZH] 陈江林只好一个人来到老太太的院子。
[EN] "Aren't you coming with me?"
[ZH] 天气越来越暖和，老人家仍旧是喜欢晒太阳，坐在院子里看几个丫鬟绣花。见儿子进来，脸上的笑容立刻消失，瞅瞅儿子身边没有带儿媳，故意问：“你不是跟她形影不离吗？怎么一个人过来？”
[EN] "I'm afraid seeing us walk in together will only set her off. Better I stay behind."
[ZH] 陈江林老实回道：“本来轻琰要来，怕您生气，就没跟着。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin went alone to his mother's courtyard.
[ZH] 老太太一听，更加生气：“你们两个平时嘴上说孝顺，心里不知道把我想成什么人？说什么怕我生气没跟着，我看她就是找借口不愿踏进我的院子！”
[EN] With the spring weather turning warm, the old lady still loved soaking up the sun, sitting in the yard watching maids embroider. The second her eldest son stepped in, her smile vanished. Noticing he was alone, she baited him. "Aren't you glued to her hip? Why did you come by yourself?"
[ZH] 老太太一生气，丫鬟们不敢再绣花，放下手中的活计，忙安慰老太太。
[EN] "Qingyan meant to come, Mother," Chen Jianglin answered straight. "She stayed back for fear of upsetting you."
[ZH] “母亲，您别一见我就生气，搞得我都不敢来您的院子。”
[EN] That only fanned Old Madam Xia's temper. "Both of you talk a big game about filial piety, yet heaven knows what kind of monster you paint me as in your heads! Afraid of upsetting me? Sounds to me like she's making excuses to stay out of my courtyard!"
[ZH] 老太太叹口气：“唉，只怪我光教你做生意，没教你如何处理家事，如何管教孩子。”
[EN] At the old madam's outburst, the maids set aside their needles and rushed to soothe her.
[ZH] “怎么能怪母亲？都是儿子不好。”
[EN] "Mother, please don't get angry the moment you lay eyes on me," Chen Jianglin pleaded. "You make me afraid to set foot in here."
[ZH] 老太太不想继续计较：“你过来有何事？”
[EN] Old Madam Xia let out a heavy sigh. "My own fault for teaching you nothing but business, and never teaching you how to run a household or raise a child."
[ZH] 陈江林这才说起正事：“一早儿子到钱塘帮找蒋爷，一番了解下来，确定浩宇和陈家家产都无事，警告过蒋爷之后，便赶回来向母亲汇报。”
[EN] "How could it be your fault, Mother? I'm the one at fault."
[ZH] “你该不会是先向你媳妇汇报后才来向我汇报？”
[EN] She waved the subject away. "What brought you here?"
[ZH] 陈江林担心母亲生气，哄道：“儿子当然是先向母亲汇报。”
[EN] Only then did Chen Jianglin state his business. "I went to the Qiantang Gang this morning to see Master Jiang. After talking with him, I confirmed Haoyu and the family estates are safe. I gave him a stern warning and came straight back to report to you."
[ZH] 夏老太太听后，更生气，将拐杖在地上敲敲：“你的谎话张嘴就来！刚才还说你媳妇本来要来，怕惹我生气就不来，这会儿又说你先到我这里。你以为我老了，脑子不中用？怎么编瞎话我都会当真？哼！”
[EN] "You didn't stop by your wife's room to tell her first?"
[ZH] 陈江林这才意识到自己说的话前后矛盾，立刻跪下：“母亲，儿子不是想骗您，是怕您生气，结果越担心，反而越容易出错，惹得您更生气，您要打要骂都行，儿子绝无怨言。”
[EN] Anxious not to provoke her further, Chen Jianglin fibbed, "Of course I came to you first, Mother."
[ZH] 老太太又将拐杖在地上敲两下：“我最讨厌你这种态度，看似态度又好，又乖，又孝顺，其实是逼我！你什么时候才能懂我想要什么？”
[EN] Old Madam Xia thumped her cane against the paving stones. "Lies roll right off your tongue! One second ago you said your wife meant to come with you and stayed back to avoid upsetting me, and now you claim you came straight to me! Think I've gone senile and will swallow any fib you spin? Hmph!"
[ZH] 陈江林更加不知所以：“娘想要什么，尽管对儿子讲，儿子无不满足。”
[EN] Catching his own slip, Chen Jianglin dropped to his knees. "Mother, I didn't mean to lie. I was so scared of making you angry that I tripped over my own words and made it worse. Beat me or scold me all you want—I won't breathe a word of complaint."
[ZH] 老太太气道：“我让你以后不要老围着你的媳妇转，你能做到吗？”
[EN] Old Madam Xia rapped her cane twice more. "That's the exact attitude I can't stand—acting so polite, docile, and dutiful that you box me in! When will you ever understand what I actually want?"
[ZH] 陈江林跪着一言不发。
[EN] Chen Jianglin stared up in bewilderment. "Tell me whatever you want, Mother, and I will fulfill it without fail."
[ZH] 老太太又叹口气：“唉，你什么都做不到，还让我说什么？你要是真孝顺，不用我说，就知道我想要什么。行了，我也懒得继续跟你生气，赶紧说完正事离开，省得我看着你生气。”
[EN] "Stop orbiting around your wife all day!" she snapped. "Can you do that?"
[ZH] 陈江林跪着说：“儿子总觉得蒋爷跟浩宇赌博另有隐情，蒋爷不说，儿子也不好继续追问，想着既然浩宇和陈家无事，其他的我们也不必计较。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin knelt in silence.
[ZH] 老太太的情绪稍微平静些：“肯定有隐情，傻子都能听出来，至于隐情是什么，你多留个心眼儿，说不定哪一日就浮出水面。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia sighed again. "You can't do a single thing I ask, so why waste my breath? If you were half as filial as you claim, I wouldn't have to spell it out. Forget it, I'm tired of losing my temper with you. Finish your report and get out of my sight."
[ZH] “是，儿子谨遵母亲吩咐。”陈江林继续跪着。
[EN] Still kneeling, Chen Jianglin spoke. "I suspect there is a hidden story behind Master Jiang's gamble with Haoyu. He refused to talk, and I had no way to press him. Since Haoyu and the estates are safe, I figured we should leave the rest alone."
[ZH] 老太太没好气地说：“起来吧，赶紧离开！”
[EN] Her temper cooled a notch. "Any fool can tell there's more behind it. Keep your eyes peeled; the truth will float to the surface sooner or later."
[ZH] 陈江林便起身行礼离开。他自认为没有做错任何事，只是不清楚母亲为何总是见到他和轻琰就生气。在别人看来母亲是嫌弃他对轻琰好，他觉得并非如此。
[EN] "Yes, Mother. I will keep watch." He stayed on his knees.
[ZH] 他们兄弟三人娶媳妇时，母亲都会叮嘱要对媳妇好，还说男人只有对自己女人好，才能守住家财。
[EN] "Get up and get out!" she barked.
[ZH] 陈家三兄弟再不济，在临安城对老婆好可是出了名的，尤其是他陈江林，简直是将老婆捧在手心里。按说也是照母亲的意思做，她没有必要为此生气。
[EN] Chen Jianglin rose, bowed, and took his leave. He could not see what he had done wrong, nor why the sight of him and Qingyan always grated on his mother. Outsiders assumed she resented him doting on his wife, yet he knew that could not be it. When each of the three Chen brothers married, their mother had lectured them to treat their wives well, insisting a man had to cherish his wife to hold onto family wealth. All three Chen brothers were famous across Lin'an for spoiling their wives, Chen Jianglin most of all. He had followed her own rule to the letter; why would she resent him for it?
[ZH] 到底因为什么？陈江林实在想不通，生意上的事她忙起来头头是道，家事总是理不清楚。他自认为自己比两个弟弟做得要好，就是不明白为何母亲总是生他的气，连带着也牵连到轻琰。
[EN] Try as he might, he could not puzzle it out. In business he kept every ledger crystal clear, yet family affairs left him lost in a fog. He did better than both his younger brothers, so why did his mother always vent her ire on him and drag Qingyan into it too?
[ZH] 话说新媳妇回门是不能在娘家过夜，千里寻对这个规矩十分不满，可一切拗不过习俗在古人心中的地位，只好跟着陈浩宇打道回府。
[EN] Because custom forbade a new bride from spending the night at her maiden home on the return visit—a rule that annoyed Qianli Xun to no end, though ancient tradition left no room to argue—she rode back to Chen Manor with Chen Haoyu before dark.
[ZH] 他们乘坐马车来到陈府大门外时，春香楼的妈妈、三个打手，还有疤子等小弟站在外面等候。
[EN] As their carriage halted outside the main gates of Chen Manor, a waiting party loomed ahead: the madam of Chunxiang Brothel, the three hired fighters, and Scarface with his pack of street cronies.
[ZH] 当陈浩宇从马车上下来时，他们全都围了过来……
[EN] The moment Chen Haoyu stepped down from the carriage, the whole lot swarmed around him.

#CH 215
[ZH] 陈浩宇见这阵仗，站住问道：“你们跑来做什么？”
[ZH] 春香楼的妈妈嘴快，第一个开口说话：“陈公子，听说您现在娶了厉害的夫人，钱财被管着，以后用钱不得自由，我们都担心您不给银子，所以亲自过来拿。”
[ZH] 三位高手中一位说：“陈公子以前都是先给银子再说事，昨日银子还没给，我们三个就帮您打了一架，您瞧，脸上的伤还在，今日是不是该给银子了？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇摸摸身上，一两银子都没有，掩饰道：“本公子近日比较忙，还没来得及到铺子里支取银两，等我今日忙完，自然会亲自支取，你们放心回去吧。”
[ZH] 疤子已从陈浩东那里得到消息，凑近说：“陈公子，听说您名下的产业已归少夫人，恐怕以后您不能再支取银子……”
[ZH] 陈浩宇瞧见疤子等人也跟着来凑热闹，生气道：“你们几个跑来做什么？不是说好跑路费免了吗？”
[EN] Confronted by the crowd, Chen Haoyu stopped in his tracks. "What are you all doing here?"
[ZH] 疤子嘻嘻笑道：“我们几个不是来要银子，是想看陈公子今日能不能继续带我们几个玩儿？”
[EN] Quickest with her tongue, the madam of Chunxiang Brothel stepped up first. "Young Master Chen, word got around that your fierce new wife has locked up your money and cut off your spending. We worried you wouldn't pay up, so we came to collect in person."
[ZH] 陈浩宇第一次被人堵在门口要钱，气不打一处来：“你们几个滚得越远越好！今日本公子没心情带你们玩儿！”
[EN] One of the three hired fighters spoke next. "You always paid upfront before, Young Master Chen. Yesterday we fought a brawl for you without seeing a coin—look, our faces are still bruised. Isn't it time to settle?"
[ZH] 千里寻早已从马车上下来，站在一旁看热闹，越看越来气，陈浩宇整日在外面结交的都是些什么人！
[EN] Chen Haoyu patted his empty sleeves and tried to bluff. "I've been swamped these past few days and haven't had time to draw silver from the shops. Once I finish my errands today, I'll fetch it myself. Go home and stop worrying."
[ZH] 转念一想，跟她也没什么关系，反正大家互不干涉，便带着苦荞向府内走去。
[EN] Already tipped off by Chen Haodong, Scarface sidled closer. "Young Master Chen, rumor says every property under your name now belongs to Young Madam. Seems you can't draw silver anymore..."
[ZH] 春香楼的妈妈算是瞧出来，陈公子今日拿不出银子。既然疤子说陈公子的财产都归少夫人，不如直接问她要。见这位少夫人要走，先用话拦住：“少夫人慢着！”
[EN] Spotting Scarface and his crew joining the circus set Chen Haoyu's blood boiling. "What are you lot doing here? Didn't we call it even on your errand money?"
[ZH] 千里寻站住，回过头：“唤我何事？”
[EN] Scarface grinned. "We're not here for silver, Young Master. Just wondering if you're taking us out for fun today."
[ZH] 春香楼的妈妈说：“少夫人一看就不是那种撇下夫君不管的人，再说昨日是您派人跟他们三个打架，砸坏春香楼不少东西，损失可不小，怎么着您也得赔我银子。”
[EN] Being cornered at his own front gate over unpaid bills pushed Chen Haoyu past his limit. "Scram as far as your legs can carry you! I'm in no mood to hang out today!"
[ZH] 千里寻瞅瞅三位高手，身上都有伤：“昨日我派去的人是跟你们三个打起来的？”
[EN] Already down from the carriage, Qianli Xun watched the spectacle from the side, growing disgusted at the lowlifes Chen Haoyu kept for company. Still, none of this concerned her—they had a non-interference pact. Turning on her heel, she started through the gates with Kuqiao.
[ZH] 一位高手回道：“就是跟我们三个，您瞧瞧伤还在。”
[EN] Seeing Chen Haoyu had no silver on him—and hearing Scarface mention that the new wife held his assets—the brothel madam pivoted fast. "Hold on, Young Madam!"
[ZH] 千里寻向春香楼的妈妈质问道：“既然他们也参与打架，东西被砸坏，你也应问他们要银子，为何只问我要？”
[EN] Qianli Xun halted and turned back. "You calling me?"
[ZH] 春香楼妈妈解释道：“是陈公子满口应下全部由他负责。”
[EN] "Anyone can see you're not the type to leave your husband hanging, Young Madam," the madam wheedled. "Besides, your servants started that brawl with these three men yesterday and smashed up half of Chunxiang Brothel. The damage wasn't cheap. You ought to compensate me."
[ZH] “既是他说的话，你只管问他要，何必前来与我搭话？”千里寻将春香楼的妈妈怼得哑口无言。见对方不言语，心中舒服一些，“既然我派去的人参与打架，你的损失我承担一半，现在算算，一共损失多少银两。”
[EN] Qianli Xun eyed the three bruised fighters. "My servants fought you three yesterday?"
[ZH] 春香楼的妈妈立刻露出笑容，能捞回多少便捞回多少：“还是少夫人大方，我已算过，共二十五两银子，您看……”
[EN] "Right here," one fighter confirmed. "Look at the marks still on us."
[ZH] 千里寻很干脆：“我给你十五两，剩下的十两你问他们三个要。”
[EN] Qianli Xun turned back to the madam. "If they joined the brawl and broke your furniture, shouldn't you collect from them too? Why come only to me?"
[ZH] 春香楼的妈妈高兴地说：“谢过少夫人。不过陈公子在我们春香楼一直跟春金沫在一起，春香楼的姑娘不能白白陪这些公子哥，算起来陈公子待了一天一晚上，共十两银子……”
[EN] "Young Master Chen promised out loud he'd cover every copper himself," the madam explained.
[ZH] 千里寻很生气，更觉自己嫁人不淑，怼道：“又不是老娘叫商妓！你给我说这些干什么？”
[EN] "If he promised it, go collect from him. Why waste breath on me?" That shut the madam up. Satisfied, Qianli Xun added, "Since my people took part in the fight, I'll pay half the damages. Tally it up. How much silver?"
[ZH] 怼完不想再理会，准备继续回府，省得看着这些人心烦。
[EN] The madam broke into a bright smile, glad to salvage what she could. "How generous of you, Young Madam! I tallied it up—twenty-five taels of silver in total. What do you say?"
[ZH] 结果被陈浩宇给堵住，小声对她说：“帮个忙，先帮我把他们打发走。”
[EN] Qianli Xun did not blink. "I'll give you fifteen taels. Collect the other ten from these three."
[ZH] 千里寻压低嗓音：“自己欠的钱，自个儿解决。”说完又往里走。
[EN] "Many thanks, Young Madam!" The madam pushed her luck. "One more thing: Young Master Chen spent a full day and night in Chun Jinmo's room. Our girls can't entertain young lords for free, so that comes to another ten taels of silver..."
[ZH] 陈浩宇又堵住，平复心情之后，继续压低嗓音：“要不我提前支取月钱？”
[EN] Qianli Xun bristled, reminded once again what a dud she had married. "Was I the one hiring courtesans? Why are you telling me?"
[ZH] 千里寻觉得这个主意倒不错：“你答应给他们三个多少银子？”
[EN] Done with them, she turned to walk inside.
[ZH] “一人十两。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu stepped into her path, dropping his voice. "Do me a favor and help me send them packing."
[ZH] “十两？你以为陈家的银子都是风刮来的吗？”
[EN] "Pay your own debts," she murmured back, sidestepping him.
[ZH] “十两都算少的，我给陆远峰五十两。”
[EN] He blocked her again, swallowing his pride to whisper, "What if I take an advance on my monthly allowance?"
[ZH] 千里寻伸出拳头，想要打过去，到半空又收回来，毕竟这么多人，怎么说也是她名义上的夫君，还是给些面子。再说这些人堵在陈家大门口也不是个事儿：“三十两加上刚才的十五两，共四十五两……”
[EN] That sounded workable. "How much did you promise those three?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇提醒道：“还有叫商妓的十两……”
[EN] "Ten taels each."
[ZH] 千里寻的拳头想再次伸出去，还是忍住：“五十五两，全部从你的月钱里扣。”
[EN] "Ten taels? Think Chen silver blows in on the wind?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇小声讨价还价：“十五两是你的人惹下的事儿，不能赖在我头上。”
[EN] "Ten taels is cheap! I used to pay Lu Yuanfeng fifty!"
[ZH] “你要是跟我讨价还价，我可什么都不管。”
[EN] Qianli Xun raised a fist, then checked herself midair. With half the street watching, he was still her husband on paper; no sense humiliating him further or leaving creditors camped outside the Chen gates. "Thirty taels plus the fifteen makes forty-five..."
[ZH] “好好好，你厉害，你想怎样就怎样。”
[EN] "Plus ten for the courtesan," Chen Haoyu muttered.
[ZH] 陈浩宇更加后悔为取回字据，什么条件都答应朱淑真，搞得他的生活一下子从天上掉到地下，处处受限制，得赶紧想想办法。
[EN] Her fist twitched again before she forced it down. "Fifty-five taels, every single copper docked from your allowance."
[ZH] 千里寻吩咐苦荞回去取来五十五两银子，分别给了三位高手和春香楼的妈妈。
[EN] "Hey," he haggled under his breath, "fifteen of those taels were your servants' doing. You can't pin that on me."
[ZH] 然后向众人叮嘱道：“往后陈浩宇再欠银子，我可一概不管。”说完甩下陈浩宇带着苦荞回屋去。
[EN] "Keep haggling and I walk inside right now."
[ZH] 春香楼的妈妈来到三位高手身边，伸出手：“三位爷，你们刚得了银子，赶紧把春香楼的损失给补上，共十两。”
[EN] "Fine, fine, you win! Have it your way."
[ZH] 三人也知春香楼不好惹，每个人很不情愿地拿出些银两，放进春香楼妈妈的手中，刚好凑够十两，春香楼的妈妈才满意地离开。
[EN] Chen Haoyu cursed himself all over again for signing away everything just to get that gambling slip back. Overnight his life had plunged from heaven to mud, boxed in on every side. He needed a way out, fast.
[ZH] 陈浩宇叫住三位高手：“你们三个拿了银子，以后我出去还得跟着。”
[EN] Qianli Xun sent Kuqiao inside for fifty-five taels of silver and paid off the three fighters and the madam on the spot.
[ZH] 一位高手说：“春香楼的损失本来应该陈公子赔，却让我们一人损失三两多，您那十两银子买我们一个月，现在只能给您算半个月。”
[EN] "Listen well, all of you," she announced before leaving. "From today on, any debt Chen Haoyu runs up is none of my concern." Leaving him standing there, she marched inside with Kuqiao.
[ZH] 陈浩宇满不在乎：“半个月就半个月，只要半个月内本公子出去，你们必须随叫随到。”
[EN] The madam wasted no time stepping up to the three fighters with an open palm. "Now that you've been paid, gentlemen, cough up the ten taels for Chunxiang Brothel's damages."
[ZH] 一位高手问：“今日可用跟着您？”
[EN] Knowing the brothel was not to be crossed, the three begrudgingly dug out silver until ten taels sat in her hand, sending her off satisfied.
[ZH] 陈浩宇身上没银子，还需回府解决些事情：“今日免了，本公子跟着夫人回门有些累，等休息好再找你们。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu called after the three fighters. "You took my silver, so you're still guarding me whenever I go out."
[ZH] 三位高手齐声说完“明儿见”，也都离开。
[EN] "We lost over three taels apiece paying for a mess you should've covered," one fighter countered. "Your ten taels bought a month before; now you get half a month."
[ZH] 疤子等人一直躲在一旁观察，他们主要是想判断以后跟着陈浩宇还能不能捞到好处。就目前情形看，陈公子的钱财的确被限制，可瘦死的骆驼比马大，说不定还有机会。
[EN] "Fine, half a month," Chen Haoyu waved it off. "Just show up on call whenever I head out."
[ZH] 几人见陈浩宇要走，立刻围上去：“陈公子，我们出去玩儿吧？”
[EN] "Need us today?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇没好气地说：“刚才没听到吗？本公子今日累了，不想出去。”
[EN] Empty-pocketed and needing to sort things out indoors, Chen Haoyu shook his head. "Not today. That visit with my wife wore me out. I'll summon you once I've rested."
[ZH] 疤子说：“我可是第一次听陈公子说累了不想出去的话，您该不会是身上没银子，不敢带哥几个出去？”
[EN] With a chorus of "See you tomorrow," the three bodyguards left.
[ZH] 被疤子说中，得尽快挽回面子：“本公子像没银子花的人吗？你们几个在大门口等着，待会儿本公子出来带你们玩！”
[EN] Scarface and his crew had lurked nearby sizing up the situation, wondering if Chen Haoyu was still worth leeching off. His funds were tight, sure, yet a starved camel still outweighed a horse. They hurried over before he could slip inside. "Going out for fun, Young Master Chen?"
[ZH] 几人高兴道：“就等陈公子这句话。”
[EN] "Didn't you hear me? I'm tired today. Not going out."
[ZH] 疤子等人在陈府大门口外找地方待着耐心等待，陈浩宇则回府想办法搞些银子。
[EN] "First time I've ever heard Young Master Chen say he's too tired to party," Scarface baited him. "Don't tell us you're broke and scared to treat the boys?"
[ZH] 他决定先去兴隆阁找方管事，以往从铺子里支取银子都是通过方管事，不如去碰碰运气。
[EN] Stung where it hurt, Chen Haoyu puffed out his chest. "Do I look like a man short of silver? Wait right outside the gate. I'll be out in a minute to take you all out!"
[ZH] 青瓜一直跟着陈浩宇，刚才忙着还银子，陈浩宇并未多留意，此时注意到，总觉青瓜显得十分多余。
[EN] "That's what we like to hear!" the hoodlums cheered.
[ZH] 陈浩宇一脚踢过去：“还跟着本公子干吗？赶紧滚！”
[EN] Leaving Scarface's gang lounging outside the gates, Chen Haoyu hurried back inside to scare up some cash. His first stop would be Steward Fang at Xinglong Pavilion, where he had always drawn silver from his shops.
[ZH] 青瓜躲闪开来，脸上挂着笑容:“我是伺候公子的人，不跟着公子，又能去哪里？”
[EN] Trailing behind him was Qinggua, whom he had barely noticed during the commotion at the gate. Now the boy looked like an eyesore. Chen Haoyu swung a foot at him. "Still tailing me? Get lost!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇命令道：“滚回墨香苑给本公子好好学功夫！再跟着本公子，小心我一脚把你踢飞！”
[EN] Qinggua hopped aside, grinning. "I'm your personal servant. Where else would I go?"
[ZH] 青瓜不敢再跟着，瞧着公子朝兴隆阁方向走去，自语道：“公子没银子花，气性变得更大，我还是躲远些。”
[EN] "Get back to Moxiang Courtyard and practice your martial arts! Follow me another step and I'll kick you into next week!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇来到兴隆阁，里面的伙计依然忙碌。以前常常来，对此再熟悉不过，他没有任何观察的兴趣，直奔方管事的地儿。
[EN] Qinggua stopped in his tracks, watching his master march toward Xinglong Pavilion, and muttered, "No money makes Young Master's temper even nastier. Better keep my distance."
[ZH] 陈浩宇见方管事正在低头看账本，凑到跟前：“忙着呢？”
[EN] Inside Xinglong Pavilion, clerks bustled about as usual. Chen Haoyu ignored them and headed straight for Steward Fang, who sat bent over a ledger. "Busy?"
[ZH] 方管事抬起头，看到是陈浩宇，赶紧站起来：“二公子来了，快请坐。”
[EN] Looking up, Steward Fang rose at once. "Second Young Master! Please, have a seat."
[ZH] 陈浩宇哪会客气，一屁股坐下：“我来呢没别的事，你是知道的，就按照以前的支取，二百两。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu plopped into a chair without ceremony. "No fancy errands—you know the drill. Draw me two hundred taels like usual."
[ZH] 方管事没有坐下，走到陈浩宇跟前，弯着腰，面带笑容：“二公子想支取银子没有问题，我即刻可办，但您需给我提供少夫人的手谕，上面必须有少夫人亲笔签名，还要盖上她的私印方可。”
[EN] Steward Fang stayed on his feet, bowing slightly with a polite smile. "Drawing silver is no trouble at all, Second Young Master. I can handle it right away—provided you bring a written order signed by Young Madam's own hand and stamped with her private seal."
[ZH] 陈浩宇埋怨道：“这么麻烦？早知道就不该把我名下的财产给她。”
[EN] "All that red tape?" Chen Haoyu groaned. "Should never have signed my properties over to her."
[ZH] 方管事继续笑道：“二公子疼爱少夫人，自然什么好东西都给少夫人，我相信少夫人不会为难您，您回去要了手谕我立刻给您支银子。”
[EN] Steward Fang kept smiling. "Because you dote on Young Madam, of course you hand her the best of everything. I'm sure she won't make things difficult. Bring her authorization and I'll hand over the silver right away."
[ZH] 陈浩宇心想：“疼爱个屁！本公子是逼不得已。”嘴上说道：“要手谕多麻烦，你就直接把银子给我得了。”
[EN] Dote on her my foot! I had no choice! Chen Haoyu fumed inside, saying aloud, "Why bother with paper? Just hand me the silver."
[ZH] 方管事说：“回二公子，是大老爷定下的规矩，不能破。”
[EN] "Forgive me, Second Young Master. Eldest Master laid down that rule, and I dare not break it."
[ZH] 拿自个爹压他，陈浩宇也没办法，只得起身：“好，你等着，我去要手谕。”
[EN] Pulling his father's name out left Chen Haoyu stuck. He got up. "Fine, wait here. I'll go get her note."
[ZH] 陈浩宇悻悻地离开，手谕他是不敢要，还是去找奶奶，既需想办法给朱淑真制造些麻烦，也需讨些银子花。
[EN] He stalked out knowing full well he dared not ask Zhu Shuzhen for a copper. Instead, he headed straight for his grandmother—both to stir up trouble for Zhu Shuzhen and to coax out some spending money.
[ZH] 夏老太太睡过一觉刚刚醒来，瞧瞧外面的天色，太阳快要偏西，想着孙子今日回门也该回来。果不其然，听到他的声音。
[EN] Old Madam Xia had just woken from her nap. Seeing the afternoon sun slanting west, she figured her grandson should be back from Zhu Manor, and right on cue his voice rang out.
[ZH] “奶奶！奶奶！我来看您！”
[EN] "Grandmother! Grandmother! I came to see you!"
[ZH] 人还没看到，老太太已心花怒放。等看到人时，更是喜笑颜开：“回来了！快坐到奶奶身边来！”
[EN] Even before he crossed the threshold, the old lady beamed. The moment he stepped inside, her face broke into wrinkles of delight. "You're back! Come sit right beside me!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇坐到老太太身边，抱住她：“奶奶，孙子想天天这么抱着您。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu slid onto the couch and threw an arm around her. "Grandmother, I wish I could hug you like this every single day."
[ZH] “又哄我。”老太太虽然不信，听了还是很开心，“你爹要是有你一半会哄奶奶，奶奶也不至于老生他的气。”
[EN] "Sweet-talking me again." She did not believe a word, yet she glowed all the same. "If your father had half your knack for cheering me up, I wouldn't stay mad at him all the time."
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续发挥哄奶奶的小伎俩：“我爹都把心思花在陈家生意和我娘身上，哪像我只为奶奶花心思。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu poured it on thicker. "Father puts all his heart into Chen business and my mother. Only I save all my heart for you, Grandmother."
[ZH] 老人被哄得满脸笑容：“别抱着奶奶，坐到我对面，陪我说说话。”
[EN] Glowing from ear to ear, she patted his arm. "Stop hugging me and sit across so we can talk."
[ZH] 陈浩宇放开手，在奶奶对面坐好。
[EN] Chen Haoyu let go and settled across from her.
[ZH] 夏老太太关切地问：“今日陪新媳妇回门，在朱家可受气？”
[EN] "How was the return visit at Zhu Manor?" she asked with concern. "Did they give you a hard time?"
[ZH] “朱家能有我这样的女婿已是前世修来的福气，高兴还来不及，哪能给我气受？”
[EN] "Landing a son-in-law like me is a blessing the Zhus earned in a past life! They were overjoyed—why on earth would they give me a hard time?"
[ZH] 老太太笑着批评：“又胡说，忘了你岳丈为何逼你娶朱家姑娘的事儿？”
[EN] Old Madam Xia tapped his wrist with a laugh. "More nonsense! Did you forget why your father-in-law forced you to marry the Zhu girl?"
[ZH] “好汉不提当年勇，此事奶奶可别再提，小心被母老虎听去，你孙子我吃不了兜着走。”
[EN] "A real hero doesn't rehash ancient battles. Please drop that subject, Grandmother. If the tigress overhears, I'll never hear the end of it."
[ZH] 为了多讨些银子，还需将老太太哄高兴，陈浩宇迅速转变话题：“奶奶，孙儿时刻希望您健康长寿，今日去朱府，专门探得朱家那位老祖宗的长寿秘方，您要不要听一听？”
[EN] Wanting to coax out more silver, Chen Haoyu kept her laughing and switched tracks. "Grandmother, I pray every day for your health and long life. At Zhu Manor today, I even uncovered the longevity secret of their Old Ancestor. Want to hear it?"
[ZH] 老太太猜到孙子不过是哄她，还是认真配合：“当然要听。”
[EN] Certain her grandson was spinning another yarn, Old Madam Xia played along. "Of course I want to hear it."
[ZH] 陈浩宇故作神秘地说：“我在朱家佛堂周围转了好久，只听到里面的木鱼声还有念佛的声音，我想朱家老祖宗肯定是因为长期敲木鱼又念佛的，才能活那么久，不如明日我也给您买个木鱼，再搞串佛珠回来，您也学学？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu leaned in, lowering his voice to a conspiratorial whisper. "I wandered around the Zhu Buddhist shrine for ages, and all I heard inside was the clack of a wooden fish drum and chanting. I figured their Old Ancestor must have lived so long from tapping a wooden fish and reciting sutras every day. How about I buy you a wooden fish and a string of prayer beads tomorrow so you can try it too?"
[ZH] “行了，奶奶不是那块念佛的料，只要你每日来看看奶奶，陪奶奶说说话，奶奶一高兴自然健康长寿。”
[EN] "Enough of that. I'm not cut out for chanting sutras. Just come visit me every day and keep me company. When I'm happy, health and longevity follow on their own."
[ZH] “好，我以后会多陪奶奶。”陈浩宇想着怎么才能让奶奶给朱淑真寻事？不如发挥装一下可怜的伎俩，“奶奶，您得帮帮我。”
[EN] "Deal! I'll visit you every day." Looking for a way to sic his grandmother on Zhu Shuzhen, Chen Haoyu put on a pitiful face. "Grandmother, you have to help me."
[ZH] “帮你什么？”
[EN] "Help you with what?"
[ZH] “朱淑真欺负我。”
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen bullies me."
[ZH] “怎么欺负你？”
[EN] "How does she bully you?"
[ZH] “她撵走我的丫鬟，夺我的院子，霸占我的财产，每个月只给我二十两银子花。我现在都成穷人，走出去没人相信我是陈家人。”
[EN] "She dismissed all my maids, took over my courtyard, seized my properties, and put me on a twenty-tael monthly allowance. I'm practically a pauper now! Nobody outside even believes I belong to the Chen family."
[ZH] 老太太笑道：“你娶的娘子口齿伶俐，我可说不过她，再说谁让你跪着求人家嫁给你？还跟她签什么婚前契约？要怪就怪你跟蒋爷赌博，差点儿输掉陈家所有产业。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia chuckled. "Your bride has a razor-sharp tongue. Even I can't outtalk her. Besides, who told you to drop to your knees and beg her to marry you, or sign some prenuptial contract? Blame yourself for gambling with Master Jiang and nearly throwing away the entire Chen fortune."
[ZH] 陈浩宇感到诧异：“您怎么会知道？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu blinked. "How did you find out about that?"
[ZH] “新媳妇说的，你爹娘也已经知道。”
[EN] "Your new bride told me. Your father and mother know too."
[ZH] 陈浩宇在心中埋怨道：“朱淑真！你在家人面前一点儿面子都不给我留！等着瞧！”
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen! Chen Haoyu cursed under his breath. Not a shred of face left for me in front of my own family! Just you wait!
[ZH] “奶奶，赌博是我不对，以后孙儿再也不敢。不过，您得好好替我教训教训朱淑真，她现在都骑到我头上。”
[EN] "Grandmother, the gambling was my fault, and I swear I'll never touch it again. Still, you have to put Zhu Shuzhen in her place. She's walking all over me."
[ZH] “怎么教训？”
[EN] "How am I supposed to do that?"
[ZH] “您没事多给她找些麻烦。”
[EN] "Find excuses to give her trouble whenever you're free."
[ZH] “她现在是你的娘子，给她找麻烦不就是给你找麻烦？”
[EN] "She's your wife now. Giving her trouble means giving you trouble."
[ZH] “奶奶，我跟她不过是名义上的夫妻罢了。”
[EN] "Grandmother, we're husband and wife in name only."
[ZH] 老太太面露愁容：“我孙子好容易娶个媳妇，却把日子过成这样，奶奶想着都难过。”
[EN] A crease of sorrow crossed Old Madam Xia's brow. "My grandson finally takes a wife, only to live like this. It breaks my heart just thinking about it."
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续哄道：“奶奶别难过，迟早我会娶个您满意的回来。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu hurried to soothe her. "Don't be sad, Grandmother. Sooner or later, I'll marry someone you actually approve of."
[ZH] 老太太真情流露：“奶奶对你现在的娘子就挺满意。”
[EN] "I'm quite satisfied with the bride you have right now," Old Madam Xia said from the heart.
[ZH] 鼓动奶奶制造麻烦的计划失败，陈浩宇心想还是赶紧讨银子：“奶奶，朱淑真不给我银子花，我又不敢向爹娘要，您能不能给我些银子？”
[EN] Seeing his plot to stir up his grandmother flop, Chen Haoyu cut straight to the silver. "Grandmother, Zhu Shuzhen won't give me a single coin, and I don't dare ask Father or Mother. Could you spare me a little spending money?"
[ZH] 老太太对孙子倒出手阔绰，命丫鬟拿来一个木盒，从里面取出一张五百两的银票：“仔细着花，不准花在商妓和赌徒身上，听到没？”
[EN] When it came to her favorite grandson, Old Madam Xia never held back. She had a maid bring a wooden coffer and drew out a five-hundred-tael banknote. "Spend it with care. Not a copper on courtesans or gamblers, understand?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇接过，喜出望外，在奶奶脸上亲了个够，哄得老太太更是眉开眼笑。又与老太太聊几句，怀揣银票直奔府外，带着疤子等人继续去逛荡。
[EN] Overjoyed, Chen Haoyu snatched the note and planted kisses all over his grandmother's cheeks until she beamed from ear to ear. After a few more minutes of banter, he tucked the banknote into his robe, hurried out the front gates, and swept Scarface and his crew off for another round of revelry.

#CH 216
[ZH] 过了两日，陈府的花匠已将墨香苑池塘里的淤泥和杂质清理干净，放些养料肥沃的泥土进去，将莲藕种好，只等生根发芽长出荷叶与荷花来。
[ZH] 一切都已按照自己的喜好布置完毕，在陈家也有了自己的财产，公爹和婆婆对她态度又好，也无人前来寻事，千里寻心情大好。
[ZH] 自回门那日的晚上，千里寻就没见过陈浩宇，他去哪里浪荡，她毫不关心，倒是关心如何搞定老太太。
[ZH] ﻿不如先将写话本之事暂搁一旁，想想哄老太太的招数。
[ZH] 穿越前的她从小身边没个奶奶，还真没有跟老人相处的经验，怎么个哄法，还需动动脑筋。
[ZH] 她想到陈浩宇见到老太太那个骚劲儿，惹得老太太满脸是笑，看来陈浩宇是用那个骚劲儿哄老太太开心。可千里寻从来没跟人这么说话过，那骚劲儿她可学不来。
[ZH] 苦荞从外面进来，发现小姐坐在书桌前没有提笔书写，而是用手撑着下巴沉思。
[ZH] “小姐，您在想什么？”
[ZH] 千里寻放下撑着下巴的手：“你来得正好，帮我想想用什么办法能把老太太哄高兴？”
[ZH] 苦荞想想说：“以前听我爹说，人越老越喜欢热闹，小姐不如想个热闹的法子？”
[ZH] 千里寻十分认同：“没想到你这丫头挺聪明，想到这么好的主意。正好有个现成的办法，不如把说话艺人请来，给老太太说回书？”
[ZH] 苦荞为难道：“好是好，只怕他认出我来，每次给他送话本的人都是我。”
[ZH] “让红豆去不就行了？说话艺人来了，你待在墨香苑，我把红豆带在身边伺候即可。”
[ZH] “小姐的主意不错，我这就去找红豆。”
[ZH] 刚要迈出步子，苦荞又想起件事：“小姐，还有一事需征求您的意见。”
[ZH] “什么事？”
[ZH] “韦嬷嬷说让我教红豆做茶，也好给小姐身边再准备个伺候的人。”
[ZH] “既然是韦嬷嬷说的，不用理会我的意思，你自己安排即可。”
[EN] Two days later, the manor gardeners finished dredging the silt and weeds from the Moxiang Courtyard pond, laid down rich soil, and planted fresh lotus roots to sprout into leaves and blossoms.
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] With every corner arranged to her taste, personal assets in her name, kind in-laws, and nobody knocking on her door looking for a fight, Qianli Xun was in high spirits.
[ZH] 苦荞来到西院，见红豆正在训练下人练习拳法，青瓜在一旁照猫画虎学习，一招一式实在不成样子，苦荞被逗得直想笑。
[EN] She had not laid eyes on Chen Haoyu since the evening of their return visit to Zhu Manor, nor did she care where he gallivanted. What did occupy her mind was winning over Old Madam Xia.
[ZH] 红豆见苦荞前来，吩咐大家继续练，她则迎上去：“苦荞姐姐过来，该不会是小姐有什么吩咐？”
[EN] Setting her huaben aside for the moment, she racked her brains for a way to win the old lady over. Having grown up without a grandmother before her transmigration, she had zero experience coaxing elders. She thought of the shameless sweet-talking act Chen Haoyu put on to make Old Madam Xia beam, and shuddered. She had never talked like that to anyone in her life; fawning was out of the question.
[ZH] “小姐让你去西三胡同找说话艺人，把他请到陈府，给老太太说书。”
[EN] Kuqiao walked in to find her mistress not writing, just propping her chin on one hand in deep thought. "What's on your mind, Miss?"
[ZH] “好，我这就去。”
[EN] Qianli Xun lowered her hand. "Just in time. Help me figure out how to cheer up Old Madam Xia."
[ZH] 红豆对众位下人说：“大家先散了，各忙各的，明日再继续练。”
[EN] Kuqiao pondered a second. "My father used to say the older folks get, the more they love a lively crowd. Why not come up with something lively for her?"
[ZH] 众人散去，红豆也离开。青瓜没法再照猫画虎练习拳法，便追上苦荞。
[EN] Qianli Xun brightened at once. "Didn't realize you were this sharp! That's a great idea, and we have the perfect fix ready to go. Why don't we invite the storyteller over to perform for Old Madam Xia?"
[ZH] “苦荞，你能不能帮我给少夫人说说，让她也教我拳法？”
[EN] "Sounds wonderful, except he might recognize me," Kuqiao worried. "I'm the one who delivers your manuscripts to him every time."
[ZH] 苦荞边走边说：“你为何要练拳法？”
[EN] "Send Hongdou instead! When the storyteller comes, you stay here in Moxiang Courtyard and I'll bring Hongdou along to attend me."
[ZH] 青瓜一脸委屈：“公子让我练，我要练不成他就把我撵到西院。”
[EN] "Smart plan, Miss. I'll go find Hongdou right now." Turning to go, Kuqiao paused. "Oh, one more thing I need to ask you about, Miss."
[ZH] 苦荞站住：“既是姑爷让你练，就让姑爷亲自去找我家小姐说，我可不管你的破事。”
[EN] "What is it?"
[ZH] 苦荞继续往前走，青瓜继续跟上，装作可怜的样子。
[EN] "Matron Wei told me to teach Hongdou how to prepare whisked tea, so you'll have another maid trained to attend you."
[ZH] “怎么能算破事？你就帮帮我吧？你跟着少夫人来到陈家，应该也看到，公子他经常踢我，少夫人如果能教我几招，我起码还可以躲过公子的腿脚。”
[EN] "If Matron Wei suggested it, don't bother asking me. Go ahead and arrange it."
[ZH] 苦荞见青瓜既可笑又可怜，答应道：“好吧，我去给小姐说说，她答不答应就看你的造化。”
[EN] "Yes, Miss."
[ZH] 青瓜把苦荞送回素心草堂，自己则待在外面充满希望地等待。
[EN] Heading to the West Courtyard, Kuqiao found Hongdou drilling the household servants in Qianli Fist, with Qinggua trailing off to the side mimicking every stance with hilariously sloppy form. Kuqiao nearly burst out laughing.
[ZH] 苦荞进入后，见小姐正坐到茶桌前做茶，动作依然很粗苯。
[EN] Spotting her, Hongdou told the others to keep practicing and walked over. "Sister Kuqiao? Does Miss have an errand for me?"
[ZH] “小姐想喝茶，我来做，您没有必要亲自动手。”
[EN] "Miss wants you to head to West Third Alley, find the storyteller, and invite him to Chen Manor to tell stories for Old Madam Xia."
[ZH] “我想着老太太的事，心中不清静，一点儿写故事的欲望都没有，也无心看书，想用做茶打发时间。本以为天天见你做茶，怎么着也能学个七分像，结果还是做得一塌糊涂。”千里寻第一次对自己感到失望。
[EN] "Got it. I'll go right away." Hongdou turned back to the group. "Take a break, everyone! Back to your chores, and we'll train again tomorrow."
[ZH] 小姐的话正说到苦荞心里：“小姐若想找事情打发时间，正好有一事，您肯定感兴趣。”
[EN] The servants dispersed and Hongdou hurried off. Left with nobody to copy, Qinggua jogged after Kuqiao.
[ZH] 千里寻放下茶筅：“什么事？快快说来。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, could you put in a word with Young Madam and ask her to teach me martial arts too?"
[ZH] “我刚去西院，小姐猜我看到什么？”
[EN] "Why do you want to learn?" Kuqiao asked as she walked.
[ZH] “别卖关子，赶紧说。”
[EN] Qinggua put on a mournful look. "Young Master ordered me to learn. If I can't pull it off, he'll banish me to the West Courtyard."
[ZH] “我看到青瓜跟着学习拳法，练得一点儿都不标准，动作实在可笑。刚刚我回来时，他求我给您说说教他拳法，我本来想拒绝，见他可怜，便应下。”
[EN] Kuqiao halted. "If the young master told you to learn, have him come ask my mistress himself. Don't drag me into your mess."
[ZH] 千里寻的确来了兴致，站起来：“他竟然想学拳法？实在好玩儿，可他一点儿根骨都没有，想学下辈子吧。不过我闲来无事，就拿他消磨消磨时间。”
[EN] She walked on, and Qinggua trailed right behind, piling on the pity. "How is it a mess? Help me out, please? You've seen how Young Master kicks me every chance he gets. If Young Madam teaches me a few moves, at least I can dodge his boots."
[ZH] 苦荞管不了那么多，只要小姐答应，她也算尽了人情。小姐出去后，她开始收拾做残的茶汤。
[EN] Half amused and half sympathetic, Kuqiao relented. "Fine, I'll mention it to Miss. Whether she says yes is up to your luck."
[ZH] 千里寻走出素心草堂，青瓜的确站在门外，伸长脖子向里张望。
[EN] Qinggua escorted her back to Suxin Thatched Hall and waited outside full of hope.
[ZH] 青瓜见少夫人出来，满脸堆笑：“少夫人是不是答应教我拳法？”
[EN] Inside, Kuqiao found her mistress seated at the tea table whisking tea with stiff, clumsy strokes.
[ZH] 千里寻将青瓜上上下下瞧一遍，问道：“你为何要学拳法？”
[EN] "If you want tea, Miss, let me make it. No need to tire yourself."
[ZH] 青瓜心想，公子与少夫人水火不容，千万不能提是公子让学：“我见少夫人十分厉害，上不怕老太太，中不怕老爷和夫人，下不怕我家公子，就想跟您学几招，不图别的，起码在公子踢我时，能迅速躲开。”
[EN] "I've been mulling over Old Madam Xia's business, and my mind isn't settled enough to write or read. Figured making tea would pass the time. After watching you do it every day, I thought I could pull off at least a decent imitation, yet it still came out a total wreck." For the first time, Qianli Xun felt disappointed in herself.
[ZH] 千里寻想到一招：“你呀，谈不上根骨，我的拳法根本学不会，即使学会招数，也只能学个皮毛而已。不过我正好有拳法之外的一招，对根骨没什么要求，你要是学了，保准陈浩宇怎么踢你都能躲开，只要你愿意还可还他一脚。”
[EN] Her words handed Kuqiao the perfect opening. "If you want something fun to pass the time, Miss, I have just the thing."
[ZH] 青瓜先是失望，本想着跟随少夫人学习好拳法，以后可以跟着公子出去耀武扬威，谁承想自己没根骨，看来他天生没有跟着公子出去混的福气。干脆退而求其次，学会一招是一招，起码还能自保。
[EN] Qianli Xun set down the tea whisk. "What is it? Spill."
[ZH] “少夫人，我学，您就教我吧。”
[EN] "I just came from the West Courtyard. Guess what I saw?"
[ZH] 千里寻说：“踢我一脚。”
[EN] "Quit stalling and tell me."
[ZH] 青瓜吓得说道：“我怎么敢踢少夫人？”
[EN] "Qinggua was trying to follow along with the martial arts drills, flailing every which way like a clown. On the way back, he begged me to ask if you'd teach him. I meant to turn him down, yet he looked so pitiful I agreed to ask."
[ZH] 千里寻命令道：“让你踢就踢！不踢我怎么教你？”
[EN] Qianli Xun's eyes lit up and she stood. "He actually wants to learn martial arts? That's hilarious. He doesn't have a single bone made for fighting—he'd have to wait for his next life to master Qianli Fist. Still, I've got time to kill. Might as well use him for entertainment."
[ZH] 青瓜大胆伸出一脚向少夫人踢去……
[EN] Having done her favor, Kuqiao stayed behind to clean up the botched tea while her mistress stepped outside.
[ZH] 只见少夫人迅速向侧方滑步闪身，同时身体微侧，他踢出的脚仅从少夫人身边掠过，接着他的身体出现短暂失衡。少夫人则迅速靠近他，用手拨开他的腿，用另一只手扭住他的手臂。
[EN] Sure enough, Qinggua stood right beyond the threshold, craning his neck indoors. The instant she appeared, he broke into a wide grin. "Young Madam, did you agree to teach me martial arts?"
[ZH] “这招叫幻影闪移。”千里寻放开青瓜，“可记住招数？”
[EN] Qianli Xun looked him up and down. "Why do you want to learn?"
[ZH] 青瓜摇摇头：“没看明白。”
[EN] Knowing full well the bad blood between his master and mistress, Qinggua made sure not to mention Chen Haoyu's order. "I see how impressive you are, Young Madam—unafraid of Old Madam Xia above, Master and Madam in the middle, or Young Master below. I just want to pick up a few tricks so I can dodge when Young Master kicks me."
[ZH] 千里寻又让青瓜踢来，如此反复，演示了不下七八次，青瓜终于入门。她喊来阿升，让阿升带青瓜到西院帮着练习。
[EN] An idea clicked in Qianli Xun's head. "You don't have the build or aptitude for my fist style. Even if you memorized the forms, you'd only scratch the surface. Lucky for you, I know one standalone move that needs zero martial talent. Learn it, and no matter how Chen Haoyu kicks at you, you'll slip right past—and even kick him back if you feel bold."
[ZH] 刚安排好，红豆从外面回来。
[EN] Disappointment flickered over Qinggua's face; he had dreamed of mastering real kung fu and swaggering down the street behind his master. No such luck. Settling for second best, one self-defense move beat getting bruised black and blue. "I'll learn it, Young Madam! Teach me!"
[ZH] “小姐，西三胡同的说话艺人已请来，在院外等候。”
[EN] "Kick me," Qianli Xun ordered.
[ZH] “你带上说话艺人随我去找老太太。”
[EN] Qinggua paled. "How could I dare kick you, Young Madam?"
[ZH] 红豆很愿意跟着小姐，可平日这种事只有苦荞有机会，不由问道：“苦荞姐姐不去吗？”
[EN] "I told you to kick, so kick! How else can I demonstrate?"
[ZH] “我给她安排了其他事，今日由你来陪。”
[EN] Steeling his nerve, Qinggua aimed a kick straight at her.
[ZH] 小姐边说边向外走，红豆赶紧跟上。
