[EN] In a flash, Qianli Xun slid sideways, angling her torso so his boot sliced empty air right past her hip. The momentum pulled him off balance for a split second—all she needed to step inside, brush his leg aside with one hand, and lock his arm behind his back with the other.
[ZH] 说话艺人见主人家出来，上前拜见：“少夫人，不知让鄙人在何处表演？”
[EN] "This move is called Phantom Sidestep," she said, releasing him. "Catch all that?"
[ZH] “跟着走便是。”
[EN] Qinggua shook his head blankly. "Moved too fast for me."
[ZH] 千里寻在前面带路，红豆带着说话艺人跟在后面。
[EN] She had him kick at her seven or eight more times until he grasped the footwork, then called Asheng over to drill him in the West Courtyard.
[ZH] 来到老太太的院外，千里寻让红豆与说话艺人在外面候着，她一个人先进去。
[EN] No sooner had she settled that than Hongdou returned. "Miss, I've brought the storyteller from West Third Alley. He's waiting outside the courtyard."
[ZH] 丫鬟们正扶着夏老太太在院子里散步，刚走出没几步，见孙媳妇进来，生气道：“太不懂礼数，没有通报就进来？还号称什么江南女词人！”
[EN] "Bring him along. We're going to Old Madam Xia's."
[ZH] 本来想学着陈浩宇说话，都已做好心理建设，被老太太没好气的话语击打得一点儿心情都没有。
[EN] Glad for the chance to accompany her mistress—a duty that usually went to Kuqiao—Hongdou asked, "Isn't Sister Kuqiao coming?"
[ZH] “我是江南女词人，心胸自然比他人宽广，奶奶瞧我不顺眼，我不跟您计较，今日我带来一人，让您这冷清的院子也热闹热闹。”
[EN] "I gave her another task. You're with me today." Qianli Xun led the way out, with Hongdou trailing behind.
[ZH] 千里寻并不理会老太太，开始指挥丫鬟：“你们两个去搬张桌子来，你去搬把椅子。”
[EN] Spotting the young mistress emerge, the storyteller bowed low. "Young Madam, where would you like me to perform?"
[ZH] 一旁的丫鬟见老太太虽生气，却不言语，便知老太太并不反对，一个个忙碌起来。
[EN] "Just follow me."
[ZH] 桌椅摆好后，千里寻亲自去扶老太太：“奶奶，您再对我不满，今日把您肚子里的气先放一放，我扶您坐下。”
[EN] Qianli Xun walked ahead while Hongdou guided the storyteller behind her. At the gate of Old Madam Xia's courtyard, Qianli Xun told them to wait outside and entered alone.
[ZH] 孙媳妇前来，安排这，安排那，院子里变得热闹，老太太心里高兴，嘴上却不饶人：“那我就坐下，看你能耍什么花招。”
[EN] Maids were helping Old Madam Xia stroll around the yard. Barely a few steps in, the old lady spotted her granddaughter-in-law and barked, "What utter lack of manners, barging in without being announced! And they call you the Poetess of Jiangnan!"
[ZH] 扶老太太坐下后，千里寻向外面喊道：“红豆，把说话艺人带进来。”
[EN] Qianli Xun had hyped herself up to try a softer tone like Chen Haoyu's, only for that sharp rebuke to puncture her resolve on the spot. "Since I am the Poetess of Jiangnan, my tolerance runs wider than most. Even if you find me an eyesore, Grandmother, I won't hold it against you. I brought someone today to liven up this quiet courtyard of yours."
[ZH] 听到“说话艺人”的名称，老太太已装不下去，嘴角露出笑容。
[EN] Ignoring the old lady's scowl, Qianli Xun directed the maids on the spot. "You two, carry a table out here. You, fetch a chair."
[ZH] 千里寻用余光瞥到，心想：“这么快将你搞定，以后我在陈府日子就好过了。”
[EN] Seeing Old Madam Xia glare without stopping her, the maids realized she did not mind and hopped to work.
[ZH] 红豆把说话艺人带进来。
[EN] Once the table and chair stood ready, Qianli Xun stepped up to take the old lady's arm. "No matter how upset you are with me, Grandmother, set your temper aside for today and sit."
[ZH] 那说话艺人见到桌子倍感亲切，自然走过去，从褡裢里取出惊堂木和折扇放在桌上，再拿出紫砂壶，对一旁的丫鬟说：“麻烦，给沏壶茶。”
[EN] Watching her granddaughter-in-law bustle around ordering things set up made the courtyard feel alive, warming Old Madam Xia's heart even as her mouth stayed sharp. "Fine, I'll sit and see what trick you're pulling."
[ZH] 老太太脸上的笑容越来越多，主动说：“给先生沏壶好茶。”
[EN] Once the old madam was settled, Qianli Xun called toward the gate, "Hongdou, bring the storyteller in!"
[ZH] 丫鬟应答后，接过茶壶去忙活。
[EN] At the word "storyteller," Old Madam Xia's stern mask cracked, and the corners of her mouth curved up.
[ZH] 老太太瞅一眼站在身旁的孙媳妇：“你也坐下吧。”
[EN] Catching that flicker out of the corner of her eye, Qianli Xun grinned inside. That easy to win over? Smooth sailing in Chen Manor from here on out.
[ZH] 旁边的丫鬟听后，赶紧又去搬把椅子。
[EN] Hongdou ushered the storyteller inside. Sighting the table, he looked right at home, pulling his wooden clapper and folding fan from his cloth satchel, followed by a purple clay teapot. He handed the pot to a nearby maid. "Trouble you for a fresh brew of tea, miss."
[ZH] 千里寻坐下说：“奶奶，说话艺人说的是千里寻才子写的故事，您听了肯定喜欢。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia's smile widened. "Brew the master our finest tea."
[ZH] “让你费心。”老太太说话的语气柔和许多。
[EN] The maid hurried off with the pot. Old Madam Xia glanced at Qianli Xun standing beside her. "You sit down too."
[ZH] 紫砂壶端上后，说话艺人先抿一口，丫鬟沏的茶不仅口感好，温度也合适，不烫嘴。他十分满意，放下紫砂壶：“谁不知陈府上下气象万千，富贵荣华满堂彩，老太太您是福寿双全，德高望重……”
[EN] Another maid scrambled off for a second chair.
[ZH] 说话艺人才说几句，被夏老太太笑着阻止：“行了，别说奉承话，赶快说故事，只要你说得好，我自然打赏。”
[EN] Taking her seat, Qianli Xun said, "Grandmother, the tale he's telling today was written by Master Qianli Xun. You're guaranteed to love it."
[ZH] 说话艺人说：“得了，听老太太您的。”拿起惊堂木一拍：
[EN] "Thoughtful of you," Old Madam Xia said, her voice much softer.
[ZH] 话说南天边有座琉璃塔，塔里困着一只玉面狐狸，足足困了上千年。那狐狸倒没闲着，以塔为家，以塔气为食，以塔土为衣，渐渐养得塔里塔气……
[EN] When the tea came back, the storyteller took a sip—fragrant and just the right temperature. Satisfied, he set the pot down and launched into his opener. "Who doesn't know that Chen Manor brims with splendor, wealth, and honor overflowing the halls, and that Old Madam Xia enjoys both fortune and longevity, revered by all—"
[ZH] 夏老太太被逗乐：“呵呵，第一次听‘塔里塔气’的说法，实在可乐。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia cut him off with a laugh. "Skip the flattery and get to the story. Tell it well, and I'll reward you handsomely."
[ZH] 说话艺人继续讲：
[EN] "Your wish is my command, Old Madam!" He slapped his wooden clapper sharp against the tabletop.
[ZH] 南天边有一神仙，名唤玄知，每日修行结束，都会盯着琉璃塔。玄知乃真神，目光如炬，充满神力。琉璃塔每日被真神注目，逐渐吸收神力。神力越积越多，琉璃塔无法忍受，只听南天边一阵巨响：“轰！”，琉璃塔被震成碎片。玉面狐狸终得自由，接触到一股仙气，化为一个女子，因长期待在塔内，满身灰尘，蓬头垢面。
[EN] "Now, at the southern edge of heaven stood a Glazed Pagoda, trapping a jade-faced fox spirit for a full thousand years. That fox didn't sit idle—she made the pagoda her home, fed on pagoda mist, dressed in pagoda dust, until every bit of her reeked of pure pagoda-ishness..."
[ZH] 这时真神玄知出现，女子被吓坏：“你谁呀？长得挺好看，就是脸看着不够喜庆。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia burst out laughing. "Heh! First time I've heard 'pagoda-ishness'! What a hoot!"
[ZH] 玄知将仙袖一挥，女子身上的灰尘全部消失，立刻变成一个美人。玄知说：“我救了你，你以后就是我的人。”
[EN] The storyteller rolled right on.
[ZH] 女子变得漂亮虽开心，可被玄知的话给气到：“凭什么说你救了我？我可是什么都没看到。”说完女子准备逃走，被玄知用仙力拉回……
[EN] "At the southern edge of heaven also dwelt a true deity named Xuanzhi. Every day after his cultivation, he gazed upon the Glazed Pagoda. Because Xuanzhi was a supreme god, his piercing gaze carried immense divine power. Day after day, the pagoda soaked up that energy until it could hold no more. With a thunderous boom across the southern skies, the Glazed Pagoda shattered to pieces! Free at last, the jade-faced fox touched a wisp of immortal aura and took human shape as a young woman—though after a millennium indoors, she stood coated in dust, unkempt and disheveled. "Right then, Xuanzhi appeared. Startled out of her wits, the girl demanded, 'Who are you? Decent-looking face, just way too grim and uncheerful!' "Xuanzhi swept one immortal sleeve, whisking every speck of dust off her and turning her into a stunning beauty on the spot. 'I saved you,' Xuanzhi announced. 'From now on, you belong to me.' "Delighted to look gorgeous yet furious at his nerve, the girl snapped back, 'Says who you saved me? I didn't see a thing!' With that, she turned to bolt, only for Xuanzhi to reel her straight back with his divine power..."
[ZH] 老太太边听边笑，自从儿子们成家，还没有人让自己这么开心过。
[EN] Old Madam Xia laughed nonstop. Not since her sons had grown up and started their own families had anyone made her this happy.
[ZH] 没过多久，说话艺人来陈府说书的事传开，陈浩飞听说，也跑来听，接着二婶、三婶和叶安雅也来到，院子里变得更热闹，老太太别提有多开心。
[EN] Word soon spread that a storyteller was performing inside Chen Manor. Chen Haofei hurried over to listen, followed by Second Aunt Madam Bao, Third Aunt Madam Cui, and Ye Anya. The courtyard filled with chatter and laughter, and Old Madam Xia could not have been more thrilled.

#CH 217
[ZH] 说话艺人说完故事离开后，夏老太太心满意足，提出要求：“光说一次有什么用？以后让说话艺人每日都来。”
[ZH] 千里寻说：“奶奶放心，我已叮嘱过，往后说话艺人每日这个时间来给您老讲故事。”
[ZH] 老人笑得合不拢嘴。
[ZH] 二婶包氏用酸酸的语气说：“真没看出来，浩宇的媳妇比浩宇还会哄老太太开心。真羡慕大嫂有这么个会哄人的儿媳妇，哪像我家这位……”
[ZH] 包氏瞪一眼身旁的儿媳妇叶安雅：“以后多向淑真学学，要不浩东把你娶回来有什么用？”
[ZH] 叶安雅不敢言语，任由婆婆批评。
[EN] After the storyteller wrapped up and left, Old Madam Xia leaned back in pure contentment and laid down an order. "One session isn't nearly enough. Have him come every day from now on."
[ZH] 老太太虽不满也不好说什么。
[EN] "Don't worry, Grandmother," Qianli Xun said. "I already arranged for him to come at this same hour every day."
[ZH] 千里寻想观察观察再说，穿越前宅斗剧看多了，自然知道后宅之事不是用拳头就能解决的，需多动脑子。
[EN] Old Madam Xia beamed from ear to ear.
[ZH] 崔氏与他的儿子陈浩飞则权当看热闹，再说他们早已习惯叶安雅被婆婆批评，见怪不怪。
[EN] Second Aunt Madam Bao chimed in with a tart edge. "Who would have guessed Haoyu's bride is even better at buttering up Old Madam Xia than Haoyu himself? I do envy Eldest Sister-in-law for landing such a clever daughter-in-law, unlike the one in my house..." She shot a glare at Ye Anya beside her. "Take a lesson from Shuzhen! Otherwise what use was Haodong marrying you?"
[ZH] 包氏继续说：“怪只怪我家浩东从早到晚都在陈家的绸缎庄、茶庄、盐庄、瓷器行、码头，还有各大客栈、酒楼忙活，哪像浩宇和他媳妇，时间一大把，只顾着哄老太太开心。老太太您可不能顾此失彼，否则浩东会伤心。”
[EN] Ye Anya kept her head lowered and said nothing. Old Madam Xia frowned at the jab, yet held her tongue.
[ZH] 夏老太太听出对方话中有话，脸上笑容顿失：“不用你拿话提醒我，我心里有杆秤，谁人品、能力如何，谁对陈家贡献大，我都跟明镜似的。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stayed quiet and watched. Having binged enough household-intrigue dramas in her previous life, she knew inner-courtyard politics required brains, not fists.
[ZH] 崔氏见二嫂为自己儿子立功，也不愿落下：“我家浩飞自然比不上他的两位堂兄，一个会筹谋，一个会玩儿，还会讨老太太欢心。只怪他一门心思用在读书上，明年就该参加会试，会试要是通过，那可是要去汴梁参加殿试。浩飞，赶紧给你奶奶背背《尔雅》。”
[EN] Madam Cui and her son Chen Haofei treated the scolding as routine background noise; they had watched Madam Bao pick on Ye Anya too many times to blink.
[ZH] 老太太说：“行了，不用背，背了我也听不懂。浩飞是好孩子，好好学，给咱们陈家考个功名回来，就是对陈家最大的贡献。”
[EN] Madam Bao pressed on. "The trouble is, my Haodong slaves from dawn till dusk across the Chen silk house, tea house, salt shop, porcelain warehouse, docks, inns, and taverns—unlike Haoyu and his wife, who have all the time in the world to hang around amusing Old Madam Xia. Don't favor one at the expense of the other, Mother, or Haodong will be heartbroken."
[ZH] 陈浩飞说：“是，奶奶。”
[EN] Catching the barbed hint, Old Madam Xia let her smile drop. "Save your reminders. I keep a clear scale in my head. Who has character, who has ability, and who contributes most to the Chen household—I see every bit of it crystal clear."
[ZH] 老太太吩咐道：“都回去吧，我要跟浩宇的媳妇说几句话。”
[EN] Not wanting Second Sister-in-law to claim all the credit for her son, Madam Cui jumped right in. "My Haofei can't compare with either of his older cousins—one schemes for business, the other knows how to play and win Grandmother's favor. He puts his whole heart into his books instead. Next year he sits the metropolitan examination, and once he passes, he'll ride to Bianliang for the palace exam! Haofei, recite a passage from the *Erya* for your grandmother."
[ZH] 千里寻目送大家离开，想想刚才二婶和三婶说过的话，完全能体会到老太太为何会喜欢陈浩宇这个只会哄她开心却一无是处的孙子。
[EN] "No need," Old Madam Xia waved it off. "Even if he recites it, I won't understand a word. Haofei is a good boy. Study hard and bring an official title back to the Chen family—that will be the greatest contribution of all."
[ZH] 待众人离开后，老太太才说：“淑真，扶我进屋。”
[EN] "Yes, Grandmother," Chen Haofei answered.
[ZH] 千里寻将老太太扶起，搀扶的过程更感觉出她腿脚实在不利索。不由想到老祖宗，一百二十岁的人不知比眼前这位强多少倍，怜悯疼爱之情油然而生。边搀老太太边说：“奶奶，陈浩宇上次说有位大夫能治腿脚疾病，改日我把人给您找来。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia dismissed the crowd. "All of you head back now. I want a private word with Haoyu's wife."
[ZH] 老太太笑道：“浩宇的话你也信？他不过是哄我开心而已，陈家有的是钱，什么大夫请不来？早请过，我这腿脚无人可医。”
[EN] Watching them file out, Qianli Xun weighed the veiled competition from Second and Third Aunts. Suddenly it made total sense why Old Madam Xia doted on Chen Haoyu despite his good-for-nothing streak: everyone else came with an agenda, while he just showed up to make her laugh.
[ZH] 千里寻带着疑惑，扶老太太在榻上坐好，自己也脱去鞋子，盘腿坐在老人对面：“从今日起奶奶不再生我的气？”
[EN] Once the yard cleared, Old Madam Xia held out her hand. "Shuzhen, help me inside."
[ZH] 老太太笑道：“我何时生过你的气？”
[EN] Supporting her across the threshold, Qianli Xun noticed how stiff and unsteady the old woman's legs felt. Compared with the Old Ancestor back at Zhu Manor, who moved sprightly at a hundred and twenty, Old Madam Xia seemed frail indeed, stirring a sudden rush of pity and fondness in Qianli Xun's chest. "Grandmother, Chen Haoyu mentioned a physician who treats leg ailments. I'll track him down for you one of these days."
[ZH] 千里寻回想片刻，立刻醒悟：“原来奶奶是故意装作生我的气？早知道如此，我也不用费尽心思讨您欢心。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia chuckled. "You believe anything Haoyu says? He only says that to cheer me up. With the Chen fortune, what physician couldn't we hire? We've tried them all; my legs are beyond curing."
[ZH] 没想到老太太却说：“在这陈府，我终于遇到你这么个明白人，知道我寻事、生气，不过是想让你讨我欢心而已，可我那几个儿子儿媳没一个懂我的心思。”
[EN] Puzzled, Qianli Xun settled Old Madam Xia onto the kang couch, kicked off her own shoes, and sat cross-legged opposite her. "So you aren't angry with me anymore, Grandmother?"
[ZH] 千里寻这才恍然大悟，原来老太太每次见到陈浩宇的爹娘又是埋怨又是生气，不过是想让他们讨好她，哄她开心而已，却适得其反，她千里寻不过是歪打正着。
[EN] Old Madam Xia laughed. "When was I ever angry with you?"
[ZH] “奶奶应该将您的喜好直接告诉各位长辈，还有我们这些晚辈。”
[EN] Qianli Xun froze a second before the penny dropped. "You were pretending to be mad the whole time? Had I known that, I wouldn't have worked so hard trying to win you over!"
[ZH] 老太太叹口气：“唉，说出来多没意思，凡是真正对我有心之人，自然知道该如何去做。就像你，愿意在我身上花心思，讨我欢心。往后你在这陈府，有我罩着，没人敢欺负你。”
[EN] "In this entire manor, I've at last found one person with sense," Old Madam Xia said. "You realized I picked fights and fumed just to get you to fuss over me. None of my sons or daughters-in-law ever figured out what I wanted."
[ZH] 千里寻故意说：“我嫁到陈府这段时日，除老太太您，还没人欺负过我，再说我也不怕，谁让我拳头厉害。”
[EN] Everything clicked at once. Every time Old Madam Xia snapped at Chen Haoyu's parents, she was only fishing for attention and affection—yet it had driven them away instead. Qianli Xun had stumbled onto the answer by sheer accident.
[ZH] “呦呦呦，看把你能的。”夏老太太笑道，接着说，“不过，奶奶就喜欢你这样的女子，天不怕地不怕，像当年的我，可惜啊，我老了。”
[EN] "You ought to just tell the elders and us younger ones straight out what you like, Grandmother."
[ZH] “奶奶，您骗我吧？我来陈家闹时您看上去对我很不满意，怎么可能喜欢我？”千里寻既开心，又不敢相信。想起敬茶那日奶奶的态度，哪像喜欢她的样子？
[EN] Old Madam Xia sighed. "Where's the joy in spelling it out? Anyone who truly cares finds out on their own—just like you, putting real thought into cheering me up. From now on, I have your back in Chen Manor. Nobody dares bully you."
[ZH] 老太太解释道：“你来闹事那日显然不想嫁给浩宇，我又何必同意？再说你那个表现，我老婆子笑脸相迎岂不是太不配合？况且我心疼孙子，怕你嫁过来真的揍他，想着干脆配合你把这婚事给搅黄了。”
[EN] Qianli Xun grinned wickedly. "Since marrying in, Grandmother, nobody except you has even tried to bully me. Not that I'd care—my fists do plenty of talking."
[ZH] 千里寻觉得夏老太太深不可测，表面一套，心里又是一套，让人难以捉摸，难怪她的儿子和媳妇们猜不透她的心思。
[EN] "Oh, listen to you brag!" Old Madam Xia laughed, her eyes crinkling. "Still, I like a girl like you—fearless of heaven and earth, just like me when I was young. Pity I'm old now."
[ZH] 说起婚前来陈家大闹一事，千里寻想起老太太那日提到父亲逼迫陈浩宇娶她一事，于是问道：“奶奶那日说是我父亲逼迫陈浩宇娶我，不知是何缘故？”
[EN] "You're teasing me, Grandmother," Qianli Xun said, half pleased and half skeptical. "You looked furious with me when I came raising a storm before the wedding, and at the tea ceremony too. How could you possibly have liked me?"
[ZH] 夏老太太立刻谨慎起来，孙媳妇还不知，看来知府大人有意瞒着她。只怪自己那日一生气没管好嘴，差点儿暴露。今日更不能说，凭孙媳的脾气和拳头，要是知道恐怕浩宇以后真没好日子过，还是赶快掩饰。
[EN] "You made it obvious that day you didn't want to marry Haoyu, so why would I beam and welcome you? Putting on a smiling face while you raged wouldn't have played along with your act! Besides, I worried you'd actually beat my grandson black and blue once you married in, so I figured I might as well help you blow up the engagement."
[ZH] “没有的事儿，那日被你气得瞎说，别多想。”
[EN] Qianli Xun marveled at how deep Old Madam Xia's waters ran—one face outside, another inside. No wonder her own sons and daughters-in-law stayed baffled.
[ZH] 千里寻从老太太的表情中看不出异样，也就不再多问：“奶奶，您觉得千里寻才子写的故事怎么样？”
[EN] Thinking back to that pre-wedding showdown reminded Qianli Xun of one slip Old Madam Xia had let drop. "Grandmother, that day you mentioned my father forced Chen Haoyu to marry me. Why did he do that?"
[ZH] “好！写得太好了！奶奶从来没听过这么新奇的故事。”老太太夸赞有加，接着疑惑道，“你刚才说写故事的人是才子，我怎么觉得像是位姑娘写的？”
[EN] Old Madam Xia stiffened at once. Her granddaughter-in-law still did not know—meaning Prefect Zhu had kept it under wraps. She cursed her own loose tongue from that day. With this girl's temper and fists, spilling the truth now would doom Haoyu for good. She scrambled to cover it up. "Nothing of the sort! You had me so flustered that day I just blabbed nonsense. Don't give it another thought."
[ZH] 被老太太夸奖，千里寻很欢喜，穿越前网暴残留的不快已荡然无存。接着她又佩服老太太的敏锐：“何以见得？”
[EN] Seeing no crack in the old lady's poker face, Qianli Xun let it drop. "Grandmother, how did you like Master Qianli Xun's story?"
[ZH] 老太太用肯定的语气说：“讲姑娘家的故事，肯定是姑娘写的。”
[EN] "Marvelous! Best tale I've heard in years—never heard anything so fresh!" Old Madam Xia beamed, then tilted her head. "You called the author a male scholar, yet why does it sound to me like a young woman wrote it?"
[ZH] 千里寻想到应对之语：“此言差矣，唐代诗仙李太白有《清平乐》，诗圣杜子美有《佳人》，诗佛王右丞有《洛阳女儿行》，写的全是女子，可他们都是响当当的男儿。”
[EN] Delighted by the praise—every last trace of her pre-transmigration cyberbullying blues gone—Qianli Xun also marveled at the old woman's sharp nose. "What makes you think that?"
[ZH] 孙媳妇书本上的知识懂得多，老太太更是乐得不行，心想，浩宇从小不怎么碰书本，也不知道哪辈子修来的福分，竟娶到一位才女：“不愧是江南女词人，懂得就是多。”
[EN] "A story all about a young lady's feelings? Had to be written by a woman," Old Madam Xia declared with conviction.
[ZH] 又被老太太夸赞，千里寻一高兴，想出讨老太太欢心的新招数：“奶奶，我认识一位负鼓盲翁，改日把他带来，给您唱蔡伯喈的故事。”
[EN] Qianli Xun had a comeback ready in a heartbeat. "Not necessarily! The Tang Poet Immortal Li Bai wrote 'Qingping Yue,' the Poet Sage Du Fu wrote 'The Fair Lady,' and the Poet Buddha Wang Wei wrote 'Song of the Daughter of Luoyang'—every one about women, yet all three were red-blooded men!"
[ZH] 老太太的头点得跟拨浪鼓似的：“好好好！”
[EN] Impressed by her granddaughter-in-law's learning, Old Madam Xia beamed all the brighter, wondering what karma her book-hating grandson had piled up in a past life to land such a gifted bride. "Worthy of the title Poetess of Jiangnan! You know everything."
[ZH] 两人越聊越开心，快到中午时，老太太要用饭，千里寻才离开。
[EN] Flushed with pleasure, Qianli Xun thought of another treat. "Grandmother, I know a blind drummer who sings ballads. I'll invite him over one of these days to perform the tale of Cai Bojie for you."
[ZH] 带着红豆走出老太太的院子没几步，被陈浩飞挡住：“二嫂好！”
[EN] Old Madam Xia bobbed her head like a pellet drum. "Yes, yes, yes!"
[ZH] 千里寻刚见过，当然认得：“原来是三弟，找我何事？”
[EN] The two talked right up until noon, when Qianli Xun excused herself so the old lady could eat.
[ZH] 陈浩飞说：“我想求二嫂件事，您一定要答应我。”
[EN] Barely a few paces beyond Old Madam Xia's gate with Hongdou in tow, Chen Haofei stepped into her path. "Greetings, Second Sister-in-law!"
[ZH] “还是先说事情，我再衡量衡量要不要答应你。”跟陈浩飞不熟，千里寻不敢轻易应下。
[EN] Qianli Xun recognized him from the courtyard. "Third Brother-in-law. Need something?"
[ZH] “我虽过乡试，可写诗作词差些，在临安也毫无名气。曾受邀参加过一些诗会，都是去凑凑热闹，从来没有机会带新的诗词前去，现在闻名江南的幽栖居士成为我的二嫂，怎么着也得给我送一两首诗词，好让我在诗会上有表现的机会。求求二嫂送我一两首吧？”
[EN] "I have a favor to beg of you, Second Sister-in-law. Please don't turn me down!"
[ZH] 原来是为此事，帮朱淑真宣传诗词，千里寻来者不拒：“我当什么事，写个把诗词不在话下，以后你想要随时找我。”
[EN] "Tell me what it is first, and I'll weigh whether to say yes." Hardly knowing him, she would not sign any blank checks.
[ZH] “现在就想要。”
[EN] "I passed the provincial exams, yet my poetry is weak and nobody in Lin'an knows my name. Whenever I'm invited to poetry gatherings, I just tag along to fill a seat because I never have fresh verses to present. Now that the renowned Youqi Jushi is my second sister-in-law, could you spare me a poem or two so I can shine at the next salon? Please?"
[ZH] “那就跟上。”
[EN] Promoting Zhu Shuzhen's poetry was right up her alley. "I thought it was something tough! Writing a couple of lyrics is nothing. Come ask anytime you need more."
[ZH] 陈浩飞高兴不已，跟上二嫂往墨香苑而去。
[EN] "I'd love them right now!"
[ZH] 当他们走到墨香苑门口时，叶安雅正站在门口等候。
[EN] "Follow me, then."
[ZH] 千里寻上前问：“大嫂怎么站在这里？身边也不带个丫鬟？”
[EN] Thrilled, Chen Haofei trailed her straight toward Moxiang Courtyard.
[ZH] 叶安雅说：“我正在等妹妹，想与你聊会儿。浩飞怎么也跑来？”
[EN] Approaching the gate, they spotted Ye Anya waiting outside.
[ZH] 陈浩飞上前说：“大嫂好，二嫂答应送我两首诗词，让我跟着前来。”
[EN] "Eldest Sister-in-law, why are you standing out here without even a maid?" Qianli Xun asked.
[ZH] “原来如此。”叶安雅笑道，“大名鼎鼎的幽栖居士进入陈家的门，倒是给你带来方便，以后参加诗会不会空手而去。”
[EN] "I was waiting for you, hoping we could chat," Ye Anya said. "What brings Haofei along?"
[ZH] 陈浩飞也笑道：“那是自然，谁让我们成一家人。”
[EN] "Greetings, Eldest Sister-in-law," Chen Haofei said. "Second Sister-in-law promised to gift me two lyrics, so I came to fetch them."
[ZH] 大家说着笑着走进墨香苑，苦荞已将饭摆好，千里寻不着急用，先坐在桌前给浩飞写诗作词。此时正值春季，便选了两首与春季相关的词，一首《西江月·春半》，一首《蝶恋花·送春》。
[EN] "So that's it," Ye Anya smiled. "Having the celebrated Youqi Jushi marry into the Chen household certainly smooths your path—no more showing up empty-handed to poetry banquets."
[ZH] 洋洋洒洒写完之后，给了浩飞。陈浩飞接过，感谢不已，高兴离去。
[EN] "Of course! We're family now," Chen Haofei laughed.
[ZH] 已到用饭时间，千里寻邀请叶安雅留下与自己一同用饭。
[EN] Chatting and laughing, they stepped into Moxiang Courtyard, where Kuqiao had already laid out lunch. Setting aside the meal for a moment, Qianli Xun sat at her desk and penned two spring lyrics for Chen Haofei: "Xijiang Yue: Mid-Spring" and "Dielianhua: Bidding Farewell to Spring."
[ZH] 叶安雅没有拒绝，这个点儿等弟妹，不留下用饭说不过去。
[EN] Accepting the crisp sheets with effusive thanks, Chen Haofei hurried off on cloud nine.
[ZH] 两人在饭桌前坐好，不过是四菜一汤，加上主食。
[EN] Since lunch sat steaming on the table, Qianli Xun invited Ye Anya to dine with her. Having waited right at mealtime, Ye Anya could not refuse and took a seat.
[ZH] 千里寻穿越而来渐渐习惯古人的生活，对饭食已没有那么新奇，倒是对眼前的大嫂很感兴趣，不知前来找她所为何事？
[EN] The spread was modest—four dishes, one soup, and rice. By now Qianli Xun had grown used to Song meals and found far more interest in her sister-in-law, wondering what had brought Ye Anya to her door.
[ZH] 苦荞给二人盛好汤和主食后，站在一旁伺候。
[EN] Kuqiao ladled soup and served rice for both women before stepping back to attend them.
[ZH] 千里寻说：“在陈家只有大嫂是与我年纪相仿的女子，以后你我要常来往。”
[EN] "You're the only woman near my age in Chen Manor, Eldest Sister-in-law," Qianli Xun said. "We should spend time together often."
[ZH] 叶安雅并不客气，拿起勺子喝口汤后说道：“我也是这么想的，所以今日才来找你聊天儿。”
[EN] Ye Anya raised her spoon for a sip of soup without standing on ceremony. "My thoughts exact—that's why I came over to talk today."
[ZH] 千里寻夹一道菜放入大嫂的碗中：“我刚嫁到陈家什么都不了解，有时间大嫂要多给我讲讲，省得我在陈家说错话，做错事，惹长辈不开心。”
[EN] Qianli Xun placed a morsel of food into Ye Anya's bowl. "I just married in and don't know half the ins and outs of this household. Fill me in whenever you have time, so I don't put my foot in my mouth and upset the elders."
[ZH] “听说你根本不愿嫁到陈家，洞房那晚跟浩宇也未圆房，他整日逛荡在外你也不管，竟然愿意在长辈身上花心思，实在让人费解。”
[EN] "Word is you never wanted to marry into the Chen family," Ye Anya remarked, "didn't consummate the marriage on your wedding night, and don't care where Haoyu wanders off to. Yet you still put real effort into pleasing the elders. I can't quite figure you out."
[ZH] “我是不愿嫁给陈浩宇，可命运捉弄，到最后不得不嫁。既然嫁过来，就把自己当陈家人看待，想在这里安心过日子。”
[EN] "I didn't want to marry Chen Haoyu, true, yet fate twisted my arm and here I am. Since I married in, I treat myself as part of the Chen family and mean to live here in peace."
[ZH] 淑真提到“不得不嫁”让叶安雅想到仪王抢亲之事，原来仪王也看上朱家姑娘，她想不通为何朱家不愿把女儿给仪王，却偏偏许给浩宇这样的浪荡子？
[EN] Those words—"fate twisted my arm"—reminded Ye Anya of Prince Yi raiding the wedding procession. Prince Yi himself had wanted the Zhu daughter; why had the Zhu family turned down a prince to hand her to a rake like Haoyu?
[ZH] 不由感慨道：“在外人看来，妹妹与我能嫁到陈家算是幸运，其中的酸甜苦辣也只有你我知道。浩宇整日不着家，也不知在哪里浪荡，我家那位表面看着比浩宇强，其实……”
[EN] She let out a quiet sigh. "Outsiders think you and I hit the jackpot marrying into the Chen family, yet only we know the bitter along with the sweet. Haoyu stays out day and night, heaven knows where, and while my husband looks respectable on the surface, in truth..."
[ZH] 叶安雅欲言又止，千里寻听出些心酸来，赶紧又夹些菜，放入大嫂碗中：“尝尝这道菜，还不错。”
[EN] Ye Anya bit off the rest of the sentence. Hearing the ache behind her words, Qianli Xun placed another dish in her bowl. "Try this. It's pretty good."
[ZH] 叶安雅吃完菜后，继续说：“我很羡慕妹妹，又厉害又有主见，既得了浩宇的财产，还能把这院子收拾成自己喜欢的样子，而我……”
[EN] After a bite, Ye Anya went on. "I envy you, Sister—tough, independent, holding Haoyu's properties in your hands, remaking this courtyard just the way you please. Whereas I..."
[ZH] 千里寻赶紧解释：“我能如此也是公爹和婆婆人好，陈家各位长辈纵着我，不愿跟我一般见识，更有像大嫂这样愿意与我吐露心声的妯娌。”
[EN] "I only got away with that because Father-in-law and Mother-in-law are decent people, the elders indulge me, and I have a sister-in-law like you willing to speak from the heart," Qianli Xun said.
[ZH] 叶安雅被夸得“噗嗤”笑出声来：“你这张嘴还真甜，难怪能把老太太搞定。我听说那日老太太带着我婆婆还有三婶前来寻事，你怼得有理有据，她们三人最后无话可说只能离开，你这张嘴啊不仅能口吐芬芳，还能吐出各种道理来，实在厉害！”
[EN] Ye Anya burst into a laugh. "What a honeyed tongue—no wonder you won Old Madam Xia over so fast! I heard that when Old Madam Xia marched in with my mother-in-law and Third Aunt to pick a fight, you argued them into a corner with solid logic until none of the three had a comeback left. That mouth of yours doesn't just compose fine verses—it shoots out airtight arguments too. Impressive!"
[ZH] “大嫂谬赞。”
[EN] "You flatter me, Eldest Sister-in-law."
[ZH] 千里寻与叶安雅说说笑笑吃着饭，她觉得陈家人多又热闹，充满温馨的气息，唯一美中不足的便是嫁人不淑，若陈浩宇是她喜欢的人那该多好。
[EN] Laughing over lunch, Qianli Xun felt the warmth of a large, lively family—spoiled only by the wrong husband. If only Chen Haoyu were the man she actually loved.
[ZH] 想到喜欢之人，自然想起申简辰。他跟梦容应该还在回汴梁的路上，等到他们再回到临安时，大家会唤梦容仪王妃。
[EN] That thought drifted straight to Shen Jianchen. He and Mengrong had to be on the road to Bianliang right now. When they returned to Lin'an, everyone would address Mengrong as Princess Consort Yi.
[ZH] 千里寻默默在心中祝福，希望仪王与他的王妃能过上幸福的生活。
[EN] In silence, Qianli Xun sent them her blessing, wishing Prince Yi and his princess consort a happy life together.

#CH 218
[ZH] 叶安雅在墨香苑用过午饭，又与淑真聊会儿，才不舍地离开。
[ZH] 她之所以愿意与淑真交好，除年纪相仿外，这位妯娌的经历让她既好奇又倾佩。
[ZH] 和离一事已让很多女子汗颜，去年被全城百姓那样非议，她竟然还活得有滋有味，搁一般女子都不知跳过多少次钱塘江。
[ZH] 与浩宇定婚后，她竟然为退婚不顾大家闺秀的形象跑来胡闹，虽以失败告终，却勇气可嘉。
[ZH] 成亲那日，当叶安雅听说仪王前来抢亲，郡主因此而受伤时，她更加对淑真产生兴趣，认为她实在是女子中的楷模，哪怕要成亲，依然能有人那么疯狂地爱上她。
[ZH] 今日是叶安雅第一次与淑真畅谈，并未提到她感兴趣的话题。她只是边说话，边用心观察，想看出她身上有哪些独特之处，更想让自己受到淑真的影响，做出一直想做又不敢做的事情来。
[ZH] 叶安雅决定以后要跟淑真多多往来。
[ZH] 当叶安雅回到自己院中，竟然发现婆婆包氏站在面前，表情严肃。
[ZH] 叶安雅上前行礼：“婆婆，您怎么来了？”
[ZH] 包氏问：“大中午的，你不在自己院中用饭，过了饭点儿才回来，跑去哪里逛荡？”
[ZH] 婆婆用“逛荡”一词说她，叶安雅心中不满，又不敢在婆婆面前表现出来，努力微笑着：“婆婆用‘逛荡’一词太过严重，不过是找淑真聊聊，她热情邀请，我不好拒绝，顺便在她院中用饭而已。”
[ZH] 包氏不满道：“哼！你瞧瞧人家淑真，嫁过来没几日，就把老太太搞定。你呢？都嫁过来两年多，到老太太屋里去过几回？更别说帮浩东搞定老太太。还有你这肚子，怎么这么不争气？到现在也不见个动静？难怪浩东瞧不上你，总不喜欢回这院子。”
[ZH] 叶安雅不想继续被婆婆训斥：“婆婆，您要无事，我进屋了。”
[ZH] 没等包氏同意，叶安雅自顾走进屋去。
[ZH] 包氏很生气，对着身边的丫鬟说：“你瞧瞧，她哪像个脾气温顺的儿媳妇？亏得别人在外面夸她温柔孝顺，搞得我好像是个恶婆婆一样！”
[ZH] 包氏说完，没好气地走了。
[ZH] 屋内，叶安雅躺在闺床上，心中生着闷气。
[ZH] 丫鬟小翠走过来：“小姐，今日一早，我去老地方，曹公子派人送来一张纸条。”
[EN] After lunch in Moxiang Courtyard and another long chat with Shuzhen, Ye Anya took her leave with reluctance.
[ZH] 叶安雅立刻来了精神，起身，从小翠手里接过纸条，打开后看到上面写着：“上邪，我欲与君相知，长命无绝衰。山无陵，江水为竭。冬雷震震，夏雨雪。天地合，乃敢与君绝。”
[EN] Beyond being close in age, her new sister-in-law's life fascinated and inspired her. Going through a mutual divorce would have crushed most women's pride, and enduring an entire city's slander last year would have driven ordinary gentlewomen to throw themselves into the Qiantang River ten times over—yet Shuzhen had come through thriving. Betrothed to Haoyu, she had thrown ladylike decorum to the wind and stormed Chen Manor to break off the match; even in failure, her nerve commanded awe.
[ZH] 叶安雅叹道：“他对我如此用情，可我已嫁为人妇，让我如何全了这份情？”
[EN] Then on her wedding day, Prince Yi had raided the bridal procession and Princess Jiahe had taken a blade over it, making Shuzhen even more remarkable in Ye Anya's eyes—a woman who could inspire that kind of mad devotion even while wed to another.
[ZH] “小姐与曹公子相爱在先，嫁给姑爷是逼不得已。姑爷对您又打又骂，这样的日子小姐愿意过下去，我都不愿看下去。”
[EN] During this first real talk, Ye Anya had not brought up the question burning inside her. She had watched and listened instead, studying what set Shuzhen apart, hoping some of that courage would rub off on her and push her to take the step she had dreamed of yet feared to attempt. She resolved to visit Moxiang Courtyard often.
[ZH] “我也不愿跟他过下去，可婚已成，该如何是好？”
[EN] The moment Ye Anya stepped back into her own courtyard, however, her mother-in-law Madam Bao stood waiting with a thunderous face.
[ZH] “小姐不是想跟姑爷和离吗？”
[EN] Ye Anya curtsied. "Mother-in-law, what brings you here?"
[ZH] “是这么想，可要走上这条路哪有那么容易？”
[EN] "High noon, and instead of eating in your own courtyard, you come strolling back past mealtime! Where have you been gallivanting?"
[ZH] “刚嫁过来的朱家姑娘和离过，小姐不如向她取取经？”
[EN] Stung by the word "gallivanting," Ye Anya swallowed her resentment and forced a smile. "That's a bit harsh, Mother-in-law. I only went to chat with Shuzhen. She pressed me to stay for lunch, and it felt rude to refuse."
[ZH] “我今日找她正是为此事，可话到嘴边一直难以开口，等下次再找机会向她请教。”
[EN] "Hmph!" Madam Bao snapped. "Look at Shuzhen—barely married a few days and she already has Old Madam Xia eating out of her hand! And you? Over two years in this house, and how many times have you visited Old Madam Xia, let alone won her over for Haodong? Not to mention that useless belly of yours—still not a sign of an heir! No wonder Haodong has no use for you and hates setting foot in this courtyard."
[ZH] 二人正说着，陈浩东从外面进来，小翠赶紧拿过小姐手中的纸条，塞进自己袖子里，躲到一边。
[EN] Unwilling to stand there taking another tongue-lashing, Ye Anya said flatly, "If you have nothing else, Mother-in-law, I'm going inside." Without waiting for dismissal, she walked straight into her room.
[ZH] 陈浩东批评道：“你这丫头越来越没礼貌，也不知道问候我一声？”
[EN] Fuming, Madam Bao turned to her maid. "Look at that attitude! Does she act like a meek daughter-in-law? Everyone outside praises her as gentle and dutiful, making me look like some wicked mother-in-law!" With a huff, she stormed away.
[ZH] 小翠慌张地问候：“姑爷好。”
[EN] Inside, Ye Anya lay down on her bed, simmering in silent misery.
[ZH] 叶安雅已站起来：“夫君今日回来得早？”
[EN] Her maid Xiaocui hurried over. "Miss, I checked our usual spot first thing this morning. Young Master Cao sent a note."
[ZH] “怎么？嫌我回来得早？这屋里该不会藏着不该藏的人？别以为我不知道成婚前你跟曹家公子有私情。”
[EN] Life sparked back into Ye Anya's eyes. Sitting up, she unfolded the slip and read:
[ZH] 叶安雅柔弱中带着倔强：“我是与他有过一段情，都是过去的事，成亲时你也知道，又何必现在拿出来说事？夫君说我，我倒也想说说你，别以为我不知道春金蝉，你跟她在外面干的勾当我也有所耳闻。”
[EN] "By Heaven above! I would know you as my own, / Our bond unbroken, undecaying through all our years. / Only when mountains have no peaks, when rivers run dry, / When thunder rumbles in winter, snow falls in summer, / And heaven and earth fuse as one— / Only then would I dare part from you!" [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 小翠料到接下来姑爷肯定会动手，她替自家小姐感到紧张
[EN] Ye Anya let out a long sigh. "He loves me with all his heart, yet I am another man's wife. How can I ever answer such devotion?"
[ZH] 陈浩东对小翠大喊：“滚出去！”
[EN] "You and Young Master Cao loved each other first, Miss," Xiaocui whispered. "You only married the young master because you had no choice. He beats you and curses you—even if you're willing to endure this life, I can't bear watching it anymore!"
[ZH] 小翠立刻跪下：“姑爷！您千万不要打我家小姐，您上次打在她身上的伤还未全好！”
[EN] "I don't want to live with him either, yet the marriage is done. What can I do?"
[ZH] “来人！”陈浩东向门外大喊。
[EN] "Didn't you want a mutual divorce from him, Miss?"
[ZH] 进来两名下人。
[EN] "I do, yet walking that road isn't easy."
[ZH] 陈浩东命令道：“给我把小翠拉出去，把门关好。”
[EN] "The new Zhu bride went through a mutual divorce before! Why not ask her how she did it?"
[ZH] 两名下人照做。
[EN] "That's why I went to see her today, yet every time the words reached my lips, I couldn't get them out. I'll find another chance to ask her."
[ZH] 门被从外面关好后，陈浩东靠近叶安雅：“你越来越张狂，看来还是我打得太轻。”
[EN] Before Xiaocui could answer, Chen Haodong strode through the doorway. Xiaocui snatched the note from her mistress's fingers, jammed it up her sleeve, and backed to the wall.
[ZH] 叶安雅向一旁躲去：“别以为你打我，我就会屈服？你错了，你越打我我越对你失望！”
[EN] Chen Haodong scowled at her. "Getting bolder by the day, aren't you? Don't even bother greeting your master?"
[ZH] 陈浩东上前抓住叶安雅的衣领：“刚才听我娘说，你跑去跟浩宇的媳妇拉关系，我警告你，少跟她学，她在全城臭名昭著，你可得好好把你的名声给我保住，一年后，是定陈家产业继承人的日子，到时别坏了我的好事！”
[EN] "Greetings, Young Master," Xiaocui stammered.
[ZH] 叶安雅依然嘴上不饶人：“你在外人面前装得好，只有我知道你是什么货色，就你这样还想继承陈家产业……”
[EN] Ye Anya rose to her feet. "Back early today, Husband?"
[ZH] 话还没说完，陈浩东一巴掌打在她的脸上：“让你胡说！”
[EN] "What, annoyed I came back early? Hiding someone in here who doesn't belong? Don't think I don't know about your little affair with that Cao boy before our wedding."
[ZH] 接着陈浩东又是一阵猛打，打得叶安雅无力反抗时，把她扔到床上，扯去衣裳：“打完你，再让你尽妻子的义务，那才叫一个爽！”
[EN] Beneath her fragile frame flared quiet defiance. "I did have feelings for him—that was in the past, and you knew before we married. Why dredge it up now? And since you're lecturing me, let's talk about you. Don't think I haven't heard about Chun Jinchan and the filthy business you carry on with her outside."
[ZH] 叶安雅倍感屈辱，却无力反抗，只能默默忍受……
[EN] Certain a beating was coming, Xiaocui went rigid with terror for her mistress.
[ZH] 完事后，陈浩东整理好衣装，对着床上衣衫凌乱，满身是伤，满脸泪痕的妻子说：“你这身子冷冰冰的，实在无趣，难怪总也怀不上孩子，你应该多向春香楼的商妓学学怎么伺候我。”
[EN] "Get out!" Chen Haodong roared at Xiaocui.
[ZH] 叶安雅听到一阵摔门声后，才流下屈辱的泪水。
[EN] Xiaocui dropped to her knees. "Young Master, please don't hit Miss! The bruises from last time haven't even healed!"
[ZH] 小翠走进屋内，看到小姐的样子，眼泪不由流下来：“小姐，以后您还是顺着姑爷说话，不要跟他对着来，否则受伤的都是您。”
[EN] "Servants!" Chen Haodong bellowed toward the door. Two men hurried in. "Drag Xiaocui out and shut the door!"
[ZH] “面对讨厌的人，我没办法顺着。顺着他只能委屈自己的心，我宁可身上受伤，也不想心里难受。我……我要离开他，哪怕是被他休了，也要结束这段婚姻。”
[EN] They obeyed at once. Once the door clicked shut behind them, Chen Haodong stalked toward Ye Anya. "Getting mouthier every day. Guess I went too easy on you last time."
[ZH] 小翠从小姐的脸上看出比往日任何时候都坚定的表情，她感到欣慰，小姐嫁到陈家没过一天好日子，还不如彻底离开，哪怕孤身一人，被人唾骂，也比过这样的日子强。
[EN] Ye Anya edged away. "Think beating me will make me submit? You're wrong. Every blow only makes me despise you more!"
[ZH] ……
[EN] Chen Haodong lunged and grabbed her collar. "Mother just told me you went cozying up to Haoyu's wife. I'm warning you: stay clear of her bad habits! Her name is mud across Lin'an, and you'd better keep yours spotless. One year from now the clan chooses the heir to the Chen fortune—don't you dare ruin my chances!"
[ZH] 接下来的每日，说话艺人都会来陈府给老太太说书，老太太的院子里每天都围满人，不再像以前一样冷冷清清。
[EN] Ye Anya spat back without flinching. "You put on a grand act for outsiders, yet I know what kind of rot you are underneath. Someone like you expecting to inherit the Chen estate—"
[ZH] 只是没怎么见过大嫂叶安雅的身影，千里寻刚来陈家，很多事情还不了解，不敢随意多打听，也不方便随意上门询问，还是等等再说。
[EN] A stinging slap cut her off mid-sentence. "Keep running that mouth!"
[ZH] 千里寻还发现姚轻琰与陈江林也一直未出现，她便趁老太太不注意，大家也都听得认真时，悄悄离开。
[EN] A barrage of blows followed until Ye Anya had no strength left to fight back. He hurled her onto the bed and tore open her robes. "Beating you first and then making you do your wifely duty—nothing feels better!"
[ZH] 结果没走出多远看到姚轻琰带着丫鬟映雪在一棵树下徘徊。
[EN] Swallowing her humiliation, powerless to resist, Ye Anya endured in silence.
[ZH] 千里寻迎上去：“婆婆，您都到门口，为何不进去？”
[EN] When he finished, Chen Haodong straightened his clothes and sneered down at his bruised, tear-streaked wife in her rumpled garments. "Lying there cold as a slab of stone—no fun at all. No wonder you can't get pregnant! You should take lessons from the courtesans at Chunxiang Brothel on how to please a man."
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“我怕老太太见到我生气。”
[EN] Only after the door slammed behind him did Ye Anya let her tears spill over.
[ZH] 公爹和婆婆什么都好，就是不懂揣摩老太太的心思，搞得大家长期误会，不如帮着提点一下。
[EN] Xiaocui rushed back in and burst out crying at the sight of her mistress. "Miss, next time just humor him and don't talk back! You're the only one who gets hurt!"
[ZH] “婆婆多虑，奶奶是个喜欢热闹之人，若婆婆以后每日让奶奶屋里热闹些，她指定开心，又怎么会生您的气？”
[EN] "I can't humor a man I loathe. Bending to him tortures my heart. Better to carry bruises on my skin than rot inside. I'm leaving him—even if he has to write a letter of repudiation to cast me out, I'm ending this marriage."
[ZH] 姚轻琰不大相信：“这办法好使吗？”
[EN] Seeing firmer resolve in her mistress's eyes than ever before, Xiaocui felt a swell of relief. Her mistress had not known one happy day in Chen Manor; walking out—even alone, even scorned—beat living in this cage.
[ZH] “当然好使，儿媳正是用此办法把奶奶哄得笑得不拢嘴，现在对我的态度大变。”
[EN] Over the next few days, the storyteller came to Chen Manor every afternoon, turning Old Madam Xia's once-quiet courtyard into the liveliest spot in the estate.
[ZH] “为何你刚来就能揣摩到老人家的心思？我可是给她当儿媳妇当了这么久，都没发现她喜欢热闹？”
[EN] Ye Anya, however, never showed up. Still new to the household, Qianli Xun refrained from prying or barging into her cousin-in-law's quarters, deciding to wait and see.
[ZH] “我哪有那么厉害？不过是得到韦嬷嬷的提点。”
[EN] She noticed too that neither Yao Qingyan nor Chen Jianglin ever stepped inside. One afternoon, while Old Madam Xia and everyone else sat absorbed in the tale, Qianli Xun slipped out on her own—and found Yao Qingyan pacing beneath a tree just outside the gate with her maid Yingxue.
[ZH] “所以我说这临安城里许多大家闺秀都羡慕朱家姑娘身边有个韦嬷嬷这号人物。”
[EN] Qianli Xun walked right up. "Mother-in-law, you're right at the gate. Why aren't you going in?"
[ZH] 千里寻趁机夸道：“临安城里最令人羡慕的则是公爹对婆婆的好。”
[EN] "I'm afraid seeing me will only upset Old Madam Xia," Yao Qingyan murmured.
[ZH] “你这张嘴，难怪这么快能将老太太搞定。”姚轻琰笑完，脸上又出现愁容，“别人羡慕我跟你公爹之间的感情，可老太太却很嫌弃。”
[EN] Both her father-in-law and mother-in-law were wonderful people, Qianli Xun thought, just clueless about reading the old lady's heart, trapped in years of misunderstanding. Time to lend a hand.
[ZH] 千里寻提醒道：“婆婆有没有想过，奶奶见您和公爹总生气，不是因为你们感情好，而是因为别的？”
[EN] "You're overthinking it, Mother-in-law. Grandmother loves lively company. If you fill her courtyard with warmth and chatter every day, she'll be thrilled—why would she get mad at you?"
[ZH] 姚轻琰很是疑惑：“因为别的？我倒想不明白。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan looked doubtful. "Does that actually work?"
[ZH] “我把奶奶哄高兴后才知道，她每次见我不高兴，并不是真正生我的气，而是想用不高兴引起我注意，然后让我在她身上多花些心思，她也就不那么孤单。”
[EN] "Of course it works! That's the exact trick I used to get Grandmother grinning ear to ear, and look how her attitude toward me flipped overnight."
[ZH] 姚轻琰立刻醒悟：“原来因为这个！怪不得我和你公爹怎么做都做不到老太太心里，反而被她骂，她越骂，我们越是避开她。老太太也真是，不明说，让我和你公爹一直误会她。”
[EN] "How did you figure out her mind right after arriving, when I've been her daughter-in-law for years and never noticed she craved company?"
[ZH] 千里寻解释道：“奶奶告诉我，说了多没意思，我估计她是想体会别人在她身上花心思的感觉，老人家就跟小孩子一样，喜欢被人关注被人重视，以后婆婆和公爹只要日日去陪伴老太太，让她的院子热闹些，保准她会开心。”
[EN] "I'm not that clever—Matron Wei pointed it out to me."
[ZH] “我终于明白为何老太太喜欢浩宇，除了因为浩宇长得跟他死去的爷爷像，还因为浩宇喜欢去老太太屋里坐，会说些哄老太太开心的话。”姚轻琰抓住淑真的手，“淑真，陈家娶你真是娶对了，你一进门，我就明白老太太的心思，谢谢你。”
[EN] "No wonder every well-bred lady in Lin'an envies you for having Matron Wei by your side."
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“你们陈家是娶对人，我可是嫁错人！”嘴上却说：“我们别站这里只顾说话，您跟我进去，奶奶这会儿开心，绝不会生气。”
[EN] Qianli Xun grinned. "What everyone in Lin'an envies most is how Father-in-law dotes on you, Mother-in-law."
[ZH] 姚轻琰点点头，跟着淑真小心走进院中，说话艺人正讲得眉飞色舞：
[EN] "That silver tongue of yours—no mystery how you won Old Madam Xia over!" After a chuckle, sorrow crept back onto Yao Qingyan's face. "Outsiders envy our bond, yet Old Madam Xia can't stand seeing it."
[ZH] 玄知为玉面狐狸取了一个名字：落英，落英从此成为玄知殿中的婢女，负责每日伺候玄知饮食用度。
[EN] "Did you ever consider, Mother-in-law, that Grandmother isn't upset because you and Father-in-law are close, just for another reason?"
[ZH] 玄知是人人称赞的真神，拥有无上神力，在天界的地位也无人能比，连天君都要给他几分薄面。可落英却瞧不上他，只因他总是冷冰冰的，不苟言笑，甚是无趣。
[EN] "Another reason? I can't imagine what."
[ZH] 落英每日伺候完玄知，喜欢待在荷花池旁，对着一朵荷花自语。这一日她又对着荷花说：“荷花啊荷花，我整日伺候那位真神实在没趣，我想离开真神殿，到别处逛逛。”
[EN] "I only figured it out after cheering her up. Whenever she acts cross, she isn't really angry—she's just putting on a frown to get attention, hoping we'll fuss over her so she doesn't feel so lonely."
[ZH] 没想到传来一个声音：“我带你去逛。”
[EN] Light dawned across Yao Qingyan's face. "So that's it! No wonder everything your father-in-law and I did backfired and earned us a scolding—and the more she scolded, the more we steered clear! Honestly, why didn't she just say so instead of leaving us misunderstanding her all these years?"
[ZH] 落英吓坏：“哪里来的声音？”她瞧瞧周围，什么都没有。便从石头上坐起来，准备离开，一转身，看到一个帅气的男子出现在眼前。
[EN] "Grandmother says spelling it out ruins the feeling," Qianli Xun explained. "She wants to feel people making an effort on their own. Elders are just like children—they want to feel noticed and cherished. From now on, if you and Father-in-law visit her every day and keep her courtyard lively, she's bound to beam."
[ZH] 落英向后退了一步：“你是谁？”
[EN] "Now I understand why she spoils Haoyu—not just because he looks like his late grandfather, but because he visits her quarters and sweet-talks her!" Yao Qingyan squeezed Shuzhen's hand. "Marrying you into the Chen family was the best decision we ever made. Barely through the door, and you unlocked Old Madam Xia's heart for me. Thank you."
[ZH] 男子说：“我是每日听你说话的荷花，你就唤我花美男。”
[EN] Good decision for the Chens, maybe—I'm the one who got stuck with the wrong groom! Qianli Xun thought, yet said aloud, "Don't just stand out here talking. Come inside with me. Grandmother's in a great mood; she won't get mad."
[ZH] 落英“噗嗤”一笑：“好俗的名字！”……
[EN] Yao Qingyan nodded and followed Shuzhen into the yard, where the storyteller was in full swing:
[ZH] 夏老太太正听得专注，没有注意大儿媳妇进来。姚轻琰也没去打扰，与淑真走到老太太跟前。
[EN] "Xuanzhi gave the jade-faced fox a name: Luoying. From that day on, Luoying served as a maid in his celestial palace, tending to his meals and daily needs. Every immortal praised Xuanzhi, supreme in power, deferred to even by the Heavenly Sovereign—yet Luoying couldn't stand him because he stayed icy and stone-faced, dull as dishwater. "Each day after her chores, Luoying liked sitting by the lotus pond chatting to a single lotus flower. One afternoon she sighed to the bloom, 'Oh, lotus, serving that supreme deity is a bore. I want to slip out of this palace and explore the world.' "Out of nowhere, a voice answered, 'I'll take you out.' "Startled, Luoying gasped, 'Who said that?' She looked all around and saw nobody. Rising from her rock to leave, she spun around—straight into a handsome young man. "Stepping back, Luoying asked, 'Who are you?' "'I'm the lotus who listens to you every day,' the man said. 'Just call me Flower Hunk.' "Luoying burst out laughing. 'What a tacky name!'"
[ZH] 千里寻请婆婆坐在自己的位置，姚轻琰拒绝，用眼神示意她坐下。丫鬟端来椅子，姚轻琰也没有落座，站在老太太身后，伸出一双手，边听说书，边给老太太的肩膀按摩。
[EN] Absorbed in the story, Old Madam Xia did not notice her eldest daughter-in-law enter. Refusing to disturb her, Yao Qingyan stepped up beside Shuzhen. When Qianli Xun offered her own chair, Yao Qingyan shook her head and gestured for her to sit. Even when a maid brought another chair, Yao Qingyan stayed standing behind Old Madam Xia, resting her hands on the old woman's shoulders to knead them as the story unfolded.
[ZH] 夏老太太感觉到，轻轻转过头，瞅到大儿媳妇，没有说一句话，脸上依然带着笑容，比刚才多了几分满意。
[EN] Feeling the touch, Old Madam Xia turned her head just enough to see Yao Qingyan. She spoke no word, yet the smile on her face deepened with quiet contentment.
[ZH] 姚轻琰心里的石头总算落地，打今儿起，她已知道如何做老太太会开心，等江林回来，一定要告诉他。
[EN] A boulder lifted from Yao Qingyan's chest. At last she knew the key to her mother-in-law's heart, and she could not wait to tell Chen Jianglin tonight.
[ZH] 包氏看到，心想：“哼！大嫂可真会巴结老太太！”
[EN] Watching from the side, Madam Bao sneered inside: Hmph! Eldest Sister-in-law sure knows how to suck up!
[ZH] 崔氏心里也不爽，又懒得理会，继续听故事。
[EN] Madam Cui felt a twinge of sourness too, yet shrugged it off and turned back to the story.

#CH 219
[ZH] 说话艺人走后，其他族中的小辈也都渐渐离去。
[ZH] 浩飞今日没来，崔氏操心儿子，拜别过老太太回屋去。
[ZH] 包氏见姚轻琰不走，担心对方巴结老太太，把自己比下去，便留着没走。
[ZH] 千里寻见有人陪着奶奶，也知姚轻琰肯定会与老太太说心里话，便拜别而去。
[ZH] 两个媳妇把老太太搀扶着进屋坐下，老太太心中无比喜悦，院子里不仅变得热闹，大儿媳已明白她的心思，大儿子很快也会明白，以后不愁没人前来哄她开心。
[ZH] 待老太太坐定后，姚轻琰跪下：“母亲，以前是我不对，误会您老人家的心思，您要打要骂都行。”
[ZH] 老太太还未说话，包氏先不满：“姐姐这么一跪，搞得我都没办法落座。”
[ZH] 夏老太太并未理会包氏：“起来吧，往后只要你们没事到我院子里坐坐，陪我解个闷儿就行，别老把我放在这院子置之不理，搞得我还得用生气引起你们注意。”
[ZH] 姚轻琰回声：“是，以后我每日都来陪母亲。”
[ZH] 丫鬟映雪扶姚轻琰起来，她安心坐在椅子上。
[ZH] 包氏埋怨道：“母亲也是，想让人陪您直说，我让浩东和他爹日日来陪您，我也来。”
[ZH] 夏老太太说：“别站着，坐下说。你们只要心里有我，我就开心。”
[ZH] 包氏也坐下。
[ZH] ﻿被两个儿媳妇陪着聊天儿，对老太太来讲实在是少有之事，她一直笑得合不拢嘴。
[ZH] 彻底搞定老太太，千里寻心里无比轻松。
[ZH] 仔细想来，她在陈家既有财产和院子，还摆平一尊大神，又有这么好的公爹和婆婆，还有像叶安雅那样好相处的妯娌，日子逐渐顺遂起来。可为何内心深处还是高兴不起来？
[ZH] 她带着稍显落寞的心情，写下一张纸条，递给红豆：“上面有个地址，是夏果的老家，你前去告诉老人家，让他什么时候有时间来趟临安城，到陈府找我，就说陈家的老太太想听他唱曲儿。”
[ZH] 红豆接过纸条前去忙活，屋里又安静下来，也不知苦荞去了哪里？千里寻无心去想，品味着自己的内心。
[ZH] 品着品着她才觉出高兴不起来的原因，身边缺少一位与她相爱之人。
[ZH] 又想起申简辰，仔细推算，他应该快到汴梁。想起往日种种，不由悲叹个人的情路崎岖坎坷，都怨来到这个感情不能自己做主的时代。
[ZH] 苦荞从外面走进来，见小姐闲坐在桌前，手撑着下巴沉思，看上不大高兴。
[ZH] “小姐，您在想什么？”
[ZH] 千里寻并未回答，而是问道：“你去了哪里？”
[ZH] 苦荞老实交代：“说话艺人来了，我不敢随便出去，待在西院看青瓜练习功夫。”
[ZH] 千里寻放下撑着下巴手：“他练的哪是什么功夫？不过是我看他可怜，老被陈浩宇踢，教给他的一招防身术而已。他练得如何？”
[ZH] “有阿升陪练，青瓜练得越来越像模像样，动作比刚教那会儿熟练多了，速度也快许多。”
[ZH] 千里寻没有心情谈论青瓜练招数之事：“我想去看看神柳。”
[ZH] 小姐心情不好，苦荞不便多问，跟着小姐出了墨香苑，走出陈府，乘坐马车去看望神柳。
[ZH] 苦荞一直不明白小姐和仪王为何总是喜欢看神柳。她听白风说起过，幽林别居离神柳很近，仪王动不动会去神柳下坐坐，有时还会自言自语，好像神柳真能听懂人的话语一般。
[ZH] 苦荞正想着，透过马车的窗户，无意中瞧见化雨和南双在一起，看那样子应该是去陈府。
[ZH] “小姐，我刚看到化雨和南双，他们该不会是去陈府讨要您的诗词？”
[ZH] 千里寻命令车夫把马车停下：“你把他们两个喊过来。”
[ZH] 苦荞从车窗伸出脑袋：“南双！化雨！你们两个过来！”
[ZH] 两人听到呼唤声，才靠近马车。
[EN] Once the storyteller finished and left, the younger clan members drifted away as well. Chen Haofei had skipped today's session, so Madam Cui excused herself to check on him. Reluctant to let Yao Qingyan hog Old Madam Xia's ear and outshine her, Madam Bao stayed behind. Seeing Grandmother in good company—and guessing Yao Qingyan had heart-to-heart words to say—Qianli Xun bowed and left.
[ZH] 苦荞问：“你们两个怎么碰到一起？”
[EN] Together, the two daughters-in-law helped Old Madam Xia indoors. The old lady glowed inside; her courtyard hummed with life, her eldest daughter-in-law had caught on at last, her eldest son would soon follow, and she would never lack company again.
[ZH] 南双解释道：“我家公子吩咐我从今日起每隔几日到陈府讨要幽栖居士的诗词，没想到半路上碰见化雨，一问才知他也为此事而来。”
[EN] The moment Old Madam Xia sat down, Yao Qingyan dropped to her knees. "Mother, I was wrong all these years to misread your heart. Beat me or scold me however you wish."
[ZH] 苦荞说：“仔细想想，自从乔公子从汴梁回来，你确实没来讨要过，不过我家小姐打算出去一趟，等明日你们再过来。放心，诗词早已写好，就等着送你们。”
[EN] Before Old Madam Xia could speak, Madam Bao cut in tartly, "Sister kneeling like that leaves me nowhere to sit."
[ZH] 化雨赶紧说：“应该全是给我家公子写的吧？不能因为南双来，就把给我家公子的数量减少。”
[EN] Ignoring Madam Bao, Old Madam Xia told Yao Qingyan, "Get up. From now on, drop by whenever you have spare time to chat and keep me from getting bored. Don't leave me stranded in this yard forcing me to pick fights just to get noticed."
[ZH] 苦荞说：“我家小姐写诗作词又快又好，已经有七八首放在书桌上，就等你们来拿，你的数量不会少，还能多送南双几首。”
[EN] "Yes, Mother. I'll come keep you company every day." Helped to her feet by Yingxue, Yao Qingyan took a chair in peace.
[ZH] 南双与化雨无比开心，目送陈家的马车离开。
[EN] Madam Bao pouted. "Honestly, Mother, if you wanted company you only had to say so. I'll have Haodong and his father visit every day, and I'll come too."
[ZH] 千里寻一直没说话，却用心听苦荞与那两个下人的对话，这会儿才问道：“以前南双也经常来讨要我的诗词？”
[EN] "Don't stand there—sit," Old Madam Xia said. "So long as you keep me in your hearts, I'm happy."
[ZH] “哪是讨要？根本就是捡拾小姐您写废的纸张碰运气而已。”
[EN] Madam Bao sat down. Surrounded by two daughters-in-law chatting away, Old Madam Xia felt a rare surge of joy and beamed nonstop.
[ZH] “文渊从汴梁回来后，南双为何没再来碰运气？”
[EN] Having won Old Madam Xia over for good, Qianli Xun felt light on her feet. On paper, everything in Chen Manor was falling into place: she held her own courtyard and assets, had neutralized the manor's biggest boss, enjoyed warm in-laws, and had a friendly sister-in-law in Ye Anya. Life was running smooth. So why did an ache still sit hollow in her chest?
[ZH] 苦荞仔细观察小姐，小心说：“小姐自己想想就明白。”
[EN] In a subdued mood, she jotted an address on a slip of paper and handed it to Hongdou. "This is Xiaguo's hometown. Go find the old blind ballad singer and ask him to come to Chen Manor whenever he's free—tell him Old Madam Xia would love to hear him sing."
[ZH] 被苦荞这么一提醒，千里寻忽然明白过来。文渊从汴梁回来后，她对他一见钟情，然后两人相爱，有了对方的爱，又何必用诗词维系？
[EN] Hongdou took the note and set off, leaving the room hushed. Kuqiao was off somewhere, and Qianli Xun did not care to wonder where. Sitting alone with her own thoughts, the source of her gloom surfaced: she had nobody beside her whom she loved.
[ZH] 后来两个人的爱情之路遇到坎坷，文渊哪有心情讨要诗词？再后来两人在神柳下分手，文渊更没有心情。
[EN] Her thoughts drifted to Shen Jianchen, who must be nearing Bianliang by now. Remembering everything between them, she sighed at how crooked her path of love had turned, cursing this era where no one got to choose their own heart.
[ZH] 现在他派南双前来讨要，说明文渊的确已走出情伤，看来那日在醉云楼相会为两人的感情之路画上圆满的句号。
[EN] Kuqiao walked in and spotted her mistress propping her chin on her hand at the desk, staring off in low spirits. "What's on your mind, Miss?"
[ZH] 想到这里，千里寻露出笑容，她觉得两个人的感情之路实在有趣，一起踏上时无比幸福，一起走出时竟然也可以轻松而又开心。
[EN] Skipping the question, Qianli Xun asked, "Where were you?"
[ZH] 很快她的笑容又消失，她悄悄踏上与申简辰的感情之路，却从未开心过，准确来讲，是不敢开心，一旦出现一丝一毫的开心，都会觉得自己是个道德败坏之人。也不知这段情什么时候结束？她也能和申简辰一起为这段情画上一个令大家都开心的句号。
[EN] "With the storyteller here, I didn't dare show my face, so I went to the West Courtyard to watch Qinggua practice martial arts."
[ZH] 其实千里寻时时刻刻想在心中将句号画上，可每次画到一半儿，她就无法再画下去，她告诫自己，为何要这么委屈？难道把喜欢的人放在心里也不行吗？
[EN] Qianli Xun lowered her hand. "Hardly martial arts—just one self-defense trick I taught him out of pity so Chen Haoyu would quit kicking him. How's he doing?"
[ZH] 于是她在矛盾中还是把申简辰放在心里。
[EN] "With Asheng sparring against him, he's looking pretty sharp—much cleaner and faster than when you first showed him."
[ZH] 马车出了城，来到山脚下。
[EN] Qianli Xun had no interest in Qinggua's drills. "I want to go see the Divine Willow."
[ZH] 千里寻走下马车，向神柳处走去。一路上，春风带着柳絮拂面而来。再瞧瞧脚下，零零散散地躺着些薄如羽翼，洁白轻柔的柳絮。
[EN] Recognizing her mistress's dark mood, Kuqiao asked no questions, accompanying her out of Moxiang Courtyard and into a carriage bound for the hills. Kuqiao had never quite grasped why her mistress and Prince Yi stayed so attached to that tree. Bai Feng had told her that since Youlin Retreat sat close by, Prince Yi went there all the time, sometimes even talking to himself as if the willow could hear him.
[ZH] 靠近神柳，眼前又多出一种色彩，如果说红绸像是为神柳穿上嫁衣，那么柳絮就像是为新娘披上白色的轻纱。
[EN] Lost in thought, Kuqiao glanced out the carriage window and spotted Huayu walking alongside Nan Shuang, both headed toward Chen Manor.
[ZH] 千里寻被自己古今结合的想象逗乐，谁让神柳是跨越千年之物，历经古代和现代，它肯定不会埋怨她不搭调的想象。
[EN] "Miss, I just saw Huayu and Nan Shuang! Must be on their way to Chen Manor to ask for your poems."
[ZH] 再向池塘中瞧去，初长出来的荷叶在水面铺开，上面也覆盖着薄薄的一层柳絮，没有荷叶的水面也盖着一层。
[EN] Qianli Xun told the driver to stop. "Call them over."
[ZH] 如此富有诗意的画面，决不能辜负朱淑真的才情，千里寻随口吟出：
[EN] Kuqiao leaned out the window. "Nan Shuang! Huayu! Come over here!"
[ZH] 缭绕晴空似雪飞，悠扬不肯着尘泥。
[EN] Hearing her call, both servants hurried up to the carriage.
[ZH] 花边娇软黏蜂翅，陌上轻狂趁马蹄。
[EN] "How did you two bump into each other?" Kuqiao asked.
[ZH] 贴水化萍随浪远，弄风无影度墙低。
[EN] "My master ordered me to visit Chen Manor every few days from now on to ask for Youqi Jushi's new verses," Nan Shuang explained. "Ran right into Huayu on the road, and it turns out he's on the same errand."
[ZH] 成团作阵愁春去，欲把东君归路迷
[EN] "Come to think of it, you haven't come asking once since Young Master Qiao got back from Bianliang," Kuqiao noted. "Miss is heading out right now, so come back tomorrow. Don't worry—she already wrote plenty, all set for you."
[ZH] 《柳絮》
[EN] Huayu jumped in at once. "Those are for my master, right? Don't cut our share just because Nan Shuang showed up!"
[ZH] 小姐刚吟完诗，苦荞拍手道：“小姐的诗写得真好！尤其是‘花边娇软黏蜂翅’，太有生活气息，我刚刚就看到有只蜜蜂只顾采花蜜，飞到那朵野花上，结果被落在花瓣上的柳絮给绊住，它扑棱翅膀，半天才甩开，刚刚飞走。”
[EN] "My mistress writes fast and well," Kuqiao laughed. "Seven or eight new sheets are already sitting on her desk. You won't lose a single one, and there'll still be extra for Nan Shuang."
[ZH] 千里寻用微笑回应，苦荞的夸赞只能说明朱淑真观察生活十分细致入微。她突然明白一个道理，无论是古人写诗作词，还是她穿越前写网络小说，都需细致观察生活。
[EN] Thrilled, both servants watched the Chen carriage roll on.
[ZH] 仔细想来，穿越前她之所以遭受网暴，不过是因为她写的书越来越脱离生活。她打算在这个时代改编话本时，一定要融入属于这个时代的生活气息，哪怕是写神仙的故事，也要让听众听了觉得是在写自己的故事。
[EN] Having listened in silence the whole time, Qianli Xun now asked, "Nan Shuang used to come asking for my poems all the time?"
[ZH] 千里寻来此并不是为想通写作之事，她是为心中的情愫而来。她看到神柳下的石头，打算过去坐坐。
[EN] "Hardly asking—he used to scavenge the drafts you threw away and try his luck."
[ZH] 走近后，发现上面盖着一层柳絮。她弯下腰，吹出一口气，那层柳絮向周围飞散而去，石头上立刻变得干净。
[EN] "Then why did he stop after Wenyuan returned from Bianliang?"
[ZH] 苦荞担心石头凉，上前掏出巾帕，在石头上铺好，然后站在一旁默默陪伴。
[EN] Kuqiao studied her mistress with care. "Think about it, Miss, and you'll know."
[ZH] 千里寻坐下，背靠着神柳的树干。
[EN] The hint made it clear in an instant. After returning from Bianliang, Wenyuan and she had fallen hard for each other; with love in hand, why cling to scrap poems? Then their romance hit rocks, leaving him in no mood for verses, and after their breakup beneath the Divine Willow, even less so. Now he had sent Nan Shuang back for poetry—proof that Wenyuan had healed at last. That final talk at Zuiyun Tower had given their story a clean, proper closing period.
[ZH] 她虽没见过，但能想象到申简辰会时常来这里坐着，他一定会像她现在一样。
[EN] A smile touched Qianli Xun's lips. Funny how love worked: stepping onto the road together felt ecstatic, yet walking off it with peace could feel light and freeing too.
[ZH] 她闭上眼睛想象他坐在石头上的情景，是否和自己一样默默体会内心的情感。
[EN] Then her smile faded. She had wandered onto a secret road with Shen Jianchen and never once felt happy—or rather, never dared feel happy, because even a flicker of joy felt like betrayal. When would this chapter close with a clean period everyone could smile at? She kept trying to draw that period inside her heart, yet stalled halfway every time. Why torture herself? Was she not even allowed to keep the man she loved locked away inside her own heart? Torn between guilt and longing, she left Shen Jianchen right where he was.
[ZH] 千里寻想到白风在树下训练小猪爬树的情景，既感动于申简辰想要让不可能变为可能的想法，又觉得好笑。
[EN] Beyond the city gates, the carriage halted at the foot of the hills. Qianli Xun stepped down and walked toward the Divine Willow. A warm spring breeze brushed her cheeks with drifting willow catkins, while more lay scattered underfoot like white down feathers.
[ZH] 过了一会儿，她睁开眼：“苦荞，你知道吗？白风竟然在神柳下训练小猪爬树。”
[EN] Drawing near the great tree, she took in the fresh contrast: if the crimson silk ribbons dressed the Divine Willow in bridal red, the swirling white catkins draped a wedding veil over the bride. The mashup of ancient and modern imagery amused her; a tree a thousand years old straddled both eras anyway and would hardly mind.
[ZH] 苦荞没亲眼见过白风训练小猪爬树，可此事她是知道的。
[EN] Across the pond, young lotus pads floated on the surface, dusted in a thin white coat of catkins, just like the open water between them. Such a scene begged for Zhu Shuzhen's voice, and Qianli Xun recited aloud:
[ZH] “我听白风说起过，是仪王让他买来小猪训练爬树，也不知仪王是怎么想的？竟然想让猪爬树，听着都觉得好笑。”
[EN] "Whirling through the bright sky like drifting snow, / Floating aloft, refusing to touch the mud and dust. / Soft and clinging by the flowers, snagging a bee's wing, / Wild and reckless on the lane, chasing a horse's hooves. / Pressed to water, turning to duckweed drifting far on waves, / Riding the breeze, shadowless, slipping over low walls. / Gathering in clusters, grieving spring's departure, / Trying to confuse the Lord of Spring on his homeward road." —"Willow Catkins" [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] “是很好笑，他不过是想让不可能的事变为可能。他却不明白不可能的事又怎么会变为可能？却傻乎乎地做着无畏的努力。”
[EN] Kuqiao clapped the instant she finished. "What a poem, Miss! Especially 'Soft and clinging by the flowers, snagging a bee's wing'—so true to life! I literally just watched a honeybee dive into a wildflower, get tangled in catkins on the petals, and buzz like crazy before shaking free!"
[ZH] 千里寻又失落地闭上眼睛。
[EN] Qianli Xun smiled. Kuqiao's praise only proved how keenly Zhu Shuzhen observed real life. A sudden insight struck her: whether composing Song poetry or writing modern web fiction, staying rooted in real life made all the difference. Back in her old world, the cyberbullying had snowballed because her books drifted further and further from reality. Adapting huaben in this era, she would weave the living texture of Song daily life into every line—even tales of gods and fox spirits should make listeners hear their own lives echoing back.
[ZH] 通过小姐的慨叹，苦荞听出些意味来，毕竟她对小姐与仪王的事情再清楚不过。
[EN] Still, she had not come here for writing advice; she had come for her heart. Spotting the boulder beneath the willow, she walked over and blew a breath across its top, scattering the coat of catkins clean away. Worried the stone would chill her mistress, Kuqiao spread a handkerchief over it and stood quiet beside her.
[ZH] 千里寻继续在神柳下感受着内心的情感，她不敢向神柳祈求任何与情感相关的希望，只因任何祈求都会是对梦容的伤害，她要将爱藏在心底，带着爱在陈家老老实实当好儿媳妇。
[EN] Qianli Xun sat with her back against the trunk. Though she had never seen him here, she could picture Shen Jianchen sitting on this exact stone, leaning against this exact bark, wrestling with his own feelings. Remembering Bai Feng trying to train a piglet to climb the tree beneath these very branches brought a lump to her throat—half moved by his refusal to accept the impossible, half amused.
[ZH] 也不知坐了多久，终于坐累，她起身带着苦荞打算离开。
[EN] After a pause, she opened her eyes. "Kuqiao, did you know Bai Feng was out here trying to train a piglet to climb the Divine Willow?"
[ZH] 刚走出没几步，瞧见一男一女从后山走来。先瞧到那男的，正是曹家公子曹班。遇到熟人，千里寻打算上前打招呼，却看到那女子正是大嫂叶安雅，她手里拿着红绸，一脸幸福。
[EN] Kuqiao had never seen it firsthand, yet knew the tale. "Bai Feng told me Prince Yi had him buy a piglet and teach it to climb trees. Heaven knows what Prince Yi was thinking—pigs climbing trees! Sounds hilarious."
[ZH] 千里寻感到惊讶，打消上前打招呼的念头，与苦荞躲到一棵树的后面，偷偷向神柳下望去。
[EN] "It is hilarious. He just wanted to turn the impossible into the possible. Doesn't get that impossible means impossible, yet throws himself into hopeless effort anyway." Qianli Xun closed her eyes again, heavy-hearted.
[ZH] 曹班牵着叶安雅的手，他们一起来到神柳下，一起将红绸挂在柳枝上，然后曹班搂抱着叶安雅，两人看上去既般配又和谐。
[EN] Knowing everything between her mistress and Prince Yi, Kuqiao caught every layer beneath that sigh.
[ZH] 苦荞小声说：“小姐，陈家大公子要是知道，恐怕大少夫人要遭殃。”
[EN] Qianli Xun sat quiet, daring not pray to the Divine Willow for any romantic miracle—any prayer of hers meant hurting Mengrong. Better to lock her feelings deep down and live out her days as a dutiful Chen daughter-in-law.
[ZH] 千里寻提醒道：“此事必然有隐情，记住，永远将今日看到的烂在心里，对谁都不能提起。”
[EN] At length, stiff from sitting, she stood with Kuqiao to head back.
[ZH] 苦荞说：“是，小姐，我定会守口如瓶。”
[EN] Barely a few steps down the path, a man and woman walked around from the back hill. She recognized the man first—Cao Ban. About to step out and say hello, she froze at the sight of the woman beside him: Eldest Sister-in-law Ye Anya, clutching a red silk ribbon with a face radiant with joy.
[ZH] 趁神柳下的人还没注意到，千里寻带苦荞匆匆离开。
[EN] Shocked, Qianli Xun pulled Kuqiao behind a tree trunk and peered out. Cao Ban held Ye Anya's hand as they walked up to the Divine Willow, knotted their red ribbon together onto a branch, and embraced—looking every inch a devoted pair.
[ZH] 她已无心想自己的感情问题，倒是为叶安雅悲叹起来。看来这个世道不止自己无法与所爱之人在一起，还有许多像朱淑真，像叶安雅这样的人。
[EN] "Miss," Kuqiao breathed, "if Eldest Young Master Chen finds out, Eldest Young Madam is doomed!"
[ZH] 千里寻更加怀念穿越前的时代，自由惯了并不觉得自由可贵，真正体验一回不自由才明白千年以来人们的努力有多重要。想要回到穿越前的时代已不可能，不如吸取经验教训，珍惜在这个时代该珍惜的。
[EN] "There's bound to be a story behind this," Qianli Xun warned in a whisper. "Remember: take what we saw today to your grave. Never breathe a word to a soul."
[ZH] 在这个时代她该珍惜什么？扪心自问，她得出一个令她满意的答案，那便是她能够拥有的。
[EN] "Yes, Miss. My lips are sealed."
[ZH] 首先想到亲情。在这个时代她既父母双全，还有哥哥嫂嫂，甚至有一位一百二十岁高龄的老祖宗，到今年老人家又长一岁。嫁到陈家，又有一位奶奶，还有明事理公爹和婆婆，这些人她都要珍惜。
[EN] Before the couple noticed them, Qianli Xun and Kuqiao slipped back down the path.
[ZH] 其次想到友情。与三位公子的诗会之情，与文渊的知己之情，与想绒姑娘的相识之情，还有与梦容的闺中密友之情。
[EN] Her own romantic woes faded behind a wave of sorrow for Ye Anya. She was far from the only woman in this era barred from being with the man she loved; countless others like Zhu Shuzhen and Ye Anya lived trapped in the same cage. Never had Qianli Xun missed her modern world more—taken for granted when free, priceless once lost. Returning was impossible, so she would treasure whatever was truly hers to hold here.
[ZH] 想到梦容，自然想到抢婚那日，她的一巴掌，还有说出的恨她的话。
[EN] First came family: loving parents, a brother and sister-in-law, a hundred-and-twenty-year-old Old Ancestor (another year older now), plus a Chen grandmother and kind in-laws. Second came friendship: the three scholars of the poetry salon, Wenyuan as a confidant, Xiangrong, and her bosom friend Mengrong.
[ZH] ﻿即便如此，千里寻从未埋怨过梦容，依然将她视作闺中密友，不管以后梦容如何对待她，她永远是她在这个时代唯一的闺中密友。
[EN] Thinking of Mengrong brought back the slap on her wedding day and those words of hatred. Even so, Qianli Xun held no grudge. No matter how Mengrong treated her from here on out, she would remain her one true confidante in this era.

#CH 220
[ZH] 陈江林从绸缎庄回来，姚轻琰将今日之事告知。他才恍然大悟，原来自己一直误会母亲，也承认自己就是那种嘴上说孝顺，其实并不懂什么是孝顺的人。
[ZH] 于是携着姚轻琰去拜见夏老太太。姚轻琰刚从老太太房中回来不久，再次前去，并不觉麻烦，现在老太太见她和颜悦色，自然乐意多去坐坐。
[ZH] 夏老太太已料到大儿子会来，坐在榻上拿本诗词集翻阅，专挑孙媳妇写的，边翻边疑惑：“淑真写的诗词悲悲切切，怎么与她的性情不大一样？”
[ZH] 旁边的丫鬟说：“少夫人嫁到陈家前经过的事儿老太太您是知道的，有那些个经历，少夫人怎能高兴得起来？”
[ZH] 夏老太太点点头：“说得也是，任谁有那些经历也高兴不起来，她现在嫁到陈家一切慢慢会好。”
[ZH] 正说着大儿子与儿媳妇进来，老太太放下诗集：“回来了，今日可辛苦？”
[ZH] 陈江林喜出望外，自从文心出生，母亲可是第一次一见面对他和颜悦色，赶紧带姚轻琰上前行礼：“为自家做事，儿子不敢谈辛苦。”
[ZH] 姚轻琰刚要俯下身，听到老太太说：“我儿媳今日都给我行过礼，不必再行。轻琰，你过来，陪我坐着，让他行礼。”
[ZH] 姚轻琰迅速站直，毫不客气，坐在老太太对面。
[ZH] 陈江林向母亲行完礼，坐在一旁的椅子上，等待母亲开口。
[ZH] 老太太说：“你们俩有个好儿媳，我则有个好孙媳，要不是她用心揣度我的心思，估计你们永远都不知道我想要的是什么？”
[EN] When Chen Jianglin returned from the silk house, Yao Qingyan told him everything that had happened. Enlightenment hit him like a thunderbolt: he had misread his mother for decades, talking about filial duty without grasping what it really meant.
[ZH] “母亲恕罪，儿子不孝，不懂得揣摩母亲的心思，一直误解母亲。多亏淑真，她一来问题就解决。说明揣度母亲的心思并非难事，更令儿子自责，这么简单的事儿子却忽略，实在有负一家之主的身份。”
[EN] Together, he and Yao Qingyan went straight to call on Old Madam Xia. Though Yao Qingyan had just left her courtyard a short while ago, she came gladly now that the old lady greeted her with warmth.
[ZH] “也不必如此说自己，以后你们常来陪陪我，让我这院子热闹一些，我便心满意足，还可多活几年。”
[EN] Expecting her eldest son, Old Madam Xia sat on her couch flipping through a volume of poetry—picking out her granddaughter-in-law's verses and frowning in puzzlement. "Shuzhen's poems read so mournful and brokenhearted. Doesn't match her spirited temper at all."
[ZH] “只要生意上不忙，我会每日和轻琰前来陪母亲，让母亲的院子热热闹闹，您一高兴定能长命百岁。”
[EN] "You know what Young Madam went through before marrying into our house, Old Madam," a maid beside her observed. "After all that, how could anyone feel cheerful?"
[ZH] 一屋子的人都乐得笑起来。
[EN] "True enough," Old Madam Xia nodded. "Anyone in her shoes would hurt. Now that she's in the Chen family, things will turn around."
[ZH] 老太太笑完，叹口气：“唉，我们是好了，我孙子还在外面逛荡，整日不着家，到现在连房都没圆，这么下去也不是个事儿。”
[EN] Just then Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan walked in. Old Madam Xia laid the book aside. "Back already? Tiring day?"
[ZH] 与老太太关系缓和，姚轻琰说话也变得大胆：“只要母亲和我不拦着，等浩宇回来，让江林好好收拾他，再把他关起来，看他怎么往外跑。”
[EN] Overjoyed—this was the first warm welcome his mother had given him at first sight since Wenxin's birth—Chen Jianglin drew Yao Qingyan forward to bow. "Working for our own family is no hardship, Mother."
[ZH] 老太太果然没有生气，反而自责起来：“我是宠着浩宇，谁让他跟他的爷爷长得像？再说以前这家里只有浩宇最会哄我开心。你们那会儿不懂我心思，每次江林教育他时，我总是忍不住发火阻止，现在想来是害了浩宇。”
[EN] As Yao Qingyan bent to curtsy, Old Madam Xia waved her upright. "You already bowed to me today, Qingyan. Sit right here across from me and let him do the bowing."
[ZH] 姚轻琰也跟着自责：“不能全怪母亲，我也做得不对，担心江林把儿子打坏，也总护着。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan straightened without hesitation and settled opposite her mother-in-law, while Chen Jianglin completed his bow and took a side chair.
[ZH] 陈江林笑着问：“听这意思，你们都同意我严厉教训那小子？”
[EN] "You two got yourselves a gem of a daughter-in-law, and I got a gem of a granddaughter-in-law," Old Madam Xia declared. "If she hadn't bothered reading my mind, you two might never have figured out what I wanted."
[ZH] 姚轻琰先说：“我提议的，当然同意，以后不管你怎么管教，我绝不拦着。”
[EN] "Forgive your unfilial son, Mother," Chen Jianglin said. "I was too thickheaded to understand you and misread you for years. Shuzhen solved it the moment she arrived—which only shames me more for overlooking something so simple as head of the household."
[ZH] 老太太神秘地笑道：“我看这家里轮不上我们三个管教浩宇。”
[EN] "Stop beating yourself up. Just drop by often to keep my courtyard lively, and I'll be content enough to live a few extra years."
[ZH] 姚轻琰醒悟过来：“母亲说的是淑真？”
[EN] "Whenever business allows, Qingyan and I will come keep you company every day so this yard stays full of cheer—and you'll live to be a hundred!"
[ZH] 夏老太太点点头：“是她，她是绝好人选。”
[EN] Laughter filled the room.
[ZH] 陈江林说：“之前儿子和轻琰让淑真教育浩宇，不过是见她不怕母亲，自然不会在意您的阻拦，现在既然母亲与我和轻琰能畅所欲言，也同意我严厉管教，就不用再麻烦淑真。”
[EN] When the chuckles died down, Old Madam Xia sighed. "We've patched things up, yet my grandson is still out gallivanting, never home, and hasn't even consummated his marriage. This can't go on."
[ZH] 夏老太太说：“道理虽如此，可我总觉得让淑真管教浩宇好处多多。一来，让她管教浩宇的话已说出，不好收回；二来，我们三个也可落得清闲；三来，也是最重要的，淑真管教浩宇，两人一来二去接触良多，就像两块石头，碰撞多了，保不准擦不出火花来。”
[EN] Emboldened by the truce, Yao Qingyan spoke up. "If neither you nor I step in to shield him, Mother, the moment Haoyu comes home Jianglin can give him a proper thrashing and lock him inside. Let's see him run wild then!"
[ZH] 陈江林对第三个好处十分认可：“母亲高明，就这么办。”
[EN] Far from taking offense, Old Madam Xia blamed herself. "I spoiled him because he looks just like his late grandfather, and back then he was the only one who knew how to cheer me up. Because you two didn't get me, I flew off the handle whenever Jianglin tried to discipline him. Looking back, I only hurt the boy."
[ZH] 姚轻琰担忧道：“可淑真跟浩宇签下婚前契约，互不干涉，我们虽叮嘱让她多管教浩宇，可她根本不当回事，浩宇都出去多少日，她跟个没事儿人似的，只顾哄母亲开心。”
[EN] "Not all on you, Mother," Yao Qingyan admitted. "I worried Jianglin would beat him too hard and shielded him too."
[ZH] 夏老太太再次自责：“都怪我，浩宇回门那日，没钱花，跑来哄我两句，我没忍住，给他五百两银票，他才又不着家。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin grinned. "Sounds like you both approve of me cracking down on that rascal?"
[ZH] 陈江林露出笑容，分析道：“母亲不必自责，由此事看来，无形中淑真已管教住浩宇。方管事给我说过，浩宇那日找他从账上支取银子，方管事说需要拿来淑真的手谕，浩宇根本不敢找淑真要，才跑到您这里来。”
[EN] "I proposed it, of course I approve!" Yao Qingyan declared. "Discipline him however you like—I won't step in."
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“如此看来，把浩宇名下的财产给淑真还是给对了。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia smiled mysteriously. "I doubt it will even fall to the three of us to whip Haoyu into shape."
[ZH] 夏老太太笑道：“淑真已从管钱财开始管教起浩宇，以后不怕管不好他。”
[EN] Understanding flashed across Yao Qingyan's face. "You mean Shuzhen, Mother?"
[ZH] 姚轻琰提醒道：“母亲以后可不能再给他银子花。”
[EN] "Precisely. She's the ideal candidate."
[ZH] 夏老太太说：“不会了，不会了。”
[EN] "Earlier, Qingyan and I asked Shuzhen to handle him only because she wasn't afraid of you blocking her, Mother," Chen Jianglin said. "Now that we're all on the same page and you'll let me discipline him, why dump the burden on Shuzhen?"
[ZH] 三人正说着，陈江树和陈江木也一起进来。他们听各自的夫人说道一番，懂得了老太太的心思，哄老太太的事不能只让大哥独占，二人赶着前来拜见，正好在大门口遇上，便一起进来。
[EN] "Fair point, yet letting Shuzhen handle him brings three big gains," Old Madam Xia counted off. "First, we already handed her the job, and taking it back looks bad. Second, it saves the three of us the headache. Third, and most important—while she disciplines Haoyu, they'll be rubbing elbows nonstop. Bang two rocks together enough times and sparks are bound to fly!"
[ZH] 二人上前拜见老太太，也向大哥大嫂问好，然后落座。
[EN] That third point won Chen Jianglin over on the spot. "Brilliant as always, Mother. Let's do it."
[ZH] 陈江树先说话：“母亲以后有什么需要，不要藏着掖着让儿子们猜，您只要说出来，我们三兄弟哪个不敢照办？”
[EN] "One snag," Yao Qingyan fretted. "They signed that prenuptial contract agreeing not to meddle in each other's lives. We told Shuzhen to rein him in, yet she hasn't lifted a finger. Haoyu's been gone for days and she acts like he doesn't exist, focused solely on entertaining you, Mother."
[ZH] 陈江木应和道：“是啊，母亲，不要瞒着我们，大哥、二哥还有我整日忙陈家的生意，总会有所疏忽，再说我们都是男子，心思没那么细腻，怎知母亲心里的小九九？”
[EN] Guilt washed over Old Madam Xia. "My fault. The day of the return visit, he came whining he was broke, sweet-talked me a bit, and I caved and handed him a five-hundred-tael banknote. That's why he vanished again."
[ZH] 要搁往日，这两兄的一通埋怨，老太太指定生气，今日她心情好，不想跟他们计较：“你们三个刚出生时，一个个不会说话，我猜你们的心思一人得猜到一岁多，怎么？现在让你们猜猜我的心思，倒不乐意？”
[EN] Chen Jianglin smiled knowingly. "Don't blame yourself, Mother. That actually proves Shuzhen already has him on a leash without lifting a finger. Steward Fang told me Haoyu tried to draw silver from the ledger that day, and when Fang demanded Shuzhen's written order, Haoyu didn't even dare ask her—so he ran straight to you."
[ZH] 三兄弟都说道：“儿子乐意猜。”
[EN] "Transferring his properties to Shuzhen really was the right call," Yao Qingyan agreed.
[ZH] 老太太先向老二叮嘱：“你把浩东教育得十分能干，我很欣慰，可也得教他对他的娘子好些。你们娶媳妇时，我都会叮嘱要对媳妇好，你们三个都做得不错，为何到自己的儿子娶媳妇时不知道叮嘱？我可是听说浩东经常打安雅。”
[EN] "Controlling his purse strings is step one," Old Madam Xia chuckled. "Give her time and she'll have him trained."
[ZH] 陈江树说：“母亲放心，等我回去，定好好批评浩东，让他对安雅好些。”
[EN] "No more slipping him silver from now on, Mother!" Yao Qingyan cautioned.
[ZH] 老太太继续给老三叮嘱：“浩飞是个好孩子，一心扑在书上，未来要能考上功名，我们陈家也算祖坟上冒了青烟。不过这孩子不能光扑在书本上，还要多与人往来，未来考上功名也需与人打交道，学问不光在书本上，与人如何交往，如何说话书本上可学不来。”
[EN] "Not a copper more, I promise."
[ZH] 陈江木说：“母亲叮嘱得是，我会好好教育浩飞。他近日倒是进步不少，从浩宇的媳妇那里得了几首诗词，高兴得不行，三天两头拿出去参加诗会，接触的都是文人才子，也不乏像他一样考取功名之人。”
[EN] Just then Chen Jiangshu and Chen Jiangmu walked in together. Briefed by their wives on the old lady's secret craving for attention, neither wanted their eldest brother hogging all the filial credit; bumping into each other at the gate, they came in side by side, bowed to Old Madam Xia, greeted their eldest brother and sister-in-law, and took seats.
[ZH] 老太太满意地点点头，更觉这位孙媳妇是娶对了。
[EN] "Mother, whenever you want something from now on, don't bottle it up and make us guess," Chen Jiangshu spoke first. "Just say the word—would any of us three sons dare refuse?"
[ZH] 一家人又聊会儿家常，等老太太累了要休息时，大家才离开，三兄弟在母亲院外也互相分开，各自回屋。
[EN] "Exactly, Mother," Chen Jiangmu chimed in. "Eldest Brother, Second Brother, and I spend all day buried in family business and overlook things. We're men, not subtle enough to guess what's turning over in your head."
[ZH] 回屋的路上，姚轻琰问：“要不要派人将浩宇找回来？”
[EN] Any other day, those complaints would have set Old Madam Xia's temper flaring; today she felt too happy to care. "When you three were babies unable to talk, I spent over a year guessing every single grunt and cry from each of you! Now I ask you to guess my mind a little, and you complain?"
[ZH] 陈江林说：“他跑出去是常有之事，以前我们不找，现在也不用，他把钱花完自然会回来，我们只管配合淑真教育他便是。”
[EN] All three brothers chorused, "We're glad to guess, Mother!"
[ZH] ……
[EN] Old Madam Xia turned to her second son first. "You raised Haodong into a capable young man, which pleases me—yet you need to teach him how to treat his wife. When each of you married, I told you to cherish your wives, and all three of you listened. Why didn't you pass that lesson to your own son? I hear Haodong beats Anya regularly."
[ZH] 大约过了七八日，墨香苑池塘里的荷叶冒出水面，铺展开来，虽不大，却也给整个院子增添不少生气。
[EN] "Rest assured, Mother," Chen Jiangshu promised. "I'll give Haodong a stern talking-to tonight and make him treat Anya better."
[ZH] 这段时日，千里寻除了陪老太太，只顾写话本，脑子里根本想不起陈浩宇，仿佛她来到陈家只是为了当女儿和孙女。
[EN] She turned next to her third son. "Haofei is a fine lad, devoted to his studies. If he wins an official degree, our ancestors will smile down on us. Still, don't let him bury his nose in books alone—he needs to mingle and learn to handle people. Real learning isn't just on paper; knowing how to converse and deal with people isn't taught in textbooks."
[ZH] 只有当婆婆和奶奶偶尔问她，浩宇可回来？千里寻才想起自己已嫁给她讨厌的人。
[EN] "Wise advice, Mother," Chen Jiangmu agreed. "Actually, he's improved lately. Haoyu's bride gifted him a few poems, and he's been thrilled, heading out to poetry salons every couple of days and mixing with scholars and fellow exam candidates."
[ZH] 婆婆和奶奶让她差人把陈浩宇找回来，严加管教。千里寻表面上应承，心里老大不乐意。
[EN] Old Madam Xia nodded, even firmer in her belief that they had picked the right bride.
[ZH] 陈家人什么都好，就是太瞧得起她，陈浩宇那等货色，她才懒得理，更别说管教。
[EN] The family chatted until Old Madam Xia grew drowsy, then dispersed to their separate courtyards.
[ZH] 也许是长辈见她没什么强烈的反应，便不好再提。她们脸上露出的失落的情绪，倒让千里寻感到自责。
[EN] Walking home, Yao Qingyan asked, "Should we send men to fetch Haoyu back?"
[ZH] 七八日的时间蒋爷身上的伤终于好得差不多，处理完一些帮中大事，想着陈浩宇可能还会到春香楼找春金沫，便带着手下冲进春香楼。
[EN] "He always vanishes for days at a stretch," Chen Jianglin said. "We never chased him before, and we won't start now. Once his silver runs dry, he'll crawl home on his own, and we'll back Shuzhen up in teaching him a lesson."
[ZH] 他只带来五人前来，主要是上次带的人多，闹得阵仗大些，把林统制都给招来，最后也没落下什么好处。他不想再招惹官府，人带少些，不那么招摇，更不会引起官府注意。
[EN] Seven or eight days slipped by. Young lotus leaves broke the surface of the Moxiang Courtyard pond, small yet breathing fresh green life into the yard.
[ZH] 蒋爷带的人虽少，进入春香楼的气势还是无人能敌，必然引起大部分人注意。
[EN] All week, Qianli Xun split her time between keeping Old Madam Xia company and writing her huaben, forgetting Chen Haoyu existed—as if she had moved into Chen Manor just to play daughter and granddaughter. Only when Yao Qingyan or Old Madam Xia casually asked whether Haoyu had come home did she remember she was married to the man she detested. Whenever they hinted she should send men to haul him back and straighten him out, she nodded on the outside while rolling her eyes inside. The Chens were great folks, yet they overestimated her willingness to reform a playboy she could not stand. Still, watching the quiet disappointment cross their faces when she did nothing pricked her conscience.
[ZH] 春香楼的妈妈迎上去：“蒋爷来了，快请里面坐。”
[EN] Meanwhile, after seven or eight days of recovery, Master Jiang's wounds had healed enough to get moving. Settling a few gang matters, he figured Chen Haoyu would still be hanging around Chun Jinmo and marched straight into Chunxiang Brothel.
[ZH] 蒋爷被引到桌旁坐下，用色眯眯的眼睛瞅着台上跳舞的春金沫：“等她一会儿表演完，让她下来陪爷爷。”
[EN] He brought only five men this time; making too big a scene last time had drawn Commandant Lin's troops with nothing to show for it. Keeping the crew small avoided attracting official eyes.
[ZH] 春香楼的妈妈面露为难之色：“恐怕不行，她现在被陈公子给包下。”
[EN] Even with five men, Master Jiang swaggered in with enough menace to turn every head in the room.
[ZH] 蒋爷起身朝陈浩宇的座位走去。
[EN] The brothel madam hurried over. "Welcome, Master Jiang! Right this way!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇早已注意到蒋爷，见对方身边只带五人，便没当回事。他身边有三位高手，对付那五人不在话下。因此，逍遥自在坐在原位继续欣赏春金沫的舞姿。
[EN] Settling into a table, Master Jiang leered up at Chun Jinmo dancing on stage. "When she finishes, send her down to keep me company."
[ZH] 蒋爷在陈浩宇的桌旁坐下：“陈公子为何还来找春金沫？”
[EN] The madam pulled an apologetic face. "I'm afraid I can't, Master Jiang. Young Master Chen has booked her exclusively."
[ZH] 字据早已毁掉，陈浩宇不再有任何顾虑：“呵呵，蒋爷设了那么大的赌局，失算了吧？我虽娶了母老虎，怎奈本公子有收服母老虎的本事，她现在乖得跟只小猫一样，根本不限制本公子自由，天天对我喵喵叫，可温柔了，我想去哪里就去哪里，羡慕吧？”
[EN] Master Jiang stood and strode straight over to Chen Haoyu's table.
[ZH] 蒋爷被气到：“陈浩宇，今日你不将春金沫乖乖给爷爷，爷爷就打得你满地找牙！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu had spotted him walking in—with just five thugs. Backed by his three hired bodyguards, he did not bat an eye, lounging in his chair watching Chun Jinmo dance.
[ZH] 蒋爷对身边的五位手下使了个眼色，五人都靠近陈浩宇。三位高手拿人钱财替人消灾，自然替金主挡住。
[EN] Master Jiang dropped into a seat across from him. "Still coming after Chun Jinmo, Young Master Chen?"
[ZH] 春香楼里所有人都朝这边瞧来，近处的人赶紧躲开，春金沫正跳着舞也不由停下，站在台上担忧地看着。
[EN] With his promissory note reduced to ash, Chen Haoyu had zero fear left. "Heh! All that fancy gambling trap you laid went up in smoke, huh, Master Jiang? Sure, I married a tigress—too bad for you I have a knack for taming tigresses! She's sweet as a kitten now, lets me roam free, purrs at me every day, gentle as silk. I go wherever I please. Jealous?"
[ZH] 疤子等人在楼上跟商妓玩乐，见此情景，也都凑到栏杆旁向下瞧。
[EN] Rage flashed across Master Jiang's face. "Hand Chun Jinmo over right now, Chen Haoyu, or I'll knock your teeth down your throat!"
[ZH] 一名小弟说：“又要打起来。”
[EN] At his sharp glance, his five thugs closed in. Paid to earn their keep, Chen Haoyu's three hired fighters stepped between them.
[ZH] 另一名说：“改日我们劝劝陈公子不要老盯着春金沫，春香楼比春金沫漂亮的商妓多得是。”
[EN] Every patron in the hall swiveled toward the showdown, nearby tables scrambling back. On stage, Chun Jinmo froze mid-step, staring down in alarm.
[ZH] 疤子说：“你们懂什么？陈公子向来感情专一，不愿随便换人。”
[EN] Upstairs, Scarface and his cronies broke off flirting with courtesans and leaned over the balcony rail.
[ZH] 两名小弟叹道：“这叫感情专一？第一次听说。”
[EN] "Another brawl's kicking off," one lackey muttered.
[ZH] 疤子说：“少废话，赶紧看热闹。”
[EN] "We should talk Young Master Chen into dropping Chun Jinmo," said another. "Plenty of prettier girls right here in Chunxiang Brothel."
[ZH] 楼下已打起来……
[EN] "What do you idiots know?" Scarface scoffed. "Young Master Chen is loyal in love—doesn't switch women on a whim."
[ZH] 陈浩宇跟蒋爷坐在原地未动，互相很不屑地瞧着对方。
[EN] "That counts as loyal in love?" the two cronies echoed. "First time hearing that!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇问：“老实说，你是不是拿了知府大人什么好处？帮他设计我，让我娶他家没人要的女儿？”
[EN] "Shut up and watch the fight!"
[ZH] 对方如此怀疑，蒋爷是万万没想到，不如顺着陈浩宇，欺骗欺骗，顺便吓唬吓唬他以后少来找春金沫。
[EN] Downstairs, fists flew. Chen Haoyu and Master Jiang stayed seated, glaring daggers at each other across the table.
[ZH] “陈公子果然聪明，一猜就中，别看爷爷我是黑道，在官府也是有人的，知府大人就是我的靠山，我不帮他还能帮谁？他交代过，让我替他好好收拾你，还说你最好别往春香楼跑，在家乖乖陪他的女儿，否则让你吃不了兜着走。”
[EN] "Level with me," Chen Haoyu demanded. "Did Prefect Zhu grease your palm to set me up and force me to marry his leftover daughter?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇被激怒，站起来：“知府大人就了不起了？还管起我的私事？我偏要天天来！气死他！……”
[EN] Stunned by that wild guess, Master Jiang decided to roll with it—both to mess with his head and scare him away from Chun Jinmo. "Sharp as a tack, Young Master Chen! Think just because I run in the underworld I don't have friends in office? Prefect Zhu is my backer—who else would I help? He ordered me to straighten you out and told me to warn you: stay out of Chunxiang Brothel and play dutiful husband at home, or you'll regret it!"
[ZH] 本来还想接着骂，瞧见三个高手占据上风，陈浩宇的怒气立刻烟消云散：“瞧你的手下，一点儿用都没有，被我身边的三位高手打得落花流水，呵呵……！”
[EN] Infuriated, Chen Haoyu jumped to his feet. "Big deal, he's the prefect! Who gives him the right to meddle in my private life? I'll come here every single day just to drive him mad—"
[ZH] 蒋爷也站起来，见自己带的五人节节败退，不想在大庭广众之下丢了面子，大喊一声：“停！”
[EN] He cut himself off as his three fighters sent Master Jiang's men reeling. His temper flipped straight into glee. "Look at your pathetic thugs! Wiped out by my three bodyguards! Hahaha!"
[ZH] 两边的人都停下，蒋爷对陈浩宇说：“爷爷我今儿饶你，下次你就没这么幸运！”
[EN] Master Jiang rose too. Seeing his five men battered backward, unwilling to lose more face in public, he barked, "Hold it!"
[ZH] 然后对台上的春金沫大喊：“春金沫！你迟早是爷爷的！”
[EN] Both sides broke off. Master Jiang jabbed a finger at Chen Haoyu. "I'll let you off today. Next time your luck runs out!" Turning toward the stage, he roared, "Chun Jinmo! Sooner or later you're mine!"
[ZH] 蒋爷走到三位高手跟前，竖起大拇指：“三位功夫不错，钱塘帮的大门对三位随时敞开，回见！”
[EN] Before leaving, he stepped up to the three hired fighters and gave them a thumbs-up. "Nice skills, boys. The Qiantang Gang's doors are always open to you. See you around!"

#CH 221
[ZH] 蒋爷吃了亏，须将此亏找补回来。该找谁呢？他想到陈浩东。
[ZH] 陈家大公子找他设计，绕那么大一圈儿，目的没达到，陈浩宇并未被厉害的娘子管住，还把他当猴耍。至于陈浩东给的那些银子哪能弥补对他心里和面子上的伤害？
[ZH] 陈浩东这日在绸缎庄忙活，最近陈家跟西南一带的绸缎生意越做越火，他基本被绊在绸缎庄内，每日都得按时来。
[ZH] 正忙着，冲进来一群人，掌柜和伙计被吓坏。
[ZH] 陈浩东仔细一瞧，是蒋爷带人前来，对方看上去不怎么高兴。他迎上去：“蒋爷大驾光临，不知所为何事？”
[ZH] 蒋爷提醒道：“大公子若不想被人知道你都干些什么，还是找个地儿你我单独聊聊。”
[ZH] 陈浩东已猜到是何事，赶紧把蒋爷带到后院的仓库。蒋爷的人一部分守在外面，一部分跟着。
[ZH] 仓库里尽是些绸缎布匹，周围散发着布匹的酸味儿。门一关，蒋爷二话不说，命令身边的人打陈浩东。陈浩东没有什么拳脚功夫，只能受着。
[ZH] 差不多时，蒋爷喊声：“停！”手下人立刻住手。
[ZH] 陈浩东忍痛问：“蒋爷什么都不说，动手就打人，实在不讲道理。”
[ZH] 蒋爷气呼呼地说：“打你都是轻的，你骗爷爷帮你使计，让陈浩宇娶母老虎，还说他被母老虎管住，不会再去找春金沫，可母老虎根本不管他，他整日在春香楼跟春金沫快活，爷爷今日找他还吃了大亏！”
[ZH] “即便如此，该给的银子都给你，蒋爷不吃亏。”
[ZH] “你给的那些银子跟爷爷的面子和精神损失相比，不值一提！”
[ZH] “我和蒋爷一样，都希望浩宇被厉害的娘子管住，可他那娘子跟他签了婚前契约，两人互不干涉，这一点我没预料到，不过我还有杀手锏，蒋爷耐心等着，过不了几日，春金沫就是蒋爷的人。”
[ZH] 蒋爷靠近陈浩东后蹲下：“你没骗我？”
[EN] Smarting from the humiliation, Master Jiang needed someone to pay for it, and his mind went straight to Chen Haodong.
[ZH] 陈浩东勾勾手指：“靠近些，我告诉你。”
[EN] Eldest Young Master Chen had hired him to run that elaborate scheme, only for none of it to pan out: Chen Haoyu was not locked down by his fierce bride, leaving Master Jiang looking like a fool. The silver Chen Haodong had paid did not come close to covering his bruised pride.
[ZH] 蒋爷将耳朵靠近陈浩东，陈浩东耳语几句。蒋爷脸上露出笑容：“没想到是陈浩宇干的，好！爷爷我就信你一回。”
[EN] That afternoon, Chen Haodong was tied up at the silk house, where the Chen family's booming southwest trade demanded his daily attention.
[ZH] 蒋爷说完，命人把一瓶药扔到陈浩东怀里，带人离开。
[EN] Without warning, a pack of roughnecks barged through the door, terrifying the manager and clerks. Seeing Master Jiang's stormy face at the head of them, Chen Haodong hurried forward. "Master Jiang! What brings you here?"
[ZH] 陈浩东把药揣好，爬起来，走出仓库，到前面吩咐掌柜和伙计不能将今日之事告诉陈家长辈，然后坐上马车回陈府。
[EN] "If you don't want everyone hearing what you've been up to, Eldest Young Master, find us a quiet spot to talk," Master Jiang growled.
[ZH] 身上的伤不算严重，休养两日便好了一大半。
