[EN] Guessing why he had come, Chen Haodong led him straight back to the rear storeroom. Part of Master Jiang's crew guarded the door while the rest filed inside.
[ZH] 这两日陈浩东一直寻思怎么才能将浩宇散播谣言之事传到朱家姑娘的耳朵里。
[EN] Between tall stacks of silk bolts smelling faintly of sour dye, the door clicked shut—and without a single word, Master Jiang signaled his men to jump Chen Haodong. With no martial skill, Chen Haodong could only curl up and take the beating.
[ZH] 一名下人走进来：“大公子，二老爷回来，让您过去一趟。”
[EN] Once he had vented enough, Master Jiang called, "Stop!"
[ZH] 陈浩东心生一计，对下人招手：“你靠近些，我有事要与你说。”
[EN] Wincing through the ache, Chen Haodong gasped, "Barging in and beating me without a word—where's the justice in that?"
[ZH] 下人靠近：“大公子有什么吩咐尽管说。”
[EN] "Beating you is getting off light!" Master Jiang fumed. "You talked me into rigging that scheme to marry Chen Haoyu to a tigress, swearing she'd cage him so he'd leave Chun Jinmo alone. Instead she doesn't lift a finger, he's lounging in Chunxiang Brothel with Chun Jinmo all day, and I took a beating over it today!"
[ZH] 陈浩东先掏出一两银子：“拿着，事成之后还会奖赏。”
[EN] "Even so, you got paid every tael promised. You didn't lose out."
[ZH] 下人高兴地接过银两。
[EN] "Your silver doesn't scratch the surface of my lost face and grief!"
[ZH] 陈浩东悄悄对着下人耳朵说些话后，吩咐道：“今日就传给墨香苑的下人。”
[EN] "I want Haoyu caged by that wife just as bad as you do, Master Jiang," Chen Haodong wheezed. "I didn't see them signing a non-interference prenuptial contract coming. Still, I have one trump card left. Give it a few days, and Chun Jinmo will be all yours."
[ZH] 下人回声：“是，大公子。”
[EN] Master Jiang squatted beside him. "Not jerking me around again?"
[ZH] 下人走后，陈浩东起身，准备去见陈江树。
[EN] Chen Haodong beckoned him closer and whispered a few lines into his ear. A wicked grin spread across Master Jiang's face. "So Chen Haoyu was behind that! All right, I'll trust you one more time."
[ZH] 还没走出屋，叶安雅进来，身后跟着小翠。
[EN] Tossing a bottle of bruise ointment into Chen Haodong's lap, Master Jiang led his men out.
[ZH] 叶安雅一脸平静没有丝毫媚态，陈浩东看着就来气：“跑哪里去？”
[EN] Pocketing the ointment, Chen Haodong hauled himself up, staggered out front to warn the manager and clerks not to breathe a word to the Chen elders, and rode home by carriage.
[ZH] “去街上买胭脂水粉。”
[EN] His bruises were surface-deep; two days of rest saw him mostly mended, and he spent both days figuring out how to slip word of Haoyu's rumor campaign into Zhu Shuzhen's ears.
[ZH] “哼！你什么时候在我面前涂过胭脂水粉？该不会在外面私会情郎，打扮给他瞧？”
[EN] When a household servant stepped in announcing, "Eldest Young Master, Second Master is back and asks for you," an idea clicked. Chen Haodong beckoned the man closer. "Come here. Got an errand for you."
[ZH] 被陈浩东说中，叶安雅心中一惊，还是装作平静的样子：“你要无事，我进屋去。”
[EN] "At your command, Eldest Young Master."
[ZH] 自成亲那日，叶安雅就是这副冷冰冰的样子，陈浩东有说不出的讨厌。
[EN] Chen Haodong pressed a tael of silver into his palm. "Take this. More waiting once it's done."
[ZH] 他以为只要把她变成自己的女人，她便会在屋里向他微笑和撒娇，谁知一点用处都没有，房也圆了，后来也不知在床上蹂躏过她多少回，她还是老样子，没有丝毫女人的媚态。
[EN] Grinning, the servant pocketed the silver, listened close as Chen Haodong whispered instructions, and nodded. "Get word to the Moxiang Courtyard servants today," Chen Haodong finished.
[ZH] 陈浩东挡在叶安雅面前，她的冷漠再次激起他占有的欲望。他一把拉住她，向小翠命令道：“出去！”
[EN] "Yes, Eldest Young Master."
[ZH] 小翠想要阻止：“姑爷！您不能这样！”
[EN] After the servant left, Chen Haodong stood to go see his father—just as Ye Anya walked in with Xiaocui behind her.
[ZH] “滚出去！”陈浩东厉声呵斥。
[EN] One look at Ye Anya's cool, unsmiling face set his temper flaring. "Where have you been?"
[ZH] 叶安雅面对陈浩东已没有反抗的欲望：“小翠，你出去吧，把门关好。”
[EN] "Out on the street buying rouge and face powder."
[ZH] 小翠走出去，把门从外面关好。
[EN] "Hmph! Since when do you wear rouge for me? Sneaking out to doll yourself up for some secret lover?"
[ZH] 叶安雅冷漠的顺从，反而让陈浩东兴致全无。
[EN] Hit right on the mark, Ye Anya felt her heart jolt, yet kept her mask ice-calm. "If you need nothing, I'm going inside."
[ZH] 他甩开叶安雅的手：“你在床上冷冰冰的跟块石头一样，以后我不会再碰你。”
[EN] Ever since their wedding night, she had worn that same frozen detachment, and Chen Haodong hated it with a passion. He had assumed claiming her as his wife would melt her into smiles and sweet compliance; instead, even after consummating the marriage and forcing himself on her countless times, she never showed him an ounce of warmth.
[ZH] 叶安雅松口气，谁知陈浩东刚要开门出去，又转身回来，抱住她狂吻起来。
[EN] Blocking her path, her very coldness ignited his urge to dominate her. He seized her wrist and barked at Xiaocui, "Get out!"
[ZH] 叶安雅喘不过气，怎么推也推不开，任凭对方胡乱狂啃。
[EN] "Young Master, please don't!" Xiaocui cried.
[ZH] 啃够后，陈浩东抱住叶安雅：“我们陈家男人向来对自己女人好，我爹劝我对你好些，可不知为什么，我一看到你只想占有，你以后能不能不那么冷漠地对待我？也许我会对你好些。”
[EN] "Get out!" he snarled.
[ZH] 叶安雅沉默不语。
[EN] Too drained to fight, Ye Anya murmured, "Go outside, Xiaocui, and close the door."
[ZH] 陈浩东放开叶安雅：“爹找我有事，我还需过去一趟。”
[EN] Xiaocui retreated and pulled the door shut.
[ZH] 等陈浩东推门离开后，叶安雅一脸厌恶，拿起丝帕擦去嘴唇上陈浩东留下的痕迹。
[EN] Strangely, her numb obedience killed Chen Haodong's appetite on the spot. He flung her hand away. "Cold as a block of stone in bed. I won't touch you again."
[ZH] 拿了银子的下人，在墨香苑外面徘徊一会儿，见阿升从里面出来，喊道：“阿升！”
[EN] Ye Anya breathed out in relief—only for him to spin back at the threshold, seize her in his arms, and crush his mouth down on hers in a rough, frantic kiss. Gasping for air, unable to shove him off, she stood trapped until he finally pulled back, still holding her tight.
[ZH] 阿升站住：“喊我何事？”
[EN] "Chen men always treat their wives well," he muttered against her hair. "Father lectured me to treat you better, yet heaven knows why—every time I look at you, all I want is to possess you. Can't you quit acting like a block of ice? Maybe then I'd treat you better."
[ZH] 下人把阿升拉到一旁：“我有重要的事情告诉你。”
[EN] Ye Anya answered with dead silence.
[ZH] 阿升继续问：“什么重要的事？”
[EN] He dropped his arms. "Father needs me. I have to go."
[ZH] “是关于你家少夫人和二公子的事。”
[EN] The instant the door shut behind him, Ye Anya wiped her lips hard with a silk handkerchief, disgust twisting her face.
[ZH] 阿升谨慎道：“他们好着呢，能有什么事？”
[EN] Meanwhile, the bribed servant loitered outside Moxiang Courtyard until Asheng walked out, then called him over and pulled him aside. "Got something huge to tell you."
[ZH] “还记得去年你家少夫人被全城人非议的事吗？”
[EN] "Huge about what?" Asheng asked.
[ZH] “都是别人胡乱编排，我家小姐根本不是那样的人。”
[EN] "About your young madam and Second Young Master."
[ZH] “可惜你家少夫人至今被蒙在鼓里，稀里糊涂的，还不知道是谁散播的谣言。”
[EN] Asheng turned guarded. "They're doing fine. What about them?"
[ZH] “难道你听说了什么？”
[EN] "Remember all those nasty rumors tearing your young madam apart across Lin'an last year?"
[ZH] “我一直在陈家当然知道是谁散播。”
[EN] "Pure slander made up by scum! My mistress isn't anything like that."
[ZH] 阿升催道：“快说，我好帮我家小姐收拾没长眼的人。”
[EN] "Too bad she's still kept in the dark, clueless who spread them."
[ZH] 那位四下瞧瞧，确定安全后小声说：“就是朱家姑爷，陈家的二公子，没想到吧，你家少夫人嫁的人正是散播她谣言之人。”
[EN] "You know something?"
[ZH] 阿升不敢相信：“你从哪里得知？”
[EN] "I've worked in Chen Manor for years—of course I know who started it."
[ZH] “二老爷和大公子说话时，我偷偷听来的，搁在心里难受，就想着告诉墨香苑的下人，正好碰见你出来，说出来我也好受些。我还有事，先去忙。”
[EN] "Spill it!" Asheng urged. "I'll help my mistress thrash the blind bastard!"
[ZH] 阿升愣在原地，突如其来的消息他听后都觉得诧异，何况自家小姐？该怎么办？要不要告诉她？
[EN] Glancing both ways to ensure nobody listened, the servant dropped his voice. "It was your own young master—Second Young Master Chen! Shocking, right? The man your young madam married is the very bastard who spread those lies about her."
[ZH] 阿升决定先去办事，办完事回来再说。
[EN] Asheng stared in disbelief. "Where did you hear that?"
[ZH] 陈府外，停下一辆乔家的马车，夏果先从马车里下来，接着艾墨扶着负鼓盲翁也从马车里走下来。
[EN] "Overheard Second Master and Eldest Young Master talking. Kept eating at my conscience, so I figured I'd tell someone from Moxiang Courtyard. Lucky I ran into you—feels better getting it off my chest. Got chores to run; see you around."
[ZH] 夏果上前搀住盲翁：“爷爷，小姐现在嫁到陈家，您一会儿见到，要称呼少夫人。”
[EN] Frozen in his tracks, Asheng reeled. Even he felt outraged—how would his mistress take it? Should he tell her or keep quiet? Deciding to finish his errand first, he hurried off with a heavy head.
[ZH] 盲翁点点头：“你都叮嘱过好多遍，我耳朵都快听出茧子来。陈家是不是跟你嫁的乔家一样富裕？”
[EN] Outside Chen Manor's gates, a Qiao family carriage pulled up. Xiaguo stepped out first, followed by Aimo helping down the blind ballad singer.
[ZH] 夏果解释道：“要说钱财，陈家是临安首富，乔家没法比；可要比贵气，乔家是皇亲国戚，陈家又比不上。”
[EN] Xiaguo took her grandfather's arm. "Grandfather, Miss is married into the Chen family now. Remember to call her Young Madam when we go in."
[ZH] 盲翁笑道：“各有千秋，都是我们乡下人无法比的。你在乔家可要谨守本分好好过日子。”
[EN] "You've drilled that into my ears ten times already!" the old man chuckled. "Are the Chens as rich as the Qiaos?"
[ZH] “爷爷不再嫌弃我给人当妾室？”
[EN] "In sheer wealth, the Chens are the richest family in Lin'an—the Qiaos can't touch them," Xiaguo explained. "In noble rank, though, the Qiaos are imperial in-laws, so the Chens can't match that."
[ZH] “爷爷已想通，嫌弃有什么用？你都已嫁过去，只要你日子好过，爷爷和你爹娘也都高兴。”
[EN] "Each has its own glory—worlds beyond country folk like us. Mind your place in the Qiao household and live well."
[ZH] “我扶爷爷进去，小姐这会儿说不定已等得焦急。”
[EN] "You aren't ashamed anymore that I married as a concubine?"
[ZH] 千里寻给说话艺人放一日假，只因今日负鼓盲翁要来唱鼓词。她便待在素心草堂一边写话本，一边耐心等待。
[EN] "I've made peace with it. What good is grumbling now that you're wed? So long as you're treated well, your parents and I are happy."
[ZH] 直到丫鬟进来报告：“乔家的夏姨娘来访。”千里寻才撂下笔，让苦荞把手稿迅速收拾好，然后带上苦荞出去迎接。
[EN] "Let's go in. Miss must be waiting."
[ZH] 正好在院中碰到，千里寻上前扶住老人：“老人家，辛苦您跑一趟，主要是现在像您这类唱曲儿的人不多，才把您专程请来，给我家老太太唱上一曲。”
[EN] Having given the storyteller the day off so the blind ballad singer could perform his drum song, Qianli Xun was writing her huaben in Suxin Thatched Hall when a maid announced, "Concubine Xia of the Qiao household has arrived."
[ZH] “少夫人对我家夏果有恩，只要您一句话，我老头子二话不说就来。”
[EN] Setting down her brush, she had Kuqiao stow her manuscript and stepped out with her to greet the guests.
[ZH] “老人家是歇会儿再去奶奶院中，还是这会儿就过去？”
[EN] Meeting them in the courtyard, Qianli Xun stepped forward to take the old man's arm. "Thank you for making the trip, Elder. Few performers still master drum ballads like yours, so I invited you to sing a set for our Old Madam Xia."
[ZH] “一路坐马车过来，已坐够，还是尽快给老太太唱曲儿。”
[EN] "You showed our Xiaguo great kindness, Young Madam. One word from you and this old man comes running."
[ZH] “既如此，我带您过去。”千里寻准备扶着老人出去。
[EN] "Would you like to rest first, or head straight to Grandmother's courtyard?"
[ZH] 夏果主动提出要求：“小姐，我就不过去，在这里等您，您安顿好后回来，我们两个说说话。”
[EN] "Sat plenty in the carriage already. Let's go sing for Old Madam right away."
[ZH] 千里寻点点头，把红豆喊来：“红豆，你带夏姨娘进屋坐，我去去就来。”
[EN] "Then I'll walk you over."
[ZH] 红豆答应后，将夏果请进素心草堂。
[EN] "Miss," Xiaguo spoke up, "I'll wait here while you settle him in, then we can chat when you get back."
[ZH] 夏果走进后，不着急坐下，在屋内到处瞧一遍，自语道：“家具和布置怎么和望月轩的闺房一模一样？走进来我还以为进入望月轩。”
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded and called Hongdou. "Take Concubine Xia inside for tea. I'll be right back."
[ZH] 红豆坐在茶桌前开始为夏果做茶，一边放茶粉一边说：“小姐刚嫁过来时，根本不是这种布置，都是嫁过来后按照小姐的要求重新布置，家具全是从望月轩拉过来的。”
[EN] Hongdou ushered Xiaguo into Suxin Thatched Hall. Instead of sitting right away, Xiaguo wandered around eyeing the furnishings. "Why do the furniture and layout look identical to Wangyue Pavilion? Felt like I stepped right back into Miss's old room!"
[ZH] “原来如此。”夏果见红豆也学会做茶，问道，“没想到你也学会做茶？”
[EN] Seated at the tea table scooping tea powder, Hongdou explained, "It didn't look like this at first. After the wedding, Miss had everything redone to her taste and hauled all her furniture over from Wangyue Pavilion."
[ZH] “韦嬷嬷让苦荞姐姐教我，她说苦荞姐姐迟早要嫁人，让我没事跟着她学习，等苦荞姐姐嫁给白侍卫后，我就可以近身伺候小姐。”
[EN] "No wonder." Watching Hongdou work, Xiaguo raised an eyebrow. "You learned to whisk tea too?"
[ZH] 夏果走到跟前，在茶桌对面的垫子上坐下：“你做茶的技术尚可，还需多练练。”
[EN] "Matron Wei told Sister Kuqiao to teach me. She said Sister Kuqiao will marry sooner or later, so once she weds Guard Bai, I can step up to attend Miss."
[ZH] “刚开始学，还有些粗苯，不过小姐说我做出的茶味道还不错。”
[EN] Xiaguo settled onto the cushion opposite the tea table. "Passable form, though you need more practice."
[ZH] “在小姐身边当丫鬟很幸运，你看我，嫁到乔家已是半个主子，嫁的还是自己喜欢之人。苦荞再不济也会嫁给仪王身边的侍卫，总比嫁给那些富人家的男仆强。你可认识幼凡？”
[EN] "I'm just starting out, still clumsy, yet Miss says it tastes pretty good."
[ZH] “不认识。”
[EN] "Serving Miss brings good fortune," Xiaguo mused with pride. "Look at me—half a mistress in Qiao Manor, married to the man I love. Even Kuqiao will marry Prince Yi's personal guard, way better than some rich family's male servant. Do you know Youfan?"
[ZH] “可认识南双？”
[EN] "Never met her."
[ZH] “知道，他昨日还来帮乔公子讨要小姐的诗词。”
[EN] "How about Nan Shuang?"
[ZH] 夏果一脸不屑：“幼凡是苗知瑜身边的丫鬟，她就不如我们幸运，一个南双把她哄得团团转，还乐得不行。”
[EN] "Him I know. He came yesterday to collect Miss's poems for Young Master Qiao."
[ZH] 红豆已将茶做好，递到夏果跟前：“夏姨娘，请用茶。”
[EN] Xiaguo curled her lip in scorn. "Youfan is Miao Zhiyu's maid—nowhere near as lucky as us. One Nan Shuang has her spinning in circles, and she's thrilled about it."
[ZH] 夏果接过，品了一口：“味道确实不错。”
[EN] Finishing the whisking, Hongdou presented the bowl. "Your tea, Concubine Xia."
[ZH] 她转头对身后的艾墨说：“回去后，你也好好学习做茶。”
[EN] Xiaguo took a sip. "Not bad at all." She turned to Aimo behind her. "You learn whisked tea too when we get back."
[ZH] 艾墨高兴道：“是，还需夏姨娘教我。”
[EN] "Yes, Concubine Xia—I'll need you to teach me!" Aimo beamed.
[ZH] “那是自然，我做茶的技术虽比不上永泰茶楼的茶艺师，可比苦荞强多了。这叫青出于蓝胜于蓝。你跟着我，对我忠心，我也会给你谋个好去处，绝对比那个幼凡强。”
[EN] "Naturally. I may not rival Yongtai Teahouse's tea master, yet I outshine Kuqiao—the student surpassing the teacher! Stay loyal to me and I'll arrange a far better match for you than that Youfan."
[ZH] 艾墨更是高兴：“多谢夏姨娘。”
[EN] "Thank you, Concubine Xia!"
[ZH] 正说得高兴，千里寻走进来：“你们说什么呢？这么开心？”
[EN] Just then Qianli Xun walked back in. "What's got you all grinning?"
[ZH] 夏果已将茶品完，放下茶盏，站起来迎上去：“小姐可回来，我有很多话跟您聊。”
[EN] Setting down her tea bowl, Xiaguo rose eagerly. "You're back, Miss! I have so much to tell you!"
[ZH] 还没聊，千里寻已猜到，夏果不过是想与她聊些乔家之事。她并不感兴趣，看在盲翁的面子上，不妨浪费些时间，听听夏果的废话。
[EN] Without even hearing a word, Qianli Xun already knew she was in for a pile of Qiao Manor gossip. Uninterested, yet out of respect for the blind grandfather, she braced herself to sit through Xiaguo's chatter.

#CH 222
[ZH] 与夏果聊天儿，必然是件很无趣的事，要是坐在屋里，眼前没个美景，会更觉无趣。
[ZH] “你我到院子里的合 欢树下说说话。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷已吩咐人打制好一把藤椅和一张木桌放在合欢树下。红豆又端把椅子搁在木桌旁，供夏姨娘就座。苦荞则端盘时令的水果摆在桌上，然后站一旁伺候。
[ZH] 有苦荞在，红豆则跑去西院看青瓜练习招式，他现在已练得七八分熟，躲避姑爷飞来的一脚，应该没有多大问题。
[ZH] 虽说还未立夏，白天的太阳也是越来越晒，坐到树下，既能零零散散地接触到透过树叶缝隙洒下的阳光，还能避免更多阳光晒坏女子娇嫩的肌肤，夏果十分满意。
[ZH] 坐下后，先赞一番院中景致：“小姐成亲那日我来时，也注意过院中的景致，没现在好，如果我没记错，连一棵树都没有。没想到今日一来，景致大变，不仅种下您喜欢的各类菊花，池塘里也长出荷花，还栽种上这棵茂盛的合欢树，不管是院子还是屋子，都能看出小姐才是这里的主人。”
[ZH] “婚前陈浩宇亲口答应把院子给我，既然是我的地盘，当然需按照我的喜好捯饬，否则每日看着岂不顺心？”
[ZH] 夏果继续叹道：“还是当主母好，我这妾室的身份在乔家哪有此等待遇？”
[ZH] 千里寻安慰道：“起码你嫁给自己喜欢的人。”
[ZH] 这话夏果爱听，眼前的小姐再怎么厉害，再怎么被人喜欢，嫁的却不是心中之人，相比之下，她幸运许多。不由脸上露出笑容：“有件事，您得替我高兴。”
[ZH] 千里寻靠在藤椅上，闭眼享受树叶的清新和一丝丝散落在她身上的阳光：“什么事？”
[ZH] “文渊已与我同房。”夏果面带羞涩，说出闺中私密。
[ZH] 千里寻没有睁眼，嘴角多出一抹笑容，继续享受着：“此等闺中私密，你竟当着我的面说，真不害羞。”
[ZH] “我把小姐当家人看待，才愿与您说。不过文渊也与苗知瑜圆了房，他倒一碗水端平，苗知瑜现在见到我态度比以前好些，可还是说话夹枪带棒的。”
[ZH] 千里寻对别人家的琐事毫无兴致，没有回应。
[ZH] “您不知道，苗知瑜表面一副大家闺秀的样子，其实心肠也没好到哪里去，过一段时日就会寻事欺负我。还好我不笨，更多情况下文渊还是向着我，她拿我也没办法。”
[ZH] 千里寻不好不再开口，不过眼睛依然闭着。
[ZH] “人的好坏最难评判，主要看评判之人站在什么样的角度。我与苗知瑜一起参加过诗会，只觉她是个温婉大方又知性的大家闺秀；后来遭流言攻击，她在背后帮我说话，那时我觉得她人不错。至于现在不敢贸然评判，毕竟我不比你，天天与她接触。”
[EN] Chatting with Xiaguo promised to be dull enough; doing it indoors without scenery would make it unbearable. "Let's talk outside under the hehuan tree."
[ZH] 夏果认为小姐的话是认可她的看法，更来了兴致。
[EN] Matron Wei had already placed a rattan chair and wooden table beneath the silk tree. Hongdou brought out an extra chair for Xiaguo, Kuqiao set down a platter of seasonal fruit and stood by to attend, and Hongdou slipped off to the West Courtyard to watch Qinggua drill—by now he had the move down well enough to dodge his master's kicks with ease.
[ZH] “苗知瑜虽跟小姐有参加诗会的情谊，可她心里并非真正喜欢您。文渊派南双每隔几日来讨您的诗词，苗知瑜很不开心。就昨日还当着文渊的面质问，是不是还放不下幽栖居士……”
[EN] Though summer had not officially started, the midday sun already carried heat. Sitting in the dappled shade beneath the leaves spared a woman's delicate skin while letting gentle shafts of sunlight through, pleasing Xiaguo immensely.
[ZH] 没想到几首朱淑真的诗词竟搅得文渊家宅不安，千里寻睁开眼，看向园中的景致，希望美好的景色能让她忘记刚才夏果说过的话。
[EN] She began by praising the yard. "I noticed the courtyard on your wedding day, Miss—not half as lovely as this, without a single tree if I recall right. Now look at it: your favorite chrysanthemums everywhere, lotus sprouting in the pond, and this lush hehuan tree. Yard and hall alike scream that you rule the roost here."
[ZH] 夏果依旧滔滔不绝：“文渊说他已放下小姐，我信，可苗知瑜不信，她要求文渊以后不要再派南双前来讨要诗词，文渊没有答应。凭我跟小姐的关系，我当然支持文渊，所以最近文渊常常去我屋中。苗知瑜更来气，她越生气我越开心。”
[EN] "Chen Haoyu promised me this courtyard before the wedding. Since it's my turf, I fix it up how I like—why stare at an eyesore every day?"
[ZH] 千里寻抬头看一眼合欢树正在生长的枝叶，想象着开花时的美景，可一切的想象显得很苍白，夏果的话一字不落，听得清清楚楚，她很想结束谈话。
[EN] Xiaguo sighed. "Nothing beats being a primary wife. A concubine like me in Qiao Manor could never dream of treatment like this."
[ZH] “夏果，麻烦你给文渊捎句话，让他以后不要再让南双前来讨要诗词。”
[EN] "At least you married the man you love," Qianli Xun offered.
[ZH] “不行！”夏果果断拒绝，“文渊就这么一个爱好，我可不想惹他不高兴。”
[EN] That hit the sweet spot. Formidable and admired as her former mistress was, she was stuck married to someone she couldn't stand, making Xiaguo feel far luckier by comparison. A pleased smile curved her lips. "There's one thing you ought to be happy for me about."
[ZH] “只好等南双来讨要时，我不给便是。”千里寻继续闭上眼。
[EN] Leaning back in the rattan chair with eyes closed, soaking in the fresh scent of leaves and warm patches of sun, Qianli Xun asked, "What's that?"
[ZH] 夏果再次果断拒绝：“不行！小姐历来洒脱，为何在乎一个苗知瑜？由此看来她以前喜欢您的诗词都是装的。您猜，她现在跟谁走得最近？”
[EN] "Wenyuan has consummated our marriage," Xiaguo confessed with a shy flush.
[ZH] 千里寻心想，讨要诗词的又不是文渊一人，别家也不见因此发生矛盾，看来罪过不在朱淑真的诗词，而在于人心。人若想计较，扁的能说成圆的，若不想计较扁的自然是扁的，圆的自然是圆的，清清楚楚。再说她内心坦荡，传播的只是朱淑真的诗词，并非她千里寻的情感。便认同了夏果说出的“不行”二字。
[EN] Without opening her eyes, Qianli Xun let a small smile tug at her mouth. "Sharing bedroom secrets right in front of me? Have you no shame?"
[ZH] 面对夏果的问题，千里寻吐出一个字：“谁？”
[EN] "I treat you like family, Miss, or I wouldn't say it. Mind you, Wenyuan also consummated his marriage with Miao Zhiyu—fair as a level bowl of water. Miao Zhiyu acts a bit nicer toward me now, though her words still pack hidden thorns."
[ZH] “林觉的娘子白梅若。”夏果提到林觉一脸不爽，毕竟曾经她是丫鬟时林觉对她动手动脚，还绑过她，“她没事就来乔府找苗知瑜，有时苗知瑜还带白梅若去结交她的闺中密友。不知为何，我很不喜欢白梅若，相反林觉的妾室人倒不错，虽然只见过一面，感觉人还行。”
[EN] With zero interest in another household's drama, Qianli Xun stayed silent.
[ZH] 千里寻又睁开眼，从上次跟白梅若遇见不过十来日，林觉这么快有了妾室，倒出乎意料之外：“林觉的妾室该不会是春香楼的春金巧吧？”
[EN] "You wouldn't believe it, Miss," Xiaguo pressed on. "Miao Zhiyu plays the poised gentlewoman outside, yet her heart isn't half so pretty. Every few days she finds an excuse to pick on me. Lucky for me I'm no fool, and Wenyuan mostly sides with me, so she can't touch me."
[ZH] 夏果惊讶道：“您也听说了？消息传得蛮快。”
[EN] Feeling obliged to say something, Qianli Xun spoke with her eyes still shut. "Good or bad in a person is hard to judge—depends where you stand. At poetry salons, Miao Zhiyu struck me as gracious and cultured; during last year's rumors, she spoke up for me behind closed doors, so I thought well of her. As for now, I don't see her daily like you do, so I won't jump to conclusions."
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“哪是消息传得快？主意还是我出的。”
[EN] Taking that as validation, Xiaguo warmed up even more. "She may share salon memories with you, Miss, yet she doesn't truly like you. When Wenyuan sent Nan Shuang every few days to ask for your poems, Miao Zhiyu fumed—even grilled Wenyuan yesterday asking if he still wasn't over Youqi Jushi!"
[ZH] 夏果继续说：“白梅若人不怎么样，对待林觉没得说，拿出自己的私房钱，亲自派人到春香楼给春金巧赎身，然后放在林觉身边当个妾室，就三天前的事。昨个苗知瑜带我去参加宴会，还说要求每家都带上妾室，本以为她好心，去了才知道不过是一群主母聚集起来奚落和批判妾室的宴会而已，我就是在那里认识春金巧。”
[EN] Dismayed that a handful of Zhu Shuzhen's lyrics were stirring trouble in Wenyuan's home, Qianli Xun opened her eyes and gazed across the garden, trying to tune out the noise.
[ZH] 千里寻第三次闭上眼，享受春日的气息：“竟然还有这等宴会？你以后还是不要参加。”
[EN] Xiaguo rolled right on. "Wenyuan told her he's moved on, and I believe him, yet Miao Zhiyu doesn't. She demanded he stop sending Nan Shuang for poems, and he refused. Given our bond, of course I backed Wenyuan, so lately he's been spending most nights in my room! Miao Zhiyu is livid—and the madder she gets, the happier I am."
[ZH] “我是没经验，下次苗知瑜再要求带我，我会断然拒绝。不过通过那次宴会，我与临安城里大户人家的妾室都认识，才知道原来她们私下也有宴会，我便入了分子，以后不愁没有机会参加宴会。”
[EN] Staring up at the new spring shoots on the hehuan branches, Qianli Xun tried to picture them in bloom, yet every word of Xiaguo's chatter drilled straight through her ears. She wanted this visit over. "Xiaguo, pass word to Wenyuan for me: tell him to stop sending Nan Shuang for poems."
[ZH] 夏果脸上露出踏实的笑容，她已找到属于妾室的大本营，以后有人与她说话聊天儿，没事了还可以吐槽吐槽每家的正室。
[EN] "No way!" Xiaguo refused on the spot. "That's his one hobby—I won't spoil his mood!"
[ZH] 千里寻闭着眼，说出充满哲理的话：“看来老子他老人家说得没错：祸兮福之所倚，福兮祸之所伏。”
[EN] "Then next time Nan Shuang shows up, I just won't give him any." Qianli Xun shut her eyes again.
[ZH] 夏果听不懂，不知该如何回应，正为难间，老太太房中的丫鬟前来传话。
[EN] "No way!" Xiaguo protested again. "You've always lived free and unbothered, Miss—why let Miao Zhiyu dictate what you do? Just proves she was faking her love of your poems all along! Guess who she's chummy with these days?"
[ZH] “少夫人，老人家已唱完曲儿，老太太想与老人家聊会儿天儿，留老人家在院中用饭。让我过来请少夫人与夏姨娘一起过去用饭。”
[EN] On second thought, Qianli Xun realized Wenyuan was hardly the only person requesting her verses, yet no other household fought over them. The fault lay in human jealousy, not Zhu Shuzhen's poetry. Someone looking for offense could twist anything; her own conscience was clear—she was only passing along Zhu Shuzhen's literary legacy, not secret love notes. She dropped the idea of cutting Nan Shuang off and tossed out one word: "Who?"
[ZH] 千里寻早想结束聊天儿，她实在对别人家的事还有临安城里正室与妾室之间的盘算不感兴趣，丫鬟的传话正好给她找到结束的理由。
[EN] "Lin Jue's wife, Bai Meiruo!" Xiaguo wrinkled her nose at Lin Jue's name, still remembering how he had groped and tied her up when she was a maid. "She visits Qiao Manor constantly, and Miao Zhiyu even drags her around to meet all her close lady friends. Don't ask me why, I can't stand Bai Meiruo—whereas Lin Jue's new concubine actually seems decent, even from one meeting."
[ZH] 她从藤椅上坐起来：“走，我们去老太太院中用饭。”
[EN] Qianli Xun's eyes popped open. Barely ten days had passed since she bumped into Bai Meiruo, and Lin Jue already had a concubine? "Don't tell me it's Chun Jinqiao from Chunxiang Brothel?"
[ZH] 老太太院中用饭的热闹自不必说，反正那种环境下，夏果是没有机会再叨叨些无聊之事。
[EN] Xiaguo gasped. "You heard already? Word travels fast!"
[ZH] 用过饭后，千里寻亲自将盲翁和夏果送出陈府。
[EN] Fast? I'm the one who handed her the playbook! Qianli Xun thought.
[ZH] 盲翁也是满载而归，真正体会到富贵人家的阔绰，拿到的银两足够整整一年的用度，一年内不必每日风吹日晒出去给人唱曲儿。
[EN] "Bai Meiruo may be awful, yet she dotes on Lin Jue," Xiaguo went on. "Used her own dowry money three days ago to buy Chun Jinqiao's freedom and install her as his concubine. Yesterday Miao Zhiyu dragged me to a banquet where every household was told to bring their concubines. Thought she was being nice—turned out to be a pack of primary wives ganging up to mock and lecture the concubines! That's where I met Chun Jinqiao."
[ZH] 老人坐上马车，一番感慨。
[EN] Qianli Xun closed her eyes a third time, breathing in the spring air. "People actually throw banquets like that? Stay clear next time."
[ZH] 夏果听后，不满道：“爷爷不必感慨，即使您不挣陈家的银子，我给家里的银子也够家人生活，以后可别再走村串户卖唱。”
[EN] "Caught me off guard once—next time Miao Zhiyu tries dragging me along, I'll say no flat out. Still, silver lining: I met the concubines from all the big families in Lin'an and found out they hold private gatherings of their own! I chipped in to join their circle, so now I'll never run short of invitations." A genuine, settled smile lit Xiaguo's face—she had found her tribe, women she could chat with and vent about primary wives to.
[ZH] “你在乔家日子也不好过，不用老给我们银子，以后给自己多攒些傍身，也好在乔家腰杆子硬些。需记住，跟你的公婆还有主母和谐相处，家和万事兴。”
[EN] With eyes still shut, Qianli Xun murmured, "Old Master Laozi got it right: 'Good fortune leans against misfortune, and misfortune lurks within good fortune.'" [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] “爷爷已叮嘱过多次，我都记着呢。”
[EN] Baffled by the quote, Xiaguo was casting about for a reply when a maid from Old Madam Xia's courtyard arrived.
[ZH] 送走夏果和盲翁，千里寻回到墨香苑，见阿升站在院中徘徊，甚是疑惑，上前问：“阿升，有事吗？”
[EN] "Young Madam, the old gentleman finished his ballad, and Old Madam Xia wants to chat with him and keep him for lunch. She sent me to invite you and Concubine Xia to join them."
[ZH] 阿升转过身，想要说出他听到的，又犹豫起来，最终说道：“没什么事，我去西院帮青瓜练招式。”
[EN] Grateful for an escape from Lin'an's wife-versus-concubine soap opera, Qianli Xun stood right up. "Come on, let's go eat with Grandmother."
[ZH] 阿升像有心事一般匆忙跑开。
[EN] Over the lively meal in Old Madam Xia's courtyard, Xiaguo had zero chance to launch back into her gossip.
[ZH] 千里寻很纳闷儿：“阿升怎么了？”
[EN] After lunch, Qianli Xun personally saw the blind ballad singer and Xiaguo to the front gate. Loaded down with enough silver from the generous Chen household to cover a whole year's living without trudging village to village in wind and sun, the old man climbed into the carriage sighing in gratitude.
[ZH] 苦荞回道：“听说阿升喜欢红豆，估计是想求小姐把红豆许给他。”
[EN] "No need to sigh, Grandfather," Xiaguo chided. "Even without Chen silver, what I send home covers the family. Stop wandering the roads singing."
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“他竟然喜欢红豆？既然没好意思说出口，我们还是装作不知。”
[EN] "Your own path in Qiao Manor isn't easy," her grandfather warned. "Quit sending us so much and stash silver away for yourself so you can stand tall in that house. Remember to stay on good terms with your in-laws and the primary wife—harmony brings prosperity."
[ZH] 千里寻并未将阿升的表现放在心上，继续回屋写话本。
[EN] "You've told me a dozen times, Grandfather. I remember."
[ZH] 阿升还没走到西院，碰见红豆。话憋在心里很难受，迅速拉起红豆躲到一边。
[EN] Returning to Moxiang Courtyard, Qianli Xun spotted Asheng pacing uneasily in the yard. "Something on your mind, Asheng?"
[ZH] “你拉我干什么？”
[EN] He spun around, mouth opening as if to spill something, then hesitated. "Nothing, Miss. Heading to the West Courtyard to help Qinggua drill." He hurried away looking weighed down.
[ZH] “憋死我了！”
[EN] "What's gotten into Asheng?" Qianli Xun wondered.
[ZH] 红豆打趣道：“去茅厕呀，解完手不就不憋了？”
[EN] "Word is he likes Hongdou," Kuqiao replied. "Probably wanted to ask you to betroth her to him and lost his nerve."
[ZH] “不是那个憋，是心里有秘密，不说出来憋死人。”
[EN] Qianli Xun laughed. "He likes Hongdou? If he's too shy to ask, let's pretend we didn't notice." Putting it out of her mind, she went back inside to write.
[ZH] 红豆早看出来阿升喜欢她，只是对方一直未明说，她以为阿升今日要说心里话，脸不由变红。
[EN] Halfway to the West Courtyard, Asheng ran straight into Hongdou. Unable to hold the secret in another second, he grabbed her sleeve and pulled her into a quiet corner.
[ZH] “有什么话你只管说，我听着呢。”
[EN] "What are you dragging me over here for?"
[ZH] 阿升小心问：“还记不记得去年有关小姐流言的事儿？”
[EN] "I'm bursting!"
[ZH] 原来不是表白，红豆失望道：“当然记得，提它作甚？都已是过去的事。”
[EN] Hongdou teased, "Go use the latrine then—you'll feel right as rain!"
[ZH] 阿升向周围看看，没其他人，大胆说：“今日大公子院中有个下人告诉我，流言是姑爷散播的。”
[EN] "Not that kind of bursting! I'm sitting on a secret that's killing me!"
[ZH] 红豆由失望变得惊讶：“什么？传流言之人竟然是姑爷？”
[EN] Having noticed Asheng's crush ages ago, Hongdou felt her cheeks flush, convinced a confession was coming. "Go ahead, I'm listening."
[ZH] 阿升叮嘱道：“小声些。”
[EN] Asheng dropped his voice. "Remember those awful rumors about Miss last year?"
[ZH] “怎么办？我都替小姐难过，她本来就不愿嫁给姑爷，要知道是姑爷传播流言毁掉她与乔公子的婚事，岂不是更加伤心？可要是不告诉小姐，她一直被蒙在鼓里，傻乎乎的，也挺可怜。”
[EN] Disappointed it was not a confession, Hongdou deflated. "Of course. Why bring up ancient history?"
[ZH] “是啊，所以我很为难，到底要不要告诉小姐？我听你的。”
[EN] Glancing around to ensure the coast was clear, Asheng whispered, "A servant from Eldest Young Master's courtyard told me today—our own young master was the one who spread those rumors!"
[ZH] “我也不知道。”
[EN] Disappointment flipped to shock. "What? Second Young Master spread them?"
[ZH] 传来一个声音：“你们两个为何在这里窃窃私语？”
[EN] "Keep your voice down!"
[ZH] 两人扭头一看，原来是韦嬷嬷，看样子应该是刚从铺子里回来。
[EN] "What do we do?" Hongdou fretted. "My heart aches for Miss already. She never wanted to marry him, and if she finds out he's the one who ruined her match with Young Master Qiao with slander, she'll be crushed! Yet leaving her in the dark like a fool feels cruel too."
[ZH] 红豆与阿升互相对视，他们都认为韦嬷嬷最厉害，这种为难的事情还是请教她为好。
[EN] "That's why I'm stuck. Tell Miss or not? I'll go with whatever you think."
[ZH] 两人上前行完礼，红豆说：“嬷嬷先回屋，我与阿升有重要的事情告诉嬷嬷。”
[EN] "I don't know either..."
[ZH] 三人来到韦嬷嬷屋中，不等韦嬷嬷坐下，两人你一言我一语将实情一五一十全部说出。
[EN] "What are you two whispering about over here?" a voice cut in.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷惊得坐在榻上，她再怎么聪明，也想不到去年的流言是姑爷所为。
[EN] Both spun around to see Matron Wei, just back from checking the shops. Trading a look, they knew at once: nobody was wiser than Matron Wei. Bowing, Hongdou said, "Please come inside first, Matron. Asheng and I have something urgent to tell you."
[ZH] 仔细想来，去年两家订亲时，陈家不计较和离一事倒能理解，可不忌讳闹得满城风雨的流言蜚语，倒令韦嬷嬷感到意外。当时想不通，现在知道实情，才想通陈家为何会答应。
[EN] Inside Matron Wei's room, before she even sat down, the two spilled every word.
[ZH] 朱家老爷是知府大人，又好面子，面对流言肯定会派人查到底。况且有一次向夫人来望月轩时，无意中提到朱家老爷正在查散播流言之人。
[EN] Matron Wei sank onto her couch in stunned silence. Clever as she was, she had never dreamed the groom himself stood behind last year's slander.
[ZH] 可查着查着就没有任何消息，然后毫无道理给姑娘说下陈家的婚事，看来朱家老爷已知此事，才想到结亲，自认为是两全其美之事，既保住朱家的面子，也给女儿找个好人家。
[EN] Yet the pieces clicked fast. Back during the betrothal, the Chen family overlooking a divorce made sense, yet shrugging off citywide scandal had puzzled her. Now it all made sense. Prefect Zhu cared fiercely about face and would never let rumors slide—Madam Xiang had even let slip once at Wangyue Pavilion that he was investigating the culprit. Then the probe went dead silent, followed out of nowhere by a sudden match with the Chen family. Prefect Zhu had clearly uncovered the truth and forced a marriage as a neat fix: saving Zhu face while landing his daughter a wealthy husband.
[ZH] 可姑娘并不这么认为，还一直被蒙在鼓里，实在可怜。
[EN] Only his daughter had been kept completely in the dark. Poor girl.
[ZH] “你们两个先下去吧，此事容我想想。”
[EN] "Go back to your duties, both of you," Matron Wei said. "Let me think this through."
[ZH] 红豆与阿升说声：“是。”一起走出韦嬷嬷的屋子。
[EN] "Yes, Matron." Together they stepped outside.
[ZH] 两人道出心里话，倍感轻松。尤其是阿升，叹道：“难题交给韦嬷嬷，我现在一身松快。”
[EN] With the weight off his shoulders, Asheng breathed out in relief. "Handed the headache to Matron Wei—feel light as a feather now!"
[ZH] 红豆想起刚才因误会期待阿升说的话，生气道：“你好好在这里松快，我还有事，先去忙。”
[EN] Still stinging from her dashed hope of a confession, Hongdou snapped, "Enjoy feeling light, then! I've got work to do!" and marched off.
[ZH] 阿升瞧着红豆离去的背影，自语道：“这人真奇怪，刚还好好的，怎么突然变脸？”
[EN] Asheng stared after her, scratching his head. "Weird—she was fine a second ago. What set her off?"

#CH 223
[ZH] 要不要将散播流言之人是姑爷的事告诉姑娘？韦嬷嬷坐在榻边犹豫着。
[ZH] 若不告诉，此事连下人都知道，姑娘知道是迟早的事；若告诉，势必姑娘对姑爷多出一分怨言，两人往后的日子不会好过。
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷回想进府后在园子里碰见姚轻琰的情景。
[ZH] 她从外面忙碌完回来，行走在陈府的道路上，听到有人唤自己，循声望去，原来是大夫人身边的丫鬟映雪站在一个亭子里唤她，而大夫人则坐在亭子里歇息，也朝她微笑招手。
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷便绕道来到亭子，先拜见：“夫人。”
[ZH] 姚轻琰笑着说：“嬷嬷是大忙人，想在园子里碰见你不容易，我今儿幸运给遇上，快些坐，陪我说两句。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷猜到大夫人应该有事要与她说，说不定还是专程坐在亭子里堵她。于是在石桌旁坐下：“我虽忙些，可要比起陈家的老爷们还是差远了。”
[ZH] 姚轻琰笑笑说：“我要感谢嬷嬷帮了淑真，也间接帮了我。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷早已忘记随口的一句话，露出疑惑的表情：“不知夫人说的是哪件事？”
[ZH] “嬷嬷帮淑真的事太多，记不住也正常，我提醒提醒你，就是哄老太太的事。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷恍然大悟：“哦，原来是此事，当时我随口那么一说，姑娘记在心里，还照着去做，实在难得。”
[ZH] “嬷嬷是有头脑之人，淑真又是有心之人。”姚轻琰夸赞完，又面露愁容，“有件事需嬷嬷帮着动动脑子，想想该怎么办？”
[ZH] “不知夫人为何事发愁？”
[ZH] “还不是为浩宇和淑真的事，他们两个成了亲，不圆房，各过各的。一个整日浪荡在外，一个该干什么干什么，就当另一个不存在，也不知道我有没有福气抱上大胖孙子。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷早料到自家姑娘迟早要面对此事，毕竟嫁了人，婚前签的契约有些陈家可以履行，有些恐怕不行。
[ZH] “夫人不能着急，一切得慢慢来。”
[ZH] “没错，是得慢慢来。抱孙子的事先搁置一旁，现在最重要的是怎么能将浩宇给管住。我与老太太还有江林商量过，想将管教浩宇的事交给淑真。之前也给淑真叮嘱过，她表面答应，心里却没当回事，现下也不好再对她提及此事，还望嬷嬷想想法子，能让淑真心甘情愿帮着陈家管住浩宇。”
[ZH] 听了这一番话，韦嬷嬷才真正明白姚轻琰在亭子里等她的原因。
[ZH] “按说教育子女是长辈的事，没想到我家姑娘嫁过来还要承担教育夫君的责任，说出去恐怕没几个人敢信。”
[ZH] “嬷嬷说得在理，我这当娘也觉得面上有愧，自己没把孩子教育好，却来麻烦儿媳妇。可老太太、江林与我思索再三，还是觉得交给淑真好处多多，起码有一大好处，他们两个会因此经常磕磕碰碰，两块顽石碰撞多了，都能碰出火花，何况是两个大活人？嬷嬷您说是也不是？”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷只觉可笑，这家人没把自家儿子教育好，现在却把希望寄托在儿媳妇身上。
[EN] Seated on the edge of her couch, Matron Wei weighed the dilemma. Keep quiet, and since even servants knew, her young mistress would find out sooner or later anyway; tell her now, and her grudge against the groom would explode, making their married life unbearable.
[ZH] 不过也能理解，老太太已习惯宠溺孙子，根本下不了狠手；眼前这位夫人一看也不是个能下狠手的主；大老爷倒能下得了狠手，可只要身边的两个女人狠不下心，他再狠心也没用。
[EN] Her mind drifted back to running into Yao Qingyan in the garden just before coming home.
[ZH] 姑娘便成为最佳人选。他们既可乐得清闲，还可维护三人之间的和谐关系；若姑娘管教姑爷成功，陈家也落下一个好儿子，说不定顺带还能增进儿子与儿媳的感情。真是好筹谋。
[EN] Walking back through Chen Manor after her errands, she had heard Yingxue calling from a pavilion where Eldest Madam sat resting and waving her over with a smile. Detouring into the pavilion, Matron Wei bowed. "Madam."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷虽看得明白，可也觉姑娘亲手调教出一个好夫君没什么不好。但她了解姑娘，既签下婚前契约说好互不干涉，绝对会遵守，又怎愿意去管教姑爷？
[EN] "You're such a busy woman, Matron—hard to catch you out in the garden," Yao Qingyan greeted her warmly. "Lucky I bumped into you today. Sit and chat a moment."
[ZH] “主意倒不错，只怕我家姑娘不乐意。”
[EN] Guessing Eldest Madam had waited there on purpose, Matron Wei sat at the stone table. "Busy as I am, I can't compare with the Chen masters."
[ZH] “正因为担心淑真不乐意，才求到嬷嬷的头上，你帮着想个法子，只要能让淑真心甘情愿管教浩宇就行。”
[EN] "I owe you thanks for helping Shuzhen—and helping me in the bargain."
[ZH] “我从不让姑娘做她不喜欢的事……”
[EN] Having forgotten her offhand remark, Matron Wei blinked. "Which matter do you mean, Madam?"
[ZH] 姚轻琰见状，站起来，走到韦嬷嬷面前：“嬷嬷要是不答应，我就给你跪下，求到你答应为止。”
[EN] "You help Shuzhen so often it's no wonder you lose track. Winning over Old Madam Xia!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰说着就要跪下，韦嬷嬷被吓坏，哪有主子给下人下跪的道理？她赶紧站起来，扶住姚轻琰。
[EN] Understanding dawned. "Ah, that. I only dropped a passing hint, and Miss took it to heart and acted on it. Credit belongs to her."
[ZH] “夫人折煞我，此事我勉强应下，不过，不敢保证姑娘会答应，但我会努力劝说。”
[EN] "You have sharp judgment, Matron, and Shuzhen has a good heart." Then Yao Qingyan's brow furrowed. "There's another matter I need your brains on."
[ZH] 姚轻琰乐得不行，抓住韦嬷嬷的手：“有嬷嬷出马，一个顶俩。”
[EN] "What troubles you, Madam?"
[ZH] 想到这里，韦嬷嬷又在心中筹谋一番。
[EN] "Haoyu and Shuzhen, of course. Married, yet unconsummated, living separate lives—him roaming wild outside, her going about her days acting like he doesn't exist. At this rate, will I ever live to hold a plump grandson?"
[ZH] 要是把真相告诉姑娘，她必然恼羞成怒，饶不了姑爷，姑爷自由自在的日子算是过到尽头。姑娘即使不愿管教，无形中也起到管教的作用。
[EN] Matron Wei had expected this sooner or later; a household might humor some clauses of a prenuptial contract, never all of them. "No rushing these things, Madam. It takes time."
[ZH] 以姑娘的为人，拳头再厉害，也不会要了姑爷的命，最多让他吃尽苦头。这位姑爷做下许多不入流的事，也该有人替天行道，好好收拾他。
[EN] "True enough. Set grandchildren aside for now—the urgent thing is getting Haoyu reined in. Old Madam Xia, Jianglin, and I all agreed to leave disciplining Haoyu to Shuzhen. We asked her, she nodded politely, yet hasn't cared one bit. We feel awkward bringing it up again, so I'm begging you, Matron—figure out a way to make Shuzhen willing to take Haoyu in hand."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷没渴望姑娘会对姑爷产生感情，但也希望姑娘身边有个好男人。
[EN] At last Matron Wei saw why Yao Qingyan had staked out the pavilion. "Normally parents raise their own children. Having a new bride take on educating her husband—folks outside would hardly believe it."
[ZH] 现在的姑爷倒是很有希望。不说别的，只需看看他的爹陈江林便知。这位陈家的大老爷对夫人真是没得说，有这样的家庭熏陶，姑爷变好的可能性极大。
[EN] "You're right, and as his mother I'm ashamed to dump our failure on my daughter-in-law," Yao Qingyan confessed. "Yet Old Madam Xia, Jianglin, and I talked it over and saw huge benefits—above all, knocking heads constantly might just strike sparks between them! Bang two boulders together enough and even stone sparks, let alone two living people. Don't you agree, Matron?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷觉得若有一日姑爷也能像陈家大老爷对待夫人那般对待自家姑娘，她肯定在梦中都能笑醒。
[EN] Matron Wei found the whole scheme half laughable, yet understandable. Old Madam Xia spoiled him too much to be harsh; Yao Qingyan lacked the heart; and Chen Jianglin could not stay stern with both women softening every blow. Handing the baton to Shuzhen let all three stay on good terms while potentially turning Haoyu around and sparking romance in the process. Neat math—and honestly, molding a decent husband with her own hands was not a bad deal for her mistress either. The only catch was that Shuzhen respected her contract to the letter and had zero urge to meddle with him.
[ZH] 打好主意后，韦嬷嬷走出屋，瞅一眼对面的屋子，自语道：“姑娘都能把姑爷赶到后院来跟我住对门儿，还有什么管教不了的？能让姑娘给自己培养一个好夫君，我有什么好犹豫的？”
[EN] "A clever plan, Madam, except Miss won't agree."
[ZH] 素心草堂内，千里寻依旧写话本。前面的故事已写完，她打算不再改编穿越前写过的网络小说，计划写一个新故事。
[EN] "That's why I'm coming to you, Matron! Find a way to make her willing."
[ZH] 到底怎么写？千里寻想到自己的感情经历，想到叶安雅与曹班一起在神柳下挂红绸的情景，忽然有了思路，不如为这个时代的痴男怨女写写故事，写写他们如何冲破不幸的婚姻追求个人的幸福。
[EN] "I never force Miss to do anything she dislikes—"
[ZH] 想好后，千里寻文思如泉涌，开始奋笔疾书。
[EN] Yao Qingyan rose and stepped in front of her. "If you don't say yes, Matron, I'll kneel right here until you do!"
[ZH] 大概写了三四页纸，韦嬷嬷走进来。
[EN] Horrified—a mistress kneeling to a servant broke every law of propriety—Matron Wei jumped up to catch her arms. "You'll shorten my life, Madam! I'll try my best, though I can't promise Miss will agree."
[ZH] 千里寻无意中抬头瞧到，撂下笔，站起来前去迎接：“嬷嬷跟我在一个院子，我却好几日没见您，想得慌。”
[EN] Overjoyed, Yao Qingyan gripped her hands. "With you stepping in, you're worth two of us!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷看一眼姑娘的书桌，故意笑着问：“今日姑娘怎么没让苦荞收拾你写的书稿？”
[EN] Replaying that scene now, Matron Wei saw the whole board click into place. Tell her young mistress the truth, and she would fly into a rage and never let Chen Haoyu off the hook—ending his carefree gallivanting overnight. Even without wanting to "discipline" him, her wrath would accomplish the exact same thing. And Matron Wei knew her mistress's character: fierce fists aside, she would never cripple or kill him, only make him sweat—which the reckless playboy had long overdue. As for hope of him turning good, one only had to look at his father Chen Jianglin's devotion to Yao Qingyan; raised in a home like that, the boy had decent roots buried underneath. If Chen Haoyu ever treated her mistress half as well as Chen Jianglin treated his wife, Matron Wei would wake up smiling in her sleep.
[ZH] 千里寻拉着韦嬷嬷坐下：“嬷嬷都知道，却不告诉我，害得我在您面前演戏。”
[EN] Mind made up, she stepped out of her room and glanced across the back courtyard at Boya Study. "If Miss can kick the groom out here to live across from me, what can't she handle? Helping her whip herself up a decent husband—nothing left to hesitate over."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷思量如何开口向姑娘道出真相，不如用沉默引起姑娘注意，让她主动引出话题。
[EN] Inside Suxin Thatched Hall, Qianli Xun was bent over her desk writing. Having wrapped up her previous serial, she had decided to quit adapting modern web novels and write an original tale—inspired by her own heartache and seeing Ye Anya and Cao Ban tying red silk beneath the Divine Willow, a story about star-crossed lovers breaking free of miserable marriages to chase happiness. Words flowed off her brush across three or four pages before Matron Wei stepped inside.
[ZH] “嬷嬷怎么不说话？”
[EN] Spotting her, Qianli Xun set down her brush and rose to welcome her. "Same courtyard, yet I haven't seen you in days, Matron! Missed you."
[ZH] 姑娘果然发问，韦嬷嬷才小心说：“有一事，我思前想后，觉得还需告诉姑娘。”
[EN] Matron Wei eyed the desk with a teasing smile. "Not telling Kuqiao to hide your manuscript today, Miss?"
[ZH] “什么事？”千里寻流露出好奇的神情，不知韦嬷嬷会说什么重要的事。
[EN] Qianli Xun pulled her into a chair. "You knew all along and never said a peep, making me put on an act for nothing!"
[ZH] “是关于去年流言一事。”
[EN] Weighing how to break the news, Matron Wei stayed silent for a beat, letting her young mistress ask first.
[ZH] “流言？已经过去，难道大家又开始议论不成？”
[EN] "Why so quiet, Matron?"
[ZH] “散播流言之人已浮出水面。”
[EN] Right on cue. Matron Wei spoke with measured care. "There is something I turned over for a long while, and decided you have a right to know."
[ZH] 还没等小姐说话，一旁的苦荞气愤不已：“嬷嬷是不是已经知道是谁？赶紧说出来，我要到那人面前将他骂得狗血喷头！”
[EN] "What is it?" Qianli Xun leaned forward in curiosity.
[ZH] 千里寻这才问：“到底是谁？嬷嬷是怎么知道？”
[EN] "Last year's rumors about you."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷变得更加谨慎：“怎么知道不重要，重要的是我要说出这人来，姑娘打算怎么做？”
[EN] "Those? Ancient history—don't tell me people started gossiping again?"
[ZH] 千里寻义正言辞：“那还用问？此人害得我好惨，只有两个字‘报仇’！”
[EN] "The culprit behind those rumors has surfaced."
[ZH] 苦荞搭腔道：“对！报仇！”
[EN] Before Qianli Xun could open her mouth, Kuqiao bristled. "You know who did it, Matron? Name him right now so I can march over and curse him out until his ears bleed!"
[ZH] 千里寻催道：“嬷嬷快说，此人是谁？”
[EN] "Who is it, Matron? And how did you find out?" Qianli Xun asked.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷狠狠心，说出两个字：“姑爷。”
[EN] Matron Wei grew even more cautious. "How I found out doesn't matter. What matters is: once I tell you the name, what will you do?"
[ZH] 千里寻惊讶道：“陈浩宇？”
[EN] "Need you ask?" Qianli Xun declared. "That bastard put me through hell. One word: payback!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷点点头。
[EN] "Yes! Payback!" Kuqiao echoed.
[ZH] 苦荞不敢再言语，她观察小姐，发现她惊讶中带着怒色，手紧紧握住椅子的扶手，只听“咔嚓”一声，椅子的扶手被掰断。
[EN] "Spit it out, Matron. Who?"
[ZH] 苦荞心中叹道：“姑爷这下可惨了！”
[EN] Steeling herself, Matron Wei uttered two words: "Young Master."
[ZH] 千里寻气得站起来，踱两步，转过身：“嬷嬷都知道此事，凭我爹的能力，他估计早已知道。知道了还给我牵下这段姻缘，让我稀里糊涂嫁到陈家，我这就回朱府，找我爹好好理论理论！”
[EN] Qianli Xun stared. "Chen Haoyu?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷起身阻止：“事已至此，姑娘找知府大人理论又有何用？还是想想接下来该怎么面对姑爷。”
[EN] Matron Wei nodded.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷这么一说，千里寻清醒许多，回朱府闹确实没用：“那就暂且饶了我爹，谁都能饶恕，唯独陈浩宇坚决不能饶恕！我要报了被全城人非议之仇！苦荞！”
[EN] Kuqiao froze in silence, watching shock darken into fury on her mistress's face. Qianli Xun's fingers clamped onto the wooden armrest—crack!—snapping the solid camphorwood clean in half.
[ZH] “在！”
[EN] Young Master is dead meat! Kuqiao winced inside.
[ZH] “去把红豆叫来！”
[EN] Qianli Xun shot to her feet, paced two tight steps, and whirled around. "If even you know, Matron, with my father's reach he must have known ages ago—and still shoved me into this marriage blind! I'm marching back to Zhu Manor to have it out with him!"
[ZH] 没过一会儿，红豆前来。
[EN] Matron Wei rose to block her. "What good does arguing with Prefect Zhu do now, Miss? What's done is done. Better figure out how to deal with Young Master."
[ZH] 千里寻吩咐道：“你派几个人在临安城的各大青楼和赌坊好好查查陈浩宇到底在哪里，查到回来告诉我，我要亲自前去，将他暴揍一顿，再将他绑回来！”
[EN] That cooled her head a fraction. Raising a storm at Zhu Manor would change nothing. "Fine, my father gets a pass for now. Anyone else I can forgive, never Chen Haoyu! I'm collecting every ounce of payback for making me the laughingstock of Lin'an! Kuqiao!"
[ZH] 红豆回道：“小姐，不用查我都知道姑爷在哪里？”
[EN] "Here!"
[ZH] 千里寻问：“难道是春香楼？”
[EN] "Fetch Hongdou!"
[ZH] 红豆点点头。
[EN] Moments later, Hongdou rushed in.
[ZH] “既如此，带人随我去春香楼！”
[EN] "Send men through every brothel and gambling den in Lin'an to track down Chen Haoyu," Qianli Xun snapped. "Report straight back—I'm going myself to beat him black and blue and haul him home in ropes!"
[ZH] 千里寻二话不说走出素心草堂。
[EN] "No need to search, Miss," Hongdou answered. "I already know where he is."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷叹口气：“唉，这下可热闹了。”
[EN] "Chunxiang Brothel?"
[ZH] 苦荞不解道：“我怎么觉得嬷嬷有种看热闹的心态？”
[EN] Hongdou nodded.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷批评道：“胡说什么？还不赶紧把桌上的手稿收了，省得被人看到，再把椅子给换了。”
[EN] "Then grab the men and follow me to Chunxiang Brothel!" Qianli Xun stormed straight out of Suxin Thatched Hall.
[ZH] 苦荞准备去收拾，见韦嬷嬷要走，上前拉住她，担忧地问：“嬷嬷，我好担心，小姐都和离过一次，该不会又得和离？”
[EN] Matron Wei let out a sigh. "Well, things are about to get lively."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷继续批评道：“能不能盼点儿姑娘的好？不要总把‘和离’挂在嘴边。现在的姑爷人是不怎么样，可第二次的婚姻跟第一次还是有很大区别，主要是陈家跟林家大不一样，等着瞧好吧。”
[EN] "Why do you look like you're enjoying the show, Matron?" Kuqiao blinked.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说完也出了素心草堂，留下苦荞站在原地纳闷：“韦嬷嬷说话总是不清不楚，不过她看上去倒不担心，说明小姐没事。是啊，错在姑爷，我家小姐多厉害，打得姑爷屁股开花！”
[EN] "Nonsense! Gather those manuscript pages off the desk before anyone sees them, and swap out that broken chair."
[ZH] 苦荞这才忙着去收拾手稿，更换椅子。
[EN] Moving to clean up, Kuqiao caught Matron Wei's sleeve as she turned to leave. "Matron, I'm scared—Miss already went through one mutual divorce. You don't think she's headed for a second one?"
[ZH] 千里寻带上十名下人，三女七男走出墨香苑，往陈府大门口而去。一路上，看到的下人好奇地议论着。
[EN] "Can't you wish your mistress well instead of harping on divorce?" Matron Wei chided. "Young Master may be a mess right now, yet this second marriage is worlds apart from the first because the Chens are nothing like the Lins. Just sit back and watch."
[ZH] 快要到大门口时，正好碰见陈浩东从外面回来。眼前的阵仗，陈浩东猜想一定是自己的计谋得逞，笑着迎上去：“弟妹这是去哪里？怎么带这么多人？”
[EN] Matron Wei walked out, leaving Kuqiao scratching her head. "Matron always talks in riddles—still, she doesn't look worried, so Miss must be fine. Right, he's the one in the wrong, and Miss's fists will beat his backside into ribbons!" Relieved, she tidied the manuscript and fetched a spare chair.
[ZH] 千里寻根本不想瞒着陈家人：“去找陈浩宇。”
[EN] Leading ten servants—three maids and seven men—Qianli Xun marched out of Moxiang Courtyard toward the front gates, drawing curious stares and whispers from every passing servant.
[ZH] 陈浩东故意问：“找浩宇让下人去就行，你没有必要亲自带这么多人去。”
[EN] Near the main gate, she ran straight into Chen Haodong returning from outside. Sighting her war party, Chen Haodong knew his bait had landed and stepped up with a bright smile. "Off somewhere with such a crowd, Sister-in-law?"
[ZH] 千里寻突然想知道，陈浩宇散播谣言一事到底陈家人是否清楚，该不会大家都知道，只有她一个人被蒙在鼓里。
[EN] With zero reason to hide it from the Chens, Qianli Xun said straight, "Going to fetch Chen Haoyu."
[ZH] “堂兄可知去年陈浩宇散播关于我的流言一事？”
[EN] "Why go yourself with a small army when you can send servants?" he baited her.
[ZH] 果然是为此事，陈浩东心中窃喜，不如承认，再把母老虎的怒气激得大些，浩宇的日子会更不好过。
[EN] A sudden question hit Qianli Xun: did the entire Chen family know about Chen Haoyu spreading those rumors, leaving her the lone fool kept in the dark? "Eldest Cousin-in-law, do you know about Chen Haoyu spreading slander about me last year?"
[ZH] “怎么说呢？既然弟妹已经知道，我也不瞒你，陈家上上下下确实都清楚此事。正因为流言是浩宇散播，陈家才不计较你名声不好，还和离过一次。浩宇是男人，得有男子汉的担当，做下错事，就得负起责任来。再说去年知府大人手拿鞭子，将浩宇五花大绑，押着来到陈家，逼大伯和大婶必须订下你跟浩宇的婚约。大伯大婶别无他法，只好应下。”
[EN] Bullseye! Concealing his glee, Chen Haodong poured oil on the fire. "Since you already know, I won't hide it—everyone in Chen Manor knows. That's the exact reason the Chens never minded your damaged reputation or past divorce. Haoyu's a man; he made the mess, so he had to own up to it. Besides, last year Prefect Zhu showed up at our gate with a horsewhip, hauling Haoyu tied like a pig, and forced Eldest Uncle and Aunt to seal the betrothal on the spot. They had no choice."
[ZH] 不管陈浩东出于什么目的，算是说清楚婚约定下的来龙去脉。也让千里寻明白去年朱晞颜确实已知此事，之所以瞒着她，不过是想赶快把没人要，名声又不好的女儿处理掉。他只顾朱家的颜面，从来没有把女儿的幸福当回事。
[EN] Whatever Chen Haodong's angle, he had laid bare the full truth: Zhu Xiyan had known all along and hidden it just to offload a "damaged" daughter fast—caring solely about Zhu Manor's face, never once about her happiness.
[ZH] 千里寻更生气，气得无话可说，顾不上回应陈浩东，只管往出走。
[EN] Too furious to waste another syllable on Chen Haodong, Qianli Xun brushed right past him out the gate.
[ZH] 陈浩东更开心，大声提醒道：“浩宇在春香楼，弟妹别跑错地方！”
[EN] Grinning wider, Chen Haodong called after her, "Haoyu's at Chunxiang Brothel, Sister-in-law! Don't go to the wrong place!"
[ZH] 计谋得逞陈浩东开开心心回屋去，耐心等待好戏开场。
[EN] Job done, he strolled back to his courtyard in high spirits to await the fireworks.

#CH 224
[ZH] 千里寻因气愤已不顾大家闺秀的形象，带上十名下人，跳下马车，冲进春香楼。红豆与另两名丫鬟见自家小姐都不怕进入春香楼，也都不再忌讳。
[ZH] 春香楼内继续往日的热闹，看表演的看表演，饮酒的饮酒，狎妓的狎妓。正当大家沉浸其中时，见一姑娘带着下人进来，下人中还有三个丫鬟。第一次见良家女子来青楼，因为好奇，大家都瞧去。
[ZH] 春香楼的妈妈认得，小声自语道：“我的妈呀！陈公子家的母老虎怎么找到这里来？”自语完，赶紧迎上去。
[ZH] “少夫人这是来寻陈公子？”
[ZH] “他现在何处？”千里寻脸上流露出的表情似乎立刻要将陈浩宇打得鼻青脸肿。
[ZH] 春香楼的妈妈不敢隐瞒，毕竟人家是找自家夫君：“在楼上，跟春金沫在一起。”
[ZH] “前面带路。”千里寻的声音气愤中透出冷漠。
[ZH] 春香楼的妈妈还是想提醒一下陈浩宇，抬头瞧见疤子，大声喊道：“疤爷！赶快告诉陈公子，少夫人来找他！”
[EN] Too furious to care a fig for ladylike appearances, Qianli Xun hopped down from the carriage with ten servants behind her and stormed straight into Chunxiang Brothel. Seeing their mistress march in without blinking, Hongdou and the other two maids cast aside their shyness and followed right behind.
[ZH] 疤子早已瞧见，赶紧进屋去报告。
[EN] Inside, business hummed as usual—patrons watching dancers, sipping wine, flirting with courtesans. Every jaw dropped at the sight of a respectable young lady striding in flanked by servants, three of them young maids. Never before had a gentlewoman raided the hall; every head swiveled to stare.
[ZH] 春香楼妈妈这才说：“少夫人，这边请。”
[EN] Recognizing her on sight, the madam whispered, "Mercy me! Young Master Chen's tigress tracked him all the way here!" Putting on a nervous smile, she hurried over. "Looking for Young Master Chen, Young Madam?"
[ZH] 刚走出几步，还未走到楼梯跟前，传来陈浩宇的声音：“朱淑真！跑到这里来干什么？赶紧回去！”
[EN] "Where is he?" Qianli Xun's face promised immediate bruised noses and black eyes.
[ZH] 千里寻抬头瞧见陈浩宇一边从楼梯向下走，一边整理衣衫，那样子显然刚刚做完猥琐之事。他的身后跟着在陈府门口讨要银子的三位高手。
[EN] Not daring to hide a husband from his wife, the madam pointed up. "Upstairs with Chun Jinmo."
[ZH] 想到散播谣言一事，再看陈浩宇一副欠揍的样子，千里寻已忍不住，用轻功飞身而上，一把抓住陈浩宇，再用轻功落下，将陈浩宇推倒在地。
[EN] "Lead the way." Qianli Xun's tone dripped ice over fire.
[ZH] 当着众人，陈浩宇很没面子，强撑着骂道：“横什么横？本公子在外面不跟你计较，等我回去，好好收拾你！”
[EN] Trying to give her patron a heads-up, the madam spotted Scarface on the landing and yelled, "Scarface! Tell Young Master Chen his wife's here!"
[ZH] 三位高手已领教过七个男仆的厉害，从刚才的轻功判断，少夫人功夫更在三人之上。三位高手面面相觑，还是赶紧撤。他们快走几步来到陈浩宇面前：“陈公子，您的家事，我们还是不掺和，改日见。”
[EN] Already spotting the raid, Scarface bolted into the private room to sound the alarm.
[ZH] 三人匆忙离开，陈浩宇很无奈，从地上爬起来。刚站稳，只听朱淑真说：“把他给我绑起来！”
[EN] Only then did the madam gesture toward the stairs. "Right this way, Young Madam."
[ZH] 阿升拿出绳子，与两个男仆上前三下五除二把陈浩宇五花大绑。
[EN] Before they reached the foot of the staircase, Chen Haoyu's shout boomed down. "Zhu Shuzhen! What the hell are you doing here? Go home!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇更是气愤：“朱淑真！你太过分！我怎么说也是你夫君，你这叫欺压夫君，传出去被人笑话！”
[EN] Looking up, Qianli Xun saw him descending the steps adjusting his rumpled robes, clearly fresh from a tryst, with the same three hired bodyguards trailing behind.
[ZH] 果然周围的人都露出看热闹的表情。
[EN] Thinking of last year's slander and staring at his smug, punchable face snapped her last nerve. Launching upward with light-foot skill, she soared straight up the staircase, seized him by the collar, dropped back to the main floor, and shoved him sprawling onto the floorboards.
[ZH] 千里寻不想在春香楼多待，命令道：“把人带走！”
[EN] Humiliated before a packed house, Chen Haoyu blustered from the floor, "Who do you think you're shoving? I won't stoop to fighting you in public—wait till we get home and see how I handle you!"
[ZH] 转身正要离开，身后传来一个女子的声音：“少夫人请留步！”
[EN] Having already tasted the seven servants' fists—and seeing from that leap that their mistress outclassed all three of them combined—the three bodyguards traded one glance and bailed. Stepping up to Chen Haoyu, they bowed quick. "We don't meddle in family matters, Young Master Chen. See you another day!"
[ZH] 千里寻回过头……
[EN] Left high and dry as they bolted out the door, Chen Haoyu scrambled to his feet—just as Qianli Xun barked, "Tie him up!"
[ZH] 从楼梯上走下一位长相俏丽，身姿窈窕，行动如弱柳扶风的女子。
[EN] Asheng whipped out a length of rope, and with two men helping, trussed Chen Haoyu tight in three seconds flat.
[ZH] 千里寻猜此人应该是春金沫。
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen, you've gone too far!" he roared. "I'm your husband! Bullying your own husband in public—everyone's going to laugh at you!"
[ZH] 春金沫来到跟前，先行礼：“少夫人万福。”
[EN] Sure enough, half the patrons craned their necks grinning at the spectacle.
[ZH] “唤我何事？”
[EN] Unwilling to linger another second in a brothel, Qianli Xun ordered, "Haul him out!"
[ZH] “少夫人的诗词我也读过，温婉可人，幽怨多情。今日得见本人，怎么看都与你的诗词不符。”
[EN] Just as she spun toward the door, a woman's voice floated down behind her. "Please wait, Young Madam!"
[ZH] “我没心情与你谈论诗词，就此别过。”
[EN] Qianli Xun turned back. Descending the stairs came a slender, pretty woman swaying like a willow branch in the breeze—Chun Jinmo, no doubt.
[ZH] 千里寻说着又要走。
[EN] Reaching her, Chun Jinmo curtsied deep. "Greetings, Young Madam."
[ZH] 春金沫赶快拦住：“少夫人且慢，我并不想谈论诗词。听说你跟陈公子立下婚前契约，互不干涉，为何今日跑来？又如此对待自己的夫君？”
[EN] "What do you want?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇竟然将这种事告诉春金沫，千里寻更是气不打一处来：“你算哪根葱？管起老娘的事来？什么婚前契约？老娘根本没听说过！懒得跟你瞎掰掰！”
[EN] "I have read your poems, Young Madam—gentle, poignant, full of tender feeling. Meeting you in person today, you look nothing like your verses."
[ZH] 千里寻第三次转身准备离开，春金沫不顾众人目光，快走几步，跪在她面前。
[EN] "I'm in no mood to critique poetry. Goodbye." Qianli Xun turned to leave again.
[ZH] “老娘又没欺负你，跪下做什么？”
[EN] Chun Jinmo stepped across her path. "Wait, Young Madam, I didn't come to talk poetry. I heard you and Young Master Chen signed a prenuptial contract promising not to meddle in each other's lives. Why barge in here today and treat your husband like this?"
[ZH] “少夫人，我自从被卖到春香楼，只有陈公子一个男人，一直是他关照我，若他不再来春香楼，恐怕我的日子不会好过，求少夫人大发慈悲放过陈公子，金沫将感激不尽。”
[EN] Learning Chen Haoyu had blabbed their private contract to a courtesan made Qianli Xun's blood boil hotter. "Who the hell are you to stick your nose in my affairs? What prenuptial contract? Never heard of it! Quit wasting my breath!"
[ZH] 春金沫跪下，陈浩宇怜爱之情泛滥，再听对方一番祈求之言，更觉怜悯：“朱淑真，你看她多可怜。你那么讨厌我，没有我你活得更好，陈家没我，还有其他子孙，可金沫不能没有我，我对她来讲实在太重要，赶紧地，把我放开，别再胡闹。”
[EN] She turned a third time—and Chun Jinmo hurried forward, dropping to her knees in front of the whole crowd.
[ZH] 千里寻还未报散播谣言之仇，岂能轻易放过？她靠近陈浩宇：“从今日起，你越想干什么我越不会让你如意。你想照顾她是吧？我偏让你照顾不了。”
[EN] "I didn't lay a finger on you! What are you kneeling for?"
[ZH] 千里寻又走到春金沫跟前：“你是挺可怜，我呢，也并非狠心之人，可我现在没心情理会你。再说你怎知我没有需要人可怜的时候？我可怜的时候你又在哪里？他陈浩宇又做些什么？老娘不是圣母，也不是救世主，你跪我没有任何用处！”
[EN] "Young Madam, ever since I was sold into Chunxiang Brothel, Young Master Chen has been my only man, looking out for me all along. If he stops coming, my life here will turn to hell. Have mercy and let Young Master Chen go—Jinmo will be forever grateful!"
[ZH] 千里寻说完，带人离开春香楼。
[EN] Seeing her kneel melted Chen Haoyu's heart into mush. "Look how pitiful she is, Zhu Shuzhen! You hate my guts—you're happier without me, and the Chen family has plenty of other sons, yet Jinmo can't survive without me! I mean the world to her! Untie me right now and quit making a scene!"
[ZH] 走到门口，陈浩宇回头对春金沫喊道：“你放心！我会很快回来！”
[EN] With her grudge still scorching hot, Qianli Xun stepped right up to his face. "Starting today, the more you want something, the harder I'll make sure you don't get it. Want to look after her? I'll make sure you never lay eyes on her again!"
[ZH] 春金沫坐在地上，眼中流出泪水。陈公子的夫人如此厉害，往后想守着陈公子已不可能，难道以后要伺候像蒋爷那种人？
[EN] Turning back to Chun Jinmo, she spoke cold and clear. "Sure, you have a rough life, and I'm not heartless—I just don't have the bandwidth to care right now. Besides, how do you know I never needed pity? Where were you when I was drowning in misery, and what was your precious Chen Haoyu doing? I'm no saint and no savior. Kneeling to me buys you nothing!"
[ZH] 春香楼的妈妈走过来，将春金沫扶起：“金沫啊，你是商妓，要明白一个道理，来咱们春香楼的爷只要点你名字，银子给够，就得伺候。除非你像金巧那么幸运，被人赎身，到大户人家当个妾室。我可是听说你给陈公子提过好几次，让他帮你赎身，他都没给个准话，我看他啊，没这心思，只不过来春香楼找你玩玩儿。他的娘子又那么厉害，赎身你就别指望，还是好好再找个爷伺候，说不定碰见一个愿意给你赎身的。我看蒋爷不错，年纪虽大些，长得也磕碜些，可对你真心，为了你跟陈公子都闹过多少回？你又不是不知道？听说他至今身边连个正室都没有，你的机会多多。”
[EN] With that, she marched her crew out the door.
[ZH] 春香楼妈妈的话，春金沫倒听进一些，抹着眼泪上二楼去了。
[EN] Dragged past the threshold, Chen Haoyu craned his neck back shouting, "Hang tight! I'll be back soon!"
[ZH] 回到陈府的墨香苑，千里寻坐在书桌前，盯着被五花大绑坐在椅子上的陈浩宇。
[EN] Left crumpled on the floorboards, Chun Jinmo wept. With a wife that fierce, clinging to Young Master Chen was over. Would she really end up serving someone like Master Jiang?
[ZH] 陈浩宇像没事人似的调侃道：“盯着本公子看什么？没见过帅哥？该不会是多日不见想我了？”
[EN] The madam stepped over and helped her up. "Jinmo, you're a courtesan—face the truth. Any lord who calls your name and puts down silver gets served, unless you get lucky like Jinqiao and someone buys you out as a concubine. I hear you hinted to Young Master Chen plenty of times to redeem you, and he never gave a straight answer. Never meant to—just came here to play. With a tigress like that at home, forget him buying you out. Pick a new patron who might actually redeem you! Master Jiang isn't a bad choice—older and rough-looking, sure, yet dead serious about you. Look how many brawls he fought with Young Master Chen over you! Word is he doesn't even have a primary wife yet. Plenty of opportunity there."
[ZH] 千里寻命令道：“红豆！替我扇他两巴掌！”
[EN] Some of the madam's words sank in, and Chun Jinmo went upstairs dabbing her eyes.
[ZH] 陈浩宇气道：“朱淑真！你是不是吃错药？”
[EN] Back in Moxiang Courtyard, Qianli Xun sat behind her desk glaring at Chen Haoyu trussed in a chair before her.
[ZH] 红豆犹豫道：“小姐，我不敢，他可是姑爷。”
[EN] Playing cool, he smirked. "Why the staring? Never seen a handsome man before? Don't tell me you missed me these past days!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇吓唬道：“你要是敢帮她打我，我就把你卖到春香楼去。”
[EN] "Hongdou! Slap him twice for me!"
[ZH] 红豆更不敢动手。
[EN] Chen Haoyu bristled. "Zhu Shuzhen, are you out of your mind?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇问：“朱淑真，你今日这么做到底想干什么？本公子跟你有婚前契约，互不干涉，你的契约精神被狗吃了？”
[EN] Hongdou faltered. "Miss, I dare not—he's the young master."
[ZH] 千里寻拿起桌上的一张纸：“契约在此，看清楚。”千里寻打开桌上的香炉，将写有契约内容的纸张卷好，放入香炉内，纸张立刻燃烧起来，很快化为灰烬。
[EN] "Touch me and I'll sell you straight to Chunxiang Brothel!" Chen Haoyu barked.
[ZH] 这波操作惊得陈浩宇瞪大眼睛，直到朱淑真盖好香炉的盖子，才说道：“你既把它烧毁，一切不作数，快将本公子的财产归还，再将这屋子和院子给本公子恢复原貌，本公子要住在这里，你快些搬到后院。”
[EN] Hongdou froze.
[ZH] “陈浩宇，你不觉得你十分可笑？老娘既然敢烧契约，就没打算把财产、院子和屋子还给你，别痴心妄想！”
[EN] "What's gotten into you today, Zhu Shuzhen?" Chen Haoyu demanded. "We had a prenuptial contract—no meddling! Did a dog eat your word?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇气愤道：“你就不怕我告诉奶奶还有我爹娘？让他们来收拾你！”
[EN] Qianli Xun lifted a sheet of paper from her desk. "Here's your contract. Watch close." Popping the lid off her bronze incense burner, she rolled the sheet tight and dropped it inside. Flames licked up and reduced it to gray ash in seconds.
[ZH] “你也得能走出这院子才行！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu's jaw dropped. Only when she clapped the lid back on did he find his voice. "Since you burned it, the deal's void! Hand back my properties, restore this courtyard and hall the way they were, and pack off to the back courtyard—I'm moving back in here!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇觉出朱淑真与往日不同，之前明明不管自己，还希望你好我好大家好，为何今日如此较真？于是平息怒气问道：“今日你是不是吃枪药了？本公子自从娶你也没做对不起你的事，为何要这么对待我？”
[EN] "Don't you feel ridiculous, Chen Haoyu? If I dared burn the contract, think I'm handing back one copper of the properties or one tile of this courtyard? Dream on!"
[ZH] 这么一问，千里寻更来气：“陈浩宇！你做过什么缺德事！难道忘了？”
[EN] "Aren't you scared I'll tell Grandmother and my parents so they come teach you a lesson?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇心中纳闷，朱淑真如此生气，还把契约给烧掉，难不成知道他散播流言之事？小心问：“你是不是知道些什么？”
[EN] "You have to make it out of this courtyard first!"
[ZH] 千里寻真想一拳打过去，她站起来，攥紧拳头，走向陈浩宇，边走边说：“去年，你散播流言，坏我婚事，让我成为全临安城的笑柄，这笔账我要跟你好好算一算。”
[EN] Sensing something had shifted—before, she had ignored him and preached peaceful coexistence; why go nuclear today?—Chen Haoyu damped his temper and asked, "Did you swallow gunpowder this morning? I haven't wronged you once since our wedding. Why pull this?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇被吓坏，原来真是为此事，心中叹道：“我小命不保！”只见朱淑真的拳头向他砸来，赶紧闭上眼睛。听到“啪嚓”一声，他掉在地上，原来朱淑真把椅子砸坏。
[EN] That question lit the fuse all over again. "Forgotten the rotten filth you pulled, Chen Haoyu?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇松口气，大胆睁开眼睛，继续强辩：“都怪你老欺负我，你要不欺负我，我也不会散播你的谣言。再说你被大家议论，不光因为我散播谣言，还有你那句‘和衣睡倒人怀’……”
[EN] A cold jolt hit his gut. Burning the contract, foaming at the mouth—had she found out about the rumors? He asked warily, "Did you hear something?"
[ZH] 千里寻大声命令：“红豆！还不赶紧扇他！”
[EN] Itching to smash his nose in, Qianli Xun rose, fists clenched, and stalked toward him. "Last year, you spread lies that wrecked my engagement and made me the laughingstock of Lin'an! Time to settle that tab!"
[ZH] 小姐再次命令，红豆不好再犹豫，走上前：“姑爷，即使你把我卖到青楼，我也要替小姐打你。”
[EN] Terror iced his veins—she knew! I'm a dead man! Seeing her fist whistle straight toward his face, he squeezed his eyes shut. Crash! Splinters exploded beneath him as he hit the floorboards—she had punched the wooden chair into kindling instead.
[ZH] 只听“啪啪”两声，打在陈浩宇的脸上。
[EN] Exhaling in relief, he cracked his eyes open and still tried to wriggle out. "Your own fault for bullying me nonstop! If you hadn't pushed me around, I never would've spread those rumors! Besides, people didn't just gossip because of me—you wrote 'falling asleep in his arms, fully dressed' yourself—"
[ZH] 苦荞在一旁看到，既替小姐感到气愤，又替姑爷感到疼痛。
[EN] "Hongdou! Slap him right now!" Qianli Xun barked.
[ZH] 陈浩宇手被绑着，没办法去揉被打疼的脸，忍着疼说：“你这丫头！使那么大劲儿干吗？”
[EN] Given a second direct order, Hongdou hesitated no longer. Stepping up, she declared, "Even if you sell me to a brothel, Young Master, I'm slapping you for Miss!"
[ZH] 千里寻蹲下，用手摸着陈浩宇被打红的脸：“老娘本想将你暴揍一顿，临时换成两巴掌，已是轻饶，你还有什么不满意的？”
[EN] Smack! Smack! Two ringing blows landed square on Chen Haoyu's cheeks.
[ZH] 陈浩宇并不领情：“朱淑真！我要休了你！”
[EN] Watching from the side, Kuqiao felt furious on her mistress's behalf yet winced at the sting on his face.
[ZH] “你想休我，没门儿，往后在陈家我会做两件事：一是好好生活，二是折磨你。”
[EN] Hands tied behind him, unable to rub his throbbing cheeks, Chen Haoyu yelped, "You little wench! Why hit so hard?"
[ZH] “你……你打算怎么折磨我？”陈浩宇感到害怕。
[EN] Qianli Xun crouched down and patted his reddened cheek. "I meant to beat you half to death, and downgraded it to two slaps. That's mercy—still complaining?"
[ZH] “凡是你喜欢的事我偏不让你做，凡是你不喜欢的事我偏让你做。”千里寻将声音放慢，每个字吐得清清楚楚。
[EN] Unmoved, Chen Haoyu spat, "Zhu Shuzhen, I'm going to divorce you!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇气得说不出话来：“你……你……”
[EN] "Divorce me? Not a chance! From today on, I have two goals in Chen Manor: live a great life, and make yours miserable."
[ZH] 千里寻站起来：“来人！把陈浩宇押到后院的博雅斋关起来！”
[EN] Fear flickered in his eyes. "W-what are you going to do to me?"
[ZH] 阿升与另一个下人进来，押起姑爷，来到后院的博雅斋，给陈浩宇松了绑。
[EN] "Everything you enjoy, I'll forbid," Qianli Xun enunciated word by cold word. "Everything you hate, I'll force you to do."
[ZH] 陈浩宇一自由，刚想回头打阿升，顺便跑出去。结果被阿升挡住：“姑爷，您还是听话，惹怒小姐可没好果子吃。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu sputtered speechless. "You... you..."
[ZH] 接着看到窗户被关上，有下人在外面用木板开始钉窗户。
[EN] Qianli Xun stood. "Guards! Haul Chen Haoyu to Boya Study in the back courtyard and lock him up!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇走到跟前，训斥道：“你们给我住手！我可是这院子的主人！”
[EN] Asheng and another servant hoisted him up, marched him to Boya Study, and untied his ropes. The instant his arms came free, Chen Haoyu swung at Asheng hoping to bolt—only for Asheng to block him solid. "Behave yourself, Young Master. Crossing Miss won't end well for you."
[ZH] 又听到阿升等人出去把门锁上的声音。
[EN] Outside, servants slammed the shutters and began nailing wooden planks across the windows.
[ZH] 陈浩宇向门外大声喊：“朱淑真！我跟你没完！你等着瞧！这是陈家！不是你们朱家！也不是你的望月轩！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu stormed to the sill roaring, "Stop that! I own this courtyard!"
[ZH] 青瓜的声音从窗外传来：“公子，公子，你还好吧？”
[EN] Behind him, Asheng stepped out and snapped a heavy padlock onto the door.
[ZH] 陈浩宇第一次觉得青瓜的声音如此可爱，凑到窗户跟前：“青瓜，你可来了，母老虎把你家公子我折磨得挺惨，快去替我向老太太、夫人和老爷告状，就说我要休了她，快去！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu pounded the wood shouting, "Zhu Shuzhen, this isn't over! Just wait! This is Chen Manor, not Zhu Manor, and not your Wangyue Pavilion!"
[ZH] “公子放心，我这就去。”
[EN] A hushed voice drifted through a crack in the boards. "Young Master? Young Master, are you all right?"
[ZH] 青瓜说完小跑着来到前院，准备跑出墨香苑前去告状，还没跑过池塘，被少夫人叫住：“青瓜！去干什么？难道是帮你家公子通风报信？”
[EN] For the first time in his life, Qinggua's voice sounded like heaven. Chen Haoyu pressed his face to the shutters. "Qinggua, thank goodness! The tigress is torturing me! Run straight to Old Madam Xia, Father, and Mother—tell them I'm repudiating her! Go!"
[ZH] 青瓜站住，掩饰道：“少夫人误会我，我是想出去找个更宽敞的地儿练习您教的幻影闪移。”
[EN] "Right away, Young Master!"
[ZH] “你已经练得十分熟悉，不必再练。我担心你没地方睡，从今日起就和陈浩宇一起待在博雅斋。”为防止青瓜到处乱说，千里寻只能出此下策。
[EN] Qinggua sprinted toward the front yard to sound the alarm, yet had not even cleared the lotus pond before Qianli Xun's voice froze him in his tracks. "Qinggua! Where are you rushing? Playing messenger for your master?"
[ZH] 青瓜求道：“少夫人，犯错误的是我家公子，您没必要连累我，我住在西院也是可以的。”
[EN] Skidding to a halt, Qinggua scrambled for a fib. "Not at all, Young Madam! I was just looking for an open space to practice Phantom Sidestep!"
[ZH] “你家公子住在博雅斋挺孤单，你就当是陪他。”千里寻上前抓起青瓜，拽着他往后院走。
[EN] "You already know it inside out—no need to practice. I worried you didn't have a bed, so from today on, you're rooming with Chen Haoyu in Boya Study." Taking zero chances on him running his mouth, she marched over, grabbed him by the collar, and hauled him toward the back courtyard.
[ZH] 博雅斋的门被打开，陈浩宇以为是青瓜找来奶奶做主，准备放他出去。刚要高兴，结果见青瓜被推进来，门又被关上。
[EN] When the padlock rattled open, Chen Haoyu beamed, sure Qinggua had summoned Grandmother to spring him—only for Qinggua to fly stumbling through the doorway before the door slammed shut again.
[ZH] 外面传来朱淑真的声音：“陈浩宇，怕你孤单，给你送个做伴的。”
[EN] "Didn't want you lonely, Chen Haoyu," Zhu Shuzhen's voice drifted in, "so I sent you a roommate!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇冲到门跟前，拍打着门，大声向外喊道：“朱淑真！等本公子出去！非休了你不可！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu hurled himself against the door, hammering the panels. "Zhu Shuzhen! The second I get out of here, I swear I'll divorce you!"

#CH 225
[ZH] 陈浩宇拍打一会儿门，又喊了一会儿，毫无用处，一肚子气得找个发泄的途径。他转过身，青瓜一脸无辜地站在面前，令他看着更来气。
[ZH] “本公子让你找老太太、老爷和夫人前来，你怎么也被关进来？”
[ZH] “公子莫怪我，少夫人厉害，我刚跑到前院，被她看出企图，然后就把我关起来。”
[ZH] “你傻啊，不会编瞎话。”
[ZH] “我编了，她不信，说让我来陪您。”青瓜走到陈浩宇跟前，“公子，不如您以后听话些，少夫人让您往东，您就别往西，跟她对着干没好果子吃，还会牵连我……”
[ZH] 陈浩宇本就气，听了这话气上加气，使出更大的力气踢出一脚：“还敢嫌弃本公子！看我不踢死你！”
[ZH] 结果青瓜轻松又迅速地闪向一边，陈浩宇没踢着，自己倒差点儿被闪倒。又上一脚，还是没踢着，接连踢出好几脚，青瓜都轻松躲开。
[EN] After pounding the door and shouting himself hoarse to zero effect, Chen Haoyu needed a punching bag. Spinning around, he laid eyes on Qinggua standing there blinking innocently, and saw red.
[ZH] 陈浩宇气得不再用脚，直接使出几招擒拿把式，拿住青瓜：“让你躲！从哪里习得的招数？”
[EN] "I sent you to fetch Old Madam Xia and my parents! How the hell did you end up locked in here too?"
[ZH] 青瓜直喊：“公子饶命！公子饶命！是少夫人教的幻影闪移！”
[EN] "Don't blame me, Young Master! Young Madam's too sharp—caught me in the front courtyard, saw straight through me, and tossed me in here."
[ZH] “又是朱淑真！”陈浩宇松开青瓜，趁他还未回头，一脚踢在屁股上，“去你娘的！”
[EN] "You idiot, couldn't you make up a lie?"
[ZH] 青瓜向前趔趄一下，倒在地上，倒也没伤着，爬起来坐在地板上：“公子别再欺负我，把我欺负得再惨，您也出不去，还是想想办法怎么出去吧？”
[EN] "I did! She didn't buy it, said I should keep you company." Qinggua edged closer. "Young Master, why not just play along from now on? If she says east, don't go west. Fighting her gets you nowhere and drags me down with you—"
[ZH] 陈浩宇虽生气，心中也明白，都是自己惹下的事，跟青瓜没关系，还是想想如何把消息传给爹娘和奶奶，只要他们来，朱淑真迫于长辈压力，肯定会把他放出去。
[EN] Already boiling, Chen Haoyu snapped and threw a fierce kick. "Daring to lecture me now? I'll kick you silly!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇走到软椅跟前坐下：“你跟院子里的下人哪个混得熟？”
[EN] Qinggua glided sideways with effortless ease; Chen Haoyu missed empty air and nearly toppled over. Another kick—missed. Three, four more kicks—Qinggua slipped past every single one.
[ZH] 青瓜得意道：“每个都熟，公子不在这些日子，我天天在西院跟他们练习招数。当然我只练幻影闪移，少夫人说我没根骨，拳法招式教了也学不会。我天天跟他们混在一处，自然混熟。公子，您不知道，这一来二去，我……我喜欢上红豆，她好像对我也有意思……”青瓜说着脸不由红起来。
[EN] Furious, Chen Haoyu ditched kicking and locked Qinggua in a basic grappling hold. "Keep dodging! Where'd you pick up that trick?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不屑道：“你那贱样儿红豆会喜欢你？别异想天开！赶紧说说，那十个下人中，你跟哪个关系最好，想办法让他帮忙通风报信。”
[EN] "Mercy, Young Master! Mercy! Young Madam taught me Phantom Sidestep!"
[ZH] 自信心被公子打击，青瓜感到委屈，顾不上说正事：“公子从来瞧不上我，我好不容喜欢个丫鬟您都要损我两句。您瞧瞧少夫人，对她身边的下人多好，您就应该跟少夫人多学习学习。”
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen again!" Releasing him, Chen Haoyu waited half a second before booting Qinggua square in the backside. "Screw her!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不满道：“什么时候轮到你教训主子？再不说正事，小心我抽你！”
[EN] Stumbling forward onto his knees, unhurt, Qinggua sat back on the floor. "Stop taking it out on me, Young Master. Beating me won't get you out of here—better figure out how to escape."
[ZH] 青瓜撇撇嘴：“十个下人都跟我关系好，只可惜，他们听少夫人的，我可使唤不动。”
[EN] Mad as he was, Chen Haoyu knew none of this was Qinggua's fault. The real trick was getting word to his parents and grandmother; once the elders intervened, Zhu Shuzhen would have to unlock the door.
[ZH] 陈浩宇起身走到青瓜跟前，拽住他胸前的衣裳：“本公子费半天口舌，你就给我这个结果，现在罚你到门口喊：‘朱淑真！我要休了你！’一直喊，不准停！”
[EN] Dropping into a cushioned chair, he asked, "Which servant in this courtyard are you closest with?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇一把将青瓜推到门口：“喊！”
[EN] Qinggua puffed up with pride. "All of them! While you were gone, I drilled in the West Courtyard with them every day—just Phantom Sidestep, mind you, since Young Madam said I lack the bone structure for fist forms. Hanging out daily made us tight. Actually, Young Master... between us, I fell for Hongdou, and I think she likes me back!" His ears turned pink.
[ZH] 青瓜趴在门口的地上：“公子，少夫人是嫁给您，又不是嫁给我，我怎么好意思那么喊？再说我是下人，怎么敢直呼少夫人名讳？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu snorted. "Hongdou falling for a mug like yours? Quit daydreaming! Tell me which of the ten servants you're tightest with so we can get him to sneak out a message."
[ZH] 陈浩宇走近，抬起脚，吓唬道：“你要再不喊，小心本公子把你踩成肉饼！”
[EN] Wounded by the dig, Qinggua pouted. "You always look down on me! First maid I ever like and you mock me. Look how well Young Madam treats her servants—you ought to take notes from her!"
[ZH] 青瓜被吓到：“我喊，我喊，公子息怒。”
[EN] "Since when does a lackey lecture his master? Talk business or I'll smack you!"
[ZH] 青瓜爬起来，靠在门口，对外说：“朱淑真，我要休了你。”
[EN] Qinggua pursed his lips. "I get along fine with all ten, problem is they answer to Young Madam. None of them take orders from me."
[ZH] 陈浩宇命令道：“大声些！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu jumped up and grabbed the front of Qinggua's tunic. "All that breath wasted for nothing? Fine—your punishment is to stand at the door and yell, 'Zhu Shuzhen, I'm divorcing you!' Don't stop until I say!" Shoving him against the door, he barked, "Yell!"
[ZH] 青瓜提高一些：“朱淑真，我要休了你。”
[EN] Sprawled by the threshold, Qinggua whimpered, "Young Master, she married you, not me! How can I shout that? And a servant calling his mistress by her full name—"
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续命令：“声音再大些！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu lifted a boot over him. "Yell, or I stomp you into meat pie!"
[ZH] 青瓜闭上眼睛，声音变得更大：“朱淑真！我要休了你！”
[EN] "I'll yell! I'll yell! Hold your temper!" Scrambling up, Qinggua leaned against the wood and muttered toward the crack, "Zhu Shuzhen, I'm divorcing you."
[ZH] 如此反复喊着……
[EN] "Louder!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇很满意，回里屋到床上躺着睡觉去了。
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen, I'm divorcing you!"
[ZH] 对面的静宜堂内，韦嬷嬷安心坐着，用心听外面的动静。当然也听到青瓜的声音，她起身自语道：“我还是到老太太屋里走一趟。”
[EN] "Louder!"
[ZH] 话说三个媳妇正陪老太太聊天儿，丫鬟急急匆匆进来报告：“老太太！老太太！不好了！不好了！”
[EN] Squeezing his eyes shut, Qinggua bawled at the top of his lungs, "Zhu Shuzhen! I'm divorcing you!" Over and over he shouted.
[ZH] 老太太批评道：“怎么说话呢？什么事着急忙慌的？有话慢慢说。”
[EN] Satisfied, Chen Haoyu strolled into the inner bedroom and flopped down for a nap.
[ZH] 丫鬟这才缓慢说道：“回老太太，刚才我看见二少夫人把二公子五花大绑给押回来，看样子二公子要遭罪。”
[EN] Right across the back yard in Jingyi Hall, Matron Wei sat calm and alert, listening to every sound—including Qinggua's howling. Rising to her feet, she murmured, "Time to pay Old Madam Xia a visit."
[ZH] 夏老太太听了，不由自主担心起来。姚轻琰也是心中一颤，担忧不已。
[EN] Over in Old Madam Xia's rooms, her three daughters-in-law sat chatting with her when a maid burst in gasping, "Old Madam! Old Madam! Terrible news!"
[ZH] 包氏知是儿子的计谋得逞，心中得意，装作不清楚的样子，急道：“这还了得？刚嫁过来的媳妇，竟然把自家男人给管住，老夫人您可得管管，要不别人还以为我们陈家的男人任由女人欺负！”
[EN] "Mind your tongue," Old Madam Xia scolded. "What's got you flustered? Slow down and speak plain."
[ZH] 崔氏也应和道：“大嫂，你得管管，教教朱家姑娘怎么当好儿媳妇，浩宇再怎么纨绔，有你和大哥，也轮不上她管教。”
[EN] Catching her breath, the maid reported, "I just saw Second Young Madam march Second Young Master through the gate tied up in ropes! Looks like he's in for rough treatment!"
[ZH] 老太太忍了忍说：“你们两个不要瞎起哄，我说过让淑真管教浩宇，自然相信她，不管她怎么管，轻琰和江林都不许插手，我也不插手。”
[EN] Both Old Madam Xia and Yao Qingyan felt their hearts clench in worry.
[ZH] 姚轻琰很想前去看个究竟，也忍住没动：“我听母亲的。”
[EN] Thrilled that her son's scheme had worked, Madam Bao feigned shock. "Outrageous! A bride barely through the door tying up her own husband! You have to step in, Mother, or folks will think Chen men let women walk all over them!"
[ZH] 一个担心孙子，一个担心儿子，都没有心情再聊天儿。包氏与崔氏觉得无趣，一起拜别老太太离开。
[EN] Madam Cui piled on. "Eldest Sister-in-law, you need to put your foot down and teach that Zhu girl how a daughter-in-law behaves! Wild as Haoyu is, disciplining him is up to you and Eldest Brother, not her!"
[ZH] 走到院外，包氏说：“也不知大哥和大嫂怎么会看上朱家姑娘？虽是女词人，可性情不好，名声也不好。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia tamped down her own unease. "Quit fanning the flames, you two. I gave Shuzhen authority to discipline Haoyu, so I trust her. However she handles him, neither Qingyan nor Jianglin is to interfere—and neither will I."
[ZH] 崔氏说：“姐姐又何必装？浩宇娶得不好，对浩东只有好处没有坏处，你就偷着乐吧。”
[EN] Itching to rush over and check, Yao Qingyan bit her lip and held still. "I'll obey you, Mother."
[ZH] 包氏说：“说的哪里话？浩宇即使娶个好娘子，也没法跟我家浩东比，他从小跟着他爹和大伯、三叔学做生意，将生意那行当摸得门儿清，怪只怪浩宇只知道浪荡，你家浩飞又不愿多在生意上费心思，只能苦了我家浩东。”
[EN] With both women too distracted to chat, Madam Bao and Madam Cui saw the fun was over and took their leave together.
[ZH] 包氏嫌弃浩飞，崔氏一脸不满：“姐姐忙，我还有事，失陪。”
[EN] Once outside the gate, Madam Bao sniffed, "Heaven knows what Eldest Brother and Sister-in-law saw in that Zhu girl—poetess or not, foul temper and stained name."
[ZH] 包氏也懒得再理会崔氏，待对方走远，自语道：“陈家祖上都是生意人，不教自家儿子好好学做生意，却妄想考功名到京城为官，我看难！”
[EN] "Drop the act, Second Sister-in-law," Madam Cui retorted. "Haoyu marrying poorly only helps Haodong. Go laugh behind closed doors."
[ZH] 发生这么一桩好事，包氏无暇顾及其他，还是赶紧回去给儿子报喜。
[EN] "What talk is that? Even if Haoyu landed a saint, he couldn't hold a candle to my Haodong, who learned trade from his father and uncles since childhood. Not our fault Haoyu only chases pleasure and your Haofei ignores business—leaving all the heavy lifting to my Haodong."
[ZH] 正要走，瞧见韦嬷嬷朝这边而来，并未多想，径自离开。
[EN] Stung by the swipe at her son, Madam Cui stiffened. "I have chores to attend to. Excuse me."
[ZH] 夏老太太与姚轻琰坐在屋里沉默不语，各自体会着内心的矛盾。一旁的丫鬟不敢言语，默默站着，气氛显得十分凝重。
[EN] Madam Bao sneered after her retreating back. "Generations of Chen merchants, yet instead of teaching her son trade, she daydreams of him winning an official post in the capital. Good luck with that!"
[ZH] 门外有丫鬟报告：“老太太、大夫人，二少夫人房中的韦嬷嬷前来拜见。”
[EN] With news this juicy, she hurried off to report to her son—barely glancing at Matron Wei walking toward Old Madam Xia's courtyard as they crossed paths.
[ZH] 两人脸上才露出活泛之色。老太太说：“快！快快有请！”
[EN] Inside, Old Madam Xia and Yao Qingyan sat in taut silence, each wrestling with her heart while the maids stood mute in the heavy air.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷进入后，先向二位主子行礼。
[EN] At last a maid announced, "Old Madam, Eldest Madam, Matron Wei from Second Young Madam's courtyard requests an audience."
[ZH] 老太太吩咐丫鬟看座：“自孙媳嫁过来，你还没来我院中走动过。”
[EN] Color rushed back to both women's faces. "Show her in at once!" Old Madam Xia ordered.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷早已注意到二位主人担心焦虑之色，她们虽愿意把管教后辈的权力交给自家姑娘，可一旦真正管教起姑爷来，眼前这二位又有万分不忍。
[EN] Matron Wei stepped in and bowed.
[ZH] “是我的疏漏，还望老太太宽宏大量，不要跟我计较。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia waved for a chair. "Since Shuzhen married in, this is your first visit to my quarters."
[ZH] 老太太甚是理解：“你不来我这里也正常，淑真院中的事务都需你打理，你可是个大忙人。”
[EN] Reading the anxiety etched across both faces—bold enough to delegate discipline, yet softhearted the moment it began—Matron Wei replied smoothly, "My oversight, Old Madam. I beg your forbearance."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷发现老太太与她说话时，露出勉强的笑容，猜到她担忧孙子，还是帮眼前二位宽宽心。
[EN] "Understandable," Old Madam Xia said with a forced smile. "Running Shuzhen's entire courtyard keeps you busy enough."
[ZH] “感谢老太太理解，今日我终于得空，过来看望您，顺便说一下我家姑娘和姑爷之事。”
[EN] Deciding to set their hearts at ease right away, Matron Wei said, "Thank you for understanding, Old Madam. Now that I have a free moment, I came to pay respects and speak with you about Miss and Young Master."
[ZH] 两人早已猜到韦嬷嬷前来是为此事，她主动提起，两人不由异口同声：“浩宇现在可好？”
[EN] Both women blurted in unison, "Is Haoyu all right?"
[ZH] “瞧二位着急的样子，该不会担心我家姑娘虐待姑爷？”
[EN] "Look how anxious you both are—worried Miss is abusing him?"
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“虐待倒不至于，我就是不忍见浩宇受罪。”
[EN] "Not abusing," Yao Qingyan hedged, "I just hate seeing him suffer."
[ZH] 老太太叹道：“浩宇从小我就宠着他，见不得他受一点儿苦头。虽知是害他，可还是忍不住不去挂心。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia sighed. "I spoiled him from the cradle and can't bear seeing him take the smallest hardship. I know it hurt him, yet I can't stop fretting."
[ZH] “由此看来，二位将管教姑爷的权力交给我家姑娘实在正确。姑娘这会儿把姑爷关在后院，钉了窗户，锁了门，只派下人送水送饭……”
[EN] "All the more reason you were wise to leave his discipline to Miss," Matron Wei said. "Right now she has him locked in the back courtyard—windows boarded, door padlocked, servants bringing him meals and water..."
[ZH] 二位又异口同声：“这如何使得？”
[EN] Both women gasped together, "How can she do that?"
[ZH] 姚轻琰接着说：“让淑真管教，也不必这么个管教法？浩宇哪能受得了？”
[EN] "Disciplining him is fine, yet isn't locking him up too harsh?" Yao Qingyan fretted. "How can Haoyu stand that?"
[ZH] 二位如此表现，韦嬷嬷认为有必要将实情告知：“今日夫人拦住我，让我劝说姑娘管教姑爷，可我家姑娘根本不乐意管教。现在她为何突然愿意？并不是我能言善辩将她说服，而是她听说了一件事……”
[EN] Seeing their panic, Matron Wei played her real card. "Earlier today, Madam, you stopped me to persuade Miss to discipline Young Master—which she had zero intention of doing. Why did she suddenly change her mind? Not because I talked her into it, but because she just learned something."
[ZH] 老太太问：“什么事？”
[EN] "Learned what?" Old Madam Xia asked.
[ZH] “去年散播我家姑娘谣言之人正是姑爷。”
[EN] "That Young Master was the one who spread all those rumors about her last year."
[ZH] 夏老太太与姚轻琰对视一番，更加担忧：“唉！此事瞒得了一时，也瞒不了一世，淑真知道也好，恐怕以后浩宇更没好日子过。还请你回去多规劝规劝淑真，管教归管教，别让她下狠手。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia and Yao Qingyan traded a dismayed look. "You can't wrap fire in paper forever," Old Madam Xia sighed. "Just as well she found out—though poor Haoyu is in for rough days now. Please go back and coax Shuzhen: discipline is fine, just beg her not to go too heavy-handed."
[ZH] 姚轻琰也急切地说：“浩宇纨绔，做下此等损淑真名节之事，只怕淑真管教起来没个轻重。”
[EN] "Haoyu acted like a scoundrel ruining her name," Yao Qingyan fretted. "I'm terrified she'll lose control and injure him!"
[ZH] “二位既然愿意让我家姑娘管教姑爷，只需充分信任。我看着姑娘长大，她的心性最是纯正，善良更不必说……”
[EN] "Since you entrusted Young Master to Miss, trust her completely," Matron Wei reassured them. "I watched Miss grow up. Her heart is upright and deeply kind—"
[ZH] 姚轻琰还是充满担忧，没等韦嬷嬷说完，立刻说：“可文心告诉我，淑真失忆后变得厉害，跟以前完全不一样。”
[EN] "Wenxin told me she turned fierce after losing her memory, nothing like before!" Yao Qingyan cut in.
[ZH] “夫人放心，姑娘再跟以前不同，骨子里的善良还是没变，她嫁过来这段时日难道二位没有丝毫体会？”
[EN] "Fierce on the surface, yes, yet her core kindness hasn't changed one bit. Haven't you both felt that since she married in?"
[ZH] 夏老太太和姚轻琰都点头承认。
[EN] Both women nodded.
[ZH] “姑娘再恨姑爷散播谣言破坏与乔家的婚事，也不会真的用拳头伤姑爷，只会教训教训他。你们只管把心放在肚子里，就当不知道此事，大不了当姑爷在外逛荡没回家。再说他往日在外遇到被人欺负之事，你们也不知道，还不就那么过来？”
[EN] "Even furious at him for ruining her match with Qiao Manor, Miss would never genuinely hurt him with her fists—just teach him a sharp lesson. Put your minds at rest and pretend you know nothing. Think of it as him off gallivanting again! Heaven knows how many scrapes he got into outside without you knowing, and he still came home bouncing on both feet."
[ZH] 两人想起浩宇从外面带伤回来，还有蒋爷设赌局之事，也许还有很多他们不知道的，浩宇还不是活蹦乱跳？于是都松口气。
[EN] Remembering the bruises he had dragged home and Master Jiang's rigged wager—who knew how many other close calls they had never heard of—both women exhaled.
[ZH] 姚轻琰狠狠心说：“韦嬷嬷说得对，就当他出去逛荡。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan steeled herself. "Matron Wei is right. We'll pretend he's out roaming."
[ZH] 夏老太太心中虽舒坦些，还是担心：“听你刚才所言，淑真只是因散播谣言之事把浩宇关起来，并非出于管教的目的，只怕达不到我们想要的结果。”
[EN] Still uneasy, Old Madam Xia asked, "Wait—from what you said, Shuzhen locked him up out of anger over the rumors, not to reform him. Won't that miss the whole point?"
[ZH] “老太太多虑，无论是出于什么目的，姑娘用的手段一致，效果必然一样，您就等着我家姑娘帮您管教出一个好孙子。说不定还真能像夫人所言，两块顽石碰撞多了，都能碰出火花，何况是两个大活人？”
[EN] "Rest easy, Old Madam," Matron Wei smiled. "Whatever her motive, her methods are identical, so the result will be the same. Just wait for Miss to whip a fine grandson into shape for you—and who knows, just as Eldest Madam said, bang two stones together enough and sparks might really fly!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷一番话，说得两人终于“呵呵”笑起来，刚才的忧虑烟消云散。
[EN] At that, both women chuckled, their gloom lifting away.
[ZH] 最后韦嬷嬷叮嘱道：“姑娘管教姑爷，还请二位千万不要插手，若姑爷有任何求助，也请二位狠下心来，不必理会。若稍加心软，恐怕姑娘所有的努力白白浪费。”
[EN] Before leaving, Matron Wei gave one final warning. "While Miss disciplines Young Master, neither of you must step in. If he sends pleas for help, harden your hearts and ignore them. Soften even once, and all Miss's effort goes down the drain."
[ZH] 两人一边暗暗为自己宽心，一边点头同意。
[EN] Bracing themselves internally, both women nodded in agreement.

#CH 226
[ZH] 青瓜已喊得有气无力，不敢停下，依然小声吐出：“朱淑真，我要休了你。”
[ZH] 传来一阵呼噜声，青瓜心中一喜：“公子睡着了，我终于可以歇息歇息，喊得口干舌燥，先喝盏茶。”
[ZH] 他起身，走到木桌旁，提起茶壶，倒上一盏，用嘴唇试了试：“唉，茶都凉了，也不见有人送热茶来？算了，口太渴，凑合着喝吧。”
[ZH] 青瓜将茶喝下，向窗外瞧瞧：“天都黑了，肚子好饿，怎么不见有人送饭？”
[ZH] 正想着，听到门外开锁的声音，青瓜兴冲冲地迎上去。门果然被打开，红豆一只手提着食盒，一只手拎着一壶热茶走进来。
[ZH] “红豆，你终于来了。”青瓜上前接住红豆手中的茶水，放在桌上，瞅见开着的门，想趁机出去，结果门又被关上。
[ZH] 红豆把食盒放在桌上，从里面取出两盘素菜，两碗饭：“小姐让我送饭过来，姑爷呢？赶紧唤他前来用饭。”
[ZH] 从听到门被打开，陈浩宇就睁开眼睛，来到前厅，瞧见红豆批评道：“等本公子出去，定将你发卖掉，报了扇我两巴掌的仇恨。”
[ZH] 打都打了，红豆也不再害怕，反正背后有小姐撑腰：“我的卖身契在小姐手里，姑爷拿我没办法。”
[EN] By dusk, Qinggua's voice had shrunk to a hoarse croak, yet he dared not stop whispering, "Zhu Shuzhen, I'm divorcing you."
[ZH] 被一个丫鬟怼，陈浩宇气道：“你去告诉朱淑真，本公子要休了她。”
[EN] Then a loud snore drifted from the inner bedroom. Qinggua lit up. "Young Master's asleep! Break time—my throat's dry as parchment. Need tea."
[ZH] “姑爷都让青瓜喊了一下午，整个墨香苑的人都知道，小姐怎能不知？何须我告诉？小姐说了，姑爷想怎样，她偏不让您怎样，您想休她，没门儿！”
[EN] Padding to the wooden table, he poured a cup and tested it with his lips. "Cold already, and nobody brought a fresh pot? Oh well, thirsty enough to drink anything." Gulping it down, he glanced at the dim cracks between the boarded shutters. "Dark already and my stomach's growling. Where's dinner?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇伸出拳头想打红豆。
[EN] Right then the padlock rattled outside. Qinggua darted to the door as it swung open and Hongdou walked in carrying a food carrier in one hand and a steaming teapot in the other.
[ZH] 青瓜见状，挡在红豆面前：“公子，别跟红豆置气，我们还指着她送饭呢。”
