[EN] "Hongdou! You're here!" Taking the teapot to set on the table, Qinggua eyed the open doorway thinking of bolting—just as the door clicked shut behind her.
[ZH] 身边的人如此表现，陈浩宇更是气不打一处来，一时忘记青瓜已学会招式，习惯性地使出一脚，
[EN] Hongdou set down the food carrier and lifted out two plates of plain vegetables and two bowls of rice. "Miss sent dinner. Where's Young Master? Call him out to eat."
[ZH] 青瓜轻松躲开：“公子别再踢我，没用的，我是心疼公子，没敢动手，要是别人，我早趁机把他推到一边。”
[EN] Woken by the clack of the padlock, Chen Haoyu strolled into the front room and glared at Hongdou. "The second I get out, I'm selling you off to pay you back for those two slaps!"
[ZH] 红豆的功夫陈浩宇见识过，根本打不过。唯一能欺负的青瓜，也被朱淑真改造，陈浩宇以后连个撒气的都没有。他站稳后，攥紧两个拳头，愤恨地说：“朱淑真！我跟你没完！”
[EN] Having already swung on him, Hongdou had zero fear left with her mistress backing her. "Miss holds my deed of sale, Young Master. Nothing you can do to me."
[ZH] 红豆觉得好笑：“姑爷还是赶紧用饭，我还等着把盘子和碗拿走。”
[EN] Snapped at by a maid, Chen Haoyu bristled. "Go tell Zhu Shuzhen I'm divorcing her!"
[ZH] 打又打不过，干生气一点儿用处没有，肚子已饿，陈浩宇松开拳头，坐到桌旁：“怎么就两道菜？本公吃饭最少也五菜一汤。告诉朱淑真，以后多加几道菜。”
[EN] "You had Qinggua shouting it all afternoon—every soul in Moxiang Courtyard heard, Miss included. No need for me to repeat it! Miss says whatever you want, she'll do the exact opposite. Divorcing her? Dream on!"
[ZH] “小姐说了，姑爷只要提一个要求，菜量立刻减少。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu cocked a fist toward Hongdou—and Qinggua jumped right between them. "Don't take it out on Hongdou, Young Master! We depend on her to bring us food!"
[ZH] “算了，就当我没说。”陈浩宇忍着怒气，提起筷子，要夹菜时，发现一点儿肉星子都没有，“怎么都是素菜？连个肉末都没有？”
[EN] Seeing his own servant jump in front of a maid made Chen Haoyu snap. Forgetting Qinggua's training, he swung a boot out of pure reflex.
[ZH] “小姐说了，姑爷被关期间，只准吃素，不准吃肉。”
[EN] Qinggua glided right past it. "Quit kicking me, Young Master—doesn't work anymore! Only reason I didn't shove you flat is because I care about you; anyone else would've hit the floor!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不再说话，不是因为屈服，而是因为气得已不想再说，便动起筷子吃起来。
[EN] Chen Haoyu knew he could not beat Hongdou, and now Zhu Shuzhen had ruined even his lone punching bag. Planting his feet, he clenched both fists and hissed, "Zhu Shuzhen, this isn't over!"
[ZH] 青瓜饿得不行，另一碗饭明显是给他准备的，大胆坐在公子对面，拿起筷子打算夹菜。
[EN] Amused, Hongdou said, "Better eat up, Young Master. I'm waiting to take the bowls back."
[ZH] 陈浩宇见青瓜坐下也要吃饭，气不打一处来：“谁允许你跟本公子坐一桌吃饭？”
[EN] Too hungry to waste more steam on a losing battle, Chen Haoyu dropped onto a stool—and scowled at the table. "Two measly dishes? I never eat less than five dishes and a soup! Tell Zhu Shuzhen to send more!"
[ZH] 青瓜委屈地放下筷子，站起来，看着两盘菜和一碗饭，咽下口水：“公子，我帮您喊了一下午，没有功劳也有苦劳，肚子饿得慌，就让我吃两口行不行？”
[EN] "Miss says every time you make a demand, we cut the portion further."
[ZH] 红豆强调道：“姑爷还是让青瓜和您坐一桌吃吧，这是小姐的吩咐。”
[EN] "Fine, forget I spoke!" Swallowing his rage, he picked up his chopsticks—and froze. "All vegetables? Not even a scrap of minced pork?"
[ZH] 青瓜趁机说：“公子，您听听，是少夫人的吩咐。”
[EN] "Miss says while you're locked up, it's vegetarian only. No meat."
[ZH] 本来陈浩宇因青瓜委屈的样子有些心软，想要答应，被这么一说，强硬道：“你是我的下人得听我的，朱淑真说的不算。要是敢坐下跟我一起用饭，看我不削你！”
[EN] Too livid to waste another word, Chen Haoyu dug in.
[ZH] 青瓜不敢继续坐下，站在一旁盯着公子一口一口地享用。公子每吃下一口菜，他都揪心不已，担心一会儿剩下的菜不够自己吃。
[EN] Starving himself, Qinggua eyed the second bowl of rice set out for him, took a bold seat across the table, and reached for his chopsticks.
[ZH] 公子终于吃完，还好每个盘子里都剩下一半儿菜，青瓜很满意，等公子站起来瞪红豆一眼回里屋去后，青瓜才大胆坐下狼吞虎咽起来。
[EN] "Who gave you permission to sit at my table?" Chen Haoyu barked.
[ZH] 吃完后，青瓜帮红豆收拾碗筷，趁机问：“红豆，你觉得我怎么样？”
[EN] Qinggua set the chopsticks down, stood up, and swallowed hard staring at the rice. "I yelled my lungs out for you all afternoon, Young Master—if not merit, at least pity! My belly's howling; let me have a couple bites?"
[ZH] 红豆将空碗放进食盒里：“还行。”
[EN] "Let Qinggua eat at the table with you, Young Master," Hongdou interposed. "Miss's orders."
[ZH] “还行是什么意思？”青瓜不太懂，继续追问。
[EN] "Hear that, Young Master? Young Madam's orders!" Qinggua chimed in.
[ZH] 红豆又把空盘放进食盒：“嗯，就是不好也不坏。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu had almost softened at Qinggua's puppy-dog eyes, yet invoking Zhu Shuzhen hardened him right back. "You're my servant, you obey me—Zhu Shuzhen doesn't call the shots! Sit down with me and see if I don't skin you!"
[ZH] 青瓜对这个回答不甚满意：“那你喜不喜欢我这种不好又不坏的人？”
[EN] Backing off, Qinggua stood hovering at his elbow, heart twisting with every chopstickful his master lifted, terrified nothing would be left.
[ZH] 对方的问题如此直白，红豆立刻明白青瓜的意思。因不喜欢对方，倒不觉得难为情：“青瓜，你脑子一天尽想些什么？陈府这么多丫鬟总有喜欢你的。”
[EN] At last Chen Haoyu finished—leaving half of each vegetable dish intact. The instant his master shot Hongdou a glare and stomped back into the bedroom, Qinggua dropped onto the stool and wolfed everything down.
[ZH] 桌上的碗筷都已收拾进食盒里，青瓜把放有凉茶的壶递到红豆手中：“别人我不管，我只关心你喜欢不喜欢我。”
[EN] Once full, he helped Hongdou stack the dishes and seized the moment. "Hongdou, what do you think of me?"
[ZH] 红豆一手提着食盒，一手拎着茶壶：“喜欢？……”
[EN] Setting the empty bowls into the food carrier, Hongdou replied, "You're all right."
[ZH] 听到“喜欢”两个字，青瓜一阵高兴。
[EN] "What does 'all right' mean?" Qinggua pressed.
[ZH] 红豆接着说：“还是不喜欢？……”
[EN] Stacking the plates inside, she said, "Not great, not terrible."
[ZH] 听到“不喜欢”三个字，青瓜一阵担心。
[EN] Unsatisfied, Qinggua pushed on. "So... do you like someone who's not great and not terrible?"
[ZH] 红豆又说：“我也不知道。”
[EN] With the question laid bare, Hongdou felt zero embarrassment since she had zero interest. "What's rolling around in that head of yours, Qinggua? Plenty of maids in Chen Manor—bound to be someone who likes you."
[ZH] 青瓜虽觉得失落，还是跟在红豆身后，边送她到门口边说：“我其实很不错，你跟我接触多了自然会了解到我的好，若是嫁给我你会发现我是最会心疼媳妇儿的人。”
[EN] Handing her the cold teapot, Qinggua said, "I don't care about anyone else. I only care if you like me."
[ZH] 红豆笑道：“你既然这么好，我帮你宣传宣传，看看墨香苑有没有丫鬟能瞧得上你，若是墨香苑的丫鬟瞧不上，我就在陈府宣传宣传，怎么着也能给你找个媳妇儿。”
[EN] Lifting the carrier in one hand and the teapot in the other, Hongdou drawled, "Do I like you..."
[ZH] 青瓜还未反应过来，门被打开，又被人从外面锁上。
[EN] Qinggua's face lit up.
[ZH] 青瓜对着门向外喊道：“红豆，你当我媳妇儿好不好？”
[EN] "...or not like you..."
[ZH] 外面没有传来任何回应，身后却传来自家公子的声音：“要脸不要脸？人家红豆明显拒绝你，还觍着脸说些没脸没皮的话，我都替你臊得慌！”
[EN] His heart dropped.
[ZH] 青瓜转过身：“公子，您能不能别神出鬼没的？背后突然来那么一句，搞得人家心慌。”
[EN] "...truth is, I don't know."
[ZH] “怎么着？还敢埋怨本公子？不想混了？”
[EN] Disappointed yet undeterred, Qinggua trailed her to the door. "I'm a really good guy! Spend more time with me and you'll see—marry me and I'll spoil my wife rotten!"
[ZH] 被关着，生气又没用，陈浩宇闲来无聊，想与青瓜聊天儿消磨时光。
[EN] Hongdou laughed. "Since you're such a catch, I'll spread the word around Moxiang Courtyard—and all of Chen Manor if need be. Guaranteed we find you a bride!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇在桌边坐好：“你过来，坐下，跟我说说话。”
[EN] Before Qinggua could blink, the door opened and clicked padlocked from outside.
[ZH] 青瓜受宠若惊，赶快坐到桌旁：“公子想跟我聊什么？”
[EN] Qinggua called through the wood, "Hongdou! Will you be my wife?!"
[ZH] “你这么追红豆根本没用。”
[EN] No reply came from outside—only a mocking voice right behind him. "Have you no pride? Hongdou just shot you down cold and you're still howling shameless nonsense through the door! Even I'm embarrassed for you!"
[ZH] “怎么追？公子教我。”
[EN] Qinggua spun around. "Can you stop creeping up behind me like a ghost, Young Master? Nearly stopped my heart!"
[ZH] “你知道本公子为何那么受姑娘欢迎吗？”
[EN] "Daring to complain at me now? Sick of living?" Bored stiff in lockup and realizing anger bought him nothing, Chen Haoyu dropped onto a chair. "Come sit and chat."
[ZH] 公子的问题，把青瓜给难住，他仔细搜索记忆：“公子，我从来没见过有姑娘喜欢过您，只知您总是在乡下抓些黄花大姑娘放在身边当丫鬟，墨香苑之前的那些丫鬟都是这么来的，听说陈家其他宅子也有……”
[EN] Flattered, Qinggua hopped onto a stool. "What do you want to talk about, Young Master?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇攥紧拳头：“再胡说，小心我的拳头。”
[EN] "Chasing Hongdou like that won't work in a million years."
[ZH] 青瓜躲开：“我没胡说，事实如此。”
[EN] "Teach me how, Young Master!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇放下拳头：“本公子以前是荒唐些，这大半年来，再没做过这种事情，难道不允许本公子改好？”
[EN] "Know why girls always fall over themselves for me?"
[ZH] “仔细想来，的确如此，公子从去年开始老往春香楼跑，看来春香楼的姑娘更好。公子，您在春香楼是不是有相好的？”
[EN] Stumped, Qinggua ransacked his memory. "Young Master, I've never once seen a girl actually like you! All I ever saw was you grabbing village maidens to serve in your courtyard—that's where every maid in Moxiang Courtyard came from before, plus other Chen properties..."
[ZH] 陈浩宇想起春金沫：“算是吧。说你呢？怎么说起我来？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu raised a fist. "Keep blabbing and eat knuckles!"
[ZH] 青瓜委屈道：“是公子问我您为何那么受姑娘欢迎，才扯到这些话题，依我看公子是因有钱才受姑娘欢迎。”
[EN] Qinggua leaned clear. "Not blabbing—just facts!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇认可道：“你总算说对，所以啊，你得让自己有钱，你有了钱，不用追，红豆自然倒贴你。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu lowered his fist. "Fine, I acted wild back then, yet I haven't touched a village girl in over half a year. Aren't I allowed to improve?"
[ZH] 青瓜并不认可：“公子说得不对，您是有钱，少夫人也看不上您，要不怎么狠心把您关起来？再说看上您钱的人，对您不一定是真心。别说我没钱，即使有钱，我也要装作没钱，这样红豆喜欢我才是真心。”
[EN] "True enough—since last year you switched to haunting Chunxiang Brothel. Guess brothel girls suit you better. Do you have a favorite there, Young Master?"
[ZH] 青瓜一番话说得陈浩宇心里很不是滋味，也许正因为清楚无论是抢来的黄花大姑娘，还是青楼女子，都是因为钱财屈服于他，陈浩宇才至今没有真正喜欢上一人，
[EN] Chun Jinmo crossed Chen Haoyu's mind. "Sort of. Wait—we were talking about you! How'd it turn onto me?"
[ZH] 以金钱为条件时，的确没有多少趣味，只是生活太无趣，他也只能从金钱的享受中获得些乐趣而已。
[EN] "You asked why girls like you!" Qinggua protested. "If you ask me, they only flock to you for your silver."
[ZH] 见公子不说话，青瓜很担心：“我该不会又说错话？公子生气了？”
[EN] "Bingo! You nailed it!" Chen Haoyu snapped his fingers. "That's why you need silver. Get rich, and you won't have to chase Hongdou—she'll throw herself at you!"
[ZH] 公子竟然平静地说：“看来，你比我懂感情，我还是不给你出主意，你怎么想就怎么追求红豆吧。但我能看得出，红豆对你没意思，还是不要做无谓的努力。”
[EN] Qinggua shook his head. "Wrong, Young Master! You're loaded, yet Young Madam doesn't want you—even locked you up! Anyone chasing silver isn't sincere. Even if I had silver, I'd pretend to be broke so I'd know Hongdou likes me for real."
[ZH] “谢谢公子提醒，我还没正式追求过红豆，今日只是热热身，等我们出去，我会使出浑身解数，相信红豆迟早是我的。”青瓜脸上露出自信的表情。
[EN] Those words left a bitter ache in Chen Haoyu's chest. Maybe that was why—knowing every coerced village girl and every smiling courtesan bent only for his silver—he had never truly fallen for anyone. Bought affection held zero real joy; bored with life, he had merely numbed himself with money.
[ZH] 陈浩宇不好再打击青瓜，还是想想怎么出去。
[EN] Seeing his master fall quiet, Qinggua winced. "Put my foot in it again? Mad at me, Young Master?"
[ZH] 天都黑了，奶奶和爹娘到现在还未出现，估计是朱淑真掩饰得好，搞得家人还以为自己没有回到陈府。
[EN] Instead, Chen Haoyu answered calm and low. "Looks like you know more about the heart than I do. Forget my advice—chase Hongdou your own way. Still, take it from me: she's not into you. Don't waste your breath."
[ZH] 可陈浩宇记得他被朱淑真五花大绑带进陈府时，明明有丫鬟看到，那丫鬟还是奶奶院子里的，奶奶应该已知道自己被绑之事，既知道为何不来解救自己？
[EN] "Thanks for the warning, Young Master, yet I haven't even tried for real yet—today was just a warm-up! Once we get out, I'll pull out all the stops. Sooner or later, Hongdou will be mine!" Qinggua declared with glowing confidence.
[ZH] 陈浩宇十分疑惑：“青瓜，你说奶奶为何不来救我？”
[EN] Not having the heart to burst his bubble further, Chen Haoyu turned back to escape. Night had fallen, yet neither Grandmother nor his parents had shown up. Could Zhu Shuzhen have kept him hidden? Impossible—he had spotted a maid from Old Madam Xia's own courtyard staring when he was dragged through the gates in ropes! Why hadn't Grandmother come storming to his rescue?
[ZH] “可能老太太不知道您被少夫人关起来。”
[EN] "Qinggua," he frowned, "why hasn't Grandmother come to spring me?"
[ZH] “朱淑真抓我进来时，奶奶院中的丫鬟明明看到，肯定会告诉奶奶，按照以往的经验奶奶很快会来。最近家里有没有发生什么？”
[EN] "Maybe Old Madam doesn't know you're locked up?"
[ZH] 青瓜仔细想想说：“还真发生过大事。”
[EN] "One of her own maids saw Zhu Shuzhen march me in tied up! Normally Grandmother would've flown over here in a heartbeat. Did something big happen while I was away?"
[ZH] “什么大事？”
[EN] Qinggua thought back. "Actually, yes—huge stuff!"
[ZH] “您不在这段时日，少夫人天天将说话艺人请到家里给老太太说书，哄得老太太笑得合不拢嘴。今儿还请了一位唱鼓词的老人，听说老太太更是高兴，饭都多吃了几碗。还有最厉害的……”
[EN] "Like what?"
[ZH] 这么说来朱淑真已将老太太哄好，陈浩宇不由紧张道：“最厉害的？是什么？”
[EN] "While you were gone, Young Madam hired a storyteller to come every single day and tell stories to Old Madam Xia—had her laughing nonstop! Today she even brought an old blind ballad singer, and Old Madam Xia loved it so much she ate extra bowls of rice! And the biggest thing of all—"
[ZH] “少夫人在中间调和，老太太跟大老爷和大夫人关系缓和，就连二老爷、二夫人、三老爷、三夫人也每日往老太太房中跑。我听老太太院子里的丫鬟说，现在老太太的院子每日热热闹闹，老太太直夸二少夫人好，连大老爷和夫人也夸儿媳妇好。”
[EN] Unease prickled up Chen Haoyu's spine. "What's the biggest thing?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇用拳头砸在桌上：“奸诈！实在奸诈！趁我不在，收买我的家人，难怪我被关到现在也没人理我。难道有了好孙媳好儿媳，奶奶不再宠我？我娘也不再管我？”
[EN] "Young Madam smoothed things over between Old Madam Xia and Eldest Master and Madam! Now Second Master, Third Master, and their wives visit Old Madam Xia's courtyard every single day too! The maids say her courtyard is packed with laughter daily, and Old Madam Xia praises Second Young Madam to the skies—Eldest Master and Madam too!"
[ZH] 青瓜在一旁偷笑起来。
[EN] Chen Haoyu slammed his fist down onto the table. "Sneaky! Pure sneaky! Bought off my whole family the second my back was turned! No wonder nobody came for me! One clever bride walks in and suddenly Grandmother stops spoiling me and Mother forgets I exist?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇没好气地问：“本公子生气，你却偷笑，是不是想找打？”
[EN] Qinggua stifled a snicker.
[ZH] 青瓜赶紧起来，闪到一边。
[EN] "I'm fuming and you're laughing?" Chen Haoyu glared. "Asking for a beating?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇反而被逗乐：“行了，我是吓唬你，赶紧坐过来，本公子无聊，陪我解闷儿。”
[EN] Qinggua jumped clear, making Chen Haoyu chuckle despite himself. "Relax, just messing with you. Sit back down and keep me from dying of boredom."
[ZH] 青瓜放心坐回原位，小心说：“公子别生气，您刚才破天荒地夸少夫人是好孙媳好儿媳，我才被逗乐。”
[EN] Settling back on his stool, Qinggua grinned. "Didn't mean to laugh, Young Master—you just called Young Madam a 'clever bride' for the first time ever!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不以为然：“会不会听？那是夸吗？闲话少说，得想想办法怎么出去。我倒有个主意，借你的姿色一用，勾引一下朱淑真身边的丫鬟，想办法将门打开，把我悄悄放出去。苦荞和红豆就算了，还有两个丫鬟，姿色平平，估计你一勾引就上钩。”
[EN] "Learn to listen! Was that a compliment? Quit stalling and help me get out of here. Wait—I've got a plan! Put your looks to work and seduce one of Zhu Shuzhen's maids into unlocking the door so I can slip out! Forget Kuqiao and Hongdou—she's got two other maids with plain looks who'll fall hook, line, and sinker!"
[ZH] 青瓜为难道：“我的姿色与公子比起来差远了，再说那两个丫鬟我实在不感兴趣，一点儿勾引的想法都没有，况且少夫人的丫鬟都不好惹。”
[EN] Qinggua pulled a sour face. "My looks don't hold a candle to yours, Young Master. Besides, I have zero interest in those two maids—and every maid under Young Madam is a tough cookie!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不满道：“什么意思？难道想让本公子亲自出马？”
[EN] "What, expecting me to step up and seduce them myself?"
[ZH] 青瓜小心说：“公子出马，绝对能成。”
[EN] "With your charm, Young Master, guaranteed success!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇伸出手掌：“小心我削你！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu raised a palm. "Want a smack?"
[ZH] 青瓜将脸闪到一边：“嘿嘿，公子，我姿色平平。”
[EN] Qinggua ducked with a sheepish grin. "Hehe... my looks are really nothing special."
[ZH] 陈浩宇收回手：“你要是愿意献出一点儿你的色相，本公子答应，等出去后，想办法帮你搞定红豆。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu lowered his hand. "Sacrifice a sliver of your charm for me, and once we're out, I swear I'll help you win Hongdou over!"
[ZH] 青瓜兴奋道：“真的？公子别骗我！”
[EN] Qinggua's eyes lit up like lanterns. "Really? Don't lie to me, Young Master!"
[ZH] “君子一言驷马难追！”
[EN] "A gentleman's word stands firm as four galloping horses!"
[ZH] “好，那我就勉为其难答应公子。”
[EN] "Deal! I'll bite the bullet and do it for you!"
[ZH] “这就对了！”
[EN] "Now you're talking!"

#CH 227
[ZH] 将陈浩宇从春香楼五花大绑带回来，千里寻根本不怕被陈家长辈批评。她还盼着他们前来寻事，正好趁此机会质问一番，为何明明知道陈浩宇散播谣言坏她名声，还要同意这门亲事？
[ZH] 天都黑了，陈家长辈连半个人影都没见着。千里寻记得老太太房中的丫鬟明明看到，她老人家溺爱孙子，应该第一个来。一切出乎意料，千里寻琢磨不出其中缘由，便不去琢磨。
[ZH] 已将讨厌之人关起来，愤恨之情没有白天时那么强烈，少有的悲情反而袭上心头。搞得她无心写话本，坐在书桌前回想流言蜚语对她的生活造成的伤害。那段时日是她人生中最黑暗的时期，不仅流下许多泪水，还觉得人生已毫无希望。
[ZH] 一切是陈浩宇一手造成，她却与他成亲。幸好没与他圆房，否则后悔都来不及。
[ZH] 想起那段黑暗的日子，千里寻自然想到家人，想到梦容，想到申简辰。
[ZH] 家人的亲情带给她温暖，才让她有力气走出黑暗的日子。虽说朱晞颜没有去过望月轩，可若没他允诺，娘和哥哥嫂嫂也不敢随便踏出朱府。父亲表面上严厉，还是在用他认为正确的方式爱护她，与陈家结亲便是他认为的正确方式之一。
[ZH] 本想找朱晞颜闹一闹，被韦嬷嬷阻止，现在千里寻的心中也没了去闹的想法。闹有何用？难道能让她回到成亲之前？显然已不可能。
[ZH] 那段黑暗的时日，梦容来过望月轩两三回。别看次数不多，却让她感受到她没有被这个世界抛弃，还是有人愿意靠近她，愿意与她一起坐在火盆旁说心里话。也是从那时起，在心里悄悄告诉自己，她会一辈子将梦容视作闺蜜。
[ZH] 后来知道仪王就是申简辰，千里寻也终于明白为何那时的仪王对她很不一般。又是送礼物，又是送美食，梦容陪她聊天时，他则在望月轩外的马车里等候，这份情谊只怕这一世无法偿还。
[ZH] 因为梦容，她果断拒绝仪王，也因为梦容，她无法接受申简辰。但她不得不承认，在她被全城人非议的日子里，那时的仪王流露出的情感，无形中让她充满自信。要知道人家可是位王爷，竟然瞧上被人骂得狗血喷头的落难女子，还真有些英雄救美的意思。
[ZH] “苦荞，把去年在望月轩时仪王送的礼物找来。”
[ZH] 苦荞想了半晌实在想不出是什么礼物：“小姐，我不记得仪王送过什么礼物。”
[ZH] “一个撒尿娃娃。”
[ZH] 苦荞猛然想起：“哦，想起来，还真送过一个，是个光屁股的娃娃，小姐要它做什么？”
[ZH] “别问那么多，快去找来。”
[ZH] 苦荞回想着，到底将那娃娃放在哪里？仪王送时，小姐只看那么一回就让她收起来，从此再没拿出过。小姐嫁到陈家后，是搬过来一些东西，也不知撒尿娃娃还在不在？
[EN] Dragging Chen Haoyu back from Chunxiang Brothel in ropes, Qianli Xun had not feared criticism from the Chen elders—in fact, she had welcomed a confrontation so she could demand why they had pushed this marriage knowing full well he had smeared her name across Lin'an.
[ZH] “我去杂货间试着找一找，一般小姐不大关注的东西我都会放那里。”
[EN] Yet night fell without a single elder darkening her doorway. She had seen a maid from Old Madam Xia's courtyard watch the whole procession; with how the old woman doted on her grandson, she should have charged over first. Puzzled yet unwilling to overthink it, Qianli Xun let it go.
[ZH] 苦荞走出素心草堂，去寻找仪王送的礼物。
[EN] With the culprit locked away, her hot fury ebbed, replaced by a rare wave of raw grief. Unable to focus on her huaben, she sat at her desk remembering how those rumors had torn her life apart—her darkest months, drowned in tears and bleak despair.
[ZH] 千里寻百无聊赖，一腔愁情，不由展纸提笔写下：
[EN] All of it Chen Haoyu's doing—and she had married him. Thank goodness they had never consummated the marriage, or she would have regretted it to her dying day.
[ZH] 春来春去几经过，不似今年恨最多。
[EN] Thinking back to that bleak winter brought up images of her family, Mengrong, and Shen Jianchen. Family warmth had kept her standing; even though Zhu Xiyan had never visited Wangyue Pavilion, her mother, brother, and sister-in-law could never have come without his nod. Stern as her father was, he protected her in his own rigid way—matching her with the Chens included. Matron Wei had been right to stop her from storming Zhu Manor; raising a fuss now could not rewind the clock.
[ZH] 寂寂海棠枝上月，照人清夜欲如何。
[EN] During those dark months, Mengrong had come to Wangyue Pavilion two or three times—few visits, yet enough to prove the world had not abandoned her, someone willing to sit beside the brazier and share heart-to-hearts. That was when Qianli Xun had vowed to treat Mengrong as her lifelong confidante.
[ZH] 《春日杂书十首·其一》
[EN] And once she learned Prince Yi was Shen Jianchen, everything else snapped into focus: the gifts, the treats, waiting outside Wangyue Pavilion in his carriage while Mengrong visited—kindness she could never repay in one lifetime. For Mengrong's sake she had turned him down cold, yet she could not deny that having an imperial prince see her worth while an entire city spat on her had restored her backbone. Real white-knight stuff.
[ZH] 刚写完，苦荞手中捧只盒子进来，她把盒子给小姐放在桌旁：“我翻了好一阵儿，才翻出来。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, bring out that gift Prince Yi sent to Wangyue Pavilion last year."
[ZH] 小姐放下笔，苦荞过去将写有诗句的纸张拿起来，用嘴吹吹还未干的墨迹：“我把它收起来，等南双或是化雨来时，正好送给他们。”
[EN] Kuqiao racked her brains. "Miss, I don't recall Prince Yi sending a gift."
[ZH] 墨迹还未完全干，诗句中的一个“恨”字引起苦荞注意：“小姐，您是不是恨姑爷？”
[EN] "The peeing clay boy."
[ZH] 千里寻把桌上的盒子挪到面前，打开，拿出光屁股的撒尿娃娃，脸上终于露出笑容，只因娃娃实在可爱，让人看后不由不笑：“恨，当然恨，他害我好惨。”
[EN] Light dawned. "Oh, right! That bare-bottomed clay figurine! What do you want it for, Miss?"
[ZH] 苦荞继续向未干的墨迹吹一口气，见小姐边笑边说，放心许多。她并不是担心姑爷，而是担心自家小姐带着恨度过第二次的婚姻生活。
[EN] "Stop asking and go fetch it."
[ZH] “小姐笑了，我就高兴。”
[EN] Kuqiao frowned trying to remember where she had tucked it—her mistress had looked once and told her to put it away, never asking for it again. "I'll check the storage room; that's where I stash things you rarely look at." She hurried out.
[ZH] 墨迹已干，苦荞将写有诗的纸张收好。
[EN] Left alone with melancholy pressing on her chest, Qianli Xun spread a sheet of paper and wrote:
[ZH] 再看小姐时，她依然将目光留在泥人娃娃的身上。
[EN] "How many springs have come and gone, / Yet never a year with resentment so deep as this. / On quiet crabapple branches shines the moon— / How shall I endure this clear, lone night?" —"Ten Spring Reflections, No. 1" [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 苦荞想起白风送她的泥人：“也不知白风和仪王什么时候回临安？”
[EN] As she lifted her brush, Kuqiao returned holding a small wooden box and set it beside her. "Had to dig for ages to find it."
[ZH] 这话正好问到千里寻的心里，她也希望申简辰快些回来，可又不知他回来后她该如何面对梦容：“我倒希望他们留在汴梁，永远别回来。”
[EN] Setting her brush aside, Qianli Xun watched Kuqiao lift the poem sheet and blow across the wet ink. "I'll save this for the next time Nan Shuang or Huayu comes asking."
[ZH] 苦荞理解小姐心中所想，还是换个话题，省得想起抢亲之事，惹小姐心烦：“小姐，您真的打算以后一直把姑爷关着？”
[EN] Catching the word "resentment" in the damp strokes, Kuqiao paused. "Miss, do you hate Young Master?"
[ZH] 千里寻将撒尿娃娃放进盒子盖好：“先关着吧，至于关多久，还没想好。”
[EN] Qianli Xun slid the box closer, lifted the lid, and took out the bare-bottomed clay figurine. A genuine smile broke across her face—the mischievous little doll made it impossible not to laugh. "Hate him? Of course I hate him. He put me through hell."
[ZH] 苦荞竟然抽泣起来……
[EN] Blowing once more on the ink, Kuqiao relaxed at seeing her mistress smile. She did not worry for the groom; she worried for her mistress spending a second marriage poisoned by hatred. "So long as you smile, Miss, I'm happy."
[ZH] 千里寻扭过头，见苦荞面带泪痕：“好好的，怎么哭了？”
[EN] Once dry, Kuqiao tucked the poem away and noticed her mistress still gazing down at the clay doll. Remembering the figurine Bai Feng had bought her, Kuqiao murmured, "Wonder when Bai Feng and Prince Yi are coming back to Lin'an."
[ZH] 苦荞边哭边说：“我为小姐难过，之前的婚姻本就不幸，好不容易和离，又嫁给散播您谣言之人，小姐以后的日子该怎么过？呜呜……！”
[EN] That echoed Qianli Xun's own tangled wish—wanting Shen Jianchen back, yet dreading facing Mengrong. "Honestly, I hope they stay in Bianliang and never come back."
[ZH] 被苦荞这么一说，千里寻也难过起来，她没有像苦荞一般啼哭流泪，而是失落地说：“我要让陈浩宇付出代价。”
[EN] Understanding her heart, Kuqiao steered away from reminders of the wedding raid. "Miss, do you really plan to keep Young Master locked up forever?"
[ZH] 苦荞继续哭：“他现在是姑爷，是您的夫君，在外人的眼中跟您是一家人，一荣俱荣，一损俱损，姑爷若付出什么代价，只怕小姐也避免不了，我都替小姐感到为难，呜呜……！”
[EN] Setting the doll back inside its box and closing the lid, Qianli Xun said, "Locked up for now. Haven't decided for how long."
[ZH] “别哭了，哭得我心烦。”千里寻把盒子递给苦荞，“把盒子放在架子上，方便我随时拿来瞧。”
[EN] Suddenly Kuqiao began to sniffle.
[ZH] 苦荞擦擦眼泪，不敢再继续哭，怕惹得小姐难过。她接过盒子，在一旁的架子上找个地方放好。
[EN] Qianli Xun turned. "Why on earth are you crying?"
[ZH] “你去休息吧，不必伺候我，我想一个人静一静。”
[EN] "I feel so awful for you, Miss!" Kuqiao sobbed. "Your first marriage was misery, you fought so hard for a mutual divorce, and now you're wed to the man who spread slander about you! How are you supposed to live like this? Boo-hoo!"
[ZH] 小姐吩咐，苦荞还是要听的，便回悦宁居休息。
[EN] Her maid's tears tugged at Qianli Xun's own heart. Without weeping, she answered grimly, "I'm going to make Chen Haoyu pay."
[ZH] 屋子里太闷，千里寻想出去透透气。
[EN] "He's your husband now, Miss!" Kuqiao wept on. "In outsiders' eyes you're one household—rising together, sinking together! If he pays a steep price, you'll get dragged down with him! I hate seeing you trapped like this!"
[ZH] 她起身来到院中，走到池塘边，水面刚长出荷叶，不大，铺展开来。月光下，池塘里只有两种色彩，除了水面映出月光的皎洁外，其他都是黑色。
[EN] "Quit crying, you're giving me a headache." Handing her the wooden box, Qianli Xun said, "Put this on the shelf where I can reach it whenever I want."
[ZH] 刚刚在屋里苦荞说的“一荣俱荣，一损俱损”的道理，千里寻岂能不知？尽管她不喜欢陈浩宇，尽管姻缘是长辈牵下，嫁过来却是她自己的选择。
[EN] Wiping her tears lest she upset her mistress further, Kuqiao placed the box on the shelf.
[ZH] 无论什么原因嫁过来，她只想将这个家当成人生的最后一站，想在这里过下去。累了也罢，屈服命运也罢，还是为还梦容一个好哥哥，她从未想过第二次和离。
[EN] "Go get some rest. I want to sit alone for a while."
[ZH] 陈浩宇今日大喊要休了她，千里寻并非害怕被人所休，也并非害怕再和离一次，而是她从未想过，突然被人提及，心中难免不适。
[EN] Obeying, Kuqiao retreated to Yuening Room.
[ZH] 该如何做？既可泄心头之恨，又能全她人生最后一站的想法。
[EN] Finding the room stifling, Qianli Xun stepped outside to the edge of the lotus pond. Small green pads floated over dark water, bathed in moonlight—pure silver reflection against deep shadow.
[ZH] 千里寻想到奶奶、公爹和婆婆让她管教陈浩宇之事，轻松中带着一丝冷笑：“那我就借着管教的理由认真报仇，只盼你一辈子都别改，我好管教你一辈子。若你改好，我就成陈家的功臣，在这家中的地位必然屹立不倒。”
[EN] Kuqiao's warning about rising and sinking together rang true. Even though she did not love Chen Haoyu and the elders had forced the match, walking into that wedding had been her own choice. Whether out of exhaustion, bowing to fate, or giving Mengrong back her brother, she meant this manor to be her final stop and had never considered a second divorce. Hearing Chen Haoyu howl about repudiating her today had not scared her, yet it had stung.
[ZH] 见惯陈浩宇吊儿郎当的样子，很难想象他变好后会是个什么样？像文渊那样？还是像申简辰？
[EN] How could she vent her grudge while still building a stable life here?
[ZH] 千里寻被自己的想象逗乐，事情想通，心情好一些。不如明日主动找长辈说明管教的想法，给他们吃颗定心丸，也好避免他们无端插手，影响自己报仇。不，应该是“管教”。
[EN] A sharp idea flashed: Old Madam Xia and her in-laws had begged her to discipline Chen Haoyu. A dry smile curved her lips. "Fine—I'll take my revenge under the banner of 'disciplining' him! Pray you never change, Chen Haoyu, so I get to discipline you for life! And if you actually reform, I become the Chen family's hero with an unshakable seat in this house."
[ZH] 悦宁居内，苦荞通过窗户看到小姐的身影，叹口气，把窗户关上，对红豆说：“我真替小姐担心，怕她再和离一次。”
[EN] Picturing scatterbrained Chen Haoyu turning refined like Wenyuan or steady like Shen Jianchen made her laugh out loud. With her mind clear, her spirits rose. Tomorrow she would call on the elders upfront and lay out her plan to "discipline" him, reassuring them so nobody meddled in her payback—sorry, her education program.
[ZH] 悦宁居有两张丫鬟床，红豆已躺下，还未睡着：“苦荞姐姐放心，小姐在陈家跟长辈处得相当和谐，陈家人舍不得小姐。”
[EN] From the window of Yuening Room, Kuqiao watched her mistress's silhouette by the pond, sighed, shut the casement, and turned to Hongdou. "I'm terrified Miss will end up going through another divorce."
[ZH] 苦荞走到红豆床前，在床边坐下：“姑爷散播谣言，小姐绝不会饶他，肯定会折磨他。姑爷可是老太太的心头肉，大夫人也心疼自己的儿子，怎么可能由着小姐？他们再喜欢小姐，亲疏关系还是分得清的。”
[EN] Lying awake on one of the two maids' beds, Hongdou replied, "Stop stressing, Sister Kuqiao. Miss gets along great with the Chen elders—they'd never let her go."
[ZH] 红豆翻了个身：“别瞎操心，韦嬷嬷都不担心，你担心什么劲儿？”
[EN] Kuqiao sat on the edge of Hongdou's bed. "After finding out Young Master spread those rumors, Miss won't go easy on him. He's Old Madam Xia's precious darling and Eldest Madam's own son—how long will they sit back and watch? Fond as they are of Miss, blood runs thicker than water."
[ZH] 苦荞一想，也对，韦嬷嬷是何等人，从姑爷被带回来，只见她出去过一趟，再没见到人，好像此事与她无关似的。于是放松心情：“你给姑爷送晚饭时，他有没有继续骂小姐？”
[EN] Hongdou rolled over. "Quit borrowing trouble. Matron Wei isn't worried one bit, so why are you?"
[ZH] 红豆转过身：“怎么能少？我真怀疑姑爷跟小姐是前世的冤家，这一世被硬生生凑到一起，也不知这对儿冤家这一世谁能斗过谁？”
[EN] True enough—since Young Master had been marched home, Matron Wei had stepped out once and stayed totally unruffled. Kuqiao relaxed. "When you delivered dinner, was Young Master still cursing Miss?"
[ZH] “还用问？肯定是小姐能斗得过姑爷，斗得他落花流水！霹雳啪嚓！”苦荞已忘记刚才的担忧，得意地说。
[EN] Hongdou turned back. "Nonstop! Swear they must've been bitter rivals in a past life forced under one roof in this one. Wonder who'll win?"
[ZH] 红豆倒担忧起来：“你说他们是夫妻，斗来斗去有意思吗？我希望小姐好，也希望有个好姑爷。你说大老爷和大夫人人那么好，姑爷又是他们生的，怎么就不像大老爷？如果姑爷对小姐也像大老爷对大夫人那样，想着都替小姐高兴。”
[EN] "Need you ask? Miss will wipe the floor with him!" Kuqiao crowed, all worry forgotten.
[ZH] 苦荞打了一个哈欠，困意袭来，走到桌前熄灭蜡烛，脱掉外衣，躺在床上才说道：“我们担心也没用，只能希望姑爷快些变好，小姐也能有好日子过。”
[EN] Hongdou frowned softly. "Still, they're husband and wife—what's the fun in fighting forever? I want Miss happy with a decent husband. Eldest Master and Eldest Madam are such wonderful people—how didn't Young Master take after his father? If he ever treated Miss the way Eldest Master treats Eldest Madam, I'd be over the moon for her."
[ZH] 晚上，陈江林从茶庄回来，姚轻琰将白天发生的事情一五一十说了。
[EN] Stifling a yawn, Kuqiao snuffed the candle, slipped out of her outer jacket, and climbed into bed. "Worrying won't fix him. All we can do is hope Young Master turns over a new leaf soon so Miss can have peace."
[ZH] 陈江林高兴道：“就应该把他关起来，我早想这么做，碍于你跟老太太，才没敢下狠手。”
[EN] Late that night, when Chen Jianglin returned from the tea house, Yao Qingyan recounted the whole day's drama.
[ZH] 姚轻琰一边给陈江林褪去外衣，一边说：“刚听说时，我跟老太太心疼不已，还好韦嬷嬷前来，宽慰我们一番，还说淑真已经知道浩宇散播谣言之事，也是因为此事才把他关起来。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin grinned. "Locking him up is just what he needed! I wanted to do it years ago—only held back because of you and Mother."
[ZH] 外衣脱去，陈江林坐在床边：“唉，纸包不住火，淑真迟早会知道，现在知道也好，那小子做下此等错事，把他关起来都是轻的，要我说就得拿鞭子好好抽打抽打。”
[EN] Helping him slip off his outer robe, Yao Qingyan said, "When I first heard, Mother and I both ached for him. Thank goodness Matron Wei came over to reassure us—turns out Shuzhen found out Haoyu spread those rumors last year, and that's why she locked him up."
[ZH] 姚轻琰准备帮陈江林脱掉鞋子。
[EN] Sitting on the edge of the bed, Chen Jianglin sighed. "Fire never stays wrapped in paper. Just as well she knows now. After pulling a stunt that foul, locking him up is mercy—he deserves a good horsewhipping."
[ZH] 陈江林说：“这种事情我来，夫人赶紧上床歇息。”
[EN] When Yao Qingyan bent to remove his boots, he waved her off. "I'll handle my own boots. Get into bed and rest."
[ZH] 姚轻琰便到妆台前去掉发饰，也将外衣脱掉，熄了灯，躺到床上：“比起鞭子来，我更怕她的拳头，你说淑真会不会把浩宇打出残疾来？”
[EN] Taking off her hairpins at the dressing table, Yao Qingyan shed her outer robe, snuffed the lamp, and slipped under the covers. "I'm less worried about whips than her fists! What if Shuzhen breaks his bones?"
[ZH] “把心放在肚子里，淑真的拳头虽厉害，自嫁过来，何曾见过她用拳头？相反，知礼、懂事，还有心，她嫁进来没几日，府中的气象大变。”
[EN] "Put your mind at rest. Tough as her fists are, has she thrown one punch since marrying in? Quite the opposite—courteous, sharp, and warmhearted. Barely days under our roof and the whole atmosphere of the manor brightened."
[ZH] “这么说来她还给陈家带来福气？”
[EN] "Sounds like she brought good luck into Chen Manor."
[ZH] “还用说？我现在倒觉得她跟林觉和离，跟乔家的事没成，都是因为老天把她给我们陈家留着，呵呵……！”
[EN] "No question! Now I'm convinced her divorce from Lin Jue and the broken Qiao match were Heaven saving her for our Chen household! Hahaha!"
[ZH] “你这么说我不认同，朱陈两家之所以亲上加亲，还不是因为浩宇散播流言一事。”
[EN] "Hardly Heaven—our two families only sealed this match because Haoyu spread those rumors."
[ZH] “所以说，冥冥中注定浩宇要给自己挣回个好娘子，呵呵……！”
[EN] "Which proves fate forced Haoyu to earn himself a fine wife! Hahaha!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰不完全认同夫君所言，还是被对方说得高兴起来，她在心中祝福淑真与浩宇的关系能慢慢好起来。
[EN] Half amused despite disagreeing, Yao Qingyan smiled in the dark, silently wishing for her son and daughter-in-law to find their way to peace.

#CH 228
[ZH] 真是一家忧愁一家笑，陈江树的屋里，一家三口笑得甚是开心。
[ZH] 陈浩东说：“呵呵……！浩宇被关起来，以后恐怕没机会自由自在到处逛荡，儿子终于舒心，凭什么我忙着给家里赚银子，他一点儿功劳没有，花钱却如流水。这下可好，母老虎起到大作用。”
[ZH] 陈江树说：“是令人高兴，我疑虑的是老太太和大嫂竟然纹丝不动，难道她们还不知道此事？”
[ZH] 包氏说：“怎能不知？我在老太太屋里时，丫鬟前来报告，你没见老太太和大嫂那脸色。”
[ZH] 陈江树继续疑虑着：“可他们为何不前去阻止？太不像往日的作风。”
[ZH] 包氏说：“我猜想是被朱家姑娘的拳头给吓住，不敢前去阻拦。”
[ZH] 陈江树并不认可：“老太太是见过大世面的人，能被小辈吓住？再说上次你与三弟妹鼓动老太太前去闹事，她还不是去了？也没见被拳头吓住。”
[ZH] 包氏说：“被你这么一说，我倒猜不出原因。”
[ZH] 陈浩东说：“儿子猜测，应该是因为朱家姑娘刚嫁过来，缓和了陈家长辈与老太太的关系，有所贡献，为了给她些面子，奶奶和大婶婶才忍着没去阻止。在儿子看来，不阻止才好，否则朱家姑娘因忌讳长辈把浩宇给放了，我的计划不就落空？”
[ZH] 陈江树认可儿子的说法：“有些道理，怕只怕今日不管，明日会前去阻止。”
[ZH] 包氏说：“我看未必阻止得了。朱家姑娘不仅拳头厉害，嘴皮子也厉害。上次跟着老太太前去寻事，明明是她的错，硬生生地找出许多理由，把错处说得让人觉不出错来，还认为她说得有道理，真是个天不怕地不怕的性子。现在知道浩宇是散播谣言之人，凭这股子恨，估计老太太和大嫂前去也未必阻止得了。”
[ZH] 父子两个被崔氏的一番言语给逗乐，都“呵呵”笑起来。
[ZH] 笑够后，陈江林夸道：“夫人此话说得在理，以后我绝不会认为你头发长见识短。”
[ZH] 陈浩东也夸道：“娘是大家闺秀出身，读过不少书，自然分析事情透彻入微。”
[ZH] 被两个对自己来讲最重要的男人夸，包氏打心里欢喜。
[ZH] 天色已晚，爹娘要休息，聊得差不多时，陈浩东拜别爹娘回到自己的院子。今日心情好，自然而然生起情 欲来，这个点儿懒得去找春金蝉。
[ZH] 抬头瞅到叶安雅的屋子，灯已灭，她应该睡下。作为自己的娘子，不等他便睡下，陈浩东心生不满。
[EN] While one wing fretted, inside Chen Jiangshu's quarters, his family of three laughed up a storm.
[ZH] 他上前敲门：“咚咚咚！”
[EN] "Hahaha! Locked up tight!" Chen Haodong crowed. "No more roaming wild for Haoyu! Feels so good—why should I slave away earning silver for this house while he does nothing and spends like water? That tigress came through big-time!"
[ZH] 叶安雅刚躺下，还未睡着，小翠已回自己的房间休息，她懒得起来，再说已猜到是陈浩东，更不愿理会，用被子蒙住头。
[EN] "Satisfying, yes," Chen Jiangshu mused, "yet it puzzles me why Old Madam Xia and First Sister-in-law haven't budged an inch. Haven't they heard?"
[ZH] 陈浩东继续敲门：“咚咚咚！”里面依旧没动静。陈浩东气不打一处来，一脚上去将门踹开，气呼呼地拐过屏风，抹黑来到床前，一把拽起叶安雅：“为何不给我开门？”
[EN] "Of course they heard!" Madam Bao said. "I was sitting right there in Old Madam's room when a maid ran in reporting it. You should've seen the looks on their faces!"
[ZH] 叶安雅并不害怕，被打习惯，变得麻木起来：“难道你不知？何必问我？”
[EN] "Then why didn't they charge over to stop her? Nothing like them."
[ZH] 陈浩东一巴掌打在她的脸上：“你是我娘子，本应每日等我回来，伺候我睡下，你却一个人早早睡去，从来不把我放在眼里。但凡表现好一些，我也不会打你。”
[EN] "My guess? They're terrified of the Zhu girl's fists and don't dare step in."
[ZH] 叶安雅冷笑道：“新婚前我就听说你跟春香楼的商妓在一起，婚后我见你就觉得恶心，怎会心甘情愿伺候你？”
[EN] Chen Jiangshu shook his head. "Old Madam Xia has seen real storms in her life—no youngster scares her. Besides, didn't she march right over to make trouble when you and Third Sister-in-law egged her on last time? Wasn't scared of fists then."
[ZH] 陈浩东上前将叶安雅按倒在床上，重重压在她身上。叶安雅倒不反抗，表情却无比冷漠，头扭向一边。
[EN] "Put like that, I'm stumped too," Madam Bao admitted.
[ZH] 黑夜中，借着窗外的月光，陈浩东感受到从叶安雅的表情中阵阵袭来的寒气。
[EN] "I bet I know," Chen Haodong speculated. "The Zhu girl just won big points smoothing over relations between Grandmother and the elders, so Grandmother and First Aunt held back to save her face. Better for us if they don't step in—otherwise she might buckle under their pressure and let Haoyu out, blowing my plan."
[ZH] “我是男人，在外面玩儿几个女人有何不可？你应该学学林觉家的娘子，既温柔懂事，还拿出自己的私房钱给春金巧赎身……”
[EN] "Makes sense," Chen Jiangshu nodded. "Only worry is they hold off today and cave tomorrow."
[ZH] 结果被叶安雅啐一口，唾液打在脸上：“想让我那样，下辈子吧，不，下辈子也不可能！”
[EN] "Even if they try, they won't stop her," Madam Bao scoffed. "The Zhu girl's tongue is just as lethal as her fists. When Grandmother confronted her last time, dead in the wrong, she spun out argument after argument until she sounded spotless and left us speechless! Fearless as they come. Now that she knows Haoyu ruined her name, even Grandmother and First Sister-in-law combined won't talk her down!"
[ZH] 林觉用手擦掉脸上的唾液：“你越是这样，我越想占有你，我今儿高兴，会对你温柔些。”
[EN] Father and son cracked up at Madam Bao's dead-on summary.
[ZH] 叶安雅闭上眼睛，忍受着陈浩东在她身上满足着个人的情 欲。
[EN] When the laughter eased, Chen Jiangshu praised his wife. "Spot on, my dear. Never again will I claim women are shortsighted!"
[ZH] 从新婚那晚，她从来没有快乐过，也不知这种日子何时是个头？何时才能与心爱的人在一起？
[EN] "Mother came from a scholarly family and read plenty of books," Chen Haodong chimed in. "No wonder she cuts straight to the bone."
[ZH] 她想到淑真，既佩服又羡慕，不仅敢于把浩宇从青楼抓回来，还敢把他关起来。不如明日去看看她，与她聊聊。
[EN] Praised by the two men who mattered most to her, Madam Bao glowed with pride.
[ZH] 老三陈江木的屋里，夫妻二人一边收拾上床睡觉，一边聊起白天发生的事。
[EN] With the hour growing late, Chen Haodong bade his parents goodnight and returned to his own courtyard. Riding high on triumph, desire stirred in his gut; too late to head out to Chun Jinchan, he looked up at Ye Anya's darkened room. Asleep without waiting up for her husband—instant irritation flared.
[ZH] 崔氏坐在妆台前去掉发饰：“当初大哥和大嫂不该给浩宇定下这门亲事，你瞧瞧，现在浩宇遭罪了吧？”
[EN] He marched up and rapped hard. Knock, knock, knock!
[ZH] 陈江木坐在椅子上，丫鬟给脚盆中添些热水，他把脚放进水中：“你又不是不知道什么原因？知府大人找上门来，大哥也没办法。婚都结了，只能认命。”
[EN] Awake under the covers after sending Xiaocui off to bed, Ye Anya knew who stood outside and pulled the quilt over her head without stirring.
[ZH] 崔氏已将发饰全部取完，放进首饰盒中，走到夫君身旁，蹲下，双手伸到盆里给夫君洗脚。
[EN] Knock, knock, knock! Still silence. Snapping, Chen Haodong kicked the door wide open, stormed past the screen in the dark, and yanked Ye Anya upright by the shoulder. "Why didn't you open the door?"
[ZH] “我至今都不敢相信去年的谣言是浩宇传播的，你说他没事传谣言干什么？以前也没听说他跟朱家姑娘来往过。”
[EN] Numbed by endless beatings, Ye Anya showed zero fear. "Don't you know? Why ask?"
[ZH] 陈江木闭上眼睛，享受娘子的手在他的脚上摩擦：“浩宇整日不沾家，谁知道他在外面干些什么。”
[EN] Smack! A hard slap cracked across her cheek. "You're my wife—supposed to wait up every night and attend me to bed! Instead you crawl under the covers early without a shred of respect! Behave half decent and I wouldn't lay a finger on you!"
[ZH] 崔氏一边给夫君的脚上撩水一边按摩：“还是我家浩飞懂事，从小到大没让我操过心，一心扑在书本上。”
[EN] Ye Anya gave a cold laugh. "Before our wedding I already heard about you cavorting with courtesans at Chunxiang Brothel. Since marrying you, the sight of you makes my stomach turn. Why would I wait on you?"
[ZH] 陈江木继续享受着：“我倒希望浩飞好好学习做生意，可他偏偏喜欢钻在书本里。”
[EN] Chen Haodong shoved her flat onto the mattress and pinned her beneath him. She offered no struggle, just turned her head away, every line of her face frozen in moonlight with a chill that radiated straight through his ribs.
[ZH] 洗得差不多时，崔氏起身。一旁的丫鬟递上布子，崔氏先将手擦了。
[EN] "I'm a man—what's wrong with playing around with a few women outside? Learn from Lin Jue's wife: gentle, dutiful, even used her own silver to buy Chun Jinqiao out—"
[ZH] 陈江木睁开眼，另一个丫鬟也递上一块布子，他接过布子，把脚擦干净。
[EN] Spit hit his cheek square on. "Want me like that? Wait for your next life—no, not even then!"
[ZH] 丫鬟退去后，两人才躺到床上，熄了灯。
[EN] Chen Haodong wiped the saliva off his face. "The colder you act, the more I want to claim you. I'm in a good mood tonight, so I'll go easy."
[ZH] 崔氏叹道：“浩飞的两个堂兄各有能耐，一个会哄老太太，一个是做生意的好手，在陈家与他们两个争，怎么都争不过。再说你也不是喜欢争的性子，生的儿子又岂能是？读书考功名，另辟蹊径，没什么不好。历来商贾之家与官宦之家相比，还是低人一等，浩飞要是能考上功名，你我可是朝廷官员的爹娘，听着都有面子。”
[EN] Closing her eyes, Ye Anya went numb while he forced himself on her. Not one happy night since her wedding—when would this nightmare end, and when could she be with the man she loved? Thoughts of Shuzhen rose in the dark, bold enough to haul Haoyu out of a brothel and lock him behind bars. Tomorrow she would go talk to her.
[ZH] 陈江木翻过身，背对着崔氏：“是这个理儿，所以我再不愿意，还是支持他。”
[EN] Over in Third Brother Chen Jiangmu's room, husband and wife chatted as they got ready for bed.
[ZH] 崔氏问：“你说陈家的家业往后会落在谁的手里？”
[EN] Removing her hairpins at the mirror, Madam Cui sighed. "Eldest Brother and Sister-in-law never should have sealed this match for Haoyu. Look at the poor boy suffering now."
[ZH] 陈江木打了一个哈欠：“浩飞是指望不上。”
[EN] Soaking his feet in a hot basin filled by a maid, Chen Jiangmu shrugged. "You know why—Prefect Zhu marched right to the gate. Eldest Brother had no choice. Married now; accept it."
[ZH] 崔氏强调道：“我是说浩东和浩宇，你希望是谁？”
[EN] Setting her ornaments in her box, Madam Cui crouched beside him and dipped her hands into the basin to wash his feet. "Still can't believe Haoyu started those rumors last year. Why on earth did he do that? Never heard of them crossing paths before."
[ZH] 陈江木含糊地回道：“这不明摆着吗？”
[EN] Closing his eyes as his wife kneaded his arches, Chen Jiangmu murmured, "Haoyu roams outside day and night—who knows what trouble he gets into?"
[ZH] “你是说浩东？”崔氏并未听到陈江木回答，只听到他的鼾声，自语道，“就目前来看，浩东很有希望。”
[EN] "Thank goodness our Haofei is steady—never gave me a day of grief, nose always buried in his books."
[ZH] ……
[EN] "I'd rather he learned family trade, yet his heart's glued to scholarship."
[ZH] 陈浩东早早起床离开陈府去绸缎庄忙活，叶安雅才觉出一身轻松，从心底来讲，她真心希望陈浩东永远待在外面别回来，若能像浩宇一样浪荡在外她才开心。
[EN] Finishing up, Madam Cui stood, dried her hands on a towel handed by a maid, while another maid handed Chen Jiangmu a cloth to dry his feet. Once the maids withdrew and the lamp went out, both lay down.
[ZH] 叶安雅心中搁着事儿，没有心思睡懒觉，早早起来，用过早饭，在小翠的陪伴下来到墨香苑找淑真。
[EN] "Haofei's two cousins each hold strong cards," Madam Cui reflected in the dark. "One charms Old Madam Xia, the other is a shark in trade. Neither you nor your son has the stomach to fight them for the family business. Studying for official exams is a smart separate path—merchants always rank beneath scholar-officials anyway. Once Haofei wins a degree, we become the parents of a court official! Talk about honor!"
[ZH] 昨晚千里寻已为收拾陈浩宇找到合适的理由，打算去找长辈表明态度，顺便理论一番。
[EN] Rolling over with his back to her, Chen Jiangmu murmured, "True enough. That's why I back him even if I'd prefer trade."
[ZH] 刚走到院子门口，瞧见叶安雅前来，热情地说：“大嫂一早过来，肯定找我有事，我暂且将自己的事搁下，与你畅聊一番。”
[EN] "Who do you think ends up running the Chen fortune?" she asked.
[ZH] 叶安雅露出歆羡的表情，只因淑真有个好心态。
[EN] Chen Jiangmu yawned. "Count Haofei out."
[ZH] 昨日听说她一脸怒气将浩宇绑回来，今日一早见她，脸上竟然看不到一丝怒色，实在想不通如此性情之人为何写的诗词多是伤春悲秋？
[EN] "I mean between Haodong and Haoyu—who do you want?"
[ZH] 也许正是那些诗词帮她疏散了情怀，人才变得更加阳光开朗。
[EN] "Isn't it obvious?" he mumbled.
[ZH] 叶安雅小心问：“我会不会来的不是时候？”
[EN] "You mean Haodong?" Hearing only a steady snore in answer, Madam Cui whispered to the ceiling, "As things stand, Haodong looks like a sure bet."
[ZH] 千里寻拉起她的手，往院内走：“哪里的话？无论大嫂什么时候来找我，都是时候。”
[EN] At dawn, Chen Haodong hurried off to the silk house, leaving Ye Anya breathing free air—wishing deep down he would roam outside forever like Haoyu. With heavy thoughts on her mind, she skipped sleeping in, ate an early breakfast, and walked to Moxiang Courtyard with Xiaocui.
[ZH] 叶安雅微笑道：“那我就去你屋里坐会儿，与你说说心里话。”
[EN] Qianli Xun had just stepped to her courtyard gate, bound for Old Madam Xia's rooms to lay out her "discipline" plan, when she spotted Ye Anya approaching.
[ZH] 千里寻点点头，两人牵着手进入素心草堂。千里寻猜到叶安雅可能要与她说些闺中私密，便把她带进里屋。
[EN] "Early visit, Eldest Sister-in-law! Must have something to talk over. My errand can wait—come chat!"
[ZH] 里屋有一处地上铺着毯子，上面放有一张方桌，桌上摆放着茶具，两边摆着低矮的方形木凳。
[EN] Ye Anya felt a rush of admiration at Shuzhen's resilience: furious enough yesterday to hog-tie her husband, yet bright and unclouded this morning. How did a spirit this sunny write such heartbreaking poetry? Perhaps pouring sorrow onto paper left her heart light in daily life.
[ZH] 两人在桌旁坐下，因凳子低矮，仿佛席地而坐一般。
[EN] "Am I interrupting anything?" Ye Anya asked hesitantly.
[ZH] 不用小姐吩咐，苦荞已在外厅开始做茶。
[EN] Taking her hand to lead her inside, Qianli Xun smiled. "Never! You're welcome anytime."
[ZH] 叶安雅坐下后，先提起昨日听到之事：“听说你把浩宇给关起来，你胆子可真大，就不怕奶奶和大伯大婶子批评你？”
[EN] "Then let me sit in your room a moment and speak from the heart."
[ZH] “大嫂都听说此事，奶奶与我公爹和婆婆怎能不知？到现在他们一个也没寻上门来，要么是他们理亏，要么就是从心底认可我的做法。”千里寻自顾揣摩着长辈的心思。
[EN] Sensing bedroom confidences coming, Qianli Xun led her straight into the inner parlor of Suxin Thatched Hall, where a woven rug lay under a low square tea table flanked by short stools that felt almost like sitting on the floor. Without being told, Kuqiao began whisking tea in the outer hall.
[ZH] 叶安雅没有兴致关注长辈的心思，反而赞叹道：“妹妹胆子大，也厉害，想做什么便做什么，令我羡慕不已。”
[EN] Seated, Ye Anya opened with yesterday's news. "I heard you locked Haoyu up! You have real nerve—aren't you worried Grandmother and Eldest Uncle and Aunt will chew you out?"
[ZH] 千里寻自我调侃道：“嫂嫂难道不知？私底下很多人唤我母老虎。”
[EN] "If even you heard, they sure heard too," Qianli Xun observed. "Not a single one showed up at my door, which means either they know they're in the wrong, or they secretly approve."
[ZH] 叶安雅笑道：“不管别人唤时是什么想法，反正我觉得是夸你。试想这世间有多少女子能像妹妹一样把自己夫君从青楼绑回来，还给关起来，这份勇气和魄力实在令人佩服。”
[EN] Careless of the elders' motives, Ye Anya praised from the heart, "Bold, decisive, doing what you please—I envy you more than I can say."
[ZH] “整个陈府，恐怕只有大嫂一个人这么认为。”
[EN] Qianli Xun grinned at herself. "Don't you know folks call me a tigress behind my back?"
[ZH] 叶安雅不想再言其他，停了一会儿，鼓起勇气：“淑真，上次我来找你，本有事请教，因第一次与你聊天儿，一直没好意思说出口。今日来，厚着脸皮大胆向你请教，还望不吝赐教。”
[EN] "Call it an insult if they like—to me it sounds like praise," Ye Anya smiled. "How many women in this world have the guts to march into a brothel, drag their husband home in ropes, and lock him up? Pure courage."
[ZH] 千里寻想到神柳下看到的情景，谨慎地道：“谈不上请教，大嫂有话直说。”
[EN] "You might be the only soul in Chen Manor who sees it that way."
[ZH] 叶安雅大胆说出心里想问的话：“你和离过一次，在这方面有些经验，能不能给我说说你和离的经过？”
[EN] Ye Anya paused, gathering every ounce of nerve. "Shuzhen, I wanted to ask your advice last time, yet felt too shy on our first real chat. Today I'm swallowing my pride to ask straight out—please don't hold back."
[ZH] 苦荞用盘子端着两盏做好的茶走进来，刚好听到大少夫人的话，听那意思并非好奇问出，她想到神柳下所见之事。
[EN] Remembering the embrace beneath the Divine Willow, Qianli Xun kept her tone careful. "No need to stand on ceremony, Eldest Sister-in-law. Speak your mind."
[ZH] 苦荞知道自己听到不该听的，但已听到，只好硬着头皮将茶盏为二位摆在桌上。
[EN] Ye Anya plunged right in. "You went through a mutual divorce once. Could you tell me how you managed it?"
[ZH] 千里寻见状吩咐道：“苦荞，你去外厅待着，别让他人闯进来。”
[EN] Right then Kuqiao walked in carrying a tray with two bowls of whisked tea, catching the question mid-air. Hearing the dead seriousness behind it, the scene beneath the Divine Willow flashed through her mind. Awkward at overhearing, she set both bowls on the table without a word.
[ZH] 叶安雅向身边的小翠示意，小翠跟着苦荞也去了外厅。
[EN] "Kuqiao, wait in the outer hall and keep everyone out," Qianli Xun ordered.
[ZH] 千里寻先仔细观察，叶安雅目光中充满期待。此事需谨慎回答，她不希望因自己的不当言论，破坏别人的婚姻，还是先了解前因后果为好。
[EN] Ye Anya nodded to Xiaocui, who stepped outside behind Kuqiao.
[ZH] “前几日，我去城外的神柳下，不巧，看到意想不到的风景……”
[EN] Studying the raw hope in Ye Anya's eyes, Qianli Xun weighed her words with care—unwilling to wreck another woman's marriage carelessly without hearing the full truth. "A few days ago, I went out to the Divine Willow beyond the city... and stumbled onto an unexpected sight."
[ZH] 叶安雅仔细回想，品出淑真话中的意思，既然已问出和离之事，没有必要藏着掖着：“你既然看到，应该清楚我为何会向你请教和离之事。”
[EN] Catching the hint at once—and having already asked about divorce—Ye Anya dropped all pretense. "Since you saw, you already know why I'm asking."
[ZH] “什么时候的事？”
[EN] "How long has it been?"
[ZH] “嫁到陈家之前，我们心中便有了对方，可叶家与曹家从祖上开始就不和，父亲坚决反对我与他在一起，便把我许给陈家。有段时间我想认命，可在成亲头一日，我的丫鬟小翠在外面听说陈浩东竟然跟商妓在一起，我便从骨子里对他生起厌恶之情。嫁过来后，没有一日不讨厌他。”
[EN] "We loved each other before I ever married into the Chen house. Because the Ye and Cao families have feuded for generations, my father forbade us to wed and betrothed me to the Chens instead. For a while I tried to accept my fate—until the very night before our wedding, when Xiaocui heard outside that Chen Haodong was spending the night with a brothel courtesan. Disgust took root in my bones, and I haven't gone one day without loathing him."
[ZH] 叶安雅说着撩起袖子，露出手臂上青一块紫一块的痕迹：“因我从来冷漠，陈浩东想要……，他就会用暴力，搞得我身上总是有伤。这种日子实在过下去，便想到和离，可我不知该如何着手，妹妹经历过一次，才想着向你请教。”
[EN] Ye Anya pushed back her sleeve, revealing angry purple-and-yellow bruises blotching her pale forearm. "Because I stay cold toward him, whenever Chen Haodong wants... his way, he uses his fists. My body is never free of bruises. I cannot live another day like this, so I thought of mutual divorce—only I have no idea where to start. Since you lived through it, I came to ask you."
[ZH] 千里寻伸过手，帮叶安雅把衣袖放下，遮盖住手臂上的痕迹：“没想到大嫂生活不易，你确定想要走上和离这一步？”
[EN] Qianli Xun reached across and drew Ye Anya's sleeve back down over the bruises. "I had no idea you were going through hell, Eldest Sister-in-law. Are you certain you want to take the step of mutual divorce?"
[ZH] 叶安雅肯定地点点头：“我已铁了心，才敢前来相问。”
[EN] Ye Anya nodded hard. "My mind is set, or I never would have dared ask."
[ZH] 千里寻经历过和离，深知女子走上这一步相当不易，看着眼前外表柔弱的叶安雅，真担心她根本扛不住：“和离不易，大嫂还是再认真想想。”
[EN] Having survived the gauntlet herself and looking at fragile Ye Anya across the table, Qianli Xun feared she might shatter under the backlash. "Mutual divorce is brutal, Eldest Sister-in-law. Think long and hard before you leap."

#CH 229
[ZH] 叶安雅端起茶盏一口饮下，用丝帕沾了沾嘴角，正色道：“妹妹不必为我担心，你若真希望我好，就告诉我，要怎么做才能结束这段姻缘。”
[ZH] 千里寻想想穿越而来的婚姻之路，甚是触动。与叶安雅比起来她算幸运许多，起码林觉和陈浩宇拿她没办法，而眼前的大嫂只能默默忍受陈浩东的暴力，不如与她说说。
[ZH] “在这个时代，女子和离实属不易，我也是费尽九牛二虎之力，最终还是靠仪王帮忙，说服我爹和林觉，才得以顺利离开林家。”
[ZH] “什么？仪王帮忙？看来的确不容易。”叶安雅露出焦虑的神色。
[ZH] 千里寻端起茶盏品口茶：“是啊，我有时在想，如果仪王没有来临安，也许我跟林觉根本无法和离。”
[ZH] 千里寻想到申简辰，他穿越成为仪王，一出现在她面前，便帮了她；后来还帮着说服乔家到朱家提亲；流言渐渐消失也与他密不可分……
[ZH] 叶安雅见淑真不语，焦虑之余，小心问：“难道仪王那时已喜欢上你？”
[ZH] 抢亲之事闹得很大，叶安雅如此问，倒也正常。
[ZH] 千里寻既要如实回答，又不想引起他人误会：“大嫂这么问，肯定是听说了抢亲之事。…… 那时仪王是什么心思我不得而知，但我与他清清白白，况且我跟佳和郡主是闺中密友，又怎能做对不起她的事？成亲那日的事完全出乎我的意料。”
[ZH] 叶安雅叹道：“你我都是可怜之人。”
[ZH] 千里寻不想再谈仪王，将话题拉回到和离之事：“嫂嫂若真想和离，不仅要说通堂兄，还需两家长辈同意，只要一方不同意，和离之事恐怕难成。”
[ZH] 叶安雅搓着手中的丝帕，继续焦虑着，因焦虑沉默不语。
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“看样子她会知难而退。”
[ZH] 谁知叶安雅搓了一会儿丝帕，开口说：“我会想办法一一说服。如若实在不行，就让陈浩东休了我。”
[ZH] 千里寻想起刚刚穿越而来时苦荞说过的话，急忙问：“大嫂在娘家可有姐妹？”
[ZH] “有两个妹妹。”
[ZH] “既如此万万不可让夫家休了你。”
[ZH] “我也知被夫家所休必然会影响我的妹妹们，可我实在不想忍受现在的生活，大不了到时叶家不认我这个女儿。”
[ZH] 千里寻被叶安雅的决心触动，握住她的手：“大嫂是这个时代少有的女子，但需记着，无论怎么做，需先筹谋好，还要保护好自己。”
[EN] Ye Anya drained her tea bowl in one gulp, dabbed her mouth with a silk handkerchief, and spoke steady and firm. "Don't worry for me, Sister. If you truly want what's best for me, tell me how to end this marriage."
[ZH] 叶安雅点点头，反过来抓住淑真的手：“淑真，谢谢你，因为你，我才有和离的勇气。”
[EN] Reflecting on her own tangled path since arriving in the Song, Qianli Xun felt a deep pang. Compared with Ye Anya, she had been lucky—neither Lin Jue nor Chen Haoyu stood a chance against her fists, whereas her sister-in-law endured Chen Haodong's violence helplessly. She owed her the honest truth.
[ZH] 千里寻不知该说些什么，她怕在这个时代自己对叶安雅的影响并非真正对她好。
[EN] "In this era, mutual divorce for a woman is uphill through thorns. I moved heaven and earth and still only pulled it off because Prince Yi stepped in to pressure my father and Lin Jue into signing."
[ZH] 两人说得差不多，叶安雅不便多打扰，起身辞别。
[EN] "Prince Yi stepped in?" Anxiety clouded Ye Anya's face. "So it really is that hard..."
[ZH] 千里寻将她送至墨香苑外，目送着离开，心中祝福道：“希望大嫂能幸福。”
[EN] Qianli Xun took a slow sip of tea. "Yes. Sometimes I wonder if I ever would have escaped Lin Manor if Prince Yi hadn't come to Lin'an."
[ZH] 她在心中自问：“我为何不祝大嫂和离成功？”
[EN] Her mind drifted to Shen Jianchen—helping her divorce Lin Jue the instant they crossed paths, helping win over Qiao Manor, helping stamp out last year's rumors...
[ZH] 仔细想想终于明白，和离结果无非两种，成或不成。成功，她可与相爱之人在一起，自然会幸福；若不成功，还得生活下去，幸福也许与她无缘，便更需要他人祝福。
[EN] Seeing Shuzhen fall quiet, Ye Anya asked in a low voice, "Did Prince Yi already have feelings for you back then?"
[ZH] 想到自己院中还有烦心事需处理，先将大嫂的事搁置一旁，尽快去找老太太。
[EN] After the wedding raid, the question was natural enough. Wanting to stay honest without sparking fresh gossip, Qianli Xun replied, "You must have heard about the wedding raid. Whatever Prince Yi felt back then I cannot say, yet everything between us has always been clean. Princess Jiahe was my closest friend—how could I ever betray her? What happened on my wedding day blindsided me completely."
[ZH] 近期公爹与婆婆每日用完早饭会去老太太屋里坐，只要去找她，便可抓住所有人，一气儿将话说了，省得与这个说完，又去找那个。
[EN] Ye Anya sighed. "We're two miserable souls in the same boat."
[ZH] 一踏进老太太的院子，听到里面说话的声音，无非是拉家常，听得出屋里人不少。
[EN] Steering off Prince Yi, Qianli Xun returned to the point. "If you truly want a mutual divorce, Eldest Sister-in-law, you don't just have to win over Eldest Cousin-in-law—you need both families' elders to agree. If a single side refuses, mutual divorce hits a wall."
[ZH] 院中的丫鬟见二少夫人前来，打算去通报，被千里寻拦住：“不必通报，我自行进去。”
[EN] Twisting her silk handkerchief between pale fingers, Ye Anya fell silent in distress. Looks like she's backing down, Qianli Xun thought.
[ZH] 丫鬟不好再说什么。
[EN] Instead, Ye Anya lifted her chin. "I'll find a way to persuade them one by one. And if all else fails, I'll push Chen Haodong to repudiate me."
[ZH] 千里寻之所以这么做，是想观察几位长辈见到她时最真实的表情。若丫鬟通报，他们提前会有心理准备，她看到的表情很有可能是长辈想让她看到的。
[EN] Remembering Kuqiao's warning from her earliest days in the Song, Qianli Xun asked sharp and fast, "Do you have sisters back at your maiden home?"
[ZH] 屋内拉家常的声音越来越大，走近后才听清是二叔的声音。
[EN] "Two younger sisters."
[ZH] 千里寻大胆踏进门槛，当她出现在众人面前时，说话的声音停止。
[EN] "Then under no circumstances let him repudiate you!"
[ZH] 老太太、姚轻琰和陈江林的脸上挂着笑容，千里寻松口气，看来这三位并未责怪她。即使他们表情不好，她也不怕，她还想拿散播谣言之事与他们说道说道。
[EN] "I know being cast out ruins my sisters' marriage prospects," Ye Anya whispered, "yet I cannot bear one more day of this. Worst comes to worst, let the Ye family disown me as a daughter."
[ZH] 再看两位婶婶，不满中又似乎等着看笑话，如此看来婆婆与妯娌之间的和谐只是表面而已。
[EN] Moved by the steel beneath her fragile exterior, Qianli Xun clasped her hands. "You're rarer than most women in this era, Eldest Sister-in-law. Just remember: whatever path you choose, plan every step in advance—and keep yourself safe."
[ZH] 两位叔叔毕竟长期在外忙，情绪掩饰得挺好。不过眼神还是有所流露，两人瞧一眼她，立刻垂下眼帘，显然不想多看一眼，说明他们也已知她把陈浩宇关了起来。
[EN] Ye Anya squeezed her fingers back. "Thank you, Shuzhen. Watching you gave me the nerve to try."
[ZH] 老太太先发话：“淑真，你来了，快坐。”
[EN] Qianli Xun had no words, nagged by quiet dread that her influence might push Ye Anya into a fire she could not survive.
[ZH] 千里寻并未落座，而是跪下，苦荞也跟着跪下。
[EN] With everything said, Ye Anya took her leave. Seeing her out beyond Moxiang Courtyard, Qianli Xun sent a silent wish after her: May Eldest Sister-in-law find happiness.
[ZH] 老太太问：“为何跪下？”
[EN] Why not wish her a successful divorce? she asked herself—and saw the answer at once. Succeed, and she won happiness with the man she loved; fail, and she still had to live on, needing every blessing she could get.
[ZH] “奶奶，昨日我把陈浩宇从青楼绑回来，将他关在后院。我自认为没有错，只怕陈家长辈不这么认为，所以先跪下谢罪。”
[EN] Setting Ye Anya's troubles aside for her own pressing business, Qianli Xun headed straight for Old Madam Xia's courtyard. Knowing her father-in-law and mother-in-law gathered there every morning after breakfast, one trip would let her face the whole clan at once instead of repeating herself room by room.
[ZH] 老太太还未发话，陈江木着急地批评道：“你既知有错，还不赶紧把浩宇放出来？不比你跪在这里谢罪强？……”
[EN] Approaching the gate, lively family chatter drifted from indoors—a full house. When a yard maid started forward to announce her, Qianli Xun raised a hand. "No need. I'll walk straight in."
[ZH] 夏老太太抬手阻止老三继续说下去，陈江木只好闭嘴。
[EN] She wanted to catch the elders' unfiltered reactions before they had time to put on polite masks.
[ZH] “你何罪之有？别跪了，起来吧。”
[EN] Recognizing Second Uncle's voice rising inside, Qianli Xun stepped boldly across the threshold. Conversation snapped off mid-sentence.
[ZH] 奶奶发话，千里寻自然起来。苦荞也跟着起来，老太太不怪罪，她替小姐感到高兴。
[EN] Old Madam Xia, Yao Qingyan, and Chen Jianglin all greeted her with warm smiles—relieving her at once, though she had come braced for a showdown over the rumors either way. Both aunts wore sour expressions laced with gleeful anticipation of drama, exposing how paper-thin their polite harmony was. Both uncles masked their feelings better, yet one quick glance and lowered eyes proved they already knew Chen Haoyu was under lock and key.
[ZH] 千里寻故意问：“奶奶与公爹和婆婆真的不怪罪我？”
[EN] "Shuzhen, come sit!" Old Madam Xia called warmly.
[ZH] 陈江林说：“何时怪罪过你？我们既然把管教浩宇的权力交给你，你想怎么管教由着你。”
[EN] Instead of sitting, Qianli Xun dropped straight to her knees, with Kuqiao kneeling beside her.
[ZH] 姚轻琰也说：“昨日我们已知此事，并未前去阻拦，足以说明对你的做法十分认可，你只管按照自己认为正确的方式管教即可。”
[EN] "What are you kneeling for?" Old Madam Xia asked.
[ZH] 老太太也笑道：“我在此向你保证，在你管教浩宇期间，我们三人绝不拖后腿。”
[EN] "Grandmother, yesterday I marched Chen Haoyu home from a brothel in ropes and locked him in the back courtyard. In my own conscience I did nothing wrong, yet I feared the Chen elders might disagree, so I kneel first to beg pardon."
[ZH] 公爹不怪罪，在意料之中；奶奶和婆婆一点儿不计较，甚至连叮嘱都没有，倒在意料之外。
[EN] Before Old Madam Xia could speak, Chen Jiangmu burst out, "If you know you crossed the line, hurry up and let Haoyu out instead of kneeling here for show—"
[ZH] 陈江树与包氏听出三人不计较的原因不过是想让朱家姑娘管教浩宇，甚觉可笑。二人互相瞧一眼，包氏立刻明白夫君的意思。
[EN] Old Madam Xia raised one hand, silencing her third son on the spot. "What pardon do you need? Get up at once."
[ZH] “昨个母亲和姐姐听说浩宇被绑，还担心不已，怎么这会儿不计较？哦，我想起来，昨儿我离开母亲房中，在院外逗留一会儿，瞧见韦嬷嬷前来，想必是她替淑真说情。还别说，淑真身边这位嬷嬷还真不一般。”
[EN] Rising alongside a relieved Kuqiao, Qianli Xun tested the waters. "Grandmother, Father-in-law, Mother-in-law—you truly aren't angry with me?"
[ZH] 不管包氏说这些话是何目的，正好帮千里寻解释了心中疑惑。还是韦嬷嬷最好，早早过来为她解决掉后顾之忧。早知如此昨晚也不必忧虑，更不必一大早过来探虚实。
[EN] "When were we angry?" Chen Jianglin asked. "We put Haoyu's discipline in your hands; handle him however you see fit."
[ZH] 老太太批评道：“淑真又不是你的儿媳妇，你少插嘴。”
[EN] "We heard yesterday and didn't lift a finger to stop you," Yao Qingyan added. "That shows we back you completely. Discipline him whichever way works."
[ZH] 包氏撇撇嘴，不再说话。
[EN] Old Madam Xia chuckled. "You have my word: while you're straightening Haoyu out, none of the three of us will get in your way."
[ZH] 崔氏本想发表意见，见包氏被批评，便把想说的话咽进肚内。
[EN] Chen Jianglin backing her came as no surprise; Old Madam Xia and Yao Qingyan not even slipping in one plea for mercy baffled her.
[ZH] 夏老太太又满脸堆笑：“快坐下吧，站着多累。”
[EN] Finding the elders' blind faith laughable, Chen Jiangshu traded a look with Madam Bao, who caught his cue at once. "Funny—yesterday Mother and Eldest Sister-in-law looked panicked when they heard Haoyu was tied up, yet today they don't mind a bit! Oh, wait—as I left yesterday, I spotted Matron Wei heading into Mother's courtyard. Must have put in a good word for Shuzhen! That matron of yours is truly something else."
[ZH] 千里寻并未落座，既然不用费口舌谈及管教陈浩宇之事，不如说说散播流言一事。即使说了没用，她还是想说，发泄发泄也是好的。
[EN] Whatever poison Madam Bao meant to stir, she just solved Qianli Xun's mystery. Good old Matron Wei had already paved the whole road for her last night!
[ZH] “奶奶，孙媳还是站着，只怕我接下来说的话，您会不高兴。”
[EN] "Shuzhen isn't your daughter-in-law—keep your nose out of it!" Old Madam Xia snapped at Madam Bao.
[ZH] 此话一出，老太太明白淑真想说什么，不如由自己直接道出：“你是想说浩宇散播谣言一事？”
[EN] Madam Bao pressed her lips together, while Madam Cui swallowed the comment she had been about to offer.
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia beamed at Qianli Xun again. "Sit down already! Why stand?"
[ZH] “此事都过去，你也嫁过来，我们成一家人，没有必要再提起。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stayed on her feet. With the lockup approved, one thorn still sat raw in her chest—useless to vent or not, she needed it off her tongue. "Let me stay standing, Grandmother. What I have to say next might displease you."
[ZH] 老太太三言两语将千里寻想要发泄的怨气给打回去，还用“一家人”三个字传播出温馨的气息，千里寻差点儿陶醉其中。
[EN] Guessing at once, Old Madam Xia beat her to it. "About Haoyu spreading those rumors?"
[ZH] 想起陈浩宇那副嘴脸，立刻变得清醒：“孙媳想知道明明是陈浩宇散播谣言毁我幸福，长辈们为何还要同意这门亲事？硬生生让两个有仇之人结为夫妻，不是害我？又是什么？”
[EN] "Yes."
[ZH] 淑真的问题像炮弹一样袭来而来，有些长辈忧心，有些则等着看热闹。
[EN] "All water under the bridge. You're married in now—we're one family. No sense dredging up the past."
[ZH] 陈江林叹口气：“淑真啊，你心中有怨气，我们做长辈的能理解，可当时知府大人绑着浩宇过来，说了他散播谣言之事，我又急又气，不知该如何是好，知府大人提出这个主意，我也觉甚好，没有想那么多。再说是浩宇犯下的错误，他就应该承担所有责任。”
[EN] Those three words—"one family"—nearly dulled Qianli Xun's edge with unexpected warmth, until Chen Haoyu's smug grin flashed back in her mind. "Granddaughter-in-law wants to know one thing: knowing full well Chen Haoyu spread slander that ruined my happiness, why did the elders still agree to this match? Tying two bitter enemies together as man and wife—if that isn't hurting me, what is?"
[ZH] 千里寻埋怨道：“承担责任的方式很多，为何偏偏选择定亲的方式？明明是把我推进火坑里，还要说得那么冠冕堂皇。”
[EN] The blunt blast landed like a cannonball—half the elders squirming, the other half secretly licking their lips.
[ZH] 姚轻琰起身走到淑真跟前，抓起她的手：“陈家哪是火坑？你嫁过来这些日子可曾吃过亏？受过苦？”
[EN] Chen Jianglin let out a heavy sigh. "We understand your resentment, Shuzhen. Yet when Prefect Zhu marched Haoyu in trussed up and laid out what he'd done, I was frantic and furious. When Prefect Zhu proposed this match, it seemed the right way to make amends, so I didn't think deeper. Haoyu caused the mess; he had to take full responsibility."
[ZH] 仔细想想，确实没有，奶奶寻事，也并非真心，可她心里就是不舒服。
[EN] "Plenty of ways to take responsibility—why shove me into a marriage trap and dress it up as noble?"
[ZH] “陈家是不错，长辈们也对我好，可陈浩宇……，我与他实在合不来，他也并非我心中理想之人。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan rose and clasped Shuzhen's hands. "How is Chen Manor a trap? Have you suffered one ounce of mistreatment since arriving?"
[ZH] 姚轻琰笑道：“那你就把她管教成你心中的理想之人，到时岂不完美？”
[EN] Honestly, no—even Old Madam Xia's "trouble" had been an act—yet the knot in her chest refused to loosen. "The Chen family is wonderful and the elders treat me well, except Chen Haoyu... we're oil and water. He is nowhere near the husband I ever wanted."
[ZH] 老太太和陈江林听了也都乐起来。
[EN] Yao Qingyan laughed. "Then whip him into the exact husband you want! Wouldn't that be perfection?"
[ZH] 包氏心中嘲笑道：“自己管教不了儿子，想靠儿媳妇管教，实在可笑！”
[EN] Old Madam Xia and Chen Jianglin joined in chuckling.
[ZH] 崔氏心想：“从未听说谁家娘子能把夫君管教成人，朱家姑娘若能把浩宇管教好，我把崔字倒着写。”
[EN] Madam Bao sneered inside: Can't discipline their own son and expect a bride to do miracles—laughable!
[ZH] 陈江木只觉是无稽之谈，见大哥脸上挂着笑容，也不好说什么。
[EN] Madam Cui thought: Never heard of a wife reforming a rake! If the Zhu girl whips Haoyu into shape, I'll write my surname Cui upside down!
[ZH] 陈江树忍了半天，终于开口说话：“母亲，大哥，依我看不如让浩宇到陈家铺子里来，放在我身边，跟我学习做生意，我也好管教他，总比让个新媳妇管教传出去好听些。虽说淑真拳头厉害，可管教人不能只靠拳头。”
[EN] Chen Jiangmu found it absurd too, yet kept quiet seeing his eldest brother smiling.
[ZH] 包氏心中明白，自家夫君说这些话并非真心，而是有两个目的，一来暗含大哥大嫂不会教育孩子，二来嘲笑他们所托非人。
[EN] After holding back, Chen Jiangshu finally spoke up. "Mother, Eldest Brother—if you ask me, send Haoyu to the Chen shops under my wing to learn trade while I keep an eye on him. Sounds far better to outsiders than letting a new bride discipline her husband! Shuzhen's fists are impressive, sure, yet raising a man takes more than fists."
[ZH] 老太太搭话道：“我看淑真甚好，此事不必再议，就这么定了。”
[EN] Madam Bao knew her husband had zero interest in actually mentoring Haoyu—just taking a clean dig at Eldest Brother's failed parenting and mocking his choice of enforcer.
[ZH] 陈江树说的那些想管教浩宇的话并非真心，母亲既发话，他没有必要继续强求，应承道：“母亲觉得好，那便是好的。”
[EN] "Shuzhen is doing splendidly," Old Madam Xia shut it down. "Case closed. End of discussion."
[ZH] 老太太继续说：“淑真，我知道你不愿嫁到陈家，对你与浩宇的婚事不满，尤其听到他是散播你谣言之人，心中更是来气。可事实上你嫁过来，并没有人用刀架在你的脖子上。仔细想想也许正是你跟浩宇的缘分，现在看来是孽缘，奶奶相信，你有能力将孽缘变成良缘。”
[EN] Not wanting the job anyway, Chen Jiangshu bowed his head. "Whatever Mother thinks best."
[ZH] 姚轻琰依然抓着淑真的手：“母亲说得对，孽缘可以变成良缘。淑真，别再想散播流言一事，你嫁到陈家好日子还长着。你要是气不过，正好借管教的机会好好收拾他，只当是泄愤……”
[EN] Old Madam Xia turned back to Shuzhen. "I know you married in reluctantly, and learning he started those rumors makes your blood boil. Still, nobody held a knife to your throat on your wedding day. Call it tangled fate—looks like an ill-starred bond right now, yet Grandmother trusts you to turn ill fate into good fortune."
[ZH] 千里寻觉得奶奶与公爹和婆婆真是异想天开，既想借她管教儿子，还幻想着孽缘变成良缘，实在可笑！
[EN] Still holding her hands, Yao Qingyan added, "Mother's right—ill fate can turn sweet. Put the rumors behind you, Shuzhen; bright days lie ahead. If anger still burns, treat disciplining him as venting your spleen—"
[ZH] 正想着，姚轻琰说出她想听的话，将谣言之事带来的不快暂时搁置一边：“婆婆既如此说，我便记下，以后管教陈浩宇只当是泄愤，若不能把他管教成长辈希望的样子，还请奶奶、公爹和婆婆不要后悔你们今日的选择。”
[EN] Finding their optimism wildly naive—expecting revenge to blossom into romance—Qianli Xun seized Yao Qingyan's slip to set firm ground rules upfront. "Since Mother-in-law put it that way, I'll take you at your word: I'll treat disciplining Chen Haoyu purely as venting my grudge. If he doesn't turn into the paragon you elders hope for, don't come regretting today's choice."
[ZH] 陈江树心中乐道：“你越是泄愤，我儿子越高兴。”
[EN] Chen Jiangshu grinned inside: Vent all you want—makes my son's victory easier!
[ZH] 包氏继续在心中嘲笑道：“大嫂真会说话，这下可好，自己给自己挖个坑，跳进坑里的滋味不好受吧？”
[EN] Madam Bao gloated silently: Smooth talk, Eldest Sister-in-law—dug your own pit and jumped right in!
[ZH] 老三夫妇只觉眼前的新媳妇实在嚣张，可二人又不敢多嘴，只能一边不满一边看热闹。
[EN] Third Uncle and Aunt exchanged scandalized looks at such brazen arrogance, yet held their tongues.
[ZH] 姚轻琰放下淑真的手，担忧道：“我说是泄愤，你可别太当真。”她走回椅子旁坐下，向陈江林使眼色。
[EN] Dropping Shuzhen's hands in alarm, Yao Qingyan fluttered, "I only joked about venting—don't take it literally!" Retreating to her chair, she shot an anxious glance at Chen Jianglin.
[ZH] 姚轻琰的表现使得千里寻明白自己的话说得重些。可丑话必须说在前头，再说她确实没有将管教陈浩宇当回事，她看重的只是用折磨他的方式报仇。只怕折磨到最后，陈浩宇并未变成长辈希望的样子。提前说好，省得陈家人到时找她麻烦。
[EN] Qianli Xun knew her warning sounded blunt, yet ugly truths belonged upfront: she was out for payback, not miracle-working, and had zero intention of taking blame if Chen Haoyu stayed hopeless.

#CH 230
[ZH] 陈江林接触到姚轻琰的目光，立刻明白她因担忧十分后悔刚才说出的话。
[ZH] 为安慰夫人，也为淑真放心管教浩宇，陈江林咳了两声：“咳咳！”然后说道：“淑真已嫁过来些时日，让我们兄弟三人明白了母亲的需求，由此看来淑真是有心之人，也是良善之辈……”
[ZH] “良善之辈”令姚轻琰想起昨日韦嬷嬷说过的话，心想：“是啊，淑真品性善良，又怎么会像恶人一样欺压浩宇？我还担心个什么劲儿？”不觉轻松许多。
[ZH] 只听陈江林继续说：“世上无难事，只怕有心人，我相信淑真。即便管不好，我既不会怪你，也不会后悔。结果无非两种，管教好皆大欢喜，管教不好，我也不会再管，儿孙自有儿孙福。只盼望我不在了，陈家新的当家人能给浩宇一碗热乎饭吃，也希望淑真你不要抛弃他。”
[ZH] 公爹说的话至情至理，千里寻不好再继续埋怨。散播谣言的人是陈浩宇，与陈家长辈无关，又何必将情绪发泄在他们身上。
[ZH] 陈江树和顾氏从大哥的话中听出些苗头来，如此看来大哥从未想过让浩宇接替家业，浩飞又一门心思扑在书本上，陈家的未来只能属于浩东。二人心中满意，不由露出笑容。
[ZH] 陈江木忍不住开口：“大哥说的哪里话，陈家是什么人家？怎么可能让浩宇只有一碗热乎饭吃？浩宇哪怕永远浪荡在外，只要他回来，不敢说锦衣玉食，那也是大鱼大肉。”
[ZH] 崔氏应和道：“是啊是啊，大哥大嫂和母亲怎么舍得浩宇受苦？还不得给他留些家产？他的堂兄堂弟也不会不管浩宇，再不济还有他娘子，淑真名下也有不少财产，除嫁过来带的嫁妆外，浩宇的财产又归她，在临安城的大家闺秀中算是有钱人，怎么着也苦不了浩宇。”
[ZH] 夏老太太观察一会儿孙媳妇，从她的话语和表情判断，她对管教浩宇之事并不感兴趣，老人家心中不免失落。
[ZH] 老三夫妇的话，老太太十分不满，才开口说：“行了，不要再说，我还没死呢，你们倒考虑起没了长辈浩宇该怎么办？常言道，家有贤妻，胜过良田万顷。浩宇能娶淑真是上辈子修来的福气，有淑真这样的贤妻，还愁他以后没好日子过？……”
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“我是贤妻没错，可惜嫁给陈浩宇这类人，还跟我结下仇怨，恐怕我这个贤妻也不得不变成大家口中的母老虎。”
[ZH] 包氏心中不悦：“什么贤妻？明明是只母老虎！”
[ZH] 崔氏瞧向朱家姑娘，心中笑道：“长得倒是一副温柔可人的样子，可惜拳头太厉害，从没听说过谁家的贤妻会把自家夫君五花大绑，还给关起来。老太太心中贤妻的标准也忒低了吧？”
[EN] Catching Yao Qingyan's panicked look, Chen Jianglin cleared his throat to reassure both his wife and his daughter-in-law. "Since marrying in, Shuzhen helped us three brothers finally understand Mother's heart. That proves she is thoughtful and deeply kind."
[ZH] 夏老太太继续说：“淑真，要是对你公爹和婆婆，还有我这个奶奶满意，就不要再去想浩宇散播流言之事，不管好事坏事都已成为过去，人总要往前看。往后你只忙管教浩宇一事，不用每日来问安陪我，一大家子人不差你一个。也不用再操心怎么哄我开心，说话艺人早已认下来我院中的路，每日他自会按时来给我讲故事。你如何管教浩宇也不用向长辈汇报，想怎么管教都行。行了，回去吧。”
[EN] "Deeply kind" echoed Matron Wei's exact words from yesterday, easing the knot in Yao Qingyan's chest. Right—Shuzhen isn't cruel; why am I panicking?
[ZH] 老太太不想再让淑真说下去，怕她心有怨气，说出更多不满的话来，让老二和老三家的听着笑话。那两个儿媳妇对自家夫君没得说，只是心胸和境界狭隘些，最喜看别人的笑话。
[EN] "Nothing is impossible to a willing heart," Chen Jianglin went on. "I trust you, Shuzhen. Even if you can't reform him, I won't blame you or regret it. Either he shapes up and we all rejoice, or he doesn't and I wash my hands of him—every child walks his own fate. All I ask is that after I'm gone, whoever takes the helm of the Chen family leaves Haoyu a warm bowl of rice, and that you don't abandon him."
[ZH] 千里寻不好再待下去，行完礼离开。
[EN] Fair and heartfelt words—leaving Qianli Xun unable to keep bristling. Chen Haoyu was the villain, not these elders; no sense taking her rage out on them.
[ZH] 回去的路上，仔细回想刚才说过的话和拜见老太太的目的，总觉得此次拜见长辈实在毫无意义。想要解决的问题韦嬷嬷早已帮她解决，想要发泄的情绪却还堵在心里。
[EN] Chen Jiangshu and Madam Bao perked up at Chen Jianglin's phrasing—clearly Eldest Brother had already written Haoyu off as heir, and Haofei cared only for books, leaving the Chen fortune practically handed to Haodong! Both hid pleased smiles.
[ZH] 既然老太太已发话，暂时还是不要再去拜见长辈，心中的情绪还未疏散，只怕见到长辈话语中总带着火药味儿，影响大家之间的和谐。
[EN] Chen Jiangmu jumped in. "What talk is that, Eldest Brother? What kind of house are the Chens that Haoyu would ever be down to a plain bowl of rice? Even if he plays around for life, he'll eat fine meats and fish whenever he walks home!"
[ZH] 还是好好想想怎么以管教为借口折磨陈浩宇。
[EN] "Exact right!" Madam Cui echoed. "How could Eldest Brother, Sister-in-law, and Mother let Haoyu go hungry? Between his own share, his cousins looking out for him, and his wife—Shuzhen holds her own dowry plus all Haoyu's personal properties, richer than almost any lady in Lin'an! Haoyu won't suffer a day!"
[ZH] 话说一大早红豆把早饭送进博雅斋，用完饭后，陈浩宇给青瓜使了使眼色。
[EN] Watching her granddaughter-in-law's cool face, Old Madam Xia sensed she still had little real interest in reforming Haoyu and felt a dull pang. Annoyed by Third Son and his wife jumping straight to dividing estates, she cut them off. "Enough! I'm not dead yet, and you're already planning Haoyu's welfare after the elders are gone! As the saying goes, 'A worthy wife outweighs ten thousand acres of fertile land.' Haoyu earned blessings in a past life to marry Shuzhen—with a wife like her, why fret over his future?"
[ZH] 青瓜领会后，凑近红豆：“你一会儿能不能帮我把丑丫叫过来？”
[EN] Worthy wife, maybe—too bad I got stuck with a grudge against Chen Haoyu that turned me into everyone's favorite tigress! Qianli Xun thought.
[ZH] 红豆正收拾碗筷：“叫丑丫做什么？”
[EN] Madam Bao scoffed silently: Worthy wife? Pure tigress!
[ZH] 青瓜解释道：“别误会，我不是喜欢她，只是有事跟她说。”
[EN] Madam Cui snickered inside: Sweet face, lethal fists—never heard of a "worthy wife" tying her husband up and locking him in a back room! Old Madam Xia's bar for worthy wives sure dropped low!
[ZH] 丑丫对青瓜有意思，红豆早已看在眼里，不如帮忙叫一下，给他们提供沟通感情的机会，省得青瓜烦自己：“你等着，一会儿我帮你叫来，可她不能进来，只能在窗户外跟你说话。”
[EN] "Shuzhen," Old Madam Xia continued, "if you think well of your father-in-law, mother-in-law, and me, leave those old rumors in the past. Good or bad, yesterday is gone—people have to look forward. Focus purely on disciplining Haoyu from now on; no need to come pay morning respects or worry about keeping me entertained. The storyteller knows the path by heart now, and this big family has plenty of people to keep me company. No need to report your methods either—handle him as you please. Go on back now."
[ZH] “没关系，窗外就窗外。”青瓜把红豆送至门口，直到门被锁上。
[EN] Not wanting Shuzhen to vent further resentment and give Second and Third Sister-in-law more fodder to gloat over, Old Madam Xia closed the session. Qianli Xun curtsied and left.
[ZH] 陈浩宇问：“为什么选择丑丫？”
[EN] Walking back, she felt the trip had been pointless: Matron Wei had already cleared the elders' approval, yet the sour lump still sat in her chest. Better to stay away from the main courtyard until her temper cooled, and focus instead on creative "discipline" for Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] 青瓜转过身：“公子布置的任务实在艰巨，只能选择丑丫。一听名字就知道是个丑丫头，我勾引起来方便些。”
[EN] Earlier that morning, after Hongdou delivered breakfast to Boya Study and they ate, Chen Haoyu shot Qinggua a look.
[ZH] “还有一个丫鬟叫什么？”
[EN] Catching his cue, Qinggua edged up to Hongdou as she stacked bowls. "Could you do me a favor and call Chouya over here?"
[ZH] “叫阿美。”
[EN] "What do you want Chouya for?" Hongdou asked.
[ZH] “阿美，一听名字就能猜到人长得好看。”
[EN] "Don't get the wrong idea—I don't like her! Just need to ask her something."
[ZH] “其实长得一般，跟红豆没法比，她是阿升的妹妹。”
[EN] Having noticed Chouya's crush on Qinggua ages ago, Hongdou happily seized the chance to pass Qinggua off onto her and get him off her own back. "Hang on, I'll send her over. She can't come inside though—she'll have to talk through the window."
[ZH] “兄妹两个一起被朱淑真使唤，真是没得救。”
[EN] "Window works fine!" Qinggua escorted her to the door until the padlock snapped shut again.
[ZH] 青瓜并不认同：“公子自小生在富贵人家，哪能理解穷人家孩子的苦？穷人家只要有孩子能到富人家当个下人，都高兴得不行。再遇到像少夫人那样的好主子，更是难得，他们兄妹两个能一起跟着少夫人练习拳法，别人羡慕还来不及。”
[EN] "Why pick Chouya?" Chen Haoyu asked.
[ZH] 陈浩宇表现出强烈的不满：“去去去！少在本公面前夸朱淑真，你是我的人，可不能被她给收买。”
[EN] Qinggua turned back. "You handed me a brutal mission, Young Master—Chouya's my only shot! With a nickname like 'Ugly Girl,' she's way easier to charm."
[ZH] “公子放心，我对您忠心不二。”
[EN] "What's the other maid's name?"
[ZH] 两人正说着窗外传来丑丫的声音：“青瓜，红豆姐姐说你找我？”
[EN] "Amei."
[ZH] 丑丫长得并不十分丑陋，只是胖些，加上眼睛天生眯成一条缝，看着没其他丫鬟顺眼，便得了丑丫的名字。
[EN] "Amei—means pretty, right? Must be a looker."
[ZH] “人来了，快去，好好施展你的魅力。”陈浩宇小声说完，坐在椅子上欣赏青瓜如何勾引丑丫。
[EN] "Pretty plain actually, nowhere near Hongdou. She's Asheng's younger sister."
[ZH] 为了公子，为了出去能把红豆追到手，青瓜狠狠心，靠近窗户：“丑丫，我被少夫人关起来，到现在已经有八九个时辰没见着你，才拜托红豆把你唤来，就是想听听你的声音。”
[EN] "Brother and sister both slaving under Zhu Shuzhen—hopeless."
[ZH] 陈浩宇皱起眉头，心想：“没看出来，青瓜说起谎话来都不打草稿，以后我可得小心些。嘿嘿，这情话真够酸的！亏他想得出来！”
[EN] Qinggua disagreed. "You grew up rich, Young Master, so you don't know how hard poor folks have it. Poor families celebrate landing a servant post in a wealthy house, especially under a good mistress like Young Madam. Both siblings get to learn martial arts from her—half the manor envies them!"
[ZH] 窗外传来羞涩的声音：“我还以为你看上红豆姐姐，没想到你对我有心思，其实人家也喜欢你。”
[EN] "Shoo, shoo! Quit singing Zhu Shuzhen's praises in front of me! You're my man—don't let her buy you off!"
[ZH] 青瓜愣住，假情假意招来个真心的，平时真没看出丑丫对他有这种心思，一时竟然说不出话。
[EN] "Relax, Young Master. Loyal to you to the bone."
[ZH] 陈浩宇抑制不住兴奋，快速起身来到青瓜身边，小声说：“快说话啊，她对你有意思，事儿就好办。”
[EN] Right then a voice floated outside the boarded window. "Qinggua? Sister Hongdou said you wanted me?"
[ZH] 公子催促，青瓜不好不接：“我们两个算是两情相悦……”
[EN] Chouya was not truly hideous—just plump with narrow, squinting eyes, less pleasing to look at than the other maids, which had saddled her with the nickname Chouya, literally "Ugly Girl."
[ZH] 陈浩宇小声提醒：“快说正事。”
[EN] "She's here! Go turn on the charm!" Chen Haoyu whispered, dropping into a chair to watch the show.
[ZH] 青瓜趁机说：“我想见你，可我出不去，你能不能帮我打开门？”
[EN] For his master—and for the promise of winning Hongdou later—Qinggua braced himself and leaned close to the shutters. "Chouya, Young Madam locked me up in here, and it's been sixteen hours since I last saw you. I begged Hongdou to call you just so I could hear your voice."
[ZH] 丑丫犹豫一会儿：“我也想见你，可小姐把你关起来，我也没办法，要不等小姐回来，我去求她把你放出来？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu's brows shot up. Who knew Qinggua could spin smooth lies without blinking? Gotta watch this kid from now on—and ugh, what syrupy cheese!
[ZH] “没用的，少夫人肯定不会放我出去。也不知会把我关到什么时候？我何时才能见到你？丑丫，我想你。”
[EN] A bashful flutter answered from outside. "I thought you only had eyes for Sister Hongdou! Never dreamed you had feelings for me... truth is, I like you too."
[ZH] “我也想你。”
[EN] Qinggua froze stiff—fake flirting had reeled in real feelings! Struck dumb, he stood gaping.
[ZH] “我没犯什么错误，少夫人就把我关起来毫无道理，害得我们见不着对方……”
[EN] Thrilled, Chen Haoyu scrambled over and hissed in his ear, "Say something! She's into you—this is in the bag!"
[ZH] “青瓜，你等着，我这就把钥匙偷来，放你出来。”
[EN] Nudged forward, Qinggua stammered, "Then... we feel the same way about each other..."
[ZH] 两人听到丑丫离开的脚步声，高兴地击了一掌。
[EN] "Get to the point!" Chen Haoyu hissed.
[ZH] “公子，待会儿是我出去还是您出去？”
[EN] "I'm dying to see you, yet I'm trapped in here! Could you unlock the door for me?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇毫不犹豫：“当然是我出去，得好好想想出去后先找奶奶和爹娘做主？还是赶紧躲到外面？干脆直接躲到外面，躲到一个朱淑真找不到我的地方，就这么办！”
[EN] Chouya hesitated. "I want to see you too, yet Miss locked you up. What if I beg her to let you out when she gets back?"
[ZH] 很快两人听到开锁的声音，兴奋地冲到门口，瞅准时机准备跑出去。
[EN] "No use—Young Madam will never let me out! Who knows how long I'll rot in here without seeing your face? Chouya, I miss you."
[ZH] 只见丑丫推开门走进来，迅速把门关上，没有给他们留下任何逃跑的机会。
[EN] "I miss you too!"
[ZH] 丑丫见到青瓜立刻抱上去：“青瓜！我真的很开心！你竟然也喜欢我！”
[EN] "I didn't even do anything wrong, yet she threw me in here keeping us apart..."
[ZH] 青瓜被抱住，十分不自在，为了自由，只能忍了。
[EN] "Wait right there, Qinggua! I'll sneak the key and let you out!"
[ZH] 门是从里面关上，陈浩宇趁着丑丫情浓时，瞅准时机，打算拉开门闩逃出去。
[EN] Hearing her footsteps hurry off, master and servant slapped palms in triumph.
[ZH] 没想到丑丫抱着青瓜迅速挡住门：“姑爷想干什么？”
[EN] "So who sneaks out first, Young Master—me or you?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇惊叹朱淑真手底下的丫鬟实在不简单，笑道：“你要是不让我出去，我就让青瓜甩了你，他可是我身边的人，什么都听我的。”
[EN] "Me, obviously! Let's see—should I run straight to Grandmother and my parents, or bolt outside? Forget the elders, I'll bolt straight out of the manor and hide somewhere Zhu Shuzhen can never find me! Deal!"
[ZH] 丑丫不再搂着青瓜，而是拉住他的手：“姑爷别吓唬我，我和青瓜两情相悦，谁也拆不散！”
[EN] Minutes later the padlock rattled. Both sprang to the door ready to dash—only for Chouya to slip inside and slam the door shut behind her, cutting off every inch of escape.
[ZH] 陈浩宇哄道：“你要是把我放出去，我就同意青瓜和你在一起。”
[EN] The instant she saw Qinggua, Chouya threw her arms right around him. "Qinggua! I'm so happy you actually like me too!"
[ZH] 丑丫说：“姑爷同意不同意不重要，我只带青瓜出去，姑爷还是好好待在里边。”
[EN] Pinned in her bear hug, Qinggua stiffened, forcing himself to endure for freedom's sake.
[ZH] 青瓜见状说道：“丑丫，你放开我的手，我跟公子说句话。”
[EN] While Chouya was distracted, Chen Haoyu reached quietly for the door latch—only for Chouya to pivot, still hugging Qinggua, and block the door solid. "Going somewhere, Young Master?"
[ZH] 丑丫很听话，放开青瓜的手。
[EN] Impressed by Zhu Shuzhen's sharp maids, Chen Haoyu tried a bluff. "Don't let me out and I'll order Qinggua to dump you! He's my servant—he obeys my every word!"
[ZH] 青瓜走到公子身边，对着陈浩宇的耳朵悄悄说：“公子再忍忍，我先出去，在外面想办法把公子弄出去。”
[EN] Releasing the hug, Chouya clasped Qinggua's hand tight. "Don't try scaring me, Young Master. Qinggua and I love each other—nobody can tear us apart!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇点点头，对丑丫说：“好吧，你把青瓜带走，本公子好好享受被关起来的日子。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu switched to sweet-talking. "Let me walk out, and I give my blessing to you two!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇亲眼目睹门被打开，外面的阳光刚进来又消失，直到钥匙碰大锁的声音消失后，才快步走到门口，向外喊：“青瓜！别忘了找老太太！”
[EN] "Your blessing doesn't matter," Chouya replied flatly. "I'm only taking Qinggua out. You stay right in here, Young Master."
[ZH] 青瓜回应道：“公子放心！”
[EN] "Let go of my hand a second, Chouya," Qinggua said. "Let me whisper a word to Young Master."
[ZH] 门外，丑丫靠着青瓜不愿意松手。
[EN] Obediently, she let go. Leaning to Chen Haoyu's ear, Qinggua breathed, "Hang tight a bit longer, Young Master. Let me get out first and I'll figure out a way to spring you from outside!"
[ZH] 青瓜很无奈，劝道：“丑丫，你还是快把钥匙放回去，省得被人发现。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu nodded and waved to Chouya. "Fine, take Qinggua. I'll stay right here enjoying my lockup."
[ZH] 丑丫这才松开青瓜：“趁小姐不在，你去陈府西边的亭子里躲着等我，我先去西院把钥匙悄悄给阿升放回去。”
[EN] Watching the door open, sunlight flash in and vanish, and the padlock snap shut again, Chen Haoyu rushed to the crack yelling, "Qinggua! Don't forget to go straight to Old Madam Xia!"
[ZH] 丑丫离开后，青瓜一身轻松，迅速跑出墨香苑，朝老太太院子的方向跑去。刚跑到半路上，瞧见少夫人带苦荞迎面走来。
[EN] "Count on me, Young Master!" Qinggua called back.
[ZH] 青瓜被吓坏，赶紧躲到一个假山的后面，待少夫人与苦荞走远，探出身来，继续加快步伐去找老太太。
[EN] Outside, Chouya clung tight to his arm. Helpless, Qinggua coaxed, "Hurry and put the key back before anyone notices it's gone!"
[ZH] 朱家姑娘离开后，陈家的长辈们见老太太不愿继续刚才的话题，也都不再提及，仍旧聊家常，同时等待说话艺人到来。今儿三个儿子将生意上的事安排好，专门等着陪老太太听说书。
[EN] Only then did she let go. "While Miss is out, go hide in the west garden pavilion and wait for me! I'll slip the key back to Asheng in the West Courtyard first!"
[ZH] 青瓜匆忙闯进来，院中不见丫鬟，他只好壮着胆子快步进入屋内，扑倒在地上，挤出几滴眼泪：“老太太！快救救公子！他被少夫人关起来！不得自由！呜呜……！”青瓜故意将哭声放大。
[EN] The second Chouya hurried off, Qinggua sighed in relief and sprinted straight out of Moxiang Courtyard toward Old Madam Xia's rooms—nearly colliding halfway with Qianli Xun and Kuqiao walking back! Diving behind a rockery until they passed, he popped back out and bolted to Old Madam Xia's courtyard.
[ZH] 陈江木说：“浩宇的媳妇实在过分！母亲，趁现在人都在，您带着我们去浩宇的院子走走，让他娘子赶紧把人给放了。”
[EN] Inside, after Shuzhen left, the family had moved back to chatting while waiting for the storyteller; all three brothers had cleared their morning to stay and listen with their mother.
[ZH] 崔氏说：“是啊，母亲，还是过去帮帮浩宇吧？”
[EN] Bursting past an empty yard straight into the hall, Qinggua threw himself to the floor squeezing out loud wails. "Old Madam! Save Young Master! Young Madam locked him up! Won't let him breathe free air! Boo-hoo!"
[ZH] 老太太抬手阻止：“青瓜，不要哭，回去告诉浩宇，就说以后他院子里的事我与他爹娘一概不理会，他是好是坏听天由命。”
[EN] Chen Jiangmu frowned. "Haoyu's wife went too far! Mother, since we're all here, lead us over and tell her to let him out!"
[ZH] 青瓜惊得停止哭声，反应半晌，才说道：“老太太，您不能不管，少夫人的拳头厉害着呢，公子恐怕小命不保。”
[EN] "Yes, Mother, go help Haoyu!" Madam Cui urged.
[ZH] 陈江树和包氏更是乐得不行，表面依然装得平静；老太太不乐意管，正好让浩宇好好被收拾收拾。故而，他们两人只顾缄默不语。
[EN] Old Madam Xia lifted one hand to halt them. "Quit wailing, Qinggua. Go tell Haoyu that from today on, neither his parents nor I will meddle in his courtyard. Sink or swim, he's on his own."
[ZH] 青瓜见老太太不再言语，又转向陈江林：“老爷，公子可是您的亲儿子，您不能不管。”
[EN] Shocked dry of tears, Qinggua gaped before stammering, "You can't abandon him, Old Madam! Young Madam's fists are lethal—Young Master's life is in danger!"
[ZH] 陈江林说：“你告诉浩宇，我巴不得他被关起来，又怎么可能救他？他自己做下错事，只好自作自受。”
[EN] Secretly thrilled, Chen Jiangshu and Madam Bao kept smooth poker faces and stayed dead silent—let Haoyu take his beating!
[ZH] 青瓜又向夫人求救：“夫人……”
[EN] Turning to Chen Jianglin, Qinggua begged, "Master, he's your own flesh and blood! You can't stand by!"
[ZH] 青瓜话还未说，姚轻琰狠狠心，阻止道：“不必再说！浩宇是死是活，他娘子说了算，以后不要再拿墨香苑的事儿烦我。”
[EN] "Tell Haoyu I'm thrilled she locked him up," Chen Jianglin said coldly. "Why would I rescue him? He made his bed—let him lie in it."
[ZH] 老太太立刻命令：“来人！”
[EN] Desperate, Qinggua swung toward Yao Qingyan. "Madam—"
[ZH] 进来两名下人。
[EN] Hardening her heart, Yao Qingyan cut him off. "Not another word! Live or die, Haoyu answers to his wife now. Don't bother me with Moxiang Courtyard's business again!"
[ZH] “把青瓜带出去。”
[EN] "Servants!" Old Madam Xia snapped. Two men stepped in. "Escort Qinggua out!"
[ZH] 青瓜被两名下人拖拽着离开老太太的院子。刚到院外，正好说话艺人前来。他在外面等了会儿，直到里面传出说话艺人说书的声音，才叹口气离开。
[EN] Dragged outside just as the storyteller arrived, Qinggua lingered until the clapper sounded and laughter rose within, then trudged off with a heavy sigh.

#CH 231
[ZH] 从老太太的院子回来，千里寻刚走进望月轩，碰见丑丫面带桃花急急匆匆而来。
[ZH] 她见到小姐小心拜见：“小姐。”
[ZH] “怎么着急忙慌的？”
[ZH] 丑丫心虚，撒谎道：“没……没什么，我还有事，不打扰小姐。”
[ZH] 看着丑丫慌张离开，千里寻纳闷：“丑丫今日怎么了？”
[ZH] 苦荞说：“看上去像是担心您批评她。”
[ZH] 千里寻开玩笑道：“不对，我怎么觉得她好像是急着私会情郎。”
[ZH] 两人议论完，并未当回事。
[ZH] 刚走到素心草堂门口，想起陈浩宇，也该实施报复计划。
[ZH] “你去把博雅斋的钥匙取来。”
[ZH] 苦荞去西院拿钥匙，千里寻则往后院拐去。
[ZH] 来到后院，她先看向静宜堂，门紧闭，韦嬷嬷应该一大早出去忙活。千里寻从心底感激这位嬷嬷，她悄悄为自己做了许多事，事事都做在她的心上。
[ZH] 然后才面向博雅斋，关着陈浩宇也不是长久之计，再说关着也不能泄心头之恨，今日不如与他好好聊聊，说一说折磨他的方法。
[ZH] 很快苦荞将钥匙取来，上前打开大锁……
[ZH] 青瓜出去后，陈浩宇一直坐在厅堂等着。
[EN] Returning from Old Madam Xia's courtyard into Moxiang Courtyard, Qianli Xun ran straight into Chouya rushing past with rosy, glowing cheeks.
[ZH] 听到有人开锁，一阵高兴，以为是奶奶前来做主，要求朱淑真放他出去。他快速起身凑到门口，期待看到老太太心疼他的目光。
[EN] "Miss," Chouya curtsied nervously.
[ZH] 结果门被打开，走进来的只有朱淑真和丫鬟苦荞。
[EN] "What's the rush?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇朝门外再瞧瞧，一个人影都没有。
[EN] Flustered, Chouya fibbed, "N-nothing! Got chores to do—won't keep you!" and scampered off.
[ZH] 千里寻不屑地问：“怎么？还希望陈家长辈来救你？告诉你，他们已经把管教你的权力交给我，老娘正好趁此机会好好折磨你。”
[EN] "What's gotten into Chouya today?" Qianli Xun wondered aloud.
[ZH] “你胡说！奶奶那么疼我，我娘也心软，怎么可能让你管教我？”陈浩宇认为朱淑真不过是欺骗他而已，门并未被关上，他打算趁机跑出去。
[EN] "Looked scared you'd scold her," Kuqiao guessed.
[ZH] 结果被千里寻挡在面前：“想跑？没那么容易，今儿这门敞开着，只要我在，你甭想走出去。”
[EN] "Nah—looked more like she's rushing off to meet a secret boyfriend!" Qianli Xun joked, dismissing it.
[ZH] 陈浩宇气得用食指指着朱淑真，说不出话来。干脆转身坐到椅子上，平复心情后，问道：“你倒是说说，怎么样才能放我出去？”
[EN] Reaching Suxin Thatched Hall, she remembered Chen Haoyu—time to roll out her payback schedule. "Go fetch the key to Boya Study."
[ZH] “我今儿来，就是打算放你出去。”
[EN] While Kuqiao ran to the West Courtyard, Qianli Xun strolled into the back courtyard.
