Glancing first at Jingyi Hall's shut door—Matron Wei out handling business as usual—gratitude warmed her chest for everything the matron quietly did behind the scenes. Then she faced Boya Study. Locking Chen Haoyu up forever solved nothing and didn't even scratch her itch for payback; better lay out her terms face to face.
[ZH] 尽管希望如此，陈浩宇还是一脸不相信：“你会这么好？”
[EN] Kuqiao returned with the key and clicked open the heavy padlock.
[ZH] “我还真没有那么好。”千里寻向苦荞吩咐，“我边说，你边记。”
[EN] Inside, Chen Haoyu had sat waiting for Qinggua's rescue. At the click of the lock, he leaped up and hurried to the door expecting his grandmother's doting face—only to find Zhu Shuzhen and Kuqiao standing alone on the threshold, not another soul behind them.
[ZH] 苦荞走到书桌前坐下，展开纸认真听小姐说的重要信息。
[EN] Qianli Xun shot him a smirk. "Still waiting for the Chen elders to ride to your rescue? Hate to break it to you, they handed me full authority to discipline you—and I plan to put it to great use making your life miserable."
[ZH] 千里寻又面向陈浩宇：“打今儿起，你可以走出博雅斋，但不能走出陈府。”
[EN] "Liar! Grandmother dotes on me and Mother's softhearted—no way they handed me over to you!" Seeing the door wide open, he lunged to bolt past her.
[ZH] 陈浩宇怒道：“凭什么？”
[EN] Qianli Xun stepped squarely across the doorway. "Running? Dream on. That door can stay propped open all day, and while I stand here, you aren't taking one step out."
[ZH] “凭你打不过我，凭陈家长辈把管教你的权力交给我。”
[EN] Shaking a furious finger at her speechless, Chen Haoyu spun back and slumped into a chair. Catching his breath, he snapped, "Fine—what does it take for you to let me out?"
[ZH] “我才不信你的一面之词！”
[EN] "I came here right now to let you out."
[ZH] “信不信由你，识相的乖乖配合，认真听老娘说话。”
[EN] Suspicion narrowed his eyes. "You're being that generous?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇捂住耳朵：“不听！不听！就不听！看你能把我怎么的！”
[EN] "Hardly generous." Qianli Xun nodded to Kuqiao. "I dictate, you write."
[ZH] 千里寻甚觉聒噪，迅速上前点了陈浩宇的穴位，他立刻不能说话也无法动弹，只能通过表情表达自己的不满。
[EN] Kuqiao sat at the desk and spread a sheet of paper.
[ZH] “这下你总算安静，我可以好好说说如何管教你。首先，取消你所有月钱，要想有钱花，必须自己努力赚取……”
[EN] Facing Chen Haoyu, Qianli Xun declared, "Starting today, you may leave Boya Study—just not Chen Manor."
[ZH] 陈浩宇口不能说话，拳头攥紧，心想：“哼！连二十两的月钱都取消，实在过分！本公子长这么大，从来都是衣来伸手，饭来张口，什么时候自己赚过银子？”
[EN] "Says who?!"
[ZH] “其次，给你列出几条赚取银子的方法。其一，每日绕陈府的主路跑十圈儿，可得十文；其二，每日完成背诵诸子百家的文章和诗词任务，可得三十文；其三，每日完成一件劳动任务，可得五十文；其四，向你欺负过的人说一声‘对不起’可得一两银子……”
[EN] "Says my fists, and says the Chen elders who put me in charge of whipping you into shape."
[ZH] 以上要求，都是千里寻临时起意想出，折磨中有教育，教育中有折磨，她甚是满意，因为这四条都是陈浩宇不喜欢做的事。还想继续添加，一时想不起来：“先说四条，后面想起什么来，再随时添加。苦荞，可记好？”
[EN] "I don't buy one word of your story!"
[ZH] 苦荞放下笔：“记得清清楚楚。”
[EN] "Believe it or not, be smart and listen close."
[ZH] “好，让陈浩宇在上面按下指印。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu clapped both hands over his ears. "Not listening! Not listening! Do your worst!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不能动弹，任由朱淑真掰开他的拳头，在苦荞拿来的印朱里摩擦两下，又在纸上狠狠按下。他心中那个气，无处宣泄，脸憋得通红。
[EN] Fed up with his racket, Qianli Xun stepped in and tapped two acupoints. Instantly he froze mute and rigid, reduced to glaring daggers.
[ZH] 完毕后，千里寻点开陈浩宇的穴位。
[EN] "Peace and quiet at last. Now listen to how I'm disciplining you. First, your entire monthly allowance is canceled. Want spending money? Earn it yourself."
[ZH] 陈浩宇二话不说，用拳头向朱淑真袭击而去：“朱淑真！你说的那些本公子一件都不会做！”
[EN] Unable to speak, Chen Haoyu's fists clenched white inside his sleeves: Canceling even twenty taels?! Outrageous! Grown up with robes handed to me and meals spoon-fed—when have I ever earned silver?!
[ZH] 千里寻迅速拿住陈浩宇，将他的手臂在背后拧住：“你倒提醒我，不做是吧？还有惩罚，一件不做替老娘倒一日马桶！二件不做替老娘倒两日马桶！全部不做替老娘连续倒十日马桶！若负隅顽抗，就替老娘倒一辈子马桶！”
[EN] "Second, here is your wage scale. Item one: run ten laps around Chen Manor's main road daily for ten wen. Item two: memorize assigned classics and poetry daily for thirty wen. Item three: complete one daily chore for fifty wen. Item four: say 'I'm sorry' to someone you've bullied for one tael of silver."
[ZH] 陈浩宇忍着痛说：“我们陈家茅厕多，又干净，上大号都去那里，根本没有马桶，只有夜壶，气死你！”
[EN] Improvised on the spot—half torture, half boot camp, every task guaranteed to make Chen Haoyu crawl out of his skin—she beamed in satisfaction. "Four items to start; I'll add more as they occur to me. Got all that, Kuqiao?"
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“这还不简单，为惩罚你，大不了老娘往后用马桶专门解大号，臭死你！”
[EN] Kuqiao laid down her brush. "Every word."
[ZH] 陈浩宇占不上任何便宜，打又打不过，骂又骂不过，不如装得态度好些：“朱淑真，我向你道歉好不好？你就别跟我计较，散播流言是我不对，要打要骂随你，只是别把我打出残疾来。”
[EN] "Good. Get his thumbprint on it."
[ZH] 对方态度好转，千里寻松开手：“苦荞，去把红豆叫来。”
[EN] Paralyzed, Chen Haoyu could only turn purple with rage as Qianli Xun pried his thumb open, pressed it into the red ink paste Kuqiao held out, and stamped it firm onto the contract.
[ZH] 苦荞在按有姑爷指印的纸张上添加完小姐刚刚说的，放下笔，赶快出去找红豆。
[EN] Done, she tapped open his acupoints.
[ZH] 博雅斋的门不会再锁上，陈浩宇也终于放心，坐回椅子上：“朱淑真，本公子这辈子最后悔的事情就是娶你，你呢？是不是也后悔嫁给本公子？”
[EN] The instant feeling flooded back, Chen Haoyu swung a wild punch at her. "Zhu Shuzhen! I'm not doing a single one of those things!"
[ZH] 自从决定嫁给陈浩宇，千里寻告诫自己，以后无论发生什么，千万不能后悔。因此她从未想过后悔之事：“老娘后不后悔关你屁事！”
[EN] Catching his wrist effortlessly, Qianli Xun twisted his arm up behind his back. "Glad you reminded me—forgot the penalties! Skip one task, empty my chamber pot for one day! Skip two, two days! Skip all four, ten straight days! Resist altogether, and you empty my chamber pot for life!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇臆测道：“你肯定也后悔，不如我们和离，你也好自由自在。再说仪王对你有意思，我看他对你那个痴情的样子，绝对不会嫌弃你和离过两次，我们两个不如心平气和商量商量和离之事？”
[EN] "Ow! Chen Manor has spotless latrines everywhere—nobody uses chamber pots for number two, only urinals! Joke's on you!" he yelped through the pain.
[ZH] 听到“和离”两个字，千里寻心中莫名悲伤。本想着第一次和离之后能获得美满姻缘，谁知再次嫁的人又开口提出这两个字。莫说她没想过，就是想过，也没有了第一次和离时的冲劲儿，也许是因为第一次和离耗去她大部分心力。
[EN] Qianli Xun grinned wickedly. "Easy fix! Just to punish you, I'll start using a chamber pot exclusively for number two—stink you right out of your boots!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇见朱淑真表现出悲伤的神情，自然开心，故意调侃：“你该不会喜欢上本公子？不舍得离开？也是，本公子玉树临风，人见人爱，花见花开，你喜欢倒十分正常，既然喜欢我，就不要折磨我，好好待我，说不定我一高兴也喜欢上你，呵呵……！”
[EN] Outmatched in muscle and outmatched in wit, Chen Haoyu tried softening his tone. "Look, Zhu Shuzhen, what if I just apologize right now? Let it go! Spreading rumors was my fault—hit me, curse me, do whatever, just don't cripple me!"
[ZH] 千里寻从悲伤的情绪中清醒过来，对方调侃的表情和语气，令她厌恶：“闭上你的臭嘴！告诉你，你想和离，老娘偏不如你的愿，等老娘哪天把你折磨够，气消了再考虑要不要跟你和离的事儿。”
[EN] Seeing him fold, Qianli Xun let go. "Kuqiao, go call Hongdou."
[ZH] 陈浩宇本想发怒，又觉发怒无用，干脆继续调侃：“听说你跟林觉和离时使了老鼻子劲儿，现在本公子主动要求和离，你却不同意，不是喜欢本公子又是什么？本公子对美女来者不拒，要不主动投怀送抱一个？”
[EN] Jotting down the chamber-pot penalty on the thumbprinted sheet, Kuqiao hurried off to summon Hongdou.
[ZH] 千里寻向来最讨厌男人的色相，遇见必打无疑，伸出拳头一拳打在陈浩宇的脸上……
[EN] Relieved the door stayed unlocked, Chen Haoyu sank back into his chair. "Zhu Shuzhen, my biggest regret in life is marrying you. What about you? Regret marrying me?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇的左半边脸立刻一片乌青，他捂着脸：“你是不是有病？”
[EN] Ever since choosing to walk down the aisle, Qianli Xun had forbidden herself from wasting breath on regret. "Whether I regret it is none of your damn business!"
[ZH] 千里寻收起拳头：“你才有病！没事调侃老娘，老娘最讨厌你刚才的样子！”
[EN] "Bet you regret it too," Chen Haoyu probed. "Why don't we get a mutual divorce and set you free? Prince Yi's clearly crazy about you—infatuated as he is, he won't care if you've divorced twice! Why not sit down calm and hash out a mutual divorce?"
[ZH] 手中的汗接触到伤口，蜇得慌，陈浩宇不得放下手：“就你这样儿！也不知道乔文仙和仪王喜欢你什么？”
[EN] The words "mutual divorce" struck an unexpected hollow note in her chest. After her first divorce she had dreamed of a happy match, only for her second husband to bring up divorce right out of the gate. Not that she wanted one—she simply had no energy left for that war; her first divorce had drained every ounce of fight out of her.
[ZH] 红豆和苦荞进来，她们见姑爷的脸上一片淤青，甚是滑稽，心中不觉偷乐。
[EN] Spotting sadness cross her face, Chen Haoyu lit up and teased, "Wait—don't tell me you fell for me and can't bear leaving? Makes sense, tall and handsome as I am, beloved by every maiden in town! Since you like me, quit torturing me and treat me sweet—maybe I'll get in a good mood and like you back! Hahaha!"
[ZH] 苦荞到书桌旁去拿写有条款的纸张；红豆则问：“小姐，有何吩咐？”
[EN] Snapping out of her gloom, disgust flared hot at his leering smirk. "Shut your filthy mouth! Want a divorce? Too bad—I'm refusing just to spite you! Maybe once I've tortured you raw and my grudge cools, I'll think about it."
[ZH] “你派两个人，从现在起跟着陈浩宇，负责监督他，每日让他按照纸上写好的一一完成。”
[EN] Too smart to blow up, he kept needle-poking. "Heard you fought tooth and nail to divorce Lin Jue, yet now I hand you a divorce on a silver platter and you say no? If that isn't secret love, what is? I never turn down a pretty woman—go ahead, throw yourself in my arms!"
[ZH] “是，我这就去安排。”
[EN] Nothing set Qianli Xun's fist flying faster than a sleazy smirk. Crack! One clean punch landed square on Chen Haoyu's left cheek.
[ZH] 苦荞把纸张给了红豆：“都在上面写着，你让监督的下人看后记在心里，然后再拿给我。”
[EN] Clutching a bruised face swelling blue on the spot, he gasped, "Are you crazy?!"
[ZH] 红豆接过纸张，出去安排人。
[EN] Qianli Xun shook out her knuckles. "You're the crazy one! Keep flirting like a creep—I hate that leering mug more than anything!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇问：“我现在是不是可以出去？”
[EN] Sweat stinging the fresh welt forced his hand down. "Acting like a monster like this—heaven knows what Qiao Wenxian and Prince Yi ever saw in you!"
[ZH] 千里寻说：“现在还不行，等负责监督你的下人来了再说。”
[EN] Right then Hongdou and Kuqiao stepped in, struggling to stifle grins at the comic purple shiner blooming across his face.
[ZH] 陈浩宇心想只要不被锁着便好，他一旦出去先找奶奶哭诉一番。
[EN] Kuqiao retrieved the rule sheet while Hongdou asked, "Your orders, Miss?"
[ZH] 想到找奶奶，不免心中疑惑，为何青瓜出去这么久还未回来？按说他不可能不去找老太太，既然去找了老太太为何到现在还没来？难道陈家的长辈真的被朱淑真收买？
[EN] "Assign two men to shadow Chen Haoyu starting now. Make sure he completes every task on this sheet daily."
[ZH] “奶奶和我爹娘果真让你管教我？”
[EN] "Yes, Miss—right away."
[ZH] 千里寻骄傲道：“那是当然，长辈们还说，他们绝不插手此事。”
[EN] Kuqiao handed the sheet to Hongdou. "Have the watchers memorize every line, then bring it back to me." Hongdou hurried off.
[ZH] “我不信！”
[EN] "Can I walk out now?" Chen Haoyu asked.
[ZH] “信不信由你。我既然敢把你放出来，就不怕你到长辈面前哭诉。”
[EN] "Not until your shadows arrive."
[ZH] 红豆带着阿升和一名男仆前来：“小姐，我把阿升和三宝带来，以后由他们两个负责监督姑爷。”
[EN] So long as the padlock stayed off, Chen Haoyu figured he could run straight to Grandmother and weep his case. Wait—why hadn't Qinggua come back yet? Had Zhu Shuzhen truly bought off the entire Chen clan? "Did Grandmother and my parents really put you in charge of disciplining me?"
[ZH] 红豆把手中的纸张还给苦荞，小声说：“他们都已记下。”
[EN] "Obviously!" Qianli Xun lifted her chin. "Promised not to meddle one bit, too."
[ZH] 苦荞点点头。
[EN] "Don't believe you!"
[ZH] 千里寻对眼前的两个下人十分满意，嘱咐道：“你们两个记着，每日紧跟着姑爷，不准让他走出陈府，当然在陈府内他想去哪里不必限制。白天可以走出博雅斋，到了晚上，他睡下后依旧给门上好锁，以免他趁大家睡着跑出去。”
[EN] "Suit yourself. Think I'd unlock your door if I feared you running crying to the elders?"
[ZH] 两名下人回道：“谨遵小姐吩咐。”
[EN] Hongdou returned with Asheng and another male servant. "Miss, Asheng and Sanbao will watch Young Master from here on out." Returning the paper to Kuqiao, she added, "They memorized every word."
[ZH] 一切安排妥当，千里寻朝陈浩宇“哼”一声：“你可以出去了。”
[EN] Satisfied, Qianli Xun instructed both men. "Stick to him like glue all day. Do not let him step outside Chen Manor's gates—inside the manor, he can roam wherever he wants. Days out of Boya Study are fine, yet once he goes to bed at night, lock the door so he doesn't slip out in the dark."
[ZH] 陈浩宇二话不说，跑着跳出门外，慨叹道：“自由的空气真好！”
[EN] "Understood, Miss!" both chorused.
[ZH] 他转过头：“朱淑真，你等着，我现在就去找奶奶！”
[EN] Everything set, Qianli Xun gave Chen Haoyu a cool scoff. "You're free to go."
[ZH] 阿升和三宝紧跟姑爷向前院走去。
[EN] Chen Haoyu shot out the door with a whoop. "Sweet air of freedom!" Spinning back, he jabbed a finger at her. "Just wait, Zhu Shuzhen—I'm heading straight for Grandmother!"
[ZH] 报复计划已开始实施，千里寻却没有多么开心，只因陈浩宇提到和离，提到仪王，她又想起梦容。她之所以愿意嫁给陈浩宇，不愿走上和离之路，多半是因为她。
[EN] With Asheng and Sanbao hot on his heels, he marched toward the front yard.
[ZH] 抢亲那日梦容说出恨她的话依然萦绕在耳边，这恨，令她望而却步，不敢对心中的感情有任何奢望。
[EN] Her revenge campaign had launched, yet Qianli Xun felt strangely flat. Chen Haoyu mentioning divorce and Prince Yi had brought Mengrong right back into her mind—the real reason she had accepted this marriage and refused divorce. Mengrong's furious vow of hatred from the wedding day still echoed in her ears, killing any dare to hope for her own heart.
[ZH] 苦荞陪小姐回到素心草堂，小姐不想说话，也不写话本，坐在书桌前发呆。苦荞则将手中的纸张收起来，默默站在一旁陪伴。
[EN] Following her back into Suxin Thatched Hall, Kuqiao put away the rule sheet and stood quietly beside her mistress as Qianli Xun stared blankly at her untouched desk.

#CH 232
[ZH] 青瓜找老太太替公子出头失败而归后，不敢进入墨香苑，又将与丑丫相约一事抛之脑后，干脆在陈府的园子里转悠转悠，等天黑后，再回墨香苑。
[ZH] 转着转着，来到西边，一抬头，瞥见丑丫正站在亭子里，想起相约之事。心中念道：“真是偷鸡不成蚀把米，没帮成公子，把自己给搭进去。还是赶快离开，只要我不去约会，丑丫自然明白我的心思。”
[ZH] 青瓜打算往墨香苑的方向走，准备在外面找个地方躲起来，瞅准机会回去向公子说明情况。
[ZH] 他来到墨香苑门口，瞅来瞅去，两边的翠竹实在好，藏在后面不容易被人发现。
[ZH] 刚藏起来没多久，竟然见公子走出来，身边跟着阿升和三宝。青瓜心想，既然公子被少夫人放出来，说明偷跑出来已无事，还躲个什么劲儿？
[ZH] 他从翠竹后面跳出来：“公子，少夫人放您出来了？”
[ZH] 青瓜出去半天，没见长辈来解救，陈浩宇认为青瓜失职，刚要用脚去踢，想到青瓜会幻影闪移，踢了也是白踢，不得已收回脚：“你早早出来，只顾自己自由自在，却不顾本公子，看我不揍你！”
[ZH] 陈浩宇伸出拳头要去打青瓜。
[ZH] 青瓜躲到一边：“公子这不出来了吗？就别生气了。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇没打着，收起拳头：“出来有什么用？还不是被他俩跟着？”
[ZH] 青瓜瞧了瞧阿升和三宝，笑道：“总比关在屋里强。”
[ZH] “少废话，陪本公子去找老太太告状！”陈浩宇说着就要走。
[ZH] 青瓜拦住：“公子还是别去，我已经去过，老太太说以后公子院子里的事她一概不管；大老爷说巴不得您被关起来，还说您是自作自受；夫人说您是死是活是少夫人的事，以后少拿墨香苑的事烦她。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇认为青瓜胡说，一把推开他，向奶奶的院子而去。
[ZH] 说话艺人已离开，老二老三也带着各自夫人回屋；陈江林还想再陪老太太，又与姚轻琰多坐会儿。
[ZH] 三人正说话，丫鬟来报：“老太太，二公子被放出来，正朝这边走来。”
[ZH] 老太太吩咐道：“去，把大门关上，顺便告诉他，以后不要再来烦我。”
[ZH] “是。”丫鬟领命而去。
[ZH] 老太太观察儿媳，她面露担忧之色，便耐心叮嘱道：“你们两个回去，也把院门关好，不要心软，心稍微一软，恐怕会前功尽弃。”
[ZH] 老太太摸摸自己的胸口：“我这个心啊，难受啊，为了浩宇好，得忍住。”
[ZH] 姚轻琰也努力忍着，不说话。
[ZH] 陈江林说：“都怪儿子没把浩宇教育好，让母亲和轻琰跟着为难。成败在此一举，我们都得狠下心来。”
[ZH] 老太太和姚轻琰都点头认可。
[ZH] 陈浩宇来到奶奶的院子门口，见院门紧闭，他命令青瓜上前敲门。
[ZH] 青瓜按照吩咐做了，门没开，里面传出丫鬟的声音：“二公子还是回去吧，以后你的事老太太不会再管。”
[EN] Turned away empty-handed from Old Madam Xia's rooms, Qinggua dared not return to Moxiang Courtyard—and clean forgot his date with Chouya—instead wandering the manor gardens planning to sneak home after dark.
[ZH] 陈浩宇气道：“你个下人敢这么跟本公子说话，小心我明日让奶奶把你发卖了！”
[EN] Strolling into the west wing, he looked up to spot Chouya waiting inside the pavilion and froze. Oh no—tried helping Young Master and got myself hooked instead! Better ghost her; once I no-show, she'll get the hint!
[ZH] 里面传出声音：“奴婢哪敢？是老太太吩咐我这么说的。”
[EN] Creeping back toward Moxiang Courtyard to wait for an opening, he slipped behind the lush green bamboo flanking the gate—and barely seconds later watched Chen Haoyu stride out with Asheng and Sanbao trailing behind!
[ZH] 陈浩宇装出哭腔，向里喊道：“奶奶！您不能不管我！母老虎虽把我放出来，可她狠着呢！不仅派两个人时时刻刻跟着我，还罚我做好多事情，最过分的是竟然让我给她倒马桶！呜呜！您若不管我，恐怕孙儿连命都没了！呜呜……！”
[EN] If Young Madam let him out, sneaking out didn't matter anymore! Qinggua jumped out from the stalks. "Young Master, Young Madam released you?"
[ZH] 听到让儿子倒马桶，姚轻琰忍不住说：“淑真怎么能让浩宇做这种事？浩宇从小连洗脸水都没自己倒过，如何能做粗鄙之事？”
[EN] Fuming over waiting half the day with zero rescue, Chen Haoyu cocked a boot—remembered Phantom Sidestep, pulled his foot back, and swung a fist instead. "Sneaking out to enjoy yourself while leaving me locked up? I'll clobber you!"
[ZH] 老太太也有些忍不住：“使不得！使不得！”
[EN] Qinggua hopped clear. "You're out now, Young Master—let it go!"
[ZH] 刚站起来想出去，犹豫后又坐下：“唉，忍着吧，去，给我拿些棉花来，把耳朵堵住。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu lowered his fist. "What good is being out with these two glued to my tail?"
[ZH] 丫鬟拿来棉花，帮老太太把耳朵堵住。
[EN] "Still beats staring at four walls," Qinggua grinned.
[ZH] 孙子的哭声还是会传进耳朵，她只能继续狠下心来。
[EN] "Quit yakking and come with me to Grandmother's!" Chen Haoyu started off.
[ZH] 老太太如此，姚轻琰不好再说什么，心中告诫自己：“忍一忍就好。”
[EN] Qinggua stepped in front of him. "Save your steps, Young Master—I already went! Old Madam Xia said she's staying out of your courtyard for good; Master said he's thrilled you're locked up and you asked for it; Madam said live or die, you're Young Madam's problem now!"
[ZH] 只有陈江林泰然自若：“吃得苦中苦方为人上人，浩宇就是吃得苦少，才养成纨绔的习性，再这么下去，恐怕还比不上掏茅厕的人，倒马桶都是便宜他。”
[EN] Convinced Qinggua was talking trash, Chen Haoyu shoved past him and marched straight to Old Madam Xia's gate.
[ZH] 没见里面有动静，陈浩宇继续装哭：“呜呜！奶奶！朱淑真就是为了报复我，昨日她还让丫鬟红豆扇了我两巴掌，到现在脸还疼，呜呜……！您不管我，难道想让我死在她的手中不成？就刚才，她还用拳头打我，到现在我的脸上还有淤青，她连药都不愿给我涂，还说以后我的月钱全部取消！呜呜……！”
[EN] Inside, after the storyteller left and Second and Third Brothers retired with their wives, Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan lingered chatting with Old Madam Xia when a maid hurried in. "Old Madam, Second Young Master is out and headed this way!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇又装哭半晌，喊了半晌，里面竟一丝声音没有，才意识到奶奶真的不再管他。
[EN] "Shut the courtyard gate," Old Madam Xia commanded. "Tell him to quit bothering me."
[ZH] 他不再装哭，大声向里喊道：“奶奶既然不愿替我做主！我去找我娘！”
[EN] "Yes, Old Madam."
[ZH] 里面传出丫鬟的声音：“大老爷和夫人就在老太太房中，二公子不必去找，他们让我告诉你，二公子还是好好回去接受少夫人管教。”
[EN] Seeing distress flicker across Yao Qingyan's face, Old Madam Xia added sternly, "Lock your own courtyard gate when you go back too. Don't go soft—soften one inch and everything collapses." She pressed a hand to her chest. "Hurts right here, believe me, yet we have to endure for Haoyu's own good."
[ZH] 所有的希望落空，陈浩宇才觉出朱淑真的厉害，尤其是收买人心这招，使得实在高明。
[EN] Yao Qingyan bit her lip and stayed silent.
[ZH] 阿升提醒道：“姑爷，该跑步了，如果不跑恐怕您得给小姐倒马桶。”
[EN] "My fault for failing to raise him right and putting you both through this," Chen Jianglin said. "Make or break right here—we all have to hold firm." Both women nodded.
[ZH] 陈浩宇使出一拳打向阿升：“你个下人！敢管本公子？”
[EN] Arriving to find the gate bolted shut, Chen Haoyu had Qinggua knock. A maid answered through the wood: "Go back, Second Young Master. Old Madam won't meddle in your affairs anymore."
[ZH] 阿升挡住姑爷的拳头：“姑爷还是省省吧。”
[EN] "A lowly maid talking back to me?" Chen Haoyu roared. "Wait till I have Grandmother sell you off tomorrow!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不甘心，又去打三宝。三宝迅速躲开：“姑爷，没用的。”
[EN] "This servant wouldn't dare—Old Madam ordered me to say it."
[ZH] 家人不管，两个下人又打不过，一生气又向青瓜踢去……
[EN] Switching tactics, Chen Haoyu put on a shrill wail toward the gate. "Grandmother! You can't abandon me! Sure the tigress unlocked my room, yet she's cruel as a demon! Assigned two thugs to tail me nonstop, piled chores on my head—and worst of all, she's forcing me to empty her chamber pot! Boo-hoo! Save me or I'm a dead man!"
[ZH] 青瓜迅速闪开，因一时没思想准备，着急中不小心推公子一把。陈浩宇向一旁趔趄，差点儿倒在地上。
[EN] At "empty her chamber pot," Yao Qingyan flinched. "How can Shuzhen make Haoyu do something that filthy? He's never even poured his own washbasin!"
[ZH] “青瓜！你是不是不想活了？敢推本公子？”
[EN] Old Madam Xia surged half off her couch. "Unthinkable! Unthinkable!" Then she froze and sank back down. "Hold firm... bring me cotton to plug my ears!"
[ZH] 青瓜意识到做错事，赶紧道歉：“我不是故意的，一时失手，公子别往心里去。”
[EN] A maid stuffed cotton into the old lady's ears, yet her grandson's howling still bled through, forcing her to clench her teeth. Seeing Old Madam Xia hold the line, Yao Qingyan swallowed her plea. Only Chen Jianglin sat calm as stone. "No pain, no gain. Spoiling him turned him rotten—at this rate he'd be worth less than a night-soil collector! Emptying a chamber pot is getting off easy."
[ZH] 被自己的下人推，陈浩宇哪能不介意？上前打算抓住青瓜揍一顿，以泄心头之愤。
[EN] Getting zero response, Chen Haoyu bawled louder. "Grandmother! Zhu Shuzhen's just out for revenge! Had her maid Hongdou slap my face twice yesterday—still throbs! Just now she punched me right in the cheek, bruised black and blue without even giving me ointment, and canceled my allowance too! Boo-hoo!"
[ZH] 结果被阿升挡住：“姑爷还是不要再耽搁时间，快些跑步吧。”
[EN] Still dead silence. Realizing Grandmother meant business, he dropped the sobbing and shouted, "Fine! If Grandmother won't back me, I'm going to my mother!"
[ZH] 青瓜心中一阵感激，否则今日绝对会被公子一顿乱拳攻击。
[EN] The maid's voice drifted back through the gate. "Master and Madam are right inside with Old Madam, Second Young Master. They told me to say: go back and take Young Madam's discipline like a man."
[ZH] 无人替他做主，连下人也打不过，陈浩宇第一次感到无助，不由蹲在地上。
[EN] Every lifeline snapped. For the first time, Chen Haoyu felt the full chill of Zhu Shuzhen's skill at winning hearts.
[ZH] 青瓜见状，凑到跟前，关切地问：“公子，您怎么了？”
[EN] "Time for your laps, Young Master," Asheng reminded him. "Skip them and it's chamber-pot duty."
[ZH] 结果被陈浩宇一巴掌打在脸上。
[EN] Chen Haoyu threw a punch at Asheng. "A servant bossing me around?!"
[ZH] 青瓜捂住脸：“公子，您又欺负我！”
[EN] Asheng blocked it clean. "Save your breath, Young Master."
[ZH] 憋在心中的气通过一巴掌得到宣泄，陈浩宇站起来：“本公子不欺负你，欺负谁？难道欺负他们两个？”
[EN] Swinging at Sanbao next, he hit empty air as Sanbao ducked clear. "No use, Young Master."
[ZH] 青瓜一脸委屈。
[EN] Abandoned by family and outmatched by servants, he vented his frustration by booting toward Qinggua—who glided sideways so fast in panic that he accidentally shoved Chen Haoyu off balance, nearly sending him sprawling.
[ZH] 想到臭烘烘的马桶，陈浩宇仿佛都已闻到臭气，一阵恶心。好汉不吃眼前亏，还是乖乖跑步。
[EN] "Qinggua! Tired of breathing?! Daring to shove me?!"
[ZH] “喂！陈府这么大，本公子怎么跑？”
[EN] "Accident! Slipped, Young Master—don't hold it against me!"
[ZH] 阿升说：“我在前面带路，三宝后面跟着，姑爷在我俩中间，跟着我跑就行。”
[EN] Boiling at being pushed by his own servant, Chen Haoyu lunged to pummel him, only for Asheng to step square between them. "Don't waste time, Young Master. Start running."
[ZH] 陈浩宇不愿放过青瓜，命令道：“你！过来陪本公子一起跑！”
[EN] Qinggua breathed silent thanks as Chen Haoyu—beaten on every front—squatted helplessly on the cobblestones.
[ZH] 青瓜为难道：“公子，我不喜欢跑步，太累。”
[EN] Stepping closer in concern, Qinggua asked, "Are you all right, Young Master?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇批评道：“你看看人家，不是我的下人却陪我跑，你是我的下人却当缩头乌龟，赶紧的，否则我可不帮你追姑娘。”
[EN] Smack! Chen Haoyu slapped his cheek.
[ZH] 为了红豆，青瓜豁出去：“公子都愿意跑，我哪敢说个不字，陪您就是。”
[EN] Clutching his face, Qinggua yelped, "Picking on me again, Young Master!"
[ZH] 陈府的主道上，阿升在前，三宝断后，中间青瓜陪着公子，大家迈开腿跑起来。
[EN] The slap let off just enough steam for Chen Haoyu to rise. "Who else can I pick on—them?"
[ZH] 陈府挺大，主道修得宽敞，倒也适合跑步。
[EN] Qinggua swallowed his grievance.
[ZH] 只是主道上有来来往往的下人，往西边去时，还有许多兴隆阁的伙计，动不动会碰到。
[EN] Then a vivid whiff of a steaming chamber pot flashed through Chen Haoyu's mind, making his stomach churn. A smart man picks his battles; better run the laps. "Hey! Chen Manor's massive—where am I supposed to run?"
[ZH] 前后两人看得紧，陈浩宇根本没有机会逃走，硬着头皮向前跑。下人们指指点点地议论，从未有过的憋屈堵在胸口。因跑步需喘气，他懒得骂那些下人，
[EN] "I'll set the pace in front, Sanbao brings up the rear, you run right between us," Asheng said.
[ZH] 到达西边的主道，来往的伙计不比下人，也不怕他，主动打招呼调侃：“二公子跑着呢？什么时候开始学会锻炼身体？”
[EN] Refusing to suffer alone, Chen Haoyu pointed at Qinggua. "You! Run with me!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇并不理会，他与青瓜跑得气喘吁吁，哪有心情理会？边跑边向阿升问：“能……能不能……歇会儿？”
[EN] "Come on, Young Master, I hate running—too tiring!"
[ZH] 阿升头也不回，继续向前跑：“还没跑上一圈儿，姑爷就喊累，您可是要跑十圈儿，坚持住，再跑跑自然会觉得轻松。”
[EN] "Look at them—not even my servants and they're running with me, while my own man acts like a coward! Get moving or forget my help chasing Hongdou!"
[ZH] 身后传来一个丫鬟的声音：“青瓜！你给我站住！”
[EN] For Hongdou, Qinggua bit the bullet. "If you're running, Young Master, I'm right beside you!"
[ZH] 声音够大，够气愤，所有人不由停下，向后瞧。
[EN] Down Chen Manor's wide main avenues they jogged: Asheng leading, Sanbao trailing, Chen Haoyu and Qinggua panting between them. Passing manor servants and Xinglong Pavilion clerks gawked and whispered, sending a burn of humiliation up Chen Haoyu's neck, though he barely had breath left to curse them.
[ZH] 陈浩宇更是感谢这声音，累得不行，正好歇会儿。
[EN] Rounding the west road, bold clerks even called out teasingly, "Jogging, Second Young Master? Taking up fitness now?"
[ZH] 原来是丑丫，她在亭子里等了半天青瓜，没见人来，慢慢明白过来，青瓜不过是利用她的感情而已。越想越气，不想再等下去，应该找他问个明白。
[EN] Wheezing hard, Chen Haoyu gasped at Asheng, "Can... can we... take a breather?"
[ZH] 刚走出亭子，来到主道，瞧见青瓜跟着姑爷跑步，丑丫没忍住，大声喊起来。
[EN] Without looking back, Asheng kept striding. "Not even one lap down and you're winded, Young Master? Nine more to go—push through and you'll catch your second wind!"
[ZH] 丑丫一脸怒气把陈浩宇逗乐：“青瓜，你完蛋了。”
[EN] Then an angry female voice boomed from behind: "Qinggua! Halt right there!"
[ZH] 青瓜小声说：“都是为了公子我才出卖色相勾引丑丫，该怎么办？公子快帮我出个主意。”
[EN] Loud and furious enough to freeze all four men in their tracks—music to Chen Haoyu's ears as he bent over gasping for air.
[ZH] 陈浩宇小声回道：“好汉不吃眼前亏，实在不行，先哄哄她。”
[EN] Storming down the lane came Chouya. After waiting half the morning in the pavilion, she had realized Qinggua played her for a fool and marched out hunting answers—spotting him jogging right down the main road.
[ZH] 青瓜认为此法可行，赔着笑脸走过去：“丑丫，让你久等，不是我不去亭子，你也看到，少夫人把公子放出来，要求我陪公子一起跑步。”
[EN] Seeing Chouya's thunderous face cracked Chen Haoyu up. "You're toast, Qinggua!"
[ZH] 丑丫一脸怀疑：“真的？”
[EN] "I only seduced her for your sake, Young Master!" Qinggua hissed. "Save me—what do I do?"
[ZH] 青瓜继续嬉皮笑脸：“那还能有假？不信你问我家公子。”
[EN] "Play smart—sweet-talk her out of it for now!" Chen Haoyu whispered back.
[ZH] 丑丫说：“你跟姑爷一个鼻孔出气，我才不问他，要问就问三宝。三宝你告诉我，是不是小姐让青瓜陪着姑爷跑步？”
[EN] Plastering on a sheepish grin, Qinggua trotted over. "Sorry to stand you up, Chouya! Wasn't skipping the pavilion—Young Madam let Young Master out and ordered me to run laps with him!"
[ZH] 三宝照实说：“小姐只让姑爷跑步，是姑爷刚才完拉着青瓜陪他一起跑。”
[EN] Chouya squinted in suspicion. "Really?"
[ZH] 丑丫转向青瓜：“你又骗我！”
[EN] "Cross my heart! Ask Young Master!"
[ZH] 丑丫已攥紧拳头，青瓜很害怕：“我没骗你，不管是公子还是少夫人，哪个的吩咐我都不敢违抗。”
[EN] "You two breathe out of the same nostril—why would I ask him? Sanbao! Did Miss order Qinggua to run with Young Master?"
[ZH] “那你说喜欢我的话呢？是不是骗我？”丑丫并不避讳旁边有人，大胆问出心中的疑惑。
[EN] "Miss only ordered Young Master to run," Sanbao answered straight. "Young Master dragged Qinggua along himself."
[ZH] 陈浩宇一脸看热闹的表情，跑步太无趣，眼前发生的事无疑是一道开胃菜。
[EN] Chouya whipped back toward Qinggua, fists balling tight. "Lying to me again!"
[ZH] 阿升本以为青瓜喜欢红豆，还一直担心不已，现在终于放心。
[EN] Panicked, Qinggua stammered, "Not lying! Servant like me can't say no to Young Master or Young Madam!"
[ZH] 青瓜迅速想到两种回答的结果。若承认说谎欺骗丑丫，肯定免不了被她暴揍一顿；若假装没骗她，以后可别想再摆脱丑丫。一辈子与被揍一次相比较，还是一辈子更重要。
[EN] "What about saying you liked me?" Chouya demanded right in front of everybody. "Was that a lie too?"
[ZH] 青瓜刚要承认是欺骗丑丫，却听丑丫说：“你若是欺骗我，我就天天跟着你，让你娶不了别人，最终只能娶我！你也别想逃走，逃到哪里我就跟到哪里！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu grinned like a spectator at a street opera—far more fun than jogging. Meanwhile Asheng exhaled in secret relief, having worried Qinggua was after Hongdou.
[ZH] 陈浩宇心想：“本公子都替青瓜感到为难。”
[EN] Qinggua weighed his odds in half a second: admit lying and take a bruising right now, or keep lying and get stuck with her forever. One beating beat a lifetime sentence—about to confess, he froze at Chouya's next threat: "If you lied to me, I'll stalk you every single day so no other girl comes near you until you have to marry me! Run to the ends of the earth and I'll follow!"
[ZH] 三宝说：“青瓜，丑丫对你一片真心，千万不要骗她。”
[EN] Even Chen Haoyu winced for him.
[ZH] 阿升说：“丑丫，你放心，青瓜对待感情还是很认真的。”
[EN] "Chouya's sincere, Qinggua—don't play games with her," Sanbao chimed in.
[ZH] 青瓜又在心中衡量着，还是继续欺骗能好些，好汉不吃眼前亏，以后再寻机会说明：“丑丫，我说过的话当然出自真心，你快些回去，我还要陪公子跑步，否则公子完成不了任务，得替少夫人倒马桶，你想想，我家公子怎么可能做这种事情，到时吃亏的还是我。”
[EN] "Relax, Chouya, Qinggua takes feelings seriously," Asheng piled on.
[ZH] 对方没有否认，丑丫一阵高兴，本想上前抱住青瓜，眼前有其他人，还是忍住：“好，你继续陪姑爷跑步，我先回去。”
[EN] Recomputing on the fly—survive today, wriggle out later—Qinggua forced a bright smile. "Of course I meant every word, Chouya! Head back now—I have to finish running with Young Master, or he fails his task and has to empty Young Madam's chamber pot, which means I'll end up doing the dirty work!"
[ZH] 丑丫害羞着迅速跑开。
[EN] Glowing because he hadn't denied it, Chouya checked an urge to hug him in front of company. "All right, keep running with Young Master. I'll go back!" Blushing bright pink, she scampered off.
[ZH] 阿升说：“姑爷，热闹看完了，该继续跑步。”
[EN] "Show's over, Young Master," Asheng announced. "Back to running."
[ZH] 陈浩宇对刚才的热闹意犹未尽，可好戏已结束，还得跑步。他无力反抗，只能忍着心中的不满，继续跑在陈府的主道上。
[EN] Reluctant to see the entertainment end, Chen Haoyu swallowed his grumbling and jogged back down Chen Manor's main avenue.

#CH 233
[ZH] 陈府挺大，跑上三四圈都让人受不了，何况十圈？陈浩宇和青瓜跑得气喘吁吁，浑身酸痛，加之心中不乐意，自然腿脚愈加沉重，到最后两圈时基本上是慢跑。
[ZH] 他们两个的速度，阿升与三宝只需跟着快走。
[ZH] 青瓜边跑边说：“公子，少夫人……只让您跑，能……能不能……让我歇会儿？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇喘着气反对道：“不……不行！我一个人跑……多丢人，有你……陪着还好些。”
[ZH] 青瓜不敢再言语。
[ZH] 跑着跑着，抬头瞧见陈浩东站在路中央，一脸笑容。
[ZH] 陈浩东心中着实爽快：“看到你受罚的样子，我真是太开心！不妨再给你们挑拨挑拨，让你跟母老虎的矛盾继续激化，我则在一旁看好戏。”
[ZH] 见到堂兄，陈浩宇像见到救命稻草一般，顿时有了力气，快跑几步，上前抱住陈浩东：“东哥！我跑了半天，陈家长辈没一个理会我，只有你最好！”
[ZH] 陈浩东假意关心的样子：“我一听说你的事就去找奶奶，可奶奶院子的大门紧闭，大伯和大婶子的院门也紧闭着，根本见不到人。其他长辈又不敢违拗老太太的意思，我便在这里等你，希望能帮你出出主意。”
[ZH] 阿升与三宝不好阻拦，站在一旁等待。青瓜觉得甚好，正好趁此机会休息休息。
[ZH] 陈浩宇放开东哥：“快帮我出出主意，怎么样才能避免被母老虎惩罚？”
[ZH] 陈浩东先不着急出主意，反而对浩宇脸上的伤颇感兴趣：“你脸上的伤是怎么回事？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇回道：“还不是被母老虎打的？”
[ZH] 陈浩东心中更乐：“打吧！打得遍体鳞伤才好！”嘴上则说：“等着，我先过去训斥他们两句。”
[EN] Chen Manor sprawled wide enough that three or four laps wrecked an untrained runner, let alone ten. By the final two laps, Chen Haoyu and Qinggua were reduced to a wheezing shuffle, forcing Asheng and Sanbao to slow to a brisk walk just to stay alongside.
[ZH] 陈浩东走到阿升和三宝面前，训斥道：“你们两个有眼无珠的下人，竟然敢公开欺负陈家的二公子，谁给你们这么大的胆量？赶明儿我回禀老太太，将你们两个卖到乡下去，永远别回临安城！”
[EN] "Young Master," Qinggua gasped, "Young Madam only... made you run... let me catch my breath..."
[ZH] 三宝想强辩，被阿升阻止。只见阿升心平气和解释道：“大公子恐怕不知，我家小姐也是得到老太太、太太和大老爷的同意管教姑爷，才让小的前来负责监督。”
[EN] "No... way!" Chen Haoyu panted. "Too embarrassing... running solo... you suffer with me!"
[ZH] 陈浩东故意生气道：“你们两个下人给我靠边站，乖乖在这里等着，我们兄弟到旁边的亭子里坐会儿，弟妹若有意见，让她来找我。”
[EN] Qinggua shut up and shuffled on.
[ZH] 陈浩东拉起陈浩宇往旁边的亭子走去。
[EN] Rounding a bend, Chen Haoyu looked up to find Chen Haodong standing right in the middle of the lane wearing a broad smile.
[ZH] 陈浩宇竖起大拇指：“东哥威武！”
[EN] Inside, Chen Haodong was walking on air: Watching you suffer makes my day! Time to pour a little oil on the fire between you and the tigress and sit back for the show!
[ZH] 陈浩东边走边说：“你啊，丢尽咱们陈家男人的脸，到那边坐坐，我给你好好传授一下如何管教自家娘子。”
[EN] Spotting his cousin felt like grabbing a lifeline. Finding a fresh burst of wind, Chen Haoyu jogged up and threw an arm around him. "Brother Dong! Been running for hours and not one elder lifts a finger for me—you're the only one who cares!"
[ZH] 两人来到亭子坐下后，陈浩宇叹口气：“唉，并非我要给陈家男人丢脸，怪只怪朱淑真太厉害，她手底下的人也厉害，就那两个，我既打不过，也跑不过，只好认栽，装作老实的样子。”
[EN] Putting on a solemn show of sympathy, Chen Haodong sighed. "The moment I heard, I hurried to Grandmother's, yet her gate was bolted tight, and Eldest Uncle and Aunt's too! Nobody else dares cross Old Madam Xia, so I waited out here to help you brainstorm."
[ZH] 陈浩东故意煽风点火：“你要是男人，就不能认栽，要努力反抗。弟妹再厉害，她也不敢伤你，更不敢危及你的性命。动动你的脑子好好想想，奶奶和婶子敢放开手不理会，肯定是因为拿准了她不会伤你。既如此你怕什么？大胆反抗便是。”
[EN] Asheng and Sanbao halted respectfully to wait, while Qinggua collapsed gratefully onto a stone to catch his breath.
[ZH] 陈浩宇立即醒悟过来：“对啊！我怎么没有想到？还是东哥厉害，难怪大嫂知书达理又温柔。剩下的一圈我不跑了，现在就回去跟朱淑真对着干！”
[EN] Pulling back, Chen Haoyu pleaded, "Give me a plan! How do I dodge the tigress's punishments?"
[ZH] 陈浩东夸道：“这就对了！”
[EN] Instead of answering right away, Chen Haodong pointed at his cheek. "Where'd that bruise come from?"
[ZH] 看着堂弟自信满满离开，陈浩东心中乐道：“回去好好跟你娘子对着干，你大哥我才有好戏看。”
[EN] "The tigress punched me!"
[ZH] 青瓜见公子回来，迎上去：“公子，您也歇息够，还有一圈，我陪您继续跑。”
[EN] Serve you right—hope she beats you black and blue! Chen Haodong cheered inside, saying aloud, "Hang tight while I put these lackeys in their place."
[ZH] “谁说本公子要跑？”陈浩宇发问道，指着阿升和三宝，“你们两个听着，剩下的一圈本公子不会跑，现在就回墨香苑，看朱淑真能把我怎样？”
[EN] Striding over to Asheng and Sanbao, he barked, "Blind, insolent servants—daring to humiliate Second Young Master in broad daylight! Who gave you the gall? Tomorrow I'll report to Old Madam Xia and have you sold off to the countryside for good!"
[ZH] 阿升和三宝毕竟是下人，不敢过分违抗姑爷，只好无奈地跟在后面。
[EN] Sanbao bristled to argue, yet Asheng laid a calm hand on his arm and bowed. "Forgive us, Eldest Young Master. Our mistress received full permission from Old Madam Xia, Eldest Master, and Eldest Madam to discipline Second Young Master, and ordered us to supervise."
[ZH] 阿升谨慎提醒道：“姑爷，您不按照要求完成，可是要倒马桶的。”
[EN] Feigning wrath, Chen Haodong snapped, "Step aside and stay put while my cousin and I sit in that pavilion! If Sister-in-law has a problem, send her to me!" Taking Haoyu's arm, he steered him toward the pavilion.
[ZH] 陈浩宇站住，对着阿升骂道：“倒你个头！本公子就是不倒！朱淑真又能将我如何？”
[EN] "Brother Dong, you're a legend!" Chen Haoyu flashed a thumbs-up.
[ZH] 阿升更无奈地继续跟着往回走。
[EN] "You're embarrassing every man in the Chen clan," Chen Haodong chided as they walked. "Sit down and let me teach you how a man keeps his wife in line."
[ZH] 青瓜凑到公子身边，边走边小声问：“大公子说了什么？您怎么突然变得大胆起来？”
[EN] Settling onto stone stools, Chen Haoyu sighed. "Not like I want to lose face, Brother Dong—Zhu Shuzhen is a beast, and her servants are beasts too! Can't outfight or outrun those two, so I had to play along."
[ZH] 陈浩宇笑着回道：“金玉良言。”
[EN] Chen Haodong leaned in to fan the flames. "A real man never rolls over—fight back! Tough as Sister-in-law is, she won't dare cripple you or endanger your life. Use your head: why did Grandmother and Aunt step back without lifting a finger? Because they know for a fact she won't actually hurt you! So what are you scared of? Push right back!"
[ZH] 回到墨香苑，院子里并排站着三个丫鬟。陈浩宇有印象，以前他调戏过一回，记得应该是三叔院中的，可为何站在自己院子？他不甚理解，也懒得理会。
[EN] Light flared across Chen Haoyu's face. "Brilliant! How didn't I see that?! No wonder Sister-in-law Anya is so gentle and obedient under you! Forget the last lap—I'm marching straight back to Moxiang Courtyard to go head-to-head with Zhu Shuzhen!"
[ZH] 正要往后院拐去，一个丫鬟堵在前面：“姑爷，小姐说了，您若跑完步，先向这三个丫鬟道歉，然后背诵《论语》和王右丞的诗，再然后负责打扫小姐的闺房。”
[EN] "That's the spirit!" Chen Haodong clapped his shoulder, grinning silently as his cousin swaggered off: Go stir up a war with your wife so I get front-row seats!
[ZH] 一连串要求，陈浩宇刚要发作，想到朱淑真不会伤他，也不会要他的命，立刻变得轻松：“你叫什么名字？我怎么从来没见过？”
[EN] Seeing his master strut back, Qinggua hurried up. "Rested up, Young Master? Just one lap left—I'll finish it with you."
[ZH] 丫鬟说：“我叫阿美，帮姑爷打过蒋爷的手下，也跟着小姐去过青楼带姑爷回来，姑爷您从来没注意过我。再说，自从小姐嫁过来，姑爷整日不着家，对我没印象倒也正常。”
[EN] "Who said I'm running?" Chen Haoyu jabbed a finger at Asheng and Sanbao. "Listen up! I'm skipping the final lap and heading straight back to Moxiang Courtyard! Let's see what Zhu Shuzhen does about it!"
[ZH] “原来是阿美。”陈浩宇指着身后的阿升，“你小子的妹妹，你们兄妹两个想管本公子，没门儿。告诉朱淑真，我不怕她，她想让我干什么，我偏不干！”
[EN] Unwilling to rough up their young master, Asheng and Sanbao fell in helplessly behind him. "Young Master," Asheng cautioned, "skipping tasks means emptying the chamber pot."
[ZH] 陈浩宇说完，正准备回博雅斋，被千里寻的声音拦住：“陈浩宇！你该不会是想给老娘倒马桶？没想到你的爱好满独特。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu spun around barking, "Empty my foot! Not doing it! Let's see what she dares do!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇站住，面向朱淑真：“本公子再强调一遍，你让我做的事，我一概不做，倒马桶我更不会做。”
[EN] Resigned, Asheng trailed along while Qinggua whispered beside his master, "What did Eldest Young Master tell you to put all that fire in your belly?"
[ZH] 千里寻迅速上前将陈浩宇的胳膊拧到背后：“做不做？”
[EN] "Pure golden wisdom," Chen Haoyu boasted.
[ZH] 陈浩宇感受到一阵疼痛，“哎吆”叫唤着：“不做就是不做，反正你不会伤我，也不会要我的命，我什么都不怕！看你能把我怎样！”
[EN] Stepping into Moxiang Courtyard, Chen Haoyu saw three maids standing in a neat line—familiar faces from Third Uncle's courtyard whom he had flirted with once. Wondering why they stood here yet not caring, he turned toward the back courtyard—until a maid stepped right across his path.
[ZH] 对方识破她的底线，倒让千里寻措手不及，她一把推开陈浩宇：“你身上可是一文钱都没有，难道不想赚银子？”
[EN] "Young Master, Miss says once your laps are done, first apologize to these three maids, then memorize the *Analects* and Wang Wei's poems, and after that dust Miss's room."
[ZH] 陈浩宇站稳后，揉揉胳膊：“哼！反正出不去，吃住都在自己家里，没有需要使银子的地方，不赚也罢。”
[EN] About to explode, Chen Haoyu remembered she dared not hurt him and relaxed into a smirk. "What's your name? Never seen you around."
[ZH] 千里寻努力吓唬：“那我就永远把你关在墨香苑。”
[EN] "I'm Amei. Helped fight off Master Jiang's thugs for you and went with Miss to Chunxiang Brothel to haul you home—you just never noticed me. Hardly surprising since you've stayed out of the manor ever since the wedding."
[ZH] 陈浩宇为拿捏住对方而窃喜：“哼！大不了本公子当个宅男，永远不出去。反正该玩儿的都玩儿够，该吃的都吃过，该见的也都见过，就是没体会过当宅男的感觉，正好趁此机会体验一回。”
[EN] "Ah, Amei!" He jabbed a thumb at Asheng behind him. "Your brother's shadow! Think you two siblings can boss me around? Tell Zhu Shuzhen I'm not scared of her—whatever she tells me to do, I'm doing the exact opposite!"
[ZH] 对方死猪不怕开水烫，稳稳拿捏住她的底线，千里寻想了一会儿，终于想到致命的一招。
[EN] Turning toward Boya Study, he froze at Qianli Xun's voice floating from the porch. "Chen Haoyu! Eager to empty my chamber pot? Didn't peg you for that kind of hobby."
[ZH] “你要是不遵照今日写下的条款，我只好差人做一个木牌，上面写着‘我是大混蛋’，然后挂在你的脖子上，再将你五花大绑，由我的下人带着你先在陈府游一遍，再到临安城日日游街。不知你是愿意按照条款来执行，还是愿意被人拉出去游街？”
[EN] He spun to face her. "I'll say it one last time: I'm not lifting a finger for any of your chores, chamber pot included!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇被气得炸毛，指着朱淑真：“你……你……，我被拉去游街，对你有什么好处？我可是你名义上的夫君！”
[EN] In a flash Qianli Xun stepped in and pinned his arm behind his back. "Doing them or not?"
[ZH] 对方越生气，千里寻越高兴：“都说名义上的，你游街丢的是你的人，跟我有什么关系？只要能报了散播谣言之仇，我就开心！再说自从我嫁过来，你日日待在春香楼，何曾考虑过我是你的娘子？我又何必考虑你是我的夫君？”
[EN] Wincing hard, he yelled, "Not doing them! You won't cripple me and you won't kill me—I'm not scared of anything you throw at me!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇又找到调侃的点，装出疑惑的表情：“你该不会喜欢上我了吧？本公子这么优秀，你喜欢上我倒也正常……”
[EN] Caught off guard that he had called her bluff, Qianli Xun shoved him clear. "Not a copper in your pocket—don't you want to earn silver?"
[ZH] “你哪只耳朵听到老娘喜欢你？老娘只喜欢折磨你！”千里寻两句话将陈浩宇的调侃打回去，立刻命令道，“阿升，找个木牌来！”
[EN] Rubbing his shoulder, Chen Haoyu sneered. "Locked in here with free food and board, what do I need silver for? Keep it!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇大声喊道：“朱淑真！你来劲儿了是吧？”
[EN] "Then I lock you in Moxiang Courtyard forever!"
[ZH] 千里寻走到陈浩宇跟前：“老娘有使不完的劲儿，怎么？还想吃一拳？”
[EN] Gleeful at reading her hand, he crowed, "Great! I'll become a homebody! Seen everything, eaten everything, played everything—only thing I haven't tried is staying home all day! Perfect chance!"
[ZH] 没想到跟朱淑真打几个回合的口水仗，被她重重击打在要害。东哥出的主意，一点用也没有。再说这女人是说到做到的主儿，若真把他拉出去游街，以后哪还有脸面见人？不如认输。
[EN] Faced with shameless immunity, Qianli Xun paused a beat—and found his true Achilles' heel.
[ZH] “得得得！不必挂牌子，本公子听你的就是，谁让本公子散播你的谣言犯下大错，就当是欠你的。”
[EN] "Break today's rules, and I'll have a wooden signboard carved reading 'I Am a Giant Scumbag,' hang it around your neck, truss you up in ropes, and have my servants parade you first through every courtyard in Chen Manor, then up and down every street in Lin'an daily! Your choice: follow the rules, or go on tour."
[ZH] “早这么想不就结了？”千里寻面向阿美说，“阿美，带陈浩宇向三个丫鬟道歉。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu's hair nearly stood on end. "Y-you... how does parading me through town help you?! I'm your husband on paper!"
[ZH] 阿美来到姑爷面前：“姑爷，这边请。”
[EN] The madder he got, the wider Qianli Xun beamed. "Keyword: on paper! You lose face, not me—so long as I get payback for last year's slander, I'm thrilled! Hanging around Chunxiang Brothel every night since our wedding, did you spare one thought for me being your wife? Why would I spare one for you?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇满心不乐意，又无可奈何。心想，朱淑真一定是自己的克星，早知今日，当初就不该娶她。想起蒋爷在妓院里说的话，一心认为是朱晞颜设计害他，转过头说：“朱淑真，你爹就是一只老狐狸，害得我好惨。”
[EN] Grabbing at his favorite needle, he feigned surprise. "Wait—did you fall for me? Makes sense, stellar catch like me—"
[ZH] 千里寻不明白陈浩宇所言，并未当回事：“少废话！赶紧道歉！”
[EN] "Which ear heard me say I like you? I only like torturing you!" Cutting him off flat, she barked, "Asheng, go fetch a wooden board!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇来到三个丫鬟面前，把她们上上下下瞧一遍，问道：“我不就是以前摸过你们的手和脸蛋儿吗？这都得让本公子道歉？老老实实告诉本公子，怎么被母老虎揪出来的？”
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen! You're pushing it!"
[ZH] 一个丫鬟说：“二少夫人身边的红豆对我们说，二公子您打算向您欺负过的下人道歉，我们听说后，都觉得好奇，就赶着过来试试。”
[EN] Stepping right into his space, she flexed her knuckles. "Got plenty of energy left—want another punch?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇质问道：“你们是好奇，本公子可遭罪，要给你们这些下人道歉，成何体统？”
[EN] Crushed across every round, Chen Haoyu cursed Brother Dong's useless advice. This woman never bluffed; let her drag him through Lin'an wearing a scumbag sign and his life was over. He threw up both hands. "Fine, fine, put the board away! I'll listen! Spreading those rumors was my fault—consider it paying my debt!"
[ZH] 一旁的阿美提醒道：“姑爷，快些道歉。”
[EN] "Saved us both time if you'd said that first." Qianli Xun nodded to Amei. "Take Chen Haoyu to apologize to the three maids."
[ZH] “要你提醒！多嘴！”陈浩宇扭头瞅一眼朱淑真，对方很不屑地盯着他，只好硬着头皮说，“对不起。”
[EN] "Right this way, Young Master," Amei gestured.
[ZH] 三个丫鬟第一次听主人家给自己说“对不起”，都觉得好玩儿，不由笑起来。
[EN] Fuming yet cornered, convinced Zhu Shuzhen was his nemesis—and recalling Master Jiang's lie at the brothel—he shot back over his shoulder, "Your father's a sly old fox, Zhu Shuzhen! Screwed me over good!"
[ZH] 阿美继续提醒：“只说一声不行，共三个人，还差两声。”
[EN] Clueless what he meant, Qianli Xun barked, "Quit stalling and apologize!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇只好又加两声：“对不起，对不起。”
[EN] Stopping before the three maids, Chen Haoyu eyed them up and down. "All I did was pinch your hands and cheeks once—and I have to apologize for that? Be honest, how'd the tigress dig you three up?"
[ZH] 三个丫鬟继续笑着，其中一个说：“二公子，您以后可别再对我们这些丫鬟无礼，否则您以后有说不完的‘对不起’。”
[EN] "Hongdou told us Second Young Master wanted to apologize to servants he mistreated," one maid answered. "Curiosity got the better of us, so we came to see!"
[ZH] 三个丫鬟满意离开。
[EN] "Your curiosity is my misery! A master apologizing to maids—where's the decency?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇转过身：“朱淑真，你可满意？”
[EN] "Apologize already, Young Master," Amei nudged.
[ZH] 千里寻回道：“一切才刚刚开始，离我满意还尚早。”
[EN] "Shut up! Nobody asked you!" Glancing back at Zhu Shuzhen's icy glare, he bit the bullet and muttered, "Sorry."
[ZH] 青瓜很羡慕三个丫鬟有幸接受公子当面道歉，他来到少夫人面前：“少夫人，我能不能也加入被道歉的队伍？”
[EN] Never having heard a master apologize before, the three maids giggled behind their sleeves.
[ZH] 千里寻说：“当然，你经常被陈浩宇欺负，他欠你很多‘对不起’。”
[EN] "One won't cut it," Amei reminded him. "Three people—two more to go."
[ZH] 青瓜很开心。
[EN] "Sorry. Sorry!" he ground out.
[ZH] 自己身边的人毫不避讳要求加入被道歉的队伍，陈浩宇既感到面上无光，也气得不行，冲上去就是一脚。结果没踢着，因用力过猛，摔倒在地上。
[EN] Still giggling, one maid piped up, "Don't get handsy with maids anymore, Second Young Master, or you'll never run out of 'sorrys' to say!" Satisfied, the three trotted off.
[ZH] 青瓜顺利躲开公子的一脚，见公子倒地，赶紧上前搀扶：“公子怎么还踢我？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu spun back. "Happy now, Zhu Shuzhen?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇趁机抓住青瓜的衣领：“谁都可以要求我道歉！就你不行！”
[EN] "Just getting warmed up—nowhere near happy yet."
[ZH] 青瓜强辩道：“少夫人已经答应。”
[EN] Envious of the three maids, Qinggua stepped right up to Qianli Xun. "Young Madam, can I join the apology line too?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇把衣领抓得更紧，命令道：“你是我的人！只准听我的！”
[EN] "Of course!" Qianli Xun smiled. "He bullies you nonstop—he owes you dozens of 'sorrys'!"
[ZH] 青瓜勉强地点点头。
[EN] Qinggua beamed—and Chen Haoyu lost his cool completely. Lunging forward with a furious kick at his own bold servant, he swung so hard on empty air as Qinggua sidestepped that his legs flew out from under him and he hit the flagstones flat on his backside.
[ZH] 千里寻懒得理会主仆二人，转身进屋去了。
[EN] "Still trying to kick me, Young Master?" Qinggua hurried to help him up.
[ZH] 苦荞给阿升使使眼色，也进屋去了。
[EN] Chen Haoyu snatched Qinggua's collar. "Anyone else can ask for an apology—never you!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇一把推开青瓜，想要努力爬起来。青瓜见状，上前搀扶公子。
[EN] "Young Madam already said yes!"
[ZH] 阿升说：“姑爷，请回博雅斋完成背书任务。”
[EN] "You belong to me! You listen to me alone!" he hissed, tightening his grip.
[ZH] 陈浩宇起身站稳后，举起拳头，在阿升和三宝面前晃晃：“你们两个阴魂不散，总有一日，让你们倒在我的拳头之下。”
[EN] Qinggua nodded reluctantly.
[ZH] 说归说，斗不过朱淑真，还得乖乖背书。可一腔怨气需找人发泄，青瓜又成为出气筒。
[EN] Ignoring the comedy act, Qianli Xun turned and went indoors, Kuqiao signaling Asheng before following.
[ZH] “青瓜，走我前面。”
[EN] Shoving Qinggua off, Chen Haoyu clambered up with Qinggua's help as Asheng reminded him, "Back to Boya Study for your memorization task, Young Master."
[ZH] “为何？”
[EN] Brushing off his knees, Chen Haoyu shook a fist at Asheng and Sanbao. "Pair of ghosts haunting my shadow—one day I'll flatten you both!" Still, beaten for now, he had to study—and needed an outlet for his bile. "Qinggua, walk in front of me."
[ZH] “让你走前面就走前面，那么多废话干什么？”
[EN] "Why?"
[ZH] 青瓜猜不透公子的想法，乖乖走到前面。
[EN] "Less mouth, more walking!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇瞅准机会，将力气集中在右脚，使劲向青瓜的屁股踢去：“去你的！朱淑真！”
[EN] Unsuspecting, Qinggua stepped ahead. Gathering every ounce of steam in his right leg, Chen Haoyu booted him square in the rear: "Take that, Zhu Shuzhen!"

#CH 234
[ZH] 青瓜的屁股上没长眼睛，被重重踢到，向前趔趄，重心失衡，没站稳，眼看即将倾斜，若倒在地上时，必然来个狗吃屎。
[ZH] 说时迟，那时快，丑丫飞快冲来，将青瓜扶住：“青瓜，你没事吧？”
[ZH] 青瓜感到庆幸：“谢谢你，丑丫。”
[ZH] “跟我不必客气。”丑丫放开青瓜，上前行礼道，“姑爷，以后能不能不要欺负我家青瓜？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇气没出成，心中正郁闷，又被一个丫鬟质问，长这么大，从来没如此委屈过。
[ZH] 瞅瞅院子，不是自己喜欢的样子，再瞅瞅眼前的下人，个个都是朱淑真训练出来的。连唯一可以撒气的青瓜也有人撑腰，而自己连青瓜都不如，眼眶不由变红，一句话没说往博雅斋走去。
[ZH] 阿升和三宝紧跟上。
[ZH] 丑丫纳闷道：“青瓜，你刚才看到没？姑爷好像哭了？”
[ZH] 青瓜并未注意：“什么？公子哭了？怎么可能？我家公子从小到大只有让别人哭的份儿，从没见他哭过。”
[ZH] 阿美走过来：“我刚才也看到，姑爷应该是感到委屈，眼睛很红，像是要哭的样子。唉，遇到我家小姐这么厉害的人，姑爷不哭才怪。”
[ZH] 青瓜在，丑丫顾不上阿美：“青瓜，有时间去西边的亭子跟我约会。”
[ZH] 阿美打趣道：“丑丫，真不害臊，我还在此，都不知道避开。”
[ZH] “我看你还是回避一下。”丑丫推开阿美，“赶紧去忙吧，别打扰我们。”
[ZH] 阿美笑一下，去了西院。
[ZH] 青瓜担心公子，也想尽快甩开丑丫：“你忙，我去看看我家公子。”
[ZH] 青瓜说完急忙向后院跑去。
[ZH] 丑丫并未去追，她还要干活，瞅着青瓜的背影议论道：“姑爷总欺负你，还老往他跟前凑，我看你就是欠揍，下次再也不管你。”
[EN] With no eyes on his backside, Qinggua took the kick square on, pitching forward off balance straight toward a face-plant on the flagstones.
[ZH] 回博雅斋的路上，陈浩宇努力控制即将落下的泪水，还是有一滴划过脸颊掉落下来，他站住脚步，用衣袖将脸上的泪水抹掉，猛然想起以前跟狐朋狗友在一起饮酒玩乐时说过的话……
[EN] Out of nowhere, Chouya darted in and caught him steady. "Are you all right, Qinggua?"
[ZH] 有一次，他与一群公子哥在聚香楼饮酒作乐，什么低俗的话题都聊，偶尔也会聊起真情，那些朋友一一谈起曾动过真情的姑娘，唯独他陈浩宇没有。
[EN] "Whew—thanks, Chouya!"
[ZH] 有位公子哥问道：“陈兄采过的花可不少，难道没有一个动真情的？”
[EN] "Don't mention it." Letting him go, Chouya turned and curtsied to Chen Haoyu. "Young Master, could you please stop bullying my Qinggua?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇左边搂一个姑娘，右边也搂一个姑娘，一脸淫笑：“本公子就是喜欢跟她们玩儿，谈什么真情？”
[EN] Denied even his lone vent and scolded by a maid in his own yard—every tile altered against his wishes, every servant loyal to Zhu Shuzhen, even Qinggua backed up while he stood utterly alone—a sudden, raw sting of humiliation burned behind Chen Haoyu's eyes. Not once in his entire life had he felt this cornered. Without another word, eyes reddening, he turned and walked back toward Boya Study with Asheng and Sanbao trailing behind.
[ZH] 另一位公子问：“我一直很好奇，陈兄到底会对什么样的女子动真情？”
[EN] Chouya blinked after him. "Qinggua, did you see that? Did Young Master just cry?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不愿谈及此话题：“谈这个多没意思，抓紧吃喝玩乐。”
[EN] "What? Cry? No way! My master only makes other people cry—never shed a tear in his life!"
[ZH] 没想到其他几位公子也对此话题感兴趣，都催促起来：“陈兄不妨说说，我们都说了，你不说，太不公平。”
[EN] Amei strolled over. "I saw it too. Looked crushed, eyes red as cherries. Well, married to a force of nature like Miss, anyone would cry."
[ZH] 陈浩宇松开两位姑娘：“你们两个去那边，我要说正事，一会儿再陪你们玩儿。”
[EN] Ignoring Amei, Chouya turned shyly to Qinggua. "Come meet me at the west pavilion when you're free."
[ZH] 两个姑娘离开后，陈浩宇认真地说：“你们既然这么感兴趣，谈谈也无妨。小时候我流过多少次眼泪，已经记不清楚，反正从我记事起，就没怎么流过泪，要是哪个姑娘能让我流泪，我便喜欢上她。”
[EN] "No shame at all, Chouya, flirting right in front of me!" Amei teased.
[ZH] 有人故意问：“如果那姑娘是个丑八怪怎么办？”
[EN] "Then make yourself scarce!" Chouya nudged her away. "Go do your chores!" Laughing, Amei strolled off to the West Courtyard.
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“本公子对颜值要求很高，长得丑一律入不了本公子的法眼，又怎能让本公子因她流下眼泪？”
[EN] Worried about his master—and eager to ditch Chouya—Qinggua said quick, "Gotta go check on Young Master!" and jogged off to the back courtyard.
[ZH] 回想到这些，陈浩宇在心中自问：“难道我喜欢上母老虎？”
[EN] Letting him go since she had work anyway, Chouya muttered at his retreating back, "Young Master boots you nonstop and you still run after him like a pup! Next time I'm leaving you on the ground!"
[ZH] 他晃晃脑袋：“怎么可能？我跟她势不两立！可为何刚才会流下眼泪？不，一定是我想多了，当初说的话不能太当真。”
[EN] Along the walk to Boya Study, Chen Haoyu fought back the burn, yet one lone teardrop slipped down his cheek anyway. Halting, he wiped it away with his sleeve—and froze as an old drinking boast rushed back into his head.
[ZH] 陈浩宇带着疑惑跨入博雅斋，阿升和三宝则守在门外。
[EN] One night at Juxiang Tower, drinking and swapping ribald stories with his rich buddies, the talk had turned to real love. Every friend named some girl who had moved his heart—everybody except Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] 红豆正在书桌旁收拾，见姑爷进来，上前行礼：“姑爷，书桌已给您收拾好，那一摞书是小姐专门为您挑选的，先背诵《论语》和王右丞的诗，小姐说了，《论语》每日背诵五篇，王右丞的诗每日背五首，背完后，您需到素心草堂由小姐检验。”
[EN] "All the flowers you've plucked, Brother Chen, never once caught real feelings?" one friend had asked.
[ZH] 陈浩宇瞧见书桌上放着高高一摞书，还未读，脑袋已经十个大，又在心中骂起朱淑真：“你就是一只讨厌的母老虎！本公子根本不可能喜欢上你！”
[EN] With a girl draped over each arm, Chen Haoyu had smirked. "I just like playing around. Who bothers with real love?"
[ZH] 红豆叮嘱道：“姑爷还是抓紧时间背诵。”
[EN] "Always wondered what kind of woman could actually make you fall, Brother Chen," another pressed.
[ZH] 陈浩宇摸摸肚子：“腹中空空，跑了一上午，也该给些东西吃，吃完再背不迟。”
[EN] Waving the girls away when the whole table clamored for an answer, Chen Haoyu had turned strangely serious. "Can't recall how much I cried as a toddler, yet since I've been old enough to remember, I've never shed a tear. If any woman ever makes me cry, that's the woman I've fallen for."
[ZH] 红豆说：“姑爷先背着，我去给您端些饭食来。”
[EN] "What if she's hideous?" someone joked.
[ZH] 红豆离开后，青瓜从外面小心进来，见公子已在书桌前坐下，书桌上破天荒地放了好些书。他笑嘻嘻地上前问：“公子，刚才听丑丫和阿美说您流眼泪，是不是真的？”
[EN] "My standards for looks are sky-high—no plain face ever catches my eye, let alone makes me cry!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇训斥道：“一边去！少烦本公子！本公子流泪跟你有什么关系？”
[EN] Memory fading, Chen Haoyu stared blankly ahead: Wait... did I just fall for the tigress?!
[ZH] 青瓜说：“看来是真的。我知道公子委屈，可有什么办法？陈家长辈不出面干涉，少夫人又厉害，您只有吃苦的份儿，不如我给您出个主意？”
[EN] He shook his head hard. Impossible! We're sworn enemies! Then why did a tear just fall? No—overthinking it! Old drunken talk doesn't count!
[ZH] 陈浩宇盯着一摞书，说起话来都觉无力：“你能有什么好主意？”
[EN] Shaking off the eerie thought, he stepped into Boya Study while Asheng and Sanbao took posts outside.
[ZH] 青瓜凑到书桌前：“硬碰硬公子已试过，根本不奏效，不如来软的？”
[EN] Inside, Hongdou was tidying the desk. Turning to curtsy, she gestured to a tall stack of bound volumes. "Desk's all set, Young Master. Miss handpicked these for you—start with the *Analects* and Wang Wei's poetry. Five chapters of the *Analects* and five poems daily, tested by Miss in Suxin Thatched Hall once you finish."
[ZH] 陈浩宇立即认同，双眼放光，盯着青瓜：“没想到你还有些脑子，说来听听。”
[EN] One look at the tower of books gave Chen Haoyu an instant migraine, firing his curses back up: Rotten tigress! As if I could ever fall for you!
[ZH] 公子没有骂他，还很感兴趣，青瓜更是来了精神。
[EN] "Better start memorizing, Young Master," Hongdou urged.
[ZH] “您跟少夫人本就是夫妻，不如拿出对待自家娘子的好来，少夫人再厉害，毕竟是女子，喜欢听些软言软语。关切的话说多了，她一心软，拳头怎么可能使得下去？您再把自己变成一个好夫君，少夫人发现您的好，会一门心思想着跟您好好过日子，肯定不会再欺负您。”
[EN] Patting his growling belly, he countered, "Starving after running all morning! Bring lunch first, then I'll memorize."
[ZH] 陈浩宇仔细思量：“软言软语好说，只是怎么做一个好夫君，我从没试过，不知道如何做？”
[EN] "Start reading while I fetch your meal."
[ZH] 青瓜继续出主意：“大老爷就是个好夫君，他怎么对待大夫人，您就怎么对待少夫人。”
[EN] After Hongdou left, Qinggua crept in, blinking at his master seated before a mountain of classics. Grinning, he sidled up. "Chouya and Amei said you cried, Young Master—true?"
[ZH] “对啊！我爹就是活生生的范本，不如向他学？”陈浩宇将手掌在桌上拍一下，兴奋道。无意中目光接触到眼前的一摞书，兴致立刻全无，“母老虎还让我背书，背还是不背？”
[EN] "Beat it! Quit bugging me! None of your business whether I cried!"
[ZH] “当然背。”青瓜把最上面的一本书翻开，摆在公子面前，“公子仔细想想，大老爷有没有违抗过夫人的意思？”
[EN] "Aha, so it's true! I know you're hurting, Young Master, yet with the elders staying out and Young Madam unstoppable, hard times are here—unless you hear out an idea of mine?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇仔细思索：“还真没有，不过，我娘从来没有欺负过我爹，母老虎明明是欺负我，我本来就不喜欢背书，想到被她欺负更来气。”
[EN] Staring gloomily at the stack of books, Chen Haoyu muttered, "What bright idea could you have?"
[ZH] “公子消消气。”青瓜用手在公子背后轻轻地从上到下捋着，“得想想少夫人为何欺负您？肯定是您做过对不起她的事，我可是听说去年关于少夫人的谣言是公子散播的。”
[EN] Qinggua leaned over the desk. "Going head-to-head flopped completely. Why not try going soft?"
[ZH] “没想到本公子散播谣言之事，你都知道。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu's eyes snapped up bright and alert. "Got a brain in that skull after all—keep talking!"
[ZH] “陈府的下人都传开了，无人不知。少夫人一个女子被全城人臭骂，您想想她的心情，估计那会儿躲在闺房哭得跟个泪人似的，我还听说本来乔家打算到朱家提亲，因为谣言，亲事也黄了，真没想到这么损的事竟然是我家公子干的。”
[EN] Thrilled not to be cursed at, Qinggua warmed up. "You and Young Madam are husband and wife! Treat her sweet—tough as she is, she's still a woman, and women melt for soft words and warm care! Pour on concern every day until her heart softens; how can she swing fists at a devoted husband? Turn yourself into a model husband, let her see your good side, and she'll want a real marriage instead of torturing you!"
[ZH] “不能全怪我，母老虎还写了句‘和衣睡倒人怀’，是她自己把自己好名声给毁了。”
[EN] Turning it over, Chen Haoyu mused, "Soft words I can do—never tried being a good husband though. How do you even do that?"
[ZH] 青瓜停下帮公子捋背的手，回想一会儿听到的谣言：“是有这句，可谣言是公子您散播的，您不散播那句子，老百姓也不可能知道。”
[EN] "Eldest Master is a model husband! Copy how he treats Eldest Madam!"
[ZH] 青瓜的话才令陈浩宇明白，普通老百姓根本不会关注酸臭文人写的诗词，朱淑真写的那句子本来只会在文人才子中传播，因他放在散播的谣言里，老百姓才得知。由此看来，他陈浩宇的罪过更大。
[EN] "Right! My own father's a living textbook!" Chen Haoyu slapped the desk in excitement—then sagged seeing the books staring back. "Still stuck memorizing these?"
[ZH] “好吧好吧，错在我，我好好背书。”
[EN] "Of course!" Qinggua flipped the top volume open in front of him. "Think about it: has Eldest Master ever crossed Eldest Madam once?"
[ZH] 他拿起书看起来，只觉古人实在无趣，写这么多字有何意义？还给后人添麻烦。
[EN] "Never—though Mother never tortured Father either! The tigress is flat-out bullying me, and I hate reading!"
[ZH] 没过一会儿，红豆把饭送来，陈浩宇吃饱后，困意袭来，坐在书桌旁直打盹儿，实在困得不行，直接趴在桌上呼呼大睡。
[EN] "Cool down, Young Master." Qinggua gently rubbed his master's back. "Ask yourself why she's mad at you—heard every servant in the manor talking about you spreading those slanderous rumors about her last year! Imagine a young lady cursed by an entire city, crying her eyes out in her room, watching her match with Qiao Manor blow up! Hard to believe you pulled something that dirty."
[ZH] 青瓜见状，叹口气，并不理会，谁让他也开始打盹儿，人虽站着，头却时不时耷拉下去。
[EN] "Not all on me—she wrote 'falling asleep in his arms, fully dressed' herself!"
[ZH] 青瓜正在享受晕晕乎乎的状态时，被人戳了一下，不由变得清醒，定睛一瞧，原来是丑丫。
[EN] Qinggua paused. "Sure, yet ordinary folks don't read scholars' poetry! Nobody on the street would've heard that line if you hadn't woven it into your gossip!"
[ZH] 丑丫小声说：“走，跟我去西边的亭子。”
[EN] That struck home like a cold splash: commoners never paid attention to highbrow verses; he really had lit the match that burned her name down. "All right, all right—my bad. I'll memorize."
[ZH] 青瓜本不喜欢丑丫，老被她缠着也不是个事儿，还容易被红豆误会，干脆想个办法解决此事。
[EN] He opened the *Analects*, muttering at ancient sages for writing so many tedious words just to torment future generations.
[ZH] 他灵机一动，拉起丑丫离公子远一些，免得吵到他。
[EN] When Hongdou brought lunch, Chen Haoyu ate his fill, grew heavy-lidded, bobbed over his book, and soon slumped face-down on the desk snoring. Sighing, Qinggua stood beside him nodding off on his own feet—until a poke jolted him awake to find Chouya standing there.
[ZH] “丑丫，你以后不要老来找我。”
[EN] "Come on, west pavilion," she whispered.
[ZH] “为何？”
[EN] Not liking Chouya and worried Hongdou might get the wrong idea, Qinggua grabbed a quick scheme and drew her clear of his snoring master. "Chouya, stop coming to look for me."
[ZH] “公子被少夫人欺负，没好日子过，我根本没有心情跟你在一起。”
[EN] "Why?!"
[ZH] “他是他，你是你。”
[EN] "While Young Master's miserable under Young Madam, I have zero mood for romance."
[ZH] “错，公子高兴我便高兴，公子不高兴，我也高兴不起来。”
[EN] "He's him and you're you!"
[ZH] “这么说只有我家小姐跟姑爷好了，你才愿意跟我去西边的亭子？”
[EN] "Nope—when he's happy I'm happy; when he hurts, I hurt."
[ZH] 青瓜点点头。
[EN] "So you won't meet me at the pavilion until Miss and Young Master make up?"
[ZH] 丑丫说：“这得等到啥时候？只怕遥遥无期。”
[EN] Qinggua nodded.
[ZH] 青瓜想到一个好办法：“要不你就别来找我，要不你就帮帮我家公子，让她跟少夫人快些和好。”
[EN] "That could take forever!"
[ZH] “和好根本不可能，看来我只能不来找你。”丑丫失落地离开。
[EN] "Your choice: stop coming around, or help Young Master and Young Madam patch things up!"
[ZH] 这么快把丑丫打发走，青瓜为自己的聪明才智感到骄傲。刚高兴一会儿，又觉不对，丑丫的表现岂不说明公子跟少夫人得永远斗下去？难道给公子出的服软的主意根本没用？
[EN] "Patching them up is impossible... guess I'll stay away then." Crestfallen, Chouya slipped out.
[ZH] 正想着，听到公子的声音：“朱淑真！你等着！我要打得你落花……流……水……”
[EN] Thrilled at shaking her off so clean, Qinggua's grin faded a second later: wait—if even Chouya thinks they'll never make up, is my soft-approach plan doomed?
[ZH] 原来是公子说梦话而已。
[EN] Just then a mumble rose from the desk: "Zhu Shuzhen... wait till I beat you... flowers falling, water flowing..." Talking in his sleep.
[ZH] 青瓜继续站在公子身边，刚站一会儿，又听到公子说出四个字：“我……喜欢……你……”
[EN] Moments later, another quiet murmur drifted out: "I... like... you..."
[ZH] 青瓜第一次听到公子说喜欢某人，不由兴致满满，凑近问：“公子喜欢谁？”
[EN] Qinggua's ears perked up. Leaning close, he whispered, "Who do you like, Young Master?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇梦中呓语，含含糊糊地说出一个名字：“朱……淑……真……”
[EN] Through sluggish sleep, Chen Haoyu slurred three syllables: "Zhu... Shu... zhen..."
[ZH] 尽管模糊，青瓜还是听明白，脸上露出笑容，自语道：“原来公子悄悄喜欢上少夫人， 他醒着时，自己都不知道，实在好玩儿。真希望少夫人和公子能好起来，我们做下人的生活也好回归正常。”
[EN] Blurry as it sounded, Qinggua caught every syllable and broke into a wide grin. "Young Master fell for Young Madam without even knowing it while awake! Hilarious! Hope they really make up so life gets normal again!"
[ZH] 熟睡的陈浩宇梦见自己被朱淑真一拳打得跌进坑里，一下子被吓醒，揉揉眼睛，坐直后问道：“什么时辰？”
[EN] Right then, dreaming of Zhu Shuzhen punching him into a ditch, Chen Haoyu jerked awake rubbing his eyes. "What hour is it?"
[ZH] 青瓜回道：“申时。”
[EN] "Hour of the Monkey—mid-afternoon."
[ZH] “都申时了，我一点儿都没背过，怎么办？”
[EN] "Already?! I didn't memorize a single line! What do I do?"
[ZH] “没办法，只能继续背。”
[EN] "Only thing to do—cram right now!"
[ZH] 青瓜话音刚落，红豆走进来：“姑爷，背书时间已结束，该到小姐跟前背上一背。”
[EN] Before he could grab the book, Hongdou stepped in. "Study time's up, Young Master. Miss is waiting to test you."
[ZH] 陈浩宇问：“能不能再让我背会儿？”
[EN] "Give me a few more minutes?"
[ZH] “不行！小姐定的时间，不能改！”
[EN] "No! Miss's schedule doesn't bend!"
[ZH] “不行就不行，声音那么大干什么？”
[EN] "Fine, no need to yell!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇悻悻地起身往出走，青瓜跟在身后担心不已，公子一句没背，去了只能被劈头盖脸一顿臭骂。
[EN] Dragging his feet, Chen Haoyu headed out with Qinggua trailing in dread, bracing for an eruption.

#CH 235
[ZH] 陈浩宇来到素心草堂，屋子里到处充满书卷之气，他竟然破天荒地觉得这个气味挺好闻。
[ZH] 朱淑真坐在桌边品茶，她不发怒的样子着实好看，难怪仪王对她倾心，连他陈浩宇此时都有些心神摇荡。
[ZH] 他晃晃脑袋，心想：“我怎么会有这种感觉？还是清醒清醒。”
[ZH] 红豆行礼道：“小姐，姑爷来了。”
[ZH] 千里寻放下茶盏：“知道了，你去忙吧。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续瞧着朱淑真，依然忍不住心绪荡漾：“母老虎要是温柔些，应该是个不错的娘子，又有才华，还会功夫。重要的是会功夫，我要是在外面受了什么委屈，还能有个人帮我出气。”
[ZH] “陈浩宇，为何盯着老娘看？”
[ZH] 对方没有一点儿温柔之气的话语，令陈浩宇清醒许多：“我来见你，当然得盯着你，难道让我盯着苦荞？或是我身后的青瓜不成？”
[ZH] 千里寻懒得与他多言：“说说你都背了哪些内容？”
[ZH] “时间太短，都没背过。”
[ZH] “什么？都没背过？我可是给了你多半个时辰，竟然都没背过！难道连一首诗也没背过？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇点点头。
[ZH] “苦荞！拿戒尺来！”
[ZH] 苦荞取来戒尺，双手递给小姐。
[ZH] 千里寻接过，起身走到陈浩宇身边：“把手伸出来！”
[ZH] 连陈浩宇自己都觉得奇怪，他竟然乖乖伸出手，像个做错事的孩子。
[ZH] 对方如此配合，没有一点儿反抗，倒令千里寻感到意外：“你怎么突然变得这么乖？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇趁机使出软招：“以后我不再反抗，你让我做什么我都会乖乖做。”
[ZH] 千里寻顿时兴致全无：“你的手，老娘才懒得打，还得留着给我收拾屋子。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇心中窃喜，软招还真是管用，总算拿捏住朱淑真。他悄悄向青瓜竖起大拇指，青瓜第一次被公子夸奖，心里乐开了花。
[ZH] 千里寻把手中的戒尺递给苦荞，坐回书桌旁：“接下来，你把我屋子里里外外的灰尘擦一遍，不许青瓜帮忙。记住，不准靠近我的书桌，保持一米开外。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续发挥软招的作用：“擦灰尘的事再简单不过，本公子会做，不用青瓜帮忙，不过他帮着洗个抹布应该没有问题吧？”
[ZH] 千里寻总觉得陈浩宇突然之间像变了一个人，她极其不适应：“少废话，赶紧干活。”
[ZH] 千里寻继续提笔写话本，只当屋子里只有她与苦荞。
[ZH] 陈浩宇伸出双手，青瓜上前帮公子把袖子向上挽起，再把布子递到公子手中。陈浩宇开始认真擦灰尘，边擦边瞧向朱淑真，她专心写字的样子一点儿不像一只母老虎。
[ZH] 想起去年在西湖游玩时，见到朱淑真与乔文仙约会的场景，乔文仙搂着她站在船头。
[ZH] 那时陈浩宇想不通，为何母老虎能在手无缚鸡之力的乔文仙怀里那么温柔？现在他终于明白，肯定是因为母老虎喜欢乔文仙，才会表现出女人的一面。
[ZH] 母老虎花落陈家，成了他陈浩宇的娘子，如果她能喜欢上他，说不定会温柔得跟一只小猫咪一样，到时自己的日子绝对好过许多。
[ZH] 可如何让母老虎喜欢上自己？
[ZH] 陈浩宇越想越觉得沮丧，母老虎喜欢过乔文仙，乔文仙不仅有才华，还天生一副令临安姑娘们神魂颠倒的外表。再就是仪王，为了母老虎连抢亲的事都能干得出，人家要身份有身份，要拳头有拳头，他陈浩宇什么都没有，母老虎怎么可能会喜欢他？
[EN] Stepping into Suxin Thatched Hall, Chen Haoyu breathed in the crisp scent of ink and paper—and for the first time in his life found it pleasant.
[ZH] 瞧到站在一旁的青瓜，长得那个样子与丑丫十分相配，偏偏喜欢红豆，不如从他身上找回些自信，于是向青瓜勾勾手。
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen sat sipping tea at her desk. Without a scowl on her face, she really was striking—no mystery why Prince Yi lost his head over her, when even Chen Haoyu felt his own pulse skip. Shaking his head, he scolded himself: Snap out of it!
[ZH] 青瓜见状靠近公子，两人都压低声音。
[EN] "Young Master is here, Miss," Hongdou announced.
[ZH] “公子何事？”
[EN] Setting down her bowl, Qianli Xun dismissed her. "Got it. Go tend your chores."
[ZH] “想请教个事儿。”
[EN] Unable to peel his eyes off her, Chen Haoyu mused silently: If the tigress softened up a bit, she'd be a catch—gifted with a brush, lethal with her fists. Best of all, if anyone messed with me outside, I'd have a wife who could flatten them!
[ZH] “公子请说。”
[EN] "What are you gawking at, Chen Haoyu?"
[ZH] “如果有人说你根本配不上红豆，你还会追求她吗？”
[EN] Her sharp bark snapped him back to earth. "Came to report to you—who else am I supposed to look at, Kuqiao or Qinggua?"
[ZH] “当然，别人说的不重要，重要的是我喜欢红豆，不需要想那么多。”
[EN] "Skip the banter. What did you memorize?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇又给青瓜竖起大拇指。
[EN] "Too short on time—didn't get any memorized."
[ZH] 第二次被公子夸，青瓜更是开心。公子又开始认真擦灰尘，他继续跟在一旁等着更换干净的布子。
[EN] "None?! I gave you over an hour and you didn't learn one line? Not a single poem?!"
[ZH] 青瓜的话给了陈浩宇启示，想那么多干什么？想怎么做就怎么做。乔文仙和仪王再优秀，母老虎还不是嫁给自己。说明老天都觉得她适合给他陈浩宇当娘子，他还担心个什么劲儿？不如放开手脚大胆去做，不信母老虎不会喜欢上他。
[EN] Chen Haoyu nodded sheepishly.
[ZH] 信心一足，擦起屋子来更起劲儿。半个时辰后，除了书桌，其他地方都被陈浩宇认真擦过一遍。
[EN] "Kuqiao! Fetch the bamboo ruler!"
[ZH] 苦荞负责检查，她用手指在擦过的地方摸了一摸，仔细瞧瞧手指，满意道：“没想到姑爷除了会逛青楼，擦灰尘的功夫不比丫鬟差。”
[EN] Kuqiao handed over the disciplinary ruler. Rising, Qianli Xun stepped in front of him. "Hold out your palm!"
[ZH] “怎么说话呢？你家姑爷我打今儿起不会再去青楼。我娶了娘子，是有家室的人，再往青楼跑会被人说三道四，家里的娘子心里肯定不好受。”
[EN] Shocking even himself, Chen Haoyu held his hand straight out like a chastised schoolboy.
[ZH] 陈浩宇边说边瞧向朱淑真，她显然没把他的话当回事。只见朱淑真放下笔：“青瓜，去把阿升叫来。”
[EN] His docile obedience caught her flat-footed. "Since when do you turn meek?"
[ZH] 苦荞见小姐已写完，上前将手稿整理好，放在老地方。
[EN] Launching straight into his soft approach, Chen Haoyu smiled. "No more fighting you. Whatever you order, I'll obey."
[ZH] 等阿升的过程中，陈浩宇继续仔细观察朱淑真。她随手拿起一本书翻阅，翻到一页，停下来，用心看，专注的样子令陈浩宇看得出神。
[EN] Instant buzzkill. Qianli Xun tossed the ruler back to Kuqiao and returned to her desk. "Not wasting energy smacking your hand—need it intact to clean my room."
[ZH] 真想不通书有什么好看的？她为何那么认真？
[EN] Score! The soft touch worked like magic! Behind his back, Chen Haoyu flashed a hidden thumbs-up to Qinggua, making the servant beam with pride at his first-ever praise.
[ZH] 陈浩宇觉得要让母老虎喜欢上自己，还需懂些书本上的知识才行，打明日起，好好背书，让她刮目相看。
[EN] "Wipe down every speck of dust inside and out of this hall," Qianli Xun ordered. "Qinggua doesn't lift a finger—and stay at least three feet clear of my desk."
[ZH] 正想着，阿升进来：“小姐，有何吩咐？”
[EN] "Dusting's a cinch—I'll do every bit myself! Letting Qinggua rinse the rag is fine, right?"
[ZH] 千里寻放下手中的书：“说说陈浩宇跑步的情况。”
[EN] Unnerved by this sudden personality swap, Qianli Xun snapped, "Less mouth, more dusting!" and went back to writing her huaben, ignoring him.
[ZH] 阿升照实说：“姑爷总共跑了八圈半，中间停了两次，一次看热闹，一次跟大公子到亭子里聊天儿。”
[EN] Rolling up his sleeves with Qinggua's help, Chen Haoyu took a damp cloth and started wiping shelves, sneaking glances toward her desk. Lost in writing, she looked nothing like a tigress.
[ZH] 千里寻正色道：“如此看来，四项任务只完成两项。陈浩宇，从明日开始，连续给老娘倒两日马桶，听到没？”
[EN] He remembered spotting her on West Lake last year nestled gentle as silk in Qiao Wenxian's arms. Back then he couldn't fathom why a fierce woman melted for a scholar with zero strength; now it clicked—she loved him. Now she was Mrs. Chen; if she ever fell for Chen Haoyu, she'd turn soft as a kitten too!
[ZH] 心中再次生起怒气，陈浩宇努力压服住，像念咒语一般在心里提醒自己：“软招，软招，使软招；软招，软招，使软招……”
[EN] Then gloom hit: how could he compete with Qiao Wenxian's literary polish and pretty face, or Prince Yi's royal rank and martial prowess? He had nothing!
[ZH] 这招还真灵，轻松将怒气压服住，他面带笑容：“娘子说什么就是什么，我都听娘子的。”
[EN] Glancing at plain-faced Qinggua—built for Chouya yet chasing Hongdou anyway—sparked an idea. He beckoned Qinggua close and murmured, "Quick question: if everybody told you you're way out of Hongdou's league, would you still chase her?"
[ZH] 此话一出，千里寻惊讶之余，甚是不习惯：“不许这么称呼老娘！”
[EN] "Course! Who cares what outsiders think? I like her—that's all that matters!"
[ZH] “怎么称呼，娘子教我。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu flashed a second thumbs-up, sending Qinggua soaring. Right—screw overthinking! For all their pedigree, she still ended up married to me! Proof Heaven picked me for her—dive in and win her over!
[ZH] “还这么称呼？”
[EN] Fired up, he scrubbed every beam, shelf, and chair for a full hour until the room shone. Kuqiao swiped a testing finger across a sill and nodded impressed. "Aside from haunting brothels, you dust just as clean as a maid, Young Master!"
[ZH] “淑真。”
[EN] "Watch that tongue—your young master is done with brothels for good! I'm a married man now; hanging out in brothels invites gossip and hurts my wife's feelings."
[ZH] “你是不是吃错药了？把姓加上。”
[EN] Glancing toward Zhu Shuzhen—who ignored the speech completely—he watched her lay down her brush. "Qinggua, fetch Asheng."
[ZH] “朱淑真。”
[EN] While Kuqiao stowed the manuscript and Qinggua went for Asheng, Qianli Xun flipped open a book and read absorbed, leaving Chen Haoyu staring in wonder. What's so gripping in those pages? If he wanted her respect, he needed some book learning—starting tomorrow, he'd actually study!
[ZH] 还是以前的三个字，可现在从陈浩宇的嘴里吐出，跟以往的感觉一点儿都不一样，这家伙到底是故意的，还是真的变了？
[EN] Asheng stepped in and bowed. "Your orders, Miss?"
[ZH] 千里寻觉得才惩罚一日，要说改变应该不可能，肯定是故意，说不定是陈浩宇想出的新招数。
[EN] "Report on Chen Haoyu's running."
[ZH] 面对硬招，千里寻可见招拆招；面对软招，她还真有些不知如何招架，提醒自己千万不要被对方迷惑。
[EN] "Eight and a half laps total, Miss. Stopped twice—once to watch a commotion, once to chat with Eldest Young Master in a pavilion."
[ZH] “苦荞，把钱拿来。”
[EN] Qianli Xun's face turned stern. "Two tasks passed out of four. Starting tomorrow, Chen Haoyu, you empty my chamber pot two days straight. Clear?"
[ZH] 苦荞取来钱，放进姑爷手里：“一两银子五十文，姑爷可拿好了。我家小姐履行承诺，也希望姑爷明日认真接受惩罚。”
[EN] Hot anger flared in his chest—and he hammered it down with an inner chant: Soft touch, soft touch, use the soft touch! Soft touch, soft touch, use the soft touch!
[ZH] 陈浩宇体会到软招的妙处，再说他对母老虎隐隐约约已产生不同以往的感觉，干脆做得更彻底一些。他把钱还给苦荞，面向朱淑真：“既然是惩罚，怎么能给我银子使？我一文钱不要，心甘情愿接受所有惩罚。”
[EN] Miracle—the rage melted. Breaking into a sunny smile, he said, "Whatever Wife says goes! I listen to Wife!"
[ZH] 别说苦荞诧异，千里寻更是诧异。
[EN] Qianli Xun froze, goosebumps rising. "Don't call me that!"
[ZH] 苦荞为难道：“小姐，该怎么办？”
[EN] "Teach me what to call you then, Wife!"
[ZH] 千里寻再次提醒自己不要被对方的伪装迷惑：“还能怎么办？他既然不要，你就拿去西院分给下人们，就当是陈浩宇给大家挣的银子。”
[EN] "Still saying it?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇听出来，不过是朱淑真激他生气的招数而已，便把咒语在心中又念两遍：“软招，软招，使软招；软招，软招，使软招。”
[EN] "Shuzhen."
[ZH] 然后露出笑脸：“对，听少夫人的，尽管拿去给下人分，本公子以后日日给你们赚银子，放心，明日赚得更多，让你们有好日子过。分钱时，别忘了我身边的青瓜，多少也给他分些。”
[EN] "Swallow the wrong pill? Put the surname back on!"
[ZH] 青瓜高兴道：“谢公子！”
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen."
[ZH] 千里寻心中纳闷，陈浩宇怎么变得这么厉害？她真有些招架不住，还是赶紧换个话题。
[EN] Same three syllables, yet delivered in a warm tone totally unlike before. Was he faking some fresh scheme? One day of punishment couldn't rewrite a man overnight! Hard resistance she could smash; this slippery sweetness threw her off balance. Do not fall for it!
[ZH] “现在说说明日之事，除了给我倒马桶，还需将跑步和背书两件事做好。明日的劳动任务……”千里寻想了想，院子难打扫些，不如交给陈浩宇，“明日你负责打扫正院，由阿升检查，不合格依然要倒马桶；至于道歉之事……”
[EN] "Kuqiao, bring his pay."
[ZH] 千里寻瞅向青瓜：“你经常欺负青瓜，明日就当着所有墨香苑下人的面，给他道歉。”
[EN] Kuqiao placed the coins into his palm. "One tael of silver and fifty wen, Young Master. Miss honors her word, and expects you to take your punishment tomorrow."
[ZH] 青瓜自知公子不乐意，肯定会生气，赶紧说：“少夫人，还是算了，公子欺负其他下人是不对，欺负我是应该的。”
[EN] Deciding to double down on the soft touch—already feeling strange new warmth toward her anyway—Chen Haoyu pressed the money straight back into Kuqiao's hand and faced Qianli Xun. "Since this is punishment, how can I pocket wages? Keep every copper—I accept every penalty willingly."
[ZH] 陈浩宇又将咒语在心中念一遍：“软招，软招，使软招。”
[EN] Both maid and mistress stared stunned.
[ZH] 心情果然轻松许多：“青瓜，别这么说，明日本公子就向你道歉。”
[EN] "Miss, what do I do with it?" Kuqiao stammered.
[ZH] 千里寻硬撑着，装出一副冷漠的样子，还是关注到陈浩宇脸上的淤青：“苦荞，拿瓶疗伤的药来。”
[EN] Refusing to bend to his act, Qianli Xun countered, "Take it to the West Courtyard and split it among the servants—treat it as silver Chen Haoyu earned for them!"
[ZH] 苦荞把手中的银钱装好，到里屋取瓶药递到陈浩宇手中：“姑爷，这瓶药效果极好，涂上一次，明日您脸上的淤青会完全消失。”
[EN] Catching the bait meant to make him blow up, Chen Haoyu chanted silently twice more—Soft touch, soft touch!—and beamed wide. "Listen to Young Madam! Split it up, and I'll earn even more tomorrow so everyone eats well! Don't forget a share for my boy Qinggua!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇兴奋地接过，母老虎破天荒第一次关心他，照这个节奏下去，不用熬多久，苦日子就到头了。
[EN] "Thank you, Young Master!" Qinggua cheered.
[ZH] 千里寻不愿再面对不一样的陈浩宇，装作不耐烦的样子：“赶紧回博雅斋，少在这里烦老娘。”
[EN] Thoroughly thrown off stride, Qianli Xun hurried onto tomorrow's slate. "Besides emptying my chamber pot, complete your full ten laps and memorization. Tomorrow's chore: sweep the entire main courtyard, inspected by Asheng—fail and earn another chamber-pot day. As for your apology..." Her gaze slid to Qinggua. "Apologize to Qinggua in front of every servant in Moxiang Courtyard."
[ZH] 陈浩宇等人离开后，屋里剩下千里寻和苦荞两人。
[EN] Panicked his master would snap, Qinggua rushed in, "No need, Young Madam! Bullying other servants was wrong, bullying me is fine!"
[ZH] 苦荞才大胆问：“小姐，我怎么感觉姑爷像变了个人？”
[EN] One silent chant later, Chen Haoyu smiled easy. "Don't talk like that, Qinggua. Tomorrow I'll apologize proper."
[ZH] “错觉，一定是错觉，陈浩宇怎么可能这么快变好？不过是他想出的新招数而已，等着瞧，过不了两日他肯定会原形毕露。再说我的大仇未报，可不希望他这么快改变。”
[EN] Keeping her mask icy, Qianli Xun's eyes caught the angry welt still darkening his cheek. "Kuqiao, bring a jar of bruise salve."
[ZH] 苦荞提醒道：“小姐，他可是姑爷，变好对您只有好处，没有坏处。”
[EN] Kuqiao fetched ointment from the inner room and handed it over. "Apply once tonight, Young Master, and the bruise clears by morning."
[ZH] “什么意思？难道你不希望我报仇？”
[EN] Thrilled—her first sign of care!—Chen Haoyu clutched the jar like treasure while Qianli Xun waved them off gruffly. "Get back to Boya Study and quit clogging my air!"
[ZH] 苦荞为难道：“我当然希望小姐报仇，要是散播谣言的人是别人，我肯定会帮小姐狠狠扇那人耳光，可他是姑爷，我不希望小姐再和离一次，只希望小姐好。若姑爷能变好，跟小姐您好好过日子，岂不是两全其美？”
[EN] Once they left, Kuqiao ventured, "Miss, doesn't Young Master seem like a whole new man?"
[ZH] “他即使真变好，跟我也没任何关系。”
[EN] "Illusion! Pure illusion!" Qianli Xun scoffed. "Nobody reforms in two days. Just some new trick—watch him show his true colors in a couple days! Besides, my revenge isn't done—don't want him reforming yet!"
[ZH] 千里寻认为她能与陈浩宇相安无事地相处，已是最大让步，根本不可能对他产生感情，更不可能愿意跟他相濡以沫安心过日子。
[EN] "He's your husband, Miss. Him turning good only helps you."
[ZH] 天已黑，博雅斋内，青瓜将苦荞给的药为公子涂在淤青处，边涂边夸：“公子，您刚才面对少夫人一直是软言软语，效果很明显，少夫人都不好意思对您发火。”
[EN] "You don't want me getting payback?"
[ZH] “还是你聪明，给我出这么好的主意，等本公子的生活恢复正常，定要好好奖赏你。”
[EN] "If anyone else spread those lies, I'd slap them senseless myself! Yet he's your husband, Miss—I don't want you going through another divorce! If he shapes up and treats you right, isn't that the best of both worlds?"
[ZH] “不用公子奖赏，您只需履行承诺，帮我追到红豆即可。”
[EN] "Even if he turns into a saint, it's nothing to me." Keeping polite peace was her max; falling for him was out of the question.
[ZH] 陈浩宇感到为难：“我看红豆对你没意思，要不换个人？丑丫不错，都不用你使力气，直接倒贴。”
[EN] After dark in Boya Study, Qinggua dabbed salve across his master's bruised cheek, beaming. "Soft words worked wonders, Young Master! Young Madam couldn't even stay mad at you!"
[ZH] 青瓜把药瓶放好，坚决地说：“倒贴的我不要，我只要红豆。”
[EN] "Smart plan, kid—once my life's back to normal, I'll reward you big."
[ZH] “好好好，等本公子把朱淑真搞定，想办法撮合你跟红豆。”
[EN] "Skip rewards—just keep your promise and help me win Hongdou!"
[ZH] 这一日应该是陈浩宇有生以来心情变化最大的日子，他一生中都不会忘记，更不会忘记第一个让他流下眼泪的女子。
[EN] "Hongdou's clearly not into you—why not switch? Chouya's practically throwing herself at you!"
[ZH] 要问他为什么会喜欢第一个让自己流下眼泪的女子，他也无法给出确切的答案，当时狐朋狗友问起，他就是这么想的，认真中带了些许随意。只是没想到说过的话今日应验。
[EN] Setting the jar down, Qinggua stood firm. "Don't want easy—I want Hongdou."
[ZH] 喜欢一个姑娘的感觉真是不错，以前母老虎身上他看不顺眼的地方竟然变得可爱起来。
[EN] "Fine, fine, once I win Zhu Shuzhen over, I'll play matchmaker for you and Hongdou."
[ZH] 陈浩宇不由自主傻傻地笑出声来：“呵呵……！”
[EN] Left alone with his thoughts, Chen Haoyu realized today had flipped his entire world upside down. He would never forget the first woman who ever made him shed a tear—an offhand tavern vow suddenly come true. Falling for a woman felt dizzy and sweet; every sharp quirk of his tigress suddenly looked adorable. A goofy grin stretched across his face, spilling into a silly chuckle. "Hehe..."
[ZH] 青瓜见公子沉默一会儿后，傻笑起来，心想：“明日都要倒马桶，竟然还笑得出！”
[EN] Staring at him, Qinggua shook his head: Emptying a chamber pot tomorrow morning, and he's sitting here giggling like a fool!

#CH 236
[ZH] 直到阿升把博雅斋的门从外面关好，传来上锁的声音，陈浩宇才从傻乐中清醒过来：“又锁门？肯定是怕本公子跑去春香楼，还不快些承认喜欢我？放心，你现在赶本公子去春香楼，本公子也不会去。”
[ZH] 青瓜摸摸公子的额头：“没发热啊？怎么说起胡话来？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇一把将青瓜的手打到一边：“瞎摸什么？本公子怎么可能发热？”
[ZH] “公子，您认为少夫人把门锁起来是喜欢您？怕您去春香楼？”
[ZH] “要不然呢？”
[ZH] 青瓜担心道：“该不会软招有后遗症？要不您怎么说起胡话来？”
[ZH] “什么胡话？是真心话。”
[ZH] “难道公子以后真的不再去春香楼？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇手撑着下巴，脸上露出美滋滋的表情：“春香楼有什么意思？本公子玩腻了，现在只想守在家里，守在墨香苑。”
[ZH] 青瓜更加确定公子喜欢上少夫人：“真好，以后我们就有好日子过。”
[ZH] “你怎么变化这么快？刚才还认为我说胡话，这会儿又高兴？”
[ZH] 青瓜做好躲避的准备，小心问：“公子该不会是喜欢上少夫人？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇并未介意：“有这么明显吗？竟然都被你瞧出来。没想到本公子喜欢上母老虎，说出去谁会信？连我自己都觉得像是做梦。”
[ZH] “是像做梦，我也感觉不真实。”青瓜倒上一盏茶，为公子放在桌上，“您白天还对少夫人骂骂咧咧，到晚上就喜欢上她，会不会太快了些？”
[ZH] “难道你没听过一个词：瞬息万变？”
[ZH] 青瓜想到公子白天说的梦话：“公子，您认真想想，会不会以前已喜欢上少夫人，只是您自己还没意识到。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇仔细回忆，自从永泰茶楼被朱淑真打，又在竹林偶遇，从此两人真正结下梁子，他有事没事揣摩如何报复她，朱淑真三个字整日在脑子里打转，此前他从未对哪个女子如此动过心思。
[ZH] 难道她就是这样住进他的心里？他只是一直被报复之心迷惑而不自知？直到今日因被她折磨感到委屈流下一滴眼泪，才真正明白自己的心意？看来是这样。
[ZH] “没想到你还能看懂本公子，以前怎么没发现你有这本事？”
[ZH] 又被公子夸，青瓜觉得荣耀，老实交代：“您白天趴在书桌上睡着，梦里唤少夫人的名字，还说您喜欢她，我就站在您身边，听得一清二楚。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇更是美滋滋的，他终于真心喜欢上一位姑娘，还鬼使神差地把她娶回家：“青瓜，从明日起，你家公子我要改变，让朱淑真对我刮目相看。”
[ZH] 青瓜提醒道：“公子不用等到明日，您今日的软言软语说得不错，我发现少夫人都快招架不住。”
[EN] Only when Asheng pulled Boya Study's door shut and snapped the padlock did Chen Haoyu snap out of his dreamy grin. "Locking me in again? Scared I'll sneak off to Chunxiang Brothel—just admit you like me already! Relax, even if you chased me out to the brothel with a broom, I wouldn't go!"
[ZH] “嘿嘿嘿……，我终于找到对付她的法宝，真没想到，原来她吃这一套，以后我可得好好利用软招，直到把她软成娇滴滴的美娇娘，倒在本公子的怀里，她就不会总用拳头对付我，说不定还会用拳头保护我，嘿嘿嘿……”
[EN] Qinggua pressed the back of his hand to his master's forehead. "No fever... why are you talking crazy?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇畅想着朱淑真躺在他怀里的情景，笑得哈喇子都快流出来。
[EN] Chen Haoyu swatted his hand away. "Quit poking me! Why would I have a fever?"
[ZH] 第二天，阿升和三宝早早来到博雅斋，把门打开，叫醒陈浩宇。
[EN] "You think Young Madam locks your door because she likes you and worries you'll visit brothels?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇从未起过这么早，本想大骂两句，想到自己的感情，立刻来了精神，在青瓜的伺候下穿好衣裳，收拾完毕，各自给脸上绑块布，用来遮盖口鼻。
[EN] "What else?!"
[ZH] 来到素心草堂门外，阿升轻轻敲了敲门。
[EN] "Does the soft-touch plan cause brain damage? Otherwise why the gibberish?"
[ZH] 门被打开，苦荞手提马桶，放在门口，一句话没说，又把门关上。
[EN] "Not gibberish—God's honest truth!"
[ZH] 一股臭气袭来，阿升和三宝捂住鼻子，把头偏向一边。陈浩宇和青瓜虽绑着布子，可一点儿用处都没有，只觉一阵恶心。
[EN] "You're seriously done with Chunxiang Brothel?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇心想：“拉这么臭的屎，跟你的外表太不相配，算了，谁让本公子喜欢上你，这点儿小毛病忽略不计。”
[EN] Propping his chin on his palm with a dreamy smile, Chen Haoyu sighed. "What's fun about Chunxiang Brothel? Sick of it! All I want now is staying home right here in Moxiang Courtyard."
[ZH] “青瓜，愣着干什么？还不赶快提了马桶去倒？”
[EN] Certain now, Qinggua beamed. "Awesome! Good days ahead for us!"
[ZH] 公子命令，青瓜不敢不照做，忍着臭气，拎起马桶，往茅厕方向而去。
[EN] "Flipped fast, didn't you? Calling me crazy one second, grinning the next!"
[ZH] 阿升和三宝被熏得不行，懒得再去理会。
[EN] Bracing to dodge just in case, Qinggua ventured, "Young Master... did you fall for Young Madam?"
[ZH] 青瓜走远后，臭气才渐渐消散。
[EN] Far from snapping, Chen Haoyu blinked. "That obvious? Even you spotted it? Me falling for the tigress—tell anyone and they'll call me insane! Feels like a dream even to me."
[ZH] 屋内，苦荞点燃香料开始熏屋子，边熏边说：“太臭了，难道明日您还要这样？”
[EN] "Sure feels unreal!" Pouring tea for his master, Qinggua marvelled, "Cursing her all morning, smitten by night—isn't that a bit fast?"
[ZH] 千里寻早已穿好衣裳，坐在茶桌前盯着茶具发呆，听到苦荞的问题，回道：“当然，再忍一日。”
[EN] "Ever heard the phrase 'shifting in a heartbeat'?"
[ZH] “我现在只想祈祷姑爷今日事事都做得好，否则我不得天天一大早出去搞些臭粪放在马桶里，恐怕以后素心草堂变成臭粪草堂。”
[EN] Remembering the naptime mumble, Qinggua asked, "Think about it, Young Master—maybe you liked her ages ago without realizing it?"
[ZH] 苦荞的牢骚千里寻听到心里，她也觉受罪，那股子臭气熏得她到现在胃里都不舒服。她并未回应苦荞，继续想着内心之事。
[EN] Replaying the past made everything click: ever since she beat him at Yongtai Teahouse and crossed paths in the bamboo grove, he had obsessed day and night over getting back at her, her name looping nonstop inside his head—more thought than he had ever spent on any other woman. Had she snuck into his heart behind his grudge, until today's tear tore off the blindfold? Had to be!
[ZH] 一早起来，小姐就坐在茶桌旁一言不发。开始苦荞没当回事，现在才觉出不对来，手拿熏香，上前问道：“小姐，您看上去不高兴，是不是哪里不舒服？”
[EN] "Didn't realize you could read me so clear!"
[ZH] 千里寻才开口问：“苦荞，你觉得我报复陈浩宇对还是不对？”
[EN] Beaming at yet another compliment, Qinggua confessed, "You muttered Young Madam's name in your sleep this afternoon saying you liked her—heard every word."
[ZH] 苦荞毫不犹豫：“当然对！姑爷散播谣言，就应该受到惩罚。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu glowed brighter—falling in love for the first time in his life, and by sheer crazy luck already married to her! "Starting tomorrow, Qinggua, I'm turning over a new leaf to blow Zhu Shuzhen's socks off!"
[ZH] “为何我已开始报复，却一点儿都不开心？”
[EN] "No need to wait till tomorrow—your soft talk today already had her reeling!"
[ZH] “那是因为小姐心善，见不得别人遭罪。”
[EN] "Hehehe! Found her kryptonite at last! Keep laying on the soft touch until she melts into a sweet little bride curling right into my arms—no more punching me, maybe even using those fists to guard me! Hehehe!" Picturing it nearly had him drooling.
[ZH] 千里寻品味着内心，真的是因为她善良吗？她觉得不尽然：“让陈浩宇倒马桶，臭到他的同时，我们也被臭到，所以说报复他人，自己势必会受到反噬，也不知值不值得？”
[EN] At dawn, Asheng and Sanbao unlocked the door and roused him. Normally waking this early would have set him cursing; energized by love, he hopped straight up, dressed with Qinggua's help, and both tied strips of cloth across their noses and mouths.
[ZH] 小姐冷不丁说这段话，苦荞听明白一半：“难道您不想再报复姑爷？”
[EN] Outside Suxin Thatched Hall, Asheng tapped the door. Kuqiao cracked it open, set a chamber pot on the threshold without a word, and slammed it shut.
[ZH] “想，怎能不想？”千里寻沉默一会儿，继续说，“只是不知是否值得？因为我一点儿高兴的感觉都没有。”
[EN] A pungent stench slammed out like a wall. Asheng and Sanbao pinched their noses and turned away; even through cloth masks, Chen Haoyu and Qinggua gagged.
[ZH] 苦荞不知该如何回应，屋子熏得差不多，估摸着姑爷等人已离开，便放下熏香，把门打开，再将窗户开得大些。
[EN] Whoa, stinky business doesn't match that pretty face at all—never mind, love overlooks small flaws! Chen Haoyu mused, barking, "Quit freezing, Qinggua! Grab the pot and haul it to the latrine!"
[ZH] 用过早饭，大概过了两刻钟，陈浩宇开始继续跑圈，今日他跑得心甘情愿，乐在其中。竟然神奇地发现原来跑步也不错，而且越跑越轻松。
[EN] Unable to refuse, Qinggua held his breath, grabbed the handle, and bolted toward the latrine while Asheng and Sanbao backed off gasping until the air cleared.
[ZH] 跑着跑着，半道上又碰见东哥，没等陈浩东说话，他边跑边打招呼：“东哥，我得完成跑步任务，不能跟你聊，否则还得倒马桶，回见！”
[EN] Inside, Kuqiao waved a lit censer around the hall grumbling, "Reeks to high heaven! Are we really doing this again tomorrow?!"
[ZH] 陈浩东目睹堂弟与三个下人跑远，心中纳闷：“浩宇怎么看上去很开心？难道我昨日出的主意泡汤？看来八成是这样。哼！你竟然被罚还这么开心，让人更来气，我得再想想办法，怎么能让你被母老虎欺负得更惨些。”
[EN] Already dressed and staring blankly at her tea set, Qianli Xun answered, "One more day."
[ZH] 经过花匠居住之地时，陈浩宇向一片玫瑰园瞧去，之前经过这里时毫无感觉，今日经过突然产生了想法，对身旁陪跑的青瓜吩咐道：“你去给花匠说说，给我准备一束玫瑰花。”
[EN] "Pray Young Master nails every chore today, or I'll be sneaking out at dawn every morning scooping stinky slop into that pot until Suxin Thatched Hall turns into Stinky-Slop Hall!"
[ZH] 有机会不用陪公子跑步，青瓜当然乐意，迅速撤离，向花匠居住的屋子走去。跟花匠说明来意，花匠自然屁颠屁颠地搞了一束玫瑰花，担心刺扎到手，找来一个普通的瓷瓶，装些水，放进去。
[EN] Truth be told, Qianli Xun's own stomach still rolled from the fumes. Watching her mistress sit stone-silent since waking, Kuqiao grew concerned and stepped up with the censer. "You look down, Miss—feeling sick?"
[ZH] 青瓜心里细数，公子才跑完五圈，不如趁机躲起来，免得再陪公子跑步。青瓜就近找到一个隐蔽的木廊，将花瓶放在长凳上，他则靠着木柱呼呼大睡。
[EN] "Kuqiao," Qianli Xun asked quietly, "am I right or wrong taking revenge on Chen Haoyu?"
[ZH] 直到一坨鸟粪掉在脸上，青瓜才被惊醒，用手摸摸脸，放在鼻子跟前闻了闻，臭烘烘的。在旁边拽了几片植物的叶子，将手和脸上的鸟粪擦干净，瞧瞧太阳，估摸时间差不多，抱起瓷瓶，赶到主道上等公子。
[EN] "Right, of course! He spread those rumors—he deserves punishment!"
[ZH] 等了不多会儿，陈浩宇与阿升、三宝跑过来，此处是目的地，最后一圈终于跑完。陈浩宇累得不行，二话不说，一屁股坐在路边的石头上。
[EN] "Then why don't I feel happy one bit?"
[ZH] 阿升夸赞道：“姑爷今日表现不错。”
[EN] "Because you have a soft heart, Miss, and hate watching anyone suffer."
[ZH] 陈浩宇并未说什么，抬眼看到青瓜怀里抱着一瓶玫瑰花走过来。
[EN] Probing her own chest, she felt it ran deeper than mere softness. "Making him empty a chamber pot stank us out right along with him. Hurting someone bounces straight back onto yourself. Wonder if it's even worth it?"
[ZH] 他的脸上露出笑容：“花不错，瓶子成色差些，一会儿回去把博雅斋的绿色瓶子换上。”
[EN] Half grasping her drift, Kuqiao asked, "Thinking of calling off the payback?"
[ZH] 歇得差不多，陈浩宇回到博雅斋，给玫瑰花换了瓶子，亲自抱在怀里，来到素心草堂门口，吩咐道：“你们两个还怕我跑了不成？老老实实站外面，别跟进来，有青瓜陪我即可。”
[EN] "I want payback—of course I do!" Qianli Xun paused. "Just wondering if it's worth it when it brings me zero joy."
[ZH] 阿升与三宝只好站在门外。
[EN] Not knowing how to reply, Kuqiao waited until the incense masked the odor and the men departed, then threw open the doors and windows for fresh air.
[ZH] 陈浩宇抱着瓶子走进屋。
[EN] After breakfast, Chen Haoyu hit the main lane for his ten laps—cheerful and willing today, surprised to find running actually felt brisk and energizing once he stopped fighting it.
[ZH] 苦荞正在茶桌前认真做茶，并未注意到。千里寻在写话本，十分专注，也不知有人走进来。
[EN] Halfway through, he spotted Chen Haodong ahead and waved without breaking stride. "Gotta finish my laps, Brother Dong—no time to chat or it's chamber-pot duty again! Catch you later!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇怀抱一瓶玫瑰花，乐滋滋地观察母老虎，怎么看怎么顺眼，开始后悔往日总是与她对着干。
[EN] Staring after his beaming cousin, Chen Haodong scowled: Why's he grinning while being punished? Yesterday's advice bombed? Irritating—need a nastier way to make the tigress wreck him!
[ZH] 苦荞做好茶，端着茶盏过来，一眼瞧到姑爷抱着一瓶玫瑰花盯着小姐傻笑。
[EN] Passing the gardeners' quarters, Chen Haoyu spotted a patch of blooming roses—never noticed them before, yet inspiration struck today. "Qinggua, go ask the gardener to cut me a bouquet of roses!"
[ZH] “姑爷，您什么时候进来的？”
[EN] Thrilled to dodge five more laps, Qinggua peeled off at once. The gardener happily snipped a lush bunch and tucked them into a plain ceramic vase of water to guard against thorns.
