Counting laps in his head, Qinggua figured he had time to kill, ducked into a shaded corridor, set the vase on a bench, and leaned against a pillar for a nap—until a splat of bird droppings hit his cheek! Wiping his face and hands clean with torn leaves, he checked the sun, grabbed the vase, and hurried back to the main road just as Chen Haoyu finished his tenth lap and dropped gasping onto a roadside boulder.
[ZH] 陈浩宇收起傻乐的表情：“进来一会儿，见你家小姐在忙，所以站着等会儿。”
[EN] "Solid run today, Young Master," Asheng commended.
[ZH] 千里寻这才搁下笔，抬起头：“你来做什么？”
[EN] Spotting Qinggua trotting up with the roses brought a grin to Chen Haoyu's face. "Gorgeous blooms—vase is cheap though. Swap it into the celadon vase in Boya Study once we get back."
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“给你送花。”
[EN] After catching his breath and switching the blooms into fine green porcelain, Chen Haoyu carried the vase himself to Suxin Thatched Hall, waving Asheng and Sanbao back at the door. "Scared I'll bolt? Wait outside—Qinggua's enough."
[ZH] 千里寻早已瞧见陈浩宇怀里的玫瑰花，再听这话，甚是诧异，一时愣住。
[EN] Stepping inside, he found Kuqiao engrossed in whisking tea and Qianli Xun bent absorbed over her writing, neither noticing him. Hugging his vase of roses, he stood gazing fondly at her, wondering why on earth he had ever picked fights with her.
[ZH] 陈浩宇吩咐身边的青瓜：“去，给少夫人把花放在桌上。”
[EN] Turning with the fresh tea bowl, Kuqiao jumped at the sight of Young Master hugging roses and grinning goofy at her mistress. "When did you walk in, Young Master?"
[ZH] 当青瓜捧着一瓶花快要走近时，千里寻立刻清醒过来，赶紧把手稿扣在桌上，目睹青瓜将花瓶放在桌上，又回到陈浩宇身边。
[EN] "A moment ago. Saw your mistress busy, so I waited."
[ZH] 苦荞看到这波操作，惊得忘记放下茶盏。
[EN] Setting down her brush, Qianli Xun looked up. "What do you want?"
[ZH] 穿越前，给千里寻送过花的男子不少，她并不会被一束花搞得心花怒放。只是陈浩宇的举动太出乎意料，一时想不通：“陈浩宇，你想做什么？”
[EN] "Brought you flowers."
[ZH] 陈浩宇话还未说，已笑得十分灿烂：“嘿嘿嘿……”
[EN] Eyes widening at the vase of roses, Qianli Xun froze speechless while Chen Haoyu nodded to Qinggua. "Set them on Young Madam's desk."
[ZH] “笑什么笑？赶紧说话！”
[EN] Snapping awake as Qinggua approached, Qianli Xun flipped her huaben manuscript face down just before the vase landed beside it, while Kuqiao stood gaping with the tea bowl still frozen in midair.
[ZH] 陈浩宇这才忍住笑：“已经这么明显，难道你瞧不出来？”
[EN] Plenty of guys had sent Qianli Xun flowers in her modern life—hardly enough to turn her head—yet seeing Chen Haoyu pull this baffled her completely. "What game are you playing, Chen Haoyu?"
[ZH] “明显什么？再不说，立刻滚出去！”
[EN] He broke into a goofy grin. "Hehehe..."
[ZH] “你生起气来也这么好看，既然想让本公子直说，我就厚着脸皮当着两个下人的面直说了。朱淑真，我喜欢你，嘿嘿嘿……”陈浩宇表白完，又乐得不行。
[EN] "Quit grinning like an idiot and speak!"
[ZH] 听到姑爷的表白，苦荞更是惊讶，手中依然端着茶盏，两只眼睛瞪得老大。
[EN] Reining in his laugh, he said, "This obvious and you still can't tell?"
[ZH] 千里寻认为定是陈浩宇耍的把戏，并未当真：“陈浩宇，你以为用这种招数，老娘就不会再报复你？想多了吧？”
[EN] "Tell what?! Spit it out or get out!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇并不生气，也不用再念咒语：“你这么认为也正常，毕竟我们两个斗了那么久，以后你慢慢就知道，我真的喜欢上你，嘿嘿，没想到我竟然娶了自己喜欢的人，乔文仙和仪王都没这福气，嘿嘿嘿……”
[EN] "Even mad you look gorgeous! Since you want it blunt, I'll swallow my pride and say it right in front of the servants: Zhu Shuzhen, I like you! Hehehe!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇傻笑的样子令千里寻心里发毛：“难道他真的喜欢上我？不管是真是假，都不能当真。”
[EN] Kuqiao's eyes popped wide, tea bowl still hovering in her hands.
[ZH] 千里寻大声说：“别笑了！”
[EN] Certain it was a scam, Qianli Xun scoffed. "Think a stunt like this makes me drop my payback? Dream on!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇很听话，立刻停止笑声。
[EN] Unbothered—no inner chant even needed anymore—he grinned easy. "Makes sense you doubt me after all our battles. Give it time and you'll see I mean it! Hehe, who'd have guessed I'd end up married to the woman I love—beats Qiao Wenxian and Prince Yi by miles! Hehehe!"
[ZH] 苦荞这才将茶盏给小姐放在桌上。
[EN] His goofy beaming gave Qianli Xun chills: Wait—is he actually serious? True or fake, never buy it! "Stop laughing!" she barked.
[ZH] 千里寻端起茶盏掩饰不自在，可不能总端着，她一饮而尽，轻轻放下茶盏：“按照昨日所说，你今日需向青瓜道歉，现在当着我的面完成任务。”
[EN] He zipped his lips instantly.
[ZH] 本以为陈浩宇昨日答应向青瓜道歉只是一时假装同意而已，今日再听到肯定会极其不爽，没想到他竟答应得相当干脆。
[EN] Kuqiao finally set the tea bowl down. Qianli Xun downed it in one gulp to hide her flustered nerves, then pointed sharp. "Per yesterday's schedule, apologize to Qinggua right now in front of me!"
[ZH] “好，我这就道歉。”陈浩宇转向青瓜，“青瓜，你家公子我经常欺负你，是我不对，今日当着少夫人的面给你说声对不起，以后再也不会欺负你。但你需明白，我虽欺负你，心里其实早已把你看成我身边唯一的跟班。”
[EN] Expecting him to bristle now that the moment arrived, she blinked as he agreed without hesitation.
[ZH] “唯一的跟班”将青瓜感动得流下眼泪：“公子，没想到我对您这么重要，早知如此，您怎么欺负我，我都受着，呜呜……”
[EN] "Done!" Turning to Qinggua, Chen Haoyu spoke earnestly. "Qinggua, I've bullied you nonstop and I was wrong. In front of Young Madam today, I'm sorry—never laying a finger on you again. Just know that rough as I was, in my heart you've always been my one true right-hand man."
[ZH] “说你贱，你还真贱。本公子既然话已说出，以后绝不会打你一拳，踢你一脚。”陈浩宇道完歉，转过身，“已道完歉，你可满意？”
[EN] "One true right-hand man" burst Qinggua's tear ducts wide open. "I didn't know I meant so much to you, Young Master! Kick me all you want—I'll gladly take it! Boo-hoo!"
[ZH] 千里寻悄悄提醒自己，一切不过是陈浩宇假装而已，千万不能当真。也不知是谁给他出了这么一个让她难以招架的主意：“好，今日的第二个任务算你顺利通过。”
[EN] "Talk about a glutton for punishment! My word stands—never throwing another punch or kick at you." Turning back to Qianli Xun, he asked, "Apology delivered. Satisfied?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇骄傲道：“第一个任务我也顺利完成，阿升还夸我，以后你让我做什么我就做什么，绝不说一句反对的话。不过……，今日那马桶真的很臭，嘿嘿嘿！可我不嫌弃，嘿嘿嘿……”
[EN] Reminding herself not to fall for this bewildering new act, she answered stiffly, "Fine. Task two passed."
[ZH] 陈浩宇又独自瞎乐。
[EN] "Nailed task one too—even Asheng praised me! From now on, whatever you say goes without a peep of complaint! Oh—that chamber pot this morning sure reeked, hehehe, yet I don't even mind! Hehehe!"
[ZH] 千里寻不想再听下去：“要是没事，尽快离开，不要在这里烦我。”
[EN] Unable to listen to one more chuckle, Qianli Xun waved him out. "Done here? Scram and quit annoying me!"
[ZH] “好嘞！这就走！背书去喽！嘿嘿嘿……”陈浩宇兴高采烈地离开。
[EN] "Right away! Off to memorize my books! Hehehe!" Chen Haoyu bounced out on air.

#CH 237
[ZH] 苦荞目送姑爷离去的背影，再瞧瞧桌上的玫瑰花，想想他刚才的表情和说过的话，不由问道：“小姐，姑爷该不会真的喜欢上您？”
[ZH] 千里寻盯着眼前的玫瑰花：“千万不要当真，不过是他使的计谋而已，可惜这招数对我根本没用，老娘又不是没脑子的人，凭几朵花，还有他一脸猥琐说出的那些话，就想让老娘轻饶，根本不可能。”
[ZH] 苦荞疑惑道：“可我看姑爷一脸享受的样子，好像已经不觉得是在折磨他？”
[ZH] 千里寻想想说：“看来折磨他的方式太轻，得好好想想还有没有更厉害的手段。”
[ZH] 小姐的想法苦荞既认同又怀疑，一时不知该如何是好。
[ZH] 千里寻吩咐道：“把这瓶玫瑰花扔出去。”
[ZH] 苦荞乖乖将花瓶抱在怀里：“这么漂亮，扔掉太可惜，小姐不喜欢，我拿去放在悦宁屋。”
[ZH] 苦荞把花瓶在悦宁屋放好，刚走出，丑丫急匆匆地上前拉住她：“苦荞姐姐，一起商量个事儿。”
[ZH] 苦荞疑惑道：“什么事儿？”
[ZH] 丑丫一脸神秘：“重要的事儿。”
[ZH] 苦荞说：“不说我可走了。”
[ZH] 丑丫凑近她耳边：“是关于小姐和姑爷未来生活是否幸福的事儿。”
[ZH] 小姐的幸福，苦荞当然关心，既然是此事，她自然乐意商量：“在哪里商量？”
[ZH] “西院。”
[ZH] “你等着，我去给小姐说一声，找个借口出来，省得她看不到我到处寻。”
[ZH] “好，我先去西院等你。”丑丫说完，迅速跑开。
[EN] Watching his retreating back and glancing at the roses on the desk, Kuqiao murmured, "Miss... do you think Young Master really fell for you?"
[ZH] 苦荞走进屋内，见小姐又提笔写话本，上前小心说：“小姐，刚才在门外碰到丑丫，她找我说些事情，我去西院一趟，一会儿回来。”
[EN] Qianli Xun glared at the blooms. "Don't buy one word of it! Pure scheme—too bad it won't work on me! Think a handful of flowers and a creepy grin buys him a free pass? Not a chance!"
[ZH] 千里寻没有抬头，边写边说：“去吧，我这里暂时不需要你伺候。”
[EN] "Still, he looked like he was actually enjoying himself, not suffering at all..."
[ZH] 苦荞应了声“是”，走出素心草堂，来到西院，打算去勤安居找丑丫，结果看到丑丫在下人用饭的屋子门口朝她招手。
[EN] Qianli Xun tapped her chin. "Means I'm going too easy on him. Need to cook up something tougher."
[ZH] 苦乔便向那屋子走去，走近后，笑道：“本以为只是你跟我商量，既在这里，看来应该不止我们两个。”
[EN] Half agreeing, half doubtful, Kuqiao held her tongue as Qianli Xun snapped, "Toss those roses out."
[ZH] 丑丫说：“除去韦嬷嬷外出办事带走的三个男仆外，大家都会前来。”
[EN] Hugging the celadon vase, Kuqiao said, "Too pretty to toss! If you don't want them, I'll put them in Yuening Room."
[ZH] 苦荞打趣道：“我就纳了闷儿，你怎么突然关心起小姐的幸福来？还主动张罗大家？”
[EN] Setting the roses in her room, Kuqiao stepped back outside—and Chouya rushed up grabbing her sleeve. "Sister Kuqiao, let's talk over something!"
[ZH] 丑丫话未说，脸先红：“苦荞姐姐有所知道，小姐的幸福关系到我的幸福；青瓜说了，只有姑爷跟小姐好，他才能跟我好。”
[EN] "What?"
[ZH] 苦荞故意批评道：“我还以为你只为小姐考虑，原来是有私心。”
[EN] "Huge business!" Chouya whispered.
[ZH] 丑丫露出害羞的笑容。
[EN] "Spill it or I'm leaving."
[ZH] 苦荞跨过门槛儿，里面放着三张下人们吃饭用的桌子，桌子旁摆放着椅子。其中一张桌子旁已坐着两名男仆。
[EN] Leaning to her ear, Chouya breathed, "About Miss and Young Master's future happiness!"
[ZH] 苦荞是小姐的贴身丫鬟，自然地位比其他下人高些，两名男仆站起来，齐声说：“苦荞姐姐好！”
[EN] Any talk of her mistress's happiness hooked Kuqiao immediately. "Where?"
[ZH] 苦荞顺带问道：“嘉祥和福禄来得倒早，其他人呢？”
[EN] "West Courtyard."
[ZH] 丑丫跟着进来，听到苦荞发问，赶紧回道：“红豆姐姐帮忙去叫，他们很快就到。”
[EN] "Hang on—let me tell Miss first so she doesn't wonder where I ran off to."
[ZH] 正说着，红豆、阿美、青瓜和阿升走进，一下子热闹起来。
[EN] "Meet you there!" Chouya dashed away.
[ZH] 丑丫十分大方，拉起青瓜跟自己坐在一起，青瓜想坐到红豆身边已不可能，眼睁睁瞅着红豆与阿升坐在一起。
[EN] Inside, seeing her mistress already buried in her huaben again, Kuqiao excused herself briefly and hurried to the West Courtyard. Headed toward Qin'an Lodge, she spotted Chouya waving from the doorway of the servants' dining hall.
[ZH] 待大家坐定后，苦荞扫视一番：“阿升，你跟青瓜都来了，姑爷谁看着？”
[EN] "Thought it was just us two," Kuqiao laughed walking over. "Clearly not!"
[ZH] 阿升回道：“三宝一个人足矣。”
[EN] "Everyone's coming except the three men Matron Wei took on errands."
[ZH] 青瓜说：“苦荞姐姐放心，我家公子现在可听话，少夫人让他往东，他不敢往西，此时正埋头背书。自从我跟着公子，从未见过他对待书本如此认真过。”
[EN] "Funny—since when are you rallying the troops for Miss's happiness?"
[ZH] 丑丫兴奋道：“太好了！岂不是稍加努力小姐和姑爷就会冰释前嫌？他们一好，我也好和青瓜……”丑丫因害羞，不好意思说出后面的话。
[EN] Chouya blushed bright red. "You know why, Sister Kuqiao! Miss's happiness decides mine—Qinggua swore he won't be with me until Young Master and Miss make up!"
[ZH] 青瓜算是明白，丑丫今日张罗大家商量公子与少夫人的事，原来是他说过的话影响到丑丫。
[EN] "Thought you were pure selfless—turns out you've got skin in the game!" Kuqiao teased, drawing a shy grin from Chouya.
[ZH] 青瓜既希望两位主人好，又担心他们好起来他会成为丑丫砧板上的肉。不由看向红豆，她坐在阿升身边，一脸开心的样子，一点儿不介意快要贴在他身上的丑丫。
[EN] Stepping across the threshold into the hall with its three dining tables, Kuqiao found two male servants already seated who jumped up chorusing, "Greetings, Sister Kuqiao!"
[ZH] 苦荞问：“大家商量事儿，总得有人主持？谁来？”
[EN] "Jiaxiang and Fulu—early birds! Where's the rest?"
[ZH] 红豆建议：“既然是丑丫叫大家前来，由她主持最合适。”
[EN] "Sister Hongdou went rounding them up—right behind us!" Chouya answered just as Hongdou, Amei, Qinggua, and Asheng walked in.
[ZH] 丑丫坐正，不再向青瓜靠去，青瓜终于松口气。
[EN] Without shyness, Chouya pulled Qinggua onto the bench right beside her, leaving Qinggua watching helplessly as Hongdou settled next to Asheng.
[ZH] “那我就不客气。”丑丫义不容辞，主持起下人们的会议，“除了青瓜，大家都是小姐的人，她对我们好，我们也都希望她好。小姐知道姑爷是散播谣言之人，开始报复姑爷，本应高兴，可我发现她没以前看着开心，苦荞姐姐贴身伺候小姐，应该最清楚。”
[EN] Scanning the table, Kuqiao frowned. "Asheng and Qinggua both here—who's watching Young Master?"
[ZH] 苦荞叹口气：“唉，嫁给散播自己谣言之人，搁谁也高兴不起来。现在最重要的是，怎么能让小姐过了这个坎儿。我可不希望她再和离一次，能遇到像大老爷和大夫人这么好的公爹和婆婆不容易。”
[EN] "Sanbao alone is plenty," Asheng replied.
[ZH] 其他下人也都应和道：“是啊是啊，我们也不希望小姐再和离一次。”
[EN] "Relax, Sister Kuqiao," Qinggua piped up. "Young Master's meek as a lamb now—Young Madam points east, he doesn't glance west! Right now he's buried deep in his books. Never once seen him study like this!"
[ZH] 青瓜笑嘻嘻地说：“其实我家公子和少夫人的关系已向前迈进一大步，苦荞姐姐今日也听到公子对少夫人表白……”
[EN] "Wonderful!" Chouya cheered. "One little push and Miss and Young Master will bury the hatchet—then Qinggua and I can..." She trailed off blushing.
[ZH] 除了苦荞，其他人都惊讶地问：“什么什么？姑爷给小姐表白？”
[EN] Qinggua finally realized his own excuse had sparked this whole summit—torn between rooting for his master and dreading falling into Chouya's clutches, especially seeing Hongdou smiling happily beside Asheng without giving Chouya a second glance.
[ZH] 苦荞说：“是有这么回事，可小姐根本不相信，还要加大对姑爷的折磨力度。别说小姐不相信，我到现在也难以置信。”
[EN] "Every meeting needs a chair," Kuqiao noted. "Who leads?"
[ZH] 除青瓜外，几人都说：“我们也难以相信。”
[EN] "Chouya called it—she chairs," Hongdou proposed.
[ZH] 青瓜说：“是真的，公子说梦话时也说喜欢少夫人。”
[EN] Straightening up off Qinggua's shoulder—letting him breathe again—Chouya took charge. "Aside from Qinggua, we all belong to Miss. She treats us well, so we want her happy. Finding out Young Master spread those rumors, she started getting payback—ought to feel great, yet she looks gloomier than before. Sister Kuqiao knows best."
[ZH] 丑丫应和道：“我相信青瓜，看来是真的。”
[EN] Kuqiao sighed. "Married to the guy who smeared your name—who could smile? Crucial question is helping Miss get past this hurdle. None of us want her going through a second divorce—in-laws as kind as Eldest Master and Eldest Madam are rare as gold!"
[ZH] 青瓜继续说：“我给公子出了个主意，让他不要跟少夫人硬碰硬，要说软言软语，还要像大老爷学习如何对待自家夫人，要搁以前公子根本不会同意，可他竟然破天荒地同意，不是喜欢少夫人又是什么？”
[EN] "Exact right! No second divorce!" everyone echoed.
[ZH] “看来姑爷的确喜欢上小姐。”苦荞不得不承认，可小姐心里想着谁，她再清楚不过，“唉，目前小姐对姑爷没那意思。”
[EN] Qinggua grinned. "Actually, Young Master and Young Madam already took a giant leap forward! Sister Kuqiao heard him confess his love to her today!"
[ZH] 阿升说出最关键的话：“姑爷态度已转变，关键还在小姐，如果她也能喜欢上姑爷，还愁没有好日子过？”
[EN] Gasps erupted all around. "What?! Young Master confessed?!"
[ZH] 红豆赞赏道：“你不开口，一开口说到重点。”
[EN] "True enough," Kuqiao admitted, "except Miss thinks he's faking and wants to turn up the heat. Honestly, even I can barely believe it."
[ZH] 二人眉来眼去，青瓜心中不爽：“要没我前面说的话，他也说不出重点来。”
[EN] "Same here!" the table chorused.
[ZH] 红豆懒得理会，想到一个主意：“从今日起，为了小姐的幸福，我们不妨帮帮姑爷，多发现姑爷的优点，瞅准机会在小姐面前夸姑爷好。尤其是苦荞姐姐，更要没事了夸夸姑爷，夸得多了，小姐对姑爷的印象自然会慢慢好起来。”
[EN] "It's for real!" Qinggua insisted. "He even confessed to her in his sleep!"
[ZH] 阿美说：“这招管用吗？小姐恨死散播谣言之人，能这么轻易饶了姑爷？”
[EN] "If Qinggua says so, I believe it!" Chouya backed him up.
[ZH] 听了这话，大家又开始为难。
[EN] "I suggested he drop fighting her, use soft words, and copy Eldest Master's devotion to Eldest Madam—old Young Master would've kicked me across the room, yet he actually listened! If that isn't love, what is?"
[ZH] 苦荞见状，安慰道：“也没更好的办法，死马当活马医，用红豆的办法试试。”
[EN] "Guess he really did fall for her," Kuqiao conceded, thinking of the hidden weight in her mistress's heart. "Problem is, Miss doesn't feel the same right now."
[ZH] 丑丫第一个认可：“我同意。”
[EN] Asheng cut straight to the core. "Young Master's already turned around; Miss is the key. If she falls for him too, smooth sailing for everyone!"
[ZH] 其他人也都一一同意。
[EN] "Quiet as a mouse, then hits the bullseye!" Hongdou praised, trading a warm smile with him that made Qinggua sour: Couldn't have hit the bullseye without my setup!
[ZH] 丑丫作为主持会议的人，需要做个总结发言：“今日大家商议得不错，接下来各忙各的，不要忘记三个字‘夸姑爷’，青瓜，你回去告诉姑爷，让他好好表现，我们才能多发现他的优点，也好有的夸。”
[EN] Ignoring him, Hongdou snapped her fingers. "For Miss's happiness, let's help Young Master out! Starting today, keep an eye out for his good points and slip praise for him into Miss's ears every chance we get—especially you, Sister Kuqiao! Hear enough good things and her view of him will soften!"
[ZH] 青瓜眼睛盯着红豆：“那是自然。”
[EN] "Will that even work?" Amei doubted. "Miss despises whoever spread those rumors—hard to let that go."
[ZH] 大家各自散去。
[EN] "No better plan on the table," Kuqiao sighed. "Worth a shot!"
[ZH] 青瓜和阿升向博雅斋走去，想着刚才的情景，忍不住问：“阿升，你该不会喜欢红豆？”
[EN] "I'm in!" Chouya voted first, followed by unanimous nods.
[ZH] 青瓜的问题问到阿升心里，他老实承认：“是……是喜欢。”
[EN] Chouya summed up briskly: "Great meeting! Remember the three magic words: Praise Young Master! Qinggua, tell Young Master to step up his game so we actually have good points to praise!"
[ZH] 青瓜哄骗道：“实话告诉你，红豆喜欢我。”
[EN] Locking eyes on Hongdou, Qinggua nodded. "Count on it."
[ZH] 阿升紧张地问：“喜欢你的不是丑丫吗？她刚才还紧挨你坐，都快贴到你身上。”
[EN] Walking back to Boya Study with Asheng, Qinggua couldn't hold it in. "Asheng... do you like Hongdou?"
[ZH] “难道丑丫喜欢我，就不允许别人再喜欢我？反正我又没成亲。”青瓜强辩完，怕露馅儿，迅速跑回博雅斋去伺候公子。
[EN] Hit square in the heart, Asheng owned up straight. "Y-yes, I do."
[ZH] 阿升站在博雅斋门口继续守着，心里一直纳闷：“红豆怎么会喜欢青瓜？”
[EN] Bluffing bold, Qinggua muttered, "Hate to break it to you—Hongdou likes me."
[ZH] 三宝瞧见阿升回来后，像是有心事的样子：“阿升，你怎么了？”
[EN] Asheng blinked hard. "Wait—isn't Chouya the one who likes you? Practically glued to your arm back there!"
[ZH] 阿升想让三宝帮忙判断判断：“三宝，你说红豆是不是喜欢上青瓜？”
[EN] "Chouya liking me doesn't bar other girls from liking me! I'm still single!" Before his fib could unravel, Qinggua bolted inside Boya Study.
[ZH] 三宝立刻否认：“怎么可能？明明是丑丫喜欢青瓜，红豆喜欢的人是我。”
[EN] Left brooding outside the door, Asheng frowned: How could Hongdou like Qinggua?
[ZH] 阿升立刻傻眼，不小心问出个情敌，他决定快些向红豆表白，免得被他人抢先。
[EN] Noticing his glum face, Sanbao asked, "What's eating you, Asheng?"
[ZH] 青瓜进屋后，公子正在以前所未有的认真劲儿背书。青瓜倒盏茶水放在桌上，听到公子叽里呱啦念书，他根本听不懂，乖乖站在一旁等待。
[EN] "Sanbao... think Hongdou likes Qinggua?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇早已注意到青瓜，只是忙着背书，懒得理会。
[EN] "No way!" Sanbao scoffed instantly. "Chouya likes Qinggua—Hongdou likes me!"
[ZH] 终于背完后，撂下书，批评道：“这么半天，跑哪里瞎逛？该不会去和丑丫幽会？”
[EN] Asheng's jaw dropped—stumbled straight onto a real rival! Better confess to Hongdou fast before anyone beats him to it!
[ZH] 青瓜说：“公子说的哪里话？我要幽会也跟红豆。告诉公子一个好消息，墨香苑所有下人从今日起都会在少夫人面前说您的好话，您可得加把劲儿，把您的优点全部展现出来，让他们有的夸。”
[EN] Inside, Chen Haoyu sat memorizing with unprecedented focus. Pouring tea, Qinggua stood listening to classical Chinese sail clean over his head until his master slammed the book shut.
[ZH] 陈浩宇不大相信：“竟有这等事？那些下人不都是朱淑真的人吗？怎么会对我这么好？”
[EN] "Disappeared for ages—sneaking off with Chouya?"
[ZH] 青瓜解释道：“他们是少夫人的人，所以希望少夫人好，不愿她再跟您和离一次。我告诉他们公子喜欢上少夫人，他们也想让少夫人喜欢上您，才答应这么做。”
[EN] "As if! If I sneak off, it's with Hongdou! Anyway, huge news, Young Master: every servant in Moxiang Courtyard just agreed to start singing your praises to Young Madam! Better bring out your A-game so they have ammunition!"
[ZH] “原来如此。”陈浩宇站起来，“走，本公子这就到朱淑真面前展示我的优点。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu blinked. "Wait—aren't they all Zhu Shuzhen's die-hards? Why help me?"
[ZH] “什么优点？”
[EN] "Because they love her and don't want her going through another divorce! Once I told them you fell for her, they want her to fall for you too!"
[ZH] “记忆力好。”
[EN] "Aha!" Chen Haoyu jumped up. "Come on—time to showcase my top talent right in front of Zhu Shuzhen!"
[ZH] 主仆二人走出博雅斋，陈浩宇回头瞧瞧门口的两人：“你们两个以后不用老盯着，本公子根本不会跑。”
[EN] "Which talent?"
[ZH] 阿升和三宝还是不放心，继续跟上，再说阿升还需观察姑爷的优点。他跟在身后，仔细观察，姑爷除样貌不错，目前看不出什么优点，悄悄问三宝：“你说姑爷有没有什么优点？”
[EN] "Photographic memory!"
[ZH] 三宝被难住，摇摇头：“看不出来。”
[EN] Stepping out of Boya Study, he waved at the two guards. "Quit shadowing me—I'm not running anywhere."
[ZH] 阿升继续问：“长相算不算？”
[EN] Still following out of duty—and hunting for praiseworthy traits—Asheng eyed him up and down and whispered to Sanbao, "Spot any good points on Young Master besides decent looks?"
[ZH] 三宝回道：“算是吧。”
[EN] Sanbao shook his head blankly. "Not a one."
[ZH] 到达素心草堂，他们没有跟着进去，站在外面等候。
[EN] "Does being handsome count?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇走进后，见书桌前空无一人，不由倍感失落。本想一进屋趁母老虎不注意，好好欣赏欣赏她，没承想希望落空。
[EN] "Guess so?"
[ZH] 他转过头，发现母老虎坐在茶桌旁，又立刻笑容满面。仔细瞧去她并未忙着做茶，手里拿着一个撒尿娃娃，脸上时不时露出笑容。
[EN] Waiting outside Suxin Thatched Hall, the guards watched Chen Haoyu stride inside—sinking for a second seeing the desk empty, then lighting up at spotting her seated at the tea table holding the bare-bottomed clay doll with a soft smile on her lips.
[ZH] 陈浩宇看得呆住，母老虎笑起来实在好看。心中暗想：“家里有这么好看的娘子，我还往外跑个什么劲儿？嘿嘿……，昨日还埋怨朱晞颜，今日倒十分感谢他，要不是他，我如何能娶到江南女词人，嘿嘿嘿……”
[EN] Stunned by how radiant she looked smiling, he thought: Gorgeous wife like this at home—why on earth did I ever roam outside?! Yesterday I cursed Zhu Xiyan; today I want to kiss his boots for handing me the Poetess of Jiangnan!
[ZH] “姑爷来了。”苦荞既打招呼，也是提醒小姐。
[EN] "Young Master is here," Kuqiao announced, alerting her mistress.
[ZH] 陈浩宇并未理会苦荞，目光也未从朱淑真身上移开：“你要是喜欢泥娃娃，往后我给你买好多，把你的屋子放满，如何？”
[EN] Ignoring Kuqiao, eyes glued to Shuzhen, he grinned. "Love clay dolls? Once I'm out I'll buy you hundreds—fill the whole hall!"
[ZH] 千里寻让苦荞把撒尿娃娃收起来，起身来到陈浩宇面前：“你已身无分文，拿什么给我买？想哄人，也得瞧瞧自个儿几斤几两。”
[EN] Handing the doll to Kuqiao to stow away, Qianli Xun rose and met him coldly. "Penniless as you are, buying them with what? Know your own wallet before bragging."
[ZH] 陈浩宇并不认输：“等你哪天心里舒坦些，愿意放我出去，我就去求我爹，让他给我安排些生意上的事，到时每个月有月钱，想给你买多少都行。”
[EN] "Once you cool off and let me out, I'll ask Father to put me to work in the family business! Earn monthly wages and buy you all the dolls you want!"
[ZH] 才管教两日功夫，陈浩宇竟然愿意参与陈家生意，又一次出乎意料。不管是真是假，怼回去才是必然：“老娘比你有钱，要你买？再说泥娃娃一个足以怡情，你给我买一屋子，想把我这里变成卖泥娃娃的铺子吗？”
[EN] Volunteering for family business after two days?! Shocked yet refusing to drop her guard, she fired back, "I've got more money than you—need you buying me toys? Besides, one doll is charming; filling the hall turns my home into a toy stall!"
[ZH] “怎么说着又生气？算我错了，行不行？你消消气，赶紧坐下听我给你背书。”
[EN] "Mad again? My bad, all right? Cool down and sit—time to test my memorization!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇一点儿脾气没有，千里寻想要臭骂人的话放在心里一句无法道出。那就听听他如何背书，找准机会再将他狠狠怼一回。
[EN] With zero temper left in him to push against, every sharp comeback jammed in her throat. Fine—let's hear him recite and shred him on the mistakes!

#CH 238
[ZH] 陈浩宇先背《论语·学而》，本以为他只是背背前十篇而已，没想到把整个学而篇全部背下来。
[ZH] 学而篇内容并不多，可对于不熟悉《论语》的千里寻来讲，仍需翻开书对照陈浩宇背诵的内容。对方不打磕绊，流利背出，一个字不差，令千里寻佩服。
[ZH] 等陈浩宇背完，骄傲地看着她：“怎么样？我厉害吧，超额完成任务。”
[ZH] 千里寻放下手中的《论语》：“有什么好骄傲的？说不定你小时候上私塾，早都背得滚瓜烂熟。”
[ZH] “本公子小时候是上过私塾，可那时心思根本没用在书上，《论语》的书名我是知道，里面的内容一点儿没记在心里，不信你可以问东哥，他什么都清楚。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇说完，准备找椅子坐下。今日表现这么好，坐下说话应该没什么关系。屁股刚要挨到椅子，被朱淑真的声音给阻止。
[ZH] “书还没背完不准坐！老老实实站着！”
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续站好着：“能不能对自己的夫君别这么凶？温柔些会更好。”
[ZH] 千里寻起身，走到陈浩宇跟前，绕着他走了两圈，仔细研究，心想：“这家伙还是有优点，如果能把心思用在正经地方，说不定也是个不错的人。”
[ZH] 朱淑真离得近，她身上的香气扑鼻而来，陈浩宇甚是陶醉。头一次被她用研究的目光注视，既意外，又欣喜，难道她已喜欢上自己？
[ZH] “怎么样？我是不是很不错？你嫁给我算是捡到宝了。”
[ZH] 这家伙毫无道理的自信也不知道是遗传谁？陈家的长辈也没有这样儿的。
[ZH] “少废话！赶紧背诗！”
[ZH] 提到诗，陈浩宇先解释一番：“本公子向来不喜欢诗词，只完成要求的数量。”
[ZH] 千里寻并未理会，站在一旁听他背诵。
[ZH] 陈浩宇依然流利地背出《山居秋暝》《渭川田家》等十首诗。
[ZH] 千里寻认为除了对方记忆力尚可外，肯定是背的书太过简单，不如给他增加难度。
[ZH] 等陈浩宇背完后，她很不屑地说：“看来我列出的书太简单，不如你以后就背《四库全书》。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇纳闷道：“《四库全书》？从没听说过？”
[ZH] 这一问，千里寻立刻醒悟过来，她从未来穿越而来，有时会把历史搞混，只想一门心思找本难背的书，脑子里蹦出《四库全书》四个字，便脱口而出，要知道那可是清代的书。
[ZH] 不小心说错，坚决不能承认：“你整日吊儿郎当不干正事又能知道几本书？没听过很正常。算了，老娘也不为难你，那就背《史记》。”
[ZH] 《史记》应该是不错的选择，汉代就有，宋朝人肯定知道。重要的是里面的文章很长，够陈浩宇背的，不难死他才怪。
[ZH] 果不其然，陈浩宇面露难色：“你已吩咐人给我书桌上放了一摞书，够背的，为何重新选书？再说《史记》篇幅太长，这不是为难我吗？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇越为难，千里寻越高兴：“既然老娘有管教你的权利，背什么书，我说了算，临时改变主意难道不可以？”
[ZH] “好吧，你说什么就是什么，但你必须答应我一个条件。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇临时提条件，千里寻气不打一处来，怒目圆睁，盯着陈浩宇：“你有什么资格提条件？去年散播谣言，害得我被全城人臭骂，叫天天不应，叫地地不灵，老娘找谁提条件？现在让你背几本书，你就有意见，告诉你，不管提什么条件，老娘都不会答应！”
[ZH] 想起散播流言一事，陈浩宇也渐渐生起悔恨之意：“好吧好吧，我不提任何条件，你想怎么折磨我都可以，我忍着，只要能让你消气，就是不知道你这气什么时候能消？”
[ZH] 千里寻继续怒目圆睁：“告诉你！我的气一辈子都不会消！”
[ZH] 陈浩宇竟然露出笑容：“这么久啊？岂不是你要跟我一辈子？嘿嘿，我觉得自己比乔文仙和仪王幸福许多，嘿嘿……”
[ZH] 千里寻的怒气就像一粒小石子被扔进湖水中，既没有改变湖面，一腔怒气还深陷在湖水里找不到出路。她不再盯着陈浩宇，担心自己盯的时间过长，会放弃报复的想法。
[ZH] 她转过身，背对着陈浩宇：“少来这一套，别以为我识不破你的真实面目？你只不过是想换一种方式对付我，老娘偏不会让你如意。”
[ZH] “随你怎么理解，我该去打扫院子，否则怎么给下人们赚银子花？再说，我现在更不愿惹你不高兴，嘿嘿……”
[ZH] 陈浩宇笑着刚要出素心草堂，见红豆和阿美端着饭食进来，才知已是午饭时间，要求道：“朱淑真，看在我今日表现好的份上，能不能让我留下来跟你一起用饭？”
[ZH] 千里寻迅速转过身：“想得美！老娘哪怕跟街上的乞丐一起用饭，也不会跟你在一张桌子上吃饭。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇并未被伤到，继续笑着说：“没关系，以后有的是机会，反正你打算跟我一辈子，我相信总有一日，我们会在同一张桌上吃饭。”
[ZH] 千里寻本想继续怼回去，怎奈对方已出去。
[ZH] 红豆与阿美将饭食在桌上放好，互相对视后，红豆先说话：“小姐，饭食已摆好，请您慢用，您边用饭我边说说姑爷今日的表现。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu started reciting the opening "Xue Er" chapter of the *Analects*. Qianli Xun expected ten short passages at best—instead he rattled off the entire chapter from start to finish. Unfamiliar with the text herself, she had to flip the pages line by line to check him: smooth, crisp, not a single character missed. Genuine respect flickered inside her.
[ZH] 千里寻并未当回事，坐到饭桌前准备用饭。
[EN] Finishing, he puffed out his chest. "Well? Impressive, huh? Exceeded quota!"
[ZH] 红豆继续说：“今日姑爷与下人讲话又知分寸又懂礼，他背书时也认真，没有打瞌睡。”
[EN] Qianli Xun set the *Analects* down coolly. "Nothing to brag about. Probably hammered it into your skull at private school as a kid."
[ZH] 阿美接着说：“我今日也没见姑爷踢青瓜，他好像真的变好。”
[EN] "Went to school, sure, never opened a page! Knew the title *Analects*, didn't know a single line inside—ask Brother Dong if you don't believe me!" Reaching for a chair—figuring stellar work earned him a seat—he froze an inch above the cushion at her bark:
[ZH] 千里寻还未体会出两个丫鬟话中的滋味，叮嘱道：“刚分到他挣的几个钱，你们两个就被收买？千万不要被表象迷惑，他今日表现好，不等于以后日日表现好，路遥知马力，日久见人心，且耐心等待。下去吧，这里有苦荞伺候。”
[EN] "Recitation isn't over—stand straight!"
[ZH] 两个丫鬟不再说什么，给苦荞使使眼色，退了出去。
[EN] He snapped upright. "Have to bark so fierce at your own husband? A softer tone wouldn't kill you."
[ZH] 苦荞将筷子给小姐放好，又给碗里盛好汤。一边观察小姐用饭，一边思考怎么夸姑爷不显得有意为之。
[EN] Rising, Qianli Xun paced two slow circles around him, sizing him up: Guy actually has real brains—put them to decent use and he might amount to something.
[ZH] 终于想到一个话题，小心说：“小姐，这两日我都没见着韦嬷嬷。”
[EN] Standing so close, her faint fragrance drifted over him, making him lightheaded; being studied like this sent hope surging. "So? Decent catch, right? Hit the jackpot marrying me."
[ZH] 千里寻一边夹菜一边说：“她忙的都是外面的事，早出晚归的，别说你没见到，我也没见着。”
[EN] Where did this bottomless ego come from? Nobody else in the Chen family had it! "Less bragging, more poetry!"
[ZH] 苦荞假装叹道：“唉，自古以来都是男主外，女主内，小姐的身边若有一个男主人，也不必韦嬷嬷一介女流之辈总是往外面跑。”
[EN] "Warning upfront—never liked poetry, so I only memorized the exact quota."
[ZH] 千里寻放下筷子：“苦荞，亏你跟我这么久，什么时候思想如此狭隘？女流之辈怎么了？照样撑起半边天，不比男子差。韦嬷嬷的智慧有几人能比？你家小姐我也是江南女词人。再瞧瞧陈浩宇，长了一副男儿的模样，草包一个，不是欺负姑娘家，就是往赌坊青楼跑，身边净跟些什么样的人？世上若都是他这样的男子，还不如多些女流之辈。”
[EN] Saying nothing, she listened as he reeled off ten Wang Wei poems—"Autumn Dusk in the Mountains," "Countryside at Weichuan," and eight more—flawlessly.
[ZH] 小姐的话语中流露出对姑爷的不满，好在主动提到姑爷，倒让苦荞接下来说得话顺理成章。
[EN] Figuring the material was just too easy, Qianli Xun scoffed, "Clearly too simple. Starting tomorrow, memorize the *Complete Library of the Four Treasuries*!"
[ZH] “其实姑爷也没那么差，他今日的表现挺好，还说以后不再去青楼，甚至打算参与陈家生意。我觉得肯定是小姐管教得好，姑爷才有这么大的变化。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu blinked. "*Complete Library of the Four Treasuries*? Never heard of it."
[ZH] 千里寻拿起勺子刚喝两口汤，听了苦荞所说，放下勺子：“你用脑子好好想想，陈浩宇从小到大就是那副德行，我才管教两日，他就能变得跟以前不一样？说出去谁信？事出反常必有妖，肯定是他的阴谋。”
[EN] Oops! Transmigrator brain slip—that mega-encyclopedia belonged to the Qing dynasty centuries ahead! Never admitting a slip, she bluffed smooth: "A slacker like you barely knows three book titles—no shock you've never heard of it! Fine, I'll take pity on you: memorize the *Records of the Grand Historian* instead!"
[ZH] 苦荞想想似乎也对，又想起青瓜说的话，认为姑爷是喜欢上自家小姐才会发生变化。
[EN] Han dynasty—safe for Song times, and massive enough to bury him!
[ZH] “我听青瓜说了件事，不知当讲不当讲？”
[EN] Sure enough, Chen Haoyu winced. "You already stacked a tower of books on my desk—why switch to something that monstrous? Out to kill me?"
[ZH] 千里寻边吃边说：“既然都提起，又何必问当讲不当讲？”
[EN] His pain brought back her grin. "My authority, my rules—changing my mind is my right!"
[ZH] 苦荞大胆说：“我去西院时，遇见青瓜，他告诉我姑爷昨日梦中说喜欢小姐您……”
[EN] "Fine, whatever you say—on one condition!"
[ZH] 千里寻刚舀起一勺汤，不得不放下：“青瓜的话你也信？”
[EN] Conditions?! Qianli Xun's eyes widened in fury. "What nerve gives you the right to set conditions?! When your lies had the entire city cursing me with nobody to turn to, who let me set conditions?! Hand you a couple books and you bargain? Zero conditions accepted!"
[ZH] 苦荞继续大胆说：“青瓜还说，姑爷之所以变化这么大，是因为喜欢上小姐……”
[EN] Guilt flickered across his face at the reminder. "All right, all right—no conditions! Torture me however you please; I'll take it all until your anger cools. Just... how long till it cools?"
[ZH] 千里寻没有说话，继续喝汤，动作比之前慢了许多。
[EN] "Never!" she glared. "Not for my entire life!"
[ZH] 苦荞不再言语，该说的已说完，一切留给小姐自己体会。
[EN] Instead of flinching, a goofy grin split his face. "My entire life? Means you're sticking with me for life! Hehe—luckier than Qiao Wenxian and Prince Yi combined! Hehe!"
[ZH] 千里寻用完饭后，一句话未说，进了里屋。
[EN] Her fury felt like chucking a pebble into a pond—swallowed without leaving a ripple. Looking away before his goofy smile wrecked her resolve, she snapped, "Drop the act! Think I can't see through your new tactic? Won't work!"
[ZH] 苦荞将碗筷收拾好，拿到西院，在西院跟下人们用过饭后，又回素心草堂。
[EN] "Think what you want—time to sweep the courtyard so the servants earn more silver! Besides, hate seeing you upset anyway. Hehe!" Stepping toward the door just as Hongdou and Amei entered carrying lunch trays, he brightened. "Hey, Zhu Shuzhen—after acing my books, let me stay and eat lunch with you?"
[ZH] 走到前院时，姑爷已开始扫地。青瓜想要帮忙，姑爷不允许，他只好站在一边看。
[EN] She whipped back. "Dream on! I'd share a bowl with a street beggar before eating at the same table with you!"
[ZH] 阿升和三宝则在一旁监督。
[EN] Undented, he grinned wide. "No rush—plenty of time ahead since you're keeping me for life! One day we'll share a table." And out he bounced before she could snap back.
[ZH] 苦荞进屋后，见书桌旁没人，往里屋拐去，才看到小姐和衣躺在床上。
[EN] Setting out the dishes, Hongdou and Amei traded a quick look before Hongdou began: "Lunch is served, Miss. While you eat, let me report on Young Master—courteous and measured with every servant today, studied hard without dozing off once!"
[ZH] “小姐，我给您宽了衣裳再午休。”
[EN] Amei chimed right in: "Didn't kick Qinggua once either! Really seems changed!"
[ZH] 千里寻坐起来：“我躺了一会儿，根本睡不着，你陪我出去一趟。”
[EN] Not catching their plot yet, Qianli Xun rolled her eyes. "Couple coins split your way and you're bought off already? Don't trust surface shows—distance tests a horse, time tests a heart. Wait and see! Leave us—Kuqiao will attend me."
[ZH] “去哪里？”
[EN] Exchanging a glance with Kuqiao, both maids withdrew.
[ZH] “去看看神柳。”
[EN] Laying out chopsticks and ladling soup, Kuqiao hunted for a natural opening. "Miss, haven't seen Matron Wei in two days."
[ZH] 小姐心中有事才会想起神柳，苦荞无须多问，只管陪着便是。
[EN] "Out running business dawn to dusk—haven't seen her myself."
[ZH] 当苦荞跟着小姐走出素心草堂，陈浩宇依旧在清扫院落，手中的扫帚一起一落，发出“沙沙”的声音。
[EN] Kuqiao put on a wistful sigh. "Traditionally men handle outside and women handle inside. If you had a capable man by your side, Matron Wei wouldn't have to wear herself out running around town."
[ZH] 千里寻一边走一边观察，他那样子看上去的确不像是假装，难道陈浩宇真的因为喜欢上她而改变？如果是这样，她心中的仇恨又能找谁发泄？
[EN] Qianli Xun clacked her chopsticks down. "Kuqiao, how long have you been with me and you're spouting narrow nonsense? What's wrong with women? Women hold up half the sky! Who matches Matron Wei's brains, or my poetry? Look at Chen Haoyu—male body, empty head, bullying girls and haunting brothels! If men are like him, give me women any day!"
[ZH] 正想着，阿升和三宝来到她面前行礼。
[EN] Perfect segue! Kuqiao jumped right in: "Young Master isn't that hopeless—did great today, swore off brothels, even wants to join family business! Must be your discipline transforming him!"
[ZH] 三宝说：“小姐，姑爷今日干得挺卖力，您瞧，凡是他扫过的地方都很干净。”
[EN] Qianli Xun set down her soup spoon. "Use your head! Rotten his whole life, reformed in forty-eight hours? Tell that to a toddler! Sudden weird behavior means hidden tricks."
[ZH] 阿升接着说：“小姐，我发现姑爷长得挺不错，个头又高，身材也不错，脸长得也好。”
[EN] Half agreeing yet remembering Qinggua's scoop, Kuqiao pressed on. "Heard something from Qinggua—not sure I should mention it..."
[ZH] 千里寻这才听出，下人们是有意在她面前夸陈浩宇，她装作生气的样子：“你们是我的人还是陈浩宇的人？”
[EN] "Already teased it—spit it out."
[ZH] 两人齐声说：“当然是小姐的人。”
[EN] "Ran into Qinggua in the West Courtyard. He said Young Master confessed in his sleep yesterday that he likes you, Miss..."
[ZH] 千里寻批评道：“既如此，为何要替他说话？”
[EN] Her soup spoon halted midair and sank back down. "You believe Qinggua's tall tales?"
[ZH] 两人不敢再言语，目送小姐走出墨香苑。
[EN] "He said Young Master changed overnight solely because he fell for you..."
[ZH] 阿升叹口气：“唉，急不得，得慢慢来。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stayed silent, lifting her spoon far slower than before. Having planted the seed, Kuqiao said no more.
[ZH] 三宝点头认可。
[EN] After lunch, Qianli Xun went straight into the inner room without a word. Kuqiao cleared the dishes, ate with the staff in the West Courtyard, and returned to find Chen Haoyu diligently sweeping the front yard—waving off Qinggua's help while Asheng and Sanbao watched.
[ZH] 两个下人的表现，陈浩宇看在眼中，听进耳朵里，对两个下人笑道：“谢了，以后要多在少夫人面前说本公子的好话，本公子亏待不了你们。”
[EN] Indoors, she found her mistress lying fully dressed on the bed staring at the canopy. "Let me help you change before your nap, Miss."
[ZH] 阿升说：“光靠我们作用不大，姑爷自己要好好表现。”
[EN] Qianli Xun sat up. "Couldn't sleep a wink. Come out with me."
[ZH] 陈浩宇低下头继续扫地：“努力表现。”
[EN] "Where to?"
[ZH] 千里寻往陈府大门口走的过程中碰见丑丫。
[EN] "The Divine Willow."
[ZH] 丑丫去陈府的后厨报完墨香苑吃食上需要的采买之物外，在陈府逛了一会儿，才往回走，不巧半道上碰见自家小姐，主动上前拜见：“小姐这是要出去？”
[EN] Knowing her mistress sought that tree whenever turmoil brewed inside, Kuqiao asked nothing and followed.
[ZH] “出去走走。”千里寻并未多停留，绕过丑丫准备离开。
[EN] Crossing the courtyard past Chen Haoyu—broom swishing crisp and steady across the paving stones—Qianli Xun watched him sidelong. Hard to believe he was faking that focus. If he genuinely fell for her and turned good, where was she supposed to aim her grudge?!
[ZH] 丑丫想到夸姑爷之事，快步挡在小姐面前：“小姐，有件事，我必须跟您说说。”
[EN] Right then Asheng and Sanbao stepped up bowing. "Young Master's working hard today, Miss—spotless sweep!" Sanbao began.
[ZH] 千里寻不得不站住：“什么事？”
[EN] Asheng added earnestly, "Noticed Young Master's actually quite a looker too—tall, solid build, handsome face!"
[ZH] “我发现姑爷变了，变得比以前好许多。”
[EN] Catching onto the coordinated campaign at last, Qianli Xun put on a sharp frown. "Whose payroll are you two on—mine or Chen Haoyu's?"
[ZH] 又是夸陈浩宇！难道自己身边的人一个个被陈浩宇收买不成？千里寻没有说一句话，绕过丑丫继续往大门外走。
[EN] "Yours, of course, Miss!"
[ZH] 丑丫搞不明白，对着小姐的背影继续大声说：“小姐！我没骗您！我说的是真的！”
[EN] "Then why are you campaigning for him?"
[ZH] 苦荞边走边扭头向丑丫摆摆手，示意她不要再说，赶紧回去。
[EN] Silenced, both bowed as she swept out the gate. Asheng sighed, "No rushing this—slow and steady." Sanbao nodded.
[ZH] 丑丫只好放弃，小跑着往墨香苑而去，她要赶着回去找青瓜。
[EN] Overhearing everything, Chen Haoyu grinned at them. "Much obliged, boys! Keep putting in good words—I won't forget you!"
[ZH] 车夫赶着马车行走在去神柳的道路上，千里寻与苦荞坐在马车里，主仆两人一路沉默。
[EN] "Words only go so far, Young Master—keep up the action," Asheng advised as Chen Haoyu returned to sweeping with gusto.
[ZH] 自从嫁到陈家，她似乎好久没出来过，这一出来，外面的空气和街市的嘈杂声仿佛变得陌生许多。
[EN] Walking toward the front gate, Qianli Xun ran right into Chouya returning from kitchen errands. Blocking her path eagerly, Chouya blurted, "Miss, gotta tell you something—Young Master really changed! Way better than before!"
[ZH] 知道陈浩宇是散播谣言之人后，她想过很多折磨和报复的方法，最终选择了并不残忍，有些管教意味的方式。本想着对方越是反抗，越不开心，她越快乐。可事实正好相反，她并不快乐。
[EN] Another cheerleader?! Had Chen Haoyu hypnotized her entire staff?! Saying not one word, Qianli Xun brushed right past her while Chouya called after her, "I'm not lying, Miss—it's true!" until Kuqiao frantically waved her back.
[ZH] 更令她没想到的是，陈浩宇变得逆来顺受，竟然说喜欢她，还时不时露出以前在他脸上从来没见过的笑容，千里寻更加不开心。
[EN] Inside the carriage rattling toward the hills, mistress and maid sat in heavy silence. First time stepping outside Chen Manor since her wedding, the bustling street noise felt almost foreign.
[ZH] 她想要将去年因谣言忍受的痛苦在陈浩宇身上好好报复一下，老天竟然都不给她提供机会。
[EN] After learning Chen Haoyu spread those rumors, she had dreamed up a dozen punishments before settling on relatively mild discipline—expecting his misery to feed her joy. Instead she felt miserable while he smiled through every chore and declared his love! Heaven wouldn't even grant her the clean satisfaction of hating him.

#CH 239
[ZH] 到达山路入口处，车夫停下马车，放好下车踩的凳子。苦荞先下去，伸出手去扶小姐。
[ZH] 一路上小姐沉默不语，苦荞也不敢多言语，而是默默观察，认真猜测。最终得出结论：小姐不高兴肯定是因为姑爷突然变好，流言带来的怨恨无从发泄，心里憋得难受，才会来神柳下。
[ZH] 千里寻从马车里走出，瞧见苦荞伸出的手，猜她因自己不高兴，才如此小心。为安慰苦荞，才将手搭上去，被扶着走下马车。
[ZH] 上山下山的人真不少，男男女女，老老少少，不用问，肯定是去山上的尼姑庵烧香拜佛。
[ZH] 穿越前千里寻对迷信的行为相当不屑；穿越而来，她再也不敢随意批判在科学的时代嗤之以鼻的迷信行为。
[ZH] 人总是需要精神寄托，对于普通老百姓来讲，寄托美好的希望，没什么不对。就像她千里寻不求神拜佛，心情不好时，总会来看神柳一样。神柳已不知不觉中成为她精神的寄托。
[ZH] 带着苦荞缓步走向神柳，明显感觉到柳絮少了许多。算算时日，仪王与梦容已回到汴梁半月有余，她嫁到陈家也已半月有余。
[ZH] 不知他们现在正在成亲，还是已在回临安的途中，无论如何，希望梦容与仪王能幸福美满，希望她在幸福美满的日子里渐渐放下内心的恨。
[ZH] 放下内心的恨？想到这里，千里寻在心中自问：“为何我希望梦容放下对我的恨，却不愿放下对陈浩宇的仇恨？还想不断折磨他？甚至一路上想出许多折磨的方法。比如用绳子绑着他在井里吊个三天三夜；再比如到野外挖个大坑，把陈浩宇埋在里面，只露出脑袋，过路人想怎么欺负都行；还有更损的，扒光他的衣裳，只穿条内裤，给他身上和脸上涂得乌七八糟，点了他的穴位，逼着他每日像乞丐一样沿街乞讨。谁让他去年害得我那么惨！”
[ZH] 一边走一边想，不知不觉已来到神柳下。有四对青年男女正在柳枝上系红绸。一段时日没来，神柳上绑的红绸多出一半儿。也许是因为春季人容易春心荡漾，才会有更多的人来这里寄托情思。
[ZH] 千里寻忽然羡慕起眼前的四对青年男女，他们互相爱着对方，一起将红绸绑好，无论以后如何，起码这一刻他们对未来充满美好的希望。
[ZH] 什么时候也能有人与她牵手在神柳上绑下属于他们的红绸？她后悔当初与文渊热恋时只顾沉浸在爱情的甜蜜中，忘记在尼姑庵请条红绸绑在神柳的枝条上。也许绑了，神柳护佑，就不会有后面的事情发生，今日她也不必在继续仇恨还是放下仇恨之间矛盾。
[ZH] 四对青年男女渐渐离去，神柳下恢复宁静。千里寻向神柳靠近几步，感受到从神柳的树干和枝叶上散发出的微微凉气，顿觉身心舒畅，刚才的怨恨之情减少许多。
[ZH] 只是减少而已，怨恨还在。身心慢慢适应凉气之后，怨恨依然活跃于内心，她不得不开口与神柳说话。
[ZH] “神柳，你告诉我，我该怎么办？原本嫁给讨厌之人已是不得已，现在知道他是散播谣言之人，即使将他千刀万剐都不为过……”
[ZH] 苦荞心想：“我猜得果然没错，小姐就是因为对姑爷有怨恨才来到神柳下。”
[ZH] “可他突然之间像变了一个人，从之前的反抗变得逆来顺受，还……”千里寻不想说出陈浩宇表白之事，“我便下不去狠手折磨他，可我的仇恨还未报，就这么无疾而终，我心有不甘。”
[ZH] 一阵风吹来，斜阳下柳条随风摆动，红绸也自然摆动起来。有些红绸在风的吹拂下，不知不觉缠绕在一旁的柳枝上。
[ZH] 此情此景，千里寻想起朱淑真的一首诗，不觉吟出：
[ZH] 高缕千丝纤暖风，带烟笼雾市桥东。
[ZH] 绾成幽恨斜阳里，折断离情细雨中。
[EN] Where the mountain path began, the driver halted and set down the stepping stool. Kuqiao hopped down first and extended her hand. Reading her mistress's long silence throughout the ride, Kuqiao felt certain of the reason: Young Master turning sweet had bottled up all Miss's bitterness over last year's rumors, driving her back to the Divine Willow.
[ZH] 《柳》
[EN] Seeing her maid's cautious gesture, Qianli Xun rested her palm on Kuqiao's to reassure her and stepped down.
[ZH] “好诗！”
[EN] Pilgrims of every age streamed up and down the trail bound for the hillside nunnery. Before transmigrating, Qianli Xun had scoffed at superstition; now she no longer judged. Everybody needed an emotional anchor—ordinary folks burned incense at temples, while she came sitting beneath a thousand-year-old willow whenever her heart knotted up.
[ZH] 身后传来一个熟悉的声音。
[EN] Walking toward the Divine Willow, she noticed far fewer catkins drifting now. Counting days, Prince Yi and Mengrong had been gone over half a month, the same stretch she had lived in Chen Manor. Wedding underway or already heading back, she hoped joy helped Mengrong let go of her hatred.
[ZH] 千里寻转过身，果然是尼姑庵的老尼，她并不知眼前的老尼正是净光法师。
[EN] Wait—why do I hope Mengrong lets go of hating me, while I refuse to let go of hating Chen Haoyu? All the way here she had fantasized crueler tortures: hoisting him in a well three days straight, burying him neck-deep in a field for passersby to mock, stripping him to his undershorts with soot smeared across his face and acupoints locked to beg on the street! All for putting her through hell last year!
[ZH] 千里寻上前拜道：“法师好，你我真是有缘，再次遇见。”
[EN] Ahead beneath the willow, four young couples were knotting red ribbons onto boughs—nearly half again as many ribbons as last visit, spring fever blooming everywhere. A sudden ache of envy tightened her throat. When would someone hold her hand to tie a ribbon here? She almost regretted forgetting to buy one during her sweet days with Wenyuan—maybe the Divine Willow would have shielded them, sparing her this bitter tug-of-war.
[ZH] 净光法师手握佛珠，双手合十：“阿弥陀佛，女施主刚才的诗不错，只是不大应景。这棵神柳处于城外的池塘边，施主为何会写‘带烟笼雾市桥东’？今日明明郎朗晴空，为何有‘折断离情细雨中’？这句显然是说与友人或恋人分别，可施主看上去并非在此与人离别。”
[EN] As the couples wandered away, quiet settled back over the pond. Stepping close, cool air radiating from bark and leaves washed over Qianli Xun's skin, soothing the tight knot in her chest—easing the anger, yet not erasing it. Out loud, she addressed the ancient tree.
[ZH] 一连串问题，千里寻只需用一句回应：“随便吟出，法师不必过分追究。”
[EN] "Tell me what to do, Divine Willow. Marrying someone I loathed was bad enough; finding out he started those rumors makes me want to slice him into pieces..."
[ZH] “随便吟出，我看不尽然。‘绾成幽恨斜阳里’，施主心中有恨，想要通过吟诗宣泄而已，殊不知宣泄并非治愈嗔恨之心的最佳途径。”
[EN] Called it! Kuqiao thought silently.
[ZH] 法师的话正好讲到千里寻的心上，她不由问道：“最佳途径是什么？还请法师不吝赐教。”
[EN] "...yet overnight he flipped into a different person—stopped fighting, took every punishment smiling, even..." Skipping his confession, she finished, "...leaving me unable to swing hard, yet walking away with my grudge unsettled leaves me choking!"
[ZH] “谈不上赐教，佛菩萨普度众生，老尼我也只是想借助佛菩萨之言教化施主而已。对付嗔恨之心的法宝只有两个字，那便是忍辱。”
[EN] A warm breeze swept past, setting willow fronds and crimson ribbons swaying in the slanting afternoon sun, silk strands tangling softly around slender twigs. Zhu Shuzhen's verse rose unbidden to her lips:
[ZH] “难道是想让我忍？不，我无法忍受害我的人不受到一点儿惩罚，我只想报复，报复到他跪地求饶。”
[EN] "High strands, ten thousand threads of slender warm wind, / Veiled in mist and haze east of the market bridge. / Knotted into hidden sorrow beneath the slanting sun, / Snapped off as parting grief amid the fine rain." —"Willows" [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] “施主之所以不愿忍辱，是因为没有领悟嗔恨的无生性，所谓无生性，即嗔恨之心产生是有条件的，只要有条件，它便是假的，施主又何必被假的情绪所困扰？”
[EN] "Fine poem!" a familiar voice called behind her.
[ZH] “假的？”千里寻难以理解，她的情绪明明就在心中，陈浩宇也明明散播过她的谣言，临安城的人无人不知无人不晓，法师却说是假的，她根本理解不了，“法师的话太过深奥，我是俗人，只会被俗世的感情牵绊，又怎会认为是假的？”
[EN] Turning, Qianli Xun recognized the elderly nun from the nunnery—still unaware she was Master Jingguang—and bowed respectfully. "Master! Fate brings us together again."
[ZH] “施主可报复过让你产生嗔恨之心的人？”
[EN] Fingering her prayer beads with palms pressed together, Master Jingguang murmured, "Amitabha. Fine verse, young donor, yet hardly matching the scene. This willow stands by a pond outside town, so why 'east of the market bridge'? Clear bright skies today, so why 'parting grief amid the fine rain'? You're not parting from anyone here."
[ZH] “当然报复过，只怨我太善良，采取的手段不够狠，对方竟然乐在其中，我更是心有不甘，想要采取更狠的方式，可又于心不忍。”
[EN] "Just recited whatever popped into my head, Master—no need to dissect it."
[ZH] 苦荞在一旁听了小姐所言，忧心不已，默默祈祷，希望眼前法师能帮到小姐。
[EN] "Not quite random. 'Knotted into hidden sorrow beneath the slanting sun'—you carry resentment inside and hoped poetry would vent it. Yet venting never cures a heart poisoned by anger."
[ZH] 只听净光法师说：“施主乃良善之辈，正因为施主善良，你我才有机缘在神柳下相见，你才能聆听到佛法要义。请问施主，既采取一些报复手段，不知可否开心？”
[EN] Struck straight in the bullseye, Qianli Xun asked, "Then what is the cure? Please enlighten me, Master."
[ZH] 千里寻仔细研究自己的内心，回想把陈浩宇从青楼绑回陈府到现在，她没有一刻真正开心过。哪怕扇了陈浩宇两巴掌，让他给下人道歉，包括今日一早罚他倒臭烘烘的马桶，她都没有真正发自内心笑过。
[EN] "Merely passing along the Buddha's wisdom. The true remedy against anger is two characters: forbearance."
[ZH] “仔细想来，我一点儿都不开心。”
[EN] "Just swallow it? No! I can't let the man who ruined me walk off scot-free! I want payback until he's on his knees begging!"
[ZH] “施主采取不狠的手段结果并不开心，若采取更狠的手段，估计你更不会开心。所以因嗔恨之心报复对方并非明智之举。最明智的做法便是忍辱之后彻底放下，只有放下才轻松自在。”
[EN] "You refuse forbearance because you have not grasped the unarisen nature of anger," Master Jingguang said calmly. "Anger arises only from conditions; anything bound to conditions is illusory. Why let an illusory emotion torment you?"
[ZH] “放下？哪有法师说得那么容易？”
[EN] "Illusory?!" Qianli Xun protested. "My rage sits solid in my chest, and his slander rang across all of Lin'an! Too deep for a mortal woman like me—I can't pretend it's fake."
[ZH] “为何不愿放下？”
[EN] "Have you taken revenge on the person who sparked this anger?"
[ZH] “对方损我名誉，阻我姻缘，本来属于我的幸福付诸东流，难道这种人我就不该恨他报复他？”
[EN] "Sure did—only I'm too softhearted, my punishments weren't nasty enough, and he ended up enjoying them! Leaves me itching to go nastier, yet my heart holds back."
[ZH] “施主有没有想过，你所认为的幸福本就不属于你。”
[EN] Standing beside her, Kuqiao silently prayed the nun's words would reach her mistress.
[ZH] 千里寻心中一阵失落，是啊，也许她与文渊本就不该在一起，可她并不愿承认法师的观点：“若按照法师所言，被欺负的人忍辱又放下，岂不是更加纵容坏人逍遥法外？这个世界不就乱套？”
[EN] "Because your nature is kind," Master Jingguang said gently, "which is why karma brought us beneath this willow so you might hear the Dharma. Tell me: after exacting your revenge, did you feel happy?"
[ZH] “请问令施主产生嗔恨之心的人可是十恶不赦的坏人？”
[EN] Searching her own chest—from hauling him out of the brothel in ropes, slapping his cheeks, making him apologize to maids, down to the stinking chamber pot at dawn—not one genuine laugh had crossed her lips. "Honestly? Not happy for one second."
[ZH] 千里寻被法师给问住，陈浩宇的确不是什么好东西，但要说他是十恶不赦的坏人，又不准确。千里寻想起他给青瓜道歉的情景，想起他扫院子时认真的样子，耳边也回荡起他顺从的话语……
[EN] "If mild revenge brought you no joy, harsher cruelty will only bring deeper misery. Acting out of anger never brings peace. True wisdom lies in forbearance and total release—only letting go sets you free."
[ZH] “他就是个不务正业的混蛋。”
[EN] "Letting go sounds easy on your tongue, Master. Why should I let go when he trashed my name, wrecked my marriage, and turned my happiness to dust?"
[ZH] “从施主的话语中听得出，你所嗔恨之人并非十恶不赦，不如退一步海阔天空。”
[EN] "Have you considered that the happiness you mourn was never yours to begin with?"
[ZH] “岂不是太便宜对方？”
[EN] A quiet ache passed through her—maybe Wenyuan and she were never meant to be—yet she still pushed back. "By that logic, if victims endure and forgive, doesn't every villain roam free until the world collapses?"
[ZH] “施主可真正关心自己？”
[EN] "Tell me: is the man you hate an irredeemable monster?"
[ZH] “当然。”
[EN] That stopped her cold. Scoundrel, sure—yet an irredeemable monster? Flashing back to him apologizing sincerely to Qinggua, sweeping the yard with real focus, smiling through every scolding... "He's just a good-for-nothing jerk."
[ZH] “既如此为何施主总是纠结于别人，搞得自己不开心？一个人若真正关心自己，哪有时间关心他人？人生短短几十年，又何必将光阴浪费在嗔恨他人的情绪中？这情绪还未伤及对方，却已自伤，实在得不偿失。”
[EN] "Not an irredeemable monster, then. Step back one pace, and sea and sky open wide."
[ZH] 净光法师说的这段话，千里寻总算听进去。
[EN] "Isn't he getting off way too easy?"
[ZH] 是啊，被惩罚的本是陈浩宇，他除了头一日反抗大骂外，现在正在墨香苑享受对他的惩罚，一点儿都不生气，还当着她的面傻乐。可她千里寻却一点儿高兴不起来，要不怎么跑来神柳下？
[EN] "Do you truly care about yourself, donor?"
[ZH] 看来伤人一千自损八百的话很有道理。不为别的，只为自己轻松快乐，她决定放下仇恨，去年陈浩宇散播谣言之事一笔勾销。
[EN] "Of course!"
[ZH] 刚刚产生一笔勾销的念头，顿觉身心轻松，更感受到放下的妙处：“感谢法师苦口婆心教诲，我愿意放下。”
[EN] "Then why obsess over another man at the expense of your own peace? Someone who truly cherishes herself has no hours to waste nursing hatred. Life spans barely a few decades—why pour your days into a poison that wounds you before it ever touches him?"
[ZH] “阿弥陀佛，施主愿意放下，也是功德一件，老尼祝施主生活幸福安康。”净光法师说完，转身而去。
[EN] That hit clean through every wall. Who was suffering right now? Chen Haoyu was grinning through chores back in Moxiang Courtyard, while she was brooding out in the hills! "Harm your foe a thousand, bleed eight hundred yourself"—pure truth. Not for his sake, purely for her own lightness and joy, she let the grudge drop. Last year's rumors: wiped clean.
[ZH] 千里寻对着法师的背影，恭敬说：“感谢法师！”
[EN] The instant she released it, weight lifted clean off her shoulders. "Thank you for your patience and wisdom, Master. I'm letting it go."
[ZH] 苦荞开心地问：“小姐，您刚才说放下的意思是不是不打算再跟姑爷计较？”
[EN] "Amitabha. Letting go brings great merit. May your days be peaceful and bright." With a bow, Master Jingguang turned and walked up the hill.
[ZH] 千里寻故意批评道：“你什么时候也被陈浩宇收买？竟然希望我不跟他计较？”
[EN] Qianli Xun bowed after her retreating back. "Thank you, Master!"
[ZH] 苦荞一脸急切地解释：“哪里的话？我怎么可能被姑爷收买？我是见小姐知道姑爷是散播谣言之人后没高兴过，即使您惩罚姑爷，也并不开心，觉得肯定是哪里出了问题，听完法师所言，才恍然大悟。”
[EN] Beaming bright, Kuqiao asked, "Miss, does 'letting go' mean you're done punishing Young Master?"
[ZH] 千里寻终于露出几天以来难得的笑容：“看来你也有慧根，不愧是我的丫鬟，以后我不会再与陈浩宇计较，你们的日子也好恢复正常，省得一个个在我面前说陈浩宇的好话，别以为我不知道你们的小心思。”
[EN] Qianli Xun arched a playful brow. "Since when did Chen Haoyu buy you off too?"
[ZH] 苦荞夸道：“我家小姐最聪明，什么都瞒不过您。”
[EN] "Never!" Kuqiao protested eagerly. "I just hated seeing you miserable ever since you found out—even punishing him made you glum! Hearing the Master talk made everything click!"
[ZH] 千里寻一高兴，玩劲儿十足，神柳周围的景色不错，不如在这里走一走玩一玩。除了池塘里景色好以外，周围的野花不少。主仆二人一起采集野花，各编一个花环戴在头上。
[EN] The first bright, unburdened smile in days lit Qianli Xun's face. "Got real wisdom in that head after all—worthy of being my maid! Fine, clean slate with Chen Haoyu. Back to normal for all of you, so quit scheming to feed me compliments about him—think I didn't notice?!"
[ZH] 时不时还有年轻的情侣前来系红绸，每一对儿，千里寻都要注目瞧去。苦荞以为小姐喜欢看热闹。只有千里寻自己明白，她是想瞧那些情侣是不是她想等的人。
[EN] "Nobody outsmarts you, Miss!" Kuqiao giggled.
[ZH] 千里寻认为自己一定是个矛盾体，想见到仪王和梦容，又怕见到他们。
[EN] Lighthearted again, Qianli Xun wandered the lakeside picking wildflowers with Kuqiao and weaving matching flower crowns for their heads. Whenever young couples strolled up to tie red silk, her eyes flicked over each pair—Kuqiao thinking she was people-watching, only Qianli Xun knowing she was half scanning for two familiar figures, torn between longing and dread of seeing them.
[ZH] 玩够后，天色渐渐变黑，她在神柳下坐了一会儿，才带着苦荞打道回府。
[EN] As dusk softened the hills, she sat a short while beneath the willow before riding home with Kuqiao.

#CH 240
[ZH] 回到陈府时，大家都已用过晚饭。千里寻并不饥饿，当苦荞问要不要摆饭时，她一口回绝。
[ZH] 法师的话回荡在耳边，千里寻打算放过自己，不想再与陈浩宇计较。换过衣裳后，准备去博雅斋亲自找陈浩宇说明。
[ZH] 刚走出素心草堂，借着院子周围灯笼里的烛光，瞧见一个身影朝这边走来，便站在原地等一会儿。身影越来越近时，才看清原来是陈浩飞。
[ZH] 陈浩飞瞧见二嫂站在面前，上前恭敬道：“二嫂好。”
[ZH] “你怎么来了？”
[ZH] “我来看二哥。”
[ZH] 千里寻敏锐地感觉出这位堂弟前来并不是那么简单：“是陈家长辈让你来的吧？”
[ZH] 陈浩飞并不隐瞒：“奶奶和大婶子嘴上说不管，心里不落忍，又不能前来过问，派我过来瞧瞧二哥。”
[ZH] “既然不落忍，又何必让我管教？”千里寻认为陈家长辈肯定会失望，她只管教两日便打算放弃，陈浩宇的不良习气根本不可能改变，“来都来了，跟我一起去博雅斋。”
[ZH] 陈浩飞答应后，跟上二嫂往后院拐去。他听父母讲过，这位二嫂十分厉害，霸占了二哥的院子，还把二哥关起来。陈浩飞便在心中祈祷，未来可不能娶一个厉害的媳妇，要不得像二哥一样遭罪。
[ZH] 博雅斋的门还未上锁，阿升和三宝站在门口两侧像两个门神。见到这架势，陈浩飞吐吐舌头，心中自语：“难怪二哥唤二嫂母老虎，管得也忒严，门口还有人把守。”
[ZH] 千里寻走上前说：“你们两个回去吧，以后不必守着。”
[ZH] 阿升和三宝早希望如此，高兴地回声：“是！”
[ZH] 等阿升和三宝离开后，苦荞负责推开博雅斋的门。
[ZH] 千里寻第一个跨进去，前厅没有一个人。
[ZH] 陈浩飞第二个进入，兴奋地喊道：“二哥！我来看你！”
[ZH] 陈浩宇和青瓜听到声音，从里屋出来。本来是因为浩飞的声音高兴，见朱淑真也亲自前来，更是乐开了花。
[ZH] 他过去抱住浩飞：“你怎么才来看我？”
[ZH] 浩飞悄悄在二哥的耳边说：“我害怕二嫂，一直不敢过来；奶奶和大婶子担心你，让我过来瞧瞧，才壮着胆子前来。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇放开浩飞：“好好瞧瞧，我一切都好，你二嫂对我可好，不缺吃不缺穿，我觉得自己这两日比以前精神许多。”
[ZH] 听到这话，陈浩飞更觉二哥可怜。看来除了跑步，二嫂应该还使出什么厉害手段，才逼得二哥见到他没有当面诉苦，反而替二嫂说话。
[ZH] 千里寻认为陈浩宇既会装又会演戏，不如站在一旁欣赏一会儿。
[EN] By the time Qianli Xun returned to Chen Manor, dinner had passed; not hungry, she waved off Kuqiao's offer of food. Master Jingguang's counsel still rang clear in her mind—time to set herself free by calling off the war. Changing into fresh clothes, she walked out to inform Chen Haoyu in person.
[ZH] 结果陈浩宇转过头向她质问：“喂！一下午去了哪里？该不会逛到现在才回来？”
[EN] Stepping from Suxin Thatched Hall under the glow of courtyard lanterns, she spotted a figure approaching and waited until Chen Haofei stepped into the light.
[ZH] “老娘去哪里何时需向你报备？”千里寻没好气地反问道，接着说，“陈浩宇，从现在起，你自由了，我不会再关着你，对你的仇恨从此一笔勾销。之前的契约我会重新写一份儿，你只需好好配合按下指印即可。”
[EN] "Good evening, Second Sister-in-law," he bowed respectfully.
[ZH] 当听到不再关他，陈浩宇一阵欣喜，又听到还要立契约，十分着急：“我不同意！”
[EN] "What brings you here?"
[ZH] “怎么？还想让老娘管教你？”
[EN] "Came to check on Second Brother."
[ZH] “我是说契约，既然烧了，没必要再重写。”
[EN] Catching on at once, she raised a brow. "The elders sent you, didn't they?"
[ZH] 千里寻面露疑惑：“什么意思？”
[EN] Chen Haofei owned up straight. "Grandmother and Eldest Aunt swore not to meddle, yet their hearts ache—couldn't come themselves, so they sent me to peek in."
[ZH] 陈浩宇靠近朱淑真：“本公子这两日想法变化甚大，以前契约上写的内容已不适合，重新写它又有何意义？”
[EN] "Why put me in charge if they can't bear watching?" Qianli Xun sighed—the elders were bound to be let down that she was calling it off after two days anyway. "Since you're here, walk with me to Boya Study."
[ZH] 原来如此，千里寻不再疑惑：“有道理，你正好利用今晚的时间好好想想，明日到素心草堂找我，一起商议新签约。你们兄弟聊，我困了，回屋睡觉。”
[EN] Trailing behind her toward the back courtyard, Chen Haofei silently prayed his own future bride would not be fierce like this, locking her husband in back rooms!
[ZH] 千里寻刚到门口，被陈浩宇叫住：“喂！你真的不再怨我散播你的谣言？”
[EN] Boya Study stood unlocked yet flanked by Asheng and Sanbao like two temple door-gods. Chen Haofei stuck his tongue out inwardly: No wonder Second Brother calls her a tigress—even posting sentries!
[ZH] 千里寻并未回头，说出一段充满哲理的话：“人被狗咬，难不成还要再咬狗一口？那人与狗又有何区别？我是人，不想把自己变成狗，只能不再与那只狗计较。”
[EN] "You two can knock off," Qianli Xun told the guards. "No need to watch him anymore."
[ZH] 陈浩宇觉得实在奇怪，喜欢上朱淑真，她怎么说话，他都不生气，面带笑容盯对方离去的背影：“你肯定是喜欢上我，要不怎么这么快放我自由？还暗示我要对你忠诚？嘿嘿……”
[EN] Thrilled, both replied, "Yes, Miss!" and hurried off while Kuqiao pushed the door open.
[ZH] 陈浩飞丈二和尚摸不着头脑：“二哥，我怎么没听出来二嫂有暗示之意？是不是搞错了？”
[EN] Stepping inside behind her, Chen Haofei called cheerily, "Second Brother! Came to visit!"
[ZH] “绝对不会搞错。”陈浩宇转过身坚决否认，“你没听她刚才提到狗吗？”
[EN] Emerging from the bedroom with Qinggua, Chen Haoyu lit up—doubly thrilled at spotting Zhu Shuzhen herself! Throwing an arm around Haofei, he crowed, "Took you long enough!"
[ZH] “听到了。”
[EN] Haofei whispered quick in his ear, "Scared of Second Sister-in-law—Grandmother and Aunt sent me to check on you!"
[ZH] “狗有什么特点？”
[EN] Releasing him, Chen Haoyu grinned wide. "Look me over—fit as a fiddle! Your second sister-in-law treats me great, plenty to eat and wear—feel sharper than I have in years!"
[ZH] “什么特点？”
[EN] Poor Second Brother—she must have broken him so bad he's terrified to complain! Haofei pitied him silently, while Qianli Xun stood amused at his theatrical act.
[ZH] “对主人忠诚，所以你二嫂就是给我暗示要对她忠诚。也是，你二哥我玉树临风，走出去不知会迷倒多少姑娘，她不放心也正常。”
[EN] Suddenly Chen Haoyu swung toward her. "Hey! Disappeared all afternoon—where were you till dark?"
[ZH] 陈浩飞苦笑着看向青瓜：“二嫂是这意思吗？”
[EN] "Since when do I report my schedule to you?" she shot back, then spoke plain. "Chen Haoyu, starting right now, you're free. Lockup's over, and my grudge against you is wiped clean. I'll draft a fresh copy of our old contract tomorrow—just stamp your thumbprint."
[ZH] 青瓜无奈地摆摆手。
[EN] Free—joy leaped up, then crashed at "fresh contract." "No way!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇叮嘱道：“你告诉奶奶和我娘，天色已晚，我就不去打扰，明日一早去看望她们。记住，不许说你二嫂坏话。”
[EN] "What, begging for more discipline?"
[ZH] 陈浩飞离开后，直接去大伯的院子，夏老太太也在。
[EN] "I mean the contract! Burned it already—no need to rewrite it!"
[ZH] 这两日老太太硬撑着没开院门，怕自己一时心软，坏了孙媳管教的计划。今日天一黑，心里毛躁躁的，不由自主担忧起来。浩宇跑十圈下来会不会跑坏了？听说还让他给下人道歉，孙子会不会被逼得毫无颜面？想来想去坐不住，便跑到大儿子的院子，要求他派人打听一下浩宇的情况。
[EN] Puzzled, she frowned. "Meaning what?"
[ZH] 姚轻琰也支持老太太的想法，在一旁催促。被两个女人要求，陈江林想来想去觉得派浩飞去打听最合适。一来儿媳送过他诗词，两人还算有些交情；二来浩飞心性纯良，不是爱嚼舌根之人。
[EN] Stepping closer, Chen Haoyu said, "My thinking flipped completely these past two days. Those old terms don't fit anymore—why waste ink copying them?"
[ZH] 于是把陈浩飞请来，说明缘由后，陈浩飞犹豫片刻欣然答应。主要是二哥受罚，他一直想去看看二哥表示关心，可爹娘不乐意，让他少掺和二哥的事。他又听话，只能放弃。
[EN] Fair point. "Suit yourself. Think over what you want tonight, come to Suxin Thatched Hall tomorrow, and we'll hash out fresh terms. Chat with your cousin—I'm turning in."
[ZH] 现在有奶奶做主派他前去，爹娘不敢说什么，也正好全了他关心二哥之情。
[EN] Just as she reached the threshold, he called after her, "Hey! Seriously done holding those rumors against me?"
[ZH] 三人在屋中坐着等待，两个女人面色焦急，陈江林倒显得轻松。
[EN] Without turning back, Qianli Xun delivered a crisp parting line. "If a dog bites a person, does the person drop down and bite the dog back? What's the difference between them then? I'm human—refuse to turn into a dog, so I'm done beefing with that hound."
[ZH] 陈江林甚是佩服儿媳妇，竟然能想出这么多招数管教浩宇，他自愧不如。表面上他并不关心浩宇，实际上也派下人打听过，将淑真管教的招数了解得一清二楚，他认为招招打在浩宇的致命之处。照这些方法管教下去，不愁儿子不会改变心性。
[EN] Even insulted, Chen Haoyu grinned beaming at her back. "Definitely fell for me—why else set me free so fast and drop hints about loyalty?! Hehe!"
[ZH] 浩飞终于回来，一进门就说：“好消息！好消息！”
[EN] Completely lost, Chen Haofei blinked. "Hint about loyalty? Did I miss something, Second Brother?"
[ZH] 听到好消息，两个女人心里轻松许多，老太太急切地问：“什么好消息？”
[EN] "Dead certain!" Chen Haoyu turned beaming. "Didn't you hear her bring up dogs?"
[ZH] 陈浩飞回道：“二嫂不再关着二哥，也不再惩罚他，还说她对二哥的仇恨一笔勾销，听那意思不再计较二哥散播谣言之事。”
[EN] "Uh, yes?"
[ZH] 三人脸上又没了笑容，尤其是陈江林：“才管教两日就不管了？浩宇也不可能两日就被教育好。”
[EN] "What are dogs famous for?"
[ZH] 夏老太太担忧道：“我们盘算得好，希望淑真既把浩宇教育过来，两人又能擦出火花，现在看来只是我们一厢情愿，淑真从来没这么想过。”
[EN] "What?"
[ZH] 姚轻琰叹口气：“唉！只能说浩宇没这福气，我们也没这福气。”
[EN] "Loyalty to their owner! She's hinting I better stay faithful to her! Fair enough—handsome as I am, girls drop at my feet outside; natural she worries!"
[ZH] 明明是好消息，三人却不开心。听他们所言，应该是希望二哥改好的同时，还能与二嫂产生感情。
[EN] Chen Haofei shot a helpless look at Qinggua. "Was that what she meant?"
[ZH] 想起在博雅斋二哥的表现和说过的话，陈浩飞有了安慰之语：“奶奶、大伯、大婶子，你们不用太担心，二嫂对二哥是什么态度目前还看不出来，不过二哥对二嫂与以前大不相同，看上去他应该是喜欢上二嫂，还叮嘱我在长辈面前不能说二嫂坏话。”
[EN] Qinggua just waved both hands in mute surrender.
[ZH] 三人脸上又露出笑容，都“呵呵”笑起来。
[EN] "Tell Grandmother and Mother it's late so I won't disturb them tonight," Chen Haoyu instructed. "I'll pay respects first thing tomorrow—and not one bad word about your second sister-in-law!"
[ZH] 笑完，老太太说：“还是有进展，而且是大进展，浩宇都懂得关心媳妇，还怕他以后会出去浪荡？”
[EN] Hurrying straight to Eldest Uncle's courtyard, Chen Haofei found Old Madam Xia waiting right beside his uncle and aunt. After two days of white-knuckling her locked gate, dusk had shattered the old woman's nerves—worried ten laps broke his legs and apologizing to maids crushed his pride—so she had marched to Eldest Son's quarters demanding news. Chen Jianglin picked Haofei as spy: Shuzhen liked him enough to gift him poems, and the boy never stirred gossip.
[ZH] 陈江林笑着说：“母亲说的是，证明淑真是会管教夫君的好娘子，仅仅两日功夫就有这么大进展，我可是教育浩宇十几年，也没她两日的效果好。”
[EN] Bursting inside, Haofei announced, "Good news! Great news!"
[ZH] “可不是？”姚轻琰十分认可，转而问浩飞，“你二哥有没有让你稍什么话来？”
[EN] Tension melted from both women's faces. "Spill it!" Old Madam Xia urged.
[ZH] 陈浩飞回道：“二哥说了，天已黑，他就不来打扰，明日一早看望奶奶、大伯和大婶子。”
[EN] "Second Sister-in-law unlocked Second Brother, called off all punishments, and wiped the rumor grudge clean!"
[ZH] 三人又高兴一阵，姚轻琰甚至笑出几滴眼泪来：“浩宇何时这么懂事过？以往想走就走，从不打招呼，现在还懂得前来看望我们，真是难得。”
[EN] All three faces instantly fell. "Quit after two days?!" Chen Jianglin frowned. "Nobody reforms in two days!"
[ZH] 夏老太太笑着批评道：“那是你们，我孙子可是经常看望我。”
[EN] "We hoped she'd whip him into shape AND spark romance," Old Madam Xia sighed. "Looks like wishful thinking—she never cared."
[ZH] 陈江林咧着嘴说：“呵呵，哪一次浩宇找母亲不是想讨要些东西？”
[EN] "Guess Haoyu—and we—just lack the luck," Yao Qingyan murmured.
[ZH] “他能向我讨要也是心里有我，何时向你们讨要过？”
[EN] Realizing what they actually hoped for, Haofei hurried to add, "Don't fret yet, Grandmother, Uncle, Aunt! Can't tell how Second Sister-in-law feels, yet Second Brother is a totally different man around her! Looks head-over-heels for her—even warned me not to say one bad word about her!"
[ZH] 老太太强词夺理，陈江林也不敢跟母亲计较。再说大家心情都不错，何必因计较惹得老太太不高兴，只等明日见到浩宇再详细了解情况。
[EN] Instant sunshine! All three burst into relieved laughter.
[ZH] 天儿也晚了，三人不好再聊下去。陈浩飞负责把奶奶送回屋，按说奶奶如此偏袒二哥，他应该嫉妒才对，怎奈他心性纯良，并不计较，还甚是理解老人家。
[EN] "Huge leap forward!" Old Madam Xia beamed. "Already protective of his wife—think he'll still roam wild?!"
[ZH] 将奶奶送至院子门口时，陈浩飞准备拜别回屋，被老太太叫住：“浩飞，你等等。”
[EN] "Proof Shuzhen is a miracle-worker!" Chen Jianglin grinned. "Two days beat fifteen years of my lectures!"
[ZH] 陈浩飞停住脚步：“奶奶还有事？”
[EN] "Did Haoyu send any message?" Yao Qingyan asked eagerly.
[ZH] “浩飞啊，奶奶相信你能考上功名。”
[EN] "Said it's too late to disturb you tonight, so he'll come pay respects first thing tomorrow morning!"
[ZH] “借奶奶吉言。”
[EN] Tears of joy brimmed in Yao Qingyan's eyes. "When has Haoyu ever been this considerate? Usually vanishes without a word—now checking in politely!"
[ZH] “你可知奶奶为何如此说？”
[EN] "Speak for yourself—he always visits me!" Old Madam Xia teased.
[ZH] “奶奶是自家人，自然向着孙子。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin grinned. "Every time he visits you, Mother, he's fishing for handouts!"
[ZH] “非也，奶奶年过半百，经过的事，见过人不少，你心性纯良，老天定不会辜负，你就是陈家改换门楣的希望。”
[EN] "Asking me means he keeps me in his heart—when did you ever ask me for anything?"
[ZH] 听了这话，陈浩飞才知自己在奶奶心中的地位：“孙儿谢过奶奶。”
[EN] Knowing better than to argue, Chen Jianglin just smiled as the night wound down.
[ZH] “好了，快去休息吧。”
[EN] Walking his grandmother home—bearing zero jealousy toward his favored cousin—Chen Haofei bowed at her gate, only for her to pause him. "Wait, Haofei. Grandmother believes you're going to win an official post."
[ZH] 第二天一大早，陈浩宇用过早饭，带着青瓜走出博雅斋，迎面碰见韦嬷嬷。
[EN] "May your words come true, Grandmother."
[ZH] 陈浩宇觉得这位嬷嬷总是神出鬼没，被罚的两日没见她人影儿，刚获自由，就出现在眼前，实在令人费解。
[EN] "Know why I say that?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷上前行礼：“恭喜姑爷重获自由。”
[EN] "Because I'm family and you root for me."
[ZH] 陈浩宇被吓一跳：“这两日也没见你，你怎么知道朱淑真把我放出来？”
[EN] "Wrong. Past fifty, I've read plenty of hearts. Yours is pure and upright—Heaven never lets a soul like yours down. You're the hope that lifts the Chen clan's standing."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“别看姑爷没见着我，院子里的事我无不知晓。”
[EN] Warmth flooded his chest. "Thank you, Grandmother."
[ZH] 陈浩宇只觉对方的眼睛似乎能看穿他的内心，不觉瘆得慌，还是赶紧撤：“你忙，我还有事。”
[EN] "Off to bed now."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷看着姑爷匆忙离开的背影，笑着自语道：“姑娘还是老样子，姑爷变得还挺快，也好，总算有进展。”
[EN] Next morning after breakfast, stepping out of Boya Study with Qinggua, Chen Haoyu bumped straight into Matron Wei—who had vanished during his entire lockup only to pop up the second he went free.
[ZH] 陈浩宇经过素心草堂，向门口瞧瞧，脸上露出笑容：“青瓜，你说说，你家公子我现在为何看到‘素心草堂’四个字觉出一股清雅之气来？”
[EN] "Congratulations on regaining your freedom, Young Master," she curtsied.
[ZH] 青瓜一语中的：“因为是少夫人题的，她就住在里面。”
[EN] He jumped a fraction. "Didn't even see you around—how'd you know Zhu Shuzhen let me out?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇没有夸青瓜，用笑声回应：“呵呵……”
[EN] "Seen or unseen, nothing in this courtyard escapes me."
[ZH] 笑声停止后，又将院子瞧一遍：“今日这院子怎么看怎么好，我家娘子还是很有眼光，池塘里种上荷花，一下子增色许多，还有合欢树栽种在那里实在妙不可言。合欢树，合欢树，名字寓意多好。你说她给院子移了这棵树，会不会早都想着跟我夫妻和睦，永远恩爱？呵呵……”
[EN] Feeling as if her sharp eyes x-rayed straight into his ribs, he backed off fast. "Carry on—got errands!"
[ZH] 青瓜觉得公子以前到处浪荡，行为完全可以用“不检点”三个字形容，那时根本瞧不出他会真心喜欢哪家姑娘。
[EN] Watching him bolt, Matron Wei smiled softly. "Miss stays steady, Young Master turns fast—progress at last."
[ZH] 现在可好，对少夫人动真情后，直接从一个浪荡公子变成傻帽公子，总是活在个人臆想的世界里。
[EN] Passing Suxin Thatched Hall, Chen Haoyu gazed fondly at the door plaque. "Say, Qinggua, how come 'Suxin Thatched Hall' suddenly looks classy and refined to me today?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇乐完，见青瓜不言语，催促道：“怎么不说话？”
[EN] "Because Young Madam named it and lives inside," Qinggua nailed it dryly.
[ZH] 青瓜不得已开口：“不管是不是，反正合欢树既然被移到墨香苑，自然预示公子与少夫人夫妻恩爱，不离不弃。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu chuckled, sweeping a fond eye across the courtyard. "Whole yard looks gorgeous today! Wife's got real taste—lotus brightening the pond, and that hehuan silk tree right there! 'Harmonious union'—think she planted it hinting she wants lifelong harmony and wedded bliss with me? Hehe!"
[ZH] “说得好！等本公子挣了银子第一个奖赏你。走，跟本公子去见我爹娘。”
[EN] From reckless playboy straight into lovesick daydreamer—Qinggua sighed silently, then answered when his master nudged him: "Hint or not, having a hehuan tree in Moxiang Courtyard definitely promises wedded bliss and devotion!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇带着青瓜兴冲冲地走出墨香苑，想要做些正经事的想法比以往任何时候都强烈。
[EN] "Sweet talk! First payday, you get first bonus! Come on—time to see Father and Mother!" Full of fresh fire to amount to something real, Chen Haoyu strode out of Moxiang Courtyard.

#CH 241
[ZH] 夏老太太想孙子，估摸着他见过爹娘后才会前来见她，干脆一大早直奔大儿子的院子，在那里用过早饭，边聊天儿边等孙子到来。
[ZH] 正聊着，丫鬟映雪进来：“二公子来了。”
[ZH] 话音刚落，陈浩宇带着青瓜已踏入屋内，先向父母行礼：“爹，娘。”然后扑进老太太怀里，开启撒娇模式：“奶奶，孙儿想死您了！”
[ZH] 老太太轻轻抚摸着孙子的头：“奶奶也想你，你可不要责怪奶奶跟你爹娘把院门关起来不理你。”
[ZH] “孙儿感谢都来不及，怎么会埋怨？”陈浩宇说着乖巧的话，三人都无比开心。
[ZH] 陈江林隐去笑容，依旧拿出父亲的威严：“天儿热，别老靠着你奶奶，站着回话。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇乖乖站起来：“爹不问我，我还有事要与爹说。”
[ZH] 陈江林说：“回完长辈的话再说你的事。”
[ZH] 姚轻琰忍不住第一个问道：“淑真真的不再与你计较散播流言之事？”
[ZH] “那还能有假？我是她夫君，她跟我是一家人，一家人又怎会与一家人计较？娘，您说是不是这个理？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇一口一个“一家人”，陈江林也绷不住，跟着两个女人笑起来。
[ZH] 姚轻琰笑着说：“是，是这个理。”
[ZH] 陈江林端起茶盏，抿一口茶水，掩饰了一会儿，放下茶盏后继续板着脸问：“淑真也太轻饶你，这么快把你放出来，要我说就应该多关多罚你几日。也不知这两日你有什么进步？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇犹豫一会儿，说道：“只管教两日，想进步都难，不过儿子还是有些变化，往后我不想在外逛荡，只想留在家里，爹能不能给我安排些生意上的事，也好让我赚些银子，省得日后想给自己的娘子买礼物连银子都没有，被她笑话。”
[ZH] 此话一出，老太太和姚轻琰先是惊讶，然后又是高兴。
[ZH] 陈江林更绷不住，笑着问：“你想跟我说的事就是这个？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇肯定道：“正是。”
[ZH] 陈江林仔细瞧着儿子：“你果真不再出去逛荡？还愿意参与家里的生意？”
[ZH] 没等陈浩宇回答，夏老太太强调道：“我孙子都说是，你还问？你这当爹的信不过自己儿子的毛病得改。”
[ZH] 陈江林说：“母亲批评的是，儿子这就改。”
[ZH] 姚轻琰眼中含着喜泪：“浩宇真的变了，我这当娘的比谁都高兴。”
[ZH] 家人越是高兴，越让陈浩宇体会到以前的他多令家人不高兴，竟然觉出从未有过的尴尬来。
[ZH] 以陈浩宇的心性，绝不会让自己尴尬太久，他主动问道：“爹，您打算什么时候给我安排？”
[ZH] 陈江林说：“随时可以安排。”
[ZH] “那就请爹快些安排。”
[ZH] 儿子催促，陈江林不能怠慢：“自从跟西南开展生意，绸缎庄比较繁忙，你就去那里，我差人给绸缎庄的秋管事打声招呼，让他带带你，他经验丰富，要多跟他学习。”
[ZH] “儿子谨遵爹的安排，不知月钱是多少？”
[ZH] 陈江林面带笑容批评道：“事还没做，就关心起银子，还是想着怎么把事先做好，事做好，银子自然少不了你的。”
[ZH] 老太太说：“难道今日就去忙？一忙起来也不知还能不能见着我孙子，要不等明日再去绸缎庄？”
[EN] Eager to see her grandson—knowing he would visit his parents first—Old Madam Xia had marched straight to Eldest Son's courtyard at dawn, eaten breakfast there, and waited.
[ZH] 母亲发话，陈江林无不答应。
[EN] Hardly had Yingxue announced "Second Young Master is here" than Chen Haoyu breezed through the door with Qinggua, bowed briskly—"Father, Mother"—and dove straight into Old Madam Xia's arms. "Grandmother, I missed you to death!"
[ZH] 三人一致认为浩宇的变化都是淑真的功劳，商量着一起前去看望她。
[EN] Stroking his hair fondly, Old Madam Xia asked, "Missed you too! Not mad at us for locking our gates on you?"
[ZH] 一早姑爷离开墨香苑后，韦嬷嬷便来到素心草堂约姑娘出去走走。
[EN] "Too grateful to ever be mad!"
[ZH] 千里寻欣然答应，自从嫁过来，还未在陈府认真逛过，正好刚用过早饭，不如陪韦嬷嬷逛逛陈府的园子，顺便消消食。
[EN] Every face beamed until Chen Jianglin put on his stern father mask. "Warm morning—quit draping yourself on Grandmother and stand straight to answer questions."
[ZH] 两人专挑风景好的园子散步，周围鸟语花香，美不胜收。
[EN] Chen Haoyu hopped upright. "Before you quiz me, Father, I have something to ask you."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷赞道：“要不是陪姑娘出来散步，都不知陈府有这么好的园子，四周都是花。”
[EN] "Answer your elders first."
[ZH] 千里寻想起陈浩宇送的玫瑰花：“陈家的园子就是花多，连玫瑰花都有。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan jumped in. "Did Shuzhen truly forgive those rumors?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷仔细瞧向四周：“没见有玫瑰花，姑娘在哪里见的？”
[EN] "Would I lie? I'm her husband—we're one family! Since when does family hold grudges against family? Right, Mother?"
[ZH] 千里寻不想提及陈浩宇送花之事：“我听丫鬟们说，好像有个玫瑰园，不大，种满玫瑰花。”
[EN] Hearing him toss out "one family" cracked even Chen Jianglin's stern mask into a chuckle. Taking a sip of tea to steady his face, Chen Jianglin probed, "Shuzhen let you off way too easy—ought to have kept you locked up longer! What progress could you possibly make in two days?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷不想再聊玫瑰花之事，她把姑娘约出来有其他事想与她聊：“只有几日功夫姑娘就不再与姑爷计较散播流言之事，如此心胸，我这把年岁的人都自愧不如。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu paused, then spoke straight. "Two days isn't long, yet something shifted in me. I'm done roaming outside—want to stay home and pitch in. Father, assign me some business work so I can earn my own silver, instead of coming up empty-handed when I want to buy my wife a gift and getting laughed at."
[ZH] “计较又有何用？不过是为难自己，我不想跟自己过不去，放陈浩宇一马，也是放过我自己。现在没有仇恨和计较，只觉一身轻松，欣赏起周围的景色来，更觉赏心悦目。”
[EN] Surprise flashed across Old Madam Xia and Yao Qingyan's faces, blooming instantly into joy. Even Chen Jianglin dropped all pretense and grinned. "That's what you came to ask?"
[ZH] “姑娘年纪轻轻就明白这等道理，实在不简单。”
[EN] "Exact right!"
[ZH] “我哪有这等本事？是受到城外山上尼姑庵的法师开示，顿悟后才明白的。”
[EN] "Seriously quitting the streets and joining family trade?"
[ZH] “那也不简单。尼姑庵的法师肯定开示过不少人，顿悟的能有几个？我家姑娘有慧根。”
[EN] "He just said yes—why keep grilling him?" Old Madam Xia chided her son. "Drop that habit of doubting your own boy!"
[ZH] “嬷嬷能夸赞我，说明嬷嬷也是有慧根之人。”
[EN] "My fault, Mother—I'll fix it," Chen Jianglin chuckled while Yao Qingyan dabbed happy tears: "Haoyu truly grew up! Happiest day of my life!"
[ZH] 两人互相赞叹，不由笑了一会儿。
[EN] Seeing how ecstatic one decent choice made his family suddenly struck Chen Haoyu with a sharp pang of sheepish guilt over years of disappointing them. Changing the pace fast, he asked, "When do I start, Father?"
[ZH] 继续向前走，来到一处池塘边，里面的荷叶比墨香苑的荷叶大很多，过不了多久，花骨朵会渐渐从湖底长出，冲破水面。
[EN] "Whenever you're ready."
[ZH] 千里寻与韦嬷嬷走累，在湖边的亭子里坐下。
[EN] "Sooner the better!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷欣赏着池水中正在生长的荷叶，冷不丁问道：“姑娘难道打算一辈子不与姑爷圆房？就这么各过各的？”
[EN] "With our southwest trade booming, the silk house is swamped. Report there—I'll send word to Steward Qiu to mentor you. Veteran hand; learn everything you can from him."
[ZH] 这个话题千里寻并不觉得尴尬，迅速回道：“嫁过来前，契约里写得明明白白，互不干涉。”
[EN] "Count on me, Father! Uh—what's the monthly pay?"
[ZH] “听说姑娘已把契约烧毁。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin laughed. "Haven't lifted a finger and already asking wages? Nail the work first, and silver won't be short!"
[ZH] “我会和陈浩宇重新立契约。”
[EN] "Not sending him today, are you?" Old Madam Xia cut in. "Once he gets busy I'll barely see him—let him start tomorrow!"
[ZH] “我一向喜欢纵着姑娘，可此事不得不多说两句。”
[EN] Nobody said no to Mother. Agreeing every bit of this miracle belonged to Shuzhen, all three decided to walk over and thank her together.
[ZH] 千里寻还是明白的，韦嬷嬷向来不说废话，从她口中说出的事情没有不重要的。
[EN] Meanwhile, right after Chen Haoyu left Moxiang Courtyard, Matron Wei had invited Qianli Xun for a morning stroll through Chen Manor's sprawling gardens—her first real look around since arriving.
[ZH] “嬷嬷请讲。”
[EN] Strolling amid birdsong and spring blossoms, Matron Wei marveled, "Didn't realize Chen Manor hid gardens this lush—flowers on every path!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷先发问道：“姑娘认为陈家如何？”
[EN] Remembering Chen Haoyu's bouquet, Qianli Xun remarked, "Even got roses here."
[ZH] 千里寻沉思了一会儿回道：“除了陈浩宇哪里都好。”
[EN] Glancing around, Matron Wei saw none. "Where?"
[ZH] “若姑爷变好了呢？”
[EN] Not wanting to bring up his gift, Qianli Xun hedged, "Maids mentioned a small rose garden somewhere."
[ZH] “他……怎么可能变好？”
[EN] Dropping flowers, Matron Wei steered to her real reason for the walk. "Letting go of last year's slander in mere days—even at my age I couldn't match your generosity, Miss."
[ZH] “据我观察，姑娘管教的时日虽短，姑爷变化却很大。”
[EN] "Holding grudges only tortures myself. Letting him off lets me off too—now the whole garden looks twice as bright."
[ZH] “嬷嬷千万不要相信，都是陈浩宇装出来的，是他对付我的招数而已。”
[EN] "Grasping that wisdom at your age is remarkable."
[ZH] “我看不尽然，姑爷是真的变了。”
[EN] "Hardly my wisdom—that elderly nun at the hillside nunnery woke me up."
[ZH] 此时，千里寻不得不相信陈浩宇有变化：“那又如何？难道他变好我就得与他圆房？”
[EN] "Plenty listen to her sermons; few actually wake up. You have genuine insight, Miss."
[ZH] “我是过来人，深知女子嫁人不易，尤其嫁到一户好人家更不容易，姑娘幸运，陈家比林家、乔家不知强多少倍？希望姑娘懂得珍惜。姑爷变好，你要学着慢慢接纳他。”
[EN] "Takes insight to spot insight, Matron!" Both shared a warm laugh.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷主动提起此事，足以说明往后的人生之路只能朝这个结果走。千里寻一时还无法接受：“可我不喜欢陈浩宇。”
[EN] Reaching a wide pond dotted with broad lotus pads soon to push up buds, they rested inside a lakeside pavilion. Gazing over the water, Matron Wei asked out of the blue, "Miss, do you truly plan never to consummate your marriage with Young Master—living separate lives forever?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷叹口气：“唉，这世道有几个女子能有幸嫁给自己喜欢的人？”
[EN] Unfazed, Qianli Xun answered crisp, "Our prenuptial contract said no meddling."
[ZH] 两人正说着，苦荞赶来报告：“小姐，老太太、大老爷和大夫人来墨香苑，您不在，我便赶过来找您。”
[EN] "Heard you burned it."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷站起来：“走吧，回去拜见长辈，如果我没猜错，他们是前来感谢姑娘。”
[EN] "We'll draw up a new one."
[ZH] 千里寻边思索往后的日子边起身，她也明白自己嫁给陈浩宇，与他圆房给他生孩子是应尽的义务和责任。可她现在还无法走出这一步，谁让她的心里藏着另一个人，也不知他何时回到临安？
[EN] "I usually indulge everything you wish, Miss, yet on this I must speak plain."
[ZH] 她想起申简辰去汴梁前在醉云楼说出的那句话：“我会为你守身如玉。”既觉得感动，又有强烈的罪恶感。他若为她守身如玉，梦容该怎么办？
[EN] Knowing Matron Wei never wasted words, Qianli Xun turned attentive. "Go ahead, Matron."
[ZH] 千里寻逼迫自己不要去想不该想的人，可越是逼迫，他的影子越是在眼前晃来晃去。
[EN] "How do you find the Chen household?"
[ZH] 回到墨香苑时，发现三位长辈正坐在合 欢树下。
[EN] "Wonderful in every way—except Chen Haoyu."
[ZH] 老太太享受着藤椅，陈江林和姚轻琰则让丫鬟把桌椅搬出来，边品着红豆做的茶边与老太太聊天。陈浩宇则坐在小凳子上靠着奶奶。
[EN] "What if Young Master genuinely reforms?"
[ZH] 合 欢树下一派祥和的家庭美景，羡慕之情油然而生，千里寻喜欢有老人，有爹娘，有晚辈的家庭，更羡慕家人之间充满温情的场景，就像眼前这样。她从心底已将陈家看成自己的家，若没有陈浩宇那该多好。
[EN] "Him? Reform?"
[ZH] 正当千里寻沉浸在没有陈浩宇的想象中时，她的手被人拉起。
[EN] "From what I've watched, brief as your discipline was, he has changed deeply."
[ZH] “喂，跑哪里去了？害得我们好等。”
[EN] "Don't fall for it, Matron! Just a fresh act to disarm me."
[ZH] 千里寻从想象中清醒过来，定眼一瞧，原来拉她手的人正是陈浩宇，她立刻甩开：“别跟老娘拉拉扯扯。”
[EN] "I disagree. He's truly changing."
[ZH] “好好，我不拉你就是。”陈浩宇又回到合 欢树下坐在凳子上靠着老太太。
[EN] Forced to entertain it for a second, Qianli Xun shrugged. "So what? Him changing doesn't mean I have to sleep with him!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷看在眼里，明在心里。姑娘从心底喜欢这个家，接受姑爷仍需时日。她先上前打招呼：“若知道三位前来，我就不把姑娘叫出去陪我散步。”
[EN] "I've lived long enough to know how hard marriage is for a woman, and how rare a decent family is. The Chens outshine the Lins and Qiaos ten times over—treasure it, Miss. If Young Master turns good, learn to open your heart slowly."
[ZH] 红豆和阿美又搬来两张椅子，供小姐和韦嬷嬷就座。
[EN] Hearing Matron Wei push this meant the inevitable loomed closer than she wanted. "I don't love Chen Haoyu."
[ZH] 夏老太太说：“快坐下，陪我们说说话。”
[EN] Matron Wei sighed. "How many women in this world ever get to marry for love?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷欣然坐下。
[EN] Just then Kuqiao hurried up. "Miss! Old Madam Xia, Eldest Master, and Eldest Madam are waiting at Moxiang Courtyard!"
[ZH] 千里寻依然沉浸在眼前美好的景象中，竟然忘记上前拜见长辈。
[EN] Matron Wei stood. "Come greet the elders—bet they came to thank you."
[ZH] 姚轻琰干脆起身走到她跟前：“淑真，怎么不过去？该不会生我们的气？”
[EN] Rising with a tangled mind, Qianli Xun knew full well society expected her to share her husband's bed and bear children—yet another man still filled her heart. Where was Shen Jianchen now? Remembering his vow at Zuiyun Tower—"I'll stay chaste for you"—flooded her with equal parts ache and guilt toward Mengrong. Trying to shove his image out only made it dance sharper before her eyes.
[ZH] “怎么会？我只觉此情此景甚是美好，想多看会儿，把它刻在心里。”
[EN] Walking back into Moxiang Courtyard, she paused at the scene beneath the hehuan tree: Old Madam Xia lounging in the rattan chair, Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan seated around the table sipping Hongdou's whisked tea, Chen Haoyu perched on a low stool leaning affectionately against his grandmother. Warmth and peace radiated from the cluster—the exact big, loving family she had always longed for. In her bones, Chen Manor already felt like home... if only Chen Haoyu weren't part of the deal.
[ZH] 姚轻琰拉起淑真的手：“既然美好，你也过去，让自己成为美景中的一员。”
[EN] Before she could finish the daydream, a warm hand caught hers. "Hey! Where'd you run off to? Kept us waiting!"
[ZH] 婆婆无意中的一句话，千里寻觉得颇具哲理，“让自己成为景中的一员”，对啊，既然觉得好，为何要身处景外欣赏？进入景中肯定别具一番滋味。
[EN] Snapping awake, she whipped her hand clean out of Chen Haoyu's grasp. "Keep your hands off me!"
[ZH] 被婆婆拉着来到合欢树下，千里寻先向长辈行礼：“早知三位长辈喜欢坐在合欢树下，我应该多给树下摆放些桌椅。”
[EN] "All right, all right, no touching!" Grinning sheepish, he trotted back to his stool beside Old Madam Xia.
[ZH] 夏老太太将身子坐起：“不必，摆太多桌椅有失美观，一个藤椅和一张木桌足矣。”
[EN] Matron Wei read the whole picture at a glance: Miss loved this home, just needed time for the husband. Stepping forward, she greeted them warmly. "Had I known you three were visiting, I wouldn't have dragged Miss off for a walk!"
[ZH] 陈江林说：“我们也不常来，偶尔过来坐坐，不必为我们特别准备，摆放多了，反而破坏院中的景致。别再站着，快坐下。”
[EN] Hongdou and Amei fetched two more chairs while Qianli Xun lingered a beat taking in the cozy tableau. Yao Qingyan rose and walked over. "Why stand back there, Shuzhen? Still mad at us?"
[ZH] 千里寻在韦嬷嬷身边的椅子上坐下。
[EN] "Not at all—just looked so peaceful I wanted to freeze the picture in my head."
[ZH] 陈浩宇的目光一直未离开朱淑真，越看越喜欢，越看越顺眼。
[EN] Taking her hand, Yao Qingyan smiled. "If it's lovely, step inside and become part of the picture."
[ZH] 千里寻感觉到陈浩宇的目光，向他瞪了一眼。陈浩宇装作没看见，趁朱淑真不注意，继续偷看。
[EN] Those simple words struck a deep chord—why stay outside gazing in when she could step inside? Walking under the tree, she curtsied. "Had I known you elders loved this spot, I'd have set more chairs out."
[ZH] 姚轻琰瞧在眼里，乐在心里。彻底明白儿子之所以愿意改变，完全是因为喜欢上淑真，慨叹男女情爱的力量实在不小，硬生生把一个浪荡子改变过来，虽不是淑真管教的结果，也与她息息相关，如此想来她真是陈家的福星。
[EN] "One rattan chair and table keep the yard uncluttered," Old Madam Xia said. "Sit down!"
[ZH] “淑真，今儿我与老太太，还有你公爹过来主要是感谢你。”
[EN] As Qianli Xun took a seat beside Matron Wei, Chen Haoyu's gaze stayed glued to her face with shameless fondness—ducking only to steal more looks whenever she glared back. Watching her son, Yao Qingyan beamed silently: Love really did move mountains, turning a wild rake overnight—making Shuzhen the Chen family's lucky star!
[ZH] 陈江林说：“一来感谢你不再计较流言之事；二来感谢你把浩宇管教得这么好。”
[EN] "We came to thank you today, Shuzhen," Yao Qingyan began.
[ZH] 千里寻并不想瞒着陈家人，更不想捞功：“长辈们不必谢我，之所以不再计较流言之事，完全出于为自己考虑；至于陈浩宇的变化，我更不敢居功，我管教他不过是报复而已，他的变化与我无关。况且才两日，即使真的管教，也不可能两日就把一个人给改变过来。”
[EN] "First for letting last year's rumors go," Chen Jianglin added, "and second for turning Haoyu around!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇趁机自夸：“所以说，我本来就好，根本不需要他人管教。”
[EN] Refusing stolen credit, Qianli Xun spoke straight. "No thanks needed. Dropping the rumors was for my own peace of mind, and I can't claim credit for Chen Haoyu—I only punished him for payback, and nobody transforms in two days."
[ZH] 老太太继续靠在藤椅上，边享受合欢树荫带来的凉爽，边说：“不管你是怎么想的，产生的效果极佳，便是你的功劳。看在你帮奶奶管教出一个好孙子，要什么奖赏，尽管开口，即使有难度，奶奶也想办法满足你。”
[EN] "Exactly!" Chen Haoyu jumped in. "I was always a gem underneath—needed zero discipline!"
[ZH] 千里寻本就不觉得自己有什么功劳，奖赏更不在她考虑的范围内，本想拒绝，猛然想起韦嬷嬷在池塘边的亭子里提到圆房一事。
[EN] "Whatever your motive," Old Madam Xia chuckled from her rattan chair, "the result is pure gold, so credit belongs to you! Name any reward you want for giving me back a good grandson—even if it's tough, Grandmother will deliver!"
[ZH] 再瞧瞧眼前的陈浩宇，虽已成为长辈口中改好的孩子，可就目前而言，她还无法对他产生感情。只怕眼前的长辈迟早会像韦嬷嬷一样与她讨论同样的话题，到时恐怕为难的是自己。不如趁此机会提出要求，避免长辈们将来难为她。
[EN] About to wave any reward aside, Qianli Xun suddenly remembered Matron Wei's warning by the lotus pond. Sooner or later these elders would press her to consummate the marriage—better cash this blank check right now to buy herself breathing room!
[ZH] “奶奶既然要给我奖赏，我就不客气。什么金银财宝、田产铺子、精美物件等等我统统不要，只需陈家长辈答应我件事。”
[EN] "Since you offered, Grandmother, I won't be shy! Keep gold, jewels, estates, and trinkets—I only ask one promise from you elders."

#CH 242
[ZH] 姑娘还未说出，韦嬷嬷已心知肚明。
[ZH] 她今日主动向姑娘提起与姑爷圆房一事，有两个目的：一是提醒姑娘若想在这个家待下去恐怕与姑爷圆房在所难免，她需有个思想准备；二是希望姑娘提前想好应对之策，省得陈家长辈提出时，横冲直撞，搞得大家关系紧张。
[ZH] 果然起到良好的效果，姑娘抓住要奖赏的时机，必然提及此事。
[ZH] 只听陈江林问：“什么事？尽管说。”
[ZH] 千里寻深感此事重大，站起来，向三位长辈行礼：“请奶奶、公爹和婆婆答应，在我对陈浩宇没产生感情之前，别要求我与他圆房。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷观察三位主人，他们均露出失落的表情，因失落沉默不语。
[ZH] 陈浩宇也是一阵失落，说明母老虎还没喜欢上他，又担心她被长辈批评，赶紧打圆场：“行了行了，我答应你。你现在不喜欢我，是因为还没发现我的优点，以后本公子会时不时在你面前展示优点，你迟早会喜欢上我。”
[ZH] 三位长辈沉默带来的尴尬被陈浩宇的话打破，千里寻心里感到轻松许多，第一次觉得陈浩宇盲目自信的话没那么讨厌。
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷的嘴角也露出笑容，心想：“姑爷的确比以前可爱许多。”
[ZH] 孙子的话已说，夏老太太不再继续沉默：“浩宇既同意，我更没有必要反对，只是孙媳的想法的确与其他女子不同，我老了，需慢慢适应。”
[ZH] 毕竟是儿媳妇，陈江林面对此话题不好表态，向姚轻琰使眼色。
[ZH] 姚轻琰还沉浸在失落的情绪中，淑真什么都好，就是讲究太多。接触到夫君的目光，不得不说：“淑真啊，你跟浩宇婚前立下契约，长辈们只当你们太年轻玩过家家。既然婚也结了，浩宇也愿意变好，没必要再把契约当回事……”
[ZH] 提到契约，千里寻忍不住说：“契约已被我烧毁。”
[ZH] 姚轻琰终于露出笑容：“烧得好！既然已烧毁，好好跟浩宇过日子，圆房是早晚的事。”
[ZH] 千里寻说：“一时情绪所迫，才烧毁，打算再与陈浩宇重新立一契约。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇立刻说：“我不同意！男子汉大丈夫，一言九鼎，答应你的事记在心里就行，何必被一契约限制？放心，我不会随便逼你圆房。”
[ZH] 千里寻很不爽，心想：“哼！老娘也是你能逼的？”
[ZH] 姚轻琰劝道：“淑真，契约既然已烧毁，没有必要再重新立。女子嫁人，就是要为夫家生儿育女，你不愿圆房，我跟你公爹何时才能抱上孙子？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇认为自己最多一年功夫便能将朱淑真搞定，见母亲依然不松口，哄道：“娘放心，一年后保准让您抱上孙子。”
[ZH] 三位长辈都“呵呵”笑起来。
[EN] Before Qianli Xun even opened her mouth, Matron Wei knew exact what was coming. Bringing up consummation by the lotus pond had served two aims: preparing her mistress for reality, and giving her space to negotiate smoothly instead of clashing head-on with the elders. Right on target!
[ZH] 千里寻对陈浩宇所言不以为然：“婆婆着急抱孙子也不难，我愿意帮陈浩宇纳妾，孩子由妾室来生。”
[EN] "Name it," Chen Jianglin invited.
[ZH] 三位长辈又没了笑容。
[EN] Rising solemnly to curtsy before all three elders, Qianli Xun stated, "Please promise me, Grandmother, Father-in-law, and Mother-in-law: until I genuinely develop feelings for Chen Haoyu, do not pressure me to consummate our marriage."
[ZH] 陈浩宇急忙说：“我不同意！我只跟喜欢的人生孩子。”
[EN] Heavy disappointment dropped over all three elders' faces, locking the yard in awkward silence.
[ZH] 已到这个份上，韦嬷嬷再不开口，恐怕两边为难：“老太太、老爷、夫人，可喜可贺。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu felt a sharp sting too—proof she hadn't fallen for him yet—yet jumped right in to shield her from scolding. "Deal, deal! I promise! Only reason you don't like me yet is you haven't seen my good points—once I show them off, you'll fall for me soon enough!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰沉浸在淑真不愿圆房的忧愁中：“喜从何来？”
[EN] Breaking the stiff silence lifted a stone off Qianli Xun's chest; for the first time, his cocky bluff didn't grate on her ears. Matron Wei hid a small smile too—Young Master was growing downright likable.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷娓娓道来：“要搁往常，姑爷早已迈出陈府大门，可他被姑娘管教几日后终于获得自由，不仅没着急往出跑，还陪长辈们拉家常，甚至还帮我家姑娘说话，明显是转了性情，陈家又多出一位做事的后辈，难道不该恭贺？”
[EN] "If Haoyu agrees, I won't object," Old Madam Xia broke the silence. "Your views sure differ from ordinary girls—takes an old woman time to adjust."
[ZH] 夏老太太立刻眉开眼笑：“是啊，今儿还主动要求他爹给安排生意上的事。”
[EN] Awkward discussing bedroom affairs with a daughter-in-law, Chen Jianglin nudged his wife with a glance.
[ZH] 姚轻琰也乐起来，暂时忘记刚才忧愁之事，见儿媳妇站着，说道：“淑真，别站着了，坐下说话。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan sighed. "Shuzhen, we elders treated that prenuptial contract as youthful games. Now that you're wed and Haoyu is reforming, why cling to paper?"
[ZH] 只有奶奶同意不要求她与陈浩宇圆房，公爹与婆婆还未松口，千里寻很不情愿坐下。又想着韦嬷嬷已开口，应该会向着她说话，不如坐下听听她说些什么，再见机行事。于是回到椅子旁乖乖坐下。
[EN] "I already burned the contract," Qianli Xun noted.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷接着说：“所以说，一切无形中自然而然往好的方面发展，急不得。既然急不得，不如顺着；顺着，大家都舒心，心情舒畅，自然顺风顺水，还愁不会有好结果出现？再说我等都是俗人，眼界有限，谁也无法预知明日之事，姑爷就是现成的例子。两日前他还在青楼逛荡，而现在……”
[EN] Yao Qingyan brightened. "Good riddance! Then live as real husband and wife—consummation is only a matter of time."
[ZH] 陈浩宇倒懂事，再说他也需在母老虎面前多多表现，此时不说更待何时？
[EN] "Burned it in a fit of anger—meant to draw up a new one."
[ZH] “往后我决不会再去青楼。”
[EN] "No way!" Chen Haoyu cut in. "A real man's word is gold! Promised you already—why hide behind paper? Relax, I won't force you!"
[ZH] 三位长辈再次笑成一朵花。
[EN] As if you ever could! Qianli Xun scoffed silently.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷见主人家开心，继续说：“刚才姑娘提的要求的确唐突些，不过是她今日所想，明日会不会如此想，很难说。”
[EN] "Skip a new contract, Shuzhen," Yao Qingyan pleaded gently. "A wife enters her husband's house to carry on the family line. If you refuse to consummate, when will your father-in-law and I ever hold a grandson?"
[ZH] 陈江林终于忍不住开口：“唉，孩子们的事，由他们自己解决，大人们操再多心也没用，儿孙自有儿孙福。”
[EN] Confident he could crack her armor within twelve months, Chen Haoyu grinned. "Relax, Mother! One year from now I guarantee you're holding a grandson!"
[ZH] 夫君已松口，姚轻琰也没必要坚持，再者韦嬷嬷都说了，急不得，需顺着，说不定顺着顺着就顺出好结果，于是笑道：“淑真，圆不圆房是你跟浩宇的事，老太太都不理会，我还理会个什么劲儿？往后可别再提起。”
[EN] All three elders chuckled—until Qianli Xun tossed cold water over it: "Want a grandson fast, Mother-in-law? Easy—I'm happy to arrange concubines for Chen Haoyu to bear children right away!"
[ZH] 听完长辈所说，千里寻反而生出愧疚来。换位思考一下，若她是陈家长辈，儿媳妇不愿与自己的儿子圆房，心里肯定不好受。
[EN] Every smile vanished.
[ZH] 她起身向三位长辈行礼：“感谢奶奶、公爹和婆婆体恤，千言万语无从表达，以后淑真会从心底把陈家当成自己的家。”
[EN] "No way!" Chen Haoyu blurted instantly. "I only have children with the woman I love!"
[ZH] 夏老太太说：“一家人不说两家话，奖赏就算给你了，以后不许再问我讨要别的。”
[EN] Sensing deadlock looming, Matron Wei stepped gracefully into the breach. "Old Madam, Master, Madam—congratulations are in order!"
[ZH] 大家都笑起来。
[EN] "Congratulations for what?" Yao Qingyan sighed.
[ZH] 以往陈浩宇没有认真体会过亲情，今日竟然觉出跟家人在一起的温暖和舒适。陈浩宇看向朱淑真，都是因为对她动情，才会体验到从未有过的感觉。原来喜欢上一个人后，周围所有事物都不由自主变得美好起来。
[EN] "Think back," Matron Wei said smoothly. "Normally Young Master would have bolted out the gate the instant he got free. Instead he stayed home chatting with elders, stood up for Miss, and completely shifted his tune—giving the Chen family another capable heir! Isn't that worth celebrating?"
[ZH] 长辈们离开后，千里寻抱住韦嬷嬷感谢一番。
[EN] "True!" Old Madam Xia lit up. "Even asked his father for business work today!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇在一旁笑眼旁观，心中思忖，这位嬷嬷实在不一般，说不定，她有办法让母老虎喜欢上自己，不如找机会向她请教。
[EN] Yao Qingyan cheered right back up and waved Shuzhen down. "Stop standing—sit!"
[ZH] 当韦嬷嬷与朱淑真分开，一个回素心草堂，一个往后院拐。
[EN] With only Old Madam Xia agreeing so far, Qianli Xun sat reluctantly, waiting to see where Matron Wei steered this.
