[ZH] 陈浩宇犹豫一会儿，决定还是先尾随韦嬷嬷，瞅准时机向她讨教一番。
[EN] "Everything is already moving naturally toward good," Matron Wei continued. "You cannot rush ripeness; push against the grain and everyone chafes, flow with it and good results bloom on their own. Who among us predicts tomorrow? Two days ago Young Master haunted brothels, and today—"
[ZH] 刚走出几步，丑丫过来：“姑爷，我能不能找青瓜说会儿话？”
[EN] "Swear I'll never step foot in a brothel again!" Chen Haoyu chimed in on cue, making all three elders beam wide.
[ZH] “随便，我忙着呢，只要别打扰我就行。”陈浩宇继续跟上韦嬷嬷。
[EN] "Miss's request sounds blunt today," Matron Wei finished lightly, "yet who knows how her heart will feel tomorrow?"
[ZH] 丑丫迅速拉起青瓜往西院走，丑丫劲儿大，青瓜挣脱不开，很不情愿地被她拽着。
[EN] Chen Jianglin finally spoke up. "Leave young folks' business to young folks. Worrying won't help—children walk their own fate."
[ZH] 刚跟到后院，陈浩宇发现韦嬷嬷站着等他：“姑爷找我有事？”
[EN] With her husband yielding and Matron Wei's calm logic sinking in, Yao Qingyan smiled warm. "Fine, Shuzhen—consummation is between you and Haoyu. If Mother isn't fussing, neither will I! Drop the subject."
[ZH] 陈浩宇嬉皮笑脸竖起大拇指：“今日才发现，嬷嬷就是这个，厉害！太厉害！”
[EN] Unexpectedly, their grace left Qianli Xun feeling a twist of guilt—any parents would ache hearing a bride refuse their son's bed. Standing to bow deep, she spoke from the heart: "Thank you for your kindness, Grandmother, Father-in-law, and Mother-in-law. Words fall short—from today on, I truly treat Chen Manor as my home."
[ZH] “姑爷想跟我说什么？”
[EN] "One family needs no fancy speeches," Old Madam Xia grinned. "Consider your reward paid—no asking for anything else!" Laughter rippled under the tree.
[ZH] “嬷嬷常年跟母老虎在一起，对她应该很了解，给我教教，怎么才能让母老虎喜欢上我？”
[EN] For the first time in his life, Chen Haoyu truly tasted family warmth, gazing fondly at Shuzhen—funny how loving one person painted the whole world bright.
[ZH] “这个简单，首先不能再唤我家姑娘母老虎。”
[EN] Once the elders left, Qianli Xun threw her arms around Matron Wei in thanks. Watching from the side, Chen Haoyu thought: That matron's a genius—maybe she can coach me on winning Zhu Shuzhen over!
[ZH] “以后再也不唤。”
[EN] When Qianli Xun returned to Suxin Thatched Hall and Matron Wei headed back toward Jingyi Hall, Chen Haoyu trailed after the matron—waving Chouya off to drag a reluctant Qinggua toward the West Courtyard—only to find Matron Wei waiting calmly at the rear archway.
[ZH] “其次，要懂得关心姑娘。”
[EN] "Something on your mind, Young Master?"
[ZH] “怎么关心？”
[EN] Grinning wide, he flashed a thumbs-up. "Just realized you're the real MVP, Matron! Amazing!"
[ZH] “多向大老爷学习。”
[EN] "What do you want to ask?"
[ZH] “没问题，还有呢？”
[EN] "You know the tigress inside out—coach me! How do I make her fall for me?"
[ZH] “姑爷的软招使得不错，继续努力。”
[EN] "Simple. Rule one: stop calling Miss 'tigress.'"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说完转身回静宜堂。
[EN] "Done! Never again!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇站在原地，甚是诧异：“我使软招时，韦嬷嬷也没在跟前，她怎么知道？真是神了，难道是哪个下人告诉她的？看来是这样。”
[EN] "Rule two: learn to care for her thoughtfully."
[ZH] 陈浩宇着急到朱淑真面前表现，顾不上想那么多，又往前院拐去。
[EN] "How?"
[ZH] 刚到前院，见青瓜耷拉个脑袋出现在面前。
[EN] "Watch Eldest Master."
[ZH] “怎么了？看上去跟霜打的茄子一样？”
[EN] "Got it! Anything else?"
[ZH] 青瓜一脸委屈：“我刚到西院，无意中听到阿升向红豆表白，红豆答应了。我一气之下拉着丑丫到红豆面前生气地说，‘你竟然答应跟阿升在一起，告诉你，我也有喜欢的人，就是丑丫。’说完，我就后悔了。”
[EN] "Your soft approach works nicely—keep at it." With that, Matron Wei turned into Jingyi Hall.
[ZH] “呵呵……，省得本公子再帮你和红豆撮合，你注定跟丑丫在一起。”陈浩宇笑完，手臂搭在青瓜的肩上，“其实丑丫挺不错，会功夫，你带她出去，都没人敢欺负你。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu blinked after her: She wasn't even around when I used the soft approach—how'd she know?! Must have spies everywhere!
[ZH] “红豆也会功夫。”
[EN] Eager to score points with his wife, he headed back to the front yard—and bumped into Qinggua drooping like frostbitten eggplant.
[ZH] “她是会功夫，可她心里有别人，即使跟你在一起迟早会红杏出墙。不像丑丫，对你一心一意，把她带出去，都不用担心她会红杏出墙，多省心。远了不说，就说西院那七个男仆，你把红豆放在他们中间，可放心？”
[EN] "What's with the long face?"
[ZH] 青瓜摇摇头：“不放心。”
[EN] "Walked into the West Courtyard and caught Asheng confessing to Hongdou—and she said yes! Mad out of my mind, I dragged Chouya right in front of Hongdou yelling, 'Fine, I've got Chouya!' Regretted it two seconds later!"
[ZH] “把丑丫放在他们中间呢？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu burst out laughing and slung an arm over Qinggua's shoulders. "Saves me playing matchmaker—guess Chouya's your destiny! Honestly she's a catch: knows martial arts, nobody dares touch you when she's around!"
[ZH] “自然放心。”
[EN] "Hongdou knows martial arts too!"
[ZH] “你若跟红豆在一起，时刻担心她被别人抢走，她跟哪个男仆多说一句话你都担心，肯定没有心思伺候本公子，时间一长，本公子对你自然不满，到时换个跟班都是有可能的事。相反，你跟丑丫在一起，什么都不用担心，可以安心做自己的事，反过来丑丫还会处处照顾你，仔细想想哪个更好？”
[EN] "Yeah, and her heart's on another guy—even if you married her, she'd stray sooner or later. Chouya's dead loyal to you! Leave Hongdou surrounded by those seven male servants—would you sleep easy?"
[ZH] “经公子这么一说，跟丑丫在一起更好。”
[EN] Qinggua shook his head. "Nope."
[ZH] “这就对了。”陈浩宇放开青瓜，“安心跟丑丫在一起。现在随本公子去见少夫人。”
[EN] "Leave Chouya surrounded by them?"
[ZH] 青瓜拉住公子：“公子，您说少夫人是哪种人？”
[EN] "Sleep like a baby!"
[ZH] “什么意思？”
[EN] "See? Marry Hongdou and you'll go gray guarding her instead of serving me, till I fire you! Marry Chouya and you live stress-free while she pampers you! Which is smarter?"
[ZH] “您放心的人？还是不放心的人？”
[EN] "Put like that... Chouya wins!"
[ZH] “本公子跟你不一样。”
[EN] "Attaboy! Settle down with Chouya and follow me to see Young Madam!"
[ZH] “可我听说您成亲那日，仪王抢亲来着。”
[EN] Qinggua caught his sleeve. "Wait, Young Master—which kind is Young Madam? Sleep-easy or worry-gray?"
[ZH] “去去去！少拿本公子的事跟你的事比，本公子要什么有什么？你哪一点能跟我比？母……”陈浩宇想说母老虎，想起韦嬷嬷叮嘱的话，赶紧改称呼，“朱淑真已嫁给我，成了你们的少夫人，我有什么好担心的？本公子没时间跟你废话，这就去讨好我娘子。”
[EN] "My league isn't your league!"
[ZH] 青瓜放开公子，跟在身后，心中想：“公子竟然用‘讨好’两个字，把自己摆得位置够廉价的。”
[EN] "Didn't Prince Yi raid your wedding though?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇悄悄走进素心草堂，按照他的猜测，朱淑真应该坐在书桌前。果不其然，她就坐在那里写写写。陈浩宇纳闷，诗词也不长，她怎么写那么久？也不知有什么好写的？
[EN] "Shoo! Quit comparing yourself to me! I've got looks, wealth, charm—she married me, she's your young madam, what's to worry about? Quit wasting breath—I'm off to woo my wife!"
[ZH] 苦荞注意到姑爷，小声提醒：“小姐，姑爷来了。”
[EN] Trailing behind, Qinggua smirked silently: Look at Young Master calling it "wooing"—dropped his ego straight through the floor!
[ZH] 千里寻抬起头：“不准向前走！就站那里说话！”
[EN] Slipping into Suxin Thatched Hall, Chen Haoyu found her bent over her desk writing—wondering how short poems took hours of scribbling.
[ZH] “我怎么说也是你的夫君，墨香苑的一家之主，总像下人一样站着跟你说话，也太不像话？还是坐着好。”没等朱淑真答应，陈浩宇找椅子坐下，“苦荞，听说你很会做茶，给你家姑爷我做盏茶来。”
[EN] "Young Master is here," Kuqiao alerted softly.
[ZH] 苦荞正要去做，被小姐拦住。
[EN] Qianli Xun lifted her head. "Halt right there! Talk from where you stand!"
[ZH] “他有青瓜伺候，什么时候轮你伺候他？”
[EN] "I'm your husband and master of Moxiang Courtyard—can't stand here like a lackey!" Plopping into a chair uninvited, he called, "Kuqiao, hear you make killer tea—whisk me a bowl!"
[ZH] 苦荞只得站住。
[EN] "He's got Qinggua—since when do you wait on him?" Qianli Xun cut her off.
[ZH] 陈浩宇并不计较：“青瓜，交给你个任务，好好跟苦荞学做茶，赶明儿我也在博雅斋安置一个茶桌，也感受感受附庸风雅的玩意儿。”
[EN] Unfazed, he told Qinggua, "New mission: learn tea-whisking from Kuqiao! I'll set up a tea table in Boya Study too and try out this classy hobby!"
[ZH] 千里寻搁下笔：“有什么事？赶紧说，老娘还忙着。”
[EN] Setting down her brush, Qianli Xun snapped, "Spit out what you want—I'm busy."
[ZH] 陈浩宇主动邀请道：“你整日没事趴在书桌上写来写去，多没意思，时间长了，腰上会长肉，影响身材，不如我带你出去逛逛？”
[EN] "Hunched over a desk all day will pad your waist—come out for a stroll with me!"
[ZH] 通过今日观察，千里寻已确定陈浩宇的确对她动起心思，只可惜她还无法接受，于是果断拒绝：“跟你出去逛，老娘毫无兴致。”
[EN] Clear now he had genuine feelings she couldn't return, she shut it down crisp: "Zero interest strolling with you."
[ZH] “你说什么我都不生气，给你提个意见。”
[EN] "Say what you like, never makes me mad. Quick tip though—"
[ZH] “有话快说，有屁快放。”
[EN] "Talk fast or leave."
[ZH] “以后能不能别自称‘老娘’？这个称呼跟你的外表实在不搭。”
[EN] "Could you quit calling yourself 'yours truly like a tough old dame'? Doesn't match your pretty face."
[ZH] “你管得着吗？老娘就喜欢这个称呼，不喜欢，以后最好少在我眼前晃。”
[EN] "None of your business! Love talking how I talk—hate it, stay out of my sight!"
[ZH] “晃肯定得晃，毕竟住在一个院子，抬头不见低头见，而且你还是我娘子，我会时不时来看你。”
[EN] "Can't stay out of sight sharing one courtyard—and as your husband, I'll drop by plenty!"
[ZH] 这家伙心思如此明显，若日日来打扰，岂不烦死？千里寻和颜悦色道：“陈浩宇，不如就答应纳个妾室？如果答应，我肯定拿你当哥们儿，若在外被人欺负，也会帮你出面解决。”
[EN] Dreading daily visits, Qianli Xun put on a breezy smile. "Seriously, Chen Haoyu, agree to take a concubine! Say yes, and I'll treat you like my best bro—even back you up if anyone picks on you outside!"

#CH 243
[ZH] 一日之内，两次提到给他纳妾之事，她得多想让别的女人把他抢走。
[ZH] 陈浩宇失落之余再次拒绝：“我身边有你一人足矣，再多个人岂不把我烦死？以后别再提纳妾之事。”
[ZH] 面对陈浩宇的表白，千里寻只想让他清醒清醒。
[ZH] “你这种人我见多了，富家公子又多金，花心又无耻，有一日突然装作转了性，还想求得一心人的样子，再让别人夸几句，好像以前从未做过坏事一般。殊不知最令人讨厌，以后别再说些肉麻的话，老娘不吃这一套。”
[ZH] 没办法，喜欢上母老虎后，她说什么话，陈浩宇都不介意：“什么富家公子？我现在身无分文。我本来就这样儿，只是以前没遇见喜欢的人，做事荒唐些，现在有了喜欢的人，不想再荒唐而已，你又何必讨厌我？”
[ZH] 千里寻“哼”了一声，露出不屑的表情。
[ZH] 陈浩宇想起韦嬷嬷说的“其次，要懂得关心我家姑娘。”又想起在捶丸场时她喜欢吃他烤的肉串，心生一计。
[ZH] “喂，你整日待在陈府也无趣，我今日有时间，带你去西湖，我们在湖边一边烤肉吃肉串，一边欣赏西湖美景，我烤的肉可香了。”
[ZH] 千里寻想起去年陈浩宇在湖边烤肉的情景，气不打一处来：“陈浩宇！你有没有公德心？”
[ZH] “约你吃肉串，欣赏美景，怎么又跟公德心扯上关系？”
[ZH] “去年你跟着一帮人在湖边烤肉，踩踏花草，搞得烟熏火燎，走时肯定会留下一堆垃圾，西湖景色那么美，全都被你破坏！你自己没公德心，还想拉老娘下水？”
[ZH] 被喜欢的人批评，陈浩宇才觉出不对来：“好吧好吧，以后我大不了不在西湖边烤肉，你别生气。那你说说，想去哪里？我陪你去。”
[ZH] “老娘哪里都不去，你最好立刻消失，不要烦我。”
[ZH] 关心不成，还被批评没公德心，陈浩宇终于觉出想让对方喜欢上他难上加难。明日才去绸缎庄，不如趁今日无事去爹娘屋里走走，向爹取取经。
[ZH] 打定主意后，起身说：“娘子忙，我就不在此打扰。”
[ZH] 千里寻巴不得他快些离开：“不送。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇出了素心草堂，叹口气：“唉，我怎么娶这么一位难搞定的娘子？还鬼使神差地喜欢上她？”
[ZH] 青瓜应和道：“这就叫报应。”
[ZH] “怎么说话呢？”照以往，陈浩宇会给青瓜一脚，可自从向青瓜道歉后，脚自然而然使不出来。
[ZH] 公子没有踢他，青瓜反而不好意思起来：“我瞎说的，公子别当回事。”
[ZH] 没想到公子却问道：“说来听听，为什么认为是报应？”
[ZH] 青瓜大胆说：“公子以前搞定的姑娘太多，现在老天派像少夫人这种难搞定的来到您身边，才叫公平。”
[ZH] “说得有道理，我还是老老实实接了，谁让本公子毫无道理喜欢上她。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇来到父母的院子，映雪看到，打算向里面报告，被陈浩宇阻止，他想悄悄进去观察爹娘如何相处，尤其要观察陈江林如何对待姚轻琰。
[ZH] 当他悄悄跨进屋内时，陈江林正在妆台前给姚轻琰描眉添妆，两人脸上都带着笑容。陈浩宇认真听他们聊天儿的内容。
[ZH] “自从嫁给你，我描眉的技术越来越差。”
[ZH] “有我在不必夫人动手。”
[ZH] “都是你把我惯的。”
[ZH] “惯你是应该的，谁让你是我夫人。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇觉得陈江林应该是他见过的最会说情话的男人，他在朱淑真面前说的那些话与此相比真是相形见绌。
[ZH] 陈浩宇咳嗽两声：“咳咳！”
[ZH] 眉毛也画完，陈江林扭头看去：“浩宇来了，快进来坐。”
[ZH] 姚轻琰起身，拉起儿子：“你怎么跑来？不在墨香苑好好陪淑真？”
[EN] Twice in one day pushing concubines on him—how desperate was she to offload him? Swallowing the sting, Chen Haoyu refused flatly. "You alone are plenty—add another woman and I'd go nuts! Quit bringing up concubines."
[ZH] 陈浩宇回道：“我有事向爹请教，专程过来走一趟。”
[EN] Hoping to splash cold water over his newfound ardor, Qianli Xun spoke blunt. "Seen your type a hundred times: rich playboys sleeping around shameless, then one day acting 'reformed' chasing one true love so everyone applauds like the dirty past never happened! Makes my skin crawl. Save the cheesy lines—not buying it."
[ZH] 儿子变成他们理想中的样子，姚轻琰打心里高兴：“你们爷俩聊，我去陪老太太说话，一会儿说话艺人也该到，听说千里寻才子又写了新话本，故事更精彩。”
[EN] Nothing she flung could dent his mood anymore. "What rich playboy? I'm dead broke! Never met anyone I loved before, so I fooled around; now I found her, so I quit. Why hate me for that?"
[ZH] 姚轻琰放开儿子走出屋，带着丫鬟映雪去陪老太太。
[EN] She let out a cool huff.
[ZH] 陈江林在桌旁坐下：“坐下聊。”
[EN] Recalling Matron Wei's rule two—care for her—and remembering how she had wolfed down his lamb skewers at the chuiwan field, an idea popped up. "Stuck inside Chen Manor gets boring—I'm free today, let's head out to West Lake! I'll grill fresh skewers by the shore while we admire the water—my barbecue's unbeatable!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇乖乖坐下，请教的事不好意思主动开口，想等爹问时再说出前来的目的。
[EN] Memories of his noisy lakeside picnic last year lit her fuse instant. "Have you zero civic pride, Chen Haoyu?!"
[ZH] 陈江林说：“明日就要去绸缎庄忙活，想请教什么尽管开口，为父知无不言。”
[EN] "Inviting you for grilled meat and scenery—what's civic pride got to do with it?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇话未出口，先“嘿嘿”笑两声。
[EN] "Last year your gang trampled the grass by West Lake, choked the air with grease smoke, and left piles of trash ruining gorgeous scenery! Trash the lake yourself—don't drag me into it!"
[ZH] 陈江林催道：“别光顾着傻笑，赶紧说话。”
[EN] Chastised by the woman he loved, shame actually pricked him. "My bad, my bad—swear I won't grill by West Lake again! Don't stay mad. Pick wherever you want to go and I'll tag along."
[ZH] 陈浩宇这才说：“不是生意上的事，我想向爹请教搞定自家娘子的方法，嘿嘿……”
[EN] "Going nowhere! Vanish right now and leave me in peace!"
[ZH] 陈江林这才恍然大悟，接着笑道：“呵呵，搞定自家娘子也是大事，不比生意上的事小，你向爹请教算是找对人。”
[EN] Struck out again—even trying to be sweet earned a lecture! Realizing cracking her shell took real strategy—and not starting at the silk house until tomorrow—he decided to go study under the family master: his father. Rising politely, he said, "Won't bother you further, Wife."
[ZH] 得到爹的认可，陈浩宇不再感到难为情，变得更加大胆：“爹，赶快把您的绝招都教给儿子，您也好早些抱上孙子。”
[EN] "Don't let the door hit you."
[ZH] “呵呵呵……”陈江林笑一阵才说，“其实也没什么绝招，关键在用心。”
[EN] Stepping outside with a sigh, he muttered, "How'd I marry the hardest nut in Lin'an—and go crazy enough to fall for her?"
[ZH] “儿子用心了，可朱淑真不吃这一套。”
[EN] "Call it karma," Qinggua piped up.
[ZH] “用心了还连名带姓唤自家娘子？”
[EN] "Watch that mouth—" Automatically checking his boot—having sworn off kicking him—Chen Haoyu left Qinggua feeling sheepish. "Just joking, Young Master!"
[ZH] “儿子以后把姓去掉。”
[EN] "No, explain why it's karma."
[ZH] 浩宇不再像以前一样跟他对着干，变得态度端正，陈江林更觉把朱家姑娘娶回陈家这步棋走得实在妙。还有什么可犹豫的，赶快传授经验。
[EN] "Too many girls fell easy for you before, Young Master. Heaven sending an impossible boss like Young Madam balances the scales!"
[ZH] “想要搞定自家娘子，需做到三点……”
[EN] "Fair point! Guess I'll own it—can't help falling for her anyway."
[ZH] “哪三点？”
[EN] Reaching his parents' courtyard, Chen Haoyu waved Yingxue quiet so he could spy on how his father actually treated his mother. Peeking past the doorway, he saw Chen Jianglin standing at the dressing table delicately penciling Yao Qingyan's eyebrows while both smiled softly.
[ZH] “其一，要对你的娘子一心一意。男子最忌讳品行不端，今儿喜欢这个，明儿喜欢那个，搞得自家娘子伤心落泪，痛苦不已，家宅难安。你也看到，你爹我何时纳过妾室？”
[EN] "Ever since marrying you, my own brow-drawing skills have gone completely rusty," Yao Qingyan teased.
[ZH] “这点我能做到。其二呢？”
[EN] "With me right here, you never need lift a brush."
[ZH] “其二，要多陪伴自家娘子。男人无论在外面多忙，都要空出时间来陪伴娘子，否则她会怀疑你在外面是不是有了别的女子，或者胡乱猜测你跑去青楼，猜来猜去，心神不安，家宅也不宁，家宅不宁男人什么事都做不好。”
[EN] "You spoiled me rotten."
[ZH] “儿子记下。”
[EN] "My wife—who else should I spoil?"
[ZH] “其三，也是最重要的，要有一项技能是你的娘子所需，而你又做得最好。当你的娘子需要时，第一个想起的人就是你，说明她已经离不开你，还愁不把你放在心里？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu blinked in awe: Father puts every smooth line I ever tried to total shame! Clearing his throat—"Ahem!"—he stepped inside as Chen Jianglin laid down the brow pencil.
[ZH] “爹就是爹，真厉害，难怪我娘从来不跟您红脸。”陈浩宇竖完大拇指问道，“爹为娘画眉添妆难道就是您学的技能？”
[EN] "Haoyu! Come sit!"
[ZH] “不然呢？”
[EN] Yao Qingyan rose beaming to take his arm. "Not keeping Shuzhen company in Moxiang Courtyard?"
[ZH] “学了多久？”
[EN] "Need to ask Father's advice on something."
[ZH] “从你娘嫁给我那日学起，刚开始技术不行，还被你娘嫌弃，一年后技法纯熟，你娘是越来越满意，自己就不怎么动手，也不让丫鬟上手，每每此时就想起我，她已经离不开我。”
[EN] Thrilled to see her son transformed, Yao Qingyan excused herself to go hear the storyteller's fresh huaben with Old Madam Xia, leaving father and son alone.
[ZH] 陈浩宇以前也见过爹为娘添妆，那时觉得一个大男人为女人添妆实在有失男子气概，今日才真正理解其中的用意，反而觉出亲爹的厉害。
[EN] "Starting at the silk house tomorrow—ask anything you want about trade, I'll hold nothing back," Chen Jianglin invited.
[ZH] “第三条有难度，我需回去认真观察，才知淑真最需要什么，再努力练习技能。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu gave a sheepish chuckle—"Hehe..."
[ZH] 陈江林说：“你愿意在淑真身上花心思，爹很欣慰。她是好姑娘，脾气暴了些，什么时候你能让她变得温柔，才说明她已经接纳你。”
[EN] "Quit giggling and talk."
[ZH] 得到亲爹真传，陈浩宇有了努力的方向，起身行礼：“爹忙，我还得回去观察淑真所需，就不在此打扰。”
[EN] "Not business, Father... came to ask your secret for winning over your wife! Hehe!"
[ZH] 陈江林微笑着点点头：“去吧。”
[EN] Bursting into warm laughter, Chen Jianglin nodded. "Winning your wife's heart is just as vital as running the business—came to the right teacher!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇出了父母的院子，一路在想，如何才能了解娘子的需要？
[EN] Emboldened, Chen Haoyu leaned in. "Spill all your master moves, Father, so you hold a grandson sooner!"
[ZH] 看到身边的青瓜，想到一个好办法：“青瓜，你一会儿悄悄把苦荞叫到博雅斋，就说我有重要的事情问她。”
[EN] "No fancy tricks—pure sincerity."
[ZH] 回到墨香苑，陈浩宇直奔后院。
[EN] "I'm sincere! Zhu Shuzhen still doesn't buy it!"
[ZH] 青瓜则乖乖靠近素心草堂，趴在门口，偷偷向里面张望。少夫人依旧在书桌前写字，并不见苦荞的身影，于是继续把脖子伸长些向里望。
[EN] "Sincere, yet still calling your wife by her full name?"
[ZH] 身后传来苦荞的声音：“青瓜，你怎么像做贼一样趴在门口？”
[EN] "Dropping the surname from now on!"
[ZH] 青瓜赶紧转过身站好，笑嘻嘻地说：“我在找苦荞姐姐，巧了，你正好在外面，省得我喊你时打扰到少夫人。”
[EN] Seeing his boy genuinely humble, Chen Jianglin happily laid out his three golden rules.
[ZH] “找我何事？”
[EN] "Rule one: total devotion. Nothing poisons a home faster than a wandering eye leaving your wife weeping. Ever seen me take a concubine?"
[ZH] “我家公子找你。”
[EN] "Check! Rule two?"
[ZH] “姑爷为何找我？”
[EN] "Rule two: real companionship. No matter how busy trade gets, carve out time beside her—leave her lonely and suspicion creeps in, unsettling the home; when home shakes, nothing outside succeeds."
[ZH] 青瓜靠近苦荞神秘地说：“我家公子今日向大老爷请教搞定少夫人的绝招，其中有一条不容易，请你去肯定是问些重要的事。”
[EN] "Got it!"
[ZH] 姑爷愿意在小姐身上花心思，苦荞觉得甚好，露出满意的笑容：“看在姑爷如此诚心的份上，我就随你走一趟。”
[EN] "Rule three—the clincher: master one skill your wife truly needs better than anyone else. Every time she needs it, your face springs first to her mind—once she relies on you, her heart follows!"
[ZH] 来到博雅斋，苦荞发现姑爷竟然破天荒地坐在书桌前翻阅书籍。
[EN] Chen Haoyu flashed a huge thumbs-up. "Genius, Father! No wonder Mother never fights with you! Drawing her eyebrows is your secret skill?"
[ZH] 连青瓜都觉得纳闷，公子被惩罚时翻书他能理解，现在既然没有被惩罚，为何还要拿本书看？他好奇地上前问道：“公子，书有那么好看吗？”
[EN] "What else?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇放下书，原来是《王右丞诗集》：“瞧你那没文化的样子，难道没听过书中自有黄金屋，书中自有颜如玉？”
[EN] "How long did it take to master?"
[ZH] 青瓜目不识丁，并不关心公子看什么书，被公子批评，忍不住回上一嘴：“以前公子可不是这么说的，您那时说书中只有臭狗屎。”
[EN] "Practiced from our wedding day—she mocked my shaky lines at first, yet after a year I perfected it. Now she won't let herself or a maid touch her brows—only calls for me! Can't live without me!"
[ZH] “来劲了是吧？以前的话你也信？”陈浩宇瞧见苦荞，“苦荞来了，青瓜，赶紧看座。”
[EN] As a boy, Chen Haoyu had thought painting a woman's brows looked unmanly; today he saw pure genius. "Rule three's tough—need to scout what Shuzhen actually needs daily, then train like mad!"
[ZH] 青瓜不满道：“公子从来没主动让我坐过，苦荞姐姐也是下人，您为何给她让座？”
[EN] "Seeing you pour heart into Shuzhen makes me proud," Chen Jianglin smiled. "Good girl underneath that fiery temper—the day she softens toward you, you've won."
[ZH] “你能跟苦荞比吗？”陈浩宇说完，又笑脸相迎，“苦荞，你随便坐。”
[EN] Armed with a battle plan, Chen Haoyu bowed out—and spotted the shortcut walking right beside him. "Qinggua, sneak Kuqiao over to Boya Study—tell her I have urgent questions."
[ZH] 苦荞行礼道：“姑爷不必跟我一个下人客气，您有什么事尽管问，只要是对我家小姐好，我知无不言。”
[EN] Returning to Moxiang Courtyard, Chen Haoyu waited in Boya Study while Qinggua crept up to Suxin Thatched Hall's doorway peering inside for Kuqiao—until Kuqiao tapped his shoulder from behind. "Creeping like a burglar, Qinggua?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇又开始批评青瓜：“你瞧瞧，同样是下人，人家多会说话；再瞧瞧你，一张嘴不是揭你家公子我的短儿，就是嫌弃你家公子我，以后好好跟人家学学。”
[EN] "Looking for you without interrupting Young Madam! Young Master needs you—just took wife-wooing lessons from Eldest Master and needs insider intel!"
[ZH] 青瓜一脸委屈，心想：“公子您也比不上少夫人，还有脸说我。”
[EN] Glad to back Young Master's efforts, Kuqiao smiled. "For his sincerity, I'll go!"
[ZH] 有更重要的事情，陈浩宇才懒得在青瓜身上多浪费时间。
[EN] Stepping into Boya Study, Kuqiao blinked to find Chen Haoyu voluntarily flipping through a poetry volume—baffling even Qinggua. "Actually enjoying books now, Young Master?"
[ZH] 他起身走到苦荞对面：“苦荞啊，你应该也看出来，我现在对你家小姐是一心一意，可她不领情。我刚想到一个好办法，你得帮帮我。”
[EN] Setting down *Collected Poems of Wang Youcheng*, Chen Haoyu smirked. "Uncultured oaf! Never heard 'Within books lie houses of gold and jade-like beauties'?"
[ZH] “姑爷请说，只要能帮到，我一定尽力。”
[EN] "Used to say 'Within books lies nothing but dog poop,' Young Master!"
[ZH] “很好！我想知道淑真每日最需要什么？”
[EN] "Asking for trouble dredging up ancient quotes?" Spotting Kuqiao, he beamed. "Pull up a chair for Kuqiao, Qinggua!"
[ZH] “最需要什么？”苦荞嘀咕着，一时想不起来。
[EN] "Never offered me a chair—she's a servant too!" Qinggua grumbled.
[ZH] “就是她每日要做的事情。”
[EN] "Comparing yourself to Kuqiao? Sit anywhere you please, Kuqiao!"
[ZH] 苦荞恍然大悟：“哦，原来是这个，我家小姐每日不是写诗作词，就是……”写话本的事肯定不能说，赶紧改口：“就是练拳法。”
[EN] "No need for ceremony, Young Master," Kuqiao curtsied. "Ask anything—if it helps Miss, I'll tell all."
[ZH] 陈浩宇被难住，诗词他是门外汉，刚刚拿《王右丞诗集》研究一会儿，看不出个子丑寅卯来；拳法更是一点儿不会，他会的那几下擒拿功夫在朱淑真面前不堪一击。再说现在学这两样也来不及。
[EN] "See how polite real servants talk?" Chen Haoyu jabbed at Qinggua before turning eager to Kuqiao. "You can see I'm dead serious about Shuzhen, yet she won't budge. Need your help: what does Shuzhen need most every single day?"
[ZH] “除了这两样，你家小姐还有没有每日必须要做的事，就是她不做会觉得少点儿什么，习惯成自然那种。”
[EN] "Need most...?" Kuqiao racked her brain.
[ZH] 苦荞又认真想想，问道：“喜欢喝我做的茶算不算？”
[EN] "Something she does every single day without fail."
[ZH] 总算问出个好学的，陈浩宇兴奋道：“算！怎么能不算？点茶的功夫本公子还是可以学的。你给我说说你家小姐多喜欢喝你做的茶？”
[EN] "Oh! Either writing poetry, or..." Catching herself before slipping huaben, she pivoted quick: "...practicing Qianli Fist!"
[ZH] “其实，谈不上有多喜欢，应该是习惯了，每日起来都要品上一盏。有一日，我忙，没来得及给小姐做茶，她总觉得少些什么，很不自在。后来我给她做了一盏茶，她才安心许多。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu wilted—poetry baffled him completely, and his puny grappling wouldn't survive one flick of her wrist! "Besides those two! Some small daily habit that throws her whole day off if she misses it?"
[ZH] “太好了！本公子就学做茶，让她离不开我，嘿嘿嘿……”陈浩宇乐够后，叮嘱道，“今日我问你的事，先不要告诉你家小姐。”
[EN] Kuqiao thought hard. "Drinking my whisked tea?"
[ZH] 苦荞答应道：“姑爷放心，我定会替您保密。”
[EN] Jackpot! Chen Haoyu sprang up beaming. "YES! Tea-whisking I can learn! How hooked is she on your tea?"
[ZH] “很好，去忙吧，本公子也要忙正事。”
[EN] "Not obsessed, just deep habit—needs a bowl first thing every morning. Once I got swamped and missed making it, and she stayed fidgety and out of sorts until I finally whisked her a bowl."
[ZH] 要学点茶的功夫，还需拜师学艺，陈浩宇想起永泰茶楼的女茶师，不如改日抽时间拜访，请她当自己的师傅。
[EN] "Brilliant! I'll master whisked tea till she can't live without me! Hehehe! Keep this secret from her for now, Kuqiao!"
[ZH] 请人家当师傅还需送上银两，自己的腰包比脸还干净，不如先向奶奶借些银子，等发了月钱再还给她。
[EN] "My lips are sealed, Young Master!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇到奶奶跟前将借银子的原委一说，夏老太太欣然答应，夸他懂得在媳妇身上花心思，将来必有出息，还说借给他的银子不用还，就当是给小两口关系升温投的本钱。
[EN] To master tea-whisking fast, he needed a real pro—the female tea master at Yongtai Teahouse! Needing tuition silver with empty pockets, he marched straight to Old Madam Xia and told her the honest reason; thrilled he was investing heart into his wife, Old Madam Xia handed over silver on the spot and waved off repayment, calling it seed capital for her future great-grandson!

#CH 244
[ZH] 陈浩宇变化之事不到一日时间，已传到老二和老三耳中。
[ZH] 白天老太太听完书，包氏和崔氏就在一旁，老太太和大嫂笑得合不拢嘴，只夸淑真好，还说浩宇变好是陈家之幸。
[ZH] 天黑后，陈江木回来，崔氏一直撅着嘴巴不说话。陈江木问原因，她才将白天发生的事说了。
[ZH] 陈江木笑道：“的确是好事，陈家又多一个好儿郎，生意上又多出一个帮手，是好事。”
[ZH] 崔氏依然噘着嘴：“我岂不是得把姓倒着写？”
[ZH] “什么意思？”
[ZH] “我曾心中不屑，认为朱家姑娘根本不可能把浩宇管教过来，还说她若把浩宇管教过来，我就把崔字倒着写。”
[ZH] “你当着他人的面说了？”
[ZH] “没有，我自个心里想的。”
[ZH] 陈江木轻松地笑道：“你不说，谁知道？何必自个跟自个较劲？快来帮我宽衣，累一天，也该好好休息。”
[ZH] 夫君这么一说，崔氏轻松许多，过去帮他宽衣：“吃一堑长一智，往后我不能再随便瞧不起别人。”
[ZH] “这就对了。”衣裳宽好后，陈江木坐在床边，“几天工夫就把浩宇管教过来，朱家姑娘还真有两下子。”
[ZH] “她会功夫，浩宇怕着呢。我算是开了眼，这女人也能把自个夫君管教好。”
[ZH] “是稀奇，也能理解。朱家姑娘嫁过两回，第一回闹到和离，第二回总不能再和离，传出去被人笑话，只能狠狠心，给自个管教出一个好夫君来。也是陈家男人好，骨子里懂得关心自家娘子，否则朱家姑娘再厉害，怎么可能这么快把浩宇教育好？”
[ZH] 崔氏把衣裳挂好，来到陈江木面前：“陈家男人是好，总有例外，浩东打他娘子，你又不是不知？今日我碰到安雅，瞧她脖子上又添新伤。”
[ZH] “浩东什么都好，唉，打媳妇的事做得太不地道，我们也不好说什么，毕竟是二哥家中的事。”
[ZH] 二人收拾完，熄灯睡去。
[ZH] 陈浩东还未想出新办法，浩宇竟然一百八十度大转弯，他难以置信。大晚上来到父母房中，还好他们并未休息。崔氏坐在妆台前，懒得卸妆；陈江树则坐在床边，耷拉着脑袋想事情。
[ZH] “爹，我听说浩宇明日要去绸缎庄跟秋管事学做生意，可是真事？”
[ZH] 崔氏先开口：“是真的，老太太和你大婶子白天都是这么说的。由此看来帮浩宇促成婚事这步棋走得不对。”
[ZH] 陈浩东自责道：“都怪我只想着浩宇被厉害的媳妇管住，我心里才舒畅，没有想到会是这么个结果。”
[ZH] 陈江树抬起头说：“浩宇去绸缎庄又能如何？你从小学做生意，还怕他赶上你？把心放在肚子里。”
[ZH] “他想赶上我，根本不可能。”陈浩东十分自信，很快忧虑道，“只是儿子没想到浩宇变化这么快？才两日功夫。该不会是被母老虎整得忍受不了，装出来给大伙瞧的？”
[ZH] 崔氏说：“有些道理，浩宇从小就会哄人。”
[ZH] 陈江树说：“我们在这里猜来猜去有何用？明日让浩东找浩宇聊聊，便知实情，也好采取下一步计划。”
[ZH] 陈浩东认可父亲所说，打算明日与浩宇一同去绸缎庄，路上将情况了解清楚。堂弟最大的优点就是对他这位堂兄十分信任，知无不言。
[ZH] 与父母商议好后，陈浩东刚回到自己的院子，丫鬟小翠走上前拜见：“姑爷，我家小姐有话对您讲。”
[EN] Within twenty-four hours, news of Chen Haoyu's sudden transformation reached Second and Third Brothers. At Old Madam Xia's courtyard after the storyteller left, the old woman and Yao Qingyan beamed nonstop praising Shuzhen and calling Haoyu's turnaround a blessing for the whole clan.
[ZH] 叶安雅第一次主动邀请，陈浩东稍有喜悦，快速来到叶安雅的屋中。对方本坐着，见他前来，立刻站起。
[EN] When Chen Jiangmu came home after dark, Madam Cui sat sulking with her lip thrust out until he pried out why.
[ZH] 陈浩东以为是迎接他，看到她冰冷的面色，心中的喜悦荡然无存：“有什么话要对我说？”
[EN] "Great news!" he laughed. "Another capable Chen son pitching into business!"
[ZH] “我已经想好，我们还是和离吧。”
[EN] "Means I have to write my surname upside down!" she grumbled.
[ZH] 此话如同晴天惊雷，陈浩东怎么都没想到她竟然会提出此等要求。他靠近叶安雅，抓起她的衣领：“你这不下蛋的母鸡，我没休了你都不错，竟然想和离？没门儿，我绝不会答应。”
[EN] "Huh?"
[ZH] 叶安雅闭上眼睛，眼角流下两滴泪：“那你休了我。”
[EN] "Swore inside my head that if the Zhu girl reformed Haoyu, I'd write 'Cui' upside down!"
[ZH] 她竟主动要求被休，陈浩东甚是怀疑：“你近期跟浩宇的娘子走得近，她和离过一次，该不会是受她影响？才敢对我提出这等要求？”
[EN] "Say it out loud?"
[ZH] “是我跟你的事，与他人无关。”叶安雅已下定决心，就是死也要走上一条不归之路。
[EN] "No—just in my head!"
[ZH] 陈浩东推开叶安雅：“我暂时不想休你，等想时再说。”
[EN] "Nobody heard—why stress yourself?" he chuckled. "Help me change; long day."
[ZH] 叶安雅坚定地问：“我怎么做你才愿意休了我？”
[EN] Relaxing as she unfastened his robe, she sighed, "Lesson learned—never underestimate folks again. Turned Haoyu around in days! That girl's got real tricks."
[ZH] 对方冷漠的话语将陈浩东本有的热情全部浇灭，他靠近她：“你最好乖乖的，暂时不要提及此事，配合我当个好娘子，等到明年陈家产业的接替人定下后，再来谈休不休你的事，说不定我一高兴答应跟你和离也不一定。”
[EN] "Her fists scare him straight—never dreamed a wife could whip a husband into shape!"
[ZH] “此话当真？”
[EN] "Makes sense though," Chen Jiangmu mused sitting on the bed. "Divorced once, can't afford a second divorce, so she rolled up her sleeves and forged herself a decent husband. Plus Chen men treat their wives right in their bones, or even she couldn't have turned him this fast."
[ZH] 叶安雅面露喜色，反而令陈浩东不爽：“你就这么想离开我？”
[EN] Hanging his robe, Madam Cui countered softly, "Most Chen men—not Haodong! Passed Anya today and saw fresh marks on her neck."
[ZH] “我无时无刻不想着离开你。”
[EN] "Pity—Haodong shines everywhere else, yet beating his wife is shameful. Still, Second Brother's branch; not our place to meddle." Snuffing the lamp, they turned in.
[ZH] 此话像一根刺，刺进陈浩东的内心。浩宇要去绸缎庄帮忙，他本心情不好，叶安雅又想离开他，一股怒气自然涌上心头，一把将叶安雅拽到床上，开始打起来。
[EN] Over in Chen Jiangshu's rooms, Chen Haodong burst in late at night reeling from the reversal. Madam Bao sat untouched at her dressing table while Chen Jiangshu brooded on the bedside.
[ZH] 小翠在外面听到声音，流泪道：“这日子什么时候是个头？”
[EN] "Father, is it true Haoyu reports to Steward Qiu at the silk house tomorrow?!"
[ZH] ……
[EN] "True enough," Madam Bao snapped. "Old Madam and Eldest Sister-in-law crowed over it all afternoon! Pushing Haoyu into that marriage backfired!"
[ZH] 第二天一大早，陈浩宇早早起来，准备去绸缎庄报到。
[EN] "My fault for getting greedy wanting him tortured by a fierce wife," Chen Haodong cursed himself.
[ZH] 经过素心草堂，见门关着，靠近后，隔着门，向里大声说：“娘子，我要去绸缎庄，头一日去，估计得适应一阵，我打算一整日待在那里，晚上才回来，你可不要太想我。”
[EN] Chen Jiangshu looked up calmly. "So he joins the silk house—you've traded since boyhood; scared he'll catch up? Relax!"
[ZH] 千里寻正坐在妆台前梳妆打扮，听到外面的声音，并未理会。
[EN] "Catch up? Never!" Chen Haodong scoffed, then frowned. "Just baffled how he flipped in two days! Think the tigress broke him so bad he's putting on an act for the elders?"
[ZH] 苦荞说：“姑爷挺懂事，走时还会说一声。”
[EN] "Could be—Haoyu's always been a smooth actor," Madam Bao agreed.
[ZH] 千里寻挑起一枚簪子边插入发髻边说：“他说他的，跟我有什么关系？你去告诉他，以后去哪里不必前来报备。”
[EN] "Quit guessing," Chen Jiangshu advised. "Ride with him to the silk house tomorrow and sound him out—he trusts you blind and spills everything. Then plan our next move."
[ZH] 苦荞来到门口，将门打开：“姑爷，小姐说了，以后您无论做什么都不必向她报备。”
[EN] Agreed, Chen Haodong returned to his own courtyard, where Xiaocui met him bowing. "Young Master, Miss asks to speak with you."
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“娘子这么说是体恤我，我更不能辜负娘子的体恤，应该时时报备，省得娘子担心我在外面拈花惹草，心中不安。她心中不安，我又怎能安心？”
[EN] Rarely summoned by his wife, a flicker of hope stirred as he hurried inside—only to freeze at Ye Anya's icy stare as she rose. "What do you want?"
[ZH] 苦荞被逗乐，掩嘴而笑。
[EN] "My mind is made up. Give me a mutual divorce."
[ZH] 千里寻来到门口，没好气地说：“别一口一个娘子，听着怪别扭，赶紧忙你的去，以后少来打扰老娘。”
[EN] Thunder from a clear sky! Lunging forward, he seized her collar. "Barren hen! Haven't cast you out yet and you dare demand mutual divorce?! Never!"
[ZH] 朱淑真刚刚装扮完，肤色更显娇嫩，陈浩宇看后喜形于色：“一早见到娘子美艳无比，真是如沐春风，我今儿忙一整天都不会觉得累。”
[EN] Two tears slipped down Ye Anya's closed lashes. "Then write a letter of repudiation and cast me out."
[ZH] 对方说话突然变得文绉绉起来，千里寻甚是不适：“再花言巧语不离开，小心老娘的拳头。”
[EN] Suspicion flared cold. "Hanging around Haoyu's divorced bride lately—did she put this poison in your head?!"
[ZH] “娘子别生气，这就离开。”
[EN] "This is between you and me—nobody else." Ready to burn every bridge, she stood unyielding.
[ZH] 话音刚落，千里寻将门“啪”地关上。
[EN] He shoved her back. "Not casting you out yet—wait till I feel like it!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇提醒自己：“不生气，不生气，我一点儿都不生气。”
[EN] "What does it take for you to cast me out?" she asked flat and steady.
[ZH] 青瓜跟上公子往外走，边走边问：“公子，少夫人根本不买您的账，您真的不生气？”
[EN] Her frozen tone snuffed every spark of warmth inside him. Stepping close, he hissed, "Play the spotless, obedient wife and keep your mouth shut until next year when the clan names the Chen heir! Once I claim the estate, maybe I'll be generous and grant you a mutual divorce."
[ZH] “她是我娘子，我跟她置什么气？再说陈家男人何时会生自己娘子的气，你瞧瞧我爹，他就是我学习的榜样。”
[EN] "Swear it?"
[ZH] 青瓜不以为然：“总有例外，我听说大公子经常打大少夫人。”
[EN] The flash of joy on her face stabbed straight through his ego. "That desperate to get away from me?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇站住：“从哪里听说的？我怎么不知道？”
[EN] "Every waking breath I want out."
[ZH] “公子喜欢逛荡在外，什么时候关心过家里的事？不知道很正常。”
[EN] Already raw over Haoyu joining the silk house, that knife-twist broke his last restraint. Yanking her onto the bed, he struck her over and over while outside the closed door Xiaocui wept silently: When will this hell end?
[ZH] “若真如此，我可得好好说说东哥。”
[EN] At daybreak, Chen Haoyu rose bright and early for his first day at the silk house. Stopping outside Suxin Thatched Hall's shut door, he called loud through the wood: "Off to the silk house, Wife! First day might run long—won't be back till dark! Don't miss me too much!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇来到陈府大门外，正好碰见东哥。他站在马车旁，像是等人。
[EN] Doing her hair at the mirror, Qianli Xun ignored him.
[ZH] 陈浩东瞧见堂弟出来，装出笑脸：“浩宇，听说你今日要去绸缎庄，我实在开心，专程等你。”
[EN] "Pretty thoughtful, saying goodbye before work," Kuqiao smiled.
[ZH] 陈浩宇兴冲冲迎上去：“东哥也要去绸缎庄？”
[EN] Sliding a hairpin home, Qianli Xun snapped, "His business, not mine! Go tell him to stop filing attendance reports at my door!"
[ZH] “最近常在那里忙。”
[EN] Cracking the door, Kuqiao relayed, "Miss says no need to check in whenever you come or go, Young Master."
[ZH] “太好了，能跟东哥一起去再好不过。”
[EN] "Just her being considerate—all the more reason I must check in so she never worries I'm chasing skirts outside! When her heart's at ease, mine is too!"
[ZH] 两人上了一辆马车坐好，青瓜负责赶车。
[EN] Kuqiao hid a giggle as Qianli Xun marched to the doorway scowling. "Quit saying 'Wife'—gives me the creeps! Beat it to work and quit bugging me!"
[ZH] 陈浩东用研究的眼神看着眼前这位曾经吊儿郎当的堂弟：“浩宇，你真的被弟妹管教好了？”
[EN] Seeing her freshly groomed and glowing, Chen Haoyu beamed. "Seeing my gorgeous wife at dawn feels like a spring breeze—won't get tired all day!"
[ZH] “哪里是她管教好？我本来就不错。”
[EN] "More cheesy poetry and you eat knuckles!"
[ZH] 陈浩东露出轻松的笑容，那神态，以及说话的语气，还是老样子：“你放心，要是你在绸缎庄待不住，尽管去玩儿，我会罩着你。”
[EN] "Easy, easy—leaving now!" Bang! The door slammed right in his face.
[ZH] “谢东哥，打小你就照顾我，有你这样的大哥真是幸运。不过，我现在娶了娘子，不能再像以前一样吊儿郎当，该做些事情，否则怎么能配得上她。人家可是江南女词人，名气大着呢？我要是再逛荡下去，只怕外人说她嫁的夫君整日不务正业，会影响她的名声。”
[EN] Chanting silently—Not mad, not mad at all!—he strolled out with Qinggua trailing in amazement. "She slams the door in your face and you aren't even mad, Young Master?"
[ZH] 陈浩东紧张起来：“你真的打算改变？不是怕弟妹管教故意装出来的？”
[EN] "Mad at my own wife? Chen men never lose their tempers with their wives—look at my father!"
[ZH] “东哥也太瞧得起我，装能装得这么像？”陈浩宇凑近东哥，神秘地说，“东哥，你想都想不到，你弟弟我鬼使神差地喜欢上母老虎。第一次喜欢上一个人，感觉真不错，打算改变改变，让她也喜欢上我。嘿嘿……”
[EN] "Exceptions exist," Qinggua muttered. "Word is Eldest Young Master beats Eldest Young Madam nonstop."
[ZH] 由此看来之前的谋划起码有一半泡汤。
[EN] Chen Haoyu halted. "Where'd you hear that? News to me!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇一脸幸福的样子，令陈浩东嫉妒，凭什么一个浪荡子阴差阳错娶了喜欢的人，还乐在其中？而他陈浩东娶了叶安雅，心中对她有情，却高兴不起来，甚至动拳头打她。
[EN] "Always out partying—when did you ever notice home drama?"
[ZH] 陈浩东隐藏好情绪，试探地问：“浩宇，你确定喜欢上弟妹？我怎么听着这么不真实？该不会在东哥面前都不讲实话？”
[EN] "If that's true, I'm giving Brother Dong a talking-to!"
[ZH] “我对天发誓，是真的。”
[EN] Outside the main gates stood Chen Haodong waiting beside his carriage with a bright smile. "Thrilled you're joining the silk house, Haoyu—waited to ride over together!"
[ZH] 堂弟一脸真诚，陈浩东不得不相信。心想：“哼！喜欢上母老虎又能如何？想为她改变又能如何？你以前做下的事，我会帮你扒出来几件来，让你跟母老虎之间不得安生。”
[EN] "Awesome!" Climbing aboard with Qinggua driving, Chen Haoyu grinned while Chen Haodong studied him keenly.
[ZH] 陈浩宇想起青瓜说的话，小声问：“东哥，听说你打嫂子，可有此事？”
[EN] "Seriously let Sister-in-law whip you into shape?"
[ZH] 虽然在陈府已不是秘密，陈浩东还是不愿在堂弟面前承认：“没有的事，别听其他人瞎说。”
[EN] "Nobody whipped me—I was solid all along!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇想想也许真是下人胡乱传谣，像东哥这么优秀的人怎么可能打老婆？便不再提及此事。
[EN] Relaxing at that familiar cocky swagger, Chen Haodong tested him. "If the silk house gets dull, slip out and play—I'll cover for you."
[ZH] 千里寻用过早饭，继续写话本。故事中写的是一对儿痴男怨女因为各种原因无法走到一起，她不由想起自己，无法再写下去，将笔放下，吩咐苦荞收起手稿。
[EN] "Thanks, Brother Dong—always had my back since we were kids! Thing is, married now, can't bum around forever. She's the Poetess of Jiangnan—if I stay a deadbeat, folks will mock her for marrying a loser."
[ZH] 苦荞了解，小姐主动让收起手稿，说明她今日不会再写，便将手稿一一收好。
[EN] Tension crept back into Chen Haodong's gut. "Reforming for real? Not faking to dodge her fists?"
[ZH] 千里寻又吩咐苦荞将撒尿娃娃取来。
[EN] "Think I'm that good an actor?" Leaning in, Chen Haoyu grinned sheepish. "Never guess it in a million years, Brother Dong—I actually fell head-over-heels for the tigress! First time truly loving someone—feels amazing! Gotta level up so she falls for me too! Hehe!"
[ZH] 苦荞将手稿收拾完毕后，从架子上取下木盒，放在小姐面前。
[EN] Half his master plan just went up in flames! Envy bit bitter inside Chen Haodong—how did this airhead stumble into love with his own wife while he, who secretly wanted Ye Anya, ended up beating her in cold rage?! Masking his venom, he probed, "Swear you're not messing with me?"
[ZH] 她打开盒子，拿出撒尿娃娃仔细端详，回想着申简辰送礼物的场景，想着想着露出笑容，很快笑容又消失。
[EN] "Swear to Heaven—dead serious!"
[ZH] 尽管身边站着苦荞，院子里也有十个下人，陈府又这么多长辈，陈浩宇还时不时来烦她，千里寻的内心却产生出有强烈的孤独感。
[EN] Certain he meant every word, Chen Haodong iced over inside: Fall in love all you want—I'll dredge up enough skeletons from your dirty past to blow your marriage sky-high!
[ZH] 瞧瞧这屋子，本是婚房，她本应与爱人成双成对进进出出，却依然形单影只，怪只怪她嫁给自己不爱之人。
[EN] "Say, Brother Dong," Chen Haoyu lowered his voice, "heard a rumor you hit Sister-in-law Anya—true?"
[ZH] 在外人看来她肯定是不近人情的娘子，新婚之夜把夫君赶到后院。可谁让她不想委屈自己，再说凭什么要与一个她并不爱的人圆房？
[EN] "Pure servant gossip," Chen Haodong brushed it off smooth. "Don't listen to trash."
[ZH] 即便现在陈浩宇对她与以往不同，想让她喜欢上他，也是难上加难。没有爱的情况下与陈浩宇双双出入，她做不到，便只能委屈自己继续品味孤独。
[EN] Accepting it—how could polished Brother Dong beat his wife?—Chen Haoyu dropped it.
[ZH] 在同一个时代，与朱淑真一样，无法跟心爱之人在一起，竟然也产生与她同样孤独的情绪来。千里寻将撒尿娃娃放进木盒，展开纸，提笔写下一首词：
[EN] Back in Moxiang Courtyard after breakfast, Qianli Xun tried writing her huaben about star-crossed lovers, only for her own sorrow to choke the ink dry. Setting down her brush, she had Kuqiao stow the pages and fetch the wooden box with the peeing clay doll.
[ZH] 独行独坐，独唱独酬还独卧。伫立伤神，无奈轻寒著摸人。
[EN] Studying the little figurine, memories of Shen Jianchen brought a soft smile that quickly died. Surrounded by maids, guards, warm elders, and Chen Haoyu hovering daily, a raw chill of loneliness still hollowed her out. This room was a bridal chamber meant for a loving pair, yet she sat solo because she refused to betray her heart by sharing a bed with a man she didn't love. Even watching Chen Haoyu transform, loving him felt miles away.
[ZH] ﻿此情谁见，泪洗残妆无一半。愁病相仍，剔尽寒灯梦不成。
[EN] Trapped in the same century, tasting Zhu Shuzhen's exact ache, she tucked the doll back in its box, unrolled fresh paper, and penned a ci lyric:
[ZH] 《减字木兰花·春怨》
[EN] "Alone I walk, alone I sit, / Alone I sing, alone I drink, alone I lie down. / Standing idle, spirit bruised, / Helpless as a light chill touches my skin. / Who sees this feeling? / Tears wash away more than half my fading rouge. / Sorrow and sickness follow close— / Snuffing the cold lamp's wick, no dream will come." —"Jianzi Mulan Hua: Spring Resentment" [TN: Translator's rendering]

#CH 245
[ZH] 写完朱淑真的词，千里寻从头到尾品味一番。很明显，朱淑真的悲戚之情与她相比更甚。从词中看得出，她应该是生病了。人生病时，往往最脆弱，需要有人相伴左右，可身边没有爱人能给予她些许温暖，唯有泪相伴。
[ZH] 千里寻想起穿越前一次生病的经历。
[ZH] 千里寻一年到头很少生病，一旦感冒，比别人严重许多，除了鼻塞流鼻涕咳嗽以外，发起烧来浑身难受。
[ZH] 有一段时日，千里拳庄的徒弟们接下商演，倾巢出动，去外地拍戏，要整整一个月才能回来，她正好清闲下来，一个人住在二十二层，宅在家里写小说。
[ZH] 谁知重感冒袭来，身上肌肉酸痛，无力再写小说，便收了笔记本，拿出体温计，夹在胳肢窝下，倒在床上。
[ZH] 估摸着时间差不多时，将体温计拿出瞧了瞧，三十八度六，果然发烧。
[ZH] 她撑着起来，想将体温计中的水银甩下去，都觉无力，只能将体温计放在床边的柜子上，打开抽屉，还有一盒降温药，可杯子和水没在旁边。后悔没留下一位女弟子，否则此时一个电话便有人前来照料。
[ZH] 没办法，只能自己动手。她努力爬起，全身的肌肉更加酸痛，脑袋晕晕乎乎，平常练习拳法积攒的功力在疾病面前根本没用，走出两步，便倒在地上。她的意识非常清晰，只是浑身无力，干脆趴在地上休息一会儿。
[ZH] 电话铃声响起，本以为是哪位徒弟打来，不如趁机叫回一个。手机放在床边的柜子上，她努力爬到柜子跟前，却看到申简辰的名字。
[ZH] 她太需要人照顾，抓住一个是一个，伸出手将绿色的通话键划向一边，再点开免提。
[ZH] “喂，千里寻，听说你的弟子们都去了外地，你一个人太无聊，不如我去陪你？”
[ZH] “我……我生病了，你……来吧，密码是……我生日。”
[ZH] “啊，你病了，等着，我马上到。”
[ZH] 那边挂掉电话后，千里寻心里变得坦然许多，第一次觉出申简辰的好来。她放心地坐在地上，趴在床边休息，谁让她连起来的力气都没有。
[ZH] 等千里寻睁开眼睛时，她已躺在床上，身上盖着被子，额头上贴着物理降温贴，一股饭香扑鼻而来。
[ZH] 仔细瞧去，申简辰身着围裙，双手捧着一碗面出现在她面前。
[ZH] “醒了，正好做了西红柿鸡蛋面，最适合生病的人吃。”申简辰说着，把碗放在床边的矮柜上，他则挨床边坐下，“我第一次见人感冒这么严重，多亏有我在。”
[ZH] “谢谢你，抽屉里有药，你帮我倒杯水，我吃完药就好。”
[ZH] “我已看过，抽屉里的药全部过期，我重新买了药，已帮你服下。”
[ZH] “什么？我已吃过药？怎么一点儿印象都没有？”
[ZH] “我来时，你坐在地上，趴在床边，昏昏沉沉，被人卖了都不知道。是我把你抱起来放到床上，发现你的药全部过期，便跑到楼下的药店买来新药。你虽昏昏沉沉倒挺配合，让张嘴就张嘴，我顺利帮你把药吃下。”
[ZH] 申简辰取掉退烧贴，用手摸摸她的额头：“烧退了一些，快坐起来，把面吃了，一会儿还要吃药，总不能空着肚子。”
[EN] Reading back over Zhu Shuzhen's lyric, Qianli Xun felt the deeper abyss beneath the original poetess's grief—clearly ill on top of lonely, crying through cold nights without one warm hand beside her.
[ZH] 身上已没有之前难受，千里寻坐起来，在床头靠好。她忽然觉得申简辰很不错，如果她想恋爱想结婚可以找他这样的。
[EN] A memory from her own previous life surfaced sharp and clear. Rarely sick, whenever a heavy flu caught Qianli Xun, it floored her completely—fever, chills, aching bones. One month all her disciples at Qianli Fist Manor had gone out of town for a film stunt gig, leaving her solo in her twenty-second-floor apartment writing web fiction.
[ZH] 申简辰将碗递到她面前：“怎么不接着？难道想让我喂你吃？我倒是想，怕你不乐意。”
[EN] A brutal fever hit overnight. Too weak to type, she slipped a thermometer under her arm and collapsed onto the mattress—thirty-eight point six degrees. Too shaky even to shake the mercury down, she spotted fever pills in her nightstand drawer with zero water nearby, cursing herself for not keeping one female student behind. Pushing herself off the bed for water, martial arts strength useless against a virus, she took two dizzy steps and crumpled onto the floorboards—mind clear, limbs lead.
[ZH] 千里寻接过碗：“老娘自己会吃，哪需要你喂？”
[EN] Her phone rang on the nightstand. Crawling over hoping it was a student, she saw Shen Jianchen's name glowing on the screen. Desperate for any help, she swiped the green answer button and tapped speaker.
[ZH] ……
[EN] "Hey, Qianli Xun! Heard your whole squad left town—bored solo? Want company?"
[ZH] 正沉浸在回忆中，陈文心进来，身后跟着丫鬟月桂。
[EN] "I'm... sick... come over... door code's my birthday..."
[ZH] “淑真，快别待在书桌前，我好不容易来一趟，陪我聊聊天儿。”
[EN] "Whoa—hang tight, I'm on my way!"
[ZH] 陈文心的声音将千里寻从回忆拉回现实，自从她嫁过来后，嫂嫂还是第一次来。
[EN] The line clicked dead, and an odd rush of safety washed over her—the very first time she realized Shen Jianchen was someone she could lean on. Resting her cheek against the bedframe, she drifted out.
[ZH] 千里寻迅速整理好心情，起身迎上去：“嫂嫂许久没来，淑真甚是想念。”
[EN] When she opened her eyes, she lay tucked under her quilt with a cooling patch on her forehead, rich tomato-and-egg aroma drifting in as Shen Jianchen walked up in an apron carrying a steaming bowl of noodles.
[ZH] 陈文心将屋子瞧上一遍：“我一进院子就觉出不同来，没想到屋子的布置也跟以前大不相同，难怪浩宇这么快被你改变过来。”
[EN] "Awake! Whipped up tomato-egg noodles—best thing for a fever!" Setting the bowl on the nightstand, he perched beside her. "Never saw anyone catch a cold this hard—lucky I called!"
[ZH] 听此话，嫂嫂应该见过长辈。
[EN] "Thanks... grab me water for the pills in the drawer?"
[ZH] “莱宝呢？怎么没一起带来？”
[EN] "Checked already—every box in there was expired! Ran down to the pharmacy and got fresh medicine—already gave you a dose."
[ZH] “在老太太院子里玩儿，有长辈们和丫鬟陪着，不缺我一个，正好抽空来看看你。没想到你嫁到陈家没多久，陈家的气象与从前大不相同。以前长辈们很少去奶奶的院子，现在倒好，我娘还有两位婶婶都聚集在那里，奶奶更是满脸堆笑。她们都告诉我是你的功劳。你倒好，反而一个人待在屋子里，冷冷清清的。”
[EN] "I took medicine? Don't remember a thing!"
[ZH] “奶奶有人陪，不缺我一个，我也好趁此机会写诗作词，省得辜负了女词人的美称。”
[EN] "Found you slumped on the floor half out of it—anyone could've kidnapped you! Carried you to bed, grabbed fresh meds downstairs, and you opened right up like a good patient!" Peeling off the patch, he tested her brow with his palm. "Fever's coming down. Sit up and eat before your next dose—can't take pills on an empty stomach."
[ZH] “又写新词？”陈文心好奇地走到书桌前将淑真写的词仔细瞧一遍，批评道，“怎么还写这么悲悲戚戚的词？好像陈家慢待你似的？”
[EN] Feeling strength return as she propped herself against the headboard, a quiet thought crossed her mind: Guy's actually decent—if I ever decide to date or marry, someone like him wouldn't be bad.
[ZH] 又走过去拉起淑真的手：“让我瞧瞧，脸色白里透红，眉头也挺舒展，精神着呢？为何要写‘愁病’二字？依我看你就是无病呻 吟。”
[EN] "Not taking the bowl?" he teased. "Want me to spoon-feed you? Gladly, just scared you'll deck me!"
[ZH] “无病呻 吟”四个字将千里寻逗乐，扑哧笑出声来：“随便写写，就当我是无病呻 吟。嫂嫂别站着，坐下说话。”
[EN] Snatching the bowl, she snapped, "I've got two hands—feed yourself!"
[ZH] “整日不是躺着就是坐着，多没趣，我们到园子里走走，大好春光，可不要辜负。”
[EN] "Shuzhen! Quit hiding at your desk—finally got free to visit, come chat!"
[ZH] 千里寻点头同意。
[EN] Chen Wenxin's cheerful voice yanked Qianli Xun back to Song-dynasty Lin'an, walking in with Yuegui—her sister-in-law's first call since the wedding.
[ZH] 二人来到南边的园子，这里的园子多种些花树，有开满白色小花的流苏树，有满树红艳艳的木棉树，还有黄花风铃木和娇艳玉兰花。
[EN] Brushing off her nostalgia, Qianli Xun rose warmly. "Missed you, Sister-in-law!"
[ZH] 千里寻一边嗅着花香，一边听嫂嫂说话。苦荞与月桂跟在后面聊着下人们之间的事。
[EN] Scanning the room, Chen Wenxin marveled, "Whole courtyard feels fresh, and this room looks totally transformed! No wonder Haoyu shaped up overnight!"
[ZH] 她们穿过小径，洒满从树上掉落下来的花瓣，即使不忍心踩，也不得不踩，满地都是，若不去踩，哪来的落脚之处？千里寻猛然想起陆游的两句词：“零落成泥碾作尘，只有香如故。”尽管是写梅花，此时倒也应景。
[EN] "Where's Laibao?"
[ZH] 花开花落，不就是人生的写照。花开时，美艳无比；花落后，被人踩在脚下，化作尘土，就像人死后亦入土为安，成为尘土的一部分。她忽然觉得女娲娘娘用泥土造人的神话并非神话，也许人真的由泥土而来，最终回归泥土。
[EN] "Playing in Grandmother's yard with plenty of elders and maids watching, so I slipped over to see you. Hard to believe how much Chen Manor brightened since you arrived—Mother and both aunts all gathering around a beaming Grandmother, everyone crediting you, while you hide solo in here!"
[ZH] 花瓣化为泥土前，在世间留下一缕清香，她千里寻在短暂的人生中又会留下什么？她有幸经历两段人生，尽管到现在都无法分清哪一段是真，哪一段是假？可却有清清楚楚的记忆。
[EN] "Grandmother's got plenty of company—gives me quiet to write verses and earn my poetess title."
[ZH] 穿越前的她留下被大家诟病的网络小说和一群练过千里拳的弟子，足以印证她来过世间一趟；穿越后，大家只知她是朱淑真，她更要努力让千里寻在这个时代留下些什么，哪怕只像脚下踩的花瓣留下一段瞬间即逝的清香，也会让她觉得没有白来一遭。
[EN] "New lyric?" Stepping to the desk, Chen Wenxin read the fresh sheet and chided, "Why still writing mournful stuff like the Chens mistreat you?" Catching Shuzhen's hand, she eyed her rosy cheeks. "Glowing skin, smooth brow, bursting with health—writing 'sorrow and sickness'? Pure melodrama!"
[ZH] “淑真，谢谢你把浩宇管教好。”
[EN] "Pure melodrama" cracked Qianli Xun up. "Just scribbling—call it melodrama! Sit down!"
[ZH] 嫂嫂感谢的话打断千里寻关于人生的思索：“这两日长辈们感谢得够多，嫂嫂又来，我怕自己承受不起，说不定过上几日大家都会失望。”
[EN] "Sitting indoors wastes gorgeous spring weather—let's stroll the garden!"
[ZH] 陈文心不解：“什么意思？”
[EN] Agreeing, they wandered into the south flower garden past white fringe trees, blazing crimson kapoks, golden trumpet blossoms, and creamy magnolias while Kuqiao and Yuegui trailed gossiping behind.
[ZH] “只怕陈浩宇坚持不了几日。”这种打击人的话千里寻不好在长辈面前说，面对嫂嫂倒可直言不讳。
[EN] The path lay carpeted in fallen petals impossible to avoid stepping on, bringing Lu You's famous line to mind: "Fallen into mud and ground to dust, only its fragrance remains." [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 陈文心笑道：“我的亲弟弟我最了解，他一旦变好，便是真的好，不会轻易改变。”
[EN] Petals blooming bright, crushed to earth yet leaving scent behind—human life mirrored in miniature. She had lived two vivid lives—modern web novelist and martial master leaving books and disciples behind; Song poetess borrowing Zhu Shuzhen's name. Surely Qianli Xun herself could leave one bright trace of fragrance in this century too.
[ZH] 嫂嫂对自己的弟弟毫无道理的信任，千里寻懒得再批判，还是让时间检验一切。她对韦嬷嬷的判断深信不疑，也相信陈浩宇是变好，但还是认为陈浩宇坚持不了多久，毕竟不良习气早已养成，想要彻底改变没那么容易。
[EN] "Thank you for reforming Haoyu, Shuzhen," Chen Wenxin broke her reverie.
[ZH] 陈文心接着说：“浩宇现在变好，你要试着接纳他，跟他好好过日子。我嫁给你哥时，也谈不上喜欢，现在我们不是过得挺好？所以，不要把感情看得太重，人好是关键，感情婚后也可以慢慢培养。”
[EN] "Too many thank-yous already—scared everyone will end up disappointed in a few days."
[ZH] 千里寻算是听出来，嫂嫂是替长辈来劝说她，不如给她个拖延时间的答复：“嫂嫂也说人好是关键，陈浩宇好不好仍需时间检验，等到我觉得他好时，再谈要不要接纳他。”
[EN] "How so?"
[ZH] 陈文心高兴道：“有你这句话就行。”
[EN] "Bet Chen Haoyu doesn't last a week," she said bluntly—safe to speak candidly with Wenxin.
[ZH] 拖延时间的答复果然奏效，嫂嫂不再提及刚才的话题，与她沉默着欣赏周围的风景，又踩踏一会儿花瓣，才问道：“你知道我今日回陈府所为何事？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin laughed. "Nobody knows my kid brother better than me—once he truly flips, he stays flipped!"
[ZH] “嫂嫂不说，我怎么知道？”
[EN] Leaving time to prove who was right, Qianli Xun held her tongue as Chen Wenxin added, "Since he's turning good, open your heart to him. I wasn't madly in love with your brother on our wedding day either, yet look how happy we are! Good character comes first; love grows after marriage."
[ZH] “公爹派我来的？”
[EN] Recognizing a gentle lobby from the elders, Qianli Xun gave a neat stall: "Character takes time to prove. Once I see he truly holds steady, we'll talk."
[ZH] “我爹？嫂嫂回娘家怎么还需我爹派遣？”
[EN] "Fair enough!" Satisfied, Chen Wenxin dropped it, crunching petals a moment before asking, "Know why I really came home today?"
[ZH] “临安又要举行大的宴会，邀请陈家参加，公爹派我回来转达他的意思，当然，我也顺便回娘家一趟。”
[EN] "Tell me."
[ZH] “这倒奇怪，往常举办宴会发帖子即可，这次倒让嫂嫂亲自来说，宴会与往常有何不同？”
[EN] "Father-in-law sent me."
[ZH] “难道你没听说？”
[EN] "My father sent you to visit your own maiden home?"
[ZH] “听说什么？”
[EN] "Big banquet coming up in Lin'an—Chen family's invited, so Father-in-law sent me personally to relay his message while visiting home."
[ZH] “仪王和佳和郡主从汴梁回来了。”
[EN] "Usually an invitation card does the trick—what's so special about this banquet?"
[ZH] 终于回来，千里寻既高兴又忧虑，不知该如何回应，半晌才问出：“他们何时回来的？”
[EN] "Haven't heard?"
[ZH] “今日一早刚到，两人走时十分低调，回来仍旧低调，多亏公爹多了个心眼儿，早早给幽林别居的李管家打过招呼，贵人一到，李管家便差人送来信儿，公爹才知他们回来。一早带领临安官员去看望贵人，明日便举办宴会，一来为贵人接风洗尘；二来算是给两位贵人在临安补办婚宴。”
[EN] "Heard what?"
[ZH] “补办婚宴。”千里寻心中嘀咕着，他们再次回到临安，已是夫妻，以后见到梦容恐怕得称呼仪王妃。
[EN] "Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe just got back from Bianliang!"
[ZH] “即使是为仪王补办婚宴，只要差人送来请帖，陈家也不敢怠慢，真想不通我爹为何如此谨慎？还让嫂嫂来传话？”
[EN] Back already! Joy and dread tangled instant in Qianli Xun's chest before she managed, "When did they arrive?"
[ZH] 陈文心小心提醒：“你认真想想便知。”
[EN] "First thing this morning—slipped in quiet as they left! Lucky Father-in-law thought ahead and tipped off Butler Li at Youlin Retreat to send word the minute they arrived. Led all the Lin'an officials to pay respects at dawn, and tomorrow they're throwing a grand banquet—half welcome-home, half makeup wedding feast!"
[ZH] 千里寻先疑惑片刻，便立刻明白，后悔不该追问嫂嫂前来传话之事。
[EN] Makeup wedding feast—married for real now. From today on, Mengrong was Princess Consort Yi. "Even for a royal wedding feast, why send you in person instead of a card?"
[ZH] 她成婚前申简辰到朱府阻挠婚事，朱晞颜冒着丢掉乌纱帽的危险带领家人下跪阻止；成亲当日申简辰又抢亲，梦容还因此受伤，陈家担心贵人怪罪，肯定无不小心。朱陈两家必然更加重视这场宴会。
[EN] "Think a second and you'll know," Chen Wenxin hinted gently.
[ZH] 陈文心接着说：“举办宴会所有的花销均由陈家出。”
[EN] Realization stung instant: Prince Yi barging into Zhu Manor before the wedding forcing Prefect Zhu to kneel, then raiding the bridal procession leaving Mengrong wounded—both families walked on eggshells terrified of royal wrath!
[ZH] 千里寻这才觉出愧疚来，她惹下的事却让陈家买单，心中着实不安：“其实仪王人很好，他不会跟朱陈两家计较。再说那些事都是他一厢情愿为之，更怪不到两家的头上来。”
[EN] "Chen family's footing the entire banquet bill," Chen Wenxin added.
[ZH] “话虽如此，仪王毕竟是贵人，面子还是要给的。捶丸场时我就看出来，他对你有意思，仔细想来，陈家可是与仪王抢人，而你却嫁到陈家，仪王肯定面子上挂不住。我爹和公爹倍加小心，仪王刚走时就开始差人筹备宴会，只等人一回来就举办，他们不过是希望仪王不要因为没有得到你而怪罪两家。”
[EN] Guilt pricked sharp. "Prince Yi isn't petty—he wouldn't punish either family, especially since he acted on his own!"
[ZH] 千里寻担心宴会也邀请自己，她还没有做好面对梦容的准备：“不知宴会都邀请哪些人参加？”
[EN] "Royal pride still matters," Chen Wenxin explained. "Even back at the chuiwan match I saw he had eyes for you—technically the Chens snatched the bride right out from under a prince! Father and Father-in-law started prepping this feast the day Prince Yi left just to smooth things over and keep both families safe."
[ZH] “临安的官员和有身份之人，只准携带正室参加，我是借了你哥哥的光，才能有幸参加，而你与浩宇根本不在被邀请之列。哦，有一个例外，乔家除乔老爷和夫人外还邀请了乔文仙，他会带苗知瑜参加。”
[EN] Dreading an invite before she felt ready to face Mengrong, Qianli Xun asked carefully, "Who's on the guest list?"
[ZH] 千里寻在内心长舒一口气，还好她不必参加。尽管见梦容是迟早的事，但不是现在。
[EN] "Lin'an officials and dignitaries with primary wives only—I'm only going on your brother's coattails. You and Haoyu aren't even invited. One exception: besides Master and Madam Qiao, Qiao Wenxian's invited with Miao Zhiyu."
[ZH] 千里寻又埋怨自己为何如此小心谨慎？她没有做过任何对不起梦容之事。可接着她又反问自己时不时想起申简辰，难道就对得起梦容？
[EN] Relief washed through Qianli Xun—spared for now. Meeting Mengrong was inevitable eventually, just not tomorrow. Yet guilt jabbed back: I did nothing wrong to Mengrong—wait, silently missing Shen Jianchen every other day, is that really clean?

#CH 246
[ZH] 汴梁的婚礼还算盛大，乔梦容很满意，总算嫁给哥哥。尽管哥哥心里有别人，官家的圣旨压着，他也不得不与她成亲。
[ZH] 只是洞房那晚，哥哥并未揭开她的盖头，只留下一些话：“梦容，我可以代替赵朴照顾你娶你，可我没有办法代替他与你洞房，只因我一直把你当妹妹看待。”
[ZH] 哥哥离开婚房，留下乔梦容一个人，她流下伤心的泪水，越伤心，越恨朱淑真，是她抢走哥哥的心。
[ZH] 成亲第二天，哥哥着急回临安。乔梦容也着急，她急着实施报复计划，朱淑真夺走哥哥的心，她要让她付出代价。
[ZH] 想要报复，身边得有人帮衬，一个喜兰不够，还需带个有主意的老人儿，邹嬷嬷成为最佳人选。
[ZH] 乔梦容将哥哥在临安的做法告诉邹嬷嬷，又说了自己的想法，邹嬷嬷满口应下。
[ZH] “京城的仪王府自会有人照看，我愿跟随郡主去临安，倒要瞧瞧，是什么样的人偷走仪王的心，搞得他连洞房都不愿入。”
[ZH] 一路颠簸，终于抵达临安回到幽林别居。舟车劳顿，乔梦容需休息，她要养精蓄锐，更要认真想想如何开始报复昔日的闺中密友。
[ZH] 申简辰当然清楚他的做法会伤害梦容，可要两眼一闭入洞房，他会觉得身心不自在，也会认为自己是个畜生。
[ZH] 也许有朝一日他会慢慢接纳梦容，让她变成真正的仪王妃。但不是现在，况且他答应过千里寻，要为她守身如玉。
[ZH] 想到“守身如玉”四个字，申简辰觉得自己既伟大又可笑。
[ZH] 世间有几个男子能像他一样娶了一位如花似玉的妻子，却遵循内心的感情不受情欲控制？在这个昏君奸臣当道即将没落的时代，对于无法施展才华的男儿来讲，守住做人的底线便是伟大。
[ZH] 也许千里寻根本没有将这四个字放在心上，申简辰的执着，又显得可笑。他随缘连婚都结了，还要为另一个女子守住真心，说出去恐怕无人能信。
[ZH] 从汴梁回临安的途中，申简辰一直在想，他必须找些事情做。一来避免日日在幽林别居对着梦容，让他既尴尬又愧疚；二来有事情做，不至于老想着千里寻，不去想，说不定会渐渐放下她。只要放下，他面对梦容就不会既尴尬又愧疚，然后心甘情愿接纳她，将她由妹妹变成他的妻子。
[ZH] 刚回幽林别居没多久，朱晞颜带领几个官员前来拜见。
[ZH] “殿下舟车劳顿，本不该前来打扰，怎奈殿下回汴梁时，我等未能相送，因此一得到殿下回来的消息，便赶过来请罪。”
[ZH] 申简辰心想，既知他舟车劳顿，又何必急匆匆前来打扰？可因有事相求，他并未过分介意。
[ZH] “知府大人严重，是我不愿麻烦大家，临安官员何罪之有？”
[ZH] 朱晞颜与身后的官员腰弯得更低。
[ZH] “感谢仪王体恤，临安官员特在明日设宴，既为殿下接风洗尘，又为您与王妃在临安举办一场婚宴，恳请殿下与王妃明日屈尊光临。”
[ZH] 申简辰从心底瞧不上昏君影响下的奢靡之风：“汴梁的婚宴已经够盛大，回临安再举办一次，得花不少民脂民膏，还是取消吧。”
[ZH] 吓得朱晞颜与几位官员立刻跪在地上。
[ZH] “下官一心只想着仪王成亲时，因路途遥远临安官员无法参加婚宴，您回来总得补上，其他的没想那么多。从您离开时就已着手筹备婚宴，临安官员对仪王的心思可见一斑。殿下放心，用的不是民脂民膏，所有花销均由陈家承担。”
[ZH] 申简辰立刻明白，抢亲一事，把陈家给吓住，想利用宴会买个心安。既然不是民脂民膏，为了让陈家安心，也希望陈家不要因为此事埋怨千里寻，便欣然答应：“既如此，明日我会与仪王妃按时参加。都起来吧，不必跪着。”
[ZH] 朱晞颜与诸位官员从地上爬起：“感谢仪王。”
[ZH] 申简辰有事相问，这么多人在场甚是不便：“其他人都退下，我有事与知府大人说。”
[ZH] 其他官员走出屋子，在院中耐心等候。朱晞颜一个人留下，不敢说话，弯腰恭敬站立，等待仪王发话。
[ZH] 申简辰说：“自从我担任临安节度使以来，从未参与过临安政务，总觉得愧对官家信任……”
[ZH] 朱晞颜心中不由紧张：“难道仪王想要参与临安政务？他若参与只怕会发现很多问题，到时我恐怕不得安生。”
[ZH] 申简辰瞧向朱晞颜，他一脸紧张，就知有问题。又怎能没问题？朝廷从上到下外面看着光鲜艳丽，里面却千疮百孔，他申简辰即使是个织补能手，也不可能扭转历史。在这样的时代不必考虑为朝廷服务，还是做些对老百姓有利之事。
[ZH] “临安的政务由知府大人负责，我不便多参与，军政上的事，也无需我多费心，我只想做些与百姓打交道之事，知府大人帮忙想想哪个职位更适合我？”
[ZH] 朱晞颜松口气，只要不参与政务什么都好说：“殿下爱护百姓之心，令下官钦佩，职位倒是有，只是历来与百姓直接接触的官职都十分低微，与仪王的身份不符，恐委屈了殿下。”
[ZH] “说来听听。”
[ZH] “有司理参军一职，掌管百姓中案件调查和审讯一事，官职从八品，甚是低微，恐怕仪王瞧不上。”
[ZH] 还别说，此官职甚合申简辰心意。
[ZH] “从八品的官职知府大人还是有权调遣，司理参军一职本王看上了，辛苦知府大人将之前的司理参军调走，找个合适的职位安置，以后本王就是临安城的司理参军。”
[ZH] 仪王欣然接受，朱晞颜自然开心：“下官今日就回府衙办理此事。只是临安节度使的身份是官家所封，下官不敢冒着掉脑袋的危险改变殿下的官职，不如就说节度使代表官家体恤百姓，体察民情，暂代司理参军一职。”
[EN] The Bianliang wedding had been grand enough to satisfy Qiao Mengrong—at last she had married her brother, imperial decree sealing the bond even with his heart elsewhere.
[ZH] 申简辰心中不满，他干司理参军的事儿，跟昏君有何关系？这些个地方官员真是时时不忘巴结昏君。
[EN] Yet on their wedding night, he never lifted her bridal veil, leaving only cold words before walking out: "Mengrong, I can step into Zhao Pu's shoes to protect you and marry you, yet I cannot share your bed as him—because to me you have always been my little sister."
[ZH] 心中如此想，嘴上却不能如此说:“甚好，明日宴会后，本王便去任职。”
[EN] Alone in the red-draped bridal chamber, her tears burned straight into venom against Zhu Shuzhen—the thief who stole her brother's heart.
[ZH] 朱晞颜与官员离开后，白风终于忍不住提出要求：“王爷，我好久没见苦荞，想去看看她，可不可以现在就去？”
[EN] The very next morning, he rushed to depart for Lin'an, and Qiao Mengrong hurried just as fast, burning to launch her revenge. Needing a sharp ally beyond Xilan, she recruited Matron Zou—who agreed on the spot: "Staff can mind the capital manor; I'm coming to Lin'an to see what kind of vixen bewitched His Highness into abandoning his own bridal chamber!"
[ZH] “去吧。”白风提到苦荞，申简辰想到心中之人，“你见到苦荞帮我问问她的情况。”
[EN] Battered from days on the road, Qiao Mengrong arrived back at Youlin Retreat ready to rest, sharpen her claws, and plot payback against her former confidante.
[ZH] “好的。”白风答应着，也替郡主感到难过。王爷都成亲，心里还想着别人。幽栖居士虽好，郡主也不差，王爷为何要那么执着？
[EN] Shen Jianchen knew full well his coldness gutted Mengrong, yet closing his eyes and claiming her without love would make him feel like a beast. Maybe someday time would heal him into accepting her as a real wife—just not now, especially after promising Qianli Xun he would stay chaste for her.
[ZH] 白风又设身处地想想，他喜欢苦荞，如若不能跟苦荞在一起，偏偏不得已娶一位不喜欢的姑娘，说不定也会像王爷一样，也就理解了王爷。还是尽快去看苦荞，顺便打听幽栖居士的情况。
[EN] "Staying chaste"—part noble, part absurd. In a rotting dynasty run by a foolish emperor and corrupt ministers where honest men stood powerless, clinging to personal honor felt noble; yet marrying one woman under fate while keeping his heart locked to another married woman sounded downright ridiculous.
[ZH] 白风走后，申简辰想到往后会经常待在衙门，担心梦容胡思乱想，不如前去说明。
[EN] All the road back from Bianliang, one resolution grew clear: he needed work. Staying cooped up at Youlin Retreat facing Mengrong drowned him in guilt, and idle hours kept Qianli Xun looping through his skull. Burying himself in real work might dull the longing—and once he let Qianli Xun go, he could finally look at Mengrong as his wife without shame.
[ZH] 乔梦容睡过一会儿，起身来到院中将闺房小院四处瞧瞧，不由黯然伤神，都已成亲，哥哥还让她住在这里。幽林别居的屋子不少，她要寻一处作为她的婚房。
[EN] Barely settled back at Youlin Retreat, Zhu Xiyan arrived leading a delegation of Lin'an officials. "Forgive us disturbing Your Highness after a tiring journey! Having missed seeing you off to Bianliang, we hurried over at once to beg pardon."
[ZH] 想来想去还是哥哥住的屋子最合适，她现在已是仪王妃，有权利把哥哥的屋子布置成婚房。
[EN] Annoyed internally—if you knew I was tired, why barge in?—Shen Jianchen kept his voice smooth since he had a favor of his own in mind. "No fault of yours, Prefect Zhu; I left quietly to spare everyone hassle."
[ZH] 不如现在就去瞧瞧哥哥的屋子，想想怎么布置会更好。
[EN] Bowing lower, Zhu Xiyan presented their proposal: "The officials of Lin'an have arranged a feast tomorrow to welcome Your Highness home and celebrate your wedding with Princess Consort Yi. We beg you both to honor us with your presence."
[ZH] 刚要抬脚，哥哥走进院门。
[EN] Sick of the wasteful luxury modeled by his imperial father's court, Shen Jianchen frowned. "Bianliang's banquet was lavish enough—no need bleeding taxpayers dry for a second feast. Cancel it."
[ZH] 乔梦容忍不住上前抱住哥哥：“哥哥，我不想继续住在小院儿，我想住你的屋子，你我已成亲，不能再像以前一样。”
[EN] Instantly Zhu Xiyan and his entourage dropped to their knees. "We merely regretted missing Your Highness's capital wedding and began preparations the day you left! Not one copper comes from public funds—the Chen family is sponsoring every expense!"
[ZH] 申简辰既不愿同意，又不能拒绝，只能言其他：“邹嬷嬷可安顿好？”
[EN] Catching on—the wedding raid had terrified the Chens into buying peace, and refusing might leave them taking it out on Qianli Xun—Shen Jianchen relented. "Very well. Princess Consort Yi and I will attend tomorrow. Rise."
[ZH] 乔梦容继续抱着哥哥：“已安顿在我旁边的院子。”
[EN] "Thank you, Your Highness!"
[ZH] “梦容，哥哥有事跟你说。”
[EN] Waving the rest out to wait in the courtyard, Shen Jianchen spoke privately with Zhu Xiyan. "Since taking office as Military Commissioner of Lin'an, I've barely touched city affairs and feel remiss toward His Majesty's trust..."
[ZH] “哥哥请讲。”
[EN] Sweat prickled Zhu Xiyan's neck: If Prince Yi starts auditing Lin'an administration, half my skeletons spill out!
[ZH] “临安官员为你我准备了婚宴，明日你随我一同参加。”
[EN] Spotting his panic—no shock in a dynasty rotten to the roots—Shen Jianchen had zero illusions about saving the empire; helping ordinary folk beat fighting hopeless court rot. "Keep managing prefectural politics, Prefect Zhu; military affairs need little of me too. I want a hands-on post dealing directly with commoners—what office fits?"
[ZH] 梦容将哥哥抱得更紧：“我当然要跟随哥哥一同参加，他们如此懂事，我们需给足面子。”
[EN] Exhaling in huge relief, Zhu Xiyan replied, "Your compassion for the people humbles me, Your Highness! There is a post—Judicial Adjutant of the Judicial Office, handling civilian criminal investigations and trials. Only rank eight-B, far too lowly for a prince..."
[ZH] 乔梦容心想，这次宴会举办得甚好，肯定会有许多临安权贵的家眷参加，她正好借此机会笼络人心，为报复朱淑真做好准备。
[EN] Perfect! "Rank eight-B falls under your transfer authority, Prefect Zhu. Move the current adjutant to a decent post—I'm taking over as Lin'an's Judicial Adjutant."
[ZH] “还有一件事，从明日起，我会以节度使的身份代替司理参军处理公务，会经常待在衙门，恐怕陪你的时间不多……”
[EN] Thrilled, Zhu Xiyan bowed. "I'll arrange it today! Only, since His Majesty named you Military Commissioner, altering your title risks my head—we'll announce that Military Commissioner Yi acts concurrently as Judicial Adjutant on His Majesty's behalf to care for the people."
[ZH] 哥哥显然是躲避她，乔梦容不由自主放开哥哥，双目微红：“哥哥就这么不待见我？想方设法远离我？”
[EN] Credit the incompetent emperor for my work? Classic bureaucrats! Swallowing his disgust, Shen Jianchen nodded. "Fine. I report for duty right after tomorrow's feast."
[ZH] “梦容……我……，你我成亲太过匆忙，给我些时日，待我慢慢适应后，也许会……”
[EN] Once the officials left, Bai Feng couldn't hold back: "Your Highness, haven't seen Kuqiao in ages—may I go visit her now?"
[ZH] “你心里还是放不下她，你越放不下她，我就越恨她。”
[EN] "Go," Shen Jianchen granted—then added quietly, "Ask Kuqiao how she's doing."
[ZH] 乔梦容发泄完，觉得自己太过情绪外露，担心哥哥窥探到她心中所想，便努力控制好情绪。她已是仪王妃，要好好想想如何利用好这个身份，既能得到哥哥的心，也能报复该报复的人。
[EN] "Yes, Your Highness." Feeling a twinge for Princess Jiahe—still pining after a married woman right after his own wedding—Bai Feng put himself in the prince's boots: forced to marry someone other than Kuqiao, he'd probably act the same! Off he rushed to see his girl.
[ZH] “哥哥想怎么做就怎么做，大不了我搬到衙门，哥哥住哪里，我就住哪里。”
[EN] Needing to explain his new post before Mengrong drew wild conclusions, Shen Jianchen walked over to her courtyard.
[ZH] “衙门的环境比不上幽林别居，你住那里不合适，哥哥答应你，每周会回来看望你。”
[EN] Waking from her nap, Qiao Mengrong stood gazing bitterly around her maiden pavilion: wedded wife now, yet still parked in a side wing! She had every right ashow could they give me a hard time?"
[ZH] 要报复朱淑真，若日日与哥哥在一起，必然被他发现，他肯定会大加阻拦。与哥哥在一起不急于一时半刻，以后有的是机会。
[EN] Old Madam Xia laughed and chided him. "Talking nonsense again. Did you forget why your father-in-law forced you to marry the Zhu girl?"
[ZH] “我听哥哥的。”
[EN] "A real man doesn't rehash old humiliations! Don't bring that up, Grandmother, or if that tigress overhears, your grandson will be in deep trouble."
[ZH] 梦容听话又温柔，倒让申简辰生出许多愧疚来，他上前抱住梦容。
[EN] Wanting a fat handout, Chen Haoyu pivoted fast to butter her up. "Grandmother, I pray every day for your long life and good health. Today at Zhu Manor, I hunted down their Old Ancestor's longevity secret. Want to hear it?"
[ZH] “梦容，你别怪哥哥，也别恨幽栖居士，她已嫁给陈浩宇，我与她已不可能，只怪我是起死回生后的赵朴，心里总装着一个人，等我把她彻底放下，会努力接受你。”
[EN] Knowing full well her grandson was spinning tall tales, the old lady played along. "Of course I do."
[ZH] 乔梦容享受着哥哥的怀抱，却对哥哥说的话甚为不满，心中想道：“你越不让我恨她，我就越恨她，是她夺走哥哥的心，以至于哥哥要接受我还需努力。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu leaned in with mock mystery. "I circled the Zhu family's Buddhist hall for ages and heard nothing but wooden fish clacking and chanting. I figured their Old Ancestor lived this long from knocking a wooden fish and reciting sutras every day. How about I buy you a wooden fish and a string of prayer beads tomorrow so you can give it a try?"
[ZH] “请哥哥答应我件事。”
[EN] "Save it. I'm not cut out for chanting sutras. Just drop by every day to sit with me and keep me company. Keep me happy, and longevity will take care of itself."
[ZH] 申简辰放开乔梦容：“什么事？”
[EN] "Deal—I'll come keep you company more often." Casting about for a way to sic his grandmother on Zhu Shuzhen, he put on his best pitiful face. "Grandmother, you have to help me."
[ZH] “我想把哥哥的屋子布置成婚房。”
[EN] "Help you with what?"
[ZH] 申简辰沉默一会儿，心想他时常待在衙门，一周回来一次，梦容想怎样就怎样，只要她高兴。
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen is bullying me."
[ZH] “想怎么布置随你。”
[EN] "How?"
[ZH] 乔梦容终于露出笑容。
[EN] "She drove off my maids, stole my courtyard, seized all my property, and doles out a measly twenty taels a month! I'm practically a pauper now. Nobody outside even believes I'm a Chen."
[ZH] 离开闺房小院后，申简辰本想去神柳下。经过木质高台，好久未登上，不如上去坐坐，在高处体验一番神柳的风韵。
[EN] Old Madam Xia chuckled. "That bride of yours has a razor-sharp tongue. Even I can't out-talk her. Besides, who told you to kneel and beg her to marry you, and sign some prenuptial contract? Blame yourself for gambling with Master Jiang and nearly losing the whole Chen estate."
[ZH] 申简辰沿着台阶登上高台，先站着望了一会儿神柳，能清晰地看到上面挂满红绸，不知他离开后，千里寻有没有去看过神柳，然后坐在树下想起自己。
[EN] Chen Haoyu blinked in shock. "How do you know about that?"
[ZH] 反正他现在心里全是她，什么时候能放下，他无从得知。他听法师的话选择随缘，可开心不起来，只因随缘的人并不是他所爱。
[EN] "Your new wife told me. Your parents know too."
[ZH] 他在木桌旁坐下，闭上眼睛，体会着神柳的气息从远处飘来，一边体会，一边回想着穿越而来与千里寻经历过的一切……
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen! You didn't leave me a scrap of face in front of my own family! Just you wait! Chen Haoyu seethed inside while keeping his voice coaxing. "Gambling was my mistake, Grandmother, and I swear I'll never touch dice again. Still, you have to put Zhu Shuzhen in her place. She's walking all over my head now."
[ZH] 哥哥走后，乔梦容让喜兰把邹嬷嬷叫来，两人坐下聊几句家常话，才说起正事。
[EN] "How should I put her in her place?"
[ZH] “明日临安官员给我和哥哥举办婚宴，嬷嬷也随我一同去。”
[EN] "Find excuses to give her a hard time whenever you can."
[ZH] “王妃邀请，我自然跟着。”
[EN] "She's your wife now. Giving her a hard time is giving you a hard time."
[ZH] “嬷嬷今日再帮我拿个主意。”
[EN] "Grandmother, we're husband and wife in name only."
[ZH] “王妃请说。”
[EN] A shadow crossed the old lady's face. "My grandson waited so long to take a wife, and this is how you two live. Breaks my heart just thinking about it."
[ZH] “我想给临安官员的家眷准备些礼物，既不能太贵重，也不能有失身份，还能令她们高兴，以后以我马首是瞻。想来想去，不知送什么礼物好，想听听嬷嬷的意思。”
[EN] "Don't be sad, Grandmother," Chen Haoyu wheedled. "Sooner or later I'll marry someone who pleases you."
[ZH] “以王妃的身份想让临安贵妇听您的再容易不过，送什么礼物她们都会觉得面上有光。既然王妃想仔细斟酌，依我看，您与王爷成亲时，官家送过一箱珍珠，个个像鸽子蛋那么大，这东西对王妃来讲不算贵重，但对临安的贵妇来说，肯定是稀有之物，不如就送它。”
[EN] The truth slipped right out of Old Madam Xia. "I'm already plenty pleased with the wife you have."
[ZH] “还是嬷嬷的主意好，那些个珍珠放在我身边也是累赘，不如就送她们，既精致又不失身份。”
[EN] Seeing his scheme to weaponize his grandmother flop, Chen Haoyu went straight for the silver. "Grandmother, Zhu Shuzhen won't give me spending money, and I dare not ask Father or Mother. Could you spare me a little silver?"
[ZH] “好，我这就吩咐下人到集市上买些小巧的锦盒回来，一个个装好，明日带到宴会上，替王妃送了。”
[EN] She never pinched pennies with her favorite grandson. Motioning a maid to bring a wooden box, she drew out a five-hundred-tael silver note. "Spend it wise. Not a copper on courtesans or gamblers, hear me?"
[ZH] 乔梦容甚是满意，认为将邹嬷嬷带来实在是明智之举，往后幽林别居的事务不用她过分操心，邹嬷嬷自会处理，省去她不少心思。
[EN] Chen Haoyu snatched it up in delight and showered her cheeks with kisses until she laughed from ear to ear. After a few more minutes of banter, he tucked the note into his sleeve, dashed out of the manor, and swept Scarface and his crew back out onto the town.

#CH 247
[ZH] 千里寻继续与嫂嫂走着聊着，踩踏落满地面的花瓣。
[ZH] 有一位陈府的丫鬟前来报告：“大小姐，二少夫人，门外有人找苦荞，说是仪王身边的白侍卫。”
[ZH] 苦荞一直和月桂跟在后面聊天儿，根本没仔细听两位主人说些什么，并不知仪王已从汴梁回来。此时听丫鬟一说，兴奋不已：“小姐……”
[ZH] 千里寻自然不会阻拦，再说她也想从白风口中得知一些关于仪王与郡主之事，苦荞了解她的心思，必然会打听一二。
[ZH] “去吧，这里不需要你伺候。”
[ZH] “谢谢小姐！”苦荞兴奋地跑开。
[ZH] 陈文心笑着批评道：“这丫头，听到白侍卫来，那个高兴劲儿，心里都没了主人。”
[ZH] “嫂嫂，我逛累了，想去陪陪奶奶，这个点儿说话艺人该来，我们顺便去听听说书。”
[ZH] 千里寻并非真的累，身边只有嫂嫂一人说话，不够热闹，搞得她时不时想起申简辰。老太太的院子里人多，热闹起来应该不会总想起他。
[ZH] 陈文心很是乐意：“听说把说话艺人请到家里来的点子还是你出的，竟然想出一招包月包年的主意来；我还听说，那位说话艺人现在被好几家包月包年来着，他每日轮轴转，这家跑完去那家，比他在勾栏瓦肆挣得多多了。都是咱们陈家带的好头，现在竟然在富贵人家蔚然成风。”
[ZH] 千里寻开玩笑道：“看来我得让说话艺人给我分成，要不是我他哪有那么多银子赚？”
[ZH] 陈文心也以开玩笑的语气起哄：“我看行！”
[ZH] 两人笑了一回，沿原路返回。
[ZH] 到达夏老太太的院子时，说话艺人已开讲，莱宝被丫鬟带出去玩儿，没有小孩子打扰，陈文心可安心听书，她牵着淑真的手找地方坐下。
[ZH] 都是自己写的故事，千里寻对情节再熟悉不过，可从说话艺人口中说出，别具一番滋味。她津津有味地听着，暂时忘记常常想起的人。
[ZH] 听完书，又与家人坐在一处聊一会儿，热闹的家常话的确可以暂时让她忘记去想申简辰。只是心生疑惑，为何不见大嫂叶安雅？
[ZH] 瞅准时机，千里寻问道：“二婶，为何没见大嫂？”
[ZH] 包氏的脸上立即没了笑容：“她向来不喜欢热闹，只爱窝在自己屋里。”
[ZH] 崔氏心想：“谁不知道安雅只有被浩东打了才不敢见人。”
[ZH] 夏老太太说：“你和江树要好好劝劝浩东，别让他动不动打媳妇，男人打媳妇能有什么出息？往后陈家的家业需要他们这些后辈承担，要懂得修身齐家，第一要务是对自家女人好。女人是男人的财库，整日把自己的财库惹得哭哭啼啼，浑身是伤，身心不安，哪能赚到大钱？”
[ZH] 包氏心中并不完全认同老太太所言，嘴上还需乖些：“母亲说的是，儿媳记下。”
[ZH] “你记下没用，重要的是浩东要记在心里。”
[ZH] “等他回来，我会和他爹一起好好教育。”
[ZH] 女人是男人财库的说法，千里寻头一次听说，既新奇，又被深深折服。
[ZH] 她想到公爹陈江林，自从她嫁到陈家，很少见他像今日这样不在婆婆姚轻琰身边，给人的感觉他像是个居家男人，从未去忙过生意上的事，但陈家的生意却没因他陪伴妻子和家人而减少。
[ZH] 千里寻相信其中一定有奥妙，只是她一时体会不出而已。
[ZH] 作为儿媳她竟然也羡慕起婆婆来，陈浩宇的身影破天荒地出现在脑海中，如果他也像公爹对待婆婆那样对待她，她会不会有朝一日心甘情愿跟他老老实实在陈家过日子？
[ZH] 想着想着，觉得自己十分可笑，竟然会蹦出这种想法。陈浩宇做过的坏事还历历在目，尤其是逛青楼，想着都恶心，又怎会心甘情愿与他举案齐眉？
[ZH] 老太太累了，大家尽数散去，嫂嫂也带着莱宝回朱府。
[ZH] 千里寻独自一人回墨香苑，当她走进素心草堂时，苦荞已经回来，坐在茶桌前做茶。
[ZH] 千里寻来到茶桌旁，在垫子上席地而坐，仔细看苦荞做茶，想着怎么问出想问的。
[ZH] “小姐回来了？”苦荞面露喜色，刚见完白风，她内心欢喜。
[EN] Still strolling the petal-strewn garden with Wenxin, Qianli Xun paused as a Chen maid hurried up. "Eldest Miss, Second Young Madam—Guard Bai from Prince Yi's household is at the gate asking for Kuqiao."
[ZH] 千里寻轻声回应：“嗯，你见过白风？他胖了还是瘦了？”
[EN] Chatting behind with Yuegui, Kuqiao had missed the news of Prince Yi's return and gasped bright-eyed: "Miss...!"
[ZH] 小姐何时关心过白风？苦荞猜到小姐想打听仪王：“白风说，仪王还是老样子，唯一不同的是，已与郡主成亲。”
[EN] Eager herself to pick up scraps of news about Prince Yi and Mengrong, Qianli Xun waved her off. "Go on—I don't need you here."
[ZH] 苦荞将做好的茶递到小姐面前：“茶做好了，您品品。”
[EN] "Thank you, Miss!" Kuqiao sprinted away.
[ZH] 千里寻接过茶盏，品完一口：“我问白风，你为何要对我说仪王？”
[EN] "Look at that girl—one mention of Guard Bai and her mistress vanishes from her head!" Wenxin teased.
[ZH] 苦荞心想：“小姐在我面前又何必装？”
[EN] "My feet are tired, Sister-in-law—let's go sit at Grandmother's and catch the storyteller." Not really tired, just craving a noisy crowd to drown out Shen Jianchen's name.
[ZH] 千里寻继续问：“白风离开这么久，难道连件礼物都没给你带？”
[EN] "Heard hiring the storyteller by the month was your brainstorm!" Wenxin laughed. "Now half the rich houses in Lin'an hire him on retainer—sprints from manor to manor earning ten times what he made at Goulan Wasi! Chen Manor started a whole city trend!"
[ZH] 苦荞心想：“白风倒是给我买了礼物，可并未带来仪王的礼物，我说了小姐会不会伤心？还是照实说吧，毕竟小姐和仪王都已成亲，他们之间已不适合再互相赠送礼物。”
[EN] "Guess I ought to charge him a commission!"
[ZH] “带了。”
[EN] "Second that!"
[ZH] “什么礼物？”
[EN] Arriving at Old Madam Xia's courtyard just as the gavel cracked—with maids taking Laibao off to play—the sisters-in-law settled in. Listening to her own plot performed live drowned out every stray thought until the tale ended and family chatter resumed, where Qianli Xun noticed Ye Anya missing once again.
[ZH] “汴梁的木版画。”
[EN] Finding an opening, she asked, "Second Aunt, where's First Sister-in-law?"
[ZH] “能拿来让我瞧瞧吗？”
[EN] Madam Bao's smile died. "Never likes crowds—prefers hiding in her room."
[ZH] 苦荞起身出了素心草堂，从悦宁屋取来木版画，递到小姐面前：“您瞧，就是这个。”
[EN] Madam Cui smirked inside: Everyone knows Anya only hides when Haodong's left fresh bruises!
[ZH] 千里寻已品完茶，放下茶盏，接过木版画，仔细瞧，上面刻着一个影像，栩栩如生，看一眼便知是苦荞。
[EN] Old Madam Xia turned stern on Madam Bao. "You and Jiangshu lecture Haodong—hitting a wife brings zero honor! Leading the Chen estate starts with putting your own house in order, and that starts with cherishing your wife. A wife is a man's treasure vault! Keep your vault bruised, weeping, and terrified, and how do you expect big fortune to stick?"
[ZH] “白风真是有心。”
[EN] Unconvinced yet obedient, Madam Bao murmured, "Yes, Mother. We'll talk to him tonight."
[ZH] 千里寻竟然羡慕起苦荞，她能毫无顾忌去见想见的人，也能大胆接受对方的礼物，还能毫无顾忌拿给别人看。而她与他都已步入各自的婚姻，想要在一起看上去已不可能。
[EN] "Not your ears—his heart needs to hear it!"
[ZH] 苦荞突然跪在地上，千里寻诧异地问：“你跪下做什么？”
[EN] A wife is a man's treasure vault—Qianli Xun marveled at the phrase, picturing Chen Jianglin hovering devoted around Yao Qingyan while Chen wealth still swelled nonstop. A wild fleeting thought flickered—what if Chen Haoyu treated her like that? She snorted at herself at once; brothel-hopping slime turning into a dream husband? No thanks!
[ZH] “小姐，呜呜……”
[EN] Once Old Madam Xia tired and Wenxin headed home with Laibao, Qianli Xun returned alone to Suxin Thatched Hall, where Kuqiao sat whisking tea with a glowing face.
[ZH] “刚见过白风应该高兴，怎么哭上了？”
[EN] Sitting on the floor cushion beside the tea table, Qianli Xun tested the waters casual. "Saw Bai Feng? Put on weight or lost some?"
[ZH] “我替小姐难过，呜呜……”
[EN] Seeing right through the detour, Kuqiao handed over a bowl of tea. "Bai Feng says Prince Yi looks the same—only difference is he's wed to the princess now."
[ZH] “替我难过什么？我高兴着呢。”千里寻放下木版画，“别跪着，起来，坐下跟我说话。”
[EN] Taking a sip, Qianli Xun arched a brow. "Asked about Bai Feng—why bring up Prince Yi?"
[ZH] 苦荞擦完眼泪，在茶桌旁坐好：“我担心以前在林家的日子会重来一遍。”
[EN] Keep pretending! Kuqiao thought.
[ZH] 千里寻当然明白苦荞的意思，慨叹人生总是走不出怪圈，好不容易离开林觉，却再次嫁给不喜欢的人。
[EN] "Gone all that time—didn't Bai Feng bring you a souvenir?"
[ZH] “怎么会？我又不是以前我的。”
[EN] Hesitating—Bai Feng had brought a gift for her, nothing from Prince Yi for Miss, though both were married now anyway—Kuqiao admitted, "Yes—a Bianliang woodblock print."
[ZH] 苦荞这才大胆说：“小姐，您忘了仪王吧，你们都已成亲，不能再想着他。陈家不错，我盼着您跟姑爷好起来。”
[EN] "Let's see!"
[ZH] 千里寻这才发现，她已走在孤军奋战的道路上。
[EN] Fetching it from Yuening Room, Kuqiao handed over a crisp carved portrait catching her own likeness to the life.
[ZH] 奶奶、公爹和婆婆希望如此；朱家人更不必说；韦嬷嬷虽未明确表态，千里寻能敏锐地感觉出她的心意；墨香苑的下人们包括苦荞也都如此。
[EN] "Thoughtful guy," Qianli Xun murmured, struck by quiet envy—Kuqiao could love open and proud while she and Shen Jianchen stayed locked behind separate wedding vows.
[ZH] 千里寻觉得实在不公，凭什么陈浩宇一时变好，大家都向着他？凭什么她努力守住的贞洁要给了她怎么都瞧不上的人？
[EN] Suddenly Kuqiao dropped straight to her knees, sobbing. "Miss... waaah!"
[ZH] “咸吃萝卜淡操心，我的事你以后少管。”
[EN] "Just saw your sweetheart—what's with the tears?!"
[ZH] “我是下人，哪有资格管小姐的事？可小姐对我好，我也希望您好，才壮起胆子说这些话。您不顾及别的，也该顾及郡主，她肯定不希望别人的心里也装着仪王。”
[EN] "Heartbroken for you, Miss! Waaah!"
[ZH] 这话还真管用，小姐的表情凝重，缓缓起身，撂下一句话：“把撒尿娃娃收起来，放在我看不到的地方，以后我不会再去碰它。”
[EN] "Save the tears—I'm thriving! Get up and sit!"
[ZH] 苦荞答应着，又感到愧疚，不该浇灭小姐的最后一点儿念想。
[EN] Wiping her eyes, Kuqiao sat down. "Terrified your Lin Manor misery is repeating all over again!"
[ZH] ……
[EN] "Not a chance—I'm not who I was back then."
[ZH] 话说陈浩宇一早来到绸缎庄，与东哥下了马车，秋管事迎上来：“二位公子齐聚绸缎庄，真是少见。”
[EN] Gathering her nerve, Kuqiao spoke straight. "Let Prince Yi go, Miss! You're both married—stop holding onto him. Chen Manor is wonderful; I'm praying you and Young Master work out!"
[ZH] 陈浩东轻车熟路，也顺便在堂弟面前展示他才是这里的主人：“西南的客商可来？”
[EN] Realization hit Qianli Xun: she was standing one against the world—Chen elders, Zhu family, Matron Wei, every maid down to Kuqiao all rooting for Chen Haoyu! Resentment flared: why did two days of decent behavior turn everybody into his fan club while she got pressured to hand herself to a man she didn't love?!
[ZH] 秋管事回道：“来了，正在会客房等大公子。”
[EN] "Meddling busybody—stay out of my heart!"
[ZH] 陈浩东面向堂弟：“浩宇，你跟着秋管事先了解了解绸缎庄的情况，我要前去与客商谈一单大生意。”
[EN] "I'm just a maid, Miss, yet I care about you too much to stay quiet! Even setting everything else aside, think of Princess Jiahe—what wife wants another woman holding her husband inside her heart?"
[ZH] 陈浩东假装关心的样子向秋管事叮嘱：“浩宇就交给秋管事，可不要辜负我大伯的期望，好好带。”
[EN] That arrow hit dead center. Qianli Xun's face turned grave as she stood up. "Pack that peeing clay doll out of my sight. I won't touch it again."
[ZH] 秋管事当然会将大老爷的话当回事：“大公子放心，我必然尽心。”
[EN] Nodding, Kuqiao felt a stab of guilt for snuffing out her mistress's last tiny keepsake.
[ZH] 陈浩东心想：“尽心又有何用？浩宇再怎么学也比不上我。哼！”
[EN] Meanwhile, Chen Haoyu had stepped down at the silk shop with Chen Haodong, greeted by Manager Qiu. Showing off his turf, Chen Haodong headed straight into the guest room for a big southwest merchant deal while tossing a smooth nod to Manager Qiu: "Take good care of Haoyu for Eldest Uncle!" (sneering inside: learn all you want, still zerohow could they give me a hard time?"
[ZH] 目送大公子离开，秋管事才对陈浩宇说：“二公子从没来过绸缎庄，我先带您到处转转，了解一下情况。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia gave him a laughing scold. "Talking nonsense again. Forgot why your father-in-law forced you to marry the Zhu girl in the first place?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇第一次来，对此十分陌生，确实需要熟悉环境：“有劳秋管事。”
[EN] "A real hero doesn't bring up past glories! Don't mention that again, Grandmother, or if that tigress hears it, your grandson will be in for a world of pain."
[ZH] “二公子这边请。”秋管事将陈浩宇带进绸缎庄最外面的铺子，“这里是售卖之处，绸缎庄生产的所有罗、绢、纱、丝、绵全部在此处售卖。”
[EN] To coax more silver out of her, he needed her in high spirits, so he switched tracks at once. "Grandmother, I always pray for your long life and good health. Today at Zhu Manor, I dug up the longevity secret of their Old Ancestor. Want to hear it?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇向里面瞧去，到处摆着各类丝织品，客人穿梭不断，伙计们十分忙碌，生意相当红火。
[EN] Certain he was just buttering her up, Old Madam Xia played along. "Of course I do."
[ZH] 秋掌柜又带陈浩宇往里走，穿过一道后门，出现一个宽敞的院落，中间有圆形的花坛，里面的花卉开得正艳，四周是一带长廊，长廊后便是一处又一处忙碌之地。
[EN] Chen Haoyu leaned in with an air of mystery. "I paced outside the Zhu Buddhist shrine for ages and heard nothing but wooden fish tapping and sutra chanting inside. That must be why their Old Ancestor has lived so long. How about I buy you a wooden fish and a string of prayer beads tomorrow so you can give it a try?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇跟着秋掌柜进入一间屋子，里面有许多织布机，每台织布机前坐着织娘，一个个正忙碌地来回蹬脚，穿着梭子。
[EN] "Save it. Your grandmother isn't cut out for chanting sutras. Just come visit me every day and keep me company. When I'm happy, good health and long life take care of themselves."
[ZH] “这里是织锦坊，前面售卖的所有织品均由这些个织娘一点一点织出来。”
[EN] "Deal, I'll spend more time with you from now on." Casting about for a way to sic his grandmother on Zhu Shuzhen, he put on his best pitiful face. "Grandmother, you have to help me."
[ZH] 陈浩宇好奇地问：“她们都是从哪里找来的？”
[EN] "Help you with what?"
[ZH] “都是从乡下或是城里一一筛选而来，要心灵手巧还得吃苦耐劳，选好后，经过半年训练才能真正上手。许多穷人家的姑娘都争着抢着来咱们绸缎庄当织娘，主要是陈家给的工钱不低，她们还能学到手艺，往后即使不在陈家干，自己做些小买卖也是好的。”
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen is bullying me."
[ZH] 接着又来到一处房屋，刚到门口，听到里面传来抚琴的声音。走进一瞧，陈浩宇才明白原来是刺绣之处，许多绣女坐在绣蹲上，在大的绣架上忙着穿针引线。一旁还有一女琴师，抚着轻柔的琴音。
[EN] "How is she bullying you?"
[ZH] 秋管事继续介绍：“这是刺绣坊，二公子请看，陈家绸缎庄最好的绣女全在这里，她们刺绣的技法在临安城首屈一指。”
[EN] "She kicked out my maids, seized my courtyard, hogged all my properties, and gives me only twenty taels of silver a month! I'm practically a pauper now. Nobody out on the street believes I'm a Chen."
[ZH] “也都要经过筛选训练才能上手吗？”
[EN] Old Madam Xia chuckled. "That bride of yours has a razor-sharp tongue. Even I can't talk her down. Besides, who told you to get on your knees and beg her to marry you, then sign some prenuptial contract? Blame yourself for gambling with Master Jiang and near losing the whole Chen estate."
[ZH] “那是自然，选绣女更需心灵手巧，对绣女的手要求极高，必须十指纤纤，还要灵活，否则复杂的针法根本使不出来。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu blinked in shock. "How did you know about that?"
[ZH] 秋管事介绍道：“刺绣的种类从针法上分平绣和钉线绣两大类，从原料是否用金又分为蹙金绣和彩丝绣两种。一般蹙金绣大多采用钉线绣法，彩丝绣采用平绣法。二公子请看……”
[EN] "Your new bride told me. Your father and mother know too."
[ZH] 陈浩宇走到一位忙碌的绣女旁边，仔细瞧去，她正在织物上用彩丝绣一只飞鸟。
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen! You didn't leave me a shred of face in front of my own family! Just you wait! he fumed inside. Aloud, he said, "Gambling was my fault, Grandmother. I'll never touch it again. Still, you have to teach Zhu Shuzhen a lesson for me. She's walking all over my head now."
[ZH] 秋管事继续介绍：“这位绣女用的是平绣法，平绣有齐针、缠针、套针、接针。再看那位秀女，她用的是钉线绣法，钉线绣有平金、圈金、圈银、拉金锁、铺绒、堆绫、贴绢等。”
[EN] "How should I teach her a lesson?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇听得晕晕乎乎，搞不明白，第一次见识了女子绣个花还有这么大学问。
[EN] "Find excuses to give her a hard time whenever you get the chance."
[ZH] 秋管事又带陈浩宇往后走，后面是大大的缫丝场。摆放有许多竹筐，里面堆满蚕茧，有煮蚕茧的大锅，周围的架起的竹竿上挂着缫好蚕丝。伙计们都忙碌着，时不时有人送蚕茧而来。
[EN] "She's your wife now. Giving her a hard time means giving you a hard time."
[ZH] 陈浩宇问：“秋管事，蚕茧都是从哪里来？”
[EN] "Grandmother, we're husband and wife in name only."
[ZH] 秋管事说：“除了陈家在城外有一庄子，专门养蚕结茧外，也会派人到乡下收质地好的蚕茧。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia's face clouded with sorrow. "My grandson gets married at last, only to live like this. Just thinking about it breaks my heart."
[ZH] “难道我们陈家还有自己的桑树林不成？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu rushed to cheer her up. "Don't be sad, Grandmother. Sooner or later I'll bring home a wife you approve of."
[ZH] “那是自然，城外的庄子里种了一大片桑树林，都用来养蚕。为了把蚕养好，结出好茧，大老爷专门请了会种植桑树的人，那片桑树养得极其茂盛，每年结的桑葚又大又好吃。主人家都不要，全让人摘下来分给绸缎庄上上下下的伙计和织娘绣女们，剩下的才会分给其他人。”
[EN] Her reply came straight from the heart. "I'm quite pleased with the wife you have right now."
[ZH] 秋管事说完，笑起来，仿佛已经吃到美味的桑葚。
[EN] With his scheme to set Grandmother on Zhu Shuzhen dead in the water, Chen Haoyu pivoted straight to silver. "Grandmother, Zhu Shuzhen won't give me spending money, and I don't dare ask Father or Mother. Could you spare me a little silver?"
[ZH] 绸缎庄的情况基本了解完后，秋管事将陈浩宇带到一间屋子。
[EN] Old Madam Xia never stinted on her grandson. She ordered a maid to fetch a wooden box and pulled out a five-hundred-tael silver note. "Spend it with care. Not a copper on courtesans or gamblers, you hear me?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇四处瞧瞧，布置得不错，不由问道：“秋管事，您打算给我安排什么事做？”
[EN] Overjoyed, Chen Haoyu snatched the note and planted kiss after kiss on her cheek until she beamed from ear to ear. After chatting a few minutes longer, he tucked the note into his sash, bolted out of the manor, and swept Scarface and his gang off for another round of carousing.

#CH 248
[ZH] 秋管事没有着急回答，走到桌前坐下，笑容立刻消失，一脸严肃：“大老爷既然把二公子交给我，您必须事事听我安排，没有出师前，哪怕是天王老子我也不会放在眼里。看到没？那个位置就是二公子的。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇瞅去，明显是个次位，桌子没有秋管事的大，椅子也不舒服。本以为秋管事的位置是他的，谁知却是如此安排。算了，不与他计较。这么想着，陈浩宇打算去桌前坐下。
[ZH] 秋管事立刻拦住：“二公子莫急，您要想坐到那个位置，还需历练一阵。”
[ZH] 青瓜也是第一次来绸缎庄，因好奇一直跟在公子身后陪他到处瞧，本以为接下来公子会在这屋子好好歇息，没想到屁股还未沾椅子，秋管事倒先坐下，还拿出威严来。心想：“公子肯定受不了，转身就走。”
[ZH] 谁知公子犹豫一会儿，脸上的确露出不爽的表情，可很快掩饰好，面向秋管事：“怎么历练？”
[ZH] 秋管事说：“二公子需先到前面当两个月的伙计，再到账房学习三个月，然后再处理三单大生意，合格后方可坐到那张桌前跟我学习做管事的本领。”
[ZH] “也就是说那个位置我现在还没有资格坐？”
[ZH] “二公子的领悟能力不错。”
[EN] Steward Qiu did not rush to answer. He walked over, took the main chair behind the desk, and let his smile vanish into stone-cold gravity. "Since Eldest Master placed Second Young Master in my charge, you will follow my orders to the letter. Until you finish your apprenticeship, I won't bow to the King of Heaven himself. See that spot over there? That seat is yours."
[ZH] “既如此，又何必把我领进来？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu glanced over. It was plainly a subordinate desk—smaller than Steward Qiu's, with a stiff, uncomfortable chair. He had assumed the steward's chair was meant for him, yet here was his setup. Fine, no point picking a fight over a chair. Pushing down his irritation, he started toward the desk to sit.
[ZH] “不将二公子领进来，您又如何能看到我作为管事的威严？”
[EN] Steward Qiu halted him on the spot. "Not so fast, Second Young Master. Before you earn the right to sit at that desk, you have some seasoning to go through."
[ZH] 陈浩宇多少感到后悔，早知如此麻烦，不该主动要求参与生意。想起自己的感情，想起家里的娘子，狠狠心，决定试一试：“东哥也是这么学的？”
[EN] Qinggua, visiting the silk emporium for the first time, had tagged along behind his master taking everything in. He had assumed Haoyu would now kick back and rest in this nice room; instead, Steward Qiu had claimed the seat of honor and started throwing his weight around. *Young Master is going to blow his top and walk straight out,* Qinggua thought.
[ZH] “大公子七八岁时已经开始像伙计一样在前面卖货，基本上把陈家在临安的铺子跑遍。”
[EN] To his shock, Haoyu paused for a moment—annoyance flashed across his face, then vanished under a composed mask as he turned back to the steward. "What kind of seasoning?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇痛下决心：“我要像东哥学习。既然我爹把我交给秋管事，你想怎么安排都行。”
[EN] "First, you'll work two months up front as a shop clerk," Steward Qiu declared. "Then three months learning the books in the counting house. After that, you'll close three major deals on your own. Pass all of that, and you may sit at that desk and learn how to manage the shop under me."
[ZH] 秋管事满意地点点头。
[EN] "So I'm not even qualified to sit in that chair yet?"
[ZH] 青瓜心中感慨：“看来公子真的变了。”
[EN] "Second Young Master catches on fast."
[ZH] 白风见完苦荞回到幽林别居，王爷独自一人坐在木质高台上。他猜王爷等着他带回幽栖居士的消息，便顺着台阶上去。
[EN] "Then why bother bringing me in here at all?"
[ZH] 申简辰注意到白风，心中很想知道千里寻的消息，想到婚姻，不得不控制住急切的心情：“你见过苦荞？她可好？”
[EN] "If I didn't bring you in here, how else would you see my authority as steward?"
[ZH] 白风清楚王爷并非关心苦荞：“听苦荞说，去年散播幽栖居士流言的人是陈公子。”
[EN] A flicker of regret pricked at Chen Haoyu. Had he known it would be this much of a headache, he never would have volunteered to join the family business. Then he thought of his feelings, and of the wife waiting at home, and hardened his resolve. "Did Brother Dong learn the same way?"
[ZH] 申简辰像是自语：“看来她已知道，担心她难过，我才瞒着，还是被她知道。知道嫁给散播自己流言的人，她岂能好过？”
[EN] "Eldest Young Master started selling bolts up front like a common clerk when he was seven or eight. He's worked his way through just about every Chen shop in Lin'an."
[ZH] “苦荞说，幽栖居士不再与陈公子计较散播流言一事。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu set his jaw. "Then I'll learn the same way Brother Dong did. Since my father put me under your wing, Steward Qiu, assign me whatever you see fit."
[ZH] “不计较？”申简辰搞不懂自己内心是该轻松还是该更加担心。
[EN] Steward Qiu nodded in approval.
[ZH] “王爷，还有一事不知当讲不当讲？”
[EN] Qinggua stared in quiet awe. *Guess Young Master really has turned over a new leaf.*
[ZH] “讲。”
[EN] Back at Youlin Retreat, Bai Feng returned from meeting Kuqiao to find Prince Yi sitting alone atop the raised wooden platform. Guessing his master was waiting for word of Youqi Jushi, Bai Feng climbed the steps.
[ZH] “听苦荞说，陈公子已变好，还喜欢上幽栖居士，一大早跑到陈家绸缎庄学做生意。”
