[EN] Shen Jianchen spotted him. Every fiber of him yearned for news of Qianli Xun, yet the reality of their marriages forced him to keep his impatience leashed. "You saw Kuqiao? Is she well?"
[ZH] “变好？喜欢？”
[EN] Bai Feng knew full well the prince wasn't asking about Kuqiao. "Kuqiao told me it was Young Master Chen who spread those rumors about Youqi Jushi last year."
[ZH] 申简辰已坐不住，迅速起身，顺着台阶向下走。走过一半，脚步逐渐放慢。如此着急能去做什么？她已成亲，陈浩宇喜欢上她，为她变好，是一件再正常不过的事情。
[EN] Shen Jianchen murmured as if to himself, "So she knows after all. I kept it from her because I didn't want to hurt her, yet she found out anyway. Discovering she married the very man who slandered her—how could she bear it?"
[ZH] 申简辰已没有力气走下去，他在台阶上坐下。
[EN] "Kuqiao says Youqi Jushi isn't holding the rumors against him anymore."
[ZH] 王爷坐下，白风不好继续向下走，也坐在台阶上。
[EN] "Not holding it against him?" Shen Jianchen could not tell whether that should bring relief or sharper dread.
[ZH] 两人坐着沉默了一会儿，邹嬷嬷走来，在台阶下拜道：“王爷此时可有空？我有话与您说。”
[EN] "Your Highness... there is one more thing, though I'm not sure I should say it."
[ZH] 申简辰起身走下台阶：“嬷嬷有事就在这里说。”
[EN] "Speak."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷瞧瞧周围：“这里不是说话的地儿，还是去王爷房中说更好一些。”
[EN] "Kuqiao says Young Master Chen has reformed. He's fallen for Youqi Jushi, and he went straight to the Chen Silk Emporium first thing this morning to learn the business."
[ZH] 申简辰一路无语，带着邹嬷嬷向他居住的院子而去。
[EN] "Reformed? Fallen for her?"
[ZH] 王爷双眉紧锁，心事重重，一路上邹嬷嬷也不敢多说一句。
[EN] Shen Jianchen could sit still no longer. He pushed to his feet and started down the steps, only for his stride to falter halfway down. What good would rushing off do? She was married. Chen Haoyu falling in love with her and mending his ways for her sake—nothing could be more natural. The strength drained out of his legs, and he sank down right onto the steps.
[ZH] 到屋中坐下后，白风很知趣地待在外面，他早已猜出这位嬷嬷要与王爷说郡主之事，他一个侍卫待在一旁不合适。
[EN] Seeing his master sit, Bai Feng dared not keep descending and sat on a step beside him.
[ZH] 连白风都能猜到的事，申简辰又怎能不知？他成亲后，邹嬷嬷还未在他面前提起过，今日定是为他与梦容而来。
[EN] They stayed there in silence until Matron Zou approached and bowed at the foot of the stairs. "Does Your Highness have a moment? I have something to discuss with you."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷见王爷依然不语，她刚坐下，又起身行礼：“想必王爷已猜到我要说什么，若说得不对，还请王爷多担待。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stood and walked down. "Say whatever you have to say right here, Matron."
[ZH] “嬷嬷不必多礼，坐下讲，无论你说什么，我都会认真听。”
[EN] Matron Zou glanced around. "This isn't the place for it. Better we speak in Your Highness's quarters."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷又坐下，大胆说：“佳和郡主已是仪王妃，王爷不该继续让她独守空房，不如今晚就宠幸了她。”
[EN] Without a word, Shen Jianchen led her toward his courtyard. His brows stayed knitted in gloom the entire way, leaving Matron Zou reluctant to make small talk.
[ZH] 申简辰沉默不语。
[EN] Once they were seated inside, Bai Feng had the sense to wait outside. He already guessed the matron was here to press the prince about Princess Jiahe—hardly a conversation for a guard to overhear. And if even Bai Feng could guess, Shen Jianchen knew it as well. Since the wedding, Matron Zou had held her tongue; today had to be about him and Mengrong.
[ZH] “仪王妃是郡主时，对王爷的付出我是看在眼里，感动在心里，能娶到这样的女子是王爷的福气，您不能身体一好，再来到临安，碰见些花花草草，便毫不顾及郡主以前的付出。”
[EN] When the prince stayed silent, Matron Zou rose from her seat to bow again. "Your Highness has likely guessed what I'm here to say. If I speak out of turn, please forgive an old servant."
[ZH] 申简辰忍不住说：“我说过，我不是赵朴……”
[EN] "No need for ceremony, Matron. Sit and speak. Whatever you have to say, I will listen."
[ZH] “王爷可别再说这些话，您不是官家的儿子仪王又能是哪个？我知道您起死回生将以前的事情忘得一干二净，可您也是血气方刚的男子，怎么就对如花似玉般的王妃不动心？看来王爷还没尝到女子的好，不妨今晚试试，说不定明日您会跟王妃如胶似漆。呵呵……”
[EN] Matron Zou sat back down and spoke with bold candor. "Princess Jiahe is now Princess Consort Yi. Your Highness shouldn't leave her sleeping in an empty bed night after night. Why not spend tonight with her?"
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷说得开心，见王爷面色平静，不好再笑下去：“听仪王妃说，王爷来到临安后喜欢上一位女词人。王爷是男子，又是官家的儿子，喜欢个把女子没什么，大不了也纳进王府，没必要因为一个普通女子影响您跟王妃之间的关系。该宠幸王妃照样宠幸，该喜欢外面的女子您照样喜欢，岂不皆大欢喜？何必搞得自己难受，王妃也跟着难受？我听说那女词人也已嫁人，王爷更不该为她伤王妃的心。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen said nothing.
[ZH] 申简辰依然沉默不语。
[EN] "Back when she was still Princess Jiahe, I watched every bit of devotion she poured into caring for you, and it moved me to tears. Marrying a woman like her is Your Highness's blessing. You can't get your health back, come to Lin'an, catch sight of a few stray blossoms outside, and toss aside everything she sacrificed for you."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷觉着无趣，站起来：“话已至此，王爷您瞧着办。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen could not stop himself from replying. "I've told you before—I am not Zhao Pu..."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷离开后，申简辰也起身走到门外，见白风站在院中，吩咐道：“随我去陈家绸缎庄。”
[EN] "Please stop saying such nonsense, Your Highness! If you aren't His Majesty's son Prince Yi, who else could you be? I know coming back from death's door wiped your old memories clean, but you're still a red-blooded young man. How can you stay cold to a wife as lovely as a flower? It must be because you've never tasted a woman's sweetness yet. Give it a try tonight, and by tomorrow you'll be stuck to the Princess Consort like glue! Hehe..."
[ZH] 白风立刻猜到王爷是想去找陈浩宇。
[EN] Her chuckle died away when Shen Jianchen's face stayed utterly impassive. She pressed on in a more practical vein. "The Princess Consort told me you fell for a female poet after coming to Lin'an. You're a man, and a son of the emperor at that—taking a fancy to a woman or two is nothing unusual. Bring her into the prince's household as a concubine if you must, but don't let some ordinary woman ruin things between you and your primary wife. Favor the Princess Consort as you ought, and enjoy your outside fancy too—wouldn't everyone be happy? Why make yourself miserable and break the Princess Consort's heart too? Besides, I hear that poetess is already married. All the more reason not to hurt your wife over her."
[ZH] 话说陈浩宇换上伙计的衣装，来到最外面的铺子，先站着观察了一会儿，发现售卖之人全部是伙计，进进出出则以女子居多。
[EN] Shen Jianchen remained silent.
[ZH] 他勾勾手，青瓜乖乖靠近公子。
[EN] Finding she was talking to a brick wall, Matron Zou stood up. "I've said my piece. Your Highness can decide for yourself."
[ZH] 陈浩宇问：“你说顾客以女子居多，为何卖货的都是伙计？”
[EN] Once she left, Shen Jianchen walked out into the courtyard and gave Bai Feng a crisp order. "Come with me to the Chen Silk Emporium."
[ZH] 青瓜被难住：“我一个下人怎知卖货的事儿？公子还是不要为难我。”
[EN] Bai Feng knew at once who his master was going to see.
[ZH] 陈浩宇批评道：“要你有何用？”
[EN] Over at the silk emporium, Chen Haoyu had changed into a clerk's tunic and taken his post on the front sales floor. Watching for a few minutes, he noticed that every single clerk was a man, while almost all the shoppers streaming in and out were women. He crooked a finger, and Qinggua stepped right over.
[ZH] 青瓜辩解道：“帮公子跑个腿应该没有问题。”
[EN] "Tell me," Haoyu asked, "if most of the customers are women, why are all the clerks men?"
[ZH] 有一位伙计招呼完顾客，瞧见陈浩宇身着伙计服，面生了些，上前问道：“喂，你是新来的？”
[EN] Qinggua blinked, stumped. "I'm just a house servant—what do I know about running a shop? Don't put me on the spot, Young Master."
[ZH] 青瓜想要上前告诉对方面前这位是陈家二公子，被陈浩宇拦住，只听公子主动说：“我是新来的。”
[EN] "What good are you, then?"
[ZH] “既是新来的还愣在这里干什么？赶紧招呼客人！”伙计训斥完，看向青瓜，“你也是新来的？”
[EN] "Running errands for you—I can handle that just fine!"
[ZH] 青瓜解释道：“我是他的下人。”
[EN] A clerk who had just wrapped up with a customer noticed an unfamiliar face in a clerk's tunic and walked over. "Hey, you new here?"
[ZH] 伙计既不解又生气：“你个当伙计的要什么下人？”
[EN] Qinggua opened his mouth to announce that this was the Chen family's Second Young Master, but Haoyu cut him off. "Yeah, I'm new."
[ZH] 陈浩宇实在装不下去：“我是陈家二公子，凭什么不能带下人？”
[EN] "Then what are you standing around gawking for? Go wait on the customers!" The clerk barked, then eyed Qinggua. "You new too?"
[ZH] 伙计一听立刻怂，赔上笑脸：“原来是二公子，怪小的有眼无珠，您忙着，我去招呼客人。”
[EN] "I'm his servant," Qinggua explained.
[ZH] 伙计变得客气，陈浩宇又觉无趣，早知不透露身份。又一想，秋管事一早带他前来瞧过，估计有些伙计已知他身份，迟早大家都会知道。
[EN] The clerk stared, baffled and irritated. "Since when does a shop clerk bring a personal servant to work?"
[ZH] 瞧瞧身边的青瓜，自己都变成伙计，再带个下人也不是个事儿：“青瓜，要不你回去，别跟着我。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu couldn't keep the act up any longer. "I'm the Chen family's Second Young Master. Why can't I bring a servant?"
[ZH] 青瓜坚持道：“我不回去，就要跟着公子，大不了我也换身伙计服，也当回伙计。”
[EN] The clerk deflated on the spot, plastering on an apologetic grin. "Oh! Second Young Master! Forgive my blind eyes—please carry on, I'll go see to the customers."
[ZH] 陈浩宇笑道：“有道理，赶紧去换衣裳。”
[EN] Now that the clerk was all bows and scrapes, Haoyu lost the thrill of it and wished he had kept his mouth shut. Still, Steward Qiu had paraded him around the workshops earlier; half the staff probably knew who he was already, and the rest would find out soon enough. Looking at Qinggua beside him, he decided working as a clerk while trailing a servant did look silly. "Qinggua, why don't you head back and leave me to it?"
[ZH] 青瓜换衣裳的空当，陈浩宇观察各位伙计如何招待买主。他发现伙计们见到买主来，会立刻迎上去，面带笑容，再将买主引到各类丝绸跟前一一介绍。
[EN] "No way!" Qinggua dug in his heels. "I'm sticking by you, Young Master. Worst case, I'll put on a clerk's tunic and be a clerk too."
[ZH] 陈浩宇觉得并不难，也想上前试试。
[EN] Haoyu laughed. "Fair point. Go get changed, then."
[ZH] 正好一位姑娘进来，他赶紧面带笑容迎上去：“姑娘，想买些什么？”
[EN] While Qinggua went to change, Haoyu studied how the other clerks handled buyers. The moment someone walked in, they stepped forward with a warm smile, led the shopper to the silk racks, and talked through each fabric one by one. Looked simple enough. He might as well try his hand.
[ZH] 姑娘径直往里走，边走边说：“想给我爹做件常服。”
[EN] Just then a young woman walked in. Haoyu hurried up with a bright smile. "What can I help you find today, Miss?"
[ZH] “姑娘孝顺。”陈浩宇趁机夸道，接着说，“陈家铺子里料子应有尽有，您随便挑。”
[EN] She walked deeper into the shop as she answered, "I'm looking for cloth to make an everyday robe for my father."
[ZH] 姑娘站住到处瞧瞧，十分为难：“我不知道该选哪种？你帮着出出主意。”
[EN] "What a filial daughter!" Haoyu praised her, adding with a wave of his hand, "Our Chen emporium has every fabric under the sun. Take your pick!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇瞧到一处料子，觉得甚是合适：“姑娘，您瞧那种料子怎么样？”
[EN] She paused, looking around with a knit brow. "I don't know which one to choose. Give me a recommendation."
[ZH] 姑娘摇摇头：“适合女子，不适合给我爹做衣裳。”
[EN] Haoyu pointed to a nearby bolt that looked fine to him. "How about that one, Miss?"
[ZH] 头一回当伙计，竟然连男女用的料子都搞混，陈浩宇尴尬的同时，顾不上埋怨自己：“姑娘，您瞧那边，有适合男子的。”
[EN] She shook her head. "That's women's silk. Hardly suitable for my father."
[ZH] 姑娘走过去，瞧来瞧去，拿捏不定：“你给我说说这些料子，好让我了解后加以判断，帮我爹选择适合的。”
[EN] Mixing up men's and women's cloth on his very first customer sent heat rushing to Haoyu's ears, though he had no time to kick himself. "Over there, Miss—those are for men."
[ZH] 陈浩宇被难住，他平时只知穿衣，哪了解过衣料：“没……没什么可介绍的，姑娘只要觉得质地好，颜色合适就可买回去。”
[EN] She walked over, fingered several bolts, and stayed undecided. "Tell me about these fabrics so I can compare them and pick the right one for my father."
[ZH] 姑娘不满道：“以前来陈家绸缎庄，总有伙计会详细介绍，你怎么什么都不懂？”
[EN] Haoyu froze. All his life he had only worn clothes—he had never paid attention to what they were woven from. "There's... not much to explain. If the texture feels nice and you like the color, just buy it."
[ZH] 有位伙计过来，赔上笑脸：“姑娘别生气，他是新来的，懂得不多，我来给您介绍。”
[EN] The customer's face soured. "Whenever I've shopped at the Chen Silk Emporium before, the clerks always explained every bolt in detail. How do you not know a single thing?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇只能靠边站，认真听那伙计介绍。
[EN] Another clerk hurried over with an apologetic smile. "Don't be upset, Miss. He's brand new and still learning the ropes. Let me walk you through them."
[ZH] “您面前的材质都是罗，质地轻薄，丝缕纤细，织物紧密结实，又有孔眼透气，最适合制作夏季衣衫。我们店铺有横罗、直罗和花罗，各类花色应有尽有。我刚在一旁听到姑娘说是想给您父亲做常服，选这种没有提花的素织罗最适合不过。”
[EN] Forced to step aside, Haoyu stood back and listened closely.
[ZH] 姑娘十分满意：“给我把素织罗包起来。”
[EN] "Everything in front of you is silk gauze," the clerk said with smooth ease. "Light in weight, fine in thread, tight and durable in the weave, yet airy with tiny open pores—ideal for summer robes. We carry weft-ribbed gauze, warp-ribbed gauze, and figured gauze in every pattern you could want. I overheard you say it's for your father's everyday wear—this plain-weave gauze without brocade patterns would suit him best."
[ZH] 伙计兴奋道：“姑娘稍等，这就给您包起来。”
[EN] The young woman beamed. "Wrap up the plain-weave gauze for me, please."
[ZH] 陈浩宇算是看出来伙计也不好当，自己需慢慢学。
[EN] "Right away, Miss! Just a moment."
[ZH] 青瓜早已换好衣裳，目睹了公子被替代的一幕，上前安慰道：“公子，您刚来，不懂也正常，时间一长，您肯定比他们强。”
[EN] Working the counter was no joke after all, Haoyu realized. He had a lot of learning ahead of him.
[ZH] 陈浩宇夸道：“总算说了句我爱听的话。”
[EN] Qinggua, already changed into a clerk's tunic, had watched his master get edged aside and sidled up to console him. "It's your first day, Young Master—nobody expects you to know it all. Give it some time and you'll outshine all of them."
[ZH] 伙计将料子包好，送到姑娘手中：“姑娘出门向右拐，有陈家的裁缝铺，您在这里买布料，做衣衫一律半价，若姑娘想买成衣，直接去隔壁的成衣店。”
[EN] "There," Haoyu grinned. "Finally said something I like hearing."
[ZH] 姑娘拿了料子往出走，经过陈浩宇身边批评道：“学着点！”
[EN] The clerk tied up the parcel and handed it over. "Turn right when you step outside, Miss, and you'll find the Chen tailor shop. Since you bought your fabric here, tailoring is half price. And if you ever want ready-made garments, our ready-to-wear shop is right next door."
[ZH] 待姑娘离开后，伙计上前行礼：“小的抢了二公子的客人，还请二公子见谅。”
[EN] Taking her bundle, the young woman walked past Haoyu and tossed him a sharp glance. "Take notes!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇并不计较：“你做得没错，叫什么名字？”
[EN] Once she stepped outside, the clerk bowed to Haoyu. "Forgive me for taking your customer, Second Young Master."
[ZH] “小的叫二贵。”
[EN] Haoyu waved it off. "You did the right thing. What's your name?"
[ZH] “以后就由你带本公子，给我教当伙计的本领。”
[EN] "Ergui, Second Young Master."
[ZH] “小的不敢。”
[EN] "From now on, you're in charge of training me. Teach me everything a shop clerk needs to know."
[ZH] “有什么敢不敢的？本公子让你教，你就好好教，否则我让秋管事把你辞了。”
[EN] "I wouldn't dare—"
[ZH] “我教我教，二公子千万不要让秋管事辞我。”
[EN] "What's there to dare or not dare? I'm ordering you to teach me, so teach me well—or I'll have Steward Qiu fire you."
[ZH] “这就对了。”
[EN] "I'll teach you, I'll teach you! Please don't have Steward Qiu fire me, Second Young Master!"
[ZH] 陈浩东在绸缎庄的会客房与客商谈完生意，将客商送出时，瞧见堂弟身着伙计服正听一位伙计介绍各类料子，陈浩东轻蔑一笑。
[EN] "That's more like it."
[ZH] 因铺子里到处都是客人，他不好说什么。将客商送走后，才过去拉住浩宇：“别听这些伙计瞎教，跟我出去逛逛，边逛我边给你教。”
[EN] In the reception parlor, Chen Haodong finished his negotiation and walked the visiting merchants out. Passing the sales floor, he caught sight of his cousin dressed like a common clerk, listening meekly while Ergui lectured him on silk weaves. A sneer curled Haodong's lip.
[ZH] 陈浩宇当然想出去逛，可一想伙计懂的他还没学会，东哥教的他肯定更不会，只好拒绝：“东哥懂得更高深，我肯定学不会，等以后提升了，再跟东哥学。”
[EN] With shoppers everywhere, he held his tongue until the merchants departed, then walked over and tugged Haoyu's arm. "Stop letting these clerks feed you nonsense. Come walk around town with me—I'll teach you as we go."
[ZH] 陈浩东担心说多会引起浩宇怀疑他的用心，不再继续邀请，给一旁的伙计叮嘱道：“你们几个听好了，他是我二弟，可不能慢待。”
[EN] Part of Haoyu itched to slip outside for a stroll, yet if he hadn't even mastered what a junior clerk knew, how could he follow his cousin's lessons? He shook his head. "You know way more advanced stuff than I can handle right now, Brother Dong. Once I get the basics down, I'll come learn from you."
[ZH] 几位伙计回道：“大公子放心，我们定会照顾好二公子。”
[EN] Afraid pushing harder would make Haoyu suspicious of his motives, Haodong dropped the offer and addressed the nearby clerks instead. "Listen up, all of you. He's my younger brother—don't you dare neglect him."
[ZH] 陈浩东很不爽地走出铺子，瞧见疤子等人在外面转悠，心想：“来得倒快，有他们勾引，不愁浩宇不跟着他们走。”
[EN] "Rest assured, Eldest Young Master," the clerks chorused. "We'll look after Second Young Master."
[ZH] 陈浩东向疤子等人使使眼色，上了马车离开绸缎庄。
[EN] Stepping out of the emporium in a foul mood, Chen Haodong spotted Scarface and his cronies loitering across the street. *Right on time,* he thought. *With them dangling temptation under his nose, Haoyu won't resist for long.* He shot Scarface a knowing glance, climbed into his carriage, and rolled away.

#CH 249
[ZH] 疤子带领几个小弟进入绸缎庄内，伙计们以为有人前来闹事，都不敢上前招呼，买料子的男男女女也都躲向一边。
[ZH] 直到见疤子等人笑嘻嘻地来到陈浩宇面前称“陈公子”时，大家才放心，各干各的。
[ZH] 二贵见有人找公子，暂时不需要他教，便去迎接进来的客人。
[ZH] 疤子等人吓到买主，陈浩宇没好气地说：“你们进来干什么？有话出去说。”
[ZH] 走出绸缎庄，没等疤子等人说话，陈浩宇先劈头盖脸地数落一顿：“瞧你们几个吊儿郎当的样子，还有脸来找本公子？以后不要再跟本公子混，我忙着呢！”
[ZH] 疤子说：“没想到几日不见，陈公子竟然变了个人，在绸缎庄当起伙计来？该不会是被少夫人打傻了？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇强调道：“怎么说话呢？我娘子对我好着呢。”
[ZH] 青瓜感到极其不爽，以前公子都是被他们勾 引走，从来不带他，现在公子不想理会这些人，不如趁此机会也将他们训斥一顿。
[ZH] 他走上前，大胆说：“喂！赶紧走，小心我告诉少夫人，把你们打得哭爹喊娘！”
[ZH] 疤子等人并未将青瓜放在眼里，他说的话就像没听到一般。
[ZH] 有人问：“难道陈公子不想春金沫？”
[ZH] 提到春金沫，陈浩宇多少有些许愧疚：“她可好？”
[ZH] 疤子趁机说：“没有陈公子罩着，怎能好？日日抹眼泪。您被少夫人带走的第二天，蒋爷就去春香楼，她宁死不从，春香楼的妈妈也没办法。还好蒋爷没逼她，还把她给包养了，等她回心转意后，再为她赎身。有银子赚，春香楼的妈妈也不会找春金沫麻烦，只是她心里时刻都念着陈公子。”
[ZH] 想到娘子，陈浩宇狠狠心：“你们帮我给春金沫捎句话，就说本公子以前太无聊，心里也没个喜欢的人，跟她不过是玩玩儿，千万别太当真，往后我不会再去春香楼，劝她别再念着我。”
[ZH] 疤子意识到陈公子真的变了，往后想跟他混吃混喝已不可能。
[ZH] 有位小弟说：“陈公子，您不带我们玩儿，我们几个往后连个依靠都没有。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇想到一个主意：“你们整日闲着没事瞎混，不如做正经事赚些银子，也好养活自己。我给东哥说说，给你们安排些搬运货物的体力活。”
[EN] Scarface swaggered into the silk emporium with a handful of lackeys at his heels. Thinking ruffians had come to stir up trouble, the clerks hung back while shoppers, men and women alike, edged toward the walls. Only when Scarface grinned ear to ear and greeted Chen Haoyu as "Young Master Chen" did everyone exhale and go back to their business. Seeing his pupil had visitors, Ergui excused himself to greet incoming customers.
[ZH] 这些人混吃混喝惯了，突然让他们干活，一个个都露出不情愿的表情。
[EN] Annoyed that they had spooked the shoppers, Haoyu barked, "What are you doing barging in here? If you've got something to say, take it outside."
[ZH] 陈浩宇见状，继续批评：“瞧你们一个个好吃懒做的样子，没事儿别来烦本公子。”
[EN] Once they stepped onto the street, Haoyu tore into them before Scarface could get a word out. "Look at the lot of you, slouching around like street bums—and you still have the nerve to come looking for me? Don't come hanging around me anymore. I'm busy!"
[ZH] 说完正要进去继续忙伙计该忙的事儿，被一个声音绊住。
[EN] Scarface blinked. "Only a few days since we saw you last, and you're a whole different man, playing shop clerk at the silk emporium? Don't tell me the Young Madam beat the sense clean out of you?"
[ZH] “陈公子。”
[EN] "Watch your mouth!" Haoyu snapped. "My wife treats me great."
[ZH] 陈浩宇转身瞧去，原来是白侍卫，再往一边瞧，的确是仪王的马车，没想到他这么快从汴梁回来。
[EN] Qinggua bristled with old resentment. These were the very loafers who used to drag his master off to carouse while leaving him behind. Since Young Master wanted nothing to do with them now, why not pile on? Stepping forward, he puffed out his chest. "Hey! Clear off, before I tell the Young Madam and she comes out to beat you all crying for your mothers!"
[ZH] “哦，白侍卫，找我何事？”
[EN] Scarface and his crew ignored Qinggua as though a mosquito had buzzed past. One of the cronies called out, "Wait—don't you miss Chun Jinmo at all, Young Master Chen?"
[ZH] “王爷有请陈公子去马车上问话。”
[EN] A twinge of guilt tugged at Haoyu at the sound of her name. "How is she?"
[ZH] 仪王邀请，陈浩宇哪敢拒绝，只能乖乖上了马车。
[EN] Scarface pounced on the opening. "How could she be, without you watching out for her? Cries her eyes out every single day. The very day after your wife hauled you home, Master Jiang marched into Chunxiang Brothel. Chun Jinmo swore she'd rather die than yield, and even the madam couldn't budge her. Lucky for her, Master Jiang didn't force her—he just bought out her exclusive patronage and says he'll redeem her contract once she has a change of heart. As long as silver keeps rolling in, the madam leaves her alone, but she's still pining for you morning and night."
[ZH] 疤子等人还未走，他们虽不认得白风，但听刚才的对话，马车上那人应该是位王爷。临安城来了位仪王谁人不知？疤子等人都吐吐舌头，有贵人在此，担心冲撞对方，还是赶紧离开。
[EN] Thinking of his wife back home, Haoyu steeled his heart. "Pass a message to Chun Jinmo for me. Tell her I used to be bored out of my mind with nobody I truly cared for, so I was only fooling around with her. Tell her not to take it to heart. I won't be setting foot in Chunxiang Brothel again, so she'd better stop pining for me."
[ZH] 青瓜见识过白风的厉害，这家伙待在马车旁，他不敢随意靠近，还是站在远处等待自家公子。
[EN] Scarface saw then that Chen Haoyu meant it; the days of freeloading off his purse were over.
[ZH] 马车上，陈浩宇坐下后，向仪王行礼：“早知殿下回来，我应该亲自登门拜访，却让您大老远跑来见我，实在不该。不过，您怎么知道我在绸缎庄？”
[EN] Another lackey whined, "Young Master Chen, if you stop taking us out, we won't have anyone left to lean on."
[ZH] “只要是我想找的人，想知道的事，再难也会变得轻而易举。再说陈家绸缎庄在临安名气不小，找起来相当容易。”
[EN] An idea struck Haoyu. "Instead of loafing around doing nothing all day, why not earn honest silver and feed yourselves? I can put in a word with Brother Dong and get you boys heavy labor hauling freight."
[ZH] “殿下的厉害我领教过，不知您今日找我有何指教？”
[EN] Accustomed to eating and drinking for free, every single one of them pulled a sour face at the mention of honest sweat.
[ZH] 申简辰瞧着陈浩宇一身装束：“你怎么穿着伙计的衣裳？”
[EN] Haoyu sneered. "Look at your lazy mugs. Don't bother me again unless you've got real business."
[ZH] “不瞒殿下，我打算重新做人，往后会参与陈家生意，需先从伙计学起。”
[EN] He pivoted to head back inside to his clerk duties, only for a voice to stop him in his tracks.
[ZH] “你真的打算改变？”
[EN] "Young Master Chen."
[ZH] “那还能有假？刚才那帮人来勾 引我去春香楼，反而被我痛批一顿，都灰溜溜地走了。”
[EN] Turning around, he spotted Guard Bai—and right beside him sat Prince Yi's carriage. Haoyu blinked. He hadn't expected the prince back from Bianliang so soon. "Oh, Guard Bai. What brings you here?"
[ZH] “她……她可高兴？”
[EN] "His Highness invites you into the carriage for a word."
[ZH] 陈浩宇明白过来，仪王前来不过是为朱淑真：“她……暂时还看不出来，但我相信，只要我坚持，总有一天她会高兴，毕竟已经嫁给我。”
[EN] Who dared refuse Prince Yi? Haoyu obediently climbed aboard.
[ZH] 陈浩宇边说边观察仪王的表情，发现他垂着眼帘，看上去心情很不佳。
[EN] Scarface and his crew had not cleared out yet. They didn't recognize Bai Feng, but hearing the word *Prince* was enough—who in Lin'an didn't know Prince Yi had taken up residence in town? Sticking out their tongues in fright at the thought of offending royalty, they scattered in a hurry.
[ZH] 半晌仪王才问出：“可还记得我回汴梁前，在钱塘帮外你曾对我说过的话？”
[EN] Qinggua, who had seen firsthand how lethal Bai Feng was, stayed well back at a safe distance while waiting for his master.
[ZH] 陈浩宇早已将说过的话抛到九霄云外，仪王提起，猛然想到曾说过要将朱淑真还给仪王。此时想起后悔不已，又担心仪王治他一个言而无信之罪，赶紧跪下，他还是第一次在马上给人下跪。
[EN] Inside the carriage, Chen Haoyu sat down and bowed. "Had I known Your Highness had returned, I ought to have paid a formal call myself. Making you come all this way to see me is unforgivable. Still, how did you know I was at the silk emporium?"
[ZH] “当时我还没喜欢上淑真，说话口无遮拦了些，殿下千万不要当真。再说您身份尊贵，只要振臂一挥，什么样的姑娘没有？又何必跟草民争娘子？”
[EN] "When I want to find someone or learn something, no obstacle stays difficult for long," Shen Jianchen said. "Besides, the Chen Silk Emporium is famous enough in Lin'an—hardly a secret."
[ZH] “你竟如此看我？”
[EN] "I've already seen what Your Highness is capable of. May I ask what brings you here today?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇心想：“难道不是吗？我成亲那日你跑来抢亲，都娶了郡主，还惦记我家娘子，你要不是王爷，我非得跟你打一架不可。你应该好好学学乔文仙，自从淑真嫁给我后，他安心过起自己的小日子，早已将以前跟我家娘子那段情抛到九霄云外。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen looked over Haoyu's plain tunic. "Why are you dressed like a shop clerk?"
[ZH] “陈浩宇……”
[EN] "To be honest with Your Highness, I've decided to turn over a new leaf. I'm going to help run the Chen family business, starting from the bottom as a clerk."
[ZH] “在。”
[EN] "You truly mean to change?"
[ZH] “我今日来，不妨跟你交个心。我对她的情以前、现在、往后都不会变，她既然阴差阳错嫁给你，我尊重她的选择。你若再往青楼跑，做对不起她的事，我定不饶恕。”
[EN] "Would I lie about that? That gang outside just came trying to lure me back to Chunxiang Brothel, and I tore strips off them until they tucked their tails and ran."
[ZH] “我要干了坏事，没轮上仪王教训，我家娘子的拳头肯定已将我打倒在地。”
[EN] "And her... is she happy?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇亲切地称呼她为“娘子”，申简辰有说不出的心痛，他决定老老实实接受现实：“你帮我告诉她，我之所以放弃全是为她好，希望……希望……希望你们白头偕老。”
[EN] Realization dawned on Haoyu—Prince Yi had come solely for Zhu Shuzhen. "She... I can't tell yet. Still, I believe if I keep at it, she'll come around one day. She's married to me, after all."
[ZH] 听到仪王的祝福，陈浩宇兴奋不已：“谢仪王殿下！”
[EN] Studying the prince's face as he spoke, Haoyu noticed his lowered eyes and heavy gloom.
[ZH] “你走吧。”
[EN] After a long pause, Shen Jianchen asked, "Do you remember what you said to me outside the Qiantang Gang before I left for Bianliang?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇小心起身下了马车，目送仪王离开后，继续回绸缎庄向二贵学习当伙计的本领。
[EN] Haoyu had banished those words clean from his mind, and the reminder hit him like a thunderbolt—he had promised to hand Zhu Shuzhen back to Prince Yi! Regret flooded him, followed by panic that the prince might charge him with breaking his word. He slid off the bench onto his knees—the first time in his life he had ever knelt to anyone inside a carriage.
[ZH] 马车停在幽林别居门外，申简辰走下马车没有回屋，朝幽林走去。
[EN] "I hadn't fallen for Shuzhen back then, so I was just running my mouth," he pleaded. "Please don't hold me to it, Your Highness! With your noble status, you only have to raise a hand and any girl you want will come running. Why fight a commoner like me over my wife?"
[ZH] 白风清楚，王爷又要去看神柳，便老实跟上。
[EN] "Is that how you see me?"
[ZH] 从情感上来讲，申简辰根本放不下千里寻，可理智告诉他必须放下。随缘而不攀缘说起来容易，做起来则十分困难。他内心的情感与所随之缘无法吻合，才搞得他难过不已。
[EN] *Isn't it true?* Haoyu fumed silently. *You crashed my wedding to steal my bride, and even after marrying Princess Jiahe you're still hung up on my wife! If you weren't a prince, I'd brawl with you right here. Look at Qiao Wenxian—ever since Shuzhen married me, he's settled down and left all that old romance behind!*
[ZH] 再难过也不能像女子一样哭泣，只能将眼泪埋藏在心底。不如去神柳下坐坐，坐久了也许他会心甘情愿屈服于理智。
[EN] "Chen Haoyu."
[ZH] 申简辰穿越密林，沿着池塘边向神柳走去。从汴梁再回来，神柳不仅枝叶更加繁茂，上面的红绸也多起来，看来世间喜谈情说爱的男女实在不少。
[EN] "Yes, Your Highness."
[ZH] 快要靠近神柳时，传来一男一女的声音。
[EN] "I came today to speak plainly with you. My feelings for her—past, present, and future—will never change. Since fate led her to marry you, I respect her choice. But if you ever go running back to brothels or do anything to wrong her, I will not forgive you."
[ZH] （女）“他说等他成为产业继承人后再考虑和离之事。”
[EN] "If I ever pull a stunt like that again, Your Highness won't even get the chance—my wife's fists will flatten me first."
[ZH] （男）“需要多久？”
[EN] Hearing Haoyu call her "my wife" with such easy warmth sent an ache straight through Shen Jianchen's chest. Still, he forced himself to accept reality. "Tell her for me that I'm stepping back for her own good. I hope... I hope... you two grow old together in peace."
[ZH] （女）“一年时间。”
[EN] Hearing the prince's blessing made Haoyu's spirits soar. "Thank you, Your Highness!"
[ZH] （男）“我愿意等。”
[EN] "You may go."
[ZH] （女）“可我等不及，恨不得立刻离开他。”
[EN] Haoyu got to his feet with care, stepped down from the carriage, and watched it roll away before hurrying back into the emporium to resume his lessons under Ergui.
[ZH] （男）“此事不能草率，我们需从长计议。”
[EN] When the carriage halted outside Youlin Retreat, Shen Jianchen stepped out and headed straight into the secluded woods rather than his quarters. Knowing his master wanted to visit the Divine Willow, Bai Feng fell in behind him without a word.
[ZH] （女）“我与他和离后，你的家人肯定更无法接受我。”
[EN] In his heart, Shen Jianchen could not let Qianli Xun go, no matter how loud reason insisted he must. Letting fate take its course without clinging sounded effortless on paper; living it was torture. His heart refused to align with the path fate had handed him, tearing him raw from within. Yet a man couldn't sit down and sob like a girl—he could only bury his tears inside. Maybe sitting beneath the Divine Willow long enough would wear his heart down until he surrendered to reason.
[ZH] （男）“没关系，我家兄弟姊妹多，爹娘不缺我一个，到时我带着你天涯海角自由自在过我们的日子。”
[EN] He cut through the thick grove and followed the pond toward the great tree. Since his trip to Bianliang, the willow's boughs had grown even lusher, and far more red silk ribbons fluttered from its branches. Plenty of lovesick souls out in the world, it seemed.
[ZH] 显然又是一对痴男怨女，听得出来那女的已嫁为人妇，想要与丈夫和离跟心爱的人在一起。
[EN] As he neared the tree, a man's and a woman's voices drifted through the foliage.
[ZH] 男子的声音听着熟悉，申简辰用手拨开高高的灌木丛，看清那男子正是曹班。曹班搂着的女子是叶安雅，申简辰并不认识。
[EN] "He said he'll only consider a heli—a mutual divorce—after he inherits the family estates," the woman said.
[ZH] 曹班搂了一会儿叶安雅，两人才牵手离开。
[EN] "How long will that take?" the man asked.
[ZH] 等二人走远后，白风大胆议论：“王爷，没想到曹公子喜欢的人已成亲。”
[EN] "A year."
[ZH] 白风很快意识到自己说错话，王爷又何尝不是？他还比不上曹公子，起码曹公子单身一枚，而王爷因圣旨所迫，不得不娶郡主。
[EN] "I'm willing to wait."
[ZH] 申简辰懒得理会白风，待那对情人离开后，才靠近神柳，一句话不说坐在那块他常坐的石头上。
[EN] "I can't wait that long! I wish I could leave him this very minute."
[ZH] 也许他离开后，千里寻也来此坐过。
[EN] "We can't rush something this serious. We need to plan it through."
[ZH] 他想象着她来到此处，也在石头上坐下，石头上仿佛留下她的体温。他更觉得这块石头不一般，用心感受起来。
[EN] "Even after I get my heli, your family will accept me even less."
[ZH] 过了一会儿，他睁开眼：“白风，我想再找一头小猪……。”
[EN] "That doesn't matter. I have plenty of brothers and sisters—my parents won't miss one son. When the time comes, I'll take you to the ends of the earth so we can live free together."
[ZH] 白风不得不再次傻眼：“王爷，猪根本不会爬树，之前那头小猪就埋在那里，它死不瞑目，您还要训练小猪？恐怕以后神柳周围会成为小猪的坟场。”
[EN] Another pair of star-crossed lovers, clearly—a married woman desperate to divorce her husband and run off with the man she loved. Something familiar in the man's voice made Shen Jianchen part the tall brush. It was Cao Ban, holding a woman Shen Jianchen did not recognize—Ye Anya. After lingering in an embrace, the two clasped hands and slipped away.
[ZH] 本来到神柳下是想让自己变得更加理智，事与愿违，他依然想要努力，也许是刚才那对恋人促使他还想为自己找寻一线生机。
[EN] Once they were out of earshot, Bai Feng ventured, "Your Highness, who would've thought Young Master Cao was in love with a married woman?" The words were barely out before he realized his blunder—wasn't his own master in the exact same boat? Worse, even: at least Cao Ban was unmarried, whereas Prince Yi had been wed to Princess Jiahe by imperial decree.
[ZH] “我不想放弃训练小猪爬树的想法，你既是我的侍卫，也不能放弃，可也不能随便选些笨猪回来，要随时帮我留心，说不定哪一日真的能遇到会爬树的小猪。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen paid him no mind. Waiting until the couple vanished, he walked up to the Divine Willow and sat in silence on his usual stone. Maybe Qianli Xun had come and sat right here while he was gone. He pictured her perched on this rock, half-convinced a trace of her warmth still lingered in the stone, and closed his eyes to hold onto that illusion.
[ZH] 王爷这么一说，白风终于放心：“只要不再伤害小猪，我定会帮王爷留心。只是这世间真的有会爬树的猪吗？”
[EN] After a spell, he opened his eyes. "Bai Feng, I want to find another piglet."
[ZH] “有没有我都想试试，我想给自己留下一线希望。”
[EN] Bai Feng stared, dumbfounded all over again. "Your Highness, pigs simply cannot climb trees! The last piglet is buried right over there, still glaring at the sky in protest! You want to train another one? At this rate, the Divine Willow is going to turn into a piglet graveyard."
[ZH] 白风隐约感觉到找到会爬树的小猪应该与幽栖居士有关，至于是什么关系，他目前还想不通。
[EN] Shen Jianchen had come here hoping to talk himself into cold reason, yet the exact opposite had happened—perhaps watching that couple refuse to give up had sparked one last stubborn ember inside him. "I'm not giving up on training a pig to climb a tree, and as my guard, neither can you. Don't just grab any dim-witted pig this time. Keep your eyes open—who knows, maybe one day we really will stumble across a piglet that can climb."
[ZH] 乔文仙带着南双也来到神柳下：“原来你真的在这里？”
[EN] That eased Bai Feng's mind. "As long as no more piglets get hurt, I'll keep an eye out for Your Highness. Though... does a tree-climbing pig even exist in this world?"
[ZH] 听到乔文仙的声音，申简辰站起来，也终于露出笑容。
[EN] "Whether it exists or not, I want to try. I want to leave myself one shred of hope."
[ZH] 乔文仙上前抱住仪王：“刚到幽林别居大门外，李管家告诉我，你进了密林，我猜你肯定跑这里来，果不其然被我逮住。”
[EN] Bai Feng had a vague hunch this obsession with tree-climbing pigs had something to do with Youqi Jushi, though he still couldn't connect the dots.
[ZH] 申简辰紧紧抱住乔文仙：“本想着明日参加完宴会再找你，你却亲自送上门来。”
[EN] Just then Qiao Wenxian arrived beneath the willow with Nan Shuang in tow. "So you really are out here!"
[ZH] 乔文仙放开仪王：“你呀，走时一声不响，回来也是，要不是知府大人送来请帖，邀请明日参加宴会，我都不知道你回来，太不够意思！”
[EN] At the sound of his friend's voice, Shen Jianchen rose and broke into a genuine smile. Qiao Wenxian strode over and threw his arms around him. "When I reached the gate of Youlin Retreat, Butler Li told me you'd gone into the woods. I guessed you'd be right here, and sure enough, caught you red-handed."
[ZH] “我不喜欢太过高调，再说被那么多官员关注着实不自在。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen hugged him tight. "I planned to look for you after tomorrow's banquet, and here you are delivering yourself straight to me."
[ZH] “再不喜欢高调也避免不了，明日宴会临安的官员全部会去。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian stepped back. "You leave without a word, you come back without a word—if the Prefect hadn't sent an invitation to tomorrow's banquet, I wouldn't even have known you were back! Some friend you are."
[ZH] “都是仪王和节度使的身份给害的。”
[EN] "I don't like making a spectacle of myself, and having a crowd of officials hovering around me makes my skin crawl."
[ZH] 乔文仙劝道：“你这次回来，不要再像以前一样什么事都不管，还是要发挥一下节度使的作用。”
[EN] "Like it or not, there's no dodging tomorrow. Every official in Lin'an will be there."
[ZH] “说的也是，总得找些事做，才好度过漫长的日日夜夜。”
[EN] "All thanks to being Prince Yi and Military Commissioner."
[ZH] 仪王的语气中充满无奈，乔文仙猜出些意思来：“你我也算是同病相怜，你应该像我一样放下该放下的。”
[EN] "Now that you're back," Qiao Wenxian urged, "don't stay hands-off like before. Put that Military Commissioner title to some real use."
[ZH] “去年文渊在这神柳下难过的样子还历历在目，没想到这么快就放下。”
[EN] "True enough. I need something to keep busy so the long days and nights don't drag forever."
[ZH] “不放下又能如何？她心中已有别人，还是我最好的朋友。”
[EN] Hearing the weary resignation beneath his friend's words, Qiao Wenxian understood. "We're brothers in misfortune, you and I. You ought to let go of what's past, just like I did."
[ZH] 申简辰叹口气：“有了又能如何？她还不是嫁给他人?我堂堂王爷也无能为力。”
[EN] "I can still picture you heartbroken right under this willow last year, Wenyuan. Didn't think you'd let go so fast."
[ZH] “所以，我才劝你像我一样放下，你和郡主也是一对佳偶，不要再想不属于自己的人。”
[EN] "What choice did I have? Her heart belonged to someone else—my best friend, no less."
[ZH] 申简辰不愿继续聊放下的话题：“你猜我这次回临安准备做些什么事？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen let out a sigh. "And what good did that do? She still married another man. Even as a prince, I couldn't stop it."
[ZH] “还能是什么？你是节度使，自然做节度使该做的事。”
[EN] "Which is why I'm telling you to let go like I did. You and Princess Jiahe make a fine pair—stop reaching for someone who isn't yours."
[ZH] “朝廷腐败，地方官员又能好到哪里？只怕我做了节度使的事儿也改变不了什么。不如交给历史，它会改变一切。所以我向知府大人谋了司理参军一职，替老百姓排忧解难，也算没白来临安一趟。”
[EN] Unwilling to dwell on letting go, Shen Jianchen shifted tracks. "Care to guess what I plan to do now that I'm back in Lin'an?"
[ZH] “司理参军？品阶很低，官家要是知道肯定会批评知府大人。”
[EN] "What else? You're the Military Commissioner—do what a Military Commissioner does."
[ZH] “放心，我还是节度使，只不过做司理参军的事儿而已。你要相信地方官员欺上瞒下的本领，再说官家正忙着与金人谈判燕云十六州的事儿，哪顾得上管我？”
[EN] "When the court at the top is rotten, how clean can local officials be? Even acting as Military Commissioner wouldn't fix a thing. Better leave that to history—history will sweep it all away soon enough. Instead, I asked the Prefect for the post of Judicial Investigator so I can handle ordinary folks' troubles. At least this trip to Lin'an won't go to waste."
[ZH] “你既然想好，我也不便多说什么，只是司理参军的府衙十分简陋，就不怕委屈了郡主？”
[EN] "Judicial Investigator? That's a tiny rank! If His Majesty catches wind of it, he'll tear the Prefect to shreds."
[ZH] “我跟梦容说好，她继续住在幽林别居，我去衙门住，每周回来看她一次。”
[EN] "Relax. I'm still Military Commissioner on paper—I'm just taking on the Judicial Investigator's workload. Never underestimate how skilled local officials are at keeping the capital in the dark. Besides, His Majesty has his hands full negotiating with the Jin over the Sixteen Prefectures of Yanyun; he doesn't have time to fuss over me."
[ZH] 乔文仙不再说话，仪王新婚不久不着急体会新婚燕尔，把王妃留在幽林别居，一个人跑去衙门住，再明显不过，也不知仪王妃会难过成什么样?
[EN] "If your mind's made up, I won't argue. Still, the Judicial Investigator's yamen is shabby as dirt. Aren't you worried about making Princess Jiahe suffer there?"
[ZH] 要是没目睹过郡主对仪王的付出，乔文仙必然不会埋怨仪王喜欢上淑真，毕竟淑真太过优秀，喜欢上她再正常不过。
[EN] "I've already arranged it with Mengrong. She'll stay here at Youlin Retreat, I'll live at the yamen, and I'll come back once a week to check on her."
[ZH] 可他亲眼见识过郡主如何无微不至照顾生病的仪王，仪王变心，他第一个会为郡主打抱不平。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian went silent. A newlywed prince skipping out on his bride to sleep alone at a shabby yamen? Nothing could be plainer—and who knew how badly that must be breaking Princess Consort Yi's heart? Had he not witnessed Princess Jiahe's devotion with his own eyes, Qiao Wenxian could never have blamed the prince for loving Shuzhen—she was extraordinary, after all. Yet he had seen firsthand how tirelessly Mengrong had nursed the sick prince back then. Seeing the prince turn cold on her now made Qiao Wenxian's blood stir on her behalf.

#CH 250
[ZH] 两位主人聊他们的话题，白风与南双许久未见，坐在池塘边也聊着两人关心的事。
[ZH] “你见过苦荞？”
[ZH] “当然，今儿一早见过她。”
[ZH] “告诉你件事，幼凡喜欢上我。公子说等我再长两岁，让我娶幼凡过门。你呢？什么时候娶苦荞？”
[ZH] 白风想了一会儿：“王爷已成亲，按说也该我，我找机会向王爷提提，他应该会同意。”
[ZH] “我觉得你跟喜兰在一起会更方便一些，可你偏偏喜欢苦荞，恐怕要娶苦荞没那么容易。”
[ZH] 白风十分不解：“什么意思？”
[ZH] 南双向公子那边瞧一眼，王爷与公子聊得甚欢，并未注意他们，放心说道：“我听幼凡说，郡主，不，现在应该称仪王妃，十分讨厌幽栖居士，恐怕你想娶苦荞，仪王妃会阻拦。”
[ZH] 白风疑惑道：“幼凡怎么知道？”
[ZH] “你回汴梁前，我家少夫人开始与仪王妃走得近，每回都带着幼凡，她什么事都清楚。”
[ZH] 白风并未当回事：“那是仪王妃与幽栖居士之间的事儿，怎么会牵连到我跟苦荞？况且仪王妃人很好，才不会阻拦我跟苦荞。再说我是王爷的人，王妃也管不上。”
[ZH] “话虽如此，我还是建议别着急娶苦荞，等幽栖居士和仪王妃之间的恩怨了结后再向仪王提及此事。”
[ZH] 白风犹豫着：“谢谢你提醒我，我会视情况而定。”
[ZH] 乔文仙与仪王在神柳下畅聊后，两人又回幽林别居畅饮一回，天黑时他才离开。
[ZH] 忙了一日伙计的事儿，陈浩宇多少学到些本事，连青瓜也跟着进步不少。眼瞅着天黑，想起学做茶一事，今日没腾出时间，只能等待明日。
[ZH] 回到陈府的墨香苑，陈浩宇见素心草堂的门关着，里面的灯也熄灭，才知自己回来得晚，再晚也需向娘子禀报一声。
[ZH] 他靠近素心草堂的木门，声音稍微放大：“娘子，我回来了，今儿忙一整天，秋管事先带我了解绸缎庄，然后让我从伙计做起，我才发现伙计也不好当。今日常跟我混的那些个人跑来勾引我，我都没上当，以后我会老老实实的，谁让我喜欢上娘子。”
[ZH] 汇报半天，没有将朱淑真吵醒，隔壁悦宁屋的门却被打开，红豆探出脑袋：“姑爷，大晚上的您吵什么？小姐已经睡下，您也赶紧休息吧。”
[ZH] 红豆说完又把门关上。
[ZH] 陈浩宇批评道：“这丫头，怎么对你家姑爷说话呢？”
[ZH] 青瓜说：“公子，赶紧回去睡吧，来来回回招呼客人，我的腿都累酸了。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇很不舍地瞧着木门：“不理我是吧，等我说多了，总有一日你会理我。”
[EN] While their two masters talked, Bai Feng and Nan Shuang sat by the pond catching up after their long separation.
[ZH] 两人向后院走去，走到半道，碰见丑丫。
[EN] "Did you see Kuqiao?" Nan Shuang asked.
[ZH] “姑爷回来了，我能不能跟青瓜说句话？”
[EN] "Of course. Saw her first thing this morning."
[ZH] 陈浩宇竟然羡慕起青瓜，一回来就有人关心，而他用心汇报，娘子理都不理。
[EN] "Got news for you—Youfan has taken a shine to me. Young Master says once I'm two years older, he'll let me marry her. What about you? When are you marrying Kuqiao?"
[ZH] “随便说，时间不能太长。”
[EN] Bai Feng mulled it over. "His Highness is married now, so I suppose it's my turn. I'll find a good moment to bring it up with him—he's bound to agree."
[ZH] “谢姑爷。”
[EN] "Honestly, pairing off with Xilan would've been way simpler for you. Of all people, you had to pick Kuqiao. Marrying her might not go so smooth."
[ZH] 丑丫高兴地把青瓜拉到一边，将手中的一块糕点塞到青瓜手里：“专门给你留的，味道很好。”
[EN] Bai Feng frowned. "What do you mean?"
[ZH] 青瓜虽然不情愿，还是接过吃起来。
[EN] Nan Shuang stole a glance toward their masters—Prince Yi and Qiao Wenxian were deep in conversation, paying them no mind—and lowered his voice. "Youfan told me that Princess Jiahe—no, Princess Consort Yi now—hates Youqi Jushi's guts. If you try to marry Kuqiao, the Princess Consort might step in and block it."
[ZH] 陈浩宇瞧一眼，羡慕不已，什么时候娘子也能这么对待他？是不是他也应该找一个喜欢自己的？很快，他否认了这一想法。
[EN] "How would Youfan know that?"
[ZH] 喜欢他的人实在好遇，他喜欢的还是破天荒头一遭。他陈浩宇就是这么贱，就是喜欢他喜欢的人，再难也要迎难而上。再说都娶了她，还有什么好担心的？朱淑真迟早会被他搞定。
[EN] "Right before you left for Bianliang, my Young Madam started getting chummy with the Princess Consort, and Youfan tags along every time. She knows all the dirt."
[ZH] 门外传来陈浩宇的声音时，千里寻刚有些迷糊，被那家伙吵醒。本想起身披上外衣，打开门上前教训一顿，但听到陈浩宇当了一日伙计，应该很辛苦，想着他的确与往日不同，便打消教训的念头。
[EN] Bai Feng brushed it off. "That's between the Princess Consort and Youqi Jushi—why would it spill over onto me and Kuqiao? Besides, the Princess Consort has a gentle heart; she wouldn't get in our way. And I belong to Prince Yi's staff anyway, not hers."
[ZH] 暂时睡不着，干脆躺着胡思乱想。先构思一会儿明日的话本该写什么故事更为合适，接着不由自主想起个人感情。想要的目前来讲已不可能，不想要的真实地摆在眼前，接还是不接？人生最为难的事情莫过于此。
[EN] "Even so, take my advice and hold off on proposing. Wait until the bad blood between Youqi Jushi and the Princess Consort blows over before you ask His Highness."
[ZH] 千里寻又想起净光法师说的“正思正念”，面对人生难题，正确的想法又是什么？
[EN] Bai Feng hesitated. "Thanks for the heads-up. I'll play it by ear."
[ZH] 思量来思量去，接受陈浩宇，老老实实当他的娘子才是最正确的念头，可她现在还无法接受。看来人生中总有一些正确的道路人不乐意踏上，还需正确的道路与心意相合。
[EN] After their long talk under the willow, Qiao Wenxian and Shen Jianchen returned to Youlin Retreat for a round of drinks, parting ways only after nightfall.
[ZH] 为什么人需有的正思正念经常会与心意不相合？也许人生是一场修行，人需时时改变心意，才能踏上正确的道路，只是这修行之路对千里寻来讲实在艰难。
[EN] Over at the silk emporium, a full day playing clerk had taught Chen Haoyu a fair few tricks, and even Qinggua had picked up plenty. Seeing darkness settle outside, Haoyu remembered he still meant to learn tea-whisking, though today had slipped away; it would have to wait until tomorrow.
[ZH] ……
[EN] Returning to Moxiang Courtyard in Chen Manor, he found Suxin Thatched Hall shut and dark. He was late—still, late or not, he had to check in with his wife. Stepping close to the wooden door, he raised his voice a notch. "Wife, I'm back! Worked hard all day. First Steward Qiu gave me the tour, then put me up front as a clerk—turns out being a clerk isn't easy at all. Those loafers I used to run with showed up trying to lure me away, and I didn't fall for it once. I'm going to keep my nose clean from now on—what else can I do when I'm head over heels for you?"
[ZH] 既然是陈家出钱，临安的婚宴必然安排在醉云楼。
[EN] His long report got no stir from Zhu Shuzhen, though the door of Yuening Room next door creaked open and Hongdou poked her head out. "Young Master, what's with all the racket at this hour? Miss is already asleep. Go get some rest yourself." With that, she shut the door in his face.
[ZH] 醉云楼虽在儿媳名下，毕竟还是陈家的产业，陈江林作为家主，有权利随时启用，而且无需向儿媳打招呼。因此，千里寻对婚宴在醉云楼举办并不清楚。
[EN] "Hey—is that any way to talk to your master?" Haoyu grumbled.
[ZH] 醉云楼比京城任何一家酒楼布置得都豪华，配仪王的身份再合适不过；加之离钱塘江近，景色宜人，既可博仪王欢心，亦可令临安官员舒心。
[EN] "Come on, let's go to bed, Young Master," Qinggua groaned. "Running back and forth waiting on shoppers all day has my legs aching."
[ZH] 从仪王回汴梁那日，陈江林就差人将各大酒楼的名厨和有经验的伙计以高价请来，加上原有的名厨和总管，人员算是齐备。接下来准备采买之物，既有后厨要用的，也有布置婚宴场地需要的。
[EN] Haoyu cast a wistful look at the closed wooden door. "Keep ignoring me, fine. Sooner or later, after I talk enough, you'll answer."
[ZH] 申简辰来过醉云楼两次，谈不上熟悉，也不陌生。尤其是第二次来时，更令他印象深刻。
[EN] Halfway down the path to the back courtyard, Chouya stepped out to meet them. "You're back, Young Master. Might I have a quick word with Qinggua?"
[ZH] 二楼的隔间里，他与千里寻单独在一起，也许那是他们最后一次单独说话，以后想有这样的机会都难。
[EN] A stab of envy hit Haoyu. The second Qinggua walked through the gate, someone was waiting to fuss over him, while Haoyu poured his heart out at a closed door and got total silence. "Go ahead, just don't take long."
[ZH] 当申简辰带着梦容走下马车，出现在醉云楼大门外时，朱晞颜携带有身份的官员和家眷迎出来。大家先向两位贵人行礼问安，然后祝二位百年好合。
[EN] "Thank you, Young Master!" Beaming, Chouya tugged Qinggua aside and pressed a pastry into his hand. "Saved this just for you. It's delicious."
[ZH] 最令乔梦容喜欢的是，大家都很知趣，称她为“仪王妃”。
[EN] Reluctant as he looked, Qinggua still accepted it and took a bite.
[ZH] 接着仪王与各位官员去了男宾区，乔梦容则被向夫人牵着来到女宾区。她一路瞧着宴会的布置，豪华而不失典雅，心中甚是满意。
[EN] Watching from a few paces off, Haoyu sighed in envy. When would his wife ever dote on him like that? Maybe he should just find some girl who already liked him—no, he squashed that thought on the spot. Finding women who threw themselves at him was easy; falling in love himself was a once-in-a-lifetime miracle. That was just how perverse Chen Haoyu was: he only wanted the woman he loved, no matter how steep the climb. Besides, she was already his wife—what was there to worry about? Sooner or later, he would win Zhu Shuzhen over.
[ZH] 来到主桌位，桌上的美食不比汴梁的婚宴差，看来临安官员颇费心思。
[EN] Inside Suxin Thatched Hall, Qianli Xun had just been drifting off when Haoyu's voice outside woke her. Her first impulse was to throw on a robe, fling the door open, and chew him out—until she heard he had spent the whole day working as a shop clerk. He must be exhausted, and he really wasn't the same brat he used to be. She let the urge to lecture him fade.
[ZH] 乔梦容坐下，各位女眷才敢坐下，她体会到在汴梁无法体会的优越感。
[EN] Wide awake now, she lay in the dark letting her mind wander. First she mapped out a plot for tomorrow's huaben chapter, then her thoughts drifted, unbidden, back to matters of the heart. The man she wanted was out of reach; the man she did not want stood right in front of her, real and earnest. Accept him, or push him away? Life offered few riddles harder than this.
[ZH] 在汴梁时，皇家女眷众多，没有多少人会将她放在眼里，就连官家身边的近臣，她都需畏惧三分。可在这里，她成为所有女眷中身份极高之人，大家都得把她放在眼里。
[EN] Master Jingguang's words came back to her—*Right Thought and Right Mindfulness*. Faced with a knot like this, what counted as the "right" thought? Turning it over from every angle, accepting Chen Haoyu and settling down as his wife was obviously the right path—yet her heart still balked at walking it. Sometimes the right road and a person's own heart pointed in opposite directions. Why did they clash so often? Perhaps life was a kind of spiritual discipline, forcing you to reshape your own heart step by step until it fit the right path. For Qianli Xun, though, that discipline felt brutally hard.
[ZH] 她的身边坐着向夫人，朱淑真的亲娘，她谨慎地陪着笑脸，生怕一不小心得罪贵人。可她的女儿却胆大妄为走进哥哥的心里，还将她看重的友情踩得稀巴烂。等着吧，报复很快会到来。
[EN] Since the Chen family was footing the bill, Lin'an's wedding banquet for Prince Yi was bound to be held at Zuiyun Tower. Even though the tavern stood under his daughter-in-law's name, it remained Chen family property; as family head, Chen Jianglin could commandeer it whenever he pleased without asking her permission, so Qianli Xun had no idea the banquet was taking place there.
[ZH] 各位官员的女眷拜过仪王妃后，又巴结奉承一回；邹嬷嬷则看准时机，吩咐喜兰将丫鬟们叫进来。
[EN] Zuiyun Tower outshone even the grandest taverns in the capital—more than worthy of Prince Yi's rank—and its riverside setting along the Qiantang River promised both to delight the prince and put Lin'an's officials at ease. The very day Prince Yi had departed for Bianliang, Chen Jianglin had hired top chefs and seasoned waiters from every major establishment at steep wages to bolster Zuiyun Tower's existing kitchen and management, then stocked every delicacy and decoration needed for the feast.
[ZH] 只见进来七八位丫鬟，一个个手里托着木盘，盘中整齐摆放着锦盒，大家都不明所以。
[EN] Shen Jianchen had visited Zuiyun Tower twice before—neither a stranger nor a regular—and the second visit was seared into his memory. Upstairs in that private booth, he and Qianli Xun had spoken alone—perhaps for the very last time. Opportunities like that were gone for good.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷解释道：“诸位夫人，仪王妃跟随殿下来到临安，承蒙照拂，自今日起，她愿与诸位成为朋友，希望在座的女眷有事没事去幽林别居坐坐，聊聊天儿，帮她解解闷儿。今日仪王妃带来见面礼，礼物不贵重，重在交个朋友。”
[EN] When Shen Jianchen escorted Qiao Mengrong from their carriage outside Zuiyun Tower's gates, Zhu Xiyan led Lin'an's titled officials and their families out to greet them. Everyone bowed and offered wishes for a hundred years of wedded bliss—and what delighted Qiao Mengrong most was how quick they all were to address her as "Princess Consort Yi."
[ZH] 只见丫鬟们从主桌起，一一将礼物给了。
[EN] Prince Yi moved on with the officials to the men's banquet hall, while Madam Xiang took Qiao Mengrong's hand to guide her to the ladies' wing. Drinking in the opulent yet tasteful decor along the way, Mengrong felt a quiet glow of satisfaction. At the head table, the spread rivaled the imperial wedding feast back in Bianliang; Lin'an's officials had spared no effort.
[ZH] 有些女眷打开，看到是鸽子蛋大小的珍珠，甚是惊讶喜悦，也觉出仪王妃真诚交友的心意来。
[EN] Not a single lady dared take her seat until Qiao Mengrong sat first, giving her a rush of supremacy she had never tasted in Bianliang. Back in the capital, imperial women swarmed the palace; hardly anyone gave a mere commandery princess a second glance, and even His Majesty's favorite courtiers had to be treated with deference. Here in Lin'an, she stood at the very pinnacle of every woman in the room, and every eye had to answer to her.
[ZH] 所有女眷拿到礼物后，均起身齐声拜谢：“感谢仪王妃垂爱！”
[EN] Right beside her sat Madam Xiang—Zhu Shuzhen's own mother—wearing a careful, anxious smile lest she give the slightest offense. Yet that woman's daughter had had the gall to worm her way into Brother's heart and trample the friendship Mengrong had treasured into the dirt. *Just wait,* Mengrong thought. *Payback is coming soon.*
[ZH] 宴会快结束时，喜兰分别在苗知瑜和白梅若耳边说了几句。
[EN] After the wives and daughters offered their bows and a round of flattery, Matron Zou chose her moment and signaled Xilan to summon the maids. Seven or eight servants filed in bearing lacquered trays neatly arranged with brocade boxes, drawing puzzled looks around the hall.
[ZH] 陈文心看在眼里，心想：“听说仪王妃与这二位走得近，看来没错。唉，以前她与淑真才是闺中密友，都怪那位挨千刀的仪王，偏偏喜欢上我家淑真，搞得朱陈两家紧张不说，淑真与仪王妃的友情想要修复都难。”
[EN] "Ladies," Matron Zou announced, "since arriving in Lin'an with His Highness, the Princess Consort has been grateful for your hospitality. From this day on, she hopes to count you all as friends, and welcomes you to drop by Youlin Retreat whenever you have a spare moment to chat and help pass the time. Today she has brought a small token of greeting—nothing grand, just a mark of friendship."
[ZH] 男宾区内，自然也是巴结奉承，申简辰甚觉无趣。人在江湖混，有些事身不由己，即使再不乐意，依然需笑脸应付。
[EN] Starting from the head table, the maids presented a box to every guest. Several ladies lifted their lids to reveal pearls the size of pigeon eggs, gasping in delighted shock and feeling certain of the Princess Consort's sincere desire to befriend them. As soon as every guest held her gift, the entire hall rose and bowed in unison. "We thank Princess Consort Yi for her gracious favor!"
[ZH] 宴会终于结束，申简辰将乔梦容送回幽林别居，他则收拾行李，继续乘坐马车前往司理参军的衙门报到。
[EN] As the feast neared its close, Xilan leaned down to whisper briefly into Miao Zhiyu's ear, then Bai Meiruo's.
[ZH] 哥哥收拾行李，乔梦容心里不好受，也并未前去阻拦。待会儿会有人来，哥哥走了，她正好光明正大住进哥哥的院子，在那里接见朋友，安排明日之事。
[EN] Chen Wenxin caught it all from her seat. *So the rumors about the Princess Consort growing close to those two are true,* she thought with a sigh. *Shuzhen used to be her closest friend. Blast that damned Prince Yi—of all women, why did he have to fall for Shuzhen? Now both the Zhu and Chen families are walking on eggshells, and Shuzhen's friendship with the Princess Consort may never recover.*
[ZH] 宴会上仪王妃身边的丫鬟悄悄耳语后，苗知瑜和白梅若心中甚喜，她们还未抽出时间到幽林别居拜访贵人，贵人竟然主动邀请她们，实在幸运。
[EN] Over in the men's hall, flattery and toasts dragged on just as tediously, boring Shen Jianchen half to death. Still, living in this world meant swallowing plenty of things you hated and plastering on a polite smile anyway.
[ZH] 宴会结束后，二人不约而同来到幽林别居。她们的马车刚停下，两人通过马车的窗户瞧见白侍卫赶着仪王的马车离开，她们连上前打招呼的机会都没有。
[EN] When the banquet finally wrapped up, Shen Jianchen saw Qiao Mengrong back to Youlin Retreat, then packed his trunks and rode off in his carriage to report for duty at the Judicial Investigator's yamen.
[ZH] 二人走下马车，进入幽林别居。喜兰正在半路上等着，将她们带进正院。
[EN] Watching Brother pack stung Qiao Mengrong, yet she did not lift a finger to stop him. Guests were on their way; with Brother gone, she could move right into his main courtyard in the open, receive her allies there, and put tomorrow's scheme into motion.
[ZH] 眼前的院子比上次来时见到的院落更大。也是，郡主已成仪王妃，自然与仪王住在一处。
[EN] Thrilled by Xilan's quiet invitation at the banquet—they hadn't even found a chance to call on the royal bride yet, and here she was summoning them herself!—Miao Zhiyu and Bai Meiruo both hurried straight to Youlin Retreat after the feast. Just as their carriages pulled up, both caught sight through their windows of Guard Bai driving Prince Yi's carriage away, leaving no chance to greet him.
[ZH] 她们一起进入前厅，先行礼拜见贵人：“仪王妃万福。”
[EN] Stepping down, the two women entered Youlin Retreat, where Xilan stood waiting along the path to escort them into the main courtyard. It was far grander than the side courtyard they had visited before—natural enough, now that Princess Jiahe was Princess Consort Yi and shared quarters with the prince.
[ZH] 乔梦容客气道：“两位快请坐，喜兰，上茶。”
[EN] Entering the front hall together, they bowed low. "Blessings upon Princess Consort Yi."
[ZH] 二人各自落座，喜兰将茶给二人摆上后继续站在郡主身后伺候。
[EN] "Please, sit," Qiao Mengrong said with smooth courtesy. "Xilan, bring tea."
[ZH] 不等二人说话，乔梦容主动说：“我想明日在幽林别居举办一场诗会，将二位请来，是想让你们帮我出出主意。”
[EN] Once they were seated and Xilan had set down their cups before taking her post behind her mistress, Qiao Mengrong wasted no time. "I plan to host a poetry salon here at Youlin Retreat tomorrow. I asked the two of you over to help me plan it."
[ZH] 二人心领神会，明白仪王妃的目的并不在诗会。
[EN] Both women caught her drift at once—poetry was hardly the real point.
[ZH] 白梅若先说：“不知王妃举办诗会想请哪些人？”
[EN] Bai Meiruo spoke first. "Whom does Your Highness intend to invite?"
[ZH] 乔梦容说：“人不在多，关键在精，该请的人一定要请。”
[EN] "Quality over quantity," Qiao Mengrong replied. "The right people must be there."
[ZH] 白梅若继续说：“幽栖居士一定要请。”
[EN] "Youqi Jushi, of course," Bai Meiruo prompted.
[ZH] 乔梦容点点头：“不仅要请她，还需品品她的诗词才好。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong nodded. "Not only must we invite her—we should critique her verses too."
[ZH] 三人不约而同露出笑容。
[EN] All three women broke into knowing smiles.
[ZH] 苗知瑜说：“既然是品幽栖居士的诗词，不如我献上一首。昨日文渊让南双到陈府向幽栖居士讨得一首词，我觉甚好，诗会中定会令仪王妃满意。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu leaned forward. "If we're critiquing Youqi Jushi's poetry, let me contribute a piece. Yesterday Wenyuan sent Nan Shuang over to the Chen Manor to ask her for a new lyric. I find it quite... fitting. It's sure to please Your Highness at tomorrow's gathering."
[ZH] 乔梦容说：“我相信苗夫人的眼光，到时还请苗夫人多带些闺中密友前来，一定要告诉她们如何品好幽栖居士的词。”
[EN] "I trust Madam Miao's judgment," Qiao Mengrong said. "Bring several of your close female friends along, too—and be sure to brief them beforehand on just how to critique Youqi Jushi's work."
[ZH] 苗知瑜说：“王妃放心，我定会安排妥当。”
[EN] "Leave it to me, Your Highness. I'll arrange everything."
[ZH] 白梅若想到一个好主意：“诗会中，我们必然高谈阔论，甚是难得，不如让人将这些高谈阔论记录下来，刊印成册，免费发放，相信要不了多久，幽栖居士的‘美名’更会广为人知。”
[EN] A bright spark crossed Bai Meiruo's face. "Our discussions at the salon are bound to be full of brilliant insights—what a waste if they vanished into thin air! Why not have everything written down, printed into booklets, and handed out free all over town? Before long, Youqi Jushi's 'illustrious reputation' will be known far and wide."
[ZH] 乔梦容听后大加赞赏：“白夫人的主意甚好，你们二人都识文断字，苗夫人更是才女，此事交由二位，事成之后，定有奖赏。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong's eyes lit with approval. "An inspired idea, Madam Bai. Both of you are well-read, and Madam Miao is a true literary talent. I'll entrust this task to you two—once it's done, you'll be handsomely rewarded."
[ZH] 三人筹谋一番后，乔梦容才与二位拉起家常：“我离开这段时日，不知临安有没有什么趣事发生？”
[EN] With the scheme settled, Qiao Mengrong eased into casual chatter. "Has anything amusing happened in Lin'an while I was away?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜说：“趣事倒没有，不过，我听闺中密友议论过一件事。”
[EN] "Nothing amusing," Miao Zhiyu replied, "though my friends have been buzzing over one piece of gossip."
[ZH] 乔梦容敏锐地感觉出与朱淑真有关：“什么事？不妨说来听听。”
[EN] Sensing it involved Zhu Shuzhen, Qiao Mengrong perked up. "What gossip? Tell me."
[ZH] 苗知瑜说：“听说幽栖居士亲自带人冲进春香楼，把陈公子绑回陈府后给关起来。”
[EN] "Word is Youqi Jushi personally led servants straight into Chunxiang Brothel, tied up Young Master Chen, hauled him back to Chen Manor, and locked him up."
[ZH] 白梅若应和道：“我也听说，她一个大家闺秀竟然跑到那种地方找夫君，真不害臊。”
[EN] Bai Meiruo chimed in with a sniff. "I heard the same! A proper lady from a respectable family barging into a place like that to drag her husband home—how shameless!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜继续说：“昨日我回娘家，听我母亲说幽栖居士十分厉害，竟然把陈公子给管教过来，也不知她使了什么手段。”
[EN] "And when I visited my parents' house yesterday," Miao Zhiyu added, "my mother told me Youqi Jushi is quite formidable—she actually whipped Young Master Chen into shape, though nobody knows what tricks she used."
[ZH] 乔梦容问：“秦夫人如何得知？”
[EN] "How did Madam Qin hear about that?" Qiao Mengrong asked.
[ZH] 苗知瑜回道：“我母亲喜欢打马吊，陈家二爷的娘子包氏偶尔也喜欢打马吊，会与我母亲约在一起。昨日两人又在一起玩儿，我母亲正是从她口中得知。我母亲还说，包氏提起陈浩宇变化之事，看上去并不高兴。”
[EN] "My mother loves playing madiao, and Second Master Chen's wife, Madam Bao, plays with her now and then. They got together for a game yesterday, and Madam Bao spilled it all. My mother also noticed Madam Bao looked none too thrilled when she mentioned Chen Haoyu turning over a new leaf."
[ZH] 乔梦容问：“为何？”
[EN] "Why?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜说：“我母亲猜测可能包氏担心陈浩宇变好会影响自家儿子继承陈家家业。”
[EN] "Mother guessed Madam Bao is worried Chen Haoyu shaping up might threaten her own son's shot at inheriting the Chen fortune."
[ZH] 乔梦容心生一计：“辛苦苗夫人向秦夫人转告，多约包氏打几回马吊，将陈家的事情了解清楚，也许以后会有用。”
[EN] A fresh plot crystallized in Qiao Mengrong's mind. "Please pass word to Madam Qin for me—ask her to invite Madam Bao for a few more games of madiao and sound out everything going on inside the Chen Manor. It may prove useful down the line."
[ZH] “是。”苗知瑜答应着，心下思量，幽栖居士得罪仪王妃，恐怕以后没有好日子过。
[EN] "Yes, Your Highness," Miao Zhiyu agreed, thinking to herself that after crossing Princess Consort Yi, Youqi Jushi's good days were numbered.
